श्री गणेशाय नमः

## SARAVALI

OF KALYANA VARMA

## सारावली

[श्रीमत्कल्याणवर्मविरचिता]

VOL II

English translation, commentary, annotatlon and editing by

R. SANTHANAM

## RANJAN PUBLICATIONS

16, Ansari Road, Darya Ganj, NEW DELHI-110002 (INDIAI

## Preface

Presented herewith is the second part of SARAVALI with Kalyana Varma's original slokas in Devanagari and my English translation with notes etc.

Chapters 27 to 29 deal with effects of Jupiter, Venus and Saturn in various signs and of various aspects thercto. Discussed in ch. 30 are results of the planets from the Sun to Saturn placed in the 12 houses individually. Planetary conjunctions in angles have specific say on the horoscope as shown in ch. 31 while ch. 32 is specially devoted to enlighten the reader about the 9th house. In the said chapter 32, Jupiter is given special importance as he is the chief indicator of fortunes. Further, conjunction of planets in groups of 2, 3 and 4 are skilfully narrated under the 'Ninth House’. The tenth house has been fully depicted in ch. 33 while ch. 34 deals with other houses. In the said ch. 34, the 8th house has been excluded as ch. 40 and ch. 47 are devoted in the context of longevity and death. A number of slokas can be found in the voluminous ch. 35 and combinations to mar Rajayogas are listed in ch. 39. Plancts enjoy certain number of rays at birth due to their positions etc., and the total contribution of rays is a yardstick to many events as could be seen in ch. 36. Pancha Maha Purusha Yogas find place in ch. 37 while ch. 38 specially deals with the three gunas, 5 tatwas and 3 temperaments. Dasa calculations, effects of periods etc., are accommodated in chapters from 41 to 44. Results of planetary dignitarics like exaltation, debilitation, own house etc., are arranged in ch. 45. Interesting information is added about female horoscopy in ch. 46. This part of the text is beautified by very useful information concerning lost horoscopy describing qualities etc. of ascending sign, Navamsa, Hora, decanate etc. In a way it can be said that these rules are rarely found in other texts. Ashtaka Varga and Manifold Births conclude the text.

After going through the whole text, it is hoped, that the reader will find the possession of this grcat work worthy.

I have relied on a vast number of texts and used instructions contained therein. These include Jataka Martanda (Telugu), Chamatkara Chintamani (Sanskrit-Hindi), Jataka Sagaram (Tamil), Jatakalankaram (Keeranuru Nataraja - Tamil) and late C. G. Rajan’s works. My grateful acknowlcdgements are due to them.

The printers—Navneet Printcrs, Delhi—have been quite co-operative, so also the publishers—Ranjan Publications. Both of them deserve a notabie mention in the completion of this unique work.

New Delbi

R.Santhanam

## श्रीगणेशाय नमः सारावली SARAVALI

## अथ गुरुचाराध्यायः ।२७।।

Chapter 27

## Effects of Jupiter in Various Signs

वादिगुर्णः सम्पन्नः प्रयत्नरत्नाभरणसंसर्गः । सत्त्वास्मजार्यबलयुक् प्रगल्भविस्यातकर्मा च ।।११। ओजस्वो बहुशत्रुबहुव्ययार्थ: क्षतांकितशरीरः । चण्डोप्रवण्डनाथो जीवे क्रियगे भवेत् पुरुषः ।।२।।

1.2. JUPITER IN ARIES: If Jupiter is posited in Aries at birth, one will be argumentative in disposition, will acquire precious stones and ornaments out of his efforts, will be endowed with strength, sons and wealth, will have eminent and famous profession, be splendorous, will have many enemies, much expenses and an injured body and will confer fierce and cruel punishments.

Notes: Varaha Mihira says that if Jupiter is placed in Arles or in Scorpio, the person will be wealthy, be a leader of army, be endowed with many wives and children, be charitable and forgiving in disposition and be brave and famous. However, for Aries ascendant and for Virgo ascendant, Jupiter in Scorpio and Aries respectively will curtall the longevity.

Jupiter in a house of Mars will give a tendency to invest in gold. There will also be chances of abdominal operatlons, particularly if the position is in the 5th house. If he occupies favourable Amsas, one may hope to obtain obedient and prosperous children; otherwise there will not be happiness from children. With the company of Saturn in Aries, Jupiter loses his benefic tendencies. (In other houses also, the conjunction of Jupiter and Saturn will spoil Jupiter and improve Saturn, except in Libra, Sagittarius, Aquarius and Pisces where both may be in good moods whereas Jupiter in Capricorn is greatly repaired by the company of Saturn).

## For the various ascendants, Jupiter in Aries will give varied effects as hinted below:

Aries ascendant: The native will be corpulent, beautiful, healthy, wealthy, liberal and famous. There may be a significant health problem for the wife of the native, relating to ears, stomach, delivery of children, chest etc. She will have very bulky breasts. In Jupiter dasa, the native will achicve a high degree of financial and professional prosperity. Taurus ascendant: The native will be endowed with long life, freedom from diseases and be successful over enemies. Financlally, this is not a good position. To add to this there will be heavy burden of expenditure. Gemini ascendant: One will have great success after marriage and will flourish well in profession. Normally the native is the eldest issue of the family. If the said Jupiter occuples the 4th quarter of Aswini, much superior results will be obtained. Cancer ascendant: Jupiter in Aries will generate a powerful Rajayoga conferring wealth and position. However, the subject will not be free from adversities in fortunes. He will invoke enmity with people relating to his profession. Leo ascendant: The subject will enjoy legendary luck, will have fortunate children, healthy body and be very intelligent. The life span will be above seventy. If the ascendant is in Vargothama with Jupiter in Bharani first quarter, birth of a prince is denoted. Virgo ascendant: This is a very unfortunate position of Jupiter for a Virgo native in the matter of

his own happiness, mother, wife etc. He will have a curtailed longevity. His financial position will be very shaky. Penury will be experienced for most part of the life or debts will be predominant. Libra ascendant: The native's co-born will be inimical to him. His marriage may not be so happy but will not be all the worse. A saved situation will, however, prevail. The wife of the native will be prone to accidents, particularly if Jupiter occuples many divisions of Mars in the process. Scorpio ascendant: Utter poverty, lack of progeny, mental diseases (more so ulcers of the brain), ups and downs in profession and gains from cousins/ enemies will come to pass. In the case of females, menstrual problems, heavy discharge of blood etc., will be felt. If the usual menses occur when transit Mars is in Libra, she is susceptible to serious vaginal diseases. For either natives, If Jupiter occupies Sagittarius Dwadasamsa in Aries, there may be a number of children. Sagittarius ascendant: One. will have notable spiritual achievements with Jupiter in Aries except in Aswini second quarter and Bharani third quarter, ie. the Navamsas of Venus. He will be highly intelligent, will hold high positions in government, will have abundant paternal inheritance and will himself progress enviably. Marks like mole, scar etc., will be found on the left ear. Capricorn ascendants: Happiness is almost absent with Jupiter in Aries. The native will be disappointed in regard to mother, landed property etc. Even the co-born will suffer some losses on account of the native. Aquarlus ascendant: The native’s birth will prove a boon to the father in the matter of fortune. He is equally auspicious for his brothers. Marriage will confer gains and happiness. Pisces ascendant: This placement may produce some slight health hazards. Otherwise, there will be plenty of education, wealth, good speech, fortunate father, high position etc.

पीनो विशालदेहः सुरद्विजगवां च भक्तमान् कान्तः ।

सुभगः स्वदरानिरतः सुवेवक्विगोधनाढ्श्च ।३। सद्वस्तुभूषणयुतो विशिष्टवाङ मतिगुणो नयज्ञश्च । वृषमे गुरौ विनीतो भिषक् प्रयोगाप्तकौशलकः ।।४।।

3-4. JUPITER IN TAURUS: Should Jupiter be in Taurus, one will be endowed with a broad body, be corpulent, will honour Bhahmins and Gods, be splendorous, fortunate, attached to his wife, be endowed with good appearance, profession, cows and abundant wealth; will possess good articles and ornaments, be distinguished in speech, intelligence and skill; will have political/judicial wisdom, be modest, be endowed with medical accomplishments and be skilful in experiments.

Notes: For Jupiter’s placement in a sign of Venus (i.e. in Taurus or in Libra), sound body, wealth, friends, sons, charitability and such other results are attributed by Varaha Mihira and Gunakara.

Venus, being the preceptor of demons, was gifted with unique possession of giving life to dead. As such life-giving power, peculiar to medical profession, is indicated by the placement of Jupiter, the Divine Preceptor, in Taurus. Broadly speaking the native with Jupiter so placed will be a doctor of high repute, particularly when born of Leo ascendant.

Jupiter in a sign of Venus, all said and done, be it Taurus or Libra, is capable of giving adverse results in the matter of progeny. The native's children may be inimical to him while in a female's case the husband may not be welldisposed to her.

For individual ascendants, Jupiter in Taurus will produce the undermentioned effects:

Aries ascendant: Jupiter in Taurus will supply plenty of money. It is not, however, safe for immunity from expenses. A good deal will be spent on charitable acts or nonreturning loans. Taurus ascendant: Even though a benefic by nature, Jupiter in this sign as ascendant will not be helpful for longevity. However, he will not cause child mortality. On the contrary he will counteract such evils. This position will have adverse effects on progeny, marriage and father. Geminl ascendant: Professional growth will be hindered by Jupiter in Taurus. Marriage will be secret if

Venus joins Jupiter but be a honourable one. Cancer ascendant: The native will have allround happiness. Loss of children, gain by marriage and good fortunes are indicated. Deafness or ear disease will follow if Jupiter is severely afflicted. Leo ascendant: There will be high degree of knowledge, particularly in subjects like electronics, cinema etc. A long life is indicated by Jupiter in Taurus, in the 10th. Though there may be almost happy times in profession, if Jupiter’s dasa occurs its end will cause miseries. Virgo ascendant: Marital misunderstanding, loss of wife, disrepute etc., will come to pass if Jupiter is heavily damaged. Even with a better Jupiter, adversities of this nature will prevail to some extent. A complete cure is assured only by a strong Mercury/Venus. Libra ascendant: The native will live only for a moderate period with Madhyamayus. If Raha is simultaneously in Taurus, death may be violent. Though the lord of the 6th in the 8th is a good augury for a long life, Jupiter in the 8th for a Libra native will prove adverse in his dasa occurring at birth and will inflict death. Scorpio ascendant: This planet in 7th will prove fatal at the age of 30. One will acquire a beautiful wife and will llve a happy married life. Sagittarius ascendant: The subject will be highly learned, highly placed and be famous. But there is a likelihood of his having some serious disease if Jupiter occuples the first Navamsa of Taurus. Capricorn ascendant: Infirm memory, instable children and irresponsible father will result, particularly, if Jupiter is heavily afflicted. If he is in Nidravastha the children will be weak in constitution. (For Avasthas see Sanketa Nidhi, Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra, Hora Ratna etc.). Aquarilus ascendant: Normally good results will be experienced in regard to wealth and learning. A very long-living mother is indicated by this placement. Pisces ascendant: A high degree of success in profession, marriage etc., will be achleved by the native, in Venus dasa while Jupiter dasa will be only ordinary. This is a prosperous indication for co-born while for the native it is likely to be fatal at the age of five. If the Moon is in the 8th simultaneously, this child mortality is confirmed.

आहितधनः सुमेधा विज्ञानविशारदः सुनयनश्च । वाग्मी दाक्षिण्ययुतो निपुणः स्याद्धर्मशीलश्च ।५।। मान्यो गुरु्बन्धूनां मण्डनमांगल्यलब्धवरशब्दः । मिथुनस्थे देवगुरौ क्रियारतिः सत्कविश्वव ।।६।।

5-6. JUPITER IN GEMINI: Should Jupiter be in Gemini, the native will be affluent, scholarly, proficient, will possess attractive eyes, be eloquent, courteous, skilful, virtuous, will honour elders and relatives, will be able to utter “bejewelled" words (i.e. be litcrarily rich in words) which are benevolent in nature, be devoted to his duties and be a good poet.

Notes: One with Jupiter in a sign of Mercury (i.e. either in Gemini or in Virgo) will enjoy ministership, happiness, wife and progeny according to Varaha Mihira.

Jupiter in a house of Mercury or vice versa will confer an unusual degree of wisdom and intelligence. Whereas Jupiter concerned confers profane knowledge and Mercury in Sagittarius makes one an expounder of divine doctrines. Jupiter in Gemini will make one a successful poet or writer.

How Jupiter remaining in Gemini acts for each ascendant can be seen from the following:

Aries ascendant: The native will be blessed with a helping father and a virtuous wife. Co-born will be a source of great strength in the advancement of the native. If Jupiter falls in Scorpio Navamsa, these good effects will considerably be minimised. Taurus ascendant: There will be a vast expansion of the native's descent. If, however, his Jupiter dasa should come in childhood, survival needs vital help from other qualified planets. After this, his longevity will be considerably good. The subject will suffer from discases of private parts, anus, kidneys etc. Gemini ascendant: Here again childhood coinciding with Jupiter's major period will be critical. Stability in prosperity will come along with marriage. Even marriage will be stable with Jupiter in

Gemini as ascendant as otherwise he is a baneful lord for this sign on the east. That is to say, for Gemini births, one of the best positions for happy marrlage is Jupiter in the ascendant itself. Cancer ascendant: Jupiter in the 12th will confer riches, learning etc. If Saturn aspects (except from Aries) Jupiter, the father of the native will be long-lived. Otherwise, this position indicates a defect for father’s life. The native will be free from diseases, inimical troubles, debts, litigations etc. He will vigorously associate himself with spiritual missions. Leo ascendant: Singly Jupiter in the 11th can ward off many afflictions relating to longevity. The native will obtain very fortunate children. He will be timid in disposition. His wife may need to perforin some propitiations so that she does not suffer from abdominal disorders. This is particularly true if Jupiter is in malefic shashtiamsas. (For shashtiamsas, see page 25, Vol. I of this edition.) Virgo ascendant: Though there will be overall satisfaction in learning, money, diseases, profession etc., some strain or the other is not uncommon. The native can, however, become very famous and popular in his undertakings. Libra ascendant: Early demise of father is indicated if Mercury is not stronger than Jupiter. Even if Mercury is with positional strength, say in Virgo, it is sufficient to overcome this defect. The native's 30th year of age may prove critical or may bring difficulties akin to death. Scorplo ascendant: A very unfortunate placement indeed for a key planet like Jupiter for this ascendant. This situation will adversely tell upon longevity, finance and the like. Jupiter in the 8th as the lord of the 5th in an unfertile sign will stall the progenic ability of the subject. Additionally if Jupiter is in dire combustion, the virility is almost burnt never to be alive again. Sagittarius ascendant: Jupiter in the 7th will bring evils to life in the 20th year of age. The native's father will die in Sun’s sub period in the major period of Jupiter or vice versa if the Sun is simultaneously in Libra /Scorplo. Capricorn ascendant: The native will face a checkered career while he will have an upper hand in litigations over enemies and will establish himself in money matters. However, there is no question of Jupiter granting big riches.

Aquarius ascendant: The native will have only one child, that too after a long spell following marriage. Pisces ascendant: He will have a long living mother, a good extent of landed property and a great success in his field of profession. What will, however, lack is personal security in the 10th year of age. Barring this year, he will have a long lease of life.

विद्वाम् सुरूपदेहः प्राज्ञः प्रियधर्मसत्स्वभावश्च। सुमहद्बलो यशस्बो प्रभूतधान्याकरधनेशः ।।७।। सत्यसमाधिसु युवतः स्थरात्मजो लोकसत्तः स्यातः । नृपतिर्जीवे शशिभे विशिष्टकर्मा सुहुञ्जनानुरतः ।८।।

7-8. JUPITER IN CANCER: If Jupiter be in Cancer at birth, the native will be a scholar, be beautiful, be highly learned, charitable, good-natured, be very strong, be famous, will possess abundant grains and riches, be endowed with truth and penance, will have long living sons, be honoured by all, will be a king, will have a distinguished profession and will be attached to his friends.

Notes: Gems, sons, wife, wealth, happiness and inteilect are the effects attributed by Varaha Mihira and Gunakara in the context of Jupiter occupying Cancer.

If he is in the first, second, sixth or eighth Navamsas, various good effects due to his placement in Cancer will be full. If however, he occupies the seventh navamsa, 1.e. being between 20° 1' and 23° 40' many good sides are eclipsed with Rajayogas becoming defunct. This statement is more specifically valid for signs Cancer, Libra and Aries if be ascendant.

If Mars joins Jupiter effectively, the benefic qualities of Jupiter are practically upset with Mars turning highly favourable. Similar is the case with others, viz., Saturn, Rahu and Ketu. In the combination with Sun, Mercury, Moon or Venus, Jupiter in Cancer does not lose his potence while both gain in the yoga.

The individual ascendants stand to reflect following effects as Jupiter occupies Cancer, his favourable sign:

Aries ascendant: Jupiter in the 4th in Cancer will give excellent results concerning the native's education, positlon etc. A great deal of paternal inheritance, particularly in the form of lands, will be granted by this placement. The native will be very lucky, long living, highly spiritual and be not much interested in sexual life. Opposed by Mars from Capricorn, Jupiter can make the person one of the best lot on the earth. Taurus ascendant: The subject will have a number of co-born. He will live long and be in a position to enjoy treasures or wealth left by others. This position can give large ears. The wife of the native may bring enormous wealth. Gemini ascendant: In Jupiter’s dasa, there will be abnormal increase in wealth, fortune, female’s wealth, position etc. If birth happens to be within two ghatis (i.e. 48 minutes) of midnight/midday in Gemini with 2nd having exalted Jupiter, the subject will have all paraphernalia akin to that of the richest. Cancer ascendant: This is an indication of the person enjoying abundant Poorva punya (merits of the past birth) and his soul may not be reborn, after the present birth. He will leave a mark of his own in spreading divine doctrines. If material planets like Venus are strong, there is more accent on worldly achievements. On the contrary if ultra-mundane planets like Saturn, Ketu etc., be pre-dominant, a great spiritual personage is denoted. Leo ascendant: As Jupiter has mixed trends for this ascendant, his occupation in the 12th is not jolly good for prosperity. However, it is not very undesirable as the native will have a moderate fortune to his credit. The longevity aspect will be unhurt. Virgo ascendant: If Jupiter simultaneously occupies his own Dasamsa (See p. 23, Vol. 1 of this edition), the native will have limitless professional prosperity and will enjoy an exemplary conjugal life. Libra ascendant: The native will maintain a decent and fortunate living. A sudden jolt in the form of a severe decline in profession will be experienced in Jupiter dasa in adverse sub periods. High learning, freedom from diseases, moderate riches etc.,

will follow the placement of Jupiter in exaltation in the 10th. Scorpio ascendant: An unafflicted and lonely Jupiter will indicate an aristocratic father. If Jupiter is spoilt by fallen Mars, the native is unhappy about father. Exaltation of Jupiter in the 9th house is a great boon for a female to be free from many misfortunes in married llfe. Male with this placement will enjoy allround prosperity. A significant feature of this planet's placement in a moveable sign is that the subject will be mostly on the move in the mission of his earning. Sagittarius ascendant: If the opening dasa be ruled by Jupiter, child mortality is severe. Otherwise, the native will be endowed with superior attributes, good learning, wealth etc. There will be a major financial loss in adverse planetary periods. Capricorn ascendant: A co-born will be instrumental for the marriage of the native. Though the married life will be a happy one, if Venus is not powerful, the woman concerned will suffer diseases of the mind. Aquarius ascendant: Enjoyment of money will be through acquiring debts rather than earnings. Professional success will not be significant unless Mars occupies good divisions. If Saturn occupies Cancer along with Jupiter, then the said adversities are mitigated. Pisces ascendant: In our practical experience, we note that Juptter in exaltation for a Pisces native throws some stigma in the matter of children. (Similarly in Pisces for a Scorpio native). From other points of view, this is a good placement, for example profession, health, father, fortunes, gains etc. A great deal of help will be given by the native to his elder brothers/sisters.

बृढ्वेरसत्त्वधीरः सुबहुरनेह: सुह्यजने विद्वान् । आद्यः शिष्टाभिजनो नृपो नृपतिपौरुषः सभालक्ष्यः ।।। त्रिदशगुरी सिहस्थे समस्तरोवोवद्धृतारिपक्षश्व । सुदृढव्यस्तशरीरो गिरिदुर्गवनालये : 100

9-10. JUPITER IN LEO: Should Jupiter be in Leo at the time of birth, the native will be lastingly inimical, be strong, courageous, will show abundant friendship, be learned, rich, will have eminent relatives, be a king, will have hero-

lam akin to that of a king, will be recognisable in an assembly, will destroy the entire band of his enemies, will posscss a strong physique and will live in hills, fortresses, forests and temples.

Notes: Varaha Mihira says that Jupiter in Leo will give same results as attributed by him for the planet's exaltation. An addition made by him is that Jupiter in Leo will give leadership too. So to say, to acquire unopposed leadership, Jupiter in Leo is better than the one in Cancer. This placement reveals in the native a bosom friend or a sworn enemy according to the disposition of Jupiter.

Jupiter in this royal sign, if well related, will confer fame, name and top positions in life. Right from youth, the subject will climb the ladder of success without slipping even a rung. Saturn’s sign if be on the ascendant, particularly Capricorn, will not derive full benefits from Jupiter in Leo.

## The effects by the placement of Jupiter in Leo will depend on the rising sign, as denoted below:

Aries ascendant: The native will suffer lack of progeny on account of parental curse. Even if children are born they will be an outlet of huge expenses. The good sides, however, are lasting fortune, praiseworthy habits, royal outlook and the like while the subject will be more after worldly pleasures. Taurus ascendant: The mother will suffer a diminished longevity. The native will face utter inimical situation in profession, earning etc. His longevity will be above 60 while it may be fatal during the 10th year. Geminl ascendant: The native will be liable to suffer at the hands of his furious wife. Many evils will crop up in boyhood. Cancer ascendant: Abundant evils will crop up in boyhood. Cancer ascendant: Abundant wealth, eloquent speech, religious studies, expounding grammar, logic etc., happy householdership and the like will follow Jupiter in Leo. If birth be in Saturn sub period or Mercury sub period in the dasa of Jupiter, the child may not survive. Leo ascendant: A great Rajayoga is formed provided the child passes its Jupiter

dasa if it opens the life. The child, however, is free from evils after 12 even though the dasa in question may be yet to conclude. Plenty of male children, enjoyment of ‘treasures below waters' etc., will come to pass. Virgo ascendant: Misfortunes will be prevalent in married life. If the Sun is simultancously in fall, then these are still greater. Libra ascendant: According to one school of thought the native will be devoid of good morals which statement, however, lacks weight in it. Scorpio ascendant: The native will be blessed with deep knowledge in occult sciences and will create an epoch in related fields. He will be highly learned, will gain abundantly from governmental sources and will be free from diseases, enemies, litigations etc. Sagittarius ascendant: The subject will be endowed with noble attributes, fortunate father, virtuous children and charitability. Needless to mention, he will be extremely rich. Capricorn ascendant: Longevity will be minimum with Jupiter in the 8th in Leo, which can be improved with good Saturn or by Cancer position in Navamsa for Jupiter. Wealth and learning are somewhat strained though not totally. Aquarius ascendant: Only moderate good results will be experienced in marriage, money and gains. Pisces ascendant: A very undesirable placement if unhelped by good planets. There will be pressure of debts, enemies, litigations etc. Longevity aspect will not be satisfactory and at the maximum, one may not ltve beyond 45.

मेधावी धर्मपरः क्रियापटुर्धर्मवान् युवतिराशौ । प्रियगन्धपुष्पवस्त्रः कृत्येषु विनिश्चितार्थश्च ।११। शास्त्रार्यशिल्पकार्यर्धनवान् दाता विशुद्धशीलश्व ।

स्याह्देवगुरौ निपुणश्चित्राक्षर विद्धनसमृद्धः ।।१२।।

11-12. JUPITER IN VIRGO: If Jupiter occuples Virgo at birth, one will be a scholar, be virtuous, be skilful in his work, be fond of scents, robes and flowers, will firmly gain in undertakings, will have rich experience in sastras and fine arts, be affluent, charitable, purehearted, skilful and wonderfully learned.

Notes: The native with Jupiter in Virgo will be highly learned and be a prominent educationalist. The virility of the native is somewhat paralysed in this sign. This posttion is not a very favourable augury for attaining wealth while it will not be altogether absent.

Following will reveal the performance of individual ascendants having Jupiter in Virgo:

Aries ascendant: The native will be unfortunate and be miserable. The success in profession may be limited. Some troubles out of debts will arise. Taurus ascendant: Limited progeny will be enjoyed by the native. Paternal inheritance, rich father, huge gains etc., follow this placement. During adverse bhuktis in Jupiter dasa there will be financial setbacks and losses. Gemini ascendant: The native will be happy in regard to mother, learning, marriage, profession etc.. If Mercury or Moon be in the 4th simultaneously, one will be highly learned. If Venus is simultaneously in fall as Jupiter is in Virgo in the 4th, the married life will be adverse and divorce, second marriage etc., may result. Cancer ascendant: Various kinds of diseases will be experienced by the co-born. The native will live a long span of life and be fortunate. Leo ascendant: Jupiter in this case may prove a killer. If the mutual major and sub periods of Jupiter/ Saturn be in operation, it may turn critical. As Jupiter is in Virgo, the prime consideration is the Sun or Mars for the prosperity of a Leon. If the respective planets are bereft of good disposition, the native may not raise his head at all and will live an obscure life. Only a moderate satisfaction will be given by an independent Jupiter in respect of money and children, his prime indications. Dire penury will follow if Mercury is in Pisces as Jupiter is in Virgo. Virgo ascendant: A very prosperous Jupiter dasa, good fortune, long span of life and a happy wedding will come to pass. If Mercury is also in the ascendant, it is akin to gold acquiring fragrance. Libra ascendant: This is not a very advantageous position for Jupiter to be in Virgo in the 12th. There will be expenses for the sake of vanity. Throughout, there will be some disease or the other troubling the native. Education

will be obstructed off and on. Scorpio ascendant: Plenty of wealth, wealth through enemies, acquisition of status for co-born, fortunate wife, happy married life etc., will emanate from Jupiter in Virgo in the 1 1th. Sagittarius ascendant: The native will have vast conveyances, ornaments etc., will enjoy high position and be very famous among his men. Progeny may be delayed to some extent. Large paternal inheritance will be had by the native. Capricorn ascendant: The native will lose his father in childhood. There is a likelthood of one’s having a step-mother. As a child the native will be prone to diseases. Loss of children, limited children and learned children are denoted by Jupiter placed in Virgo. Aquarius ascendant: The native will lose everything and virtually be penniless at one stage. In the front of learning, one will be well placed. Pisces ascendant; If Jupiter is alone in the 7th in Virgo or with Mercury, marriage will be a happy one. Planets like Saturn, Sun, Mars etc., in company of Jupiter will cause an unpleasant marriage. Sometimes Jupiter alone in the 7th can cause death, for Pisces native.

मेधावी बहुपुत्रो विवेशचर्यागतः प्रमूतघनः। चूचाप्रियो विनीतो नटनर्तकसंगृहीतघनः ॥ १३। कान्तः श्रुताभिनिरतो महत्तरः सार्थवाहवणिजां हि। बणिजीन्द्रमन्त्रिणि गते सुरातिथीज्यारतः प्राज्ञः ॥१४।।

13-14. JUPITER IN LIBRA: If Jupiter occuples Libra at birth, one will be a scholar, will have many sons, be endowed with foreign assignments, will be very affluent, interested in ornaments, modest, will earn money through dance and drama, be pleasing in appearance, be splendorous, learned in sastras, be superior among his colleague businessmen, will honour gods and guests and be very learned.

Notes: Jupiter (or Mercury or Venus) in Libra will bestow a high degree of latitude in business matters. (Capricorn ascending is advantageously placed with 10th house Jupiter for business success.) The native will make

enviable financial status for himself, particularly through dance, drama, movies, music, traditional sciences etc. In this sign if Jupiter occupies the 22nd decanate while Pisces is ascending, he will not be a protector but literally be cruel in the matter of longevity.

## How the varlous ascendants react with Jupiter in Libra may be known from the following:

Aries ascendant: The native will have a traditional and happy marriage. Major gains in business (or in employment) will be advantageously enjoyed. He will possess a very pleasing voice akin to that of a flemingo. If Jupiter is afflicted, discases may torment the native and his life partner. Taurus ascendant: If Venus joins Jupiter in Libra; splendid results will accrue in many matters of importance. Singly Jupiter in the 6th will strain the fronts of enemies and discases and will cause distress in married life. Gemini ascendant: One will enjoy abundant fortunes and a number of children particularly female progeny. If Jupiter is afflicted, there may be severe affliction to liver, spleen, abdomen, intestines etc., and diseases like jaundice, dropsy, indigestion, enlargement of splccn/liver etc., will show their existence. Cancer ascendant; Only mixed results will be felt in respect of mother, education etc.. If Venus or the Moon be not co-operative, then these aspects will trouble father. Jupiter in the 4th in Libra will, however, make some reasonable contribution to the longevity of the native. Leo ascendant: There may be some loss of brothers. If Jupiter dasa is past before obtaining progeny, the native may be happy in this respect. Otherwise, it will be somewhat distressing. Virgo ascendant: Plenty of money, wide success in profession etc., will come to pass but the subject is liable to suffer adversely because of debts. Marriage life will not be a happy one. High degree of learning is bestowed by this placement of Jupiter. Scorpio ascendant: Here again distress due to debts will be experienced while otherwise there will be excellent results revealed by Jupiter in the 12th in Lbra. Sagittarius ascendant: Ancestral property, earning in fair ways, attachment to virtuous acts, freedom from

planetary blemishes, enjoyment of vast paraphernalla etc., will result. Capricorn ascendant: A great deal of business success will be achieved. Yet the native should be cautious to avert a crisis in profession. Aquarius ascendant: The native will be very fortunate, wealthy and healthy. He will be happy in regard to children. Pisces ascendant: This is not a happy position for Jupiter to give good results in the matter of employment. Only ordinary effects will follow in many respects related to Jupiter, while dire poverty will torment the native.

बहुशास्त्राणां कुशलो नृपतर्वहुभाध्यकारको नियुणः । देवालयपुरकर्ता सद्बहुदारोऽल्पपुत्रश्च ॥ १५। व्याध्यार्तः श्रमबहुलः प्रसक्तरोषो गुरी भवत्यलिनि । दम्भेन धर्मनिरतो \_\_ जुगुप्सिताचारनिरतश्च ।१६।।

15-16. JUPITER IN SCORPIO: Should Jupiter at the time of one’s birth be in Scorpio, he will be expert in sastras, be a king, will be a commentator of many Bhashyas (1.e. a commentary which explains sutras word by word with comments of its own, for example, on Vedas), be skilful, will construct temples and towns, will have many wives but few sons, be troubled by diseases, will undergo many difficulties, be very flerce, be ostentatious in his performance, be virtuous and will indulge in contemptuous acts.

Notes: There will be mixed results by Jupiter’s placement in Scorpio as denoted by the text. Diseases and distresses are also resultant as are wealth and knowledge. Still the nattve will be very learned, will become skilful in the subjects of his chokce, be particularly successful in branches like Vedas etc. Modern fields of learning denote victorious space scientists, surgeons, bankers etc. From discase point of view, mostly venereal discases, piles, failure of kidneys, obstructed blood circulation etc., will result.

The 12 different ascendants get various results as mentioned below with Jupiter remaining in Scorpio:

Aries ascendant: The main accent will be on diseases

and difficulties while wealth will be very little. Misfortunes, distresses and displeasures will eclipse the life of the native. Longevity itself is severely lable to suffer. Taurus ascendant: The native will acquire a wife that may not be healthy. (With the joining of Mars the position slightly improves.) The native will himself be sickly. Financial success will, however, be significant. Limited number of children is indicated by this placement. Gemini ascendant: There will be Avayoga (impediment to prosperity), difficulties from enemies and debts, checkered career etc. Marriage will be quite delayed. Cancer ascendant: This placement may almost deny progeny. (If Mars is strong one child may be possible). A good Rajayoga is generated by Jupiter which will particularly fructify in his major period. If the Moon is simultaneously in Anuradha avoiding the 4th quarter, Jupiter will prove more efficacious and favourable. Leo ascendant: There will be allround good indications in matters relating to personal happiness, longevity etc. The sad part of this position of Jupiter is that the native will have mixed trends in livelthood. If Venus occupies the 10th, then extremely auspicious effects will come to pass in this regard. Virgo ascendant: Defects relating to throat, lungs etc., will come to surface which may even warrant severe surgical operations. The wife may inherit something very significant from her parents. Libra ascendant: Jupiter in the 2nd for this ascendant will not produce any significant Rajayoga but will increase debts and diseases, see Yogahtnamcha Runaroga vivardhanam. As the lord of the 6th, monetary difficulties will always be there with the progress of the native declining. Scorpio ascendant: The native will suffer from diseases like hernia, swollen testicles etc.. Otherwise, Jupiter in this sign as ascendant will produce excellent results in the matter of progeny, wife, family etc. Sagittarius ascendant: Litigations, misfortunes, diseases, failure in educational pursuits etc., will result. Capricorn ascendant: The native will live a happy life and through fair means. Monetarily he will be a significant person. His attainments in the fields of learning will be worth their mention. Aquarius ascendant: One will be very famous and

will pursue an independent profession. Subordination will not suit him to beget a happy meal. Financial attainments will be far too great. Pisces ascendant: Jupiter alone in the 9th will confer position of authority and high returns. An unparallel success will be achieved in the field of music, writing etc. Famous ENT specialists can be produced by Jupiter in the 9th in Scorplo.

आचार्यो ब्रतदीक्षायज्ञादीनां स्थरार्यश्च। सुहृत्स्वपक्षः परोपकारश्रुताभिरतः ।।१७। माण्डलिको मन्त्री वा धनुर्घरस्ये भवेत् सदा जीवे। नानाबेशनिवासी विविक्ततीर्यायतनबुद्धिः ॥।१८।।

17-18. JUPITER IN SAGiTTARIUS: If Jupiter occupies Sagittarius at birth, one will be a preceptor, will conduct religious vows, initiations, sacrifices etc., will have lasting wealth, be charitable, be friendly to his own men, be fond of helping others, interested in sastras, be the head of a zone or a minister, will live in many countries, will prefer loneliness and be interested in visiting shrines.

Notes: Immense wealth and a very high status is indicated by expounders like Varaha Mihira and Gunakara for Jupiter’s position in Sagittarius or in Pisces. Spiritual success, financial attainments, helpful tendency, liking for remaining aloof etc., will come to pass with Jupiter in Sagittarius. If Saturn joins, the ultra-mundane side will reveal itsclf effectively.

The undermentioned specific reactions will be made by the 12 ascendants in their turn as Jupiter occupies his own sign, Sagittarius:

Aries ascendant: The first opportunity of attaining good fortunes is grabbed by this ascendant. Its native will further have very famous and happy children and co-born while he will be free from diseases. Taurus ascendant: Even to this native, this planet will give a fair longevity, good monetary state following marriage and the like. The native

will contribute huge sums to charitable institutions for public welfare. Gemini ascendant: There will be perfect harmony in married life with Jupiter in the 7th. The wife will be corpulent and beautiful, yet will suffer health defects at some stage. Cancer ascendant: Jupiter will only give ordinary results in his dasa. If Rahu gives company, there may be infliction of some killer-disease. Leo ascendant: Obtainment of progeny will be after a great deal of ordeals. And the progeny will however be endowed with long life. Virgo ascendant: One will be happy in regard to mother, conveyances, learning etc. Marriage will be early and fortunate. Long life and successful profession is denoted. Libra ascendant: The first half of Jupiter dasa will produce favourable results to be offset in the second half. Scorpio ascendant: The native will have attractive face, big teeth, big lips, big nose and thick eye-brows. He takes birth in a very affluent family and will not be aware of any kind of monetary difficulties till the end of his life. He will unfailingly have a long life, will be honoured by one and all and will be a successful man. Sagittarius ascendant: Here is a native with divine grace. There is a school of thought saying that the native will either seek relationship with other women or will marry twice. It is hard to reconcile that one with Jupiter in Sagittarius as ascendant will seek extramarital pleasures while two marriages are not ruled out depending however on Mercury and Venus. If one of them is simultaneously in unfavourable dual signs as Jupiter occupies the ascendant identical with Sagittarius, the native is obviously destined to go in for a second marriage. Capricorn ascendant: For this ascendant Jupiter turns auspicious if he occuples benefic divisions with at least six points in his Ashtakavarga. (See the principle in regard to Capricorn ascendant—Subhavargabalairyutah ashtakavargeth shadbindoradhikam labhet yadt). Thus if Jupiter in Sagittarius obtains six bindus in his ashtakavarga, he will prove extremely favourable and act as a yoga karaka Par excellence. Aquarius ascendant: If Jupiter being in the 11th in Sagittarius is simultaneously angular to the Moon, there prevails more than the Akhanda Samrajya Yoga conferring

unhindered empire on the native. This yoga is operable right from 16 according to classical authorities. Pisces ascendant: This is a native with happiness, wealth, professional success etc. He will be free from diseases as well.

लघुवीर्यो मकरस्थे बहुत्रमक्लेशधारको जीवे। नीचाचारो मूर्षो बुरन्तनिःस्वः परप्रेष्यः ।१६।। मांगल्यदयाशीचस्वबन्धुवात्सल्यधर्मपरिहीनः दुर्बलवेहो भीरु: प्रवासशीलो विवादी च ।।२०।।

19-20. JUPITER IN CAPRICORN: Jupiter in Capricorn denotes that the native will be less virile, will experience much grief and difficulties, will be mean in conduct, be a dunce, will meet a bad end, will suffer from penury, will serve others, will be bereft of auspiciousness, mercy, purity, affection to his rclatives and of religion, will have an emaciated body, be timid, interested in living in other countries and be depressed of spirits.

Notes: Varaha Mihira and Gunakara declare that Jupiter in Capricorn will give poverty, unhappiness and meanness. In fact postted in this sign, this divine planet is bereft of all its benevolence and golden splendour. Kalyana Varma has aptly described the evils of Jupiter's fall the summary of which is that the native will have nothing to feel happy in life. It is so unless other important planets are not auspicious and strong enough to cure the evils of Jupiter in question. Preferred and qualified planets in this context depend on the ascending sign. A powerful antidote is Saturn's placement in Libra or in Aquarius. This is not that frequent as the divine scheme of planetary transits will have it. Then is not the person with good Jupiter a blessed one?

I have cited a dictum in ‘DOCTRINES FROM SUKA NADI' to say that if the 4th lord is in the 5th while the 8th is occupied by its own lord, the child of the native looks like a monkey or like a devil. Practically applied, this combination can give a very unsightly child. It may be futile to apply

this rule to each and every case of a nativity where the 4th lord is in the 5th and 8th is occupied by its lord. Instead this can befittingly be referred to Virgo ascendant having Jupiter in the 5th (4th lord in 5th in fall) as Moon is in Aries. Or refer to a Sagittarius native whose 4th lord is Jupiter. If he is in the 5th while the Moon is in Cancr, the said defect can be noticed. As for other lagnas, I doubt the theory’s direct applicability. In the two cases specified, Jupiter is himself in the house of progeny.

Now let us have a glance at the varlous ascendants having Jupiter in Capricorn, a sign of his dislike and discomfort:

Aries ascendant: Jupiter in fall in the 10th is a sign of great misfortune making one a synonym of miseries. As the lord of the 9th house, a key place in the nativity, the fall of Jupiter in another key house is very disastrous. There can be no happiness in many respects like fortune, money, profession, children, personal comforts, father etc. What else a human being needs other than these? So to say with the fall of Jupiter alone, the Aries radix loses all its charm and the life becomes worthless. Simultaneously the worst Navamsa is Capricorn (as Vargothama Neecha is further worse) and a little better Navamsa is Cancer/Aquarius for Jupiter. The other navamsas are modcrate. Taurus ascendant: The native will have a short life, will lack paternal bliss, will be bereft of co-born and will wander aimlessly. Physical distress, undue expenses etc, will be experienced. Gemini asccndant: The immediate strain will be on longevity as well as money matters. Marriage will be wrecked with wife who will be mostly sickly. Peace in family will be a white elephant to such Geminian with Jupiter in the 8th. More often he will come in trouble in his professional life. Cancer ascendant: Severe and lasting diseases, sworn enemies, father that cannot fetch even a loaf of bread, physical and fiscal defects etc., will be experienced by the native. Leo ascendant: This native is somewhat better than the natives so far discussed under the previous four ascending signs. A Leon will still be hit by diseases and will

suffer most of the weaknesses so far mentioned, but in a lesser degree. Virgo ascendant: We have already seen the applicability of a dictum from 'DOCTRINES FROM SUKA NADI. In general, the native will lack intelligence, happiness from children and merits of the past birth. When Jupiter is in fall in the 5th (or in fall in the 9th) it should be understood that the native had indulged in some irreconcilable attrocity against a divine incarnation, religious institutions and the like. The act is reflccted in the current birth in the form of Jupitcr's dire fall in the 5th (or in the 9th) which will give various kinds of unbearable miseries. Libra ascendant: Early demise of mother, enmity with brothers, financial debacles, fall from vehicles etc., will come to pass. Scorpio ascendant: Jupiter in fall in the 3rd will make him liberal—as described by Ramanujacharya in his Bhavartha Ratnakara. Truly such a nalive will be very kind to other’s difficulties and will be in readiness to sacrifice his possession for their relief. He may not have progeny at all. His married life will lack peace—an ingredient every householder looks forward to. Sagittarius ascendant: There will be unhappiness relating to children, own health and education. The face will resemble that of a crocodile (i.e. ugly) and the native is susceptible to premature fall of teeth. He will suffer a chequered career also. Capricorn ascendant: If Jupiter has Neechabhanga, the native will become very famous and rich. Otherwise simply Jupiter in this ascendant will generate all kinds of miseries particularly through brothers, friends,. expenses etc. Aquarius ascendant: Financial and progenic defects will be severe for this native. Pisces ascendant: Same as for Sagittarius.

The degree of results so far stated will be more pronounced if Jupiter is in deep fall, that too in Capricorn Navamsa. In Aquarius Navamsa, he remains greatly amended. In other Navamsas, the effect will vary with scope for hope.

## पिशुनो न साधुशीलः कुशिल्पतोयाश्रमेषु कर्मरतः । मुश्यो गणस्य सुतरां नीचाभिरतो वृशंसश्च । २१।।

## लुब्धो व्याधिप्रस्तः स्ववाष्यदोषेण नाशितार्थश्च । प्रज्ञादिगुर्णहीनो घटे गुरी स्याद्गुरुस्त्रीगः ।२२।।

21-22. JUPITER IN AQUARIUS: If Jupiter falls in Aquarius at the time of birth, one will be a tale-bearer, be ill-disposed, interested in evil jobs, be chief among his racemen, be always attached to base men, be malicious, miserly, will suffer from diseases, will lose wealth on account of his own utterances, be devoid of intelligence and virtues and will violate elder's beds.

Notes: Jupiter in Aquarius is as benefic as he is in Cancer according to Varaha Mihira and Gunakara. Varaha Mihira has ably done away with one single phrase, whereas Kalyana Varma is definite and more specific in regard to results of Jupiter in Cancer and Aquarius. This is evident from what is discussed in the respective verses. Whereas in regard to Cancer, all good effects have been stated while for Aquarius some evils are also denoted by our author. Varaha Mihira gives general effects while Kalyana Varma deals with specific effects.

In practical experience, Jupiter in Aquarius is of course as good as the one in Cancer as far as the good attributes are related. Additionally Jupiter in Aquarius suffers the Saturnine stigma and troubles the native with discases, malignant nature, self-made loss of wealth and a devil’s lust to sleep with elderly women. As far as wealth aspect is concerned, Jupiter in Aquarius docs prove helpful. To that extent Varaha Mihira is absolutely right. In fine, Jupiter is free from evils in Cancer while he will be somewhat adverse in Aquarius.

How the Aquarius placed Jupiter reacts for the 12 ascendants may be noted from the analysis infra:

Aries ascendant: With Jupiter in the 11th, there may not be any elder brother/sister born or be not alive if born. Major ear diseases, affliction to foot etc., will follow. Even a cardiac deformity can be noticed at one stage or other as

the sign Aquarius is aptly called Hridroga' (heart disease). These hints are true only when Jupiter is adversely aspected. If well related, or if Saturn is strong, relief can be declared. Monetary gains will be plenty for this native though there are chances of huge expenses. In the dasa of Jupiter, only mixed effects will follow, as this is Badhakasthana for Aries ascending. Taurus ascendant: Such a person will like an independant mode of living than subordinatlon. A strong Jupiter in the 10th can make one a politician, a lawyer, a doctor and the like. Money will flow to him uninterruptedly. There is a chance of obtaining a lame child. Gemini ascendant: The longevity aspect will reveal itself well if Mercury is well placed. Otherwise a lone Jupiter contributes to a medium span of life. The native will take to noble profession and become famous. Cancer ascendant: There will be miseries galore in the life of the native. He will lack paternal happiness, physical comforts, fortunes etc. He will acquire a wife who will be reckless in money matters and torment the native with unusual expenses. Leo ascendant: Marital harmony will not be there with even the marriage getting abnormally delayed. Children will be disobedient and shortlived. Virgo ascendant: The wife will enjoy good health. She will be mean-minded and will rage even at the slightest shortcoming. Monetary losses will be prevalent on her account. Professional hurdles will frequently disturb the peace of mind of the native. Libra ascendant: He is a bad sufferer in respect of children. They will be very unhealthy and will cause miseries to the native. The person himself is apt to risks of severe dental problems, abdominal discases and affliction to major limbs on right side. Scorpio ascendant: Abundant landed property, education, fame through high occupation, Judiclal achievements etc., will come to pass. If in the said placement, the navamsa positions of the ascendant and Jupiter respectively fall in Libra and Capricorn, severe heart problems will fructify. Sagittarius ascendant: There will be enjoyment of fortunes and pleasures. If Rahu joins Jupiter in the 3rd, disorders of throat, lung, brain etc., will come to pass. Capricorn ascendant: One will be a success.

ful householder, be wealthy and prosperous. The degree of wealth and prosperity will however depend on Mars and Venus. Aquarius ascendant: There will be mixed results in regard to wealth whilc the adverse effects state in the two slokas under comment will significan'ly come to mature. Pisces ascendant: The native will not live a happy and worthy life.

वेदार्थशास्त्रवेत्ता सुहुदां पूज्यः सतां च नृपनेता ।

श्लाध्यः सधनोऽधृष्यो ह्वाहीनवर्षस्थिरारम्भः ।।२३।। राज्ः सुनीतिशिक्षाव्यवहाररणप्रयोगवेता च । स्यातः प्रशान्तचेब्टी मीनयुगे भवति ना जीवे ।२४।।

23-24. JUPITER IN PISCES: If Jupiter is in Pisces at birth, one will be expert in knowing the meanings of Vedas and other Sastras, will be honoured by friends and virtuous pcople, will be a headsman in the king's employ, be praiseworthy, inconquerable, rich, devoid of fear, be proud, firm in undertakings, be a king, be skilful in policies, training, behaviour and war tactics, be famous and will be calm in his doings.

Notes: The good effects to be given by Jupiter in Pisces will be comparatively lesser than the ones for his Sagittarlus placement as the latter happens to be his Moolatrikona Rast as well. In the 7th Navamsa of Pisces, i.e. debilitation Navamsa, the benefic disposition of Jupiter is far less.

How the various ascendants stand to gain or lose with Juplter in Pisces may be seen from the following paragraph:

Aries ascendant: Long life, good health, auspicious acts in the family etc., are indicated. The dasa of Jupiter will give moderate results. Taurus ascendant: He will be an exponent of scriptures or a famous writer. Gemini ascendant: Abundant monetary gains, happy profession, fortune, learning, broadmindedness etc., will come into effect. Cancer ascendant: An exceedingly long living father leaving

behind a large fortune is indicated. If Jupiter is devoid of malcfic aspects, he will bestow great strength on the nativity. Leo ascendant: Long life to the native and high learning to children are denoted. The native himself will leavc behind a vast landed property for his children. As afflicted Jupiter may cause heavy financtal losses. Virgo ascendant: A chastc, beautiful and stout wife is granted by this placement. The post-marital life will be very happy. There may not be much children. Libra ascendant: Swelling in body, financial gains to a moderate extent, financial losses, moderate living etc., will follow this position of Jupiter. The native will be in distress due to debts. Scorpio ascendant: There may be snags in regard to progeny though Jupiter is in own house. There will be in some cases a chance of no progeny at all. The native will be very inteiligent and prosperous. His fortunes will be through government, justice, involvement in public life and the like. Sagittarius ascendant: If Jupiter falls in Leo Navamsa along with the ascendant, there will be serious heart problems, drying of blood, damage to bones, and loss of vision at an early age. Otherwise, Jupiter in the 4th in Pisces will increase the longevity of the native and will keep him generally happy. Capricorn ascendant: One is apt to achieve success in spiritual, religious and other similar fields. If Saturn joins in the 3rd, Jupiter in Pisces can make a Capricorn native to take to religious order giving up his mundane involvements. Aquarius ascendant: There will be huge monetary gains, a good span of life, great honour and freedom from diseases. If Rahu joins Jupiter in the 2nd with Mars/Saturn relegated to Virgo, the person will indulge in such illegal acts to earn money, viz., smuggling, black-marketing etc., Pisces ascendant: The native will be endowed with fame, learning, strength, happiness etc. A person fortunate in various sectors is thus born with Jupiter in ascendant in Pisces.

धामिष्ठमनृतमीरं

विस्यातसुतं महाभाग्यम्।

भौमगृहे रविवृष्टो ह्यातिरोमचितं गुवः कुस्ते ।२५।।

इतिहासकाव्यकुशलं बहुरत्नं स्त्रीषु वल्लभं कुर्ते। कुजगेहे शशिदृष्टस्त्रिदशनुरुः पाथिवं प्रज्ञम् ।।२६।। नृपपुरुषशूरमुग्रं नयविनयसमनिरितं च धनिनं च । अविधेयभृत्यदारं जनयति वश्रेक्षितो जीवः । २७॥। अनृतं वञ्चनपापं परविवराऽन्वेषणेषु निपुणं च । सेवाविनयकृत्तं \_\_\_ कापटिकं \_\_\_ सौम्यसंबृष्टः । २८।। गृह्शयनवसनगन्धर्मात्यालंकारयुवतिभिर्विभर्वः I संयुतमतीव भीरं कुर्ते शुक्रेक्षितो जीवः । २६। मलिनं लुद्धं तीक्ष्णं साहसिकं सम्मतं प्रसिद्धं च। अस्थिरमित्रापत्यं त्रिदशगुरुः सौरसंदृष्टः ।।३०।।

25-30. JUPITER IN ARIES/SCORPIO IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Jupiter occuples Aries/Scorpio at birth and is aspected by the Sun, the native will be charitable, will be truthful, will have famous sons, be very fortunate and will have abundant hair on the body. If the Moon aspects the said Jupiter, he will be a historical and poetical writer, will be endowed with many precious stones, be dear to women, be a king and be highly learned. If Mars lends his aspect to the said Jupiter, one is of royal scion, will be valorous, fierce, endowed with knowledge of politics, be modest, affluent, and will have a disobedient wife and disobedient servants. Mercury aspecting Jupiter as above denotes that the native will be a liar, be crafty, sinful, will be skilful in detecting other’s defects, will serve others, be grateful, be modest, and be not outspoken. If Venus aspects Jupiter as posited above, one will be very happy in respect of residences, sleeping comforts, robes, scents, garlands, ornaments and wife and be very timid. If Saturn be the aspecting planet, the subject will be dirty, miserly, sharp, adventurous, amiable, famous and will not have lasting children and friend ship.

Notes: As per standard rules of aspects the Sun has only the 7th aspect. Hence he can aspect the Aries placed

Jupiter only from Libra, i.e. the sign of fall for the Sun. Yet there may be good effects like having a famous son, being fortunate etc. It is but right to conclude that these good effects will manifest fully in the Sun’s dasa for ascendants like Leo, Cancer, Sagittarius, Scorpio and Pisces, to wit, the ascendants for which generally both these planets are favourable. In the dasa of Jupiter the degree of these effects will be less in comparison. In the process if the Sun falls in Aries Navamsa simultaneously, the good effects may be felt pronouncedly corresponding to their strengths in both the dasas. The Moon’s aspect on Jupiter placed in Aries may be a favourable planetary acquisition for lecturers/teachers connected with poetry and history, such researchers, such writers and criminologists. If the vargas of tenth house prevail effectively, then these results will be sure to follow advantageously. The aspect of Mars on Jupiter in Aries is a good augury for proficiency in politics, policy-making or justice. Mars can aspect in this connection from Capricorn (his exaltation sign), Virgo and Libra. A puissant aspect from Capricorn is capable of giving much more than stated, viz., position as a senior judge, head of inquiry commissions, head of steel/gold mines and the like. When aspecting from Capricorn Mars takes full advantages of Saturn's bright indications. The aspect from Libra is less effective followed by that of Virgo. If the Libra aspect, Libra happens to ascend, then there will be a certainty of the person going mad. In the case of aspect from Virgo, good results like good fortune, wealth etc., will follow if both the planets own good houses. Even with the good results, one can be a liar, be crafty, will involve in illegal acts, fault others, face occasional debacles and the like. For, these effects are not mutually irrelevant. The aspect of Venus from Libra is a sign of great fortune while that of Saturn in exaltation is far superior. Yet the text attributes malefic effects for Saturn’s aspect. Obviously, there is some evil in regard to progeny. That far and not more.

Jupiter posited in Scorplo and in aspect to the various planets will not be comparattvely beneficial and it may be inferred that good results will mature in a significant way only when-Jupiter is in Aries and be aspected etc.

द्विपदचतुष्पदभागिनमत्यटनं व्यायतांगमिह पुर्षम् । प्रार्तं नरेन्द्रसचिवं करोति सूर्येक्षितो जीवः ।३१। अतिधनमतीव मधुरं जननीदयितं प्रियं च युवतीनाम् । अत्युपभोगं कुश्ते चन्द्रेण निरीक्षितो जीवः ।।३२।। वयिर्त बालस्त्रीणां प्राप्तं शूरं च धनसमृद्धं च। सुलिनं नरेन्द्रपुर्वं भूगुचे जीवो रुधिरवृष्टः । ३३। प्रातं चतुरं मधुरं सुभगं विभवान्वितं गुणसमृद्धम्। सुरुचिरशीलं कान्तं जनयति बुधवीक्षितो जीवः ।।३४। अतिललितमति च धनिनं वरभूवणधारिणं दयाशीलम् । बरशयनं वरवसनं भूगुभे भूयुवीक्षितो जीवः ।।३५।। प्रार्त बहुधनधान्यं महत्तरं ग्रामनगरपुरुषाणाम् । मलिनमरूपममार्यं कुर्ते सौरेक्षितो जीवः ।।३६।।

31-36. JUPITER IN TAURUS/LIBRA IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Jupiter occupies a house of Venus at birth and is aspected by the Sun, one will be endowed with attendants and quadrupeds, will wander verily, will have a long body, be learned and be a king’s minister. If the Moon be the aspecting planet, he will be abundantly rich, be very calm, sweet, be dear to mother and wife, and will enjoy much pleasures. If Mars be the aspecting planet, he will be dear to the fair sex, be learned, courageous, affluent, happy, and be of royal scion. If Mercury be the aspecting planet, he will be learned, skilful, sweet, fortunate, endowed with riches, be highly virtuous, and be splendorous. If Venus aspects Jupiter as above, he will be very attractive, affluent, will wear excellent ornaments, be merciful and will enjoy excellent sleeping comforts and excellent robes. Should Saturn aspect the said Jupiter, he will be a scholar, will be endowed with abundant wealth and corns, be excellent among the people of his village/town, be dirty, ugly, and be devoid of wife.

Notes: If Jupiter is in Taurus (a sign denoting bull) the

aspect of Sun will prove favourable for having quadrupeds and related business. In Libra placcment, the chances are lesser. The good effects stated for lunar aspcct on Jupiter in a Venusian sign will manifest more when the aspect is from Arics rather than from Scorpio, an inconvenient sign for the Moon. There may be good effects relating to Neechabhanga Rajayoga, but this is limited to Moon’s dasa only, whereas Jupiter’s dasa will be compensatingly adverse. When Mars aspects a planet in Libra or Taurus the person may give too much of importance to matters relating to sex and such satisfaction remains a hobby and routine for him. (Similar is Venusian aspect on Aries or Scorpio.) In our present discussion the aspected planet being Jupiter, a virtuous planet, sexual instinct may be more by Mars aspecting, but the native will have a code of conduct. To wit, he camnoi go out of the way. Richness, happiness, royal disposition etc., are inherent conceptions of this aspect of Mars on Jupiter in Libra/Taurus. Since Jupiter can be aspected by Mercury in the present case only from a house of Mars, the disposition of the native will be less satwic. So, the attribute of 'highly virtuous' may not always manifest unless Jupiter is exceptionally strong to subdue Mercury. Riches, fortunes, skill, learning etc., of course will be at the disposal of the nativc. The aspect of Venus on Jupiter in the former’s sign will make him more given to vanity and false pridc. Such a native will be highly opulent and be endowed with all paraphernalia. Saturn’s aspect from Aries to Libra may not give a high degree of wealth but the evil effects stated. If the aspect is from Capricorn to Libra then the person will not only be very rich but will make money for generations. Irrespective of the place, in a sign of Venus, Jupiter being aspected by Saturn seems to give distress in marriage.

आर्य प्रामश्रेष्ठं कुटुम्बिनं दारपुत्रधनयुक्तम् ।

बुधभे बिनकरदृष्टस्त्रदशगुर्मानवं कुस्ते ॥ ३७१। चन्देक्षितस्तु कुर्ते वसुमन्तं मातृवल्लभं धन्यम्। सुखयुवतिपुत्रबन्तं सुरगुरुरतिरूपमनुपमं बुधमे । ३८।।

शाश्वतसूलब्धविजयं विहृतसुगात्रं धनान्वितं कुर्ते। धरणिसुतेक्षितदेहस्त्रिदशगुरुः सम्मतं लोके ।३६। कुस्ते ज्योतियकुशलं बहुसुतदारं च सूत्रहकारं च। अतिशयविक्पवाष्यं बुधभे सौम्येक्षितो जीवः ।।४o11 वेवप्रासादानां कृत्यकरं वेशदारभोक्तारम्। हृदयहरं नारीणां कुस्ते शुक्रक्षितो जीवः ॥ ४१३। ीगणराष्टणपरगमरतनai जनयति शनिना वृष्टः सुतनुं जीवो नरं बुधच्चे । ४२।। 37-42. JUPITER IN MERCURY’S HOUSE AND IN AS- PECT TO OTHERS: If Jupiter occupies Gemini/Virgo and is aspected by the Sun at biith, one will be great, important in his village be a houscholder and will be endowed with wife, sons and money. If by the Moon, he will be affluent, dear to mother, fortunatc, happy, will have wife and sons and be uncomparable. If by Mars, onc will be easily successful at all times, be ugly, rich and be amiable to all. If by. Mercury, one will be a skilful astrologer, will have many children and wives, and will be exponent of many aphorisms and precepts and speak with great excellence of many aphorisms and precepts. If by Venus, he will undertake acts of temple construction, will visit prostitutes and will win women’s hearts. If by Saturn, he will be the head of a group, State or village and be beautiful.

Notes: The aspect of the Sun and the Moon individually on Jupiter in a house of Mercury is essentially good for mundane riches. Mars aspecting Jupiter in Virgo from Aquarius will prove very detrimental for the progeny of the native, particularly when the ascendant is Libra. Mercury's aspect on Jupiter either in Geminl or even in Virgo is a good possession for success in occult studies. One will perform highly religlous deeds if Venus aspects Jupiter in Gemini. If Jupiter is placed in Virgo and be in aspect to exalted Venus, the tendency of liasing with whores will not be predominant in the native. The native may be inclined to

such acts but will not be in a position to convert his thoughts in practical act. Saturn’s aspect on Jupiter in a house of Mercury will bestow leadership depending on strengths.

रविदृष्टः शशिभवने विस्यातं ह्चप्रगं समूहानाम् । सुखधनदारविहीनं पश्वादाद्यं गुरुः कुस्ते ।४३।। अत्यर्थं श्युतिमन्तं नृ्षति बहुकोशवाहनसमृद्धम् । उत्तमयुवतोपुत्रं जनवेच्छशिभे गुरुहिमगुदृष्टः ।४४।। कौमारदारमाद्यं हेमालंकारभागिनं पातम्। शूरं स्रणगात्रं रुधिरांगनिरीक्षितो गुरुः कुस्ते ।४XI बान्धवमित्रसमेतं धनिनं कलहान्वितं विगतपापम्। जनयति बुधेन दृष्टः प्रत्ययिनं मनित्रणं जीवः ।।४६।। बहुदारं बहुविभवं नानालंकारमागिनं सुखिनम् । भुगुतनवदृष्टमूर्तिः सुभयं पुरुवं गुरुः कुस्ते ।४७।। सौरेण दृष्टमूर्तिमहत्तरं ग्रामरसन्यनगराणाम् । वाबाट बहुविभवं वार्धक्ये भोगमागिनं जीवः ।।४८।।

43-48. JUPITER IN CANCER IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Jupiter occupies Cancer at birth and is aspected by the Sun, the native will be famous, be ahead of others, and will (in the beginning) be devoid of happiness, wealth and wife all of which will be acquired by him later. If by the Moon, the native will be very rich, splendorous, be a king, will enjoy abundant wealth and conveyances, and will have excellent wife and sons. If by Mars, one will marry in the boyhood itself, will be endowed with gold and ornaments, be learned, valorous and will have a wounded physique. If by Mercury, one will be endowed with relatives and friends, be rich, will promote quarrels, be bereft of sins, be a minister and be trustworthy. If by Venus, one will have many wives, extraordinary riches, various ornaments, be happy and fortunate. If by Saturn, one will be important in his village or in the army or in his town, be talkative, be very

affluent, garrulous and will enjoy pleasures in old age.

Notes: The aspect of the Sun on the exalted Jupiter from Capricorn will delay marriage and begetting children. The Moon’s aspect, being that of the dispositor, will give immediate and better results than that by the Sun. (It not only applies to Jupiter but to any planet that is exalted and is aspected by its dispositor. This one single combination is enough to lead a successful and happy life in many respects.) The aspect of Mars on exalted Jupiter from Sagittarius, Aries or Capricorn is supcrior in ascending order. Mercury, Venus or Saturn aspecting an exalted Jupiter will give unfailingly good effects but these are only secondary in degree as compared to the luminaries and Mars.

सिहे दयितं स्यातं सतां च नृपाति महाधनसमृद्धम् । जनयति दिनकरदृष्टस्त्रिदशगुर्न रमतीव हि सुशीलम् ॥ ४६।। अतिसुभगमति च मलिनं स्त्रीमार्येर्पचितार्थमत्याद्यम् । लेये चन्द्रसुदृष्टो जितेन्द्रियं जनयति सुरेज्यः ॥ ५०। भक्तं सर्तां गुर्णां विशिष्टकर्माणमग्रयम तिनिपुणम् । शुद्धं शूरं क्रूरं गुरुरिह भौमेक्षितः सिहे ॥ ५१। गृह्वास्तुज्ञानरतं विज्ञानगुणान्वितं रुचिरवाक्यम् । सिहे मन्त्रिणमग्रयं बुधेक्षितो विश्रुतं जीवः ॥५२।। बयितं स्त्रीणां सुभगं भूतिसत्कारसत्कृतं पुरुवम् । सितवृष्टः सुरपूज्यो जनयति सिहे महासत्त्वम् ।।५३।। बहुकयनमधुरवचनं सुखरहितं चित्रभागिनं तीक्षणम् । अधमस्त्रीसृतपुरुषं सिहगुरुः - सौरसम्बुष्टः ॥५४।।

49-54. JUPITER IN LEO ASPECTED BY OTHERS: If at birth Jupiter be in Leo and be aspected by the Sun, one will be dear to good men, be famous, be a king, be extremely affluent, and virtuous. If by the Moon, he will be very beautiful, dirty, will be very rich through the fortunes of his wife and will conquer his Five Senses. If by Mars, he will

honour elders at all times, will perform distinguished acts, be very skilful, pure, adventurous and cruel. if by Mercury, he will have knowledge of civil works of building construction, will be endowed with profane knowledge, be virtuous, be a swect-speaker, be a minister and be highly learned. If by Venus, he will be dear to females, be fortunate, will receive royal honours and be very strong. If by Saturn, one will be garrulous, be an eloquent speaker, be devoid of happiness, be sharp and will have mean children and mean wife.

Notes: Jupiter in Leo in aspect to others except Saturn gives wealth and fortune. When Saturn aspects Jupiter in Leo from Aquarius, indeed good results should come in this regard. But as far as children are concerned, Jupiter in Leo is not very favourable and these aspects will be helpful, only when strong planets do aspect. Saturn's aspect is a great defect representing progeny.

नृपतिविरद्धं जनयति विबुधगुरुः संस्थितः स्वगुहे। रविवृष्टः परितप्तं धनबन्धुजनेन परिमुक्तम् ॥५५।। नानाविघसौव्ययुतं स्वगृहे चन्द्रेक्षितो जीवः । अतिसुभगं युवतीनां मानधनेश्वर्यगावितं कुश्ते ॥५९६।। सङ्प्रामे विक्तांगं कूरं वधकं परोपतावहरम्। जन्यति कुजेन दृष्टो देवगुर्नव्टपरिवारम् ॥५७। मनित्रिणमच नृर्पाति वा सृतधनसोभाग्यसोख्यसम्पन्नम् । स्वगृहे बुधेन वृष्टः सकलानन्दं गुरुः कुस्ते ॥५८)। सुविनं प्राहं व्यपगतदोषं चिरायुवं सुधगम्। स्वगृहे सितेन वृष्टो लक्मीपरिवेक्टितं गुरः पुर्षम् ॥ ५१।। मलिगमतीब च सभयं प्रामपुरेणिधिक्तं दोनस्। स्वगृहगुरः शनिवृष्टो जनयति सुखभोगधर्मपरिहीनम् ।१०।। 55-60. JUPITER IN OWN HOUSE AND IN ASPECT TO

OTHERS: If Jupiter ls posited in Sagittarius/Pisces at birth

and is aspected by the Sun, the native will be inimical to the king and will be miserable being bereft of wealth and relatives. If by the Moon, he will enjoy many kinds of happiness, be very fortunate in respect of wife and will have thc pride of honour, wealth and possessions. If by Mars, onc will be injured in war, be cruel, will cause torture, will harm others, and will lose children. If by Mercury, he will be a minister or a king, will be happy with wealth, fortunes and progeny and will enjoy all kinds of delightments. If by Venus, he will be happy, learned, be devoted of blemishes, be long-lived, fortunate and will be blcssed by Goddess Lakshmi. If by Saturn, he will be dirty, will be fear-stricken, be neglected by the people of his village / town and be devold of happiness, pleasures and virtues.

Notes: The aspect of the Sun on Jupiter in Sagittarius or in Pisccs is not congenial for having a satisfactory relationship with government, and relatives in the family circle. Wealth will be limited or will diminish in thls aspect, particularly for Virgo Lagna with Sun in it and Jupiter in opposition. The aspect of Mars will causc injury due to falls, quarrels, fire etc. Saturn's aspect will deprive one of honour and will torment him with fcar.

प्राज्ञं पृथिवीपालं सौरय्हे भानुना च संदृष्टः। प्रकृतिसमृद्धं जनयति बहुमोगसमन्वितं सुविकान्तम् ।।११। पितृमातृभक्तमार्ये कुलोद्भवं प्राजमाद्यमावेयम् । चन्द्रेक्षितस्तु जीवः सुशीलमतिधार्मिकं शनिभे ।६२।। शूरं नरेन्द्रयोधं गर्वितमोजस्विनं सुवेषं च। विख्यातमार्यमान्यं शनिभे बक्रेक्षितो जीवः ॥६३। कामरति गणिमुख्यं मानवमय सार्थवाहमाद्यं वा । ख्यातमतिमित्रवन्तं बुध दृष्टो गुरुः शनिमे ॥ ६४। भोज्यान्नपानविभवं वरगृहशयनासनोत्तमस्त्रीकम् । आभरणवसनवन्तं शनिभे शुक्रेक्षितो जीवः ।६५।

## अनुपमविद्यावृत्तं महत्तरं देशपार्थिवं शनिमें। द्विपदचतुष्पदमाद्यं भोगिनमथ सौरवीक्षितो जीवः ।६६।।

61-66. JUPITER IN A HOUSE OF SATURN IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Jupiter is in a house of Saturn at birth and is aspected by the Sun, one will be learned, be a king, be rich by birth, will enjoy various kinds of pleasures and be very courageous. If by the Moon, he will be devoted to his parents, be superior by birth, be learned, rich, virtuous and very charitable. If by Mars, one will be valorous, be an army chief with the king, splendorous, well-dressed, famous and honoured. If by Mercury, one will be highly libidinous, important among his folk, rich by conveyances and wealth, famous and will have many friends. If by Venus, he will be endowed with food, drinks, excellent residence, sleeping comforts, wealth, conveyances, excellent wife, ornaments and robes. If by Saturn, he will be endowed with uncomparablc learning, be supreme, be a king of his country, be rich with attendants and quadrupeds and will enjoy pleasures.

Notes: If Jupiter in Aquarius is in aspect to the Sun in good bhavas, then the richness will be right from birth. Otherwise, it may be later on but essentially father will be an instrument in acquisition of wealth. Conversely Jupiter in Capricorn versus Saturn in Cancer is supreme while Jupiter-Saturn alignment in Aquarius-Leo is of less efficacy and less benefical.

इति कल्याणवर्मविरविताया सारावस्यां गुरुचारो नाम सप्तविशोऽध्यायः ।२७।।

Thus ends the 27th chapter, entitled "EFFECTS OF JUPITER IN VARIOUS SIGNS” in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अथ शुक्रचाराध्यायः ।।२८।।

## Chapter 28

## Effects of Venus in Various Signs

## नैशान्धो बहुदोषो विरोधशीलः परांगनाचोरः।

वेश्यावनाद्रिचारी स्त्रीहेतोर्बन्धनं \_\_ : 11

क्षुद्रः कठोरचोरश्चम्पुरश्रेणिवृन्दनायश्च । मेषे स्थाद्भूगुतनये नो विश्वासी प्रगल्मश्च ।।२।

1-2. VENUS IN ARIES: Should Venus be in Aries at birth, one will be night-blind, will have many blemishes, be inimical, will join other housewives, will visit whores, will move in forests and hills, will be imprisoned on account of women, base, hard in disposition, be an army chief or chief of men, be not reliable and be eminent.

Notes: Venus in a sign of Mars will make one a disgrace to the family, take to unethical means to satisfy his lust for sexual pleasure and be poor. That is how Varaha Mihira and Gunakara describe Venus in Aries/Scorpio.

Venus in Aries will reduce the strength of sight early. Even in prime youth, one will suffer night-blindness. In the matter of sexual satisfaction, the person will not govern himself by any moral standards. With a well-disposed Venus, there will be acquisition of landed property, conveyances, ornaments etc. An ill-disposed Venus may land one in confinement.

Remaining in Aries, what is the behaviour of Venus when individual ascendants are considered? See the following:

Arics ascendant: The person will be very beautiful, soft, learned, joyous and sensuous. He will be gifted with

the ability of a poet and with tastes for fine arts etc. He will be weak-sighted and may even be blind by birth if Saturn effectively gives company. Venus singly can prove a maraka in his dasa bhuktis while he may ward off such defects in the dasas of other planets. Taurus ascendant: He will be ordinarily wealthy, will have a happy marriage and will incur debts on account of auspicious functions in the family. If Mercury joins him there will be moderate riches and if Jupiter joins both in the 12th, the Taurus native will be very opulent. Gemini ascendant: This placement will give abundant wealth, coupled with some financial downfall, and sick children. The elder child will be particularly prone to hurt by fire, chemicals, violent animals etc., while other children will be less prone. Cancer ascendant: Venus in Aries ensures a famous position in society. The native will enjoy abundant money in business of his own. If Mars is in fall simultaneously in the ascendant, then it portends severe and early evils to mother. Leo ascendant: The occupation of Venus in Aries will cause the father to have extramarital relationship. If Venus occupies Virgo Navamsa in the process, it denotes loss of children. The native may even suffer from disorders related to breathing. Virgo ascendant: While the native’s family may be large, he will suffer from acute penury or financial losses. He may lose his father early. Libra ascendant: The native will have a wife with short-life unless Mars (ruling the 8th from the 7th) is strong. The married life will be generally happy. Scorpio ascendant: He will suffer irrepairable damage through venereal diseases. Consequently, a major organ will develop serious malfunctions. His wife will be sick. In other matters, Venus will be auspicious. Sagittarius ascendant: There may be a birth of child to trouble the native's wife with major surgical operation. They may however be happy in regard to progeny in general. In a female's chart, this placement of Venus will cause burning sensation in the vagina and some other vaginal diseases. Venus in Aries gives the native (whether a female or a male) a tendency to be libidinous. Capricorn ascendant: Aries is a very excellent placement for Venus to confer all sorts of benefic results

relating to learning, profession, conveyances, wealth etc. Aquarius ascendant: He will depend on his sisters for livelihood. A strong Venus will however confer some paternal help/inheritance. Pisces ascendant: Though acquisition of wealth may be considerable, one may be in troubled waters on account of severe losses. He may have a peculiar voice.

## बहुयुवतिरस्नसहितः कृषीवलो गन्धमाल्यवस्त्रयुतः । गोकुलजीवी दाता स्वबन्धुमर्ता सुमूतिश्च ।।३।। आढचस्त्वनेकविद्यो

## व बहुप्रदः सत्त्वहितकारी ।

वृषमे गुणैः प्रधानः परोपकारी सिते भवति जातः ।४।।

3-4. VENUS IN TAURUS: Should Venus at birth be in Taurus, one will be endowed with many wives and gems, be an agriculturist, will possess scents, garlands and robes, will live on account of cows, be charitable, will maintain his relatives, will have good appearance, will be learned in many branches, will give away many things, will help the living beings and be a principal person.

Notes: One will be valorous, will earn out of his intelligence, be honoured by the king, be famous and will be endowed with relatives. This is what Varaha Mihira and Gunakara declare, in connection with Venus occupying Taurus or Libra.

The native with Venus in Taurus will generally have assoclation with many females. This is true when the planet applies badly to bad planets or remains severely afflicted. Otherwise, one may only be very sensuous but be normally within the limits. The Taurus-placed Venus will confer all the required domestic articles and possessions of a householder. There may be gain in pursuits like farming, breeding quadrupeds, running dairies, veterinary clinics etc. Gencrally the native will tend to help others with whatever he has and will become a jewel of his clan. This placement is further capable of obstructing secondary afflictions to alght if not the major ones by the luminaries. To wit, some

minor eye diseases can be easily cured with Venus in Taurus while major faults of the sight cannot be.

Taurus holding Venus holds the undermentioned effects for the individual signs of the zodiac ascending.

Aries ascendant: Abundant wealth, beautiful face, affliction to longevity of the native as well as his wife, acquisition of underground wealth and wealth through females are resultant out of Venus in Taurus. Taurus ascendant: Though the native may be generally healthy throughout, he will be susceptible to urinary diseases, veneral diseases, vision defects of minor grade, wounds on the head and the like. He will obtain good results due to Malavya Yoga. Gemini ascendant: The native will enjoy a fortunate life through marriage. He will flourish in business particularly like real estate, automobile, beauty parlour, cinema etc. However, at one stage, all his earnings or a lion’s share thereof will be spent away on charitable or auspicious deeds.
