Progeny may be delayed unless Jupiter is wellplaced. Cancer ascendant: He will have maternal and monetary gains. However, his Venus dasa will give ordinary results though a text states that Venus dasa for this native will be very good. Leo ascendant: Venus will mix the life of the native with happiness and miseries. Virgo ascendant: Family property will be enjoyed more by the coborn than by the native. He will however be rich and happy. Libra ascendant: Though Venus can give good effects in general, his dasa will inflict many troubles equal to death. While making this statement, I have not lost sight of the principle that the ascendant lord becoming the 8th lord as well will not be inauspicious. I lay more welght on the strength of the principle that a planet becomes necessarily evil when he is illplaced being Lagna Lord. This is my conviction against the dual lordship stated above. If however Venus enjoys good Shadbala, good aspects and the like these evil effects will vanish and good effects will come to pass. Scorpio ascendant: His marriage may be beneficial in general context but his conjugal partner will be prone to sickness. His Venus dasa may trouble him much. Sagittarius ascendant:

The native may be free from sickness, debts etc., but will lose quite often in financial matters. He is generally immune to diseases but if other planets are qualified to bring forth sickness, it will be in the form of anaemia, sexual insufficiencies, venereal discases, urinary ones, frequent dream discharges, troubles from ghosts (and consequent mental diseases) and crookedness of walking limbs. Capricorn ascendant: Venus in the 5th in Taurus will give huge financial benefits, high intelligence, number of female children, career sans troubles. happy marriage etc., But in Venus dasa during adverse bhuktis downfall, difficulties and distress may show their heads. Aquarius ascendamt: Parental happiness, auspicious functions, conveyances, learning, early marriage etc., will come to pass. Pisces ascendant: Long life is conferred on the native who will however lack paternal bliss.

विज्ञानकलाशास्त्रे प्रथितः सततं सुमूर्तिकः कामी । क्षालेख्यलेश्यनिरतः काव्यकर: स्यात् प्रियः साधुः IIXII धृतगीतनृतविभवः सुह्ज्जनाढचः सुरहिजानुस्तः 1 संख्डस्नेहो बै मियुनस्थे भार्गवे भवति ।।६।।

5-6. VENUS IN GEMINI: Should Venus occupy Geminl at birth, one will be famous in sciences and sastras, be beautiful, libidinous, be skilful in writing and in poetry, be dear to good people, will derive wealth through music and dances, will have many friends, will honour gods and brahmins and be firm in friendship.

Notes: If Venus is in Gemini at birth, one will be rich skilful in arts and will carry out royal orders according to Varaha Mihira and Gunakara.

A very notable character of Venus in Gemini if affilcted is a second marriage. Generally Venus in this sign will make one suffer from artheritis, lung disorders, nervous breakdown, fall from height etc. Favourable characters of Venus in this sign are: Intelligence, love for poetry, writing. decorative articles, divine worship, musical instruments

etc. To wit a strong Venus in Gemini will give these qualities and liking for these articles.

When the horoscope reveals with Venus in Gemini in the case of individual signs ascending may be understood as under:

Aries ascendant: If the Ashtaka Varga bindus are less in Gcmini, Venus may inflict early dcath on co-born—particularly sisters. Venus in this sign can give a mellifluous voice and confer legendary success in fields related to music, drama, cinema etc. The native’s fortunes are hindered occasionally. Taurus ascendant: Conferring great monetary gains cannot be looked upto Venus alone. Normally wealth will be limited with Venus in Gemini. In the dasa of Venus there will be bencficlal effects in the beginning followed by inimical activities, diseases and debts in the later part. Gemini ascendant: The native will be short (or of moderate height), attractive, intelligent, long living and will enjoy worldly pleasures to the full. He will become a poet, a writer etc. He will have moles on the face which may add to his beauty. Cancer ascendant: The native's happiness will be limited and the mother may be subjected to difficulties in her personal life. He will enjoy a smooth and prosperous married life. Leo ascendant: There will be huge monctary gains. Professionally the native will be wellplaced. He will obtain happy progeny. Virgo ascendant: Financial gains will be abundant. But for rare fall, profcssional happiness will prevail throughout. An earning wife is denoted through Venus in the 10th. Libra ascendant: There will be only mixed effects in Venus dasa. Generally Venus in the 9th denotes a second wife for a Libran. If Mars occupies the 10th, this effect is doubly confirmed. Scorpio ascendant: Married life will be most unpleasant and the marriage itself will be at a very late age. The native will attach himself to girls selling their flesh. Venereal diseases will be a daily affair in his life. His life itself will remain out short in span. He will spend away whatever he will earn. He will be susceptible to acute sight defects. Sagittarius ascendant: In this case the married life will be somewhat peace-

ful; yet the native will have to pay a heavy price for his wife’s health who will be tormented by diseases like consumption, asthma and dysentery. She will find no borders for lavishness. Capricorn ascendant: Though Venus will be auspicious in regard to marriage of the native, the native will have troubles arising out of family properties. He will suffer from burden of heavy debts. A decent monetary flow is not ruled out. Aquarius ascendant: The subject will be endowed with fortunes, happiness, learning, paternal bllss, progenic bliss, merits of the past birth and association with entertainments. The number of children will remain very limited. Pisces ascendant: His mother will be of short life span. He will sometimes suffer from serious miseries but be generally happy. His co-born will make huge riches.

मतिधर्मरतः प्राज्नो बली मृदुर्गुणवतां प्रधानश्व । आकांक्षितः सुखार्थर्युक्तः प्रियदर्शनः सुनीतिश्च ।७।। योवित्पानप्रभरव्र्याधिभिरधिकं प्रपीडितो मनुजः । शुक्रे कर्कटसंस्थे स्ववंशभवबोवसन्तप्तः ।।।।

7-8. VENUS IN CANCER: If Venus occupies Cancer at thc timc of onc's birth, he will be wisc, virtuous, learned, strong, soft, chicf among men, will have desired happiness and wealth, be good-looking, just, very much troubles on account of women and wine and will be miserable with famlly troubles.

Notes: If Venus occupies Cancer at birth, the native will have two wives, be timid, will seek help or beg from ollcrs, be greally arrogant and will suffer miseries accordlng lo Brihat Jataka and Hora Makaranda.

If thce Moon is with diminishing rays, then Venus in Cuncer will aptly give two marriages. The Moon being full will mcud Venus and the said adverse result will disappear alul lhc nallve will have only one marriage. Coming to what Kulyiu Varina says independent of the above two authorllles, ouc will be wealthy, learned, will achicve the happinean und weulth he will aim at and be famous. These effects

are not contradictory to what Varaha Mihira states. The indication of 'misery’ by Varaha Mihira has been more specifically put as troubles on account of women. wine and in family matters. Venus rules women. wine and also family—family through marriage—and his placement in Cancer denotes troubles through these sources. As stated earlier by me, these bad effects depend on Moon also and her Paksha Bala etc.. are to be reckoned with. These adversities are more referred to Aries and Virgo natives.

What is the role of Venus in Cancer for the different ascendants? See the following explanations:

Aries ascendant: The family life will not be a happy one. He will be addicted to intoxicants etc. If Mars is in fall at the same time and be in effective orbs with Venus, dual marriage or a concubine will result. Taurus ascendant: Blood diseases, mental affliction etc. will trouble the coborn. The native will have a number of sisters. He will be fortunate and wealthy depending on the phases of the Moon. (If Venus is conjunct Moon, digital evaluation of the Moon need not be done as Venus will automatically become auspicious in Samagama). Gemini ascendant: He will attain financial success but will spend on evil accounts. If the Moon falls in the process of Scorpio Navamsa he will be troubled by poisonous creatures. Leo ascendant: In this specific case, his marriage llfe will be happy. Professional debacles will be however experienced by the native. There will be mixed results in regard to wealth and general happiness. If Rahu, Ketu, Saturn or Mars join Venus in the 12th, a co-born is llable to death by drowning. Virgo ascendant: Even with slight affliction to the Moon, there will come to pass a remarriage. Family displeasures, associatlon with courtezans, gambling, drinking wine and the like, wasting diseases will mark the native. Libra ascendant: There will be fame’ and wealth through agriculture, in literacy fields and through precious stones. Scorpio ascendant: He will be a significant person in his race, be wealthy and fortunate. Sagittarius ascendant: He will be a wretch in marriage. Monetarily he will gain through or after marriage.

His family will be a scene of violent events. Capricorn ascendant: This is a very favourable placement for Venus to bestow all kinds of happiness. wealth. joyous family and a high degree of learning. Landed property will be abundant. The native will be a notable personage in at least more than one field. Aquarius ascendant: Venereal diseases, financial losses. ill-health to mother. decline in fortune and the like will result. He will be troubled by unending debts. Pisces ascendant: There will be loss of children, particularly female ones. Sometimes. with severely afflicted Venus, there may be no progeny at all.

युवतिजनोपासनको सन्धमुन्द्रविणसम्प्रमोदश्च । सघुसस्तवः प्रियबन्धृविचित्रमोग्यश्च दुःश्री च ।६। उपकारी च परेवां गुरद्विजाचार्यसंमतो निरतः। सिहस्वे भृगृतनये बहुचिन्तास्वनाभियुक्तः स्यात् ॥।१०।

9-10. VENUS IN LEO: If Venus occupies Leo at birth, the native will respect women, will enjoy wealth and happiness, will have less virility, be dear to relatives, be miscrable inspite of his happiness, will help others, will respect brahmins, elders and preceptors, and will be devoid of much discrimination.

Notes: Both Varaha Mihira and Gunakara state that Venus in Leo will give wealth through females, a bcautiful wife and a few sons (only). ‘Mandatanayah'and 'Alpathmajah'are the respective expressions in the versions of Varaha and Gunakara denoting limited sons—limited children rather. That is why Kalyana Varma's "Laghusatwa” has been interpreted by me as the native having less virlle power. The sign Leo itself is known as Alpaputra Rasi, l.e. capable of limiting progeny. It is a fiery sign and hence is capable of buring the virile power if Venus (semen) or Jupiter (progeny) occupy this sign. Otherwise Laghusatwa’can be interpreted as the person having not much physical strength etc. Be it clear that physical strength and progenic ability are two distinct possessions.

‘Upasana’has a meaning 'respect’. As this word befits the context, accordingly 'yuvatijanopasanako’has been rendered as 'respecting womcn’.

Venus in Leo is capable of causing premature seminal discharge, eye ailments and burning scnsation of the privatc organs.

The individual ascendants with Venus constantly in Leo will stand to act as under:

Aries asccndant: In the 5th, this planct is considered auspicious according to some authorities. While this cannot be wholly denied, a fear of this planct causing marital disharmony is to be had. Not only will progeny be limited, but also the children will be a source of displeasure to the native. If Venus is combust both the marriage and progeny». are reduced to ashes. Venus alonc in the 5th is liable to cause abdominal disorders. Taurus ascendant: He will be skilful in Sanskrit language. The mother of the native will lack good hcalth. He will cnjoy good position and financial attainments. Gemini asccndant: Disorders of respiratory organs, digestive insufficiencies and gencral swellings will be expericneed by the native. If the Sun docs not join, he can pursue music and the likc. Cancer ascendant: Monctary gains, blindncss or defects of sight, destruction of thc family and fear from polson/firc thesc effects will comc to pass. Leo ascendant: The native will have a happy marriage and will enjoy very fricndly terms with his lifc-partner. Hc will be attractive, intelligent, liberal and spendthrift. Hc will be chief among his folk and be famous. If Mercury joins he will aptly receive honours, tilles etc. Virgo asccndant: Burden of expenses, posttional fall, perennial wants and lack of peace will come into effect. Libra asccndant: A person with a high degree of intelligence capable of encashing all possible influence from government sources as well as from person in position is dcnoted. A mole or scar on the ear/s may be noted. Scorplo ascendant: Venus sheds hia malefic tendencies born out of the 12th lordship if Mars joins him in Leo. in such case the native will be very

famous, wealthy and prosperous. Venus alone in Leo will give a short-tempered wife (this is so even when with Mars). checkered position and association with religious activities. Sagittarius ascendant: Paternal gains, affliction to elder sister/brother. timidity. ear diseases etc., will come to pass. Capricorn ascendant: A very important planet for this ascendant going to the 8th house if left alone will mar the horoscope. There will be everlasting penury and unhappiness. Aquarius ascendant: He will acquire an amicable, chaste and beautiful wife who will be an instrument in his prosperity. He will be immoral and progeny will be markedly low. Pisces ascendant: The native will be longlived though Venus dasa may not be prosperous but will inflict miseries.

लवुचिन्तो मृदुनिपुणः परोपसेवी कलाविधिज्ञस्त । स्त्रीस्थे भाषणमघुरः शुक्रे नानार्थकृतयत्नः ।११।। नारीवु दुष्टरतिषु प्रणयी बीनो न सौस्यभोगयुतः। कन्याप्रजोऽल्पपुत्रस्तीर्थसभापष्डितो जातः ।१२।

11-12. VENUS IN VIRGO: Should Venus be in Virgo, the native will not be quite discriminative, be soft in disposition, be skilful, will be helpful to others, will speak sweetly, will earn money through several sources, will cohabit with bad women, be mean, be devoid of happiness and pleasures, will beget more daughters and less sons, will vialt shrines and be a scholar in an assembly.

Notes: Gunakara and Varaha Mihira declare that the person with Venus in fall will do very base acts. To expand he will not have a righteous profession and will derive no happiness in livelihood. The very fall of Venus will tell upon the prospects of marriage, happiness in marriage and duration of the marriage. These evils will be marked as the planet is close to the 28th degree of Virgo—its deep degree of displeasure. Second marriage will be in the offing in most given cases. This position in Virgo is a stumbling block for attainments in conveyances, residences, fortune etc. There is a school of thought that Venus in deep fall will mar the

beauty of the whole horoscope and nullify even powerful Rajayogas.

How this unfortunate placement of Venus is ca pahle of playing its role in one’s destiny may be studied with reference to the individual sign ascending at birth, from the following:

Aries ascendant: There will be miseries galore. Marriage will be full of miseries. Taurus ascendant: Sick progeny or lack of progeny will result. Gemini ascendant: Early demise of mother, fallure in learning, frequent change of residences ctc., will come to pass. Professionally, a person who will always be unhappy can be seen in this mapping of Venus. If Venus be in the 4th in Virgo as the 6th is occupied by the Moon, Mars and Ketu, one will suffer from dental discascs, vide my work 'DOCTRINES FROM SUKA NADl.’ Cancer asccndant: Many discases, foolhardiness and misfortuncs will torment the subject. Leo ascendant: Lot of family difficulties lasting in nature, monetary insufficiencics, short lifc and a troubling marriage will be realised by the nativc. Virgo ascendant: One will be miserable on all counts and will be a repository of many diseases, evil acls and mal-attributes. Libra asccndant: Short life, sick physiquc, loss of sight due to one’s own fault and early demisc of wife will come to occur. Scorpio ascendant: Marrlage will prove a big misfortune. One will be troubled with numerous evll cxpenses, will visit prostitutes and will Incur imprisonment. Sagittarius ascendant: Happiness in profcssion will be only in drcams. An early demise of molher, illilcracy and litigations with relatives are indicaled. Capricorn ascendant: Fortune will be rarity in the life of the nattve. Nonc of his undertakings will yield success. Aquarlus ascendant: He will be a subject of very short life and will never be happy in lifc. Pisces ascendant: This is the worst affecied ascendant of the 12 so that the native has a hell on the carth in the form of marriage. His publice relatlons will be quite poor. He will have a diminished longevity.

श्रमलब्धधनः शूरो विचित्रमात्याम्बरो विदेशरतः । निजजनरक्षणकुशलः कर्मसु निपुणः सुदुष्करेषु तथा ।।१३।। आढघो रुचिरसुपुष्यो द्विजदेवार्चनविलब्धकीतिश्च । शुषे तुलाधरगते भवति पुमान् पण्डितः सुभगः ।।१४।।

13-14. VENUS IN LIBRA: If Venus occupies Libra, the native will acquire hard-earned money, be valorous, endowed with superior robes etc., interested in living in foreign countries, will protect his own people, be skilful in his duties, rich, meritorious, famous, be honouring gods and brahmins, be a scholar and be fortunate.

Notes: The view of Varaha Mihira and that·of Gunakara have been explained in the notes under slokas 3 and 4 of this chapter. Money does not seem to come so easily though one will be rich out of his own labour. This statement need not be misconstrued that earning itself will be difficult. He will be successful in earning in places other than his native one. The word ‘Subhaga’ at the end of sloka 14 can well be interpreted as ‘beautiful' apart from 'fortunate'. Venus in own house will be inclined to give fortune as well as beauty. The number of possible interpretations for one phrase or a word is not going to tend so many effects to come to pass, but the planet in question here is capable of giving both the results irrespective of their mention in the text. That is why I favour this dual interprctation, otherwise not.

How the Libra-placed Venus affects the various natives born in individual ascending signs may be noted from the following account:

Aries ascendant: There will be Malavya yoga effects bestowed on the native. He may additionally seek pleasures from prostitutes. If Venus is adversely aspected he may prove a powerful maraka. Taurus ascendant: One will procure landed property by going in for loans. Occasionally, there may be marring of fortunes. Otherwise the nattve will

enjoy reasonable wealth and health. He will prevail over his enemies. Gemini ascendant: One will have more female issues and be happy in association with the virtuous. He will enjoy abundant monetary gains but some financial debacles will come to occur in adverse bhuktis of Venus dasa. Cancer ascendant: This native will eke out his livelihood in his native place itself and will acquire maternal properties, lands, good learning and the like. Leo ascendant: Long life, number of children, fame, wealth and position are indicated. Virgo ascendant: One will be very rich with Venus in Libra in the 2nd house. A large family, eloquent speech and attractive appearance are additionally denoted. Scorpio ascendant: An early and happy marriage, wealth from cousins, spiritual attainments and fortunes through wife will come to pass. The native may marry a rich glrl. Sagittarius ascendant: The native will be highly intelligent, will have fortunate and happy children (with at least one child becoming prone to sickness) and will gain through enemies and litigations. Vide DOCTRINES FROM SUKA NADI’one born with Mercury in Moolatrikona Rasi (i.e. in Virgo) as Venus is in own house (i.e. only in Libra). Moon in the 5th and Jupiter in the ascendant will be oneeyed. To wit, there will be an eye sightlessly. This can aptly fit other Lagnas as well, but more suitably Sagittarius ascendant. For logic, see the notes in the said work. Capricorn ascendant: The native will be very influential, famous and rich. He will enjoy vast landed properties. If the Sun is simultaneously in fall, the person will be liable to serious health afflictions. Aquarius ascendant: One will equally enjoy good results as mentioned for Capricorn. Pisces ascendant: Wicked wife, long span of life, dental/ sight defects etc., will come to pass.

विद्वेषरतिनृशंसो विमुक्तधर्मा विकत्यनोऽतिशठः ।

सहजविरक्तोऽधन्यो विपन्नशत्रुस्तथा पापः ।१५। आर्तः कुलटाद्वेषी बधनिपुणो बह्लूणो दरिद्रश्च । अलिनि सिते भवति पुमान् गहितशीलः सुगुह्यमदः ।१६।।

15-16. VENUS IN SCORPIO: If Venus occupies Scorplo, the subject will be jealous, be very malicious be not religious, be argumentative, be crafty, be not attached to brothers, be not fortunate, will be troubled by enemies, be distressed, will be inimical to unchaste women, be skilful in killing, will incur heavy debts, will suffer penury, be proud and will contract venereal discases.

"

Notes: Only cvil effects have been attributed by Kalyana Varma, Gunakara and Varaha Mihira. The only good effect stated by Kalyana Varma is winning over encmics. Gencrally Venus in Scorpio is found to be bereft of his good qualities and specifically financial insufficiencies, misfortunes, attachment to base women, debts etc., will manifest. In contrast if Venus rcmaining in Scorpio applies to quali. fied planets he can bestow wealth, fame and name if related to favourable houses. Still some qualities like extra-marital relationship, venereal diseases, distress etc., cannot be totally ruled out, unless and until there are other natal indications to the contrary.

How the individual ascendants come up with Venus remalning in Scorpio can be broadly understood from the following paragraph:

Aries ascendant: Onc will be inimical to his wifc. There will always be wants in the family lifc. His wife’s longevity will be quitc diminished. The chances of Venus'improving many of his advcrsitics will be quite remote unless a powerful Jupiter is placed in the 5th or in the 9th. Taurus asccndant: This native will be better in many respects viz., he will be somcwhat wealthy and fortunate. He will only obtain a sick and irascible wife. Gemini ascendant: This position will confer riches madc by the native himself which will be liable to diminish sooner or later. Only modcrate results will be expcricnced in regard to marriage and chil. dren. Cancer asccndant: Here we see an exceptionally good Venus to bestow high learning, favourable mother, properties, huge gains, happy marriage and the like. The planet may even prove a very powerful Rajayoga karaka conferring

significant and enviable position. To wit he partakes the good role of favourable Mars for this ascendant, yet asso ciation with bad women will not be liable to leave the native as it is an essential trait of Venus-Mars blend. Leo ascendant: This native will also reap similar good effects as for Cancer but in a lesser degree. Here the accent will be more on professional success which the Cancer native will enjoy less than a Leo native with Venus in Scorpio. Virgo ascendant: In this case specific emphasis will be on good fortuncs. There will be influx of wealth through brothers, friends and socicty. The native may even inherit a good deal of paternal wealth. Libra ascendant: Wealth will be gathered by the native but with great difficulty and hindrances. His family life will be moderately happy. There may be some misfortunc in appropriate sub periods of Venus dasa. Scorpio asccndant: A short living person is born with Venus in Scorpio on the east. He will be an outlet for money as a child and will enjoy moderate happiness in married life. Sagittarius ascendant: Most of the malcfic effects described in the text will be aptly experienced by this Sagittarius native. Capricorn asccndant: A number of female prc-born is indicated. Abdominal disorders, general unhappiness etc, will be felt if Vcnus is afflicted. Aquarius ascendant: He will acquire government favour, be learned and rich. If the Moon joins Venus (the Moon will naturally be with deficient rays) an Aquarian will occupy very superior position in life. (As stated in the notes on page 273 of Vol 1 of this edition, the Moon with diminishing rays in fall in the 10th is a yogakaraka.) Pisces ascendant: The native will be fortunate, will associate with spiritual activities and be a leader of men.

सद्धर्मकर्मधनर्जः फर्लेरूपेतो जगत्प्रयः कान्तः। आर्यः कुलाब्जसूर्यः विद्धान् गोमानलंकरिच्णुश्च ।१७।। सहित्तदारसुभगो \_\_\_ नरेंद्रमन्त्री \_\_\_ सुचतुरोऽपि । पीनोच्चतनुः पूज्यः सतां समूहस्य धनुषि कबो । १८।। 17-18. VENUS IN SAGITTARIUS: One who has Venus

in Sagittarius will be endowed with good results accruing out of virtues, dutifulness and wealth, be dear to all people, be splendorous, be an excellent personage, will shine like the Sun before his family members, be a scholar, will be endowed with cows, be fond of decoration, will enjoy wealth, wife and fortunes, be a king’s minister, be skilful, will have a stout and long physique and be respected by all.

Notes: The native with Venus in a sign of Jupiter will bring him honours for his virtues as per Varaha Mihira and Gunakara.

Many Virtues are attributed to Venus in Sagittarius by Kalyana Varma. Firstly, he compares the native to the shining Sun and the native’s race to the proverbial lotus. To wit, the members of his race will be so happy with’his supermacy as the lotus will blossom with the appearance of the Sun. The native will accrue the wealth of dutifulness, righteousness and money. He will be fortunate, happy in marital life, will hold positions akin to a minister's, will enjoy great honours and be learned. Venus in a favourable sign will give success if one pursues to live through quadrupeds. (Though Varaha Mihira specified similar results for Venuslan placement in Pisces akin to that of Sagittarius, Kalyana Varma gives more specific results, vide slokas 23-24.)

As to how this beautiful planet in Sagittarius keeps cach native under the 12 different ascendants may be gauged from the following:

Aries ascendant: While as a natural benefic he will confer good fortunes on the native if the Sun joins him in the 9th, Venus will avall of the first opportunity to prove a killer as far as the native’s father is concerned. The native will generally enjoy good results in respect of money and marital life. He will obtain a wife bejewelled with Jupiterian virtues brightened with Venusian splendour. If Jupiter and Venus join in the 9th both the husband and wife will be blessed with divine grace and they will hold the family flag aloft. Taurus ascendant: Though Venus is the lord of the

ascendant, posited in Sagittarius he seems to be more favourable to the neighbour Arien than to his own native. This is divine irony—nay a demon's wishful fancy. The native under Taurus banner will be hammered with occasional diseases and penury. Here Venus needs dire help from Saturn. Venus alone in the 8th can give a long span of life as the aphorism is that the 6th lord in an evil house will increase the span of life. One thing cannot be ruled out—the native will face many obstacles in boyhood. Geminl ascendant: Marriage and progeny will only enjoy moderately good effects. Sometimes, if Venus occupies more Virgo divisions there will be utter dissatisfaction and frustration in these two spheres. Cancer ascendant: The good effects described in the present slokas will come to pass to a great extent but the native will be prone to serious diseases uiz., abdominal, urinary and kidney disorders. Leo ascendant: This native will enjoy much better results than the Cancerian. Not only this, he will not be prone to these diseases, as applicable to birth in Cancer. One born in Leo will obtain progeny quickly and numerously. Virgo ascendant: This is a good placement for high learning, in fields like literature, painting, drawing, astrology, forestry, and growing of crops like tea, sugarcane, cotton etc. The native will own abundant landed property around hilly areas. He will enjoy all kinds of happiness in life. Libra ascendant: As stated under Taurus ascendant for this sign also Venus in the 3rd proves very detrimental and the dasa of Venus will bring many miseries, expenses and loss of income. If the Moon sign is favourable these adversities will vanish. Scorpio ascendant: Though lord of the 7th and the ‘12th, Venus will be favourable in matters like money, famlly, learning etc., representing Jupiter, a true benefic for this ascendant, the native will be very attractive in appearance, be intelligent, very opulent, famous etc. If Venus is afflicted, acute facial diseases, eye diseases etc., will follow. In general, health will be affected adversely. Sagittarius ascendant: Though one may make abundant wealth and fortunes, physical distress will be experienced more frequently. The native will be very learned, famous and beau-

tiful. Capricorn ascendant: Venus will produce only mixed effects in finance and fortunes. The native will acquire a noble wife and good progeny. He will prove a yogakaraka for the co-born. Aquarius ascendant: Venus will be highly beneficial in the 11th if he is conjunct Saturn. Otherwise only ordinary results will come to pass. Pisces ascendant: Generally Venus in the 10th in Sagittarius will give great honours and position. His aspect on the 4th house will confer a high degree of learning, happiness, wealth etc. As he is the lord of the 8th, he may not shed his stigma fully and will, in adverse bhuktis under his dasa, inflict greater harm, exceeding the goods given by him.

व्ययभयपरिसन्तप्तां दुर्बलदेहो जरांगनासक्तः । हुद्रोगी धनलुब्धो लोभानृतवञ्चनो निपुणः ११६।। क्लीबो विपन्नचेष्टः परार्थचेष्टः सुदुःखितो मुढः । मकरे दानवपूज्ये क्लेशसहो जायते पुरुषः ।।२०।।

19-20. VENUS IN CAPRICORN: IfVenus occupies Capricorn at birth, one will be miserable due to heavy expenses, will have an emaciated body, will be fond of aged women, will suffer from heart diseases, be miserly in the matter of money, be a liar, be skilful in cheating, be a eunuch, be devoid of (good) acts, interested in others' work, very distressed, be a dunce, and will, however, endure misery.

Notes: One with Venus in a house of Saturn will be fortunate, be defeated by women and be attached to bad women. So say Varaha Mihira and Gunakara.

His progenic ability will be marred when Venus is in Caprlcorn. He and his wife will be weak in constitution. These two are important attributes of Saturn that will be imparted into Venus in the context. He will frequent prostitutes, will cheat others and be always unhappy in regard to marriage. In many cases marriage will prove a misfortune or shortlasting. The native will face many obstacles in his livelihood. This placement is not, however, devoid of some advantages in general. In the later part of life one may be exceedingly fortunate.

Placed in Capricorn, let us see how Venus affects births in 12 different ascendants:

Arles ascendant: Venus in the 10th will only give a moderate yoga.'Sri Ramanuja says that even if Venus be in aspect to Juplter he will not cause any yoga. This is partlally true if Jupiter occupies in the process one of the Vipat, Pratyak and Vadha nakshatras (i.e. 3rd, 5th and 7th asterisms counted from radical star.) Otherwise, a relationship between these two will prove reasonably good. Taurus ascendant: Fame, position and lortunes will be on the increase throughout. In a female's horoscope, this position Is very favourable for progeny. In the dasa of Venus, a child of the native may be susceptible to injury of longevity. Gemini ascendant: The subject will incur short life, many discases and miseries. Venus in the 8th will confer little happiness out of marriage and progeny. For night births as Venus is in the 8th while Saturn is in fall (in the 4th from the 8th), the mother of the child will face instant death. Cancer ascendant: Remaining in Capricorn if Venus occupies Taurus Navamsa while the Moon is in Scorpio Navamsa, the native will live at the cost of the virtues of his wife. Leo ascendant: The native's marriage will be delayed, will itself be highly inimical and will inflict great miseries. He will suffer from venereal afflictions. Professionally there will be only stalled progress. He will frequently suffer displacement. Virgo ascendant: He will have a lot of family problems, financial insufficiencies and will suffer on account of his own tongue. From progeny point of view, he will have only female children. These will be very virtuous. If the native gets his Venus dasa, he will reap very favourable results. Libra ascendant: There will be ordinary wealth and ordinary learning in the dasa of Venus—this is how a Sanskrit rule relates Venus in the 4th for Libra. See Swalpavldyavan saamanyam bhavamapthavan. If Venus and the Moon are in the 4th house, the child antenatally caused distress to the mother’s physique. Scorpio ascendant: Defective-co-born or their early death will come to pass with this placement of Venus. The native will have little wisdom. Sagittarlus ascendant: Moderate yoga, limited

wealth, literature, music etc., are denoted for this birth. Capricorn ascendant: Right from birth the native will be very rich. His wife will bring still added fortunes for the native. So to say, there will be influx of wealth throughout. If Venus falls in Virgo Navamsa, thc last leg of the native's life will see utter poverty. Aquarius ascendant: The native will have a secret marriage and will indulge in sexual immoralities. He will have to undergo many ordeals in regard to landed properties. He will be very unfortunate. Pisces ascendant: Alone in the 1 1th, Venus will give gains through foreigners. Onc will makc his livelihood through ups and downs.

उद्देगरोगतप्तः कर्मसु विफलेषु सर्वदाभिरतः । परयुवतिगो विधर्मा गुरुभिः पुत्रेश्व कृत्वरः ।२१।। स्नानोपभोगभूषणवस्त्रादिनिराकृतो मलिनः । कुम्मघरे भुगुपुत्रे भवति पुमान्नात्र सन्देहः । २२।।

21-22. VENUS IN AGUARIUS: Should Venus bc in Aquarius, one will suffer from discases and excitement, be not successful in undertakings, will go to other women, be not virtuous, be inimical to clders and to his children, be bereft of ablutions like bathing and will not be endowed with (good) robes, ornaments etc., and be dirty.

Notes: With Venus in Aquarius, the native will be of Tamasik disposition, be lazy and will not even willingly attend to his regular ablutions like bathing etc. Thus he will be dirty physically. He will not have good terms with his elders as well as with his own progeny. He will always suffer from unnecessary fear.

The individual role of Venus with reference to 12 ascendants as he is in Aquarius can be understood from the following:

Aries ascendant: Self-earned wealth will be abundant. The nattve’s wife will be sexually cold, subjected to diseases and be a source of trouble to the native. Taurus ascendant: He will have a sickly mother and will prove ominous for her. He will not have good relationship with his parents. Gemini

ascendant: The native will be financially well off. He will be happy with his children. He will posscss lot of prccious stones and incur favour from rich men. Cancer ascendant: His children will not enjoy a fair longevity. The native will be a synonym of poverty. His married life either will be short or will be unpalatable. Leo ascendant: Venus in the 7th will contribute to the wealth and fortunes of the native. He will make the marital life quite happy. But during the dasa of Venus the native will be apt to suffer physical distress. If in the 6th navamsa in Aquarius (i.e. in Pisces Navamsa), the native will live an aristocratic life after marriage. Virgo ascendant: The native will incur heavy loans and will be devoid of learning. He will have many family problems and will not be a successful house-holder. If a node joins him, his wife will be of a qucstionable character. If alone, Venus will give him immoral tendencies. Libra ascendant: He will have a medium span of life. He will suffer diseases relating to heart. If the Sun is there, this ailment is doubly certain. Scorpio ascendant: He will attain mastery over Sastras of the ancient lore. Abundant landed properties, very decent livelihood and an afflicted mother will result. To wit, the mother will not maintain a good health with the birth of this native. Sagittarius ascendant: His livelihood will be with difficulty. His sisters will flourish later on. If Venus occupies malefic shashtiamsas, the longevity of the native will be afflicted. Capricorn ascendant: He will attain some paternal property. His married life will not have much charm in it. Even then he will prove a fairly good householder. His monetary achicvemcnts will be limited. Aquarius ascendant: He will have plenty of riches, be modest, learned and fortunate. Pisces ascendant: He will make notable spiritual achievements, and will engage in religious activities. His cmphasis will be on ultra-mundane sphere.

वाक्षिण्यवानगुणवान् महाधनोऽधः कृतारिपक्षश्च ।

लोके स्यातः श्रेष्ठो विशिष्टचेस्टो नृपतिदयितः ।२३।। वाग्बुद्धियुतोदारः सज्जनपरिलब्धविभवमानश्च । स्वावेयवचा मीने बंशधरो ज्ञानवान् शुक्रे ।२४।।

23-24. VENUS IN PISCES: If Venus is posited in Pisces at birth, one will be courteous, liberal, virtuous, very wealthy, will destroy enemies, be famous in the world, excellent, distinguished, dear to king, be endowed with good speech and wisdom, be liberal, will derive wealth and respect from the virtuous, will keep up his promise, will maintain his family members and be endowed with knowledge.

Notes: The exaltation of Venus carries with it many supreme effects. One will attain world-famous positions, or at least positions of very high importance, if Venus is in Pisces related to good houses at birth. For example, one with exalted Venus in the 10th, 7th, 4th or in ascendant will hold greater positions. This placement will confer abundant riches if the exaltation is in the 2nd, 11th, 9th, 5th etc. Being a preceptor, Venus will bestow unsurpassing wisdom when he acquires exaltation strength. This abundantly applies to Cancer or Capricorn ascending. Other attributes of Venus like courtesy, destruction of enemies, excellence, distinction etc., will be well-enjoyed by the native. The native’s marriage will prove extremely beneficial; he will be very fortunate particularly in the postmarital part of llfe. His wife will be pretty, charming, eloquent, longliving and be a source of recognition for the family. Those who have a good Venus in exaltation, unaffected of other strengths adversely, will serve as example for all the attributes stated in the two verses under comment. The good effects of the exaltation of Venus will remain marred if he goes to the third Navamsa and it may even turn adverše.

How does this planet react when different signs ascend as he is in Pisces? Note the following.

Aries ascendant: The native will be honoured even by the wealthy, will himself be very wealthy and will enjoy all comforts required by a householder. If Jupiter joins Venus in the 12th along with Mars, the native will surpass others in riches and will be surprisingly opulent. He will own vast

properties and will lead a very happy life. Taurus ascendant: Wealth and fortune will be conferred uninterruptedly on this native also as Venus is in the 11th. Sickness will occasionally torment the native if Venus is otherwise spoilt. Eyes, feet, private parts, kidneys etc., will be susceptible to serious attacks in the sub periods or major period of planets posited in evil houses, evil asterisms (3rd, 5th or 7th from natal star), evil navamsas etc. Venus should also be connected by rulership of periods. Gemini ascendant: Very high position, professional prosperity, very famous status, opulence, happy married life, expenses on religious and auspicious deeds, very fortunate children etc., will come to pass. In the company of Mercury, most of these results stand spoiled in Venus dasa, only to pass fully in Mercury dasa. If Venus dasa when to precede the dasa of Mercury, then these are only in dreams, or in very little quantities. Cancer ascendant: He will enjoy legendary luck and will amass plenty of wealth. Grcat wealth is possible even at birth. All his undertakings will prove auspicious and successful. He will achieve an enviable socal status. His coborn will also be very rich. He will have a very mellifluous voice, singing talents and the like. Leo ascendant: The strength of Venus will be converted into a long lease of life more than anything. Professional attainments will follow but with due scope of reversal. This reversal will not be to the rockbottom in any case. It will only be in the form of a temporary drawback and distress. Virgo ascendant: Venus will bless the native with a successful householdership, happy and advantageous marriage and fortunes galore. If he shifts to Virgo navamsa, then the Virgo subject will have all he should not in the above cited departments of life, Libra ascendant: The native will live theoritical full span of llfe of 120 years. So to say, he will live upto a pretty ripe age proving the adage that a sinner lives one of the longest lives. So further to say, he will be miserable in his old age and will be dependant on others. His later part of life will be full of sickness, misery etc. This does not mean that he will be an insignificant person in the early periods with Venus in the 6th in peak. This power grants Venus to elevate the native

to high state of the society, make him affluent and very happy. Scorpio ascendant: During the first stage of life the native will be highly attached to worldly pleasures, be wealthy and popular. In the later half, particularly covered by Venus dasa he will engage himself in abstract meditation and will take to the path of the Supreme Self. Sagittarius ascendant: He will make huge gains in his own business, be endowed with wife, sons, patience and fortunes. He may find wealth/treasures on the way (ie. when he will be on his move), will associate himself with thieves and will gain through quadrupeds and agriculture. Capricorn ascendant: Happy profession and happy progeny are denoted by this position. The native’s father will have a long lease of life. Aquarius ascendant: Abundant money, inheritance of paternal property and beneficial friends will come to pass. Pisces ascendant: One will take birth with silver spoon- - - nay golden spoon—in the mouth. Long lease of life will come to pass but if Venus dasa is first to occur, then it may prove adverse.

स्त्रीहेतोर्वुःखार्त युवतिनिमित्ताद्विनष्टधनसौस्यम् । कुजभवने रविवृष्टो जनयति शुको नृपं प्राजस् ।२५। उद्बन्धनमतिवपलं कामातुरमधमयुवतिभर्तारम् । जनयति शुक्रोऽपत्यं रजनीकरबीक्षितः कुजमे ॥२६।। धनसौश्यमानरहितं परकर्मकरं मलिनचेष्टम् । जन्यति रुधिरक्षेत्रे रुधिरेण निरीकितः शुक्रः ।२७। मूर्व धृष्टमार्ये स्वबन्धुपरिवादकं विनयहीनम्। खोरं क्षुदं कूरं बुधदृष्टो भार्गवः कुर्ते ।२८। सुनयनमुदारवारं सुशरीरं व्यायतं बहुसुतं च। त्रिदशगुरुवृष्टम्तिर्जनयति सुधिरालये शुक्रः । २६। अतिमलिनमलसमटनन्त्वभिमतजनसेवक कुर्ते । भूतुतनयो रुधिरय्हे दिनकरपुत्रेण बीक्षितश्वोरम् ।।३०।।

25-30. VENUS IN ARIES OR SCORPIO IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: Should Venus occupy Aries/Scorplo at birth and is in aspect to the Sun, one will be miserable on account of women, and will lose wealth and happiness on account of them, will be a king and be learned. If by the Moon, he will be imprisoned, very fickle-minded, be libidinous, will marry a base lady, and will be bereft of children. If by Mars, he will be devoid of wealth, honour and happiness, will do others’ jobs, and will perform dirty jobs. If by Mercury, he will be foolish, profligate, unworthy, be not in good terms with his own relatives, be immodest, thievish, mean and cruel. If by Jupiter, he will be endowed with beautiful eyes, will have a charitable wife, will possess a beautiful and long body, and will have many sons. If by Saturn, he will be very dirty, lazy, and wandering-natured, will serve others and be a thief.

Notes: If Venus occupy Aries or Scorpio at birth and be aspected by the Moon, the native will beget progeny with great difficulty. It may not be that he will go without an issue. The statement in the text will only serve a hint to this effect and it need not be literally interpreted. (Venus in Scorpio will get the exalted Moon’s aspect and it is all the more superior). Acquisition of a mean wife is denoted in this context. As a house of Mars is not congenlal for marital felicity with placement of Venus therein, his opposition to the Moon will bestow confirmed adverse results in this regard. Venus alone in a house of Mars will make one highly sensuous and immoral and if he is in aspect of Mars these qualities will be predominant. The aspect of Mars on Venus may equally give an immoral tendency but abundant riches. Jupiter aspecting Venus will confer wealth, progeny and happiness. Saturn applying to Venus in a house of Mars will give all evil tendencies not to be possessed by anyone.

दिनकरदृष्टः शुक्रो वरजायाभोगिनं धनसमृद्धम् ।

जनयत्युत्तमपुरुषं स्त्रीहेतोनिजितं स्वगृहे ।३१।।

परमकुलीनापुत्रं सुबधनमानैः सुर्तर्पेतं च। अत्यार्यमति कान्तं स्वगृहे चन्देक्षितः शुक्रः ॥ ३२।। बुःशीलाभर्तारं प्रमदाहेतोश्व नष्टगृह्वित्तम् । जनयति भूगुनन्दकरो मदनवशं बक्रसन्दृष्टः ।३३।। कान्तं मयरं सुभगं सुखधृतिमतिसंयुतं विपुलसतवम्। जनयति बधेन दृष्टः सर्वगुणसमन्वितं स्यातम् ॥३४।। प्रमदापुत्रनृहाणां भागिनमथ यानवाहनानां च। स्वक्षे गुरुसन्दृष्टः कुर्ते मृगुरिष्टचेष्टाइयम् ।।३५। स्वल्पसुखं स्वल्पधनं वुःशीलं वर्धकोपति खेव । सौरेक्षितस्तु जनयेत् व्याधितबेहं नर शुक्रः ।।३६।

31-36. VENUS IN OWN HOUSE IN ASPECT TO OTH- ERS: Should Venus at birth occupy Taurus/Libra and is aspected by the Sun, the person will acquire an excellent wife and abundant wealth, will become a great man and will be subdued by women. If by the Moon, it indicates that he is born of an excellent mother, will be endowed with happiness, wealth, respect and sons, will have excellence and be splendorous. If by Mars, he will marry a bad female, will lose home and wealth on account of females and be sensuous. If by Mercury, one will be splendorous, sweet, fortunate, happy, bold, wise and virtuous and will possess distinguished strength. If by Jupiter, he will be endowed with wife, sons, abodes, conveyances, riches etc., and will achieve desired objects. If by Saturn, he will have little happiness, little wealth, will be reprobate, will marry a mean lady and will suffer from discases.

Notes: Venus when in Taurus or in Libra if aspected by the Moon denotes one of a good descent. Even though the Moon may be in fall, this is true, as the Full Moon in opposition to Venus will prove very beneficial. If other

planets join Venus in Taurus as the Moon is in fall, the native’s family will improve its riches soon after the birth of the native. Wealth will be widely enjoyed if Venus-Moon complex emanatcs either from Taurus or Libra. The aspect of Mars on Venus as the latter is in his own house will cause financial losses on account of laising with women. His marriage will be an unhappy one. (This applies to their involvement in any houses). Saturn’s aspect is equally ominous. It will be more awful if from Scorpio to Taurus. A decent householdership follows the aspect of Jupiter on Venus in Taurus/Libra.

नृपजननीपत्नीनां कृत्यकर पण्डितं धनिनम्। विनकरदृष्टः शुरो जनयति सुखभागिनं बुध्चे । ३७।। क्रव्णनयनं सुकेशं शयनासनयानभागिनं कान्तम्। सुकुमारमिन्दुवृष्टो जनयति शुको नर शुभगम् ।।३८। कामपरस्तवति सुभगं युवतिहते चार्थनाशनं कुर्ते। वक्रेक्षितस्तु शुक्रो बुधमवनमुपाश्रितः प्रसवे ॥ ३६।। प्राज्ञं मधुरं धनिनं वाहनपरिवारभागिनं सुभगम् । गणपतिमयेश्वरं वा बुधदृष्टो भार्गवो बुधभे ॥४०। बुधभवनगतः शुक्रस्त्रिदशगुरुनिरीक्षितः : कुर्ते । अतिसुखमतीव दीप्तं प्रतिक्पकरं ज्ञमाचार्यम् ॥४१। दिनकरसुतेन दृष्टो बुधमवनगतोऽतिदुःखिनं शुक्रः । जनयति खलु परिभूतं चपलं द्वेद्यं व मूर्खं च ।४२।

37-42. VENUS IN GEMINI/ VIRGO IN ASPECT TO OTH- ERS: If Venus is in Gemini/Virgo, and aspected by the Sun, one will be well disposed towards the king, his own mother and wife, be learned and rich. If by the Moon, he will have dark eyes, beautiful hair, be endowed with sleeping comforts, conveyances etc., be splendorous, beautiful in appearance and be fortunate. If by Mars, he will be highly sensuous, be fortunate and will destroy his wealth through

women. If by Mercury, he will be learned, sweet in disposition, wealthy, and endowed with conveyances and children, be fortunate, and be leader of men or be a king. If by Jupiter, he will be highly happy, be radiant, courageous, learned and be a preceptor. If by Saturn, he will be very miserable, insulted, fickle-minded, green-eyed and be a dunce.

Notes: The good effects for aspect on Venus in a house of Mercury will manifest in a better way if Venus is in Gemini. The position of Venus in Virgo will give the good effects in a lesser manner and bad effects in a severe manner. However, Venus in Virgo is aspected by Jupiter, his exaltant dispositor, will confer abundant riches and the like, but the marital life will not improve fully. Saturn's aspect on Venus in Virgo is absolutely baneful for an honourable marriage and for having a chaste thinking about females. The aspect of Mars is equally ominous.

कर्मपराममलांगीं नृपतिमृतां रोषणां धनोपेताम् । भार्यां ददाति शुक्रश्चन्द्रगृहे भानुसन्दुष्टः ।।४३।। मातृसपत्नीसुखदं कन्यापूर्वप्रजं बहुसुतं च । सुखिनं सुभ्यं ललितं कुस्ते चन्देक्षितः शुकः ।४४।। सुकलाविदमत्याइच स्त्रीहेतोर्दुःखितं सुमगम् । भौमेक्षितस्तु जनयेद्वृद्धिकरं बन्धुवर्गस्य ।४५।। पच्टितचार्यापतिकं बन्धुनिमित्तं च दुःखितं नित्यम् । अटनं धनिनं प्राज्ञं करोति शशिमे बुधेक्षितः शुषः ।४६। चूर्वधनेश्व पुत्रैवाहनभोर्गेश्च बान्धवेमित्रेः । क्षुर्ते नरमिह युक्तं नरपतिदयितं च गुर्दृष्टः ॥४७।। ह्श्रीनिजित्तं दरिद्रं पतितमरूपं तर्यव चपलं च। जन्यत्ति सुर्खेविहीनं सशिमे शनिवीक्षितः शुकः ।४८। 43-48. VENUS IN CANCER IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Venus is in Cancer and aspected by the Sun, the native’s wife will be attached to her duties, will posscss a spotless

body, will be a king’s daughter, (i.e. she will be of a rich heritage), be short-tempered and will be cndowed with wealth. If by the Moon, he will keep his step-mother happy, will have a daughter first and later on many sons, be happy, fortunate, and beautiful. If by Mars, he will be skilful in arts, be very rich, will suffer on account of women, be fortunate and will promote the cause of his relatives. If by Mercury, he will marry a learned woman, will suffer miserles on account of his relatives, will wander, be wealthy and learned. If by Jupiter, he will be endowed with many servants, sons, happincss, relatives and fricnds and be dear to king. If by Saturn, he will be subducd by women, be poor, base, ugly, fickle-minded and unhappy.

Notes: When the Moon aspects Venus in Cancer, it seems evident that there will be a stepmother for the native who will be happily looked after by him. This aspect will also give a daughter followed by many sons. The position of Venus in a female sign will give a daughter (whether firstly or later) while the Moon (in Capricorn) bcing full or with digital vigour aspecting from a male sign will confer a number of sons. Wealth and fortunc will follow this relationship. If Mars aspects Venus from Capricorn or cven from Aries, the latter will give abundant wcalth. The aspect from Sagittarius will be less efficacious. Venus bcing In a sign of Moon applying to Mars will trigger of some difficulties either with his own wife or with females outsidc the family, the latter are probably those unsoclal clements on whose account many housewives lament. So to say, as a householder the native will be a wretch, wandcring in the lanes of the prostitutes, if the aspect of Mars is very puissant. Saturn’s aspect will not only give all thesc advcrsitics but add to one's miseries in the form of dire penury. The sign occupled by Venus being that of the Moon, mcntal diseases or mental distress will follow Salurn's aspect.

मेध् कान्ता-दवितं कामार्तँ युवतिकारणाद् धनिनम् ।

चाविनम्च करभाणां जनयति सिहे रवीक्षितः शुकः ।४EII

मातृसपत्नीजलदं युवतिकृते दुःखितं विभववन्तम् । सिहे नानामतिकं करोति चन्द्रेक्षितः शुक्रः ॥५०।। नृपपुरुषं विस्यातं युवतिङ्ते वल्लभं धनसमृद्धम् । सुभगं परदाररतं सिहे वक्रेक्षितः शुषः ॥५१। संतृहनिरतं लुब्धं स्त्रीलीलं पारदारिकं शूरम्। शठमानृतिकं घनिनं सिहे बुधबोक्षितः शुषः ॥५२।। वाहनधनमृत्ययुतं बहुदारपरिग्रहं रविक्षेत्रे । कुर्ते नरेन्द्रममित्रणमिन्द्रगुरुनिरीक्षितः शुक्रः ॥५३।। नृपति नृपतिप्रतिमं विस्यातं कोशवाहनसमृद्धम् । रण्डापति सुरूपं दुःखयुतं सौरसन्दुष्टः ॥५४।।

49-54. VENUS IN LEO IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: II Venus occupics Leo at the timc of birth and is aspected by the Sun, one will be jealous, be dcar to the fair sex, libidinous, will acquire moncy through woncn and will possess elephants. If by the Moon, he will present obsequial water to his step-mother, will be miserable on account of women. be rich and will have various kinds of mental disposition (l.e. be infirin in disposition). If by Mars, he will be a royal person, be famous, dear to women, be afflucnt, fortunate and be attached to other's wives. If by Mercury, he will be ever engaged in carning, be miserly in disposition, be addicted to women, will join other’s wives, be couragcous, crafty, false and wealthy. if by duptter, he will be cndowed with conveyances, wealth and servants, will marry many women and be a king's minister. If by Saturn, he will be a king or equal to a king, be famous, will have abundant wealth and convcyances, will marry a whore (or a widow), be beautiful in appearance and be miscrable.

Notes: Venus placed in Leo if aspected by the Moon from Aquarius will make the nattve offer obsequial water to his sicp-mother. That is,he will perform Sraddhas for her.

(Sraddha means a death anniversary as per religious prescriptions). This follows that the natve will have a step mother. The native will suffer on account of women. may be even through his own wife. be of varied dispositions and will enjoy wealth. Wealth may be limited as the Moon m Aquarius is not that auspicious. Jupiter’s aspect is said to give many marriages but it may be interpreted that there will be more than one marriage. Saturn’s aspect on Venus in Leo will bestow a wife from among immorals. or one who is a widow.

अतिरोप्रमति च शूरं गुर्मे प्राजं च घनिनमतिदयितम् । रक्षिणा दृष्टो जनयति विदेशगमनं नरं शुकः ।५५।। श्वातं नरेन्दपुर्वं भोगरशने समन्वितं विनुलेः। कुर्ते ह्यानुपमसारं गुरुमे चन्द्रेक्षितः शुकः ॥५६।। अधिकट्वच्यं स्त्रोणां विचित्रसुखदुःसमर्थबन्तं च। कुस्ते योधनमग्यं गुरुषे भौमेक्षितः शुकः ॥५७। आवसन-भूषणानां भागिनमपि खान्नपानानाम् । बुधवदृष्टो मृगुतनयः कुस्ते गुरुमेऽर्थवाहनसमृद्धम् ॥५८।। गजतुरगयोधनाइच \_\_ बहुपुत्रकल त्रमतिसुखिनम् । गुरुणा दृष्टो गुरुमे जनयति शुक्रो महाविभवम् ॥५ENI नित्यं च धनप्रायं सुखिनं भोगान्वितं धनसमृद्धम्। गुरुभवने शनिवृष्टः कुस्ते शुको नरं सुभगम् ।६०।

55-60. VENUS IN SAGITTARIUS/PISCES IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Venus occupies Sagittarius/Pisces at birth and aspected by the Sun, one will be very short tempered, lcarned, wealthy, be liked by all and will go to foreign countries. If by the Moon, he will be famous, be kingly, will eat rich food, be distinguished and will possess incomparable strength. If by Mars, he will be very much ill-disposed towards women, will be both happy and miserable, be rich and will possess cows. If by Mercury, he will be endowed

with all kinds of robes, ornaments, foods and drinks, and will possess plenty of money and conveyances. If by Jupiter. he will be rich with elephants, horses and cows, will possess many wives and many sons, be very happy and be very wealthy. If by Saturn, he will be always amassing financial gains, be happy, will enjoy pleasures and abundant wealth and be fortunate.

Notes: Venus in a sign of Jupiter in aspect to the Moon, Mars, Jupiter or Saturn will confer a high degree of financial attainments. The aspect of Mars on Venus in Sagittarius is not favourable for amicable relations with one's wife and with other females in general.

स्तिमितं वृषमं स्त्रीणां महाधनं सत्यसौख्यसम्पन्नम्। सौरगृहे रविदृष्टः कुर्ते शुक्रो नर शूरम् ।६१।। ओजस्विनमतिसूरं स्वाढच वपुवान्वितं सुभगम्। कुस्ते शशिना दृष्टो रविजगृहे मार्गवः कान्तम् ।६२।। जायाविनाशकारणमनर्यबहुलं च रोगिणं शुक्रः । कुस्ते श्रमाभितन्तं पश्चात्मुखिनं च कुजदृष्टः ।६३।। प्राज्ञे धनचरनिरतं विधानरुचिमतिशयेन विद्धांसम्। जनयति बुधेन दृष्टा चुगुतनयः सत्यसौष्यसम्पन्नम् ॥ ६४।। प्रियवस्त्रमात्वगन्धं सुकुमारं गीतवादितविधिशम् । जनयति गुरुणा वृष्टो भूगुपुत्रः सर्कलत्रयुतम् ।।६५।। शौरगृहे शनिदृष्टः शुक्रो नरवाहनार्षभोगयुतम् । मलिनं श्यामशरीर सुरचिरगात्रं महावेहम् ।।६६।

61-66. VENUS IN SATURN'S HOUSE IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: Should Venus be in Capricorn/Aquartus and be in aspect to the Sun, the native will be firm, very dear to females, very wealthy, very happy and truthful and be courageous. If to the Moon, he will be very splendorous. very valorous, very affluent and fortunate. If to Mars he will

lose his spouse, will suffer many evils and discases, will undergo difficulties and later on be happy. If to Mercury, he will be learned, rich, skilful in sacred precepts, highly scholastic, truthful and happy. If to Jupiter, he will enjoy robes and garlands, be beautiful, will be skilful in music and musical instruments and will possess a good wife. If to Saturn, he will be endowed with servants, conveyances and wealth, be dirty and will possess a black, beautiful and broad physique.

Notes: The position of Venus in Aquarius is itself ominous for the span of the life of the spouse of the native. If Mars aspects the said Venus, it is doubly inauspicious and loss of wife is certain. The aspect of Mars further adds evils, diseases and difficulties. The native will be happy after undergoing these ordeals, ie. will be happy when alone in life, losing his wife and getting purified with all these 'saturnine' exercises.

If Venus occuples Aquarius ascendant as Mars is in Cancer in the 6th one will suffer from (major) ear disease. It may be in the form of deafness right from birth. The aspect of Saturn on both Mars and Venus is an essential requirement here so that he remains in the I 1th from Mars. Mars placed in the house of diseases will give car discases if he has a malefic in the 11th, abdominal diseases if a malefic is in the 5th from Mars so placed, lung disorders if afflicted by malefics in the 3rd and so on and so forth. To be very clear, if Mars occupies the 6th in fall, or any planet in the 6th in fall, let that house be treated as the ascendant and the limbs denoted by the house occupied by malcfics counted from Mars in fall etc., will cause defect of the said limb. (See DOCTRINES FROM SUKA NADI.

## इति शुक्रवाराध्यायः ।।२८।।

Thus ends the 28th chapter entitled 'EFFECTS FROM VENUS IN VARIOUS SIGNS’ in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अथ शनिचाराध्यायः ।।२६।।

## Chapter 29

## Effects of Saturn in Various Rasis

ध्यसनपरिश्रमतप्तः प्रपञ्चशीलः स्वबन्धुपक्षधनः । निर्दुर्घृष्टातिवचा विगाहितो निर्धनः कुवेषश्च ।१। मेवेऽर्कंजे सुरोचो जघन्यकर्मा ख लब्धदोषोऽपि । प्रियर्वरो नेकृतिको नृशंसकोऽसूयकः : पापः ।२।।

1-2. SATURN IN ARIES: If Saturn occuples Aries at the timc of onc’s birth, one will be miserable due to his vices and hard labour, be deceitful, will hate his relatives, be blameworthy, garrulous, reprobated, poor, bad in appearance, ill-tempered, inimical to his pcople, will do base acts, be jealous and sinful.

Notes: When Arics is occupied by Saturn, the native will be foolish, wandcring, deccitful and fricndless as per Varaha Mihira. Gunakara who has always becn following the footsteps of Varaha Mihira dcparts at this point in as much as he uses the words ag' instead of of Varaha Mihira. Gunakara's hint is at the nativc’s enjoying maternal happincss with Saturn in Arics. In his own inimitable style, Kecranuru Nataraja statcs in his voluminous Jatakalankara that one wilh Saturn in Arics will be very eunning, casily irritable, will not be well-disposed to his relatives and will cause utter surprise to others with his power of speech.

Except for the maternal happiness denoted by Gunakara and for the efficaclous vocal power denoted by Nataraja, we scc thronghout only bad results in all these statements including that of our current text. Maternal happiness may be in tact if Saturn is in fall unrelated to the Moon, the 4th house from the Moon, and the 4th house

from Lagna. In these three cases, Saturn’s fall proves utterly harmful. See what an esotery taken from DOCTRINES FROM SUKA NADI states:

If Saturn is in fall in the case of a Capricorn birth and is aspected by the 8th lord (i.e. the Sun in fall) as Mars is in the 10th (i.e. conjunct the Sun), the native’s mother will leave him to his own fate and will die (instantly). To wit, the effects relating to mother's happiness should be wiscly applied. If Saturn in fall afflicts Jupiter, Mercury or the 2nd lord the effect of powerful speech will not come to pass.

As Saturn occupies his sign of fall, the native will suffer from penury and enmity, will have bad habits, fruitless efforts, sinful acts, jealousy and the like. Saturn rules two important houses in the natural zodiac. These are the 10th (Karma) and 1 1th (Labha, Abhista etc.), and hence his weakness will deprive one of a good means of living, general monetary gains, fulfilment of desires and the like. His fall will produce windy diseases (like rheumatism), consumption, asthma, piles, eye disorders, premature fall or greying of hair, frequent colds and the like. If the lord of the 6th is simultaneously in his fall, indications for these defects get confirmed and the native will suffer from a disease as above. The discases caused by Saturn will not be apt to cure quickly. They are likely to cause confinement for longer and longer periods.

Bcing in Aries, Saturn has the following effects for the 12 different ascendants at birth:

Arics ascendant: All the bad attributes enlisted above will be literally experienced by the native. He will essentially suffer a cut in longevity and will quit the world in prime youth. If he lives, he only lives like a corpse. Taurus ascendant: The only pure benefic for this ascendant going to the 12th in fall will make other good aspects in the radix defunct. The 2nd, 6th and 9th houses coming under his adverse aspect from the 12th will deprive the native of monetary bliss, family bliss, bliss of health, bliss through father and bliss of enjoying good fortunes. Thus the native

will be left with no bliss. If in the process he occupies Libra Navamsa. the Taurus native will raise his head against these odds. Gemini ascendant: One will have a very short life. will suffer misfortunes in respect of ltvelihood, will contract severe diseases in boyhood, be devoid of progeny, be a fool of first rate, will lose paternal property and will himself end in loss of gains. Cancer ascendant: This is one of the worst suffering ascendants from longevity point of view. Marital life will be the most unhappy for its native, and if the Moon in the process goes to the 12th, 4th, or the 7th unaspected by others, marriage will be wholly denied. He will spend even his petty possessions on undesirables. Leo ascendant: He will be devoid of relatives like father, brothers etc. In a female's nativity, early widowhood will follow. Virgo ascendant: The native will experience all kinds of miseries of the earth. He will be short-lived with Saturn in fall in the 8th. Libra ascendant: The native will face loss of wife and loss of children. If the Moon is in Taurus, as Saturn is in Aries, he will take birth with a coil around his face. Scorpio ascendant: This ascendant will also not lag behind to get bad results of Saturn's fall. Its subject will lack maternal happiness or will lose his mother early. Sagittarius ascendant: Birth in this ascendant with Saturn in fall in the 5th house is apparently an exception in that there is an auspicious yoga. See the following:

पंचमे मन्द संयुक्ते तत्पाके शुभयोगदम् अधिकारयुतेद्योगी स्वल्पसंतानवान्भवेत् ।

The meaning of the above mentioned verse is: If Saturn occupies the 5th (in fall—as the sloka relates to Sagittarlus ascendant) there will be auspicious results in Saturn's dasa, a profession with power and a few children. Capricorn ascendant: An aphorism relating to mother has already been narrated in the second paragraph of the commentary on the present two slokas. Further, this position will give physical distress and monetary expenses. Aquarius ascendant: One will be devoid of virtues and happiness. This is a critical placement for the health of lungs, larynx, volce

box etc. The native will tremble in speech and writing. Incidentally one may note that the handwriting of such a native will present an awful sight. Pisces ascendant: If Saturn dasa operates at birth, end will be imminent. In a normal nativity, the person will severely stammer and may be incurable with more afflictions of planets.

अर्य विहीनः प्रेथ्यो न युक्तवाक्यो न सत्यकर्मा च। वृद्धस्त्रीहृदयहरः कुसुहृत् स्त्रीव्यसनसंसक्तः ।।३।। सौरे वृषमं याते भवति व जातः परांगनाप्रेध्यः। नक्तिकः स्फुटदृष्टो बहुक्रियासंगतो

## मूढः ।४।

3-4. SATURN IN TAURUS: If Saturn occupies Taurus at the time of one’s birth, he will be bereft of wealth, be a servant, will speak undesirable words, be untruthful, will win the hearts of old women, will have bad friends, will be addicted to women, will serve other women, be not outspoken, will have strong sight, be related to numerous assignments and be a fool.

Notes: Varaha Mihira in this respect mentions that the native will not have "much of wealth" which indicates some possible wealth. (If Saturn is well related to the ascendant, he is capable of giving abundant wealth posited in Taurus). He further adds that the native will join such women who are discarded and will have many wivcs. It follows that the native will have more than onc marriage if not literally 'many marriages'. Sec the cffecla given by Nataraja for the position of Saturn in Taurus: The native will atrongly ycarn for pleasures from semales, (yct) will be gified with virtucs, will not lack in attention and will join a number of females selling their (invaluabic) physiqucs. Thius the inherent trait of Saturn in a sign of Venus the planet of acxual joys—is to make the person enjoy such joys fully by some means or other. That is his only and prime alm. When we come to Kalyana Varma, we find him alao describing the native's association with females females past their princ- not necessarily to gratify his passion. Yet he will he addicted to other women, will serve women and will destrucl his own

welfare and that of his family. If Saturn joins the Moon in the 5th house, particularly belonging to Venus, and more particularly Taurus, the female concerned will premaritally conceive. For this, the said conjunction be not interfered by other heavenly bodies. The condition must be remembered with the very presence of this frightful yoga.

Let us see as usual as to how Saturn in Taurus reacts for the individual ascendants from the following account:

Aries ascendant: Saturn in Taurus will deprive the native of opportunities of amassing wealth. (If he has considerable strength or be in potential aspects, attaining riches should not be ruled out). This planet may inflict death in adverse periods. Taurus ascendant: This native will ordinarily earn abundant money, fame and name. He can look forward to occupying dizzy heights. Yet his health will be a drawback in more than one way, particularly in boyhood. It is said that in the latter part of life Saturn in this ascendant will shed his disposition of telling upon the health. Gemini ascendant: The native’s expenses will acheive a devil's form and will torment him severely. He will losc everything in bad company. All said and done, he will have a long lease of llfe. Cancer ascendant: As the adage gocs, लाचस्थेतु मन्देन अन्यायेन घनार्जनम् (this is with reercnce to Saturn's placement in the 11th for Cancer births), the native will carn (only) in unfair ways. The native’s marrlage will prove a boon for him, particularly if Venus also joins in thc I ith house. Saturn in the I Ith will give insatiablc lust to his woman. The native will be free from discasc and lcading a happy life to that extent. Leo ascendant: He will add cveryday one cncmy in the matter of his livellhood which will be the cause of utter dissatisfaction. Sometimcs, the natives with this placement of Saturn will fall to the nadir. If Saturn is strong and be in favourable aspecls, one can hope to reach surprising heights in political/judiclal lincs. Saturn's dasa will, however, cause him great favours but not debacles. The debacles liable to occur nre likely to take place in other adverse periods. Virgo

ascendant: Though he will lack paternal happiness it is with his own efforts that he will make his good fortunes. The native will suffer some affliction in regard to progeny. Libra ascendant: An unfortunate position for a key planet, this will deprive one of all possessions and will reduce him to a miserable state. Professional aspect will also be apt to a severe hit. Scorpio ascendant: The native will be a profligate, will take to evil women’s company, will suffer adversities in married life and will be bereft of issues. Sagittarius ascendant: Fame and long life will come to pass while occasional financial hindrances will be a source of distress. The native will be free from diseases and will improve his wealth by self-made efforts. Capricorn ascendant: An afflicted Saturn will cause childlessness while a strong Saturn will possibly give happy children capable of making prosperity. He will enjoy abundant wealth and gains. Aquarius ascendant: The native will lose his mother early, particularly if the Moon joins Saturn in Taurus. He will be a great person through his family profession. Mostly people born in Aquarius with Saturn in Taurus will choose livelihood pursued by their parents. Pisces ascendant: With Saturn in Taurus, the subject will be blessed with vast wealth and longevity. If the Sun joins Saturn in Taurus, the subject is liable to lose his father in childhood.

बह्ध णबन्धनतातः श्रमान्वितो दाम्भिकोऽ्नुमन्त्री च। शस्तगुर्णः सन्त्यक्तः सदेव गुद्धाश्च कामशीलश्च ।५।। छलकृच्च मन्युदुष्टः क्रियातिशायी शठः कुशीलश्च । बन्धनविहारसक्तो बाह्चक्रोडानुगो मियुने ।६।

5-6. SATURN IN GEMINI: If Saturn occupies Gemini, one will contract debts and imprisonments, will toil (much), will have vanity in disposition, will consecrate by hymns and prayers, be bereft of virtues, be always in hide-out, be libidinous, cunning, wicked, and fond of wandering and of sports.

Notes: If Saturn occupies a house of Mercury, accord. ing to Varaha Mihira and Cunakara one will be shameless

and be devoid of wealth, happiness and progeny. He will tremble while writing and will serve as a protector (in the charge of protecting others' possessions). He will be a principal leader. This hints at the possibility of the subject being in employment related to guarding an establishment and the like. A chief position is assured as the indication is “Mukhyapati". If we go to the school of Keeranuru Nataraja, we are taught tersely two things. Firstly the native will have limited progeny and secondly he will befriend those devoid of reasoning and wisdom. Progeny is limited because Saturn as well as his dispositor are neutrals. (Conversely when Mercury is in the 5th in Capricorn, a Virgo native will obtain twins as mentioned, on p. 344, vol 1.) Reasoning, intelligence, wisdom etc., are the properties of Gemini also and Saturn adversely occupying this sign will drive one to those who are bereft of these attributes.

Kalyana Varma uses the word “Anumantri" which hints at the subject's engagement in acts of consecration by hymns and prayers. It may be as a goodwill measure or for obtaining livellhood, as prescribed for a Brahmin by the sacred Vedas. Though the word 'Bandha’ has been translated here (and elsewhere) as imprisonment, this should not be as a rule construed or misconstrued as 'imprisonment'. It can mean a simple confinement. It can as well mean imprisonment. Mercury is a planet of sports, vanity, cunning-wicked-wandering dispositions and fruitless tolls. These are conveyed by Saturn from Gemini. Our author adds that the person will remain in hideout or in exile. The native may be away from his people and his whereabouts will be unknown to many.

The individual ascendants with Saturn in Gemini will produce the following effects:

Aries ascendant: The native will have to undergo many ordeals in life particularly in the periods of Saturn. His father will not be any source of help and will be a person involved in religious activities. Taurus ascendant: In spite of the fact that the native will receive Rajayoga results from

Saturn, he will not be in a position to retain anything for his rainy days. The subject will enjoy long lease of ltfe, will not be in a position to repay his debts and will thereby suffer humiliation. Gemini ascendant: He will be ugly, will fall in bad company, be wicked, devoid of happiness and will be miserable. He will obtain defects in limbs with birth. Cancer ascendant: He will have a disturbed married llfe, will incur financial losses and will not enjoy paternal bliss. He will join bad people and will diminish his own wealth. Leo ascendant: In the dasa of Saturn one will obtain abundant landed properties and wealth—see 'Tatpake bhoomi labham cha dhana labham cha viseshatah’(describing effects of Saturn in Gemini for a Leo birth). The native will, however, face some physical distress in general. Virgo ascendant: Frequent changes in career, gains and losses with an equilibrium and distress to the wellbeing of the wife will come to pass. Libra ascendant: If Saturn occupies favourabie sub divisions the native will be rich from birth. Even an ordinary Saturn will give a moderate Raja Yoga and fortune. Scorpio ascendant: He will have weak sight, will have only a few children and be devoid of maternal happiness. His education will be through ups and downs. If Jupiter is not favourable, he will be poor, untruthful and inimical to brothers. (The qucstion of relationship with coborn will be unrelated to Jupiter’s disposition). Sagiltarius ascendant: He will be devoid of conjugal bliss, will lose his wife early or will suffer separation and will suffer from heart diseases. Capricorn ascendant: Contrary to traditional rule that Saturn in the 6th will subside debts and enemies, for this ascendant these will be on the increase as per the rule "Shashtamastha Sanerdaya satruroga rlnavaham” in the context of Makara Lagna. Aquarius ascendant: Saturn will give abundant gains and good fortunes. Pisces ascendant: He will be highly learned and be a successful professional. Occasional downfalls will also trouble him. Such natives are hardly brought up by their own mothers but by somebody else. Breast-feeding for the native is almost ruled out with this position.

सुभगान्वितो दरिद्रो बाल्ये रोगातिपीडितः प्राज्ञः। जननीरहितोऽतिम्बुबिशिष्ट निरतः सदातुरश्चापि ।।७।। परबाधको विशिष्टो बन्धुविरुद्धो विलोमशीलश्च । मध्ये चूपतितुस्यः परिभोगविर्वाधितः शशिभे ।८।।

7-8. SATURN IN CANCER: If Saturn is in Cancer, one will possess a beloved wife, be devoid of wealth in boyhood, will suffer many diseases, be learned, motherless, softspoken, distinguished in acts, will always contract diseases, will trouble others, be inimical to relatives, crooked, be kingly in his mid-life and will enjoy growing plcasures.

Notes: One will be devoid of wealth, deprived of teeth and will not have progeny, mother and learning, Varaha Mihira thus declares effects of Saturn in Cancer. Keeranuru Nataraja describes in this context thus: the native is born of a mcan scion, will be devoid of maternal inheritance, be indigent, will have yellowish (unclean) teeth and be foolish.

There are some additional effects stated by Kalyana Varma, uiz., beloved wife, lcarned, distinguished, growing pleasures etc. All are agreed that Saturn's placement in Cancer is detrimental to the mother of native. In as much as our author states that one will be devoid of wealth in boyhood, it is implied that the native will improve his financial state by dint of his own efforts. Obviously if Saturn is strong and the Moon is equally favourable these good effects will flow uninterruptedly and doubtlessly.

How the various ascendants are placed with reference to Saturn's stationing in Cancer may be seen from the following:

Aries ascendant: The native will be ominous to his mother, see 'Chaturthe maatrupeedakrith’ in the context of Aries versus Saturn in Cancer. To wit, one with Saturn in 4th identical with Cancer will lack the good fortune of a happy mother. Opposed by full Moon from Capricorn, Saturn in Cancer will yield exemplary results in regard to

livelihood. Taurus ascendant: The native will be issueless but will enjoy good effects in Saturn’s dasa. Gemini ascen. dant: Tooth decay, poverty, family problems etc.. will come to pass. Cancer ascendant: One will be ugly, despised and sick—see 'Kuroopam Kutsitam and rujam’in the context of Saturn in Lagna in Cancer. He will be liable to suffer diseases of the head and tooth and rheumatism. He will lose his mother soon after his birth. Leo ascendant: There will be many debacles in the dasa of Saturn. If a malefic joins him in being in the 12th, one will be even imprisoned. That is the declaration by an adage. However, Mars in company of Saturn in Cancer in the 12th for a Leo native is capable of giving a Rajayoga according to Sri Ramanujacharya. (This statement does not bar one from being imprisoned as per the previous reference). Virgo ascendant: There will be superior effects of Raja Yoga kind and the native will amass good fortunes. Libra ascendant: One will acquire conveyances, good status of living and will prosper. Scorpio ascendant: This will hinder the fortunes of the native greatly if the Moon is devoid of digits (i.e. pakshabala) and results will be modified favourable with the improved imposition of the Moon. Sagittarius ascendant: Long life, moderate wealth, gains through profession, childlessness, occasional difficulties etc., will come to pass. Capricorn ascendant: According to Mantreswara, if Mars joins Saturn in Cancer identical with the 7th one will acquire a beautiful and chaste wife. Saturn alone in the 7th will give a moderately happy though delayed marriage. Aquarius ascendant: There will be accumulation of not wealth but debts. Pisces ascendant: Lack of progeny will be inevitable with this placement. Married life will be through many obstacles and the native will be distressed with the ill health of his spouse.

लिपिपाठ्चपरोऽभिज्ञो विर्याहितो विगतशीलञ्च।

स्त्रीवियुतो मृतिजीवो स्वपक्षरहितो मुदा ह्रोमः ।।६।। नीचक्रियासु निरतो विवृद्धरोधो मनोर्यर्चात्तः। भाराध्वत्रमदुर्खः प्रकोर्णदेत् यमे सिहे ॥ १०।।

9-10. SATURN IN LEO: If Saturn occupies Leo, one will be interested in writing and reading, be skilful, be disdained, devoid of virtues and wife, will live by servitude, be devoid of his own men and happiness, be interested in doing base acts, ill-tempered, be mad with (undue) desires, will carry loads, will toil hard, and will have a wrinkled body.

Notes: Saturn in Leo will make one mean, devoid of happiness, childless and carry loads (vide Brihat Jataka).
