In Jatakalankara of Nataraja it is stated that the native will be devoid of any worthy behaviour, be bereft of happiness, be vile and will not have a son who can wax the fame of the descent.

Let us see how the inimically placed Saturn from Leo treats the various births in the 12 ascendants from the following:

Aries ascendant: This native is more prone to suffer from disorders of belly, indigestion and constipation, loss of memory, loss of children, foolishness and the like. He will have no good relationship with his own wife and children and will be poorly placed on family front. He will not gather much of wealth. Taurus asccndant: The ailments likely to occur will relate to the heart. The native will be bereft of mother but will enjoy conveyances and landed properties though dry. Gemini ascendant: He will enjoy some prosperity in favourable sub periods of Saturn dasa but will lose in regard to brothers. He will not have happiness from father. Cancer ascendant: Sick wife and straining expenses will come to follow this position. He will enjoy a long lease of life but be reduced to many miseries. Leo ascendant: Ugly and sick physique, poor intelligence and ficklemindedness will result in effect. Married life and professional career will produce only normal effect. If the Sun is simultaneously in the 7th house, as Saturn is in the ascendant identical with Leo, he will bestow smooth married life. (Conversely if the Sun alone is in Aquarius with Leo rising, it will create health problems for the wife and a strained relationshlp with her—vide p 249 Vol 1). Virgo ascendant: One will have financial implications, freedom from diseases, heavy ex-

penses and unhappy life. Childlessness may also result in effect. Libra ascendant: If Saturn dasa operates, it will confer fame, position, wealth and the like. Normally the native will have adopted children and will receive favours from rich men. Windy diseases like rheumatism, artheritis etc., will be experienced. Scorplo ascendant: Though the native will occupy good positions in life, he will have danger of displacement and being humiliated. During academic life, obstacles will be felt. Sagittarius ascendant: He will not be happy in respect of father and progeny. He will gather plenty of wealth through the assoclation of rich men and through government. Capricorn ascendant: The native will be shortlived and may face such obstacles even in the boyhood. There will be plenty of monctary problems throughout. Aquarius ascendant: Though there may be inimical relationship in marriage, the bonds will not be severed and the couple will get on well. Good fortunes will arise after marriage. The native will inherit some paternal properties. Pisces ascendant: The native will not have any brothers/sisters after him. He will have to face many ups and downs in his earnings.

षण्डाकारोऽतिशठः परान्नवेश्यारतोऽर्पमित्रश्च । शिल्पकलास्वनभिजनो विष्कर्यवेष्टो लसत्सृतार्थश्च ॥1१३॥। अलसः परोपकारी कन्याजनदूषक-क्रियामुश्तः । कन्यायां रवितनये ह्यवेक्ष्यकारी पुमान् जातः ॥ १२३।

11-12. SATURN IN VIRGO: If Saturn occuples Virgo at birth, the subject will resemble a eunuch, be very crafty, will depend on others for food, be addicted to prostitutes, will have a few friends, be unaquainted with arts, be desirous of indulging in ugly acts, will possess sons and wealth, be indolent, helpful to others, will be intent upon spoiling virgins and be cautious in his actions.

Notes: 'Kanyajanadooshaka’ has been interpreted here as spolling virgins as 'dooshaka’aptly means spoiler in this context. Thus the native with Saturn in Virgo will join many virgins for his sexual satisfaction. Unlike Gem-

ini, Virgo will give potence to Saturn in the matter of progeny. He will be addicted to courtezans apart from injuring the honour of the virgins confined to household. The meaning of word 'dooshaka’is so flexible that one may interpret this position as giving tendency of raping also. It follows that if malefics heavily afflict the rclativc houses and Saturn, one may even turn a rapist. Without having seen sufficient horoscopes in this context, a judgement is not wise.

## Remaining in Virgo, Saturn produces the following effects for the 12 ascendants.

Arics ascendant: The native will have success over enemies. He will find it easy to gain in litigations. He will incrcase his wcalth, will live long, will spend on vices, be healthy and will suffer adversitics in Saturn’s dasa. If Mercury joins Saturn in Virgo, the native will be highly intelligent, will have a very high position and will multiple his wealth. This combinations, however, will produce asthmatic problems. Taurus ascendant: The native will cnjoy in respect of children and will obtain many gains in his undertakings. Significantly, he will acquire a rich and fortunatc wifc. However, his married life will have want of peace. Another grave defect with Saturn in Virgo for this asccndant is inherent financial difficulties in spite of any amount of wealth. This does not mean insufficicncy in the matter of moncy but many irksome situations. Gemini ascendant: The native may lose his mother carly. If the mother survives it will be only to suffer dire health problems and many difficultics. Saturn through the lord of the 8th will prove very favourable in his dasa (apparently by owning the best trine). Cancer ascendant: This nattve will also be llable to suffer respiratory disorders. It may even somelimes be so grave as to take away the life of the native if maraka period be cffcctive. He is liable to lose his co. born, one by onc. So to say loss of brothers/sisters will be frequcnt. Yct Saturn will give the native some happiness at some limc or the other, depending on the periods in operaton. Leo ascendant: Saturn will prove a thorn close to the

eye. The native will always suffcr financial wants and will be never happy in life. He will incur diseases of the face, teeth and spleen. He will throughout have weak eye sight. In the family front he will be among the worst-placed. Virgo ascendant: Diseases will very frequently attack this native. He will be inimical to one and all, including to those in his family. He will be dark in complexion, ugly in appearance. black-and-long-haired and be with protruding teeth. If Saturn is in the rising decanate, some peculiarities in appearance can be noticed, like excess fingers, blunt thumb, thumblessncss, uneven eyes and the like. Libra ascendant: Life will not be happy with an important planet going to the 12th house. This will trigger of many go-backs in activities relating to advancement. Either there will be a number of children or there may be none depending on Jupiter. The native will, however, make distinct academic achievements but against many odds. Scorpio ascendant: He will be famous, longlivcd and fortunate. If Saturn dasa occurs, it will be far better and the native will derive a great Rajayoga from Saturn. Sagittarius ascendant: A dignified Saturn will elevate the native to lofty levels. Agriculture may add new dimensions to his livelihood. The native will be opulent and happy. He will gain through mother and wife also. Capricorn ascendant: Financial gains and paternal misfortunes will come to pass. The native will be brought up by other relatives than his own parents. Aquarius ascendant: He will pass through much difficulties and poverty in boyhood. Later on he will live a happy life and will. be blessed with a long lease of life. Pisces ascendant: He will acquire a wretched and contemptible spouse, will live a miserable life liable to a break in marriage and will always be at others’ mercy. Saturn’s dasa may inflict death either on him or on his spouse.

अर्थपरश्चारवचा नरो

विदेशाटनाप्तमानधनः।

नृपति सुबोधनो वा स्वपक्षगुप्तस्थितार्थः स्यात् ॥१३।। वृन्दसमानां ज्येष्ठो बचःप्रकर्षात्कृतास्पदः साधुः। कुलटानटीविटस्त्रोरमणो तुलायाते ॥१४।।

13-14. SATURN IN LIBRA: Saturn posited in Libra at birth indicates that the subject will be rich, softspoken, will earn money and honours from foreign countries, be a king or a scholar, will have his wealth protected by his relatives, be senior in the circle, will attain a high status owing to his efficacious speech in an assemblage, be good and will join corrupt females, dancers and prostitutes.

Notes: One with Saturn in exaltation will be famous, be a leader and be wealthy, according to Brihat Jataka and Hora Makaranda. He will have limitless wealth, be virtuous and be a leader of villages, as per Jatakalankara. Saturn's placement in exaltation will give wealth, position, fame and good longevity.

How the various ascendants stand to gain or lose with Saturn's exaltation may be noted from the following:

Aries ascendant: All may be well in general for this ascendant. The married life will be happy but loss of spouse will follow. Taurus ascendant: A good deal of wealth will be accumulated by the native. He will live long and his co-born will also become rich. Gemini ascendant: Birth of twins, rich father, paternal inheritance and high financial status will come to pass. This will, however, inflict h alth problems for progeny. Cancer ascendant: Though the effects of Sasa Yoga will be enjoyed by the native, in the end he will be reduced to abject poverty or severe fear. Leo ascendant: Though he will incur heavy debts and troubles from cousins, he will have a virtuous wife and live a happy life. Virgo ascendant: Financial gains and loss of children will come to effect. Libra ascendant: He by birth belongs to a very rich family, will increase the family property, will obtain prosperous children and be very fortunate. He will be highly learned, will enjoy several conveyances, will have vast business undertakings, yet will not be very intelligent. Sometimes he will behave in a very incredible manner. Scorpio ascendant: He will earn limitless wealth but will lose a major portion of it. He will specifically be in litigations in regard to family properties. Sagittarius ascendant: This

position will ward of many planetary blemishes relating to child mortality. The native as a youth will make abundant money and will save a good deal. He will have happy progeny, high degree of intelligence and be very fortunate. He will occupy famous positions in life. Capricorn ascendant: He will have a lot of money earned through his own exertion in his undertakings apart from parental wealth. He will be a successful houscholder. He will visit many shrines and will assoclate himself with many religious undertakings. He will propagate the teachings of the ancient. Aquarlus ascendant: Long lease of life, good fortunes and wealthy circumstances will come to pass. Pisces ascendant: Longevity will be unlimited. Though monetary influx will be there, the native will be llable to spend away on vices and immoral undertakings.

ह्वेवपरो विषमो वा विवशस्त्रध्नः प्रचण्डकोपश्च। लुब्धो वृप्तोऽर्थयुतः परस्वहरणे समर्यश्चः ।।१५।। बाह्यो मंगलवार्यानुशंसकर्मा ह्वानेकदुःखः स्यात् ।

## अष्टमराशौ रविजे क्षयव्ययध्याधिभिस्तप्तः ।।१६।

15-16. SATURN IN SCORPIO: If Saturn occupies Scorpio at birth, the native will be hostile, be crooked, affected by polson and weapons, very ill-tempered, miserly, egoistic, rich, capable of stealing others' money, averse to instruments played on festive occasions, malicious, very miserable and will face destruction, misery and diseases.

Notes: "The native with Saturn placed in Scorplo will have a mark on the body caused in confinement (or in fighting etc.), will not have lasting wealth and will be at large having committed a murder.” This is the verslon of Keeranur Nataraja in an emphatic manner. From this we may take a clue that if Saturn is in Scorpio at birth (particularly with great affliction from other planets and himself in evil Bhavas) one may commit a ghastly act and may be in dire legal troubles. He may be in exile or at large due to such a crime. (Any malefic planetary affliction in Scorplo may lead to performance of such deeds.). According to Nataraja,

the subject may have wealth but his financial prosperity will be duly checkered. This gives a hint that one will lose and gain in the matter of money. A permanent physical mark, like a scar, is left on the body of the native due to his involvement in fights and the like. Thus Saturn in this sign is more adverse for a peaceful and honourable life.

As per Kalyana Varma, Saturn in this sign will cause (some) wealth. It obviously depends on the disposition of the planet. “Bahvo Mangalavadye” has becn interpreted as having aversion for musical instruments played at festive occasions. This hints at the dislike of the native for festivals as well as music. The word 'Kshaya’though means tuberculosis, befitting the phrase, has been interpreted as destruction. A planet of destruction, Saturn, will contribute to one’s destruction remaining in Scorpio, a sign of destruction, the natural 8th house. It may be self-destruction or affliction to longevity, as Nataraja himself does not exto! in the context of longevity the placement of Saturn in the 8th identical with Scorpio for Aries ascendant (or in the 8th in Gemini for Scorpio ascendant).

The role of Saturn in Scorpio for the 12 signs ascending is denoted below:

Arles asccndant: In the 8th Saturn will give the native all kinds of difficultics. (The expression used in this context is 'ashtakashta’denoting eight kinds of evils as against 'ashta mangala’, i.e., possession of eight kinds of auspiclous things). According to Nataraja, as stated above, the native will suffcr cvils in the matter of longevity. Taurus ascendant: He will have a very bad taste in his married life. With Saturn's placement in the 7th the wife's longevity will not always suffer reduction but she will be unhealthy, quarrelsome ctlc. She may not have any praiseworthy qualities and will prove an cvil partner. The native will have ugly tecth, will risk dental deficiencies but will, however enjoy good results in the matter of prosperity. Gemini ascendant: The native will suffer ulcer, wound, burns and the like. He will mect a bad end. The wealth to be given by Saturn will only be moderate. Cancer ascendant: The native

will not beget progeny or will suffer great miseries if at all he begets. He will suffer disorders of belly, ulcer in intestines and piles and early symptoms of old age and utter poverty. He will lose his spouse early. Leo ascendant: As per our experience, a well placed Saturn in Scorpio has bestowed high learning. One can do Ph.D., IAS and the like with such a position. An afflicted Saturn will deprive one of maternal happiness, learning and general happiness. Such a native will always be in wants. Virgo ascendant: Loss of children, inability to repay loans, paternal loss etc., are denoted. Libra ascendant: One will gather much wealth and inherit from parents. But he will be tormented by expenses in astronomical proportions. Mostly he will spend on evil things, litigations, health of the family members and the like. He will not be in good terms with his mother and will indulge in acts causing loss of reputation to the family. Scorpio ascendant: He will be shortlived, be very miserable, poor and sick. Sagittarius ascendant: He will acquire money to the extent of his requirements but will be a spendthrift. His family llfe will be sorrowful and he will always fight with others. He will not perform any virtuous act. Capricorn ascendant: There will be a mixed trend in the matter of Yoga effects to be given by Saturn. He will maintain a healthy body and will live a happy life. Aquarius ascendant: He will achieve very great success in money and profession. Yet he will be miserable in regard to position. Pisces ascendant: The native will incur loss of coborn, be sick but moderately rich.

व्यवहारबोध्यशिक्षाश्रुतार्य विद्याभियानुकूलमतिः पुत्रपुर्णविस्यातः स्वघमंत्रत्तेश्च शीलंश्च ११७। अन्त्ये वयसि च लक्षमीभुनक्ति परमां प्रलब्धमानस्तु । अल्पवचा बहुसंतो मृदुर्यमे कारमुकस्बे स्थात् ॥१चा।

17-18. SATURN IN SAGITTARIUS: If Saturn occupies Sagittarius, one will be skilful in behaviour, teaching, vedic meaning, learning and denotation, (ie. he will be best placed in these respects), be famous due to virtuous chil.

dren, family profession and his own virtues, will enjoy excellent affluence in his old age, will speak less, will have many names, and be soft in disposition.

Notes: With Saturn in Sagittarius or in Pisces one will be acquainted with kings, will possess excellent and faultless speaking power, be happy, will acquire wealth through hls own progeny, be virtuous, will obtain a very favourable spouse and will favour widows and whores (vide Jatakalankara of Nataraja).

“Abhidha” used by Kalyana Varma indicates 'denotatlon’one of the three powers of a word. That is a different aspect and for common application we can take a clue from Nataraja's statement as well as our present text that the subject will be in general a gifted speaker. “Sangna" means name and hence one may hope to be honoured with titles and the like. This also means intelligence. The sign of Jupiter denotes honour as well as intelligence. Saturn in this sign will thus be in a position to confer honour, intelligence etc.

Let us see how this planet posited in Sagittarius in an anxious mood to return home after many years will act for each ascendant:

Aries ascendant: With a strong Saturn, the native will have a happy and rich father, will have prosperous and happy progeny, will have many achievements to his credit. be exceedingly fortunate, religious, God-fearing and widely honoured. He will throughout enjoy good health. If Saturn is adverse these effects will be liable to alteration. Taurus ascendant: Fortunes will be limited. Expenses will be controlled but be on good accounts. He will suffer monetary adversities and reversals in education. If Saturn is wellconnected with Karakas (indicators concerned) the adversitles will be to a lesser degree. The planet Saturn will, however, being in the 8th as Ayushkaraka will make great contribution to the longevity of the native. Gemini ascendant: Though marriage and wife’s health stand to be affected, these can be easily overcome by the native, depend-

ing on the strength of and aspect of Saturn. An afflicted Saturn will make married life very miserable. There may be a chance of two marriages. Saturn in the 7th in a house of Jupiter, i.e. either in Sagittarius or in Pisces, will give an ugly and stout wife. Cancer ascendant: One will enjoy a long lease of life, will incur more expenses through his wife and will spend on cvils. This placement indicates loss of brothers or no co-born and a poor wife. Leo ascendant: There may not be many children but one will surely obtain progeny, particularly sons. If Jupiter exchanges with Saturn, the former falling in Capricorn, there will be allround adversities in regard to children, money and longevity. So to say for a Lco native with Saturn in the 5th as Jupiter is in the 6th, these indications will greatly suffer. Virgo ascendant: Troublcs to famlly properties and to own education will crop up. Progeny may be many and be well off. Libra ascendant: The native may not have good terms with his parents, be unfortunate, will lose his brothers/sisters and will enjoy only a moderate fortune. Evcn in respect of these limited fortunes, there will be obstacles. Scorpio ascendant: Earning of money will be with great difficulty and one will have a big face and will suffer ailmcnts of eyes. He may go even illitcratc if Saturn falls in Aries in Navamsa. Sagittarius asccndant: Though there may be health problems in childhood, one will flourish later on in the matter of health and be wealthy, fortunatc and happy. Aquarius asccndant: He may suffer deafncss and other afflictions to the car. Financially one will gain and be happy with children. Pisces ascendant: One will be great, will carn a lot, be happy in profession and may be liable to suffer somc reversals.

परयोविरक्षेत्राणां प्रभुः श्रुतिगुर्णयुतश्च बहुशिल्पः । श्रेष्ठश्चर जंशजातेः पूज्यः परवृन्दसत्कृतः स्यातः ।१६।। स्नानविभुवणनिरतः श्रियाकलाज्ञः प्रवासशीलश्च। कोणे मृगभे जातः प्रजातशौर्योपचारः स्यात् ।।२०॥।

19-20. SATURN IN CAPRICORN: If Saturn is posited at birth in Capricorn, the native will lord over thc lands of

others’ females, will be endowed with vedic knowledge, virtues and knowledge of many branches of fine arts, be excellent among his castemen, be honourable, will respect others, be famous, be interested in bathing and decoration, will be skilful in performance, will live in foreign places, be courageous and be polite in behaviour.

Notes: The native with Saturn in Capricorn or in Aquarius will enjoy the wealth of other women, will be a leader of city, village etc., be weak-sighted, dirty, will have lasting wealth and will enjoy. This is how Varaha Mihira and Gunakara describe the context. Jatakalankara by Nataraja states that the native will lead over beautiful towns and citics, will have small eyes, will suffer eye afflictions, will continue to earn wealth unintcrrupledly, will spend, be of modcrate height, be spiritual and be (yet) a thicf. One can draw similarity in the statcments of Varaha Mihira and Nataraja, thc latter bcing no less an authority than other ancients, that the native will be a thicf. Varaha Mihira uscs a mild phrase, viz., "Anyangana-vittachauryam" mcaning that the nalive will steal wcalth of other women. So to say one will be a thief of wealth of others—about which there is no contradiction between the thrcc, viz., Varalıa Mihira, Gunakara and Nataraja. Kalyana Varma while dealing with Saturn in Capricorn uses the phrasc "Parayoshithkshetranam prabhuh” which mcans: will lord over landed propertics (or abodes) of females not of his own. This is the litcral translation. From a practical point of vicw onc should infer this that the native will corner the lands of other helpless women. This inference is morc than fairly supported by the other three authoritics.

As far as other indications arc conccrned, with Saturn's position in Capricorn onc will have bad cye-sight, be always cndowed with wcalth (though there is no specific cluc about onc's being rich or poor in our text), be learned, exccllent, honourable elc. Saturn being the owner of the zodlac's 10th house, his placcment in own house will make onc a succcss in his carcer. He will be more successful in his nativc land than in other places.

How Saturn in Capricorn acts upon each ascending sign may be understood from the following:

Aries ascendant: Primarily the native will confine to his own birth place for obtaining his livelihood. He will get Raja Yoga results in Sani dasa. His childhood will be spent happily with prosperous education. He will be free from diseases and will maintain a healthy body. Taurus ascendant: While the native will be generally fortunate he may lose his father early or will not be fortunate in that respect. This will be dependent on the disposition of the Sun. The native will also suffer loss of co-born. If the Moon is declining when Saturn is in Capricorn, the relationship with brother, friends etc., will not be smooth. Gemini ascendant: One will be long lived, will undergo many difficulties for livelihood, will be unlucky and will not have a happy family life. These adversities will not, however, take a very adverse turn. Cancer ascendant: This position is detrimental for the longevity of the spouse if Saturn is alone in the 7th. If he is helped by the Moon or Sun this affliction will be overcome while normally Saturn with the Sun or the Moon in the 7th is very bad. If Saturn is alone in the 7th the native will be very sickly and will suffer from one disease or the other from time to timc. In a femalc’s chart bleeding will be expericnced. Leo ascendant: In the dasa of Saturn the native will gain wealth through enemies or from litigations. He will throughout be free from sickness. He will not be very intelligent and will live long. Virgo ascendant: The native will obtain twins with Saturn in the 5th. His children in gcneral will be a source of concern with diseases. The native will be short of hearing or will suffer from some ailments of the ears. Moneywise, only moderate results will be experienced with susceptibility to loans. Libra ascendant: He will have a longliving mother and will respect her very much. He will be learned, skilful in business, will have protruding teeth, long hair and thick nails and will enjoy a very long span of life. Houses to be owned by such a native will normally be built-ones (ie. old) and will not bear a good look. Scorpio ascendant: He will not be very learned, will not live with his father, will suffer on account of children

and will enjoy only moderate fortunes. The native's wife will suffer from disorders relating to breathing, lungs, breast etc. If Rahu joins Saturn in Capricorn the person will be lame. Sagittarius ascendant: He will be not very happy (but indeed not miserable) in his family life. He will acquire money with some difficulty. He will have two marriages. His wife will be harsh in speech and will not honour the elders in his family. Capricorn ascendant: He will severely undergo the three kinds of miseries in life (i.e. Tapatraya which denotes miseries of Adhyatmika type, Aadhidalvika type and Aadhibhautika type). [The first relates to self (and the soul), the second to tutelary deity or caused by fate and the third to material kind caused by animals as pain. Every individual born in this world will have to undergo these sufferances or Tapatraya in varied degree and at various periods according to one's poorvakarmaj. The native will find his earnings in an employment but with obstacles. He will be sick and will be deprived of his parents. Raja Yoga results will, however, be experienced in Saturn dasa. Aquartus ascendant: He will acquire long living children. More expenses, financial difficulties, facial discases etc., will also come to pass. Pisces ascendant: He will have a long living mother and will inherit some maternal property also. He will suffer great miseries in boyhood. He will himself enjoy good span of life.

बह्लनृतः सुमहान् स्यान्मद्यस्त्रीव्यसनसम्प्रसक्तश्च । घूर्तो वंचनशीलः कुसौह्दो ह्यतिवृदश्चण्डः ।२१। ज्ञानकचास्मृतिबाह्याः परारंगनार्थः सुकर्कशाभावी ।

रवितनये कुम्पस्थे बहुक्रियारम्मकृतयत्नः ।२२।।

21-22. SATURN IN AQUARIUS: If Saturn is in Aquarius, the native will be a great liar, be eminent, be addicted to women and wine, be wicked, crafty, will fall prey to evil friendship, be very ill-tempered, be averse to knowledge, conversation and traditional law, be addicted to other women, be harsh in speech and will attempt at many undertakings.

Notes: The views of Jatakalankara, Hora Makaranda and Brihatjataka for Saturn's placement in Aquarius are not different from those applicable to Capricorn, vide notes under the two previous slokas. Our author gives different results for Saturn's placement in Capricorn and Aquarius. Only evil results have been attributed to Saturn’s placement in Aquarius by Kalyana Varma.

How each ascendant will stand to gain or lose with Saturn in Aquarius may be seen from the undermentioned results:

Aries ascendant: The native will stand to gain highly in his undertakings. He will not enjoy a happy married life and will suffer from disorders of stomach and anus. Taurus ascendant: Though he will have a prosperous career, he will sometimes greatly suffer adversities. He will incur litigations on account of paternal properties. Gemini ascendant: He will suffer miseries and be not happy with regard to father. This position will cause loss of co-born and will cause discases of legs. An attack of paralysis will always have to be feared with Saturn in Aquarius. Cancer ascendant: Though married llfe will be strained with the native becoming a widower or a widow as the case may be, one will have a long lease of life. Life will be unfortunate and unhappy for the native. Financial problems will always be on the increase. Leo ascendant: Almost similar results will come to pass with the addition of the native getting addicted to whores, gambling and the like. Virgo ascendant: Financial problems will be plenty with a long lease of life. A disturbed married life will be evident. Libra ascendant: Progeny will be delayed but be surely acquired. One will acquire a life partner from a rich place. Financial losses are not ruled out. Scorplo ascendant: One will be learned, will suffer severe diseases like piles, rheumatism, swollen diaphragm and the like. Maternal happiness will lack if the Moon is simultaneously devoid of strength. Saglttarlus ascendant: He will have a very rough volce which will not be pleasing to listen. He will have danger for his arms early in life. There will not be any happiness in the family of the

native. Capricorn ascendant: One will be wealthy and with a strong Jupiter his riches will be still greater. Saturn in Aquarius will give a long lease of life to this native but there will be some miserable events in life. Marriagc will not be so happy with the scope of a second marriage not being ruled out. Aquarius ascendant: One will not fail to get Raja Yoga results from Saturn who may sometimes prove a maraka or may inflict somc miseries equal to death. Such a native will lament over many deaths in the family. Pisces ascendant: Financial implications, involvement in monetary misappropriation, suffcring severe health troubles etc., will come to pass.

प्रिययज्ञशिल्पविद्यः स्वबन्धुसुद्धदां प्रधानशान्तश्च । सं्बाधितार्थमुनयो रत्नपरीक्षासु इतयत्तः ।।२३।। धर्मव्यवहाररतो विनीतशीलो गुर्णः समायुवतः । मीने भास्करतनये पश्चाद्भावास्पदं पुरुषः ।।२४।।

23-24. SATURN IN PISCES: If Saturn occupies Pisces at birth, one will be fond of Sacrifices and arts, be cliicf among his relatives and friends, be calm, will have increasing wcalth, be skilful in policy-making, be capable of diamond testing, be virtuous, modcst and will lalcr on acquire an authoritative position.

Notes: Whilc Kecranuru Nataraja treats Saturn in Sagittarius and Pisces alike, Kalyana Varma attributes specific cffects for occupation of the two diffcrent signs. In the second quartcr of sloka 24, we find the words "paschadbhavaspadam” which when properly rendered mcans that one will in the later part of life procure a positlon with authority. Pisces bcing the last sign of the zodlac and Saturn being a planet to give slow and steady effects, it aplly mcans that the person will receive the goods from Saturn not just in the beginning of life but later on. Some versions read this phrase as "vishadam paschad bhavet purushah" which hints at the possibility of administering polson (to somebody).

Remaining in Pisces Saturn bestows following effects for the various ascendants:

Aries ascendant: The native will gather abundant wealth but will lose everything on account of vices and evil expenses. He will suffer from severe eye defects. One born in day time will lose his father early while the one born in night time will get favourable results about his father. Taurus asccndant: Mentally a much distressed native is denoted. Positionally he will be happy and will earn much wealth. He will make money through business vla sea (i.e. exports) and by water-related products, (utz. salt, fish, pearls, steamers and the like). Gemini ascendant: He will be an eminent person and will carn honours. He will be addicted to many a vice. His mother will be prone to risk from water. Cancer ascendant: He will be devoid of paternal happincss but will enjoy marital felicity. His wife will be stout, intelligent and lucky. Leo ascendant: He will not be successful in financial matters provided Jupiter or the Sun render help to Saturn. Virgo ascendant: One will procure a sick wife. There will be no happiness either in marriage or in respect of progeny. Libra ascendant: A long span of life, distress due to children and inharmony with co-born will follow. Saturn in the 6th for this ascendant will prove a boost for accumulating wealth. Scorpio ascendant: The native’s children are greatly affected adversely. There will be heavy financial burdens and losses coupled with distress in married life. Sagittarius ascendant: One will receive high education and will be well placed in profession. Capricorn ascendant: One will have good conduct, philosophical disposition and wealth. Aquarius ascendant: Long life, financial prosperity etc., will come to pass followed by heavy cxpenses and losses. Pisces ascendant: Ill-health, lack of happiness, physical defects, grotesque growth etc., will be felt with Saturn in this ascendant.

## कर्षणनिरतमयाढच गोमहियाजाविसंयुतं धन्यम्।

सूर्षेण वृश्यमानो जनयति कर्मोद्धतं सौरिः ।२५१।

चपल नीचप्रकृति नीचविरूपांगनासु संसवतम् । शनिरिग्युदृष्टमूतिः सुखधनरहितं गरं कुस्ते ।२६।। प्राणिवधपर क्षुद्र कुर्ते चोराधिपं सुविस्यातम्। प्रिययुवतिमांसपानं सौरो बक्रेक्षितः कुजभे ।।२७।। आनृतिकमधर्मपरं बद्धाशं तस्करं प्रकाशं च । कौजे शनिर्शदृष्टः सुखविभवविनाङ्गतं पुरुवम् ।।२८।। सुखधनसौभाग्ययुतं नृपमन्त्रिणमप्रगं च सचिवानाम् । गुरुदृष्टो रवितनयः कुजगेहे मानवं कुस्ते ।२६।। अतिचपलमतिविरूपं परांगनापण्ययुवतिसंसक्तम् कुजभवने भुगुदृष्टो जनयति रविजो विर्वाजतं भोर्गः ।। ३०।।

25-30. SATURN IN ARIES/SCORPIO IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Saturn occupies a Rasi of Mars and is aspected by the Sun, one will be interested in agriculture, be very affluent, be endowed with cows, buffaloes and horses, be fortunate, and industrious. If by the Moon, he will be fickleminded, base, will join mean and ugly women and be devoid of happiness and wealth. If by Mars, he will kill animals, be base, be a leader of robbers, be famous and be fond of (joining other) women, meat and wine. If by Mercury, he will be untruthful, not virtuous, will cat much, be a famous thief, and be devoid of happiness and riches. If by Jupiter, he will be endowed with happiness, wealth and fortune, be a king’s minister and be chief. If by Venus, he will be quite unsteady in disposition, very ugly, will join other women and courtezans and be bereft of pleasures.

Notes: Saturn's lonely position in Aries will make one poor but rich if in Scorpio (vide respective slokas of this chapter). If he is aspected by the Sun as stated in the present slokas one will be rich, as the Sun is a benefic for both the signs. The aspect of the Moon is equally favourable for both the signs. Without meaning any disrespect of Kalyana Varma, it is pointed out that the native will be

wealthy as the aspect of the Moon will be either from Taurus (when she is in exaltation) or from Libra (Saturn is favourable for Libra Janmarasi). There are of course bound to be some financial reversals, for Saturn in the 7th from the Moon is not conducive to lasting riches. Mercury's aspect is a felicious augury for wealth as well as that of Jupiter. The aspect of Venus will make one highly immoral.

स्फुटवाष्यं विगतधनं विद्वांसं परगृहेषु भोक्तारम्। रविणा दृष्टः सौरिः सितभे परिपेलवं पुरुषम् ।।३१।। युवतिजनजनितसारं नृपमनित्रिपुरस्कतं युवतिकाम्तम्। शशिना दृष्टः सौरिः कुस्ते सितमे कुटुम्बपरिवृत्तम् ॥।३२। संग्रामकयाभिज्ञं संत्र(मपलायिनं सुबहुवाव्यम् । भुगुचे कुजसन्दृष्ट। जनधनपरिवेशिटितं सौरः ।।३३।। नित्यं विहसनशीलं बलीवसमं युवतिसेवकं नीचम्। बुधवृष्टो रवितनयः शुक्रम्हे मानवं कुस्ते ।।३४।। परविषयदुःखसुखिनं परकार्यकरं प्रियं च लोकस्य । कुस्ते गुरुणा दृष्टो बातारं सोद्यमं सौरिः ।३५।। मद्यस्त्रीकृतसौर्यं रत्नानां भाजनं महासत्वम् । शुक्युहे सितवृष्टो जनयति सौरो नृपतिदवितम् ।।३६।।

31-36. SATURN IN TAURUS/LIBRA IN ASPECT TO OTH- ERS: Should Saturn be in a sign of Venus and is aspected by the Sun, the native will be clcar in speech, will lose wcalth, be a scholar, will eat in other’s houses, and be weak in constitution. If by the Moon, he will gain wealth (12X) through women, will be honoured by ministers of kings, be dear to women, and be endowed with family. If by Mars, he will be skilful in war preparations but will be away from war, will speak much and be endowed with wealth and family. If by Mercury, he will always be jouclarly disposed,

be equal to a neuter, will serve females and be base. If by Jupiter, he will share happiness and misery of others, will do others' jobs, be dear to people, charitable and industrious. If by Venus, he will be happy on account of women and wine (i.e. will enjoy these), be endowed with gems, be very strong and be dear to the king.

सुक्रहितमथात्यन्तं धनरहितं धामिकं जितक्रोधम् । क्लेशसहिष्णुं धीरं बुधभे रविवीक्षितः सौरिः ।।३७। नृपतुल्यं स्निग्धतनु नारीभ्यः प्राप्तविभवसत्कारम् । स्त्रीणां वा कृत्यकरं सौरश्वन्द्रेक्षितो बुधमे ।।३८।। विस्यातमल्लमोहितमतिभारवहं तथा विक्तगात्रम् । रुधिरांगवीक्षिततनुर्जनयति सौरो नर बुधमे ।।३९। धनिनं नियुद्धकुशलं नृत्ताचार्य च गीतकुशलं च। शिल्पकमतीव निपुरणं बुधभे बुधवीक्षितः सौरः ॥४०।। प्रात्यविकं राजकुले सर्वगुणसमन्वितं सतामिष्टम् । गुणगुह्चधनं कुरुते गुरुणा दृष्टः शनश्चारी ।।४१।। स्त्रीमण्डनेषु कुशलं योगाचार्यमय योगिनं वाऽपि । शुश्रेक्षितोऽर्कपुत्र: स्त्रीणामिष्टं नरं बुधमे ।४२।।

37-42. SATURN IN GEMINI/ VIRGO AND IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Saturn occupies a Rasi of Mercury at birth and is aspected by the Sun, the native will be devoid of happiness and wealth, be virtuous, bereft of anger, will endure difficulties and be valorous. If by the Moon, he will be equal to a king, will possess a bright physique, will earn wealth and honour through women and will do women’s jobs. If by Mars, he will be a famous boxer, be stupefled, will carry heavy loads and will possess an ugly body. If by Mercury. he will be rich, skilful in war, be a dance master, a skilful singer and an expert in arts. If by Jupiter, he will be trustworthy in the king's circle, will possess all kinds of (good) qualities, be liked by good men and will earn wealth

through his virtues. If by Venus, he will be skilful in beautifying women, be a teacher of Yogas, or a saint and be dear to the fair sex.

वित्रा रहितं बाल्ये दिनपतिदृष्टः शनेश्वरः शशिमे । धनस्खदारबिहीनं कदशनतुष्टं नरें पापम् ।४३।। जन्मनि मातुरनिष्टं धनवन्तं सहजपोडितं चैव । श्ञशिदृष्टः शशिभवने दिनकरपुत्रो नरं कुस्ते ।४४।। नृपतिसमापितविभवं विकलांगं कनकरत्नं सपरिवारम् । कुजवृष्टः शशिभवने कुबन्धुपत्नीरतं सौरिः ।४५ निव्युरमतिप्रवाचं शमिताराति च वाम्मकं चापि । जनवत्युत्तमचेष्टं बृधद्ष्टो भास्करिः शशिमे ।४६।। क्षेत्रगृहाणां सुहूदां पुत्राणां भागिनं नरं कुर्ते । धनरत्नदारबन्तं शशिचे गुरुवीक्षितः सौरः ।।४७।। आवतकुलजातानां रूपविलासे: सुर्लेश्च रहितानाम् । कुर्ते जन्म नाराणां भुगुदृष्टः कर्कटे सौरिः ।४८।। 43-48. SATURN IN CANCER IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Saturn occupies Cancer at birth and is aspected by the Sun, the native will lose his father in his very boyhood, be bereft of wealth, wife and happiness, will eat bad food and be sinful. If by the Moon, he will be a source of evil to his mother, be wealthy and will be troubled by his co-born. If by Mars, he will enjoy king’s wealth, be defective bodied, will possess gold and gems, be endowed with a famlly and will have a bad group of relatives and wife. If by Mercury, one will be hard-hearted, garrulous, will conquer his enemies, will show vanity and will do noble acts. If by Jupiter, he will be endowed with lands, houses, friends, sons, wealth, gems and wife. If by Mercury, one will be hardhearted, garrulous, will conquer his enemies, will show vanity and will do noble acts. If by Jupiter, he will be endowed with lands, houses, friends, sons, wealth, gems and wife. If by Venus, he is of a noble descent but will be

bereft of beauty, grace and happiness.

Notes: If the Sun aspects Saturn in Cancer it is inauspicious for the father of the native while the aspect from the Moon is adverse for mother.

सुखधनहीनमनार्य प्रियान्तं पानसक्तकुतनुं च। भृतक बुःखिनमेकं सिहे सूर्वेक्षितः सौरः ॥ ४६।। मानाररनधनानां युवतीनां भाजनं विपुलकीतिम्। सिसिरयुवृष्टः सिहे सौरो नृपवल्लभं पुरक्षम् ॥ ५०। प्रतिदिनमटनमधन्यं चोरं गिरिदुर्गवासिनं क्षुद्रस्। चार्यापुत्रविहोनं सिहे सौरो रुधिरदृष्टः ॥ ५१)। नैह्तिकमससमधनं स्त्रीकर्मकरं मलीमसं दीनश्। बनयति बुधेन दृष्टो दिनकरभवनाश्रितः सौरिः ।५२।। ग्रामपुरश्रेणीनां पुरोगमादयं च तुत्रवन्तं च। गुवुवृष्टः प्रात्ययिक सिहे सौरः सुशीलं च ।५३।। युवतिह्वेव्यं कान्तं मन्यरसुखभागिनं धनसमृद्धम्। शुक्रेक्षितस्तु कुक्ते भानुगृहें रविसुतः स्वन्तम् ॥५४।।

49-54. SATURN IN LEO IN ASPECT OF OTHERS: If Saturn occupies the Sun-owned Rasi, and is aspected by the Sun himself, the native will be devoid of wealth, happiness and nobility, will be a liar and a drunkard, will possess a bad physique, be a servant and be very miserable. If by the Moon, he will enjoy abundant wealth, precious stones and women, be widely famous and be dear to the king. If by Mars, he will everyday move from place to place, be unfortunate, will live in fortresses and hills, be base and be bereft of wife and sons. If by Mercury, he will not be outspoken, be lazy, poor, will do females' job, be dirty and miserable. If by Jupiter, he will be chief and rich in his town or among his men, will be endowed with progeny and be trustworthy. If by Venus, he will be averse to women, be splendorous,

slow (or tardy), happy, rich and will attain a good end. परपुत्राणां पितरं गुरुचे सूर्वेक्षितः सौरः। तेस्यो धनं च लभते नाम ख्याति व पूजां च ॥५९॥ मात्रहितं सुशीलं नाम्यसंयुतं रवेत्तनवः । कुष्ते ससिना वृष्टो मार्यासृतवित्तसम्पन्नम्।।५६। वात व्याधिगृहीतं लोक्वब्यं च पारशीलं श । शुद्रं निन्दितशीलं गुरुषे भौमेक्षितः सौरः ॥ ७॥। जनयति गुरुभवनस्यो नृपतिसवं सौख्यवन्तमाच्वार्धंश्। मान्यं घनिनं सौम्यं सुभगं सौम्येक्षितः सौरः ॥इच।। नृपति नृपतुल्यं वा मन्त्रमथ नायकं च सेनायाः। जनयति गुरुणा दृष्टः सर्वापद्वजितं सौर: ।५६।। कुस्ते द्रिमातृ पितृकं विविनाद्रिवु जीविनं विविधशीलम् । जनयति सितेन दृष्टो रवितनयः कर्मसम्पन्नम् ॥ १०।। 55-60. SATURN IN JUPITER'S RASI AND IN ASPECT TO OTHERS: If Saturn occupies Sagittarius or Pisccs at birth, in aspect to the Sun the native will be the father of others' children, and through these children he will attain wealth, namc, fame and honour. If by the Moon, he will be bereft of mother, will have two names and be endowed with wife, children and wealth. If by Mars, he will be troubled by windy diseases, will dislike pcople, be sinful, mcan, blamcworthy etc. If by Mercury, he will be equal to a king, be happy, be a preccplor, be honourable, rich and fortunate. If by Jupiter, he will be a king or equal to a king or a minister or an army chief and be free from all kinds of danger. If by Venus, he will have two mothers and two fathers, will live in forests and hills, will be unstcady, and be endowed with many kinds of assignments.

Notes: In sloka 60 it is stated that Venus aspecting Saturn in a sign of Jupiter will indicate two mothers and

wo fathers. This only means that the native will be brought up by such relatives who are equal to his mother and father. Alternatively, if Jupiter is indicative of a yoga for adoption the native may go in adoption whereby he will have father and mother again.

रोगिणमर्पभार्य परान्नभोगिनमतीव वुःखसहम् । अटनरतं भारसहं सौरः सूर्वेक्षितः स्वगुहे । ६१। चपलमसत्यं पापं मातुरनिष्टं प्रियानृतं स्वाइद्यम्। उत्पन्नाटनदुःखं स्वगृहे चन्द्रेक्षितः सौरः ।६२।। अतिशूरं विक्रान्तं विस्यातगुणं महाजनपुरोगम् । तीक्षणं साहसनिरतं स्वगृहे बक्रेक्षितः सौरः ।।६३।। भारसहं तामसिकं शोभनमटनज्ञमल्पावित्तं च । धन्यं जनयति शनिमे बुधेन संवीक्षितः सौरः ।६४।। समुदितगुणं नरेन्द्रं नृपवंशकरं विरायुषमरोगम् । त्रिदशगुरुदृष्टमूतिर्जनयति सौरः स्वगृहसंस्थः ।६५।। घनिनं परवाररतं सुमगं सुखिनं च वित्तवन्तं च। उत्पन्नपानभक्ष्यं स्वगृहे शुक्रेक्षितः सौरः ।६६।।

61-66. SATURN IN OWN HOUSE IN ASPECT TO OTH- ERS: If Saturn is in Capricorn/Aquarius and is aspected by the Sun, he will be sick, will have an ugly wife, will cat other’s food, be miserable, wandering in nature and will carry loads. If by the Moon, he will be fickle-minded, untruthful, sinful, will not have good terms with his mother, be rich and be sorrowful due to wandering. If by Mars, he will be very valorous, famous, be superior among great men and sharp. If by Mercury, he will carry loads, be clouded in mentality (or Tamasik in disposition), be good-looking, wandering-natured, learned, be not quite wealthy and be fortunate. If by Jupiter, he will be famous for his good qualities, be a king or be of royal scion, long-lived and be

free from discases. If by Venus, he will be rich, addicted to other women, fortunate, happy and will enjoy food and drinks.

इति कल्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां शनिचाराध्यायः ।।२६।

Thus ends the 29th Chapter entitled "EFFECTS OF SATURN IN VARIOUS RASIS” in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## UNIQUE CLASSIC

(Original Text with Translation, Notes & Exhaustive Commentary in lucid English) by

## Dr G. S. Kapoor

Excellent Work on Predictive Aströlogy

## अथ ग्रह्मावफलाध्यायः ॥ ३०॥

## Chapter 30

## Effects of Planets in Bhavas

मूरत्यावया पदार्था जायन्ते येन सर्वजन्तूनाम्। तस्मावघुनः वक्ष्ये भावाध्यायं विशेषेण ।।१।।

1. In order that the effects of physique etc., for the various human beings are understood, I explain below things relating to (various) Bhavas (of a horoscope) in a special manner.

Notes: In the earlier chapters the effects due to various planets in various Rasis and aspects thercto have been explained and now dealt with are the effects of each planet in various bhavas. Before arriving at the results of various positions one will have to make a meaningful mix of a planet’s position in a sign, in a house and the aspect related to the context.

लग्नेऽकऽल्पकचः क्रियालसमतिः कोधी प्रचण्डोन्नतो मानी लोचनर्क्षकर्कशततुः शुरोऽक्षमो निर्यृणः। स्फोटाक्षः शशिचे क्रिये स-तिमिरः सिहे निशान्धः पुमान् वारिद्योपहतो विनष्टतनयो जातस्तुलायां नरः ।२।। THE SUN IN VARIOUS BHAVAS (upto sloka 13)

2. SUN IN ASCENDANT: The Sun in the ascendant denotes that the native will have less hair (on the head), be lazy in function, given to anger, will have prominent personality, be honourable, will have weak sight and coarse physique, be courageous, impatient and unkind. If the ascendant is Cancer and is occupied by the Sun.

the native will have swollen eyes; if it be Aries ascendant with Sun therein, his sight will be weak and in Leo ascendant, the Sun makcs him night-blind. One suffers poverty and loss of children if the Sun occupies Libra Ascendant.

Notes: Our commentary for the positions of planets in various bhavas owes its acknowledgement to authorities like Jeeva Natha, Jaya Deva, Chamatkara Chintamani, Jataka Parijata, Bhrigu Sutra, Hora Ratna, Manasagari, Phala Deepika, Jataka Sagara, Kheta Kautuka. Yavanas havc specifically treated Bhava Phalas with reference to female births. Such notes have also been added in this context which are of course interchangcable for male nativities as well if applicable.

The native with the Sun in ascendant will be tall in stature, will be tormented on account of thoughts related to his wife and children, will suffer from windy and bilious diseases, will be happy in money matters (though there may be occasional debacles) and be happy. He will havc lcss number of children and will suffer from allments of eyes. He will be fond of quarrels, dance, literature etc., and be strong. He will have an emaciated body, bald head and reddish eyes. He will speak only when required. If the Sun is in Pisces as ascendant one will Join many women; in Capricorn or in Aquarius as ascendant, he will be prone to hcart diseases; in the signs of Venus identical with ascendant, he will suffcr from severe eye diseases and if in Leo, Cancer or in Aries as ascendant, there will be nightblindness or a floral mark on the pupll. Some texts say that the Sun in the ascendant will not give progeny while Bhrigu says that in Virgo ascendant the Sun will, however, give female children. The Sun in the ascendant will afflict the virility of the native and hence with simultaneous affliction to Jupiter, progeny may not be begotten. Otherwise, with favourable Jupiter, the Sun is the ascendant will not totally deprive one of progeny.

In a female's chart, the Sun in the ascendant will cause her many diseases and will make her dependant on

others. She will be ungrateful, base and emaciated.

## द्विपदचतुष्पदभागी मुखरोगी नष्टविभवसौस्यश्च । नृपचोरमुषितसारः कुटुम्बगे स्याद्रवौ पुरुषः ।।३।।

3. SUN IN 2nd: If the Sun is in the 2nd bhava, the native will be endowed with servants and quadrupeds, will suffer facial diseases, will be deprived of happiness and wealth and will lose money through royal displeasure or through thicves.

Notes: The native with the Sun in the 2nd will be wealthy, will have family problems will not enjoy much sexual pleasures, will have financial losses, be not wellrelated to his relatives, be sometimes very distressed, will have afflicted progeny, will frequently change his residence and will have checkered learning, afflicted speech, ugly hair, reddish eyes and small teeth. He will suffer facial diseases and will incur the wrath of the government. Many texts including Brihat Jataka altribute richness to the Sun's placement in the 2nd house. However, Phala Deepika, and Yavanas say that one will not be wealthy. Bhrigu Rishi indicates possible financial loss in the 25th year, and if the 2nd is identical with Aries/Leo, there will not be any loss. As per Vridha Yavanas, a female with the Sun in the 2nd will be devoid of wealth, good speech and friendliness. She will always be treachcrous, fond of promoting disputes in the family and be not pious. (Such a woman may be llable to lose the confidence of her family members).

## विक्रान्तो बलयुक्तो विनष्टसहजस्तृतीयगे सूर्थे।

## लोके मतोऽभिरामः प्राज्ञो जितदुष्टपक्षश्च ।४।।

4, SUN IN 3rd: Should the Sun be in the 3rd bhava, the native will be valorous, strong, will lose co-born, be dear to people, goodlooking, very learned and will conquer his enemies.

Notes: One with the Sun in the 3rd will not be happy in regard to co-born. Bhrigu Maharshi adds that he will not even retain his elder brothers, which is confirmed by Parasara also.

He will achieve easy success in litigations, be wealthy, be highly inimical to brothers, intelligent, very learned, famous, eloquent in speech, liberal, devoid of sickness, but will suffer difficulties of health in his 4th, 5th, 8th and 12th years. He will be brought up by another female equal to his mother. If a female has the Sun in the 3rd, she will be ever devoid of happiness, devoid of sickness, be beautiful and will have prominent and stout breasts.

## वाहनबन्धुविहीनः पीडितह्दयश्वतुर्थके सूयें । पितृगृह्धतनाशकरो भवति नरः कुनृपसेवी च ।५।।

5. SUN IN 4th: Should the Sun be in the 4th bhava, the native will be devoid of conveyances and relatives, will suffer heart discases, will destroy paternal house and wealth and will serve a bad king.

Notes: "Will serve a bad king" denoted in the text should mean that one with the Sun in the 4th will serve a bad employer.

The Sun in the 4th house will acquire a job with great prosperity. One will be insulted by enemies, will lose in litigations, will lack amity with brothers and other relatives, will never be peaceful, will fall in bad iriendship, will not be financially happy, will not stick to one place, will diminish his wealth, be addicted to whores, will lack paternal happiness, be a discredit to the family, be not endowed with conveyances, landed properties and the like, will not have a wor thy history, will have defective limbs, will long to have a beautiful wife, will lose some children and will be very beautiful to attract women. In the case of a female, the Sun in the 4th will make her sick, ugly-teethed, devoid of splendour and happiness and go against many.

## सुखसुतवित्तविहीनः कर्षणगिरिदुर्गसेवकश्वपलः ।

मेधावी बलरहितः स्वल्पायुः पंचमे तपने । ६॥

6. SUN IN 5th: If the Sun occupies the 5th bhava, the native will be bereft of happiness, sons and wealth, will live by husbandry, will move in hills and fortresses, be fickleminded, scholarly, devoid of strength and be short-lived.

Notes: The Sun in the 5th will afflict the progeny on which many authorities are unanimous. some say he will losc the first son while some say he will have only one issue. Yet some declare he will be devoid of happiness on account of progeny. In summary there will be loss of issue or miserable children or delayed birth of children. The placement of the Sun in 5th is harmful for progeny in some form or other. “He will be so intelligent that he can understand anything and evcrything on the earth." He will be particularly skilful in Mantra Sastra and will derive great pleasures in cheating others. He will not have much wealth and will be wandering in lonely places, will steal others' articles and be irreligious.

Kheta Kautuka is the lonely popular work to say that the native will be foolish while others praise the native’s wisdom. He will have a second marriage also. His death will be through diseases of the belly, liver and spleen, heart defects and the like. A female with the Sun in the 5th will honour her parents, will have few children, will have promlnent face and teeth, be eloquent in speech and will follow religious discipline.

While a moveable sign is on the ascendant: If the Sun occupies the 5th and be in a movable Navamsa, he will lose his father at the age of 12 and in a fixed Navamsa, the death of father will come at 36 while the Sun in a dual Navamsa. will cause early exit of father apart from danger to his own son when his child is 4. Without reference to the ascendant being movable etc., if the Sun is in the 5th identical with a malefic's Navamsa as his sign dispositor is weak, predket danger to father at the age of 17.

## प्रबलमदनोदराग्निर्वलवान् पृष्ठं सुमाश्रिते भानौ। श्रोमान् विस्यातगुणो नृपतिर्वा दण्डनेता वा ।७।।

7. SUN IN 6th: If the Sun is in the 6th at birth, the native will be very libidinous, will have powerful digestive fire (i.e. capable of digesting fast), be strong, affluent, famous for virtues and be elther a king or an army chief.

Notes: If the Sun is in the 6th house, one will have loss of cousins. While Chamatkara Chintamani denotes that the native will destroy his enemies. Sage Bhrigu says there will be increase of enemies. (Both are possible.) Financial success, discase of the eyes in the 20th year, reputation, happiness, affectionate disposition etc., will follow. He will spend his money to save others from legal punishments. He will spend for friends in gencral. There will be evils for the mother’s family (ie. maternal uncle and other such relatives). He will be subjected to danger from quadrupeds. He will lose on account of thefts. He will bc of yogic disposition, be intelligent, will keep his brothers and rclatives happy, be emaclated, very beautiful in appearance, will inherit through maternal relatives, be close to the king, will gather lands and other propertics, will kcep up his promise, will gain through his own sons, be happy at the prosperity of his progeny and will help even his enemies and thercby dcrive satisfaction. He will suffer breakage of tecth by wood or by stone. He will incur pains in legs and his end will come through poison, weapon, thirst, hunger, encmics and vices. A female with the Sun in the 6th will be cminent, be devoid of enemies, beautiful, virtuous and fortunate.

## निःश्रोकः परिभूतः कुशरीरो व्याधितः पुमान् दूने।

नृपबन्धनसन्तप्तोऽमार्गर्तो युबवतिविह्वेषी

8. SUN IN 7th: If the Sun is posited in the 7th, the native will be poor, insulted, will suffer bodily discases, royal displeasures and imprisonment, will take to bad ways and will not be well-disposed to his wife.

Notes: Almost every authority declares that the Sun in the 7th is not congenial for a smooth married life. One will incur grief on account of his spouse, will incur physical distress and mental worries. Due to fear of others, he cannot even sleep peacefully. He will gain in a limited way in his undertakings. He will consider females to be everything in this world and will remain sexually tormented. He will have fear from governmental circles and from enemies. Though he will merge himself in the thoughts about fair sex, no woman will bother for him. He will have limited progenic happiness. He will indulge in sinful acts, be sick, will have a damaged body due to excessive sexual enjoyment with many females and will eat anything available. He will not derive much physical pleasures from his own wife. He will enter into quarrels with his wife within a single moment purposclessly. If the Sun is in the 7th house with Saturn, his wife remains distressed due to unfruitful sexual thoughts. If the Sun is in the 7th identical with a malcfic's sign, dual marriage will occur. If it be Libra, he will undergo many kinds of miseries. A female with the Sun in the 7th will be bcreft of respect for her husband, be devoid of every kind of happincss, be always irascible, phlegmatic, sinful and ugly.

## विकलनयनोऽच्टमस्थे धनसुक्हीनोऽत्यजोक्षितः पुश्वः । भवति सहरलमयूले स्वभिमतजनविरहसन्तप्तः ।।।

9. SUN IN 8th: If the Sun occupies the 8th bhava, the nalive will have deformed eycs, be devoid of wealth and happiness, be short lived and will suffer separation from his relatives.

Notes: With the Sun in the 8th house, one will always undergo miseries, will establish relationship with women of forelgn origin, will lose his wealth in thefts, will face severe and accretive dangers, will have a beautiful physique akin to that of (waxing) moon, will incur financial dangers from all sldes and will befriend neighbouring females. He will suffer from piles, weak aight and physical distress. He will

contact incurable venereal diseases. He will always fight with others. He will have a short life, few friends and will have some wealth. He will not be steady in his career. He will have limited progeny but be a skilful astrologer. A female with the Sun in the 8th will have poor and miserable relatives, will indulge in irreligious activities, be mlserable and will suffer from severe tumours.

## धनपुत्रमित्रभागी द्विजववतनुजनेऽतिरक्तश्च । वित्योधिद्विट्रेधी नवमे तपने सुतप्तः स्यात् ।१०।१

10. SUN IN 9th: If the Sun occupies the 9th bhava, the native will be endowed with wealth, children and friends, will be very interested in worshipping gods and Brahmins, will not be well-disposed towards his father and wife and be not calm.

Notes: One with the Sun in the 9th will be wicked, showy, will not be interested in doing any auspicious acts, be not well rclated to his co-born, be not learned, be devoid of good conduct, and be liable to lose his father early. He will be endowed with wealth and progeny. His children will be quite virtuous. He will be inimical to maternal relattves. He will change his religion and enter a new religion. He will be long-lived. He will face many obstacles in his undertaking and will enjoy others' wealth. He will seek carnal pleasures during day tme. One of the children will become famous through musical accomplishments. The native will be extremely intelligent if the Sun occupies a benefic's Navamsa. If the Sun is conjunct Rahu in the 9th one will be troubled by base deities, will have enmity with parents, wife etc., will patronise the children of others and that of his paternal uncle, will live with whores, will have many daughters, and will have a beautiful face and good learning. If the Sun being in the 9th and falls in the 8th Navamsa there will be penury, grief and enmity with his own people. If the Sun is in the 9th with the lord of the 9th, with strength, there will be unhappiness and fortunes till the end of life. The Sun alone in the 9th will cause evils to father at the age of

1. 18. 24. 26 or 30. For a Leo native if the Sun is in the 9th in Bharani 3rd quarter (i.e. in Libra Navamsa), the mother will be long-lived but the native will not have a happy loaf of bread and will not derive good effects in general. As the Sun is in the 9th in general while the 9th lord is in exaltation or in good divisions one will enjoy royal fortunes and be akin to a king. In a female's nativity, the Sun in the 9th will deprive her of fortunes, wealth and health. She will suffer from severe diseases.

## अतिमतिरतिविभवबलो धनवाहनबन्धुपुत्रवान् सूर्ये ।

## सिद्धारम्भः

## सूरो दशमेऽधृष्यः प्रशस्यश्च ।।११।

11. SUNIN 10th: Should the Sun be in the 10th bhava, the native will be extremely intelligent, rich, strong and will be endowed with conveyances, relatives and sons, will succeed in his undertakings, be valorous, unconquerable and great.

Notes: The Sun in the 10th will give a position equal to a king. The native will be separated from his near and dear. His mother will contact many kinds of illnesses. He will be wealthy, happy in regard to his father, be very learned, famous, strong, pure, liberal and happy. He will be interested in music and dances. He will suffer illness in the later part of his life. He will, however, have mental worries throughout. A female with the Sun in the 10th will always indulge in mean acts, be lazy and wicked.

## संचयनिरतो बलवान् इरेष्यः प्रेष्यो विमृत्यश्च । एकादंशे विधेयः प्रियरहितः सिद्धकर्मा च ।१२।।

12. SUN IN 1 Ith: Should the Sun be in the I lth bhava, one will be interested in gathering moncy, be strong, will hate others, be devoid of servants, be himself a servant, be devoid of affection, be modest and will be successful in undertakings.

Notes: If the Sun occupies the 11th house, one will acquire a kingly position with power, will destroy his ene-

mies with his own valour, will incur grief on account of progeny, will gain in many ways, be satwic in disposition, virtuous, wise, will have knowledge of music, be very wealthy and be so pleasing in appearance that cute dames will lose their hearts for him and be after him. He will acquire a beautiful wife. He will live long, be devoid of grief, will acquire sudden wealth and be very opulent. If the Sun be in the I I th in a female's radix, she will acquire sons and grandsons, will conquer her five senses, be skilful in many arts, be patient in disposition and be honoured by her relatives.

## विकलशरीरः काणः पतितो बन्ध्यापतिः पितुरमित्रः। ह्वादशसंस्थे सूर्ये बलरहितो जायते क्षुद्रः ।।१३।।

13. SUN IN 12th: If the Sun is in the 12th bhava at birth, he will have a deformed physique, be one-eyed, fallen (in moral sense), will marry a barren lady, be inimical to his father, weak and mean.

Notes: If the Sun is in the 12th house at birth one will be devoid of wealth, will have difficulties from paternal relatives, be successful in winning his enemies, will indulge in irreligious activities, will have defective limbs, be endowed with sons, will spend on vices, be libidinous, be a womanizer, be inimical to his own father, will kill birds and cows (or quadrupeds), will be poor, will suffer diseases of eyes, liver etc., will wander in foreign places, and will attain the world of Manes on dcath. A female with the Sun in the 12th will spend on evil fronts, be immodest, spendthrift, be a drunkard, be malicious and impure.

वाक्षिष्यरूपधनभोगगुर्णः प्रधान-

श्चन्दे कुलोरवृयमाजगते बिलग्ने।

उन्मत्त नीचबधिरो विकलश्च मूकः

क्षीणे नरो भवति शेवगृहे विशेषात् ॥१४।।

THE MOON IN VARIOUS BHAVAS (upto sloka 25)

14, MOON IN ASCENDANT: The Moon in the ascendant identical with Cancer, Taurus or Aries indicates that the native will be liberal, beautiful, rich and enjoying pleasures greatly. Should the ascendant be other than these, the native will be intensely passionate, base, deaf, distressed, dumb and will decline (in prosperity).

Notes: The placement of Moon in the ascendant is praised by Narayana Bhatta and Jeevanatha only in case of Taurus, Aries and Cancer, as done by Kalyana Varma.

As per Jayadeva, the Moon in Cancer ascendant will make one happy and in Taurus or in Aries quite wealthy.

In general context it is stated that the Moon in the ascendant will make one cunning, short-statured, valorous, happy, like curd and ghee, interested in astrology, famous in the later part of life, subject to perils four times in life and disbelieve his wife.

In a female's nativity, if increasing Moon is in the ascendant she will be beautiful. Waning Moon will make her thin, poor, sick, argumentative and dirty.

## अतुलितसुखमित्रयुतो धनेश्च चन्दरे दितीयराशिगते । सम्पूरणेऽतिधनेशो भवति नरोऽल्पप्रलापकरः ।१५।

15, MOON IN THE 2nd: Should the Moon be in the 2nd bhava, the native will enjoy incomparable happiness and friends and be wealthy. If the said Moon be full, the native will be very affluent and will speak less.

Notes: If the Moon is in the 2nd house, one will be plentiful in wealth and grains, be physically sound, will spend his time joyously with females, will have less interest in his family, be famous and will be so charming as to attract even angels. Goddess Lakshmi will happily dwell in his house. He will be wise, will have defective speech and will be honoured by king. According to Jataka Ratna such a native will lose money on account of his sisters and daughters.

A female with the Moon in the 2nd house will be wealthy, be modest, amicable to her husband and justly disposed.

It is advisable to evaluate the results due to the Moon in the above context, or in any context, with reference to her Paksha Bala etc. Otherwise, one will be misled.

## च्यातृजनाश्रवणीतो मुदान्वितः सहजगे बलिनि । चन्द्रे भवति च शूरो विद्यावस्त्रान्नसङ् प्रहणशीलः ।।१६।।

16. MOON IN THE 3rd: Should the Moon be in the 3rd bhava, the native will protect his co-born, be always delighted, valorous and will be endowed with learning, robes and food.

Notes: Should the Moon be in the 3rd house, one will enjoy happiness with beautiful women, will acquire wealth with his own valour. be very virtuous, pious, learned sensuous, phlegmatic and important in his race. He will cause pains to living beings, will prattle and will be interested in literature etc. Vaidyanatha, however, declares that he will not be wcalthy while Jataka Ratna states that the native will have two brothers and three sisters all of whom will be sound in health. Bhrigu Rishi states that one with the Moon in 3rd will be windy, will not acquire required food, and be unfortunate. He adds that the native will incur royal wrath at the age of 24 and later on will lose his wealth. As per a Tamil authority he will acquire many houses and conveyances, be very intelligent, very affectionate to his children, very affluent and will acquire a charming female. A female with the Moon in the 3rd house will be phlegmatic, windy, harsh and miserly.

बन्धुपरिच्छदवाहनसहितो दाता चतुर्थचे चन्द्रे ।

जलसंचारानुरतः

## सुखासुखोत्कर्षपरिमुक्तः ।१७१।

17. MOON IN THE 4th: If the Moon be in the 4th bhava, the native will be endowed with relatives, paraphernalla,

and conveyances, be charitable. fond of travelling by water and will neither be very happy nor be miscrable (i.c. will have balanced combination of these).

Notes: The words “Sukhaasukhotkarsha parimuktah" find different expressions in different versions. Our version means that onc will be free from the excess ('utkarsha) of happiness and unhappiness. So to say, he will have a balanced position in regard to this aspect and will be generally happy. He may yet be miserable but not verily.

When the Moon is in the 4th house, one will gain position through relatives, be a kings treasurer (i.e. will occupy good positions in banks, financial institutions etc.), will not be very happy in boyhood but will be later on happy with his family members, be learned, addicted to women, endowed with various kinds of wealth, will pleasc the fair sex, will eat non-vegetarian items and will live in beautiful abodes. (One may be always sick if the Moon is not strong being in the 4th house.)

His business will be in fish products, pearls, conch etc., and through water. This is more certain if the 4th house happens to be Cancer, Scorpio, first half of Capricorn or Pisces. He may become a temple priest to earn his livelihood. Bhrigu Sutras declares that he will be enthroned. So to say, in modern days he will occupy minlsterial and other similar positions. Weak Moon in the 4th will cause diseases to the mother of the native.

In a female's nativity if the Moon is in the 4th house one will be very happy, will beautify herself richly, be firm in disposition, will take to virtuous means, will abundantly enjoy pleasures, be well-disposed towards elders and be plous.

## चन्द्रे भवति न शूरो विद्यावस्त्रान्नसङ प्रहणशीलः। बहुतनयसौम्यमित्रो मेधावी पञ्चमे तीक्णः ॥१८।।

18. MOON IN THE 5th: Should the Moon be in the 5th bhava, the native will be timid in disposition, will earn

learning, clothes and food, will have many sons and friends, be a scholar and be passionatc.

Notes: In regard to the Moon’s position in the 5th housc, Varaha Mihira, Kalyana Varma, Mantreswara, Jayadeva, Kasinatha elc., have used thc word ‘Suta' denoting sons. It is said by thesc slokas that onc will have many sons. In actual practice and responding to common logic, the Moon in the 5th gives morc daughters than sons. Throughout the parlance of astrology, the expression 'son’ does not expressly exclude, however, oblainment of female progeny. This expression was being used those days as more importance was given to onc’s having sons to save him from bcing thrown into the hell called g Garga specially says that one with the Moon in the 5th will have only daughters and be devoid of a malc issue, see “Pan- " chame Rajaninathah Kanyayamaputrakam.…”

One with the Moon in the 5th will unfailingly obtain excellent progeny, wlll reccive a deity’s blessings by upasana, will gain through his wife, will lend moncy on usary and prosper, be highly intclligent, fortunate, sick, horriblefaced, will obtain a dcvoled wife, be wealthy and will acquire two wives. He will gain through quadrupeds, milk ete., will feed many, and will involve in rescarch work. The native's wife will have a natal mark betwecn her breasts. Only one ehild is retainable with the Moon in the 5th. (This should be applied only when the Moon is devold of strength or with malefic aspects. Otherwise there will be many children). A lady with the Moon in the 5th will obtain many children, be devoted to her husband, be very virtuous, beautiful and agreeable to all.

## घच्डे नर उदरभर्व रोर्गः सम्पीडितो भवति । रजनिकरे स्वल्पायुः वच्ठगते भक्षति संलीणे ॥१६।।

19. MOON IN THE 6th: If the Moon be in the 6th bhava, the native will suffer stomachial diseases. If it be the weak Moon, he will be short lived.

Notes: Whatever be the valour of enemies one with the

Moon in the 6th will always conquer them. They will not raise thcir heads again before the nativc. The subject will not have happy relations with his mother. Ncither she will be happy. He will incur discascs of stomach. eyes, mind ctc. He will be emaciated, miserly and unfruitful. Varaha Mihira says that the nativc will not have much sexual appetite. Vaidyanatha is specific in saying that weak Moon reduccs longevity and Full Moon increases longevity—if in the 6th.

The Moon in the 6th is not congenial for paternal happiness. His maternal uncle will havc loss of children or will have only femalc issucs. His maternal aunt will become a widow. A female with the Moon in the 6th will have mcan conduct, be trcachcrous, fickle minded, weak-bodied and will join cvil fricnds.

## सौम्यो घृष्यः सुजितः सुशरीरः कामसंयुतो दुने । बन्यर्गावितदेहः कृष्णे संजायते शशिनि ।।२०।।

20. MOON IN THE 7th: If the Moon is in the 7th bhava, the nativc will be amiable, happy, will possess a good physiquc and be sensuously disposed. If the weak Moon be so posited, the native will be pitiable and sick.

Notes: if the Moon is in the 7th one will be happy with his wife, will carn wcalth in business in foreign places, will enjoy food with women, will sexually enjoy many supreme womcn, will be very sensuous and be liberal. Meena Raja says that difficultics akin to death will come to pass in the 15th ycar. According to Sukra Jataka one will have many wives. There is difference of opinion among the various authors inasmuch as some say there will be wealth with this placcment while yet others say this position will cause poverty. It obviously depends on the state and lordship of the Moon.

Jataka Sagara states that one with the Moon in the 7th will acquire an excellent wife who will, however, be sickly, will gather abundant wealth, be famous, interested in music and will not be infamous (or be not humiliated) till the end of life.

A female with the Moon in the 7th will be affluent, devoted to her husband, splendorous and eloquent.

## अतिम तिरतितेजस्वी थ्याधिविबन्धक्षपितदेहः निधनस्थे रजनिकरे स्वल्पायुर्भवति संक्षीणे ।।२१।

21. MOON IN THE 8th: If the Moon is in the 8th bhava. the native will be very intelligent, very splendorous. and will suffer from diseases. If the Moon be weak, he will be short lived.

Notes: Narayana Bhatta and Jeevanatha say that one will suffer from severer than the severest of diseases and will be attended to by many doctors if the Moon is in the 8th house. This follows that one will be ever sick and will always need medical attention and medicines. He will be always poor, quite intelligent, learned and will suffer from diseases of the eyes and epilepsy. He will not have firm livelihood and will be wandcring hither and thither. He will not enjoy much conjugal happiness. For a day birth Sukla paksha (increasing Moon) and for night birth Krishna Paksha (declining Moon) in the 6th/8th respectively will protect the native akin to his mother. In a female's radix if the Moon be in the 8th, she will be blameworthy, will have sick eyes/ breasts/vagina, be ugly and will contact diseases of various kinds.

बैवतपितृकार्यपरः सुखधनमतिपुत्रसंपन्नः 1 युवतिजननयनकान्तो नवमे शशिनि प्रजायते मनुजः ।।२२।

22. MOON IN THE 9th: If the Moon is in the 9th bhava, the native will be devoted to divine and paternal assignments, will be endowed with happiness, wealth, intelligence and sons and will attract the fair sex.

Notes: If the Moon is in the 9th house one will be honoured by all including his enemies. His valour and health will wax and wane like the Moon’s rays. He will have a beautiful face akin to the full Moon. He will be wealthy,

virtuous. endowed with wife and children and will gain abundantly in the 24th year of his age. Full Moon will confer fortunes to the native and a long lease of life to his father. According to Garga. one with the Moon being strong in the 9th will be fortunate in the middle of his life and will be devoted to his father and weak Moon will totally destroy the native. The Moon in the 9th will give a jocular disposition and skill in three languages. A female with the Moon in the 9th will be endowed with abundant gold and wealth, be supreme in the family, virtuous and truthful.

## अविषादी कर्मपरः सिद्धारम्भश्च धनसमृद्धश्च। शुचिरतिबलोऽ्य दशमे शूरो दाता भवेच्छशिनि ।।२३।।

23. MOON IN THE 10th: If the Moon occupies the 10th bhava, the native will not suffer grief, be dutiful, successful in his undertakings, affluent, purc, very strong, valorous and charitable.

Notes: If the Moon be in the 10th at the time of one’s birth, he will be fortunate, wealthy, happy with relativcs, will gain from the king, will enjoy physical pleasures with many women, be a successful householder, will have increasing and decreasing wealth, will not enjoy good terms with his first child, will gain through his in-laws, be very highly learned and will head a religious institution. If there are other afflictions, the native will lose his mother in the 10th month vide Jataka Sagara. According to Jaageswara one will gain through conducting brothel houses. A female with the Moon in the 10th will be fortunate and happy.

## धनवान् बहुसुतमागो बह्वायु स्विष्टमृत्यवर्गर्च।

## इन्दौ भवेत्मनस्वो तीव्णः शूरः प्रकाशश्च ।२४।।

24. MOON IN THE 11th: Should the Moon occupy the 1 1 th bhava, the native will be wealthy, will have many sons. be long-lived, will have attendants to serve, be intelligent, sharp, valorous and splendorous.

Notes: Should the Moon be in the I1th one will be happy, wealthy, fortunate, be honoured by the king, will face failures in his undertakings, will obtain a son in his 50th year, be famous, endowed with conveyances and palacial buildings, will have more daughters, be intelligent, very learned, long lived and will keep some hidden wealth. In a female’s nativity, if the Moon is in the 11th house, she will be devoid of sickness and be happy.

## ट्वेव्यः पतितः क्षुद्रो नयनरुगार्तोऽलसो भवेद्विकलः ॥ चन्द्रे तथान्यभातो द्वादशगे नित्यपरिभूतः ।२५।।

25. MOON IN THE 12th: If the Moon occupies the 12th bhava, the native will be odious, be fallen in moral sense, be mean, will suffer eye diseases, be indolcnt, distressed (or deformed), is born of other’s loins and will be insulted at all times.

Notes: If the Moon be in the 12th house at the time of birth one will have many enemies, will suffer from ailments of cyes, indigestion etc., will have mental worrics, expenses on religious and auspicious activitics (cvil cxpenses are also indicated by the author of Kheta Kautuka), will incur unhappiness through the relatives of his parents, will fecl demoralized during coition, be very sinful, addicted to intoxicants, be mean among his family members, will cause troubles to living beings, will not have good terms with his friends, be indolent, humillated, will live in foreign places (out of miseries), be very irascible, cmaciated, weak, addicted to mean professions, will suffer scparation from his wife frequently, will lose his virility, be one cyed, will suffer financial losses, will eat bad food, will many timcs be deprived of food (out of povcrty), and be foollsh. He will have risk from water in his 45th year. There Is, however, an authority to state that the Moon in the 12th will cause prosperity in the later part of life. Frequcnt misunderstandings with spouse will be experienced. Jyolisha Kalpataru states that one with the Moon in the 12th will not have much appetite while Kasinatha of Lagna Chandrika indi-

cates a voracious eater. The former view is more acceptable. A female in whose radix the Moon is relegated to the 12th will be a spendthrift and will not cnjoy much wealth.

In thc above, effects like 'one-eyed’, illegal birth’ etc.. should be carcfully applied after taking other relative factors into consideration.

## ऋूरः साहसनिरतः स्तब्धोऽल्पायुः स्वमानशौर्ययुतः । क्षतगात्र: सुशरीरो वक्रे लम्मात्रिते चपलः ।२६।

## MARS IN VARIOUS BHAVAS (upto sloka 37)

26. MARS IN ASCENDANT: If Mars occupies the ascendant, the native will be cruel, adventurous, dullwitted. shortlived, honourable, couragcous, will have an injured physiquc, be attractive in appcarance and fickleminded.

Notes: If Mars be in the ascendant, one will have fear or danger from wood, firc, wcapons ctc., will losc his wife and other relatives, will face failures in his undertakings though he is synonymous of a lion in valour, be cver distressed, be a liar, will join other femalcs, will have wounds and scars on his physiquc, be a talcbearer, will have a thin body, fickle minded, will join mcan men, be always unhappy, sinful, belligerent, will suffer from discascs of blood, discascs of anus, itch in navel, pains in waist, windy disorders, vencrcal diseases, ulcers in the private parts and the likc. He will not firmly be in one place, be inimical to all, will always suffer separation from his family, will ineur the wrath of the government, will be addicted to cating meat etc., will find faults with his own wife, be ever fond of sex, be proud assuming that none would cqual him, and will not honour his father's words. There is an authority, however, to state that with Mars in the ascendant one will be blessed by the benign looks of botli Saraswati (the Goddcss of Lcarning) and Lakshmi (the Goddcss of Lucrc) tagainst the common belief that lucre and lcarning do not go together). He will further be skilful in music.

If Mars occupies the ascendant of a female. she will suffer from severe blood diseases, be devoid of cfficacy. be discarded by her husband, be unfortunate and proud. Thus. Mars is not a welcome planet in the ascendant of either a female or a male for a smooth married life.
