## अधन: कदशनतुष्ट पुरुषो विङ्गताननो घनस्याने । कुज्जनाब्यश्व रुधिरे भवति नरो विद्यया रहितिः ॥२७।।

27. MARS IN 2nd: Just as the proverbial garland is held namesake by the monkey around its neck (without any use) one with Mars in the 2nd will behave with his family and wealth. By this. Narayana Bhatta indicates that the native will not derive use of wealth and family. He will misuse these. He will not eat good food. He will be without any wealth, be merciless. inimical, be not interested in any of his work, be truthful, will have sons. will suffer eye defects, will join bad men, will have wounds on face, eyes, ears and shoulders, and misunderstandings with his female, will face loss of wealth, will be intelligent, be always worried, will suffer grief on account of progeny, will have obstacles in learning, and will have to face many problems on account of his wife's health. If Mars happens to be in the 2nd house in identical with Scorpio or Capricorn, (ascendant being Libra or Sagittarius), one will suffer diseases of the tecth and eyes, fear from government and thefts. Mars in the 2nd in general will deprive one of carnal pleasures (apparcntly from wife as otherwise he will be addicted to many females) and will cause wife to predecease, if malefics aspect the 2nd. A woman with Mars in the 2nd will be very sensuous, will undergo many miseries, be sickly and devoid of wealth.

## शूरो भवत्यवृष्यो भ्रातृवियुक्तो मुदान्वितः पुरुषः।

## भुप्त्रे सहजस्ये समस्तगुणभाजनं स्यातः ।।२८।।

28. MARS IN 3rd: If Mars be in the 3rd bhava, the native will be courageous, inconquerable, bereft of co-born,

be delighted, will have all virtues and be famous.

Notes: If Mars be in the 3rd house, one will be free from diseases, be ever successful, will receive honours from government (or the king), be very religious, very rich, mild. valorous, crafty, famous, and will be devoid of fraternal happiness. Varaha Mihira denotes a wise person with Mars in the 3rd. One will be happy, sensuous and be strong. Posstble diseases with Mars in the 3rd are brcaking of bones, fear from poison, burns, ear afflictions, insatiable thrist etc. Garga specifically says that the native will acquire two charming sisters only to lose them and two other brothers will die due to hit by weapons. It follows that ordinarily the co-born are susceptible to perils. Saunaka Hora declares that if malefics be in the third along with Mars or aspected powerfully by Mars, the native will first acquire a daughter followed by sons. If this occurs in a Rasi of Saturn, i.e., Sagittarius or Scorpio ascending with malcfics in the third and Mars so related as abovc, there will be abortions to the native’s wife. It follows that Mars related to third house has a say on progeny as well. Bhrigu Maharshi hints at the possibility of the native’s wife being corrupt in character. He adds that if a node joins, the native himself will go after prostitutes. A female with Mars in the 3rd house will always be fortunate, virtuous, liked by her relatives, interested in virtuous people, devoid of sickness and be very efficacious.

## ब्रन्युपरिच्छदरहितो भवति चतुयऽय वाहनविहीनः । अतिहुःखै: संतप्तः परगृह्वासी कुने पुरुषः ।।२६।।

29. MARS IN THE 4th: If Mars is posited in the 4th bhava, the native will be devoid of relatives, paraphernalla and conveyarces, be very miserable, will live in others’ houses and be distressed.

Notes: Narayana Bhatta is emphatic on even other planets becoming unfavourable if Mars is in the 4th. That is, if Mercury is in the 4th, he destroys the benefic tendencies of even other heavenly bodies. The splendour of the

other planets ranging from the Sun will be futile before a single Mars in the 4th house, says Jeevanatha. It follows that Mars in the 4th will cause all kinds of miserics nullifying the beneficence of the other planets. The native will derive no happiness from his friends, but will gain lands etc., through the king. The latter is the only gain that the native will have, say Bhatta and Jeevanatha.

He will wander in foreign places with dire unhappiness and miseries. He will be surrounded by enemies on all sidcs. He will be devoid of children but be very libidinous. He will be at the disposal of his wifc. He will own only dilapidated houscs which will also be liable to burning. His mother will be apt to illhcalth. He will lose in quarrels (or litigations). Heart defects, wounds, imprisonment, troubles from spirits, living in dangerous houscs, fall of his residence, fear snakes, ever uncnding disputes in the family, illhealth of wifc and progeny, loss of own moncy and parental property etc., will come to pass.

A femalc with Mars in the 4th will be of thicvish disposition, be devoid of wealth and happiness, be not virtuous and will be discarded by her family.

An explanation is necessary here about other plancts' becoming incapable of giving good results on the face of Mars being in the 4th. This statement nced not be interpreted Iterally. It, however, indicates ‘Yoga Bhanga' (obstruction of good results) with due wcight to the various factors of the horoscope. It follows that the nalive's prosperity, happiness ctc., will not be casily achicved.

## सौद्यार्यपुत्ररहितस्वलमतिरपि पंक्मे बुजे भवति।

## पिशुनोऽनर्यत्रायः वलश्च विकलो मरो नीचः ॥ ३०।।

30, MARS IN THE 5th: If Mars occupies the 5th bhava, the native will be devold of happiness, wealth and sons, be fickieminded, be a talebearer, will incur evils, be wicked, distressed and mean.

Notes: If Mars occupies the house of progeny, I.e., the

5th bhava, one will enjoy strong digestive faculty and his state will be such that he will always want to eat and eat: he will commit sinful acts one after the other, with his thirst for sins never subduing. A single Mars in the 5th will inflict harm on the children born to the native or being born. So to say, children who have already taken birth of the loins of the native will risk death while frequent abortions will be experienced by the native’s wife. He will not have wealth worth mentioning. He will be devoid of happiness from his own wife. He will face many obstacles in shaping his fortunes. He will always be sickly, will suffer from disorders of the stomach (i.e. diaphragm, ltver, spleen, intestines etc.). apart from phlegmatic and windy ones, will have marks caused by the weapons on the right leg and will incur wrath of the government. He will feed many poor people. However, in boyhood during his 6th ycar, he will be liable to dangers. He will join many women to subduc his sexual thirst and be misecrable till his death. Authors like Bhrigu etc, denote poverty for thc position of Mars in the 5th while Parasara and Vaidyanatha indicate riches in this context. A fcmale having Mars in the 5th will have a prodigal son, be devoid of shamc, will indulge in sinful acts, will join bad company and be devoid of learning and relatives.

## प्रबलमदनोवराग्निः सुशरीरो व्यायातो दली धर्हे। रुधिरे सम्भवति नरः स्वबन्धुविजयो प्रधानश्व । ३११

31. MARS IN THE 6th: If Mars occupies the 6th bhava, the native will be highly libidinous, will have powerful digestive fire, be beautiful, tall, strong, and great among his relatives.

Notes: Should Mars be in 6th, one will have strong digestive power according to Kalyana Varma. Narayana Bliatta and Jecvanatha attribute such power to Mars in the Ith. In line with the statement of Kalyana Varma and Meena Raja, Jayadeva, Dundhiraja, Vaidyanatha etc. Both the groups of authors are not, however, mutually contfadictory.

One will win even the mightiest of his enemies. will prove ominous to mother and maternal relatives. He will be liable to lose his ancestral property but will in no time build up wealth of his own. He will be happy, rich, will honour others, be excellent among his racemen, famous, will live through quadrupeds, will obtain a daughter at 27, will face loss of children, and will be short in stature. A female with Mars in the 6th will be endowed with husband and be devoid of sickness.

## मृतदारो रोगार्तोऽ्मार्यरतो भवति दुःखितः पापः । श्रोरहितः सन्तप्तः शुष्कतनुर्मवति सप्तमे भौमे ।३२।१

32. MARS IN THE 7th: If Mars is posited in the 7th, the native will lose his wife, will suffer from diseases, will take to bad ways, be miserable, sinful, devoid of wealth, distressed and emaciated.

Notes: If Mars is in the 7th house, one will seek limited carnal pleasures from his wife. He will commit many crimes, sins etc. He will always be in difficulties. He will be insulted by his wifc, will be troubled by enemies, will suffer loss of or separation from his spouse, will have fear from fire in his 27th year of age, will suffer stomachial ailments, incur wounds etc., and will not return to native place due to marriage (with an unagreeable bride) or due to gains in business. Parasara says his wife will have hard and prominent breasts. According to Vasishta his spouse will be corrupt in character. The wife of the native will incur death due to fire, poison wound, vaginal diseases and the like. His spouse will not be youthful. His marriage may be with a widow, and he will join other widows for sexual satisfaction and will always be interested in having sexual intercourses. A female with Mars in the 7th will attain widowhood, be devoid of wealth, will herself be spoilt physically and be devoid of virtues.

## व्याधिप्रायोऽत्पायु कुशरीरो नीचकर्मकर्ता ।

## निधनस्ये क्षितितनये भवति पुमान् शोक्सन्तद्दः । ३३।।

33. MARS IN THE 8th: If Mars occuples the 8th bhava, the native will suffer from diseases, be short-lived, will possess an ugly or deformed body, will do base acts and will suffer grief.

Notes: If Mars is in the 8th, one will possess an ugly body or some defect therein as the word ‘Kuthanu’in our text denotes.

One with Mars in the 8th house will have a body wounded by weapons, be devoid of splendour, friendship and kindness, will always utter undesirable words, be devoid of conjugal happiness, and will suffer diseases of blood, private parts and of eyes, leprosy, early death, urinary disorders etc. On the issuc of wealth the authorities are divided. Mars in the 8th will however have adverse effects on one's wealth. He will have limited issues, be troubled by enemies, will not have good conduct, will indulge in illegal acts and will be blamed by all. There is an authority to denote that one will have three marriages or his spouse will be subjected to adversities during confinement. Bhatta and Jeevanatha hint at Mars being more powerful occupying the 8th in the matter of adversities defeating even a benefic Jupiter or Venus in the 9th house. So to say the bad effects of Mars in the 8th house will be predominant inspite of good Jupiter/Venus in the 9th house. A female in whose nativity Mars is in the 8th will lose her husband, will suffer from diseases, will be discarded by her own men and will be addicted to meat, intoxicants and the like.

अकुशलकर्मा हरेष्यः प्राणिवधपरो भवेन्नवमसंस्थे। धर्मरहितोऽतिपापो नरेन्द्रकृतगौरवो 1

34. MARS IN THE 9th: If Mars occupies the 9th bhava, the nattve will not be skilful in acts, be odious, will kill living beings, be not virtuous, be very sinful and honoured by the king.

Notes: One with Mars in the 9th house will be extremely intelligent, will always have lltigations to trouble him, will prove ominous to his father, will lose his paternal

wealth and will be inimical to his father, king (i.e. rich men) and the virtuous. He will be lean in the earlier part of his life and will grow stout in the later part. He will be irreligious, unfortunate, devoid of honour, will suffer physical distress; he will have wealth given by others than earned by his own efforts. His wife will be devoid of co-born. He will join other women, will wander on futile missions, but will become famous. He will be humiliated by others. He will indulge in acts akin to that of a butcher although he may propagate Buddhism. He will lose two of his brothers. As regards fortunes, there are two opinions among the varlous exponents. That the native will be devoid of fortunes or will have obstructed fortunes is only logical. A female with Mars in the 9th will suffer many blemishes in her married life and in regard to progeny. The affliction caused by Mars in the 9th can be counteracted by only strong benefics. She will be charming in appearance, sick, unfortunate, discarded by her family members and will indulge in evil acts.

## कर्मोद्युक्तो दशमे शूरोऽवृष्यः प्रधानजनसेवी ।

## सृतसौस्ययुतो रुधिरे प्रतापबहुलः पुमान् भवति ।।३५।।

35. MARS IN THE 10th: If Mars occupies the 10th bhava, the native will be proficient in his acts, be valorous, inconquerable, will serve important people, be endowed with sons and happiness and be very courageous.

Notes: Should Mars be in the 10th, one will befriend kings and the like, will be honoured by great men, will not take others' opinion into consideration, will be chief among his racemen, will enjoy perennial wealth, will prove auspicious for co-born, his coborn will prosper very well, will attach importance to physical pleasures in the first part of life whereas he will take to spiritual course in the latter part and will achieve great Realisation, will spend heavily, will feed the poor liberally, and be a metallurgist. There will be many auspicious functions in his family, one after the other. He will achieve easy success in all his undertakings. Just as a lion in the woods assumes regality of his own, the

native with Mars in the 10th, will become a 'self-made king'. He will be always willing to help others, will be endowed with landed properties, ornaments, wealth and the like and will enjoy happiness from his progeny. He will lose his father early. Vasishta adds that the native will beget evil children.

Mars in the 10th will make one fortunate of a high order, while co-born will be endowed with long span of life.

A female with Mars in the 10th will be devoid of virtues, will be of evil disposition and be bereft of wisdom and shame.

## एकादशगे गुणवान् प्रियसुखभागी तथा भवेच्छरः । घनधान्यत्तः सहितः क्षितितनये विगतशोषश्च ।।३६।।

36. MARS IN THE 11th: If Mars occupies the 11th bhava, the native will be virtuous, happy, courageous, endowed with wealth, grains and sons and be devoid of sorrow.

Notes: With Mars in the 11th house, one will incur enmity with some of the relatives, will not fear anybody, will always aim at gains, will conqucr his enemies, will suffer progenic grief and will gain lands, wealth, conveyances ctc. If the native invests others' wealth in his business, there will only be losses. He will be learned, will seek union with many females, will incur troubles from thicves and firc, will suffer disorders of belly and will not have elder brothers. A female with Mars in the 1 lth will gain much, be devoid of desires and be religious.

## नयनविकारी पतितो जायाघ्नः सूचकश्व रौद्रश्च । द्वादम्ये परिमूतो बन्धनभाक् भवति भूपुश्रे ।३७।।

37. MARS IN THE 12th: Should Mars be in the 12th bhava, the nattve will have diseased eyes, will fall in moral sense, will kill his wife, be a talebearer, be flerce and will contract humility and imprisonment.

Notes: One with Mars in the 12th house will lose his mother in his 27th year and will incur great financial losses in his 45th year. He will be wounded by weapons etc., will incur fear from thieves and enemies, be insulted, be alleged for thefts and the like, be himself a thief, fickle-minded, smiling faced, will sleep with other women, will tender legal witnesses (ulft), will injure his family members by harsh words, will kill or cause harm to his wife, will suffer from diseases of the eyes, spleen, blood, ears and throat, be dishonoured by others, will face dangers equal to death, will not believe in God, be imprisoned, sinful, will lose his brothers, will suffer dire poverty for sometime, will have many vices, and will have squint eyes. According to Punja Raja, the native will acquire a wife who will have some damage to her left ear, left eye, left leg or left hand. A female with Mars in the 12th will be malicious, addicted to intoxicants, spendthrift, ever sick, and inimical to all.

## अनुपहतवेह्बुद्धिवेशकलाज्ञानकाव्यगणितज्ञः

## अतिचतुरमधुरवाष्यो दीर्घायुः स्याद्बुधे लग्ने ॥ ३८।।

## MERCURY IN VARIOUS BHAVAS (upto sloka 49.)

38. MERCURY IN ASCENDANT: Should Mercury occupy the ascendant, the native will have a flawless physique, be intelligent, will know proper (or beneficial) place and time, be well versed in poetry and mathematics, be a skilful and sweet speaker and be long-lived.

Notes: Should Mercury be in the ascendant one will be free from afflictions caused by other planets. His physical complexion will be akin to purified gold (whereas Datvagna Vllasa denotes that there will be mix of many hues in his complexion), will be fond of beautification, be learned, calm, will have knowledge of medicine, justice, religion, dance, music, etc. Manasagari and Phala Deepika extol Mercury in the ascendant stating that such a native will have knowledge of all fine arts. He will be beautiful in appearance, skilful, long-lived, and happy. Jaageswara attributes no growth to the descent, while Meenaraja (of

Brihadyavana Jataka) and Bala Bhadra (Hora Ratna) state that the native will have many children. He will derive marital happiness in the middle age of his life. He will be skilful in Mantras and Tantras. He will know many lan. guages, will enjoy many women and will like to enjoy others' possessions. He will live a happy life throughout. A female with Mercury in the ascendant will be very charming, friendly, religious, just, broad-eyed, endowed with abundant food and drinks, be eloquent and truthful.

## बुद्ध्योपाजितविमवो घनभवनगतेऽन्नपानभोगी च । शोभनवाक्यः सुनयः शशितनये मानवो भवति ।३ह।।

39. MERCURY IN 2nd: If Mercury is in the 2nd bhava, the native will earn wealth through his own wisdom, will enjoy food and drinks, be auspicious in speech and will have good course of conduct.

Notes: If Mercury occupy the 2nd house, one will be learned and will shine like Brihaspatt. He will be so liberal that he will be akin to the celestial tree (Kalpa Vriksha, granting all boons) and be equal to the consort of Lakshmi in the matter of wealth. So to say the native is comparable to Brihaspati (Jupiter) in intelligence, Kalpa Vriksha in bestowing and to Maha Vishnu in riches. Narayana Bhatta compares the native with Mercury in the 2nd to Veda Vyasa. Vyasa Maharshi is the author of Maha Bharata, (acting as a scribe to Lord Ganesa) and also the composer of Ashtadasa Puranas like Bhagavata, Skanda, Varaha etc. He is one of the seven deathless beings. Such was his splendour that even Lord Ganesa could find the best scribe in Vyasa in depicting Maha Bharata. When Bhatta extols the position of Mercury in the 2nd and elevates the native to the status of Maharshi Vyasa, and when Jeevanatha compares the native to Brihaspati (the preceptor of Gods) it follows that the person will achieve limitless learning that cannot be narrated in words. All of this, when Mercury has untarnished placement.

If Mercury occupy the 2nd house, one will be exceed-

ingly rich, happy, will perform auspicious acts, will earn and shinc out of his own abilities, will fcar sins, be famous, and will live a happy life. Therc is an authority to state that one will lose wealth due to his sexual appetite with Mercury in the 2nd. Sage Bhrigu and Yavanas denote that the native will become a 'koteeswara’ (multi-millionaire). His 25th year of age will be significant in his literary achievements. According to Garga he will suffer from diseases of the skin frequently, see twagdoshe kuruthe nithyam somaputrah kutumbagah. One will carn for three generations if Mercury is in the 2nd house in Leo (or Jupiter in the 2nd house in Aquarius).

A female with Mercury in the 2nd will be chaste, beautiful, virtuous and wealthy.

## श्रमनिरतः प्रियहोनस्तृतीयमावे बुधे भवति जातः। निपुण: सहजसमेतो मायाबहुलो नरश्चपलः ॥४०।।

40. MERCURY IN THE 3rd: If Mercury occupies the 3rd bhava, the native will always toll hard, be devoid of near and dear, skilful, endowed with co-born, very cunning and fickleminded.

Notes: If Mercury is in the 3rd house, one will have many friends, will lose some of his close relatives, will have a few co-born, be a skilful businessman, be very wicked out of lust for money, be always sickly and be wealthy. Mantreswara denotes short life for this position. According to Vaishnava Tantra, he will have two sons and three daughters. As per Jaageswara, such a native will have five sisters.

See what Garga says: “One with Mercury in the 3rd will have happiness from his co-born. He will have 5 brothers and 4 or 5 sisters. If malefics aspect Mercury in the 3rd house, brothers will be lost and if Saturn/Mars aspect Mercury in the 3rd house, his sisters will be devoid of progeny."

A female with Mercury in the 3rd house will be endowed with sons, wealth and valour, be liked by all and will

top the clan.

## धनजनसहितः सुभगो वाहनयुक्तो बुध्ये हिवुकसंस्थे। सुपरिच्छ्दः सुबन्धुभवति नरः पण्डितो नित्यम् ।।४१।

41. MERCURY IN THE 4th: If Mercury is in the 4th bhava, the native will be endowed with moncy and relatives, be fortunatc, will have conveyances, all paraphernalia and relativcs and be very learned.

Notes: One with Mercury in the 4th house will not derive any benefits out of paternal properties. He will have many friends, will occupy a big position with the king, and will have many servants to attend to him. His words will be inviolable and honourable. He will be rich, virtuous, interested in music, writing etc. He will be a scholar, and be grieved on account of death of a child. He will be devoid of enemics and discase. He will be a good adviser and an advocate, justice etc. (Nyayavadi), be merciful, famous like the Sun, be a skilful speaker and be endowed with jewels, lands etc. According to Garga, if Mercury is in the 4th devoid of malefic company, one will acquire abundant wealth and many friends, otherwise not. As per Badarayana one will have limited number of children with Mercury in the 4th. Yavanas state that if Mercury in the 4th house is related to malefics one will live in others' houses. Meenaraja mentions that there will be destruction of wealth in the 22nd year of age. Sage Bhrigu attributes monetary gains in the 26th year by grabbing others' wealth.

A female with Mercury in the 4th house will be pious, happy, modest, religious etc. and belong to a popular family.

## मन्त्राभिचारकुशलो बहुतनयः पञ्चमे सौम्ये ।

## विद्यासुखप्रभार्वः

## समन्वितो हर्षसंयुक्तः ।४२।।

42. MERCURY IN THE 5th: If Mercury occupies the 5th bhava, the native will be an expert in Mantras (sacred spells) and Abhichara (using magical spells for malevolent

purposes), will have many sons, be endowed with learning, happiness and cfficacy and be delighted.

Notes: Kalyana Varma denotes many sons for the 5th house placement of Mercury. Jataka Sagara mentions that the native will not retain any living children while Narayana Bhatta states that the person will not obtain progeny in the initial period of life (i.e. there will be belated progeny). Jccvanatha hints at the possible loss of first child. As per Garga, the native will obtain progeny but if Mercury in the 5th is combust or in aspect of malefics, death of children will come to pass; otherwise the number of issues will be limited. Vasishta, too, opines that Mercury gives less number of children when in the 5th house. (In specific reference to Aries Lagna Mercury in the 5th house will give many daughters—see Chandrasute sanjate ravigehe darika bahulah syat.) Kalyana Varma generally denies children with Mercury in Leo. Jatak Ratna goes a step further by specifically saying that Mercury in the 5th will cause death of the son immediately following marriage. Bhrigu Rishi is of the view that this placement of Mercury will cause hindrances in achieving progenic happiness.

It follows the above quotations that Mercury in the 5th house will have adverse effects on progeny unless he is well placed or the 5th lord, 9th lord and Jupiter are well-related in the radix.

Should Mercury be in the 5th house, one will be very learned, be a good adviser, will have growth of the descent, be happy, virtuous, skilful in Mantra Sastras (Abhichara is added by Kalyana Varma), be wealthy, will have a worthy history, will have danger relating to the mother in the 5th year (but according to Balabhadra of Hora Ratna death of mother will take place in the 26th year), will lose his. maternal uncle, and will suffer from diseases.

A female with Mercury in the 5th will have a few sons, be quarrelsome, will get limited food and drinks, will wander aimlessly, be devoid of wealth, will indulge in blameworthy deeds and will not be well-disposed to the virtuous.

## वादविवादे कलहे नित्यजितो व्याधितो बुचे घध्ठे। अलसो विनष्टकोपो निध्ठुरवावयोऽतिपरिभूतः ।४३।।

43. MERCURY IN THE 6th: If Mercury occupies the 6th bhava, the native will always be successful in litigations and disputes, will contract diseases, be indolent, not given to anger, be harsh in speech and much insulted.

Notes: One with Mercury in the 6th will be very fierce (or powerful) in arguments, will win over enemies in arguments (i.e. can possibly become a skilful lawyer etc.), learned, famous in astrology, will achieve unachievable things, be always worried (in spite of many advantages), be an eloquent speaker, be liked by servants, will not violate his promise till the end of his life and will suffcr windy diseases. He will contribute for the welfare of his co-born. One of his co-born will suffer from hernia, swollen testicles etc. The native will, however, be inimical to his own men without any valid reason. He will be jealous, libidinous, and quarrelsome. His maternal uncle will have only female issues. A female with Mercury so placed will be endowed with abundant wealth, be devoid of enemies, shortlived, attached to mundane affairs and be distressed.

## प्रज्ञां सुचार्वेवां नातिकुलीनां च कलहशोलां च । भार्यामनेकवित्तां यूने लभते महत्वं च ।४४।।

44. MERCURY IN THE 7th: If Mercury is in the 7th bhava, the native’s wife will be very learned, beautiful in appearance, will not be of good descent, will promote quarrels and be very affluent. The native himself will be very great.

Notes: One with Mercury in the 7th will be splendorous like processed yellow metal. He will obtain a very charming wife possessing eyes akin to that of a beautiful deer. Yet he will be a failure to satisfy her in sexual cohabltation. His seminal discharge will follow a mere ‘touch' of f female. Nobody will stand any comparison to him in riches.

He will be learned in religious matters. He will obtain many children, be sickly, skilful in many arts, will be amicable to his wife. He will inherit huge wcalth through his wife. He will suffer from venereal diseases in the middle part of his life. Hc will be blessed by the Goddess of Learning also. If a female has Mercury in the 7th house she will be excellent in disposition, skilful, learned, will obtain a beautiful husband, be very offciacious and modest.

## विस्पातनामसारश्चरजीवो कुलधरो निवनसंस्थे । शशितनये भवति नरो नृपतिसमो दण्डनायको वाऽपि ।४५।।

45. MERCURY IN THE 8th: If Mercury occupies the 8th bhava, the native will win famous names (ie. titles), be strong, long-lived, will support his family and be cqual to a king or will become a justice.

Notes: If Mercury is in the 8th one will have many defects, be very fricndly, will suffer from phlegmatic and windy disorders, will have his fame sprcad in all thc cight corners, will cnjoy joyously thc company of females, will sexually unite with many women, be imprisoned, bc inimical to many yet be helpful, will hinder others' undertakings, be a liar (yet some say he will be truthful), will gain in many ways, will have seven children, will achievc great placcmcnt in his 25th year, but will have downfall by his scxual attrocities if malefics join Mercury in the 8th. Kalyana Varma, Narayana Bhatta, Mantreswara, Jecvanatha clc., attribute a long life for Mercury's posltion in the 8th. Bhrigu Rishi while declaring long life adds a condition that the 8th lord should be strong. The native's death will be in a shrinc. A female in whose radix Mercury occuples the 8th will be ungrateful, be not self-respected, be irreligious, be always sickly, fearing and will go agalnst many.

## नवमगते भवति पुमानतिधनविद्यायुतः शुभाचारः। वागोश्वरोऽतिनिपुणो धमिष्ठः सोमपुत्रे हि ।।४६।।

46. MERCURY IN THE 9th: If Mercury occupies the 9th bhava, the native will be very affluent, scholarly, of good conduct/habits, be eloquent in speech, skilful of a great order and be virtuous.

b

Notes: If Mercury occupy the 9th house one will have many children and be happy with them, be religious. will be famous, wealthy and will hold his family's flag aloft, be libcral, be interested in Yogabhyasas, Vedas, music etc. He will be a skilful mathematician and be capable of achicving success in any undertaking. If Mercury is malefic in disposition while being in the 9th one will have obstructed fortuncs and will become proud. If he remains a bencfic, one will be religious and fortunate. Difficultics to father or carly death of father will occur if Mereury is in the 9th in a malefic's Rasi, or with a malefic or in aspect to a malcfic. Not only this, the native's fortunes will be limited and he will take to Buddhism. If in the proccss thc 9th lord is cndowed with strength the father of the native will have a long span of life. If a woman has Mercury in the 9th house she will be religious, praiscworthy, famous, skilful, paticnt. iruthful and fortunate.

## प्रवरमतिकर्मवेष्टः सफलारम्भो विशारदो दशमे । धोरः सत्वसमेतो विविधालंकारसंयुतः सौम्ये ।४७।

47. MERCURY IN THE 10th: If Mercury occupies the 10th bhava, the native will possess distinguished intelligence, will perform distinguished acts, will attain fruition in undertakings, be very learned, courageous, strong and will be endowed with various kinds of ornaments.

Notes: If Mercury occupy the 10th house, he will be charming in appcarance, happy, famous, wealthy, fortunate, cndowed with conveyances, will have many kinds of lncome, will serve in goverument, be skilful in justice, will lulerlt paterual properties, will enjoy progenie happiness, will incur severe cye afflictions in the 27th year of his age and be cndowed will gems, residences, servants and the like. Il Mercury be so in a female's chart she will be very

wealthy, modest, endowed with gold and will honour her husband.

## धनवान् विधेयसृत्यः प्राजः सौर्यान्वितो विपुलमोगी । एकादशे बुधे स्याद्वद्धायुः स्यातिमान् पुरुषः ।।४८।।

48. MERCURY IN THE Iith: If Mercury occupies the 11th, the native will be rich, be an amicable worker. be learned, happy and endowed with wide enjoyments, be long-lived and famous.

Notes: Should Mercury be in the 1lth house the native will adjust with pcople of all kinds of temperaments and be frec from misunderstandings with anyone in regard to his activitics like livelihood. He will be charming in appcarance, will gain abundantly, will have sufficicnt money to give away his daughters in marriage (kritah kanyadanam bhavati..), be wealthy, devoid of debts, will gain through encmies and litigations, be fortunate and happy. He will be ever free from diseases. He will be learned in arts, Vedas etc. Hc will enjoy a long span of life. He will acquire only daughters. According to Garga, Mercury in the 1Ith will obstruct onc’s appetite. The native will be endowed with auspiciousness in life. His married life will have linitcd pleasures of the physique. If a female has Mercury in the 1 Ith house she will be chaste, very affluent and be liked by all her relatives.

## सुगृहीतवाष्यमलसं परिभूतं वाग्मनं तथा प्राज्ञम् । व्ययगः करोति सौम्यः पुरुषं दीनं नृशंसं च ।।४EI।

49. MERCURY IN THE 12th: Should Mercury be in the 12th bhava, thc native will be able to keep up his promise, be indolent, be humillated, be a good speaker, be learned, pitiable and cruel.

Notes: If Mercury be in the 12th house, one will face obstructions in his educational pursuits, will be fallen in moral standards, will be devoid of enemies, be interested in

sinful acts, will keep up his promise, will suffer from windy diseases during the last period of life, be away from his own people, and be malicious. He will acquire abundant landed properties. His paternal uncles will be happy. At one stage the native may face financial losses/decline. If Mercury be in the 12th house in a female’s nativity, she will be impatient, miserable, weak, and be discarded by her pcople.

## होरासंस्ये जीवे सुशरीरः प्राणवान् सुदोर्धायुः । सुसमीक्षितकार्यकरः प्राजो धीरस्तयार्यश्च ।५०।।

## JUPITER IN VARIOUS BHAVAS (upto sloka 61)

50. JUPITER IN LAGNA: If Jupiter occupies the Ist bhava, the native will be attractive in appearancc, be energetic, long-lived, will act after assessing consequences, be learned, courageous and great.

Notes: Should Jupiter be in the ascendant, one will be wealthy, free from evils, will honour eldcrs, be quickly irritable, be majestic, be fond of Brahmin ladies, endowed with good conduct, be happy with his father and learned. He will shine with his own virtues, be very charming, be not very strong (alpa veeryam, vide Chamatkara Chintamani), will spend on various auspicious deeds, be bold, excellent, liberal, will enjoy happiness from wife and children, will have longliving children, be a singer, and will keep his thinking unrevealed. Vasishta indicates some fear for Jupiter’s position in the ascendant, see Sukha Kanthidah syuh bhayam. Gargarshi gives special preference to Jupiter in the ascendant (or in trine or in angle) identical with Cancer, Pisces or Sagittarius to have no evils. “The native will suffer windy and phlegmatic diseases and will be subjected to false blame”, adds Garga. It obviously applies to Jupiter in other positions than stated by him. Bhrigu Maharshi mentions that if Jupiter is in the ascendant identical with fall, inimical sign or malefic’s sign, the native will be devoid of issue, will be a Madhyayu (moderate longevity), will indulge in mean acts, be separated from his people, be ungrateful, addicted to prostitutes, will wander

almlessly and will reap the fruits of his sins (of the past). A female with Jupiter in ascendant will be excellent among women, will have a majestic voice, be truthful and will enjoy excellent paraphernalia.

## धनवान् भोजनसारो वाम्मी सुभगः सुवाक् सुवक्षत्रश्च । कल्याणवपुस्त्यागी सुमुखो जीवे भवेद्धनगे ।।५१।।

51. JUPITER IN THE 2nd: If Jupiter occupies the 2nd bhava, the native will be rich, will enjoy good food, be an eloquent speaker, be fortunate, be charitable and will have a beautiful body and face.

Notes: If Jupiter occupies the 2nd house one will be a writcr of great repute, a noted poet and the like. He will be wealthy, will possess a splendorous body, will enjoy richcs through quadrupeds, will obtain a beautiful wife, will deal in pearls and the like, be dcvoid of encmics, will occupy big positions delivering justicc and punishmcnts, be a leader, be inimical to his own pcople and will gain royal honours in the 27th ycar. (So to say, his 27th ycar will be significant to reccive awards, titles etc.)

Narayana Bhatta and Jecvanatha state unlike other authors that the native will undergo many difficulties in his attempts to carn money. However, exponents like Vasishta, Kasinatha, Kalyana Varma, Dundhiraja, Mccnaraja, Mantreswara etc., specifically touch the effect of one's becoming “wealthy" with Jupiter in the 2nd house but make no mention on the lincs of Bhatta and Jeevanatha. The other group of authors do not in any way mention easy wealth but leave it at that. It however seems that Jupiter in the 2nd is detrimental for money, in the 5th for children, in the 4thrfor learning and the like. It does not mean that the native will reap adverse results in these directions wholly. The implication is that the native will have to face tough time in establishing himself in these matters. If however the houses involved are strong, the lords being strong and such other factors being present, the difficulties are minimised. The views of Bhatta and Jeevanatha are worth considering

based on practical experience and based on the following special rule relating to Jupiter and Saturn:

जीव स्वस्थानहंता वदंति मुनिवरोदृष्टिरस्यप्रशस्ता सौरि: स्रस्थानपाल: परभयकरी दृष्टिरस्यप्रणष्टा

The above verse of Tatwa Pradeepa Jataka, means: Jupiter destroys the house occupied by him and improvcs the houses aspected by him. Saturn gives good cffects relating to his occupation but very fearful (Paramabhayakart...) cffects by aspects. It follows that Jupiter’s aspect is more helpful than occupation while Saturn’s case is rcverse. Perhaps, this aphorism finds truth in thc statement that Jupiter in the 2nd cause harm to money, in the 5th causes harm to progeny, and the likc. Thus this rule as above has its own worth if not a universal applicability.

In this contcxt, sage Bhrigu's suggestions are equally thought-provoking. “One with Jupiter in the 2nd identical with Cancer or Sagittarius (note Pisces not included) becomes wealthy. If the planct in qucstion is with malefics, there is obstruction of lcarning. He becomcs a thief, a cheat, a liar and an unworthy spcaker. If the sign is identical with Capricorn or that of a malefic, thc native is a drunkard, is morally fallen, addicted to othcr women and is devoid of children”. Thus, the sage has differentiated the signs idcntical with the 2nd housc.

A female with Jupiter in the 2nd will be free from harm and frec from desires, be wcalthy, fortunate and charming.

## अतिपरिभूतः ्ृपणः सदाजितो मानवो भवति जीवे। मन्दाम्तिः स्त्रीविजितो दुश्चिव्ये पापकर्मा च ।।५२।।

52. JUPITER IN THE 3rd: If Jupiter occupics the 3rd bhava, the native will be grcatly humiliated, be vile, cver successful, will have digesttve dcficiencics, be defcated by women and be sinful in acts.

Notes: Let us scc the various views relating to the 3rd

house placement of Jupiter. The native will be mean, will enjoy fraternal happiness, be ungrateful and unfriendly. He will be wealthy but not that wealthy as may be due to his fortunes. So to say he will not enjoy entitled riches. Jeevanatha puts it saying that though the native may be influential in royal circles he will not cnjoy that happiness. He will be a miser, will not be affectionate to his wife and children, will have famous brothers and will rule over hundreds of men. He will face financial losses. Vide Jataka Sagara, "He will have five co-born or five children. His father-in-law has three wives. He himself will have two marriages and will be a deep-thinker”. See what Garga says: “The native will have four or five brothers. If malefics aspect Jupiter in the 3rd the co-born will be subjected to dangers. Although he may be rich, he will pose to be poor”. A female with Jupiter in the 3rd will be poor.

## स्वजनपरिच्छदवाह्नसुखमतिभोगार्थसंयुतो भर्वति ।

## श्रेष्ठः शत्रुविषादी चतुर्थसंस्थे सदा जीवे ।५३।।

53. JUPITER IN THE 4th: If Jupiter occupies the 4th bhava, the native will be endowed with relatives, paraphernalia, conveyancc, happiness, intelligence, pleasures and wealth, be grcat and be a source of misery to his enemies.

Notes: When Jupiter occupies the 4th house the native will be happy, endowed with conveyances, be liked by people from maternal side, wealthy, honourable, famous, eloquent, beautiful, will have long lasting friends, will inherit anccstral property, will acquire landed/housing properties and will enjoy all kinds of pleasures. Bhrigu Rishi denotes that if Jupiter is in the 4th but with malefics, onc will not have houses, conveyances etc., will live in others' houses, will losc his mother and be inimical to his relatives.

The traditional description by Bhatta and Jeevantha is: He will have many houses at his disposal and will always enjoy the benedictory Vedic chantings by learned Brahmins visiting his house. To wit, the person will be very rich with

all paraphernalla and religious patronage.

Jataka Sagara hints at two ominous effects which are worth noting: (a) The native with Jupiter in the 4th house, will be too arrogant to maintain his mother. (b) He will face loss of progeny or will have limited progeny. Limited progeny was a concern those days when orthodox people laid no restriction on the birth of children. But 'loss of children’ mentioned by Jataka Sagara needs attention in that a karaka in the 12th from the bhava concerned is not capable of promoting the good effects related to that house. For example, Mars in the 2nd is ominous for brothers, the Moon in the 3rd for mother and so on. Venus in the 6th though generally good, is not as a rule good for marriage. Saturn in the 7th, i.e., 12th from the 8th, cannot confer full span of life or even a long life. Thus Jupiter in the 4th is not wholly auspicious for progeny and one is likely to lose something on the front.

A female with Jupiter in the 4th will be happy, very learned, endowed with ornaments, be famous, virtuous and honourable.

## सुखसुतमित्रसमृद्धः प्राजो धृतिमांस्तथा विभवसारः। पंबमभवने जीवे सर्वत्र सुखो भवति जातः ।५४।

54. JUPITER IN THE 5th: If Jupiter occupies the 5th bhava, the native will have abundant happiness, many sons and friends, be learned, courageous, wealthy and will always be happy.

Notes: If Jupiter occupy the 5th house, one will be a writer and be gifted with extraordinary imagination power. He will incur obstacles in getting the fruits of his efforts. He will not be very wealthy, according to some. Most texts subscribe to the view that he will amass wealth while there is one version to say that the native will not be quite rich. He will have knowledge of religious scriptures, Mantra Sastras and the like and be very learned. He will enjoy unexpected fortunes.

The statements of Garga, Vasishta and Bhrigu are that one will have numerous children. Kalyana Varma also hints at the same. Yet there are specific authorities to state that if Jupiter is in the 5th there is grief on account of progeny (putraklesa). To wit, there may be belated progeny, loss of progeny, prodigal, sickly, disobedient or foolish children, depending on the nature. There is yet an authority to emphatically state that Jupiter in the 5th Cancer /Aquarius will give no children, in Pisces a few children and in Sagittarius children with great difficulty. Dundhiraja and Kasinatha declare that one with Jupiter in the 5th will enjoy progenic happiness. In my own view, Jupiter in Scorpio is more specifically harmful to progeny than in other houses. Pisces is equally adverse while Cancer position gives a number of children. The placement in Sagittarius gives not many children but does not deny. In fact the 5th house Jupiter causes affliction of progeny and needs a careful evaluation.

A female with Jupiter in the 5th will obtain good children.

## सन्नोदराम्निपुंस्त्वः परिष्ूतो दुर्बलोऽलसः वध्ठे । स्त्रीविदितो रिपुह्नता जीवे पुरुषोऽतिविश्यातः ।५५।।

53. JUPITER IN THE 6th: If Jupiter is in the 6th bhava, the native will lack digestive fire and masculine virile, be humiliated, weak, indolent, will become famous on account of females, will destroy his enemies and be widely famous.

Notes: The word ‘Pumstwah’ in the text means: masculine vigour, potence, semen, virility etc. Jupiter's position in the 6th will deprive of masculine potence (as well as digestive power). The native will hence be not capable of obtaining children, easily. It cannot be said however that he will suffer adversities on account of children. Hence, 'Swalpodaragni-pumstwah' means that the native will have deficiencies of digestion and masculine potence.

One with Jupiter in the 6th will be troubled by sickness, will destroy his enemies (see: Rajah sambhaveyu ripau vakpate satruhantutwamethi..), will not be happy with maternal relatives and will be skilful in music. His mother will face various ailments. He himself will suffer from many diseases if Jupiter is in the 6th in Capricorn or in Aquarius along with Rahu. As per Vasishta, he will be miserable. He will be tormented by heavy debts but will improve later on. He will be skilful in astrology, addicted to other females and will gain through such addiction, will tactfully grab others' properties, will see his progeny happily placed and will instantly get vexed with female company.

"If Jupiter in the 6th occuples his own house or that of another benefic, there will be destruction of enemies. But if it is an inimical sign, there will be increase of enemies. If Jupiter is strong, one will be endowed with many quadrupeds and will prove auspicious for co-born and maternal relatives. If Jupiter is in the 6th along with the Moon, only malefic results will come to pass.” So says Carga.

A female with Jupiter in the 6th will have many enemies, be diplomatic, be subjected to many dangers, will face many miseries and be very irascible.

## सुभगः सुरुचिरबारः पितुरधिकः सप्तमे भवति जातः । वक्ता कविः प्रधानः प्राजो जीवे सुविद्यातः ।९।।

56. JUPITER IN THE 7th: If Jupiter occupies the 7th bhava, the native will be charming will acquire a beautiful wife, be greater than his father, be an eloquent speaker, a poet, a superior person and be learned and famous.

Notes: If Jupiter occupies the 7th house the native will be intelligent, will reach high position in life at a very quick pace, be wealthy, be sensuous, will enjoy abundant sexual pleasures, will have a wife who will be averse to carnal pleasures, be an artist, will study deeply many sastras and will hate elders and parents. He will obtain excellent children. (According to Jatakalankara, he will be unhappy in

the matter of progeny). He will face loss of wife if Jupiter is in the 7th for a Cancer native. Otherwise Jupiter in the 7th will give the native a happy married life. If he joins or is aspected by Rahu, Ketu, Saturn or Mars, the native will join other's females. A female with Jupiter in the 7th will have more enemies, be dear to her husband, will do virtuous acts and will be happy.

## परिभूतो दीर्घायुर्मृतको दासोऽ्यवा निधनसंस्थे। स्वजनप्रेष्यो बीनो मलिनस्त्रीभोगवान् जीवे ।।५७)।

57. JUPITER IN THE 8th: If Jupiter occupies the 8th bhava, the native will be insulted, longlived, be a servant, will serve his own people, be pitiable and will have union with dirty women.

Notes: Should Jupiter be in the 8th house the nativewill indulge in base acts, will not live with his father for a long time, will suffer from some ailment or the other on a lasting basis, be devoid of intelligence, modesty, reasoning and beauty, be very miserable, be a servant (i.e. will hold only small positions to earn his bread), be bereft of fortunes, happiness, progenic happiness and the like, be not strong and be very poor. As regards the span of life, Kalyana Varma, Jeevanatha, Mantreswara, Vaidyanatha etc., all say that the native will be long lived. But Bhrigu rishi says that the subject will be short-lived unless another benefic is related to the 8th. The first statement is only general while the sage's version is specific. Depending on the horoscope, Jupiter can also act as a premature killer while in some cases he will bestow a long span of life. A female with Jupiter in the 8th will be involved in distress, be given up by her husband, be devoid of virtues, will possess stout hands and stout legs, be sick and be a voracious eater.

## बेवतपित्कार्यरतो विद्वान् सुभयो भवेत्तथा नवमे ।

## नृपमन्त्री नेता वा जीवे जातः प्रधानश्च ॥५८।।

88. JUPITER IN THE 9th: If Jupiter is in the 9th bhava, the native will be attached to divine and paternal duties, be learned, fortunate, be a king’s minister or a leader and be chief.

Notes: If Jupiter occupies the 9th house one will own big houses, will enjoy patronage from great men, will involve in spiritual and religious activities, will enjoy honour from brothers and relatives, will perform sacrificial acts, be learned, famous, fortunate, wealthy etc., and be pious. He will enjoy a healthy physique and progenic happiness. A female with Jupiter in the 9th house will engage herself in religious activities, be beautiful, famous, very rich and grateful.

## सिद्धारम्भो मान्यः सर्वोपायः कुशलसमृद्धश्च। दशमस्थे त्रिदशगुरौ सुखधनजनवाह्नयशोभाक् ।।५६।।

59. JUPITER IN THE 10th: If Jupiter occupies the 10th bhava, the native will attain successful beginning in his undertaking, be honourable, effortful and will be endowed with abundant welfare, happiness, wealth, relatives, conveyances and fame.

be Notes: The native with Jupiter in the 10th house will endowed with 'Hasta Vridhdhi’ (identical with “Sidhdharambha” of our text, meaning that the person will be able to give a golden touch to an undertaking and achieve easy success), will be fond of eating much, will have limited sexual enjoyments, will make religlous contributions of lasting nature, of famous, short-tempered, will be lean in the early years but will grow fat later on, will enjoy the patronage of eminent persons, be endowed with royal insignia (i.e. will occupy very top positions), be very affluent, be diplomatic, be gifted with deep knowledge of Gita and be very learned in many Sastras.

Bhatta and Jeevanatha denote some misery to the native on account of progeny. Jupiter as indicator of progeny relegated to the 10th, i.e. the 6th from the 5th, obviously will be in a disadvantageous position for happy

progeny. Bhatta further adds that the native will everyday feed many people.

A female with Jupiter in the 10th house will perform excellent deeds, be famous, be endowed with many servants and be very modest.

अपरिमितायद्वारो बहुवाहनभृत्यसंयुतः : एकादशमे जीवे न चातिविद्यो न चातिसृतः ।।६०।।

60. JUPITER IN THE 11 th: If Jupiter occupies the 1 1 th bhava, the native will enjoy many gains, many conveyances and many servants, be virtuous but will have limited education and few sons.

Notes: The native with Jupiter in the 11th will have few sons and limited learning. Bhatta, however, says that the native will have five sons. Sage Garga's statement, not different from Kalyana Varma's is: "...Naathividyo alpathanayah sadhurekadaso gurau” meaning that one with Jupiter in the 11th will not have abundant learning and numerous progeny but be virtuous. Mantreswara, too, does not see a number of children in this context.

One will be very intelligent, will be successful over enemies, will keep everyone delighted, will enjoy all kinds of gains and he will have nothing on the earth unavailable to him (alabhyam kim bhoomau). He will enjoy a long span of life, will be endowed with vast conveyances, be devoid of illness, will have knowledge of Mantras, be very wealthy, and will make spiritual achievements. A female with Jupiter in the I 1th will be skilful in arts and be endowed with welfare.

## अलसो लोकदेष्यो ह्वापगतवार्दैवपक्षभग्नो वा । परितः सेवानिरतो द्वादशसंस्थे गुरौ भवति ।६१।।

61. JUPITER IN THE 12th: If Jupiter occuples the 12th bhava, the native will be indolent, odious, be devoid of speech and luck, and will be in all probability in servitude.

Notes: Should Jupiter be in the 12th, one will be reduced to a state of infame, will spend on evil things, will face financial losses, be very arrogant, will always be intent on grabing others' wealth, will have vices to torment him, be inimical to his own relatives, be shameless, will suffer from venereal discases, eye afflictions and tuberculosis and be worried. He will be susceptible to heart disease in boyhood. He will obtain some paternal wealth. Bhrigu Maharshi and Garga Maharshi denote that the native with Jupiter (favourable) in the 12th will amass abundant wealth of his own. His financial loss will be through thieves. He will be belligerent, will have limited progeny, and will have a peaceful death. A female with Jupiter in the 12th will be sickly, will incur losses, will take to others' religion, and will be poisonous in disposition.

## सुनयनवदनशरीरं सुखितं दीर्घायुवं तया भीरुम् । युवतिजननयनकान्तं जनयति होरागतः शुक्रः ।।६२।। VENUS IN VARIOUS BHAVAS (upto sloka 73)

62. VENUS IN LAGNA: If Venus is in the Ascendant, the native will possess beautiful eyes, face and physique, be happy, long-lived, timid and attractive to females.

Note: The native with Venus in the ascendant will have afflicted longevity in the initial part of life. To wit he may face some risk to life and will later on be sure to live a long span of life. In the 12th year of his age, there will occur a mark (like scar etc.) on his head. He will be wealthy, skilful and will always have auspicious occasions in life. He will be skilful in coition and will unite with beautiful and excellent females whose eyes will be akin to that of a charming female deer, so says a traditional author of earlier school of thought. The native will be susceptible to deficient semen, loss of eye sight and venereal diseases. He will be troubled by dogs and horned animals. He will be skilful in mathematics (ganitasastragnah) fine arts, music and many other subjects. A female with Venus in the ascendant will be beautiful, fortunate, virtuous and will

be devoid of enemies.

## प्रचुरान्नपानविभवं श्रेष्ठविलासं तथा सुवाष्यं च। कुर्ते द्वितीयराशी बहुधनसहितं सितः पुरुषम् ।।६३।।

63. VENUS IN THE 2nd: If Venus is in the 2nd bhava, the native will enjoy abundant food, drinks and wealth, will dcrive excellent pleasures, be endowed with good speech and will amass great wealth.

Notes: If Venus occupies the 2nd bhava, the native will be wealthy, free from discases, will acquire other’s wealth and be endowed with beauty, eloquent speech, learning in various branches, conveyance, jewels, family etc. He will gain potentlal lands and a beautiful life partner in his 32nd year of age. If Venus is afflicted in the 2nd, particularly by luminaries, the native will contract severe defects. A female with Venus in the 2nd, will be endowed with all virtues, be artful, charming, fortunate, wealthy and famous.

## सुखधनसहितं शुक्रो दुश्विष्ये सत्रोजितं तथा क्रपणम् । जनयति मन्दोत्साहं सौभाग्यपरिच्छवातीतम् ॥ ६४।।

64. VENUS IN THE 3rd: If Venus occuples the 3rd bhava, the native will be happy, rich, conquered by women, be vile, little enthusiastic and will be bereft of luck and paraphernalla.

Notes: If Venus occupies the 3rd bhava, the native will be fond of women according to various authorities. (Narayana Bhatta, however, indicates aversion to females in this connection). The subject will feel unhappy in regard to children without any valid reason. So to say, his children will actually not be a source of displeasure. He will be timid, sinful, rash and irascible. He will be affectionate to his co-born and relatives. He will suffer from eye afflictions. He will be devoid of happiness from his wife and of wealth (vide Phala Deeptka). Dundhiraja too denotes poverty with Venus in the 3rd while Manasagari attributes riches.

“Vittabhogaparah” is the effect indicated by Bhrigu, meaning that the native will enjoy wealth and pleasures. It may be understood that Venus well related to the horoscope being in the 3rd will cause one to be rich and if afflicted poverty will come to pass. According to Bhrigu, he will have 3 younger brothers and many younger sisters.

Jataka Sagara, a Taiml work, says in this context thus: “The native will be cunning, will cheat others, will enjoy many women, will not hesitate even to have coition with his own mother, be very sinful, will boldly indulge in acts befitting the most fallen, will gain by union with females, will be too miserly to be considerate even to his own co-born and will harm his own race. His mother will be a corrupt woman and he will force her in immoral traffic". These are the words of the text concerned nceding a cautious and sensible application. It can be seen that Jataka Sagara outright condemned Venus in the 3rd house. Even other texts have generally given bad effects.

A female with Venus in the 3rd will perform good acts, be modest and will have co-born. Her sisters will enjoy physical strength.

## बन्धुसुह्तसुखसहितं कान्तं वाहनपरिच्छदसमृद्धम् । ललितमदीनं सुभगं जनयति हिबुके नरं शुक्रः ।।६५।।

65. VENUS IN THE 4th: If Venus occupies the 4th bhava, the native will be endowed with relatives, friends and happiness, be splendorous, will have conveyances and paraphernalia, be beautiful, rich and fortunate.

Notes: Should Venus be in the 4th one will be extremely rich, learned, happy with mother, highly honoured by all, endowed with conveyances, lands, long-life, co-born and royal honour and be very happy in life. There is an authority to indicate that he will, however, lack marital happiness. A female with Venus in the 4th will be very fortunate and rich.

सुखसुतमित्रोपचितं रतिपरमतिधनमलपिडितं शुक्रः । कुरुते पञ्चमराशौ मन्त्रिणमथ दण्डनेतारम् ।६६।।

66. VENUS IN THE 5th: If Venus is posited in the 5th bhava, the native will be endowed with happiness, sons and friends, be fond of sexual union, be very affluent, full of everything, and be a minister or a justice.

¹ Notes: Our author's hint is at the native’s being endowed with all that one requires in life and the subject’s chance of achieving distinction in legal profession, with Venus in the 5th.

One with Venus in the 5th will have many children. He will be very learned and be very intelligent. He will be a successful astrologer, will lose his sight due to sexual excesses, will suffer high loss of semen, be devoid of enemics and will amass abundant wcalth. If a fcmale has Venus in the 5th, she will be wealthy, will beget many daughters, and be very charming.

## अधिकमनिष्टं स्त्रीणां प्रचुरामित्रं निराकृतं विभर्वः। विक्लवमतीव नौचं कुर्ते वष्ठे भूगोस्तनयः ।।६७।।

67. VENUS IN THE 6th: If Venus occupies the 6th bhava, the native will greatly dislike his wife, will have many foes, be devoid of wealth, be very much startled and be mean.

Notes: Should Venus be in the 6th, the native’s enemies will be nectar-dipped, i.e. they will never be destroyed, so say Narayana Bhatta and Jeevantha. But Brihat Jataka and Phaladeepika differ in the matter of enemies stating that the subject will not have enemies. However, in practical experience, Venus in the 6th will cause enmity without valid reasons and the native will have less number of friends. He will have a tendency to spend liberally and his expenses will never subside. He will bring harm to the family by practising evil Mantras. He will be miserable and

be subjected to calumny. He will suffer many sicknesses and will obey parcnts. A conimentator quotes a principle from Harivamsa that one with Venus in the 6th will be averse to sexual acts although he may face charming se nales. However, this view as a rule cannot be subscribed to. A female with Venus in the 6th will be llable to be discarded by her husband and childrcn.

## अतिरूपदारसौर्यं बहुविभवं कलहवजितं पुरुषम् । जनयति सप्तमधामनि सौभाग्यसमन्वितं शुक्रः ।६८।।

68. VENUS IN THE 7th: If Venus occupies the 6th bhava, the native will greatly dislike his wife, will have many foes, be devoid of wealth, be very much startled and, be mean.

Notes: One with Venus in the 7th house will obtain an excellent and chaste wife and will be happy in regard to his marriage. He will be learned in many branches, be rich, diplomatic, will have a defective limb, will suffer pains in the hips, will be attached to whores, will acquire happy progeny, be skilful in sports played in water, will gain in business undertakings and be free from serious diseases. He will be very libidinous and will join even pregnant women. According to Vasishta, the native may be subjected to repeated miseries. A Tamil text states that there may be loss of wife with Venus in the 7th house.

A female with Venus in the 7th house will be dear to her husband and will be very rich.

## दीर्घायुरनुपमसुखः शुक्रे निघनात्रिते घनसमृद्धः । भवति पुमान् नृपतिसमः क्षणे क्षणे लब्धपरितोषः ।६६।।

69. VENUS IN THE 8th: If Venus is in the 8th bhava, the native will be longlived, will enjoy incomparable happiness, be very rich, be equal to a king and moment after moment will feel delighted.

Notes: If Venus be in the 8th, the native will be short-

lived according to sage Bhrigu and also Balabhadra, the author of Hora Ratna. Whereas a host of other authors, like Kalyana Varma, Jeevanatha, Narayana Bhatta, Mantreswara, Jageswara etc., denote long life to the native.

If Venus be in the 8th house, there will be miseries on account of wife and progeny. His financial prosperity will face many ups and downs, but he will make abundant wealth. Sometimes he may be troubled by debts. He will be fond of meat and the like. He will be subjected to diseases and be humiliated on account of women. See what Garga specifically states: “The native will clear paternal debts. His death will be in a shrine due to imbalances of wind and phlegm or due to hunger. If Venus be in the 8th identical with a fixed sign, there will be always miseries galore".

A female wi n Venus in the 8th will be devoid of wealth and will cheat others. Her marital life will be miserable and short.

## बिमलायततनुवित्तोदारयुवतिसुखसुह्रजनोपेतः I

## मृयुतनये नवमस्ये सुरातिथिगुरुप्रसक्तः स्यात् ॥७०।।

70. VENUS IN THE 9th: If Venus occupies the 9th bhava, the native will possess a spotless and broad physique, be endowed with wealth, charitability, wife, sexual pleasures and friendship and will honour Gods, guests and preceptors/clders.

Notes: Should Venus be in the 9th, one will be happy with wife, children, wealth and fortune. He will spend on religlous activities. He will command learning, conveyances and the like. He will receive high honours from the Government, public etc. He will be always happy, but given to anger. He will have auspicious marks on his soles. His father will be longlived. He will enjoy a long span of life. His brother will have two wives or will suffer some kind of marital discord. A female with Venus in the 9th will be endowed with wealth, robes, food etc., be very important among people, will have strong willpower and wtll resemble a male.

## इत्यानविवादाजिताः सुखरतिमानार्थकीर्तयो यस्य । इशमस्थे भूगुतनये भवति पुमान् बहुमतिस्यातः ।।७१।।

4. 71. VENUS IN THE 10th: Should Venus occupy the 10th bhava, the native will earn success in litigations and will be endowed with happiness, sexual unions, honour, wealth, fame and great wisdom.

Notes: If Venus is in the 10th house at the time of one’s birth, he will be very affectionate to his wife and children, be skilful in poetry, will have many conveyances, will do many meritorious activities for the welfare of the public, will be deaf, and will have many sons. But Narayana Bhatta and Jeevanatha specifically state that Venus in the 10th will weaken the semcn of the native so as to stall obtainment of progeny for the growth of the descent. According to Manasagari the native will have royal status even though he may be in forests, see “vanagatopi cha rajyaphalam”. A female in whose nativity the 10th is occupied by Venus will be very liberal, fortunate and rich.

## प्रतिरूपवासमृत्यं बल्लायं सर्वशोकसन्त्यक्तम् । जनयति भवमवनगतो भूगुतनयः सर्वदा पुरुषम् ॥७२।।

72. VENUS IN THE 11th: If Venus is positioned in the 11th bhava, the native will have obedient scrvants, be bereft of all kinds of misery, and will gain abundantly.

Notes: The word sfay in the text means: suitable or proper. However, to make sensc it has been translated here as ‘obedient'.

If Venus occupy the I1th house, the native will be devoid of enemies, be empirical in status, endowed with all kinds of wealth, learning, fortunes, virtues etc., attached to prostitutes, will be fond of music, dance, drama, poetry, etc., and will be happy in family life. Vide Jataka Sagara, “One will have three wives with Venus in the I Ith, will be short-lived and endowed with happiness”. According to

Bhrigu, If Venus in the I1th is conjunct malefics, one will gain in sinful ways while benefics conjunct will confer gains through good acts. Garga denotes that the native will acquire a jewel-like wife and be devoid of diseases. Should Venus be in the I I th in a female’s chart, she will be devoid of afflictions (or grief), be very learned and will have many dependents.

## अलसं सुखिनं स्थूलं पतितं मृष्टाशिनं भूगोस्तनयः। शयनोपचारकुशलं द्वादशगः स्त्रीजितं जनयेत् ।७३।।

73. VENUS IN THE 12th: Should Venus be in the 12th bhava, the native will be indolent, happy, corpulent, fallen in moral sense, will eat cleansed food, will be skilful in providing sleeping comforts and attendants and will be won over by women.

Notes: If Venus occupics the 12th house, the nativc will be in a position to entertain guests, as hinted by "Sayanopachara Kusalah” in the text. Hc will provide food, bed etc. for their comforts.

The native with Venus so placed will be ever devoted to seeking pleasurcs of the sex, be immoral, will have free flow of money but the intent on spending in many ways, will perform excellent acts though he may not be very virtuous, be inimical to many, will suffer from many discases, be a. liar, will have many vices, and will attain heavens after death. There are two opinions about wealth being given by Venus in the 12th—one school of thought denotcs poverty while the other wealth. Sage Bhrigu denotes that Venus (alonc) in the 12th will give much penury but great wealth if conjunct with benefics. It follows that singly Venus in the 12th is not very auspicious for riches, if not literally capable of conferring utter poverty in the real sense. Unless a favourable planet joins him in the 12th, there wiil be difficulties in money matters like checkered earnings anc2 unnecessary and heavy expenses beyond income. And wit the march of time the native may lose a major part of hi= earnings.

A female with Venus in the 12th will bc a spendthrift, be devoid of intelligence and will suffer diseases. Both in the case of a male and female, Venus in the 12th will tell upon the marriage of the native concerned.

स्वोच्चस्वकोयमवने क्षितिपालतुल्यो

लग्नेऽर्जे भवति देशपुराधिनाथः । शेषेषु बुःलगदपीडित एव बाल्ये

दारिद्युकर्मवशगो मलिनोऽलसश्च ।७४।।

## SATURN IN VARIOUS BHAVAS (upto sloka 85)

74. SATURN IN THE ASCENDANT: If Saturn happens to be in the ascendant identical with exaltation or. own house, the nativc will cqual a king in status, or will head a country or city. In other Rasis in ascendant, the planct will give misery in boyhood, dirty disposition and indolence.

Notes: If Saturn occupies the ascendant, the native will be wealthy (unless Saturn is dignified). (Otherwise he will be poor.) Saturn in a sign of Jupiter apart from his own exaltation/own sign will give very good results in the matter of wealth etc. The native with Saturn in the ascendant (identical with signs other than the above) will be lame, will have a defective limb, will bc very miserable, will lose many things, will suffer nasal discases, will indulgc in gambling, drinking, womanising and the like and will destroy his enemies Just by a "mere sight”. Vide Jataka Sagara, “The native will be devoid of fortunes, will enjoy a long span of life, will hate his wife, will be separated from his family, will not have smooth living, will have co-born with their diminishing prosperity, be fond of travels and will become infamous.” Garga is however specific saying that Saturn in the ascendant in a sign other than Libra, Sagittarius and Pisces will diminish longevity. He adds, “The native with Saturn in the ascendant will suffer itches all over the body, be phlegmatic, will have deficient/excessive limb in the lower part of the body and will suffer diseases of the inner ear due to phlegmatic afflictions. Even with an aspect of Saturn on the ascendant, the native will be emaciated, miserable, foolish,

immoral, will give up his religion and will have troubles of head caused by iron etc.” Another authority states that the native will burn at the progress of others.

A female with Saturn in the ascendant will have many diseases or will suffer from lasting constipation, will have a very ugly appearance, be not respected by others, will have strong bones and teeth but be devoid of sight.

## विकृतवदनोर्र्यभोक्ता जनरहितो व्यायकृत्कुट् म्बगते ।

## पश्चात्परदेशगतो घनवाहनभोगवान् सौरे ।।७५।

75. SATURN IN THE 2nd: If Saturn occupies the 2nd bhava, the native will have an ugly face, will enjoy wordly prosperity ( t ), be dcvoid of his own men, will render justice, will latcr on (l.e. in the course of his lifc) go to other countrics and will earn moncy and conveyances.

Notes: If the Saturn be in the 2nd, one will be rich according to some, will be poor according to others and will have monetary losscs yet according to others. Thus it follows that even though Saturn in the 2nd can give wealth, the samc will be susceptible to loss. The native will not enjoy happy family llfe and will discard the family for his own plcasures. Most texts agree that the native will in the later part of life leave his place of birth and go elsewhere.

Hc will be skilful in learning and will grasp the meanings of many sastras. He will be devoid of co-born and will face many dangers according to Meena Raja. He will lose his physical weight due to mental worrics, will have illformed teeth, will look old at a young age and will spend away his money on account of females. A female with Saturn in the 2nd house will be devoid of wealth, be discarded by her family members and be always troubled and blamed.

## श्यामः संस्कृतदेहो नीचोऽलसपरिजनो भवति सौरे।

## शूरो दानानुरतो बुश्चिक्यगते विपुलबुद्धिः ।७६।।

76. SATURN IN THE 3rd: If Saturn occupies the 3rd bhava, the native will be dark in complexion, will maintain

physical cleanliness, be base, will have indolent attendants, be courageous, charitable and will have great intelligence.

Notes: Should Saturn be in the 3rd, the native will be valorous, ominous for co-born, very intelligent, helpful, will maintain many, will have obstructed fortunes, be longlived, will disregard even those respectful of him, will lose in his undertakings, be wealthy, will not eat much, will face many obstacles in getting married, be then happily married, affectionate to his spouse, will have a wide circle of acquaintance, be free from sickness, will however be not happy in boyhood but be happy later on, will beget progeny with difficulty, be not happy with progeny, will enjoy many conveyances, will spread his fame in all the four corners, will have mean conduct and be honoured by the king. With Saturn in the 3rd one will be liable to suffcr some defect/ diseases of the hand. He will not live with his father and will marry a girl having no mother. He will be specifically inimical to his co-born. A femal with Saturn so placed will be skilful, pralseworthy and chief among her family members. Though Hora Ratna quotes Yavanas saying that a female with Saturn in the 3rd will beget many children the female will be susceptible to have problems in this direction depending on the locus standi of Saturn and Jupiter.

## पोडितहृदयो हिबुके निर्वान्धववाहनार्थमतिसौस्यः । बाल्ये ध्याधितवेहो नखरोमघरो भवेत् सौरे ।।७७।।

77. SATURN IN THE 4th: If Saturn occupies the 4th bhava, the native will suffer heart disease or be brokenhearted, be devoid of relatives, conveyances, wealth, intelligence and happiness, will suffer sickness in boyhood and will have (prominent) nalls and hair.

Notes: If Saturn is found located in the 4th house, the native will lose his mother early or will be separated from her, will be a source of misery to his parents, will not beget anything paternal, will be inimical to his co-born and relatives, will gain happiness through enemies, be always distressed, will not get good houses to live in, will be devoid

of own properties, will frequently change places, and wil! suffer from windy diseases, epilepsy, cardiac diseases and the like. Normally Saturn in the 4th will bring up the person by another female than the mother. In some cases, the father of the subject may marry twice.

If Saturn is retrograde in the 4th, he destroys the native's wife and progcny. (ulde Manasagart).

A female with Saturn in the 4th will be dcvoid of happiness, wealth and intelligence. She will be particularly miserable following her marriage.

## सुखसुतमित्रविहीनं मतिरहितमचेतसं त्रिकोणस्थः । सोन्मादं रवितनयः करोति पुरुषं सदा दीनम् ॥७८।।

78. SATURN IN THE 5th: Should Saturn occupy the 5th bhava, the nativc will be bereft of happiness, sons, friends, intelligence and kindness, be agitated and be poor.

Notes: Should Saturn be in the 5th house in a nativity, the subject will not have happiness from progeny, be a heterodox, be foolish, unfricndly, be always sick, be troubled by debts and enmity, will lose wealth in securing others and will not have a happy marriagc. See what Garga says: "If Saturn is in the 5th in an cnemy’s camp, all the children of the native will be destroyed. If the planet be not combust and be in exaltation sign, the native will beget a son with difficulty. If he be in the 5th identical with Aquarius, therc will be acquisition of five children and if in Capricorn in 5th there will be three female children". Another authority states that Saturn in the 5th identlcal with Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius or Pisces will make one adopt a child. It follows that Saturn in a dual sign in the 5th will deny altogether a child of one’s own loins. A female with Saturn in the 5th will be devoid of progeny.

## प्रबलमदनं सुदेहं शूरं बह्वाशिनं वियमशीलम् ।

## बहुरिपुपक्षक्षपितं रिपुभवनगतोर्कंजः कुस्ते ।७९।।

79. SATURN IN THE 6th: If Saturn occupies the 6th bhava, the native will be very licentious, be beautiful, courageous, will eat abundantly, be crooked and will conquer nany of his enemies.

Notes: Should Saturn be in the 6th, the native will be happy with his progeny, will be miserable due to his wife's illhealth, will suffer afflictions from spirits and dead souls, will win over his cnemies, be very rich, fortunate will become a famous writer, and will suffer from urinary disorders and venereal diseases. A female with Saturn in the 6th will be endowed with robes and jewels, be wealthy, will obtain progeny and be affectionate to them.

## सततमनारोग्यतनुं मृतदारं धनविवाजितं जनवेत् । खूनेऽर्कजः : कुवेषं पापं बहुनीचकर्माणम् ।।८०।।

80. SATURN IN THE 7th: If Saturn is posited in the 7th bhava, the native will always be subjected to ill-health, will lose his wife, be bereft of wealth, will present himsclf ugly, be sinful and will do very mean acts.

Notes: If Saturn occupies the 7th, the native will have a weak body, depressed chest, depressed belly and stout thighs, be bony in structure and be quite irascible. He will not enjoy both marital happiness and maternal happiness, will suffer breakdowns in education and will be unfortunate. His wife will incur many discases. The native will subordinate others and will not occupy pre-eminent position. He will be very much addicted to illicit relationship with females. He will have many enemies and will be tormented by frequent litigations. A female with Saturn in the 7th will be ever sick, be given up by her spouse, be addicted to intoxicants, will befriend bad females and be quite sinful.

## कुष्ठभगन्दररोगैरभितप्तं ह्हस्वजीवितं निवने । सर्वारम्भविहीनं जनयति रविजः सदा पुरुषम् ।।८१।

81. SATURN IN THE 8th: If Saturn occupies the 8th bhava, the native will suffer from leprosy and fistula in the anus or pudendum, will have short life and will fail in his undertakings.

Notes: Kalyana Varma denotes short-life resulting from Saturn’s position in the 8th house. Without meaning any disrespect to the author, it may be stated that many other exponents like sage Bhrigu attribute a long lease of life. However Nataraja of Jatakalankara indicates adverse effects on longevity if Saturn is in the 8th for only Scorpio ascendant and Aries ascendant. Generally Saturn in the 8th is conducive to long span of life.

The native concerned will incur financial losses, be not wealthy, will have frequent misunderstandings with his wife and other family members, will have obstructions in career, will not be happy with progeny, be mocked at by others, be addicted to whores and will not honour his parents. His life will be in danger during his 35th year. He will suffer afflictions of blood, teeth, eyes, stomach, anus, liver, spleen etc.

A female with Saturn so placed will destroy herself on account of her own faults, be base and dirty and will cheat others.

## घर्मरहितोऽल्यधनिकः सहजसुतविर्वाजितो नवमसंस्थे । रविजे सौर्यविहीमः परोपतापी च जायते मनुजः ।८२।।

82. SATURN IN THE 9th: If Saturn occupies the 9th bhava, the native will be devoid of religlous merits, will not have much wealth, be bereft of co-born, sons and happiness and will cause sorrow to others.

Notes: If Saturn is stationed in the 9th the native will lose his wife early, will take to spiritual path in the later part of life, will undertake to reconstruct dilapidated public places and will join bad men. A female with Saturn in the 9th will be devoid of learning and providence.

## धनवान् पातः शुरो मन्त्रो वा दण्डनायको वाऽपि । वशमस्थे रवितनये जन्दपुरग्रामनेता च ।।८३।।

83. SATURN IN THE 10th: If Saturn occupies the 10th bhava, the native will be wealthy, learned, valorous and be a minister or a justice or be the leader of a group, city or village.

Notes: If Saturn occupies the 10th the native will be devoid of parental happiness and ancestral properties, will financially prosper gradually, will hold judicial positions, will have prodigal sons and will amass wealth in foreign places. A female with Saturn in the 10th will be ugly and sinful.

## बह्वायुः स्थरविभवः शूरः शिल्पाश्रयो विगतरोगः ।

## आयस्षे भानुसुते धम-जनसम्पद्युतो भवति ।८४।।

84. SATURN IN THE 11th: If Saturn occupics the 11th bhava, the native will be long-lived, endowed with lasting riches, be courageous, will have knowledge of arts, be devoid of sickness and be endowed with money, people and wealth.

Notes: If the 11th housc is occupied by Saturn, the subject will gain indestructible wealth and longevity. He will have no children, be famous, happy, fortunate, will suffer only minor (curable) sicknesses, (or be free from discases as stated by Kalyana Varma) and will be unhappy with his elder brothers/sisters. A female with Saturn in the I lth will have abundant blood in her physique, be devoid of wisdom and be distressed.

## विकलः पतितो मुखरो विषमाक्षो निघुणो विगतलज्जः । व्ययभवनगते सौरे बहुव्ययः स्यात् सुपरिभूतः ।।८५।।

85. SATURN IN THE 12th: If Saturn occupies the 12th bhava, the native will be distressed, fallen in moral sense, talkative, will have defective eye sight, be unkind, shame-

less, will spend much and be insulted.

Notes: If Saturn be in the 12th house, one will be poor, dcformed of some limb, tormented by cxpenscs, addicted to courtezans and will not have a permancnt livelihood. A female with Saturn in the 12th will not have lasting family tics.

पाया निध्ननिति मूर्त्यादीन् भावान् पुर्णक्ति शोभना।

विपरीतं सदसत्फलम् ।।८६।। योगा प्रयबलयोगात् सौस्यसुहृद्रिपुनिरीक्षणाच्चंव । उच्चादिभवनसंस्थप्रहैश्च फलमन्यथा भवति ।८७।।

86-87. If malefics occupy various bhavas (other than 6th, 8th, and 12th), they bring harm to the bhavas while benefics incrcase their potence. Malefics are auspicious in evil houses, i.e. 6th, 8th, and 12th, while benefics prove adverse in these bhavas. According to thc strength of yogakaraka planets, their bencficial relationship, fricndly/ enimical aspects etc., and position in cxaltation/debilitation, the (good or bad) results of bhavas can vary (ie. be maximum, medium or nil).

Notes: If a planet is well-disposed to the native by virtue of its ability to cause good yoga, the malcfic effects attributed in the foregoing slokas will be comparatively lesser with auspicious effects increasing. If the planet in question be in own house, friendly sign etc., then even this approach holds good.
