If it is in inimical sign, combustion or debilitation, the adverse results stated earlier will be aggrandised while auspicious results will diminish. The strength of the planet, its relationship with the ascendant etc. also be considered wisely.

इति कल्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां ग्रह्भावफलाध्यायस्त्रशः ॥ ३०॥

Thus ends the 30th chapter entitled “EFFECTS OF PLANETS IN BHAVAS” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## अथ द्विग्रहकेन्द्रफलाध्यायः ।।३१।। Chapter 31

## Two Planets in Angles

यथा इवयोर्द्योर्हयोः । होराचतुर्यसप्तमदशमेय् भवति फलसम्प्रयोगी जातस्य तथायमुपदेशः ॥१।।  
1. Now explained arc effects of two-planet conjunc-. tions in the Ascendant, the 4th, the 7th and the 10th. मातृपितृदुःखतपतः सूर्येन्द्रोरुदयसंस्थयोर्मनुजः । मानसुतविभवहीनः परिभूतो जायते दुःखी ।।२। बान्धवसुतसुखहीनो दारिद्ययृतो महाजडप्रकृतिः । चनद्दे रसातलस्थे भास्करसहिते पुमान् जातः ।३। मित्रैः सुतैश्च हीनः परिभूतो युवतिभिः सदा पुरुषः । चन्द्रे सप्तम-मवने दिनकरसहिते भवेट्रीनः ।।४।। सुशरीर बलनायं राजसिकं निर्दयं विवमशीलम् । सूर्येन्ड्क गगनस्थौ कुस्तः क्षपितारिपक्षं च ।।५।

2-5. SUN-MOON CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If the Sun and Moon occupy the ascendant, the native will suffer grief regarding parents, will be devoid of honour, sons and wealth, be insulted and miserable. If these two join the 4th bhava, the native will be deprived of relatives, happiness and sons, will suffer penury and will be a great dunce. If in the 7th, he will be devoid of friends and sons and be insulted by females. If in the 10th, he will possess a beautiful physique, be an army chief, be endowed with the quality of rajas or passion, be unkind, crooked and will

destroy his enemies.

रविभौमयोविलग्ने पितप्रकृतिरमहाहवे शूरः । क्रोधो विक्षतगात्रः ऋरूरश्च शठः कठोरः स्यात् ।६। बन्धुजनवित्तहीनः समस्तसुखर्वाजितः क्षुभितः । कुजनूर्ययोश्चतुर्थे भवति पुमान् सर्वतो ह्वेच्यः ।७।। स्त्रीविरहदुःखखिन्नः स्त्रीहेतोः परिभवं सदा प्राप्तः। रविरुधिरपोर्युवत्वां विदेशगमने रतो भवति ।।।। विफलारम्भो भृतको नित्योद्रिम्तः प्रधाननृपसेवी । भूतनयदिवाकरयोः कर्मणि रतयोर्भवेद्विकलः ।।।।

6-9. SUN-MARS CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If the Sun and Mars join in the ascendant, the person will be bilious, be bold in war, short-tempcred, will have injured body, be cruel, crafty and harsh. If these two join in the 4th, he will be devold of relatives and wealth, be bereft of all kinds of happiness and be agitated. If these join in the 7th, he will suffer from separation from his wife, be insulted on account of females and will be fond of moving in forcign places. If the conjunction is in the 10th, the native will face failure in his undertakings, be a servant, be emotional, be a principal employee with the king and be always distressed.

प्राज्ञो बहुप्रलापी कठिनांगः शूरवल्लभो मतिमान् । लग्ने बुध्दिनकरयोर्दर्घायुः संभवेत्पुरुषः ।।१०।। नृपतिसमो विस्यातो गृहीतवार्यः पुबेरसमविभवः । रविशाशितनयौ हिबुके स्थूलतनुर्वक्रनासश्च ।।११। बधबन्धनकुम्मृत्युग् हीतवावयो न चातिधनलुब्धः ।

स्त्रीरतिहीनश्बोरो द्यने बुधसूर्ययोर्भवति ॥।१२।। त्रिप लोकेपु स्यातो गजाश्वनाथो भवेन्महीपालः। विनकरब्धयोर्दशमे न नोचराशिस्थयोरेव ॥१३।।

10-13. SUN-MERCURY CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If the Sun and Mercury are in the ascendant, the native will be very learned and garrulous, will have hard limbs, be fond of adventures, be wise and be long-lived. If the conjunction is in the 4th, the native will be equal to a king, be famous, will keep up his promise, will be endowed with riches equal to that of Kubera (the God of Wealth in Hindu Mythology) and will have a corpulent body and crooked nose. If in the 7th, the native will meet his death due to murder or imprisonment, will not keep up his word, be a great miser, bereft of sexual pleasures and be a thief. If in the 10th, he will be famous in the entire world, will own elephants and horses and be a king, provided none of these two is in debilitation. जीवार्कयोर्गुणयुतो मन्त्री बलनायकोऽ्यवा साधुः।

लग्नस्थयो प्रसुतो विद्याधनमोगवान्स्यातः ।१४।। श्रुतिनतिकाव्यनिरतं भव्यं जनसम्पदं प्रियालापम् । हिबुके सुरेज्यस्यौं निम्ताचारं नर कुस्तः ॥१५। जीवार्षयोर्युवत्यां मदनवशारस्त्रीजितः पितृद्धेषी। कनक्षमणिरजतमौक्तिकसमन्वितः शुभशरीरः स्यात् ॥ १६। क्कीरतिसुखमानविभर्वः समन्वितः पायिवो भवेन्नमसि । रविवेवपुरोहितयोनि द्येऽपि कुले नरो जातः ॥ १७।

14-17. SUN-JUPITER CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If the Sun and Jupiter join in the ascendant, the native will be virtuous, be a minister or an army chief or be sagely in disposition and will be endowed with learning, wealth, pleasures and fame. If the Yoga is in the 4th, he will be interested in Vedas, justice and poetry, be eloquent, virtuous, fortunate and will be endowed with servants. If in the 7th, he will subdue to women out of sexual passion, will dislike his father, be endowed with gold, ruby, silver and pearls and will possess a defect-free body. If in the 10th, he will be endowed with fame, happiness, honour and wealth and will become a king though belonging to a base lineage.

प्रियकलहस्त्वविनीतो मलिनावारः सुदुःखितो नीचः । लग्ने रविभ्गुसृतयोरत्वर्थकलत्रसम्परित्यक्तः ॥ १८॥ आदित्ये हिबुकस्ये भार्यवसहिते भवेन्नरो जातः । परभृत्यः शोकार्तो लोकट्टेप्यो दरिद्रश्च १।१६। स्त्रीभिः सम्परिभूतो द्रविणविहीनो बृहत्तनुह्वेच्यः । शैलवनेतु च विचरति रविसितयो सप्तमस्थाने।२०।। कर्मणि दिनकरसितयोर्व्यवहार रतो नरेन्द्रसचिवः स्यात् । शास्त्रकलानिनुणमतिर्धनवाह्नसौर्यसम्पन्नः 11

18-21. SUN-VENUS CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If these two join in the ascendant, the native will be fond of quarrels, be immodest, will take to bad conduct, be sorrowful, mean and be devoid of wealth and women. Should these join in the 4th, one will serve others, be sorrowful, will fill himsclf with hatred towards others and will suffer penury. One will be insulted by women, be devoid of wealth, will have a corpulent body, will wander in hills and forests, if the Sun be in the 7th along with Venus. Should these two join in the 10th, he will be engaged in trade, be a king's minister, be skilful in sastras, arts etc., and will enjoy happiness of wealth and conveyances.

निन्दितजननीपुत्रः कुत्सितवृत्तिः सदा मलिनबुद्धिः । लग्ने सूर्यार्कजयो: पापाचारो भवेत्पुरुषः ।२२।। सौरे चतुर्यराशौ भास्करसहिते पुमान् भवति नीचः। दारिद्रपविहितसूतिः स्वबन्युभिश्वापि परिभूतः ।।२३। भान्वर्कजयोर्मदने मन्दालसदुर्भगाश्च जायन्ते। युवतिघनेः सन्त्यक्ता मृगयाभिरता महामूर्णाः ।२४।। भानुः स्वपुत्रसहितो गगने भृत्कं विदेशगं जनयेत् । नृपतेः क्वविवाप्तधनञ्चौरेरमुचितं सदाप्यप्रवम् १२५।। 22-25. SUN-SATURN CONJUNCTION IN

ANGLES:

Should the Sun and Saturn be together in the ascendant at birth, thc native is born of a blameworthy mother, will have blameworthy conduct or profession, will always be of dirty d disposition and be sinful. If their conjunction occurs in the 4th house, the native will be base in disposition, will be synonym of penury and will be humillated among his relatives. Should they join in the 7th house, the native will be inactive ( 4 has other meanings like stupid, foolish, tardy, indiffcrent, weak-brained, addicted to drinking, defective etc.), indolent, unfortunate, devoid of a female and of wealth, will hunt animals and be highly dullwitted. If the Sun joins his son in the 10th, one will serve in low position ip foreign countrics (i.e. places not of his own), will earn wealth through kings sometimes but will lose the same in theft and be always poor.

रक्ताग्निपित्तदोर्षरभिभूतो जायते नरो राजा । क्षोणीसुतहिमकरयोर्लग्ने तीक्षणस्वभावश्च ।२६।। सक्लेशो निद्राव्यः सुखसृतधनबन्धुहीनश्च । पाताले कुजशशिनोविकलश्च भवेत्तथा जातः ।।२७।। कषुद्रः परघनलुब्धो बहुप्रलापी न सत्यवचनश्च। ईर्थ्यायुक्तो मनुजः कुजशशिनोः सप्तमस्थाने ।२८।। तुरगगजपत्तिसम्पत्समाकुलं तुहिनगुनर कुस्ते। रुधिरेण समायातो गगनतले विक्रमर्युक्तम् ।२६।।

26-29. MOON-MARS CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: The native who has the Moon and Mars together in the ascendant will have defects of blood, digestve fire and bile, will be a king (i.e. will be wealthy) and will be harsh. If the two Join in the 4th house, the native will incur grief, be devold of wealth, happiness, progeny and relatives and be defective of some limb. These two in the 7th in conjunction denote that the person concerned will be base, be after others' money, be garrulous, untruthful and jealous. Their conjunction in the 10th will make the person rossess the wealth of army consisting of horses, elephants and bipeds

and brave.

सुखबुद्धिसत्वयुक्तः सुभगः कान्तो विलग्ने शशिनि । बुधसहिते भवति नरो बाचालश्चातिनिपुणश्च ।।३०।। बन्धुसुह्तनयसुख प्रतापकनकाश्वरत्नसंयुक्तम् बान्धवराशाविन्दुर्जनयति बुधसंयुतः सुभगम् ।३१। द्यने बुधसंयुक्तो जनयति चन्द्रः प्रतापिनं पुरुवम् । नृपसंमतं नृपं वा विस्यातं सरकावि ललितम् ।३२।। दशमे बुधहिमकरयोर्मानी धनवानतिस्यातः । नृपसचिवो वयसोऽते दुःखी स्याव्बन्धुपरिहीनः ।।३३।

30-33. MOON-MERCURY CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: Should the Moon and Mercury be together in the ascendant, the native will possess happiness, intelligence, strength, fortune and splendour, be talkative and very skilful. If the conjunction occurs in the 4th, he will be endowed with relatives, friends, progeny, happiness, valour, gold and precious stones and be fortunate. If they join in the 7th, one will be valorous, be dear to king or be a king himself, be famous, be a great poet and be soft in disposition. Their joining in the 10th indicates that the subject will be honourable, be wealthy and very famous, be a royal minister, will grieve during the last leg of his life and be devoid of relatives.

लग्ने सुरेव्यशशिनो क्षितिपः पृचुपीनवक्षः स्यात् । बहुतनयमित्रभार्यः सुशरीरो बन्धुभिर्जुष्टः ।३४।।

मन्त्री राजप्रतिमः सुखबन्धुसमन्वितो महाविभवः । बहुशास्त्राक्षतबुद्धिहिबुके स्याज्जीवशशिनोश्च ।३५।। जायाभवने कुर्तः सुप्राशनं पाथिवं कलाकुशलम् । वाणिजकं जोवेन्डू नृपवस्लभमचवत्स्फीतम् ।३६।। कर्मणि सुरेव्यशशिनोविद्यादानार्थमानकीतियुतः सौम्यः प्रसम्बबाहुः सर्वनमस्यो नरो भवति ।३७।।

34-37. MOON-JUPITER CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If the Moon joins Jupiter in the ascendant, the person concerned will be a king, will possess large and broad chest, will have many sons, friends and wives and will be endowed with a good physique and with relatives. These two joining in the 4th indicate that one will be a minister or be of royal status, be endowed with happincss, relatives and great riches (or prowess) and will master many sasthras. The Moon and Jupiter in the 7th will make one very learned. kingly, an expert in arts, a good businessman, dear to king, wealthy and prosperous. In the 10th this conjunction givcs learning, charitable disposition, wealth, honour and fame. Further he will be soft, will possess long arms and will be revered by all.

बेश्वास्त्रीकृतसौख्यः कान्ततनुः संमतो गुरुणां च । मास्याम्बरगन्धयुतो लग्ने शशिशुजप्रोर्भवति ।।३८। पाताले शशिशुको स्त्रीजनसुखभागिनं नर कुरुतः । अलसंयानाप्तधनं जनप्रियं भोगसम्पन्नम् ।।३६।। जामित्रे सितशशिनोबहुयुवतिरतो न चातिधनपुत्रः। स्त्रीजननो मेधावी भूपतिवरितो भवेत्पुर्वः मानाज्ञाविमवयुतः कर्मणि शुक्रे शार्शाक्युते । राज्ञो मन्त्री स्यातः क्षमान्वितः स्याद्बहुजनश्च ।।४१।।

38-41, THE MOON-VENUS CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If the Moon and Venus join in the ascendant, the native will derive happiness through association with courtezans, be splendorous, be agreeable to elders and will possess garlands, clothes and scents. These two joining in the 4th will make one derive happiness through females, carn wcalth through water, dear to people and enjoy abundant plcasures. If these two join in the 7th, one will join many women, will not have much wealth and not many sons, be an intellectual and will leave a royal history behind. One with the Moon and Venus in the 10th will be endowed with honour, command and riches, be a king or a minister, be

famous and will be endowed with many people.

दासाः खलाः सुरौद्रा भबन्ति लुब्धाश्व मानवाहोनाः। भास्करसुतहिमकरयोर्लग्ने निद्रालसाः पापाः ।४२।। जलमुक्तामणिपोतजीवन्ति नरास्तथा खननवृत्या। हिबुके शशिरविसुतयोः श्रेष्ठाः जनसंमता जाताः ।४३।। नगरप्रामपुराणां महत्तरा राजपूजिताः पुर्वाः । जायन्तेऽर्जशशिनोर्जायाभवने युवतिहीना: ४४।। प्रचुर तुरंगमदलितारतिः स्यातः कुयोधित पुत्रः । भवति नराणामधिपः शशिशनियोगे खमध्यगते ।।४५।।

42-45. THE MOON-SATURN CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If the Moon and Saturn join in the ascendant, ihc native will be in servitude, be base, given to anger, be miscrly, sleepy, lazy and sinful. If the said two plancts be together in the 4th, one will carn through water, pearls and boats, be engaged in cxcavation (or in mines etc.), be excellcnt, and agrecable to others. If they join in the 7th, the subject will be devoid of wife, be supremc among his townsmen and be honoured by the king. If Saturn joins the Moon in tne 10th, the person concerned will win the enemies with his army of horses, be famous, is a bad women’s son and will be head of men.

सौम्यग्रहसंयुक्तः प्रायेण शुभावहो भगणनाथः ।

भौमाकियुतो नेष्टो दशमे च चमूर्पाति कुर्यात् ॥४६।।

46. DISPOSITION OF THE MOON: If the Moon is conjunct benefics she will always prove beneficial. If she Joins Mars/Saturn, she is always inauspicious, this does not apply to her joining Mars/Saturn in the 10th so that the native becomes an army chief.

हिल्नोऽन्निकर्मकुशलो धातोर्वदि कृत्रमो दूतः

भौषबुधयोविलग्ने गुप्त्यधिकारी भवेत्युर्षः ।४७।।

बान्धवरहितः सहितो मित्रेश्च धनान्तभोगवाह्नवान् । हिबुके बधभूसुतयोः स्वजनेयु निराकुतो जातः ।४८।। स्रमति च देशाहेशं कर्मकरो नीचपरिभूतः। भौमेन्दुजयोर्यूने सुविवादकरो मृतप्रथमदारः ।४६। सेनाधिपतिः शुरः शठस्वभावो भवेदतिक्रूरः । बुधकुजयोराकाशे राज्ञोऽभिमतो नरो धीरः ।।५०।।

47-50. MARS-MERCURY CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: One who has Mars and Mercury in conjunction in the ascendant will be murderous (or cruel or mischievous), be skilful in dealing with jobs related to fire, be a metallurgist / mineralogist, be industrious, be an ambassador (or a messenger) and will enjoy concealed/secret properties. If the conjunction in question occurs in the 4th, onc will be devoid of relatives but endowed with friends, wealth, food, pleasures and convcyanccs and will be discarded by his own people. Should they join in the 7th, one will wander from country to country (aimlessly), will serve base people, be skilful in arguments and will lose his first wife. Their conjunction in the 10th denotes that the person will be an army chief, be valorous, crafty, very cruel, dear to king and courageous.

मन्त्री गुणप्रधानो धर्मक्षेत्रे प्रलब्धपरिकोरतिः। जीवकुजयोविलग्ने नित्योत्साही भवेत्पुरुषः बन्धुसुहुत्सम्पन्नः स्यरवित्तः सौख्यभाक् भवेद्विबुके । भौमामरपूजितयोत् पसेवी देबगुरुभक्तः 1५२।। गिरिदुर्गतोयकाननविवरणशीलः सुबान्धवः शूरः । कुजजीवयोर्युवत्यां जायाहीनः पुमान्भवति ॥५३। त्रिदशगुरुभूमिसुतयोराकाशे पार्थिवो विपुलकोतिः । बहुधनजनपरिवारः कर्मसु निपुणो भवेत्पुरुषः ।।५४।।

51-54. MARS-JUPITER CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If Mars and Jupiter are in conjunction in the ascendant, one will be a minister, will possess prime virtues, become famous in the front of charity and will be ever enthuslastic. With these two planets joining in the 4th, one will be endowed with relatives and friends, will have firm wealth, be happy, will serve the king and be devoted to the Almighty and elders. If they join in the 7th, one will be inclined to wander in hills, forts, water-resorts and forests, will have good relatives, be valorous and will be devoid of spouse. Their conjunction in the 10th will confer kinghood, wide fame, abundant wealth and attendants and extreme skill at work.

शुक्रकुजयोविलग्ने वेश्यानिरतः कुशित्पकर्मा च। स्त्रीहेतोर्नष्टवनो न तु चिरजीवी भवेत्पुरुषः ।५५।। बन्धुसुतमित्रहीनो मानसपोडामिरवितः पुरुषः । भौमसितयोश्चतुयें नानादुर्लर्भवेत्तप्तः ॥५६।। स्त्रीलोल्पः कुचरितः स्त्रीहेतोः प्राप्तवान्महादुःखम् । भौमसितयोर्युवत्यां हीनाचारो भवेत्पुरुषः ॥५७। अस्त्राचार्यों मतिमान् विद्यावनवस्त्रमाल्यवान्भवति । श्यातो नरेन्द्रसविवः सितकुजयोर्र्योम्नि संस्थितयोः ॥ ५८।।

## 55-58. MARS-VENUS CONJUNCTION IN

ANGLES:

Should these two join in the ascendant, the native will be addicted to prostitutes, will indulge in bad acts, will lose wealth on account of females and will not live long. If the conjunction of Venus and Mars occur in the 4th, he will be bereft of relatives, friends and progeny, will be tortured by mental pressures and will be very miserable. Their joining in the 7th denotes that he will wander after women, will have a bad history, will experience abundant grief on account of females and will possess mean conduct. Should these two planets join in the 10th, he will be an expert in missiles, be intelligent, be endowed with learning, wealth, robes and flowers, be famous, and be a king's minister.

संग्रामलब्धविजयो भौमार्कजयोर्लग्ने जननीद्वेच्यो भवेत्पुरुषः । ह्वस्वायुः क्षीणभाग्यश्च ॥५६।। पानान्नसौख्यरहितः स्वजनेस्त्यष्तो भवति जातः । भौमार्कजयोहिवुके मित्रेश्व विर्वाजितः पापः ।१०।। जायासुखसुतहीनो बैन्यपरो व्याधितो व्यसनशीलः । भौमार्कजयोरस्ते जनपरिभूतो भवेत्कृपणः । ६१। राशः सम्प्राप्तधनो महापराधाञ्च दण्डितस्तेन । भौमार्कजयोर्दशमे न सत्यवचनो नरो भवति । ६२।।

59-62. MARS-SATURN CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If Mars and Saturn are together in the ascendant, one will be successful in wars, will hate his mother and will face curtalled longevity and declining fortunes. If these two join in the 4th, one will be bereft of food, drinks and happiness, be given up by his own men, be devoid of friends and be sinful. Their joining in the 7th indicates that the native will be devoid of conjugal happiness and progeny, be indigent, sick, will house vices in him, be insulted by people and be a talebearer. If Mars join Saturn in the 10th, one will acquire wealth through king, but be punished for a great crime and be a liar.

शुभमूतिः शुमशीलो विद्वान्नुपसत्हतो विषयनाथः । बुधजोवयोविलम्ने वाहनसुखभोगवान् भवति ॥६३।। बन्धुसुह्त्सुलसहितः स्त्रीधनसौभाग्यसम्पन्नः । बुधजीवयोश्चतुर्थे निपुणी नृपसम्मतो भवति १६४। सुकूलत्रो हतशत्रबहुजनमित्रार्थसत्वसम्पन्नः । ब्रुधजीवयोर्युवत्यामतीत्य पितृपक्षमधिषः स्यात् ॥६५। क्षोधनगुर्वोर्देशमे नरेन्द्रमन्त्री नृपोऽयवा भवति । मानाश्तस्यातियुतः श्रौतार्थपरो विनीतश्च ।६६।। 63-66. MERCURY-JUPITER IN ANGLES IN CONJUNC-

TON: If Mercury and Jupiter be together in the ascendant, one will have an auspicious (pleasing) appearance, will be endowed with auspicious (excellent) qualities, be learned, honoured by the king, will conquer his five senses ( fव aI also means a head of province) and will enjoy conveyances, happiness and pleasures. If these two join in the 4th, one will be blessed with relatives, fricnds, happiness, female, fortune, wealth, royal favour and skill. Thcir being together in the 7th denotes that one will obtain a virtuous wife, will destory his enemies, will be endowed with many friends, people, wealth and prowess, and will be famous on account of his father. In the 10th, these two planets together will make one a minister of a king, honourable, commanding, famous, modest and interested in Vedas.

बुधशुक्योविलग्ने सुशरीरः पण्डितः सतां सुभगः ।

नृपपूजितोऽतिधन्यो दिजसुरभक्तो भवेत्स्यातः ।।६७।। बुधशुको हिबुकस्यी पुत्रसुह्द्बन्धुसंयुतं सुभगम् । मन्त्रिणमयवा नृपति कुर्तः कल्याणसम्पन्नम् ।६८। बुधशुक्योर्युवत्यां सद्बहुयुवतिपरिवेष्टितः पुरुषः । भोगधनेश्वर्ययुतो भवति सुखी संमतो राज्ञाम् ।६६।। बोधनसितयोः कर्मणि नीतिज्ञो भवति भूपतिः साघुः । नीचात्रयो न, चाढचः सफलारम्भः समर्यश्च ।।७०।

67-70. MERCURY-VENUS IN CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If Mercury and Venus join in the ascendant, one will have a good physique (i.e. beautiful physique), be learned, always fortunate, be honoured by the king, be very wealthy, worshiper of Brahmins and God and be famous. If these two join in the 4th, he will be endowed with sons, friends and relatives, be fortunate, be a minister or a king and be endowed with happiness. (Kalyana apart from meaning happiness, indicates excellence, fortune, auspiciousness etc.). When the two planets in question join in the 7th, he will be surrounded with many good women, be

endowed with pleasures, money and sovereignty, be happy and be dear to king. Should this conjunction occur in the 10th, he will be endowed with knowledge of the science of ethics, (or Policy or Politics), be a king, be virtuous, will not patronise base men, be wealthy, will attain good beginning in his undertakings and be skilful.

मलिनशरोरः पानी विद्याधनबाहनंः परित्यक्तः । सौम्यार्कजयोर्लग्ने हृदस्वायुः क्षीणभाग्यश्च ।७१। पानान्नबन्धुरहितः स्वजनेवु तिरस्हतो भबति मूढः । सौम्यार्कजयोहिबुके मित्रेश्च विवाजितः पापः ॥७२।। ईश्वरभृतको मूर्खः परापकारी न साधुरतिमलिनः । सौम्यार्कजयोरस्ते न सत्यवचनो नरो भवति ।७३।। प्रशमितसमस्तशत्रुः स्वजनसु हुदाहनार्थसम्पन्नः । सौम्यार्कजयोर्दशमे द्विजगुरुसुरपूजको भवति ।७४।।

71-74. MERCURY-SATURN IN ANGLES IN CONJUNC- TION: If Mercury and Saturn be in conjunction in the ascendant, one will have a dirty body, be sinful, be bereft of lcarning, wcalth and vehicles, be short-lived and will have declining fortunes. If the conjunction in question occurs in the 4th, he will be devoid of drinks, food and relatives, be rejected by his own people, be foolish, devoid of friends and be sinful. Their company in the 7th indicates that he will be the servant of a rich person, be foolish, will harm others, be not virtuous, be very dirty and be not truthful. If these two join in the 10th, he will conquer the entire band of his enemies, will be endowed with relatives, friends, conveyances and wealth and will honour the Almighty and Brahmins.

जीवसितयोविलग्ने गुरूपदिष्टो भवेतक्षमानायः ।

श्राह्माणकुलसम्भूतोऽप्यनुकूने भवति नृपतुल्यः ।७५।। प्रशमितसमस्तशत्रः स्वजनसुहृद्राह्नार्यसम्पन्नः । जीवसितयोषश्चतुर्थे द्विजगुस्सुरपूजको भवति ।७६।।

सुस्यीरत्नार्ययुतः स्त्रीजननो लब्धसौस्यकोतिष्य । गुरुगुक्रयोर्यु त्यां वरवाहनभोगवान्मवति ।७७।।

गगनस्थो गुरुशुकौ मानाज्ञाविभवविस्तरेः सहितम् । जनयेतां पृथिवीशं बहुमृत्यधनं सुशीलं च ।।७e)।

75-78. JUPITER-VENUS IN ANGLES IN CONJUNCTION: With Jupiter and Venus in tne ascendant, the native even though a Brahmin by birth will on account of his preceptor’s teaching become a king or equal to a king. If these two planets join in the 4th the native will win all his enemies, will be endowed with relatives, friends, conveyances, wealth and will honour the Almighty and Brahmins. If these two join in the 7th, one will acquire a virtuous female, be endowed with precious stones and riches, will acquire happiness, female and female issue, and will procure excellent conveyances and enjoy pleasures. If they join in the 10th, he will widely command, be widely honoured and endowed, wide riches, be a king, will have many servants and be virtuous.

लग्ने जीवार्कजयोर्मदालसा निष्ठुराः समभिजाताः । विद्वांसो धनसाराः किञ्चित्सुखिता भवन्ति खलाः ॥७८।। नृपसचिवो निर्जतनुर्जयोदयी बान्धरवैः सुह्गृद्भश्व । पातालेऽर्कजगुर्षोः प्रीतिधनः सौष्यवान्भवति ।।eo।। स्त्रीवरान्नव्टधनः शुरो व्यसनी शठो न शुभमूतिः । द्यूने सुरेज्ययमयोः पितृधनलुब्धश्व जायते मूर्खः ।१। दशमे भास्करिजीवो नरेन्दयितं च भूर्पति कुस्तः। अल्पापर्यं न चलं गोकुलबहुवाहनार्यं ् ।८२।

79-82. JUPITER-SATURN IN CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If Saturn and Jupiter are together in the ascendant at birth, the native will be languid with passion (or intoxication), hard-hearted, is of a noble descent, learned, rich, will have limited happiness and be wicked. If in the

4th, he will be a king's minister, be bereft of diseases, be successful, be endowed with relatives and friends, will have the wealth of affection (i.e. extremely affectionate) and be ‘happy. Should they join in the 7th, he will lose wealth due to enmity with females, be courageous, will possess vices, be crafty, will not be cndowed with pleasing appearance. will be greedy of patcrnal property and be foolish. If in the 10th, he will be dear to the king, be a king himself, will have limited progeny, be intransiotry (or steady) and will possess quadrupeds and conveyances.

रमते सर्ववधूभिः कान्तशरीरः सुखार्थभोगयुतः । लग्ने भूगुसृतयमयोर्बहुमृत्यः शोकसन्तप्तः ।८३।।” मित्रेभ्यो धनलामं बन्युभ्यः सक्तक्रियाः समाप्नोति । शनिशुक्रयोश्चतुर्थे नृपतेश्च तथाप्ततां याति ।।८४।। स्त्रीरत्नानि सुखानिश्व धनानि कीति च भूतिमखिलां च । मन्दसितयोर्युवत्यां प्राप्नोति पुमान् विवयलाभम् ।८५।। सर्वह्न्क्षिमुक्तो लोके स्यातो विशिष्टकर्मा च । मेवूरणे सितार्क्योन् पतेर्मन्त्री भवेषधिकः ।।८६।। 83-86. VENUS-SATURN IN CONJUNCTION IN ANGLES: If Venus and Saturn join together in the ascendant, one will be seeking union with all women (i.e. he will enjoy many women), will posscss a splendorous body, be endowed with happiness, pleasures and wealth, will have many servants and be highly bereaved. If in the 4th, he will gain abundantly through friends, be highly honoured by his relatives and will be close to the king. Their conjunction in the 7th denotes that the nattve will be bestowed with women, precious stones, wealth, fame and sexual pleasures. If the conjunction is in the 10th, he will be devoid of all duals (i.e. good and bad, grief and happiness, poverty and wealth etc.), will perform excellent acts and be a famous minister.

एवं त्रिभिश्चतुमिः पञ्चभिरय सप्तभिश्च वड्भिर्दा ।

वक्तव्यं केन्द्रस्यैर्ह्योगफलं विचिन्त्य धिया ।८७।।

87. EFFECTS OF MORE NUMBER OF PLANETS: Similarly, the effects of conjunctions of 3, 4, 5 or 6 planets in angles should be suitably understood.

इति कत्वाणवर्मविरवितायां सारावल्यां ग्रहकेन्द्रफलं नाम एकविशोऽध्यायः II

Thus cnds the 31st Chapler entitled "EFFECTS OF TWO PLANETS IN ANGLES" in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.
