## अथ भाग्यचिन्ताध्यायः ।।३२।।

## Chapter 32

## Ninth House and Effects Thereof

सर्वमपहाय चिन्त्यं भाग्यर्ष प्राणिनां विशेषेण ।

भाग्यं किना न ज्तुर्यस्मात्तद्यत्नतो बक्ष्ये ।१।

1. Leaving aside all the other houses, one should first analyse the house of forlune (or Bhagyasthana) for a native cannot obtain bencfic results sans fortune. So, narrated below is an account in this direction.

Notes: The 9th house or the house of fortunes is the most important one in a horoscope. If this house, its lord and its occupant are favourable, the native will generally be happy in respect of wealth, livelihood etc. Otherwisc not. Hence the author devotes an exclusive chapter in this direction.

लग्नान्निशाकराद्वा यन्नवमं तद्गृहं भवेद्भाग्यम् । अनयोर्यो बलयुक्तो भाग्यफलं चिन्तयेदस्मात् ।।२।।

2. Bhagyasthana (house of fortune) is that which is the 9th counted from the ascendant or from the Moon. Effects of the 9th counted from the stronger of the Ascendant and the Moon will come to pass.

Notes: Among the ascendant and the Moon, note which is more strong. So to say the Shadbala and disposition of Ascendant lord and the Moon will have to be taken into consideration and the stronger of the two shall be the point to count the 9th house. Such 9th house—from Lagna or from the Moon—will yield predominant effects relating to

the fortunes of the native. Let us assume that Lagna is stronger than the Moon in a given case. If the 9th house from Lagna is indicative of good fortunes, even maleficeffects rclating to the 9th house from the Moon will not fully come to pass. So to say in this case more favourable results will mature.

## भाग्वर्षपतिः कस्मिन् को वा भाग्यर्षमाश्रितो विहगः । बलवान्मन्दबलो वा तस्याधिपतिस्तु कारको ज्ेयः ।३।।

3. The house occupied by the lord of the 9th and the planet occupying the 9th will have a say on one’s fortune. Their strength or weakness will indicate the fortunes.

Notes: Who is the indicator of fortunes in a given case? Note where the 9th lord (as arrived at as per indications given in sloka 2) is in the horoscope and the planet occupying the said 9th house. If these plancts are endowed with strength one will enjoy fortunes and if they be devoid of strength there will be obstructions to fortunes. For example, the 9th lord should be in good bhavas like ascendant, 4th, 7th, 10th, 5th etc., and avoid 6th, 8th, 12th etc. He should not be combust, nor be in fall, in inimical house and such Navamsas. He should be in good aspects. Then only will good fortunes wed the native easily. Also note the planet occupying the 9th house. If this occupant be ruler of evil houses, then it is defect for fortune. He should not be debilitated, combust etc., being in the 9th. Also, the occupant should preferably be a natural benefic so that one enjoys lasting and easily made fortunes. Even if the planet in the 9th be a natural malefic like Saturn, Mars etc, if he is a yogakarka for the ascendant, then also good results will mature.

## स्वस्वामिदृष्टयुषतं स्वदेशफलदायकं मुनिमिर्ष्तम् ।

## अन्येन सहितवृष्टं परदेशफलप्रदं भवति भाग्यम् ॥४।।

4. If the 9th house is aspected or occupied by its own lord, the native will make his fortunes in his own country.

If others aspect or join the house in question, the dawn of fortune will be elsewhere.

Notes: The native will flourish in his own birth place if the 9th is related to its lord by aspect, conjunction etc. If by others, he will acquire his fortunes elsewhere.

दुश्चिष्यगतो भाग्यं पञ्चमभवनस्थितो प्रहः पश्येत् । होरागतश्च बलवान् येषां ते मानवाः श्रेष्ठाः ।५।।

5. The native will be extremely lucky if a planet lends full aspect to the 9th from the 3rd, 5th or ascendant. The planet in question should be strong in the process.

Notes: The author discusses here the 9th house to be in full aspect from certain houses so that the native will be excellent in the matter ot lortunes. He has specitically used the word “full" in the matter of aspect as only full aspect is potential and not others. Jupiter is the only planet that can fully aspect the 9th from the ascendant or from the 5th. And from the 3rd, every planet can lend its full aspect. The hint here is that the 5th or the ascendant be occupied by Jupiter or any planet be in the 3rd. It does, however, not mean that simply Jupiter in the said place or anyone in the 3rd house will ensure good futures. In the process the planet should be endowed with strength. If it be weak, the fortunes will be only meagre, or even adverse.

केवगुरौ भाग्यस्थे मन्त्री रविवीक्षिते नृपतितुल्यः । भोगी कान्तः शशिना काञ्चनभाग् मवति भं मेन ।६।। सौम्येन धनी ज्ञेयः सितेन गोवाह्नार्थसंयुषतः । सौरेण स्थावरभाक् दृष्टे खरमहिवसंयुक्तः ।।७।।

6-7. JUPITER IN THE 9th AND ASPECTS THERETO: If Jupiter is placed in the 9th, the native will become a minister. If the said Jupiter in the 9th is aspected by the Sun, he will be equal to a king; if by the Moon he will be splendorous and will enjoy pleasures; if by Mars, he will be endowed with gold; if by Mercury affluent; if by Venus he

will have quadrupeds, conveyances and wealth and if by Saturn he will possess immovable properties, asses and buffaloes.

Notes: The occupation of Jupiter in the 9th with strength is a felicitous augury for acquiring powerful positions. The other relcvant effects have also been laid down in chapter 30. Further if Jupiter in the 9th is in aspect to the 9th lord one will enjoy royal status, will acquire paternal inheritance and will hold prominent positions in government level. The Moon aspecting the said Jupiter will bestow all kinds of pleasures. Mars in this context will give riches through gold, land, houses etc. Mercury will give plenty of wealth, income through teaching, consultancies and the like if aspecting the said Jupiter. Saturn or Venus related by aspect will prove bencficial for acquiring wealth through quadrupeds. Particularly Saturn is favourable to deal with immovable properties, real estates, house building and the like while Venus in particular will be advantageous in aspccting Jupiter for gains in dealing with conveyances, like manufacture/sales/repair etc., of automobiles and the like.

ऐश्वर्यरत्नकाञ्चनसाहसभागुतमश्च वीर्येण । रविरुधिराभ्यां दृष्टे वाहनपरिवारवानपुरुषः ।८।। चन्द्रार्काभ्यां दृष्टे सुसमृद्धो बल्लभः पितृजनन्योः । श्यातो नरेन्द्रतुल्यो बहुदारसमन्वितः पुरुषः ।।६। ललितः कान्तः सुभगो वरयुवतिविभूषणार्थसम्पन्नः । बुधसूर्याभ्यां दृष्टे काव्यकलापण्डितः प्राशः ॥ १०। उत्सवसमाजशीलो गोमहिषाजवरवारणोपेतः । रविसितवृष्टे जीवे भाग्यस्ये स्याद्विनीतश्च ।११। अर्कीकभ्यां बृष्टे देशपुरश्रेणिनायकः स्यातः । प्रात्नो गुणवा सघनो निधिनायः संग्रहणशीलः ॥१२। 8-12. JUPITER IN THE 9th IN ASPECT TO TWO PLAN-

ETS: If the 9th house is occupled by Jupiter and aspected

by both the Sun and Mars, the native will be endowed with sovereignty, precious stones, gold, courage, excellence, strength, conveyances and servants. If the aspecting pair is the Sun and the Moon, he will be blessed with plentifulness (of all things), be devoted to his parents, be famous, be equal to a king and will have plurality of wives. If the Sun and Mercury jointly aspect the Jupiter in the 9th, he will be graceful, splendorous, fortunate, be endowed with an excellent wife, ornaments and wealth and will be expert in poetry and arts. If the aspecting pair consists of the Sun and Venus, one will be festive in disposition, will have social (wide) activities and will be endowed with cows, buffalocs, elephants etc. If the Sun and Saturn aspect Jupiter in the 9th, one will be head of a country, town or group, þe famous, learned, virtuous, affluent and will have a tendency of accumulating.

Notes: In these 5 slokas, the effects of Jupiter placed in the 9th house and in aspect to any two planets are described. When two planets should be together to aspect Jupiter, it can only be possible from the 3rd by usual standards of aspects. However, in the case of Mars or Saturn constituting a planet of the pair, Mars can be elsewhere just as Saturn but simultaneously aspect Jupiter. For example, the Sun can be in the 3rd and Mars in the 2nd or the 6th counted from the ascendant in which case both the Sun and Mars can aspect the 9th house. The aspect from the Sun and Mars to Jupiter in the 9th will be very favourable for Aries, Scorpio, Cancer, Leo, Sagittarius or Pisces ascendants as for these ascendants, the 3 planets in question are very favourable. Similar inferences can be made by suitable extension of the rules.

gणिe : in sloka 12 in normal sense means a sense of accumulating (wealth) or a tendency of providence. Saturn joining the Sun in aspecting the 9th house placed Jupiter will give a tendency not to spend liberally and hence the native will be in a position to accumulate sufficient wealth. However, the word galso means “adulterty”. The planet in the 9th, the planet aspecting the 9th, the lord

of the 9th etc., have a say on the character of the native as well, apart from fortunes. Saturn’s aspect on the 9th will give a tendency to commit adultery. Jupiter alone in the 9th will, however, combat such a tendency but if in being in aspect to Saturn (and the Sun) Jupiter be not well disposed, the native may indulge in immoral acts.

सेनाचार्यः स्फीतो मन्त्री वा जायते गुरौ भाग्ये । दृष्टे कुजचन्द्राभ्यां नानाविधसौर्यभाजनं सुभगः ।१३।। उत्तमगृह्शयनानां भोगी तेजोन्वितः क्षमाप्रतिमः । चन्द्रबुधार्यां बृष्टे जातः पुरुषोऽतिमतियुक्तः ॥१४।। आद्यः कर्मोदुष्तः शूरः परवारबान् सुतविहीनः । इन्दुसिताग्यां वृष्टे माग्यनुहे स्याद्गुरी जातः ॥१५।। मदबहुलः स्थिरजीवी परदेशरतो विवादशीलः स्यात् । अनृतवचनो गुणोनः शशियमवृष्टे गुरी चाच्ये ।१६।।

13-16. JUPITER IN THE 9th IN ASPECTS TO TWO PLANETS (continued): If Jupiter occupies the 9th in aspect to both Mars and the Moon, one will be an army chief or a minister and be endowed with various kinds of happiness. And if by both Mercury and the Moon, he will enjoy excellent houses, sleeping comforts and pleasures, be splendorous, patient and very intelligent. And if by the Moon and Venus, he will be very rich, dutiful, valorous, attached to others’ wives and be devoid of sons. And if both by the Moon and Saturn, he will be very lascivious, endowed with permanent livelihood, will live in foreign places, be argumentative, be a llar and be not virtuous.

सुप्राज्ञोऽतिसुशीलः सुगुणो विद्वास्पृह्रोतवाश्यश्च । सुरुचिरवेषो जीवे कुजबुधवृष्टे भवेद्भाग्ये ।।१७।। धनवान्वद्यायुष्तो विदेशगः सात्विकोऽतिनिपुणश्च । क्षितितनयभार्गवाभ्यां दृष्टे ऋरूरो नरो जातः ॥१्।।

नीचः पिसुनो द्वेध्यो विदेशगश्वलजनेः समायुक्तः । मौमाकिष्यां वृष्टे भाग्यग्हे सुरगुरो जातः ।१८।। शिल्पज्ञोऽतिसुशीलो विद्वान्सुभगो गृह्हीतवाष्यश्च । सुरुविरवेषो जीवे बुधसितदृष्टे भन्देद्भाग्ये ।।२०। सुभगो विद्वान्वय्ता ललितः शूरः सुली विनीतश्च। जीवे भाग्योपगते बुधाकदृष्टे पुमान्भवति ।।२१।। वैबंगुरौ माग्यस्ये काव्यार्कजवीक्षिते पुमान्मवति। राजेश्वर राष्ट्राणां \_\_\_ पुरोगमो घनसमृद्धश्च ।।२२।।

17-22. JUPITER IN THE 9th ASPECT TO TWO PLANETS (continued): If Jupiter is in the 9th and is in aspect to both Mars and Mercury, one will be highly learned, very virtuous, will keep up his word and will have a pleasing appearance. If Mars and Venus be the aspecting pair, one will be wealthy, learned, will live in foreign countries, be sincere, very skilful and cruel. Mars and Saturn being the pair denotes that the native will be base, talebearing, spiteful, llving in foreign places and will be in the company of unsteady people. if Mercury and Venus jointly aspect Jupiter in the 9th, one will be an artisan, be very virtuous, learned, fortunate, will keep up his promise and will possess a pleasing appearance. Mercury and Saturn being such a pair, the native will be fortunate, learned, eloquent in speech, pleasing, courageous, happy and modest.

राष्यधियेन च वृष्टे जीवे भाग्याश्रिते नृपं ज्ञेयम् ।

एभिः कथित्तरदुष्टे फलमिदमन्यरसन्बुष्टे ।२३।।

23. Jupiter in the 9th in aspect to 9th lord will make one a king. The effects stated in connection with Jupiter’s position in the 9th and aspects thereof are valid only when no other planets (than the mentioned) aspect.

Notes: The effects of two planets aspecting Juplter in the 9th house as stated will come to pass in full unless

there is no interference from a different planet. The dignity of the planets aspecting the 9th house and of Jupiter will also count. If Jupiter alone is in the 9th and is exclusively aspected by the lord of the 9th, the native will acquire royal status.

## उत्तमर्पो गुणवान् तेजस्वी पार्थिवो महाविभवः ।

## देवगुरौ भाग्यस्थे सर्वग्रहवीक्षिते भवति ।।२४।।

24. If all the planets aspect Jupiter posited in the 9th, the native will possess excellent guise, virtues, splendour, sovereignty and abundant riches.

भाग्ये शुभगगनसदो बलिनो राज्यप्रदास्तु त्रिज्ञेयाः ।

स्थाबरधनधान्यकरा

धर्मायुर्वर्धनार्चब ।२५।

25. If the 9th is occupied by a strong benefic, it confers kingdom, according to the wise, in addition to movable properties, wealth, grains, righteousness and increased longevity.

## नीचारिराशिसंस्थाः पापा भाग्ये न सौम्यसंदृष्टाः ।

## बुर्बलमधनं कुर्युविगतस्याति नरं मलिनम् ।२६।।

26. AFFLICTION TO THE 9th: If the 9th house gives place to a debilitated planet or a combust planet or a malefic planet and be without a benefic's aspect, the native will be devoid of (moral) strength, wealth and fame and be dirty.

स्वे स्वे भवने पुंसां ऋरूरा भाग्यर्षसंस्थिता ये स्युः ।

ज्ञेयास्त उत्तमशुभा बहुतरगुणसंयुताः शुभदुष्टाः ।।२७।।

27. If the malefic in the 9th is the owner of that house then it proves auspicious. And with a benefic's aspect it is more auspicious.

पूर्णन्दुयुते भाग्ये वक्राकिबुधाः प्रधानवीर्याश्व । व्यस्ता वाऽ्य समस्ताः प्रधाननृपसंभवो ज्ञेयः ।२८।

28. If Full Moon is in the 9th while Mars, Saturn and Mercury are strong the native is born to a king.

Notes: The Full Moon should be in the 9th. The other 3 planets mentioned in the sloka should be strong whose position is, however, not described. In any case Mercury cannot be in the 9th along with Full Moon and will be elsewhere.

सकलगगनगेहा: स्वोच्चगा भाग्यराशौ धनकनकसमृद्धं श्रेष्ठमुत्पावयन्ति । अथ शुभविहगेन्द्रस्त्र दृष्टे नरेन्द्रं विनिहतरिपुपक्षं विव्यकारग्ति सुकीतिम् ।।२६।

29. If a planet is exalted in the 9th, an excellent person is born with plentifulness of wealth and gold. And if a benefic aspects the exalted planet in question, the native will be a supreme ruler, destroyer of the band of his enemies, will possess divine splendour and great fame.

Notes: An exalted planet occupying the 9th house will augment the overall fortunes of the native and can work as a powerfui antidote to many planctary blemishes. If the exalted planet placed in the 9th is aspected by Jupiter, Mercury, Venus or a yogakaraka of the horoscope, the native will be very greatly fortunate, exceedingly affluent and will be among the most well-placed few. He will be endowed with divineness to which many submit respectfully. He will live for a long time enjoying all the amenities and vast happiness.

सूर्यश्चन्त्रसहायो भाग्ये स्वल्पायुषं नर कुश्ते । नयनव्याधितमादयं सुभयं कलहप्रियं चाषि ।।३०।

भानुर्वक्रसमेत्ले नानादुःखान्वितं नर कुरुते । कलहप्रियं प्रवण्ड शूरं नृपवल्लभं निपुणम् ॥३१।। रविसहितः शशितनयो निपुणं दुःखान्वितं बहुविपक्षम् । जनयति भाग्ये पुरुषं नानारोगः परिगृहीतर्म । ३२। सुरगुरुसहितः सूर्यो भाग्ये कुर्याद्धनाम्बितं पुरुवम्। पितरं च धनसमृद्धं दीर्धायुषमार्यमतिगूरम् । ३३।। शुक्र्सहायः सूर्यो व्याधितवेहं नरं कुरुते। प्रियगन्धमाल्यभूषणवस्त्रालंकारसंयुतं भाग्ये ।३४।। सूर्यः सौरसहायो धनिनं नेत्रातुरं कलहनिव्ठम् । ब्याधितपितरं कुर्ते भाग्ये स्वल्पायुवं पुरुषम् ।।३५।।

30-35. EFFECTS OF OTHER PLANETS IN CONJUNC- TION WITH THE SUN IN THE 9th HOUSE: If the Sun and the Moon are together in the 9th, the native will have a short life, will be afflicted by eye diseases, be rich, fortunate and fond of quarrels. If Mars and the Sun be together in the 9th, the native will experience many kinds of miseries, be fond. of quarrels, be flerce, courageous, dear to king and be skilful. If Mercury and the Sun are together in the 9th, one will be skilful, miserable, will have many enemies and suffer from many diseases. If Jupiter and the Sun be in conjunction in the 9th, the native will be endowed with wealth, will obtain abundant paternal wealth, be long-lived, very courageous and be a worthy person. Should Venus and the Sun be together, in the 9th, he will incur diseases, and will be endowed with scents, garlands, ornaments, robes and decorative articles. If Saturn and the Sun be so placed one will be rich, will suffer from eye diseases, be fond of quarrels and be short-lived.

चन्रो रुधिरसहायो माग्यं समुपेत्य मातरं हुन्यात्।

कुर्याच्च विकलगात्रं सत्रणयेहं समृद्धं च ।।३९१।।

खन्त्रः स्वसुतसमेतः शास्त्रत्तं पण्डितं विकलयेहम्। अनयत्युश्मपुरवं बहुवाचं विश्रुतं चैव ।३७।। सुर्गुर्सहिते बगद्दे भाग्ये प्रवरः प्रसूयते पुरुषः। सौभाग्यधनसमृद्धः सर्वत्र सुखान्वितो धीरः । ३८।। खन्द्रो भार्गवसहितो भाग्यगृहे व्याधितं नरं कुर्ते। कुलटारपति समृद्ध मातृसपत्नीप्रदं सचिववश्यम् ।।३९। रविसुतसहितश्चन्द्रो नवमे राशी विक्ष्टधर्माणम् । जनयति मनुजं पापं माता च कुलच्युता भवति ।४०।।

36-40. EFFECTS OF OTHERS JOINING THE MOON IN THE 9th: If Mars joins the Moon in the 9th, one will be devoid of mother (i.e. will lose his mother early), will have a deformed body and with ulcers, and be wealthy. If Mercury joins the Moon, he will have knowledge of Sastras, be scholarly, will have some physical deformity, will be excellent among men, will talk much and be famous. If Jupiter joins the Moon, he will be distinguished, will be fortunate, will be endowed with abundant riches, will be always happy and be courageous. If Venus be the accompanying planet, he will suffer from diseases, will marry an unchaste lady, be plentiful, will have a step-mother and will be in a minister’s custody (i.e. will serve a significant person). Should Saturn join the Moon in the 9th, he will be devoid of moral merits, be sinful and will have a fallen mother.

रुधिरः सोमजसहितो भाग्यक्षे जनयति प्रधानं च। नित्योद्विग्नं सुभगं भोगयुतं शास्त्रकुशलं ख ।।४१।। भौमः सुरगुर्युक्तो भाग्ये धनधान्यभामिनं पुरुवम् । पूज्यं व्याधितवेहं क्लिष्टं ब्रणवीक्षितं प्रसवे ।४२।। चार्गबसहितः क्षितिजः परबेशरतं विवादिनं ऋरूरम्। स्त्रीट्रेविणं कृतघ्नं जनयति मिष्याभिमानं च ।।४३।।

## वापं मलिनाचारं परदाररतं विनष्टधनसौर्यम्। कुजरविजौ भाग्यपूहे स्वजनविहीनं नरं कुर्ते ॥४४।

41-44. EFFECTS OF MARS JOINING OTHERS IN THE 9th: If Mars be in the company of Mercury in the 9th, one will be chief of men, will be always emotional, be fortunate, will enjoy pleasures and be skilful in Sastras. If it is Jupiter with whom Mars in conjunct in the 9th, one will be endowed with wealth and grains, be honourable, be troubled by sickness and will be pained with bodily wounds. If Venus is that planet joining Mars in the 9th, one will live in foreign places, be argumentative, cruel, will hate women, be ungrateful and will indulge in falsehood. If Saturn is with Mars in the 9th, one will be sinful, will be of dirty conduct, be interested in other housewives, be deprived of wealth and happiness and will not be with his own men.

सौम्यः सुरगुर्सहितः शास्त्रज्ञं पण्डितं धनसमृद्धम्। त्रियवादिनं कलाज्ञं प्रभविव्णुमह्त्तरं भाग्ये ।।४५।। चुगुसृतसहितः सौम्यः स्यातं च सुपण्डितं धीरम्। सुभगं ववनसमर्यं जनयति गीतप्रियं भाग्ये ।४९।। सूर्यजसहितः सौम्यो व्याधितमाइच प्रियातिवितं निपुणम् । जनयति भाग्ये पुरुषं सङ्गेध्यं बहुकयं चंब ।४७।।

45-47. MERCURY IN CONJUNCTION WITH OTHERS IN THE 9th: If Mercury is with Jupiter in the 9th, one will be learned in Sastras, be a scholar, be wealthy, will speak sweetly, be an artisan and an efficacious and excellent person. Venus and Mercury in the 9th denote that one will be famous, very learned, valorous, fortunate, skilful in speech and be fond of songs. If Saturn and Mercury are in ( the 9th in conjunction, he will suffer from diseases, be very rich, be endowed with dear and near, be skilful, envious and be garrulous.

शुक्रः सुरगुर्सहितो भाग्यगृहस्थो नराधिपं कुर्ते। चिरजीविनं सुवाक्यं नानाविधसौस्यसम्पन्नम् ।४८। जीवः सौरसहायो भाग्ये धनरत्नभागिनं ‘फुर्ते। पूज्यं व्याधितदेहं स्वजनविहीनं सदा पुरुषम् ।।४६।।

48-49. JUPITER JOINING OTHERS IN THE 9th: If Venus and Jupiter be together in the 9th, one will be a leader of men, be longlived, eloquent and will be endowed with many kinds of happiness and wealth. If Jupiter and Saturn are in the 9th, the native will enjoy wealth and gems, be honourable, will have diseases to trouble him, and be devoid of his own men.

## सौरसहायः शुत्रो व्याधितबेहं नर कुर्ते। बहुपुत्रं नृपतीष्ट यशस्विनं शीलसम्पन्नम् ॥५०।।

50. VENUS-SATURN CONJUNCTION IN THE 9th: This conjunction denotes that the native will be sickly, will have many sons, be dear to king, famous and virtuous.

## एवं स्थानविशेषदुष्टिविशेषेश्च निपुणमधिगम्य।

ब्रूयात्फलनिर्वेशं शास्त्रादनुर्पतः प्राजः ।५१।।

51. In analysing the said effects, the signs and aspects involved should be skilfully understood according to Sastras and results declared accordingly, so say the learned.

## सत्रणगात्रं र्षं मृतपितरं मातृर्वाजितं कुर्युः।

बाल्ये क्षुद्रं देव्यं हिसं शशिरुधिरमानवो भाग्ये ।५२।।

52. EFFECTS OF 3 PLANETS IN CONJUNCTION IN 9th: If the Sun, Moon and Mars are together in the 9th, one will lose his parents in his very boyhood, be base, odious, and hurtful.

रविचन्द्रबुधा भाग्ये वलीवाकारं सुदुःखितं कुर्युः।

सर्वजनार्ना ह्वेष्यं विक्रान्तं सत्यवचनं च ।।५३।।

53. If the Sun, Moon and Mercury join in the 9th, the native will be a eunuch in appearance, be miserable, will hate all people and be valorous and truthful.

## चन्द्रविवाकरपुरवो नवमै पुरुषस्य सम्भवे यह्द।

स भवत्युत्तमपुर्वो वाह्नधनसौस्यसम्पन्नः ॥ ५४।।

54. If the Sun, the Moon and Jupiter join in the 9th, one will be excellent and will possess conveyances, wealth and happiness.

## रविचन्द्रसिता नवमे सत्रीकलहैनष्टसर्वधनसौस्यम् ।

नृपसंमतं नयज्ञं जनयन्ति नरं प्रियालापम् ॥५५।।

55. If Venus, the Moon and the Sun be together in the 9th, one will lose all his happiness and wealth due to quarrels with females, be dear to king, will have knowledge of politics and will be an eloquent speaker.

## सूर्यनिशाकरसौरा नवमे राशी नरं सदा कुर्युः।

## प्रबलं चण्डाचारं परमृत्यं लोकविद्विटस्।५६।।

56. If the Sun, the Moon and Saturn join in the 9th, one will be very strong, will have cruel conduct, will serve others and will hate others.

## रविभौमबुधा नवमे कुर्वन्ति नरं प्रियालापम्।

भुजगमिवातिशुद्धं समरपरं निष्ठुरं प्रवासरतम् ।५७।।

57. If the Sun, Mars and Mercury be in the 9th, one will be an eloquent speaker, be akin to a snake when in anger, be fond of quarrels, be hard-hearted and will be interested in living in foreign countries.

## रविगुर्वका नवमे जनयन्ति नरं सदोद्युक्तम्। बेवपितृपूजनरतं समृद्धदार . गुणोपेतम् ।५८।।

58. If the Sun, Jupiter and Mars are together in the 9th, one will be always diligent, be intent on worshipping his father and God, will have an affluent wife and be virtuous.

## कलहप्रियं कुलीनं कन्यानां दूषकं च चपलं च। विवसकरवक्रशुका नवमे देव्यं गरं कुर्युः ॥५६।।

89. If the Sun, Mars and Venus be together in the 9th, one will be fond of quarrels, is of a high descent, will spoil virgins, be fickle-minded and be odious.

## साहसिकमति्षुद्रं लोकदेध्यं प्रियानृतं क्रूरम्।

## पित्रा रहितं बाये कुर्युवक्राकिमानवो नवमे ।६०।।

60. If Mars, Saturn and the Sun join in the 9th, one will be valorous, very mean, will hate people, be fond of speaking lies, be cruel, and be fatherless from boyhood.

## रविबुध्युरवो नबमे भाग्यसमेतं घनान्वितं सुभगम् ।

## नृपतिप्रियं सुबेवं जनयन्ति नरं सुधौरं च ।।६१।

61. Should the Sun, Mercury and Jupiter join in the 9th, one will be endowed with fortunes, wealth and happiness, be dear to king, be appealing in appearance and be courageous.

## रविबुध्शुक्रा भाग्ये जनयन्ति नरं प्रकाशिनं फान्तम् ।

## रिपुपक्षपरिक्षीणं नृपतिसमं सारबन्तं च ।६२।।

62. Should Venus join the Sun and Mercury in the 9th, one will be splendorous, famous, will destroy his enemies, be equal to a king and be rich.

## वरदाररतं पापं प्रवासशीलं च निपुणमतिप्रष्टम्। आनृतिकमर्ववपरं रविबुधसोरा नरं भाग्ये ।६३।।

63. Should the Sun, Mercury and Saturn join in the 9th, one will be fond of others' women, be sinner, be interested in living in foreign places, be skilful, be a profligate, be a llar, and be unfortunate.

## चाप्यगृहे रविशुक्रौ जीवश्च नरं सुपण्डितं कान्तम्।

बहुविषयमति वीरं जनयन्ति सुमेधसं प्राज्ञम् ॥६४।।

64. Should the Sun, Venus and Jupiter join in the 9th, one will be very learned, splendorous, will know many things and be intelligent.

## त्रिदशगुरुसौरसूर्या नवमे यरयेह जायमानस्य।

स भवेदुत्तमवीर्यो राजा धनवान्गुर्णः समृद्धश्च ।६५।।

65. The conjunction of the Sun, Jupiter and Saturn in the 9th is indicative that the native will be highly valorous, be a king, be wealthy, virtuous and plentiful.

कान्तिविहीनं मलिनं भूपतिपरिदश्डितं विभवहीनम् ।

जनयन्ति नरं भाग्ये रविशुक्रशनेश्वरा मूर्लम् ।६६।।

66. If the Sun, Venus and Saturn be together in the 9th, one will be devoid of splendour, be dirty, punished by the king, be devoid of riches and be foolish.

ध्नकनकरत्नमाजं जनयन्ति शशिजभूमिजाः पुर्वम् ।

प्रयमे वयसि च तप्तं भाग्यपृहे सर्वमावेन । ६७।।

67. The company formed by the Moon, Mercury and Mars in the 9th denotes that the subject will enjoy wealth, gold and gems and will be miserable throughout his boyhood.

सौमनिशाकरजीवाः कुर्बन्ति नरं जितेन्द्रियं प्राज्ञम् ।

गुरुदेवमक्तिनिरतं विद्याधनमागिनं स सुभवम् ।।६दा।

68. If the Moon, Mars and Jupiter join in the 9th, one will conquer his five senses, be learned, will honour elders and gods and will be endowed with learning and wealth and be fortunate.

ब्रणितांगमरूपं वा प्रभक्षणं स्त्रीप्रियं युवतिवश्यम् ।

## युवतिविनाशितसारं मृगुशशिवक्रा नरं नवमे । ६६।

69. If Venus, Moon and Mars are together in the 9th, one will have a wounded body, be ugly, will eat a lot, be fond of women, be at their disposal and will lose his strength (or wealth) through them.

## व्यापन्नमातृवंशं क्षुद्रं बाल्ये निराकृतं मात्रा।

## सौरो भौमश्चन्द्रो जनयन्ति नराधमं नवमे ।७०।

70. Should Saturn, Mars and the Moon be in the 9th, one will not possess a good maternal history, be base, be given up by his mother in boyhood and will be mean among men.

## गुर्बुधचन्द्रा नवमे कुलवंशविवर्धनं कुर्युः।

## आवार्यं बहुमित्रं नृर्पात बहुसाधनोपेतम् ॥७१।।

71. If the Moon, Mercury and Jupiter are together in the 9th one will bring credit to his descent, will be a preceptor, will possess many friends and will be a king endowed with many things.

मातृसपतनीजनकं प्रमुदितमानान्वितं प्रचुरमित्रम् ।

कुर्बन्ति सामशीलं भूगुबुधबन्द्रा नरं भाग्ये ।७२।।

72. If the Moon, Mercury and Venus be together in the 9th, one will have a stepmother, be endowed with delight and honour, will have many friends and will be gentle or pacific.

## शशिबुधसौरा नवमे क्रूराचार सुविक्रमं मलिनम्।

## जनयन्ति कुत्सितवियं संप्रामपराङमुखं बीनम् ।७३।।

73. If the Moon, Mercury and Saturn are together in the 9th, one will be bad in conduct, be courageous, dirty, narrow-minded, be averse to war and be poor.

## चन्द्रबृहस्पतिशुक्का नवमे यस्येह जायमानस्य ।

## स भवति महोपतुस्यो नृपतिकुले भूपतिश्चेब ।७४।

74. If the Moon, Jupiter and Venus be in the 9th, one will be equal to a king and be a king.

## शशिगुरुसौरा नत्रमे कुर्वन्ति नरं प्रियालापम्।

## सत्यन्नतं सुशीलं विस्यातं सर्वशास्त्रकुशलं च ।७५।

75. If Juplter, Saturn and the Moon be in the 9th one will speak sweetly, be truthful, virtuous, famous and skilful in all Sastras.

## शुश्रेनदुयमा नवमे कुविवृत्ति योविदनुरक्तम्।

## कुर्युर्नुजमपापं क्तक्कत्यं \_\_ लोकविस्यातम् ।७६।

76. If Venus, the Moon and Saturn join in the 9th, one will be in agricultural profession, be interested in women, will not indulge in sinful acts, will accomplish his objects (Kritakritya), and be world-famous.

## तेजस्विनं विशोकं विद्वांसं वाक्स्थिरं विशिष्टं वा।

## कुजबुधजीवा नवमे कुर्वन्ति च मण्डलाधिपतिम् ॥७७।

77. If Mars, Mercury and Jupiter join in the 9th, one will be splendorous, devoid of grief, be learned, firm in his word, famous and be head of a zone.

## बहुविषयपाति स्यातं नरेन्द्रसत्कारसत्हतं चण्डम् ।

## कुजबुधशुका भाग्ये कुवन्ति नरं सदासवतम् ।।७८।।

78. If Mars, Mercury and Venus are conjunct in the 9th, one will head many provinces or affairs, be famous, honoured by the king, be fierce and be always zealous.

## परवञ्चनासु निपुणं तमोधिकं सर्वशास्त्रमतिबाह्याम् ।

## बुद्भौमयमा नबमे कुर्युः परतर्कसंमूढम् ।।७६।।

79. If Mars, Mercury and Saturn join in the 9th, one will be skilful in cheating others, will be highly gloomy, will not be in a position to understand Sastras (i.e. be not so learned) and cannot understand others'notions.

## बृधगुरुशुक्का भाग्ये जनयन्ति मनं सुरोपमं विशदम्।

विश्यातं नरनाथं विद्वांसं धर्मंशीलं च ।।८०।।

80. If Mercury, Jupiter and Venus be together in the 9th one will be equal to a god ( g ), be spotless, famous, kingly, learned and virtuous.

## शुश्शनेश्वरशशिजा नवमस्या जातकं प्रकुर्वन्ति ।

मेराविनं प्रकारं सुरुचिरवाक्यं सुक्षोपेतम् ।।१।

81. Should Mercury, Venus and Saturn join in the 9th, one will be intelligent, splendorous, eloquent and happy.

शनिशुकामरगुरशो भाग्यगृहस्या नर प्रकुर्जन्ति।

प्रचुरान्नपानविभवं सुभगं सुखितं सुरुषं च ।८२।

82. One with Saturn, Venus and Jupiter in the 9th, will have plenty of food, drinks and riches, be fortunate, happy and beautiful.

रविचन्द्रसौमशशिजा जन्मनि भाग्यर्षगा नरें कुर्युः।

## धनिनं विद्याहुशनं सुत्यं नृपसंन्तं चंत्र ।८३।

## FOUR PLANETS IN CONJUNCTION IN 9th:

83. If the Sun, the Moon, Mars and Mercury join in the 9th, one will be rich, learned, fortunate and dear to king.

शुक्रेन्बुमौमरवयो मायाचतुरं स्त्रदारसन्तुष्टम् ।

जनयन्ति सदा भाग्ये पुरुषं बहुनीचकर्माणम् ।।८४।।

84. If Venus, Moon, Sun and Mars are together in the 9th, one will be cunning, will be happy with his wife and will indulge in many mean acts.

शशिसुरगुरुबुधर्यो जन्मनि भाग्यर्षमाश्रिताः कुर्युः।

पुर्षं प्रधानमचलं नरेन्द्रपूज्यं तथा हृष्टम् ।८५।।

85. One who has the Moon, Jupiter, Mercury and the Sun in the 9th, will be principal, steady, honoured by the king and be delighted.

## चन्द्रबुधशुक्ररवयो ध्रनेश्वर धामिक समृद्धं च।

जनयन्ति नवमसंस्थाः पुरुषं प्रियवादिनं शान्तम् ।८६।।

86. One with the Moon, Venus, Mercury and Sun in the 9th, will be a lord of wealth, morally meritorious, plentiful, eloquent in speech and be calm.

तरणियुधचन्द्रसौरा जन्मनि नवमाश्रिता नरं कुर्युः।

धनिनं कान्तं भूपं बहुपशुमन्तं गुणाढचश्च ।।८७।

87. If the Sun, Mercury, Moon and Saturn be together

in the 9th, one will be rich, splendorous, kingly, will have many quadrupeds and will possess rich qualities.

रविगुरुबुधभृगुतनया जन्मनि नवमे नरं प्रकुर्वन्ति। बेवपितृपूजनपरं समृद्धदारं गुणोपेतम् ।।८८।

88. One with the Sun, Jupiter, Mercury and Venus will honour God and father, will have a rich wife and be virtuous.

शुक्र्जमौमसूर्या नवमे जनयन्ति निष्ठुरं सुमगम्।

साहसनिरतं विधनं रिनुपक्षकापितविभवं च ।।८९।।

89. If Venus, Mars, Mercury and the Sun join together in the 9th, one will be hard-hearted, beautiful, interested in valorous acts, be not wealthy and will lose his wealth through his enemies.

रविसौरिचान्द्रिभौमा नवमे यस्येह जायमानस्य।

स भवति परदाररतो विनष्टकोशः सदा दीनः ।।६०।।

90. If the Sun, Mars, Mercury and Saturn be together in the 9th, one will be interested in others’ wives, will incur loss of wealth and be always pitiable.

शुक्रगुरुमौमरवयो लोके देष्यं पिपासार्तम् ।

जनयन्ति नवमसंस्थाः कन्यानां दूदकं चपलचित्तम् ।६१।

91. One with the Sun, Venus, Mars and Jupiter in the 9th, will hate people, be troubled by thirst, will spoil virgins and be fickle-minded.

गुरुभौमसौरसूर्या नवमे सुख्याजितं सदोद्युक्तव् । जनयन्ति नरं चण्डं विक्रमयुक्तं महासर्वम् ।।६२।।

92. If the Sun, Mars, Jupiter and Saturn are in the 9th at birth, one will be devoid of happiness, be diligent, fierce

and courageous.

## रविबुधजीवसिता: स्गुर्नवमे यस्येह जायमानस्य। स भवत्युत्तमपुर्वो

धनकनर्कश्वर्यसम्पन्नः ॥ १ ३।।

93. If the Sun, Jupiter, Venus and Mercury are in the 9th, one will be an excellent person endowed with wealth, gold and sovereignty.

## भानुजरविबुधगुरवो नवमे जनयन्ति मानवं विधनम्।

## वावं परदाररतं विद्धिष्टं नीचकर्माणम् ।।६४।।

94. If the Sun, Mercury, Jupiter and Saturn are together in the 9th, one will be devoid of wealth, be sinful, be interested in other house-wives, spiteful and will indulge in mean acts.

## बुधरविजरविसिताः स्युर्भाग्यस्याने नर सुभगम्।

## जनयन्ति धनसमेतं सत्यरतं लोकविस्यातम् ॥६५।।

95. Should the Sun, Mercury, Venus and Saturn be in 9th, one will be fortunate, wealthy, truthful and worldfamous.

## रविगुरसितानुसुता जन्मनि नवमर्षंगा नरं कुर्युः।

सत्यवरतं सुवाष्यं गुरुद्विजातिथिषु सब्-मष्तम् ।९६।।

96. With the Sun, Jupiter, Venus and Saturn in the 9th, one will be truthful, eloqucnt and will honour elders, Brahmins and guests.

## चन्द्र्कुजसुरेव्या जनयत्ति नरं परिच्द्समृद्धम्।

बाल्ये मातृवियुक्तं घनान्वितं संस्थिता भाग्ये ।८७।।

97. If the Moon, Mars, Mercury and Jupiter are in the 9th, one will have plenty of robes, will lose his mother in boyhood and be wealthy.

भौमसितशशिजचन्द्रा माग्ये जनयन्ति तापसं स्यातम् ।

वाग्मिनमतिदातारं परलोकपर \_\_

महाप्राशम् ।।९८।।

98. With Mars, Venus, Mercury and the Moon in the 9th, one will practise penance, be famous, be a good speaker, be very charitable, ultra-mundane in disposition and be very learned.

## रविजबुधचन्द्रभौमा जनयन्ति नरं पराङ्मुखं दीनम् ।

## क्षुद्रं मायाचतुरं परदाररतं स्थिता भाग्ये ।९९।

99. If Saturn, Mercury, Moon and Mars are together in the 9th, one will be dejected poor, base, cunning and interested in others’ wives.

## भौमेन्दुशुक्जीवा नृपवंशभवं प्रधानमतिशूरम् ।

## विद्याधनसुसमृद्धं विश्यातं लोकसंमतं चंव ॥१००।

100. If Mars, Moon, Venus and Saturn, are together at birth, one is born in a royal family. He will be a principal person, be valorous, will be endowed with plenty of learning and money, be famous and dear to people.

## शशिवक्राकिसुरेव्या जनयन्ति नर पिपासार्तम् ।

## कसहप्रियं च नवमे सौभाग्यपरिच्छवातीतम् ।।१०१।

101. One with Moon, Mars, Saturn and Jupiter in the 9th, will be troubled by thirst, be fond of promoting quarrels and be plentiful in the matter of luck and robes.

## चन्दारमानुजसिता नदमे जनयत्ति निष्ठुरं पापम्।

## मायाविनं च पुरुषं शौवाचारविहीनं च ।१०२।

102. If the Moon, Mars, Saturn and Venus are together in the 9th, one will be hard-hearted, sinful, cunning and be bereft of cleanliness and behaviour.

सितगुर्शशिजशशांका जन्मनि माग्यर्षमाश्रिता धनिनम् ।

## जनयन्ति धर्मसक्तं नरं कलासु प्रसिद्धं च ।।१०३।

103. If Venus, Jupiter, Mercury and Moon are together in the 9th, one will be wealthy, be endowed with moral merits and be famous in arts.

## भानुजबुधगुरुचन्द्रा जनयन्ति नर परं सुनयम् ।

## भाग्यस्थिता समृद्धं नीतिज्ञं चारुवेषं च ।।१०४।।

104. One with Saturn, Mercury, Jupiter and Moon in the 9th, will be knowledgeable in politics and ethics and be pleasing in appearance.

## शनिशुक्रब्रुघशशांका जन्मनि भाग्यस्थिता नरं कुर्युः।

मेत्रात्रिनं प्रचण्डं जननीतिविशारद धन्यम् ।।१०५।।

105. With Saturn, Venus, Mercury and the Moon in the 9th, the native will be intelligent, fierce, will be an expert in democratic matters and be fortunate.

## चन्द्रशनिशुक्रजीवा जन्मनि नवमस्थिताः प्रकुर्वन्ति ।

मायाविनं प्रचण्डं नरेष्वजेयं नरं धीरम्।।१०६।

106. One with Moon, Saturn, Venus and Jupiter in the 9th, will be cunning, fierce, unconquerable and bold.

## भौमज्ञसूरिशनयो नवमस्थानोपगा नरं कुर्युः।

## रिपुपक्षपरिक्षीणं रणप्रचण्डं सुधीरं च ।१०७।।

107. If Mars, Mercury, Jupiter and Saturn be together in the 9th, one will destroy his enemies, be ficrce in battle and bold.

## भौमजशुक्रशनयः कुर्वन्ति दशानुगतं सुशीलं ख। जनयन्ति नवमसंस्या धनयुक्तं भक्तियुवतं च ।१०च।।

108. If Mars, Mercury, Venus and Saturn join together in 9th, one will be virtuous, wealthy and plous.

मौमभ्गुजीवरविजा जनयन्ति नरं धर्नः परित्यक्तम् ।

शुद्र व्याविरहितं विहीनसर्वं स्थिता भाग्ये ।।१०६।।

109. If Mars, Jupiter, Venus and Saturn are in the 9th, one will be devoid of wealth, be base, unkind and weak.

त्रिचतुःपञ्चखगेन्द्रास्तथा च षट् सप्त संस्थिता भाग्ये ।

प्रात्ययिक धनबन्तं कुर्यनुपति च बुधसहिताः ।११०१।

110. SPECIAL EFFECTS: If including Mercury there are 3 to 7 planets in the 9th, the native will be trustworthy, wealthy and be a king.

जनयन्ति भाग्यसंस्था गुरुसौम्य विर्वाजिता ग्रहाः पुरुवम् ।

## व्याधिप्रायमकान्तं जनहीनं बन्धनार्तमतिदीनम् ।।१११।।

111. If the 9th is occupied by other planets, without the company of Mercury or Jupiter, the native will be sickly, ugly, devoid of relatives, be troubled by imprisonments and will suffer penury.

Notes: Out of the many occupants of the 9th, one should be Jupiter or Mercury so that the evil concentration on the 9th and 3rd, apart from important houses of the horoscope getting hindered. Practically, many planets in the 9th will affect the horoscope adversely. So to say, the good effects of such conglommeration in the 9th will be far less, than the evils. This has been my personal experience. The author also states that at least Jupiter/Mercury be a participant in a combination of 3 to 7 planets to save the person from adversities like imprisonment, poverty etc.

## उक्तं बहुप्रकारं भाग्यभृहे बादरायणाविकतम् ।

ग्रह्योगेक्षणभार्वविचिन्त्य बुद्धचा बबेदन्यत् ॥ ११२।।

112. Preccptors like Badarayana have explained still detailed results arising out of conjunction or aspects relating to the 9th housc. One should understand these also and declare effects after using his wisdom.

इति कल्याणवरमंविरचितार्यां सारावत्यां भाग्यचिन्तानाम द्वातिशोऽध्यायः ।।३२।।

Thus ends the 32nd Chapter entitled “NINTH HOUSE AND EFFECTS THEREOF” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

A Glimpse of KERALA ASTROLOGY OR U - -

## अय कर्मचिन्ताध्यायः ॥३३।।

## Chapter 33

## Tenth House and Effects Thereof

लग्नाट्शमे राशौ कर्मफलं यताकोतितं मुनिभिः । राशिग्रहस्व मार्वरप्रहदृष्टचा तदह्मपि वक्ष्ये ।१।।

1. Sages have explained effects relating to the signs coinciding with the 10th house, planets posited therein and plancts aspecting the 10th house. I shall now cxplain those results.

होरेन्द्रबलयोगाद्यो दशमस्तत्त्रमावज् कर्म । तस्याधिपपरिवृद्धचया वृद्धिज्याऽन्यथा हानिः ।।२।।

2. The 10th house counted from the stronger among the ascendant and the Moon, is called Karmasthana and if the lord of that (10th) house is with strength, the effects in regard to Karma will prosper. Otherwise, the same will decline.

जांगलमथवानूपं तयोभयं वा गृहं परीक्षेत। ग्राम्यमयारप्यं वा सौम्यक्षे पापभवनं वा ।।३।। द्विपदचतुप्पदरूपं सरीसृपं वा तथोमयं खंब । यद्रूपं तद्भवनं यादृशकं यत्स्वभावं च ।।४।। प्रवदेत्तत्समवेशे कर्मप्राप्ति नरस्य तत्सवृशीम्। तस्माङ्शमं भवनं प्रसवे बुध्येत यत्नेन ।५।। दशमे नक्षत्रपतेर्लग्नात्पुरुषस्य यादृशी भवति। सर्बारम्भे वृत्ति विनिविशेत्तादृर्शों तस्य ।६।।

3-6. The effects relating to the 10th house will depend on the nature of the sign, viz. watery, biped, Aranya (like Leo) or Jangala (Centiped), the form of such sign and the places indicated by such sign. The 10th house should be carefully examined on these lines. The occupant of the 10th house counted from the Moon or from the Ascendant will influence the livelihood of the native according to his (i.e. planet’s) nature and disposition.

प्रहकेन्त्रफलाध्याये कथितं कर्मस्थितंग्रहैलंग्नात् । चन्द्रादत्र विशेषो ग्रहै स्थितंवर्यवतमिह बक्ष्ये ।।७।।

7. In a previous chapter, results of placements of planets in the 10th house from the ascendant have been explained while dealing with angular positions of various planels. Now specially explained are with reference to the Moon.

Notes: In the 31st Chapter, effects of planets occupying the 4 angles viz. ascendant, the 4th, the 7th and the 10th have been explained. Those are specifically with reference to the ascendant. Now he explains the special effects of planets in the 10th counted from the Moon. However, the clues given in the earlier chapter and in this chapter can be wisely exchanged between the Moon and the ascendant in appropriate contexts, keeping Kalyana Varma's instructions in mind.

चन्त्राट्शमे सूर्यः सिद्धारम्मं घनः समृद्धं च। जनयत्यृत्तमसर्वं नृपतिमुदप्रं जनाश्रयं पुष्टम् ।।। भौमः साहसनिरतं प्रत्यन्तनिवासिनं विषयलुब्धम् । ऋकूरं निषादचरितं जनयति दशमे स्थितः पुर्वम् ।।। विद्वासिं धनबन्तं बहुश्रुतं नृपतिसम्मतं ख्यातम् । जनयति सौम्यो दशपे पुरुषं बदृशिल्पिनं प्राज्ञम् ।१०।। गुरुरपि दशमस्थाने सिद्धार्य धामहं धनममृद्धम्। जनयत्युत्तमचरितं नरेन्द्रसचिवं नरं स्यातम्।११।

शशिनो दशमे शुक्रः सुमगं ललितं च वित्तवन्तं च । जनयति सिद्धारम्भं धनिनं नृपपूजितं पुर्वम् ।।१२।। सौरो व्याधितबेहं निःस्वं दुःखान्वितं प्रजाहीनम्। कर्मसु नित्योद्विग्नं जनयति दशमे स्थितः पुरुवम् ।।१३।।

8-13. EFFECTS OF PLANETS IN THE 10th FROM THE MOON: If the Sun occupies the 10th from the Moon, the native will be successful in his undertakings, be very wealthy, very strong, be a king, will patronize others and be healthy in physique. Mars so placed denotes that the native will be interested in valorous deeds, will live in foreign places, will long for sensual enjoyments, be cruel and will be akin to an outcaste in conduct. If Mercury occupies the 10th from the Moon, one will be learned, rich, will have abundant knowledge of sacred affairs (like Veda etc.), be dear to king, famous, aquainted with many an art and be intelligent. If it is Jupiter that occupies the 10th counted from the Moon, the native will be successful in achieving his desires, righteous, affluent, will have excellent history among men, be a king's minister and be famous. If Venus occupies the 10th from the Moon, one will be fortunate, beautiful, famous, will be successful in his undertakings, be rich and be honoured by king. Should Saturn occupy the 10th with reference to the Moon, the native will have physical diseases, be poor, grieved, bereft of children and be always emotional.

Notes: In sloka 12, the words “vittawantam” and "dhaninam" appear separately. Both can be interpreted as “rich" in which case it will become redundant. "Vittavantam’ also means famous and hence, these two words are separately rendered as "famous" and "rich” respectively in different senses.

भानुभौ मेसमेतः कर्मकरान् कासशोवगदबहुलान्।

ज्योतिविदः प्रकुर्याल्लाक्षणिकारताकिकांश्चापि ।१४।।

सृर्यः सौम्यसमेतो वस्त्रालंकारभागिनं वणिजम्। जनयति मेपूरणगः पुरुषं जलजीविनं वाऽपि ।।१५।। जीवसहायः सूर्यः सिद्धारम्भान्नरेन्द्रमान्यांश्च । जनयति दशमे पुरुषान् बीरान् शूरान् सुविश्यातान् ॥ १६।। सूर्यसहायः शुक्रो दशमे स्वजनाश्रितं नरं कुरते। स्त्रीसंश्रयात्समृद्धं सुभगं नृपवल्लभं चापि ।१७।। सूर्यः स्वपुत्रसहितो बशमे बन्धभागिनं भृतकम्। जनयति बीनं कृपर्णं चोरमुषितं प्रलापकरम् ॥१८।।

14-18. EFFECTS OF THE SUN JOINING ANOTHER PLANET WHEN IN THE TENTH FROM THE MOON: Should the Sun be with Mars whilc bcing in the 10th counted from the Moon, the native will be a labourer (Karmakaran), wil] suffer severely from cough and pulmonary consumption or consumption in general, be an astrologer and will be an expert in the science of Marks and Signs and also in Logic. Should the Sun get the company of Mercury, remaining in the 10th from the Moon, one will enjoy robes and ornaments, be a trader and will live through the means of water. If the Sun is conjunct Jupiter being in the 10th from the Moon, he will be successful in his undertakings, be honoured by the king, brave and famous. The pair of Venus and the Sun in the 10th from the Moon denotes that the subject will patronize his people, be fortunate, dear to the king and will increase his possessions through the help of his wife (or women). The Sun and Saturn being together in the 10th from the Moon will make one liable to imprisonment, a servant, poor, wretched (or miserly), lose to thieves and prattle.

मौमः सोमजसहितो जनयति दशमे नर्रं बहुविवक्षम् अस्त्रकलावेत्तारं कौशलमतिजीविनं महाशूरम् ॥।११।।

भौवः सुरगुर्सहितो दशमे कुर्ते बलस्य नेतारम् । मिश्रेभ्यो लब्धनं तदात्रयाज्जिवितं धन्यम् ।२०।। जनयति विदेशनिरतं काञ्चनमुक्तादिभिर्वणिम्वृत्या । भौमः शुक्रसमेतो दशमे स्त्रोसंत्रयाद्वाऽपि ।।२१।। भौमः सौरसहायो जनयति दशमे स्थितो नरं प्रसवे । साहसशीलं क्षुद्रं कर्मोद्युक्तं रुजा सहितम् ।।२२।।

19-22. EFFECTS OF MARS JOINING ANOTHER PLANET IN THE 10th FROM THE MOON: When Mars gets the company of Mercury being in the 10th from the Moon, one will have many an enemy, be skilful in archery, and be very valorous. If Jupiter be with Mars in the 10th of the Moon, one will be a mighty leader, will gain wealth via friends, will live thereby and be fortunate. Venus and Mars being in the 10th from the Moon: the native will be interested in living in foreign places, will trade in gold, precious stones etc., and will progress through women. Saturn helping Mars with his company being in the 10th from the Moon, indicates that the native will be adventurous, base and will suffer from diseases.

सघनं नरेन्द्रपुर्यं धमिध्ठं वृन्दनायकं स्यातम्। सौम्यो जीवसहायो जनयति मेषूरणे पुरुषस् ।।२३। सौम्यः शुक्रसहायो जनयति दशमे सुह्रज्जनोपेतम् । विद्यास्त्रोधनसौर्यं नृपसचिवं विषयनाचं वा ।।२४। सौम्यः सौरसहायो मृद्भाण्डकरं करोति दशमस्थः। स्यातं विद्याचार्य पुस्तकलिपिलेस्यकारं च।२५।

23-25. If Jupiter and Mercury be together in the 10th from the Moon, the subject will be rich, honoured by the king, righteous, be leader of men, and be famous. The placement of Venus and Mercury in the 10th counted from the Moon denotes that one will be endowed with friends, learning, wife, wealth and happiness, be a king's minister

and a provincial head. If Mercury is helped by Saturn with his company in being posited in the 10th from the Moon, the person conccrned will be a potter, be famous, imparter of learning and be an author of books.

वचसां पतिः सितयृतः कर्मणि कुर्ते नरेग्द्रबरभृत्यम्।

ब्राह्मणर्पात विशोकं विद्याचार्य समर्थं च ।२९।।

सुरराजगुरु: साकिर्दगमे नीचं कुस्ते प्रवृद्धचेष्टं स्थरास्पदं

परोपतापकरम्। सुस्थिरारम्भम् ।।२७१।

26-27. EFFECTS OF JUPITER JOINING ANOTHER PLANET IN THE 10th FROM THE MOON: If Jupiter joins Venus in being in 10th from thc Moon, one will serve a superior king, will head Brahmins, be devoid of grief, be an imparter of learning and be skilful. Saturn and Jupiter in the 10th from the Moon denote that one will be base, will cause grief to others, will act like an aged person, will have a firm residence, and will successfully begin his undertakings.

शुक्रः सौरसहायश्वित्रकर गन्धजीविनं वेद्यम्। जनयति दशमे पुरुषं नीलकरं चूर्णकारं च।२च॥।

28. EFFECTS OF VENUS-SATURN CONJUNCTION IN THE 10th FROM THE MOON: One with Venus and Saturn in the 10th from the Moon will be an artist, will trade in scents, be a doctor, and will deal in black-salt and camphor.

Notes: "Neelam” apart from meaning black-salt also indicates poison, antimony, sapphire etc., while Churna means apart from camphor—flour, chalk, lime etc.

रविभौमचन्द्रपुत्राश्वन्द्राट्शमस्थिता नरं धन्यम् ।

जनयन्त्युतमपुरुषं नृपतिसमं रविभौमदेवपूज्या वशमस्थाने शत्रूणां हन्तारं जनयन्ति सर्वजनपूज्यम् ॥।२६॥ नरं सुभगम्। समृद्धिसंयुक्तम् ॥ ३०।।

चन्द्राङ्शमे भानुर्भुपुत्रो भार्गवश्च जनयन्ति। ऋूरं साहसनिरतं परधनहरणेऽतिनिपुणमतिम् ।।३१। भानुजरविभूपुत्रा दशमस्थाः कऋरूरकर्मनिरतं तु। उत्पादयन्ति मनुजं मूढं पापं बुराचारम् ।।३२। रविबुधगुरवो दशमे विह्वांसं रूपसंयुक्तम् । उत्पादयन्ति पुर्वं धामिष्ठं बन्धुवल्लभं चैव ।। ३३। बुधसूर्यभार्गवमुता यशस्विनं धारमिकं विगतरोषम् । जन्यन्त्यपराभूतं सौभाग्यपरिच्छदसमृद्धम् ।।३४।। रविबुधशनयो दशमे क्रूरं चपलं नरं विशीलं च। सर्पादयन्ति नियतं शस्त्राम्निपरिक्षतांगं च ।।३५। रविभूगुज्देवपूज्या दशमस्थानोपगा नरं कुर्युः। सुचवं विद्याप्तधनं धर्मरतं भोगभागिनं नित्यम् ।।३६। ब्रिदशगुरुमन्दसूर्या दशमे युक्ता नरें भकुर्वन्ति। ब्राक्षेण लोकमान्यं चारित्रविलोपिनं घोरम् ।३७। चहर्बरविचानुसुता दशमस्थानोपगा नरं कुर्युः। लोच्यान्वितमतिचपलं समस्तमनवित्युप्तं चोचेन्दुजमुर्पूज्या धमिव्ठं बहुकुदुम्बपरिवारम् । खमदन्ति इशमसंस्था विद्याधनभागिनं पुरुषम् ।।३६।। शोचन्शित्याभिरतान्मालाकारान्मुवर्णकारांर्च हुर्वशबुधचुषन् इशमस्थाः सर्वलोकवयितांश्च ।४०।। श्वोचबुधसूक्षपुत्रा जनयन्ति तया न धर्मशीलं च । विह्वाविर्तं प्रखलं दशमस्थानोषगा नर मलिनम् ।४१।

मार्गवसुरेज्यभौमा दशमस्थानाश्रिता नरान् कुर्युः। घनसंयुक्तान् शूरान् देवद्विजपूजनानुरतान् ।।४२। विद्याधनजनहीनान् कापुरुषान् सौर्यर्वाजतान् विकलान् । जीवांगारकसौरा वशमे कुर्युनरान्नीचान् ॥४३। सचिवानुत्तमपुरुषान् परधर्मरतांश्व धनिन्च। मौसमृदुरसर्यपुत्राः कुर्वन्ति नराननेककर्मरतान् ॥४४।। शुकाब्हस्पतिसौस्या दशमे पुर्वस्य शुक्राशिस्थाः। बहुविधमिष्टं क कुर्युव्याधि चाप्यन्यगृहसंस्थाः ।।४५।। लिपिलेस्यकाव्यनिरतं धनवन्तं बहुविध्वेवस्नादं च। अटनप्रियं सुरूपं बुधगुरसौरा नरं दशमे ॥४६।। दशमे विज्ञानयुतान् मल्लान् परदेशनिरतांश्च। कुर्युर्बुधमृगुरविजाः कर्मोसुप्तं सदा दान्तम् ।।४७।। विद्धांसं धर्मरतं दयान्वितं सत्यवन्तं च।

भानुजगुरुमृगुपुत्रा दशमस्थानोपगा नर कुर्वुः ॥४e1। 29-48. EFFECTS OF THREE PLANETS IN CONJUNC- TION IN THE 10th FROM THE MOON: If the Sun, Mars and Mercury are in the 10th from the Moon, one will be fortunate, excellent among men, equal to king and be honoured by all. The Sun, Mars and Jupiter in the 10th of the Moon denote that he will be fortunate, will destroy his enemies and will be plentiful. The Sun, Mars and Venus in a similar position will make one cruel, adventurous, steal others' wealth and highly skilful. Mars, Saturn and the Sun posited in the 10th of the Moon denote that he will be intent on doing cruel acts, be foolish, sinful and bad in conduct. The Sun, Mercury and Jupiter so placed denote that he will be learned, beautiful, righteous and be dear to his relatives. Famous, righteous, devoid of anger, unconquerable, fortunes and plenty of robes—these are the effects produced

by the conjunction of Mercury, the Sun and Venus, in the 10th of the Moon. When one has the Sun, Mercury and Saturn in the 10th of the Moon, he will be cruel, fickleminded, be not virtuous and will be afflicted by weapons and fire. If the Sun, Venus and Jupiter be together in the 10th of the Moon, one will be fortunate, will earn by learning, be rightcous and will always enjoy pleasures. Saturn, the Sun and Jupiter remaining in conjunction in the 10th counted from the Moon will make one devoid of (worthy) history, still honourable and valorous. Venus, Sun and Saturn so placed will make one miscrly, fickleminded and prohibited by all. If Mars, Mercury and Jupiter are in the 10th, one will be righteous, will have a large family and many children and will be learned and wealthy. Mercury, Mars and Venus in the 10th of the Moon will make one interested in fine arts, a dealer of garlands, goldsmith and dear to all. Saturn, Mars and Mercury in similar position will make one righteous, addicted to sleep, wicked and dirty. If at the time of birth one has Mars, Venus and Jupiter in the 10th from the Janma Rasi, he will be wealthy, valorous and be interested in honouring gods and Brahmins. If Saturn, Jupiter and Mars are in the 10th counted from the Moon, one will be bereft of learning, wealth and near and dear, be mean, bereft of happiness and be deformed (or distrcssed). Mars, Venus and Saturn so placed denote that one will be a minister or an excellent personage, interested in others’ religlons, wealthy and have many professions. Mercury, Venus and Jupiter in the 10th (with reference to the Moon) identical with Taurus/Libra denote that all his desires will be fulfilled (i.e. he will be happy in all respects) while this position in other houses, will make one suffer from diseases. Mercury, Jupiter and Saturn in the 10th of the Moon denote that one will be interested in authorship and literature, be wealthy, be a very modest servant, be fond of wandering and be beautiful. If Saturn, Mercury and Venus be together in conjunction in the 10th counted from the Moon one will be endowed with wisdom, be a boxer, be interested in living in foreign countries, diligent and tamed (like an elephant). If Jupiter, Saturn and Venus join in the

10th as above, one will be learned, righteous, kind and truthful.

वं ब्यादिवु वाच्यं जन्मनि पुंसां फलं हि वर्मोत्यम् । व्यधिकद्रहसंयोगे योऽत्र विशेषस्तमपि वक्ष्ये ।४६।।

49. Those are the results of planets upto 3 in number joining in the 10th from the Moon. Different results will be experienced if more than 3 planets join in the 10th of the Moon as explained below.

रविभौमबुधसुरेड्चा दशमस्यानोपगा नरं कुर्युः। शूरं विक्षतगात्रं दातारं सर्वकर्मरतम् ।।५०।। बत्रार्षशुक्रसौम्याश्वनद्राद्दशमं समाश्रिताः प्रसवे । कुर्युर्मालाकारास्लेस्यकरांश्चापि चित्रकर्मकरान् ॥५१।। रविकुजबुधभानुसुता दशमस्थानस्थिताः प्रसवकाले। उत्पादयन्ति पुर्वं धनवाह्नवारणोपेतम् ॥ ५२।। रविजीवशुकसौम्या जनयन्ति नभःस्थिता नरान्सौम्यान् । कुत्सितवृत्तीनामपि सुतान् वरान् कर्षणोपेतान् ॥५३।। रविजीवसौम्यसौरा दशमस्थानस्थिताः कुर्युः। पुरुषं मायानिपुणं कूरं परवञ्चनानुरतम् ॥५४।। रविसितबुधभानुमुताश्चन्द्राङ्शमस्थिता नरं कठिनम् । उत्पादयन्ति सुभगं वाम्मिनमय कर्वणानुरतम् ॥५५।। रविगुरुभार्गवशनयो मेवूरणसंस्थिता: प्रसवकाले। उत्पादयन्ति मनुजं प्रवासशीलं विविधचेष्टम् ॥५९६। भौमसितबुधसुरेडचा जन्मनि दशमर्कगा नरं निपुणम् । उत्पादयन्ति चतुरं शूरं समरेष्ववृष्यं च ।५७।।

भौमबुधमन्दगुरवो जन्मनि दशमस्थिता नर मलिनम् । जनयन्ति सदोद्युदतं संग्रामे रिपुविनाशकरम् ॥५८। श्रौमबुधशुत्रसौरा नमःस्थले संश्रिताः प्रसवकाले । विद्याबहुलं शूरं जनयन्ति नर विशालांगन् ।।५६। भौमगुरुशुक्रमन्दाश्चन्द्रान् मेवूरणर्क्तगाः प्रसवे । जनयन्ति नरं धीरं कुटुम्बयुवतं धनोपेतम् ।६०। बुधगुरुभार्गवशनयो जन्मनि दशमस्थितः कुर्युः। पुर्वं शान्तमनस्कं सुमेधसं लोकदयितं च ।६१।।

50-61. EFFECTS OF 4 PLANETS JOINING IN THE 10th FROM THE MOON: If the Sun, Mars, Mercury and Jupiter be in the 10th from the Moon, one will be valorous, physically hurt, charitable and interested in all deeds. With Mars, Sun, Venus and Mercury in the 10th from the Moon one will be a garland dealer, writer or an artist and with Saturn replacing Venus in this group one will be endowed with wealth, conveyances and elephants. The Sun, Mercury, Jupiter and Venus in the 10th from the Moon dcnote that even though one will have a mean profession, he will be excellent, will have sons and will have agricultural living, and Saturn replacing Venus in this formation indicates that he will be skilfully cunning, cruel and will cheat others. The Sun, Mercury, Jupiter and Saturn being in the 10th of the Moon, will make one hardhearted, fortunate, eloquent and interested in agriculture while Jupiter replacing Mercury in this formation will make one interested in foreign places and have many an acts to do. If Mars, Venus, Mercury and Jupiter are in the 10th from the Moon, he will be skilful, courageous and unconqucrable in battle while Saturn participating in this combination removing Venus denotes that he will be dirty, ever longing for war and will destroy enemies. Should Mars, Mercury, Venus and Saturn join in the 10th of the Moon, one will have abundant learning, be valorous and will be broad-bodied while Jupiter in the place of Mercury in this context denotes that he

will be eourageous and endowed with family and wealth. Lastly, if Mercury, Jupiter, Venus and Saturn are placed in the 10th from Janma Rasi, one will be calmly disposed, intelligent and dcar to people.

## पार्पर्नभःस्थलस्थः सौम्यग्रहवीक्षितेः प्रजायन्ते ।

वैद्यपुरोहितगणकाश्चन्द्रात्

परवञ्चनानुरताः ।।६६्।।

62. MALEFICS IN THE 10th FROM THE MOON: If Malcfics occupy the 10th house counted from the Moon and aspected by benefics, one will be a doctor, Purohit (a religious guidc), an astrologer or will be interested in cheating others.

Notes: The occupation of malcfics in the 10th from the Moon will give good results and produce professionals like doctors, astrologers and Purohits (religious guides helping the householders in performing due rites). This will happen if the said planets are favourable and strong. For example if the 10th house concerned belongs to Venus and malefics like Saturn, Mars etc., arc placed therein it will produce doctors. Similarly if the 10th house belongs to Jupiter or Mercury, these malefics will make one an astrologer, a purohit and the like. If the 10th house in question is owned by Mars and occupied by adverse Saturn (for example Saturn in the 10th in fall as the Moon is in Cancer), or the 10th house bcing Aquarius and occupied by Rahu/Ketu/ Mars one will use cheating in making his livelihood. To takc to such evil tendencies, the favourable disposition should be less and benefic planets unhelpful.

## एते समस्तयोगाः सौम्यग्रहवीक्षिता: प्रशस्यन्ते।

पापग्रहदृष्टियुताः प्रायेण न

## भद्रकाः प्रोक्ताः ।६३।

63. Whatever effects have been ascribed so far in this chapter will specially reveal beneficially if benefics aspect the 10th house from the Moon. If malefics aspect, the benefic results may not be full.

पापास्तृतीययच्ठा होरेन्दुस्थानतो नृणामिष्टाः । नेष्टा निधनान्यगता लग्नोपगता विशेषेण ।६४।।

64. MALEFICS AND EFFECTS: Malefics in the 3rd or in the 6th with referencc to either the ascendant or the Moon will prove favourable while in the 8th, 12th, or the ascendant they are inauspicious—more so in the ascendant.

## होराशशिनोर्बलवान् यस्तस्मात्कर्ममेन वा कययेत्।

यो बलयुक्तो भावस्तदधिपतेर्वाऽदिशेद्गुलिम् १९५।।

65. LIVELIHOOD: One’s livelihood should be predicted based on the 10th from the Moon or from the ascendant; whichever is stronger will prevail. The earnings (livelihood) will also be indicated by the stronger of the lords of the 10th counted from the Moon or from the ascendant.

Notes: Our author suggests the 10th housc as a basis in evaluation of livelihood counted from the Moon and the ascendant. There are other authorities who takc the 10th house counted from the Sun also.

आराम-बुद्धि-सेवाक्विरसवाणिज्यद्रतकार्येण

जीवन्ति नरा नितयं मेषगणे दशमराशिस्थे ।६६॥ वृवभगणे दशमस्थे शकटचतुप्पदविहंगमृगजीवी। धान्यादिसंग्रहेण च जांगलवेशे फलं प्रायः ।६७।। जलवणिजादिसमृद्धचा मुष्ताशंखप्रवालभाष्डेश्च । लिपिगणितलेश्यजीवी नृमियुनवर्गे दशमसंस्थे ।६८।। शत्त्राम्नियोनिभूषणमुवता शल्योपजोवनं ◦ चंव । आख्लेटकवृश्या वा ककिणि वर्गे च दशमस्ये ।६९।i सन्नाहका मणीनां पावाणस्वर्णर्प्यकूटाश्च । कर्षणनिरता लेये गोजीवा धंान्यवाणिजकाः ।७०।।

शाकटिका मणिकारा हैरप्या गन्धविक्रये निपुणाः। गान्वर्वशिल्पलेर्यः कन्यावर्गे सवा विभवाः ।७१। सामविकादुपदेशाद्धिरथ्यपरिवर्तनाच्च मित्राच्च । जायन्ते च मनुध्या नानाव्यवहारभागिनः सततम् ।।७२।। वाणिज्यविपणिजीवा गोजीवा महिषजीवाश्च। नानापथ्यसमृद्धाः सलिलोद्भवपथ्यवृत्तयः स्याताः ।७३।। घनधान्यमूलवणिजः फलम्लक्षीदलाश्चव । जायन्ते घटवर्गे बशमस्थानस्यते कलावृत्ताः ।७४।। स्त्रोसम्पर्कजविभवा जायन्ते कर्षणानुनिरताश्च। नित्योद्युर्ताश्वोराः पृथिवीपतिसेवका: पापाः ।७५।। देहचिकित्सानिरता लोहकरा जीविनोऽलिसंदरक्षें। बगें नभस्तलगते धान्यानां खोपजीविनो नित्यम् ॥७६। नृपसचिव दुर्गपालनगोजीवनवाजिकाब्ठशकुनैश्च यन्त्रोपस्करगणितैजोवन्ति नराश्विकित्सया धनुषः ।७७।। दशमे कुरंगवर्गे जलपष्यधनी भवेन्महाविभवः । खटवारामारोपणरसायनर्वतते : M cIl शस्त्रदहनप्रभेदेश्चीयेण च दशमे घटधरबर्गे वर्तते खननवृत्या । भारवहस्कन्धबाहुबलात् ।।७६।। शस्त्रातसलिलाद्योनिप्रपोषणादश्वविक्याहाऽपि I वर्गे मोनप्रभवे दशमस्थे जायते वृत्तिः ।८०।

66-80. SIGNS AND PROFESSIONS: If Aries or many of its divisions be in the 10th, one will live through gardens, intelligence, service, agriculture, trading in juices (Rasa has other meanings like liquor, poison, mercury, mineral/ metallic salt, etc.), or be an envoy. With Taurus or a majority of its divisions in the 10th, one will live through carts, quadrupeds, birds, wild animals and by gathering grains. Similarly Gemini denotes trading through water, precious stones, vessels, conch, writing, accountancy etc. Cancer in this context means weapons, golden ornaments, arrows and hunting. Leo on the 10th or many of Leo divisions related to the 10th denote livelihood through precious stones, gold, stones (like construction material) agriculture, cows and the like and grains. Wealth comes through vehicles, gems, gold, sccnts, music, arts and writing if Virgo features as above. Libra in similar condition indicates livelihood through teaching, dealing in gold, many ways and means, friends, cows, buffalocs, many saleable articles, things emanating from water, grains, fruits, agriculture and arts. Similarly Scorpio denotes livelihood through female association, agriculture, thieving, scrving the king, sinful acts, rendering physical treatments (i.e. medical profession), blacksmithy and grains. Ministership under a king, protection of forts, cattle, horses, firewood, indicating omens, machinery, mathematics, medical treatment etc., are denoted by Sagittarius in a similar manner. Capricorn in similar condition denotes livelihood through cots, gardening, chemicals etc., and abundant income through waterborn items. Aquarius indicates weapons, burning, separation (or creating inimical relationship between two, i.e. 9w), thieving, carrying headloads and through physical stamina. Pisces in similar manner denotes living through weapons, waterborn items, sale of horses etc.

Notes: Note whichever is stronger among the Moon and the ascendant. See which sign holds many of the divisions of the 10th house from the ascendant or from the Moon as the case may be. If Aries holds many of the 10th house dtvisions, the native will eke out his living through agriculture, juice trading, liquor sales, poison etc. Similarly

the other divisions may be understood. The 10th lord as relevant falling in many such divisions will also produce identical results. For more information, please refer to Brihat Jataka, Phala Deepika, Horasara, Hora Ratna etc.

## दिवसकरार्यः खरस्थः शशिहोराभ्यां भवन्यादधाः।

पितृमातृशत्रु हितजनसहजस्त्रीभृत्यवर्गेभ्यः 1511

81. PLANETS IN THE 10th AND THEIR EFFECTS: The Sun in the 10th indicates income through father, the Moon mother, Mars enemies, Mercury friends, Jupiter brothers, Venus females and Saturn servants.

Notes: In this sloka, hints are given relating to the native’s relatives from whom gains will follow. For examplc, if the Sun is in the 10th, thc native will gain through father. Similarly by placemcnts of other planets.

If the planets abovementioned are strong and favourable, gains will follow. Conversely if the planet in question is weak, in fall, in enemy's camp etc., being in the 10th, the native will lose through such relatives. So to say, Mars in Cancer or being devoid of strength, eclipsed etc., if in the 10th, the native will lose through his brothers. These clues not only relate to the relatives but also to the other Karakatwas of the planets. For example, Mars as above, weak in Cancer can cause loss of agriculture, lands, houses etc., for a Libra native. These effects should be wisely applied according to circumstances.

होरागर्तर्घनगतेरायगृहस्थेश्च बलसंयुतर्गहेनद्वेरनेकधा -

चिन्तयेदर्यम्। Sऽविष्टमाचायँः ।२।।

82. There will be gains in many ways if the ascendant, the 2nd and the 11th are respectively occupied by strong (favourable) planets, so say the preceptors.

Notes: It is not enough if the 10th house and its lord are well-disposed for gains in livelihood. The 2nd being the house of money and the I Ith the house denoting net gains

will have to be strong apart from their Karakas and lords. The 12th house and its lord should be comparatively weak so that losses do not predominate gains.

इति हाहवाणबर्मदिरचितायां मारावस्यां कर्मचिन्ताध्यायस्त्रयासिवरणः ॥ ३३।

Thus ends the 33rd Chapter entitled “10th HOUSE AND EFFECTS THEREOF” in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अय लोकयात्राध्यायः ॥३४॥ Chapter 34

## Different Houses and Worldly Affairs

रविवृष्टे प्राग्लग्ने विश्वान्ताः स्त्रीवु रोषणः ब्रूरः। पितृपक्षलब्धविभबो नरेन्द्रसेवी भवेज्जातः ।११। स्त्रीणां वश्यः सुभगो दाक्षिष्यमहोदधिः प्रचुरकोशः । जनोक्षिते विलग्ने मार्ववजलपष्यवान्भवति ।।२।। साहससंप्राम रचिश्चण्डस्कुटबान्धवोऽतिधर्मरतः उदये कुजसन्दृष्टे भवति नरः स्थूलशेकश्च ।३। बुधवदृष्टे प्राम्लम्ने शिल्पकलाविद्यया तमन्नपूर्णः। भवति नरो विपुलमतिः कीतिकरो मानसंयुक्तः ॥४। इज्याव्रतनियमपरा नृपपृष्याः श्यातकीर्तयो मनुजाः । लने सुरगुरुदृष्टे सञ्जनगुर्वतिविसंयुक्ताः ॥ ५।। वेश्यास्त्रीजनबहुलास्तरुणाः सम्पन्नभोगधनसौद्याः । शुक्रेक्षिते विलग्ने भर्वन्ति मनुजाः सुर्पाश्च ।६।। भाराध्वरोगतप्ताः ऋृद्धा वृद्धस्त्रिया युता विसुखाः । मन्वेक्षिते विलग्ने मलिना मूर्खाश्च जायन्ते ।।७।। 1-7. EFFECTS OF SEVERAL PLANETS ASPECTING THE ASCENDANT: If the Sun aspects the ascendant, the native will be valorous, will be irascible with females, be cruel, will gain wealth from paternal side and will serve the king. If the Moon aspects the ascendant, he will be under

the control of women, fortunate, be very courteous, be very wealthy, softly disposed and will gain by dealing in waterborn articles. If Mars lends his aspect to the ascendant, one will be fond of valorous acts and battles, irascible, will have severed relationship, be very righteous and will have a long penis. Should Mercury aspect the ascendant, the native will have rich knowledge of arts and will thereby gain, and be quite intelligent, famous and honourable. If Jupiter be the aspecting planet with reference to the ascendant one will be interested in religious observations, be honoured by the king, be famous and will be endowed with (the company of) virtuous people, elders and guests. Venus aspecting the ascendant denotes that he will be addicted to many prostitutes, be youthful, will have happiness of wealth and pleasure and will be beautiful in appearance. If Saturn aspects the ascendant one will be troubled by heavy loads, travels and diseases, be irascible, will be attached to aged women (ie. will be fond of seeking union with aged females), be devoid of happiness, be dirty and foolish.

## पश्यन् ग्रहः स्वलग्नं सर्व विदधाति सौस्यम्यं च। प्रायो नृपप्रियत्वं पापः पापं शुभं च शुभः ।।।

8. GENERAL ASPECT TO ASCENDANT: If a planet (whether natural benefic or natural malefic) aspects the ascendant identical with its own sign, that confers happlness and wealth, apart from close relationship with the king. If the ascendant is not owned by the aspecting planet, a benefic then confers auspicious effects and a malefic evil results.

Notes: Even though a planet may be a natural malefic, like Saturn, its aspect on the ascendant owned by it will produce auspicious results like wealth, freedom from sickness, good fortunes etc. (Similarly yogakaraka’s aspect on the ascendant is auspicious for fortunes, though he may be a natural malefic). A melefic not being the owner of the house will bring evil effects like sickness, misfortunes etc. while a benefic though not an owner by aspecting the

ascendant will be favourable.

एकेनापि न वृष्ट समस्तगुण्वाजितं लग्नम्। स्वगृहस्वभावसहितं जनयति खलु केवलं नित्यम् ॥।६।

9. UNASPECTED ASCENDANT: Should the ascending sign be not in aspect to even one planet, the native will be devoid of (other) qualities and will acquire exclusive qualitles of the sign concerned.

Notes: The effects due to a planet aspecting the ascendant will correspond to the innate nature of the planet, its lordship etc. If the sign assurgent is unaspected, then the results due to the nature of the sign will come to pass fully. The nature of planets and the nature of signs ascending can be found explained in this work elsewhere and in other standard works.

ह्व्यादिग्रहसन्दृष्टं प्रायेग सुखार्थदं भवति लग्नम् ।

एकेनापि शुभेन ख न तु पापेव्यनेते सद्भः ।।१०।

सर्वर्गगनध्रमर्णद् ष्टे र लग्ने -चवेग्महोपालः ।

बलिभिः समस्तसौस्यो विगतभयो दीर्घजीवी च ।।११।

10-11. SPECIAL EFFECTS: If two or more planets aspect the ascendant, the native acquires happiness and wealth. Even one benefic aspecting the ascendant is auspiclous while a malefic so aspecting is inauspicious. If the ascendant is aspected by all the planets one rules over the earth. Should all the planets be endowed with strength, he will enjoy all kinds of happiness, be free from fear and be long-lived.

लग्ने त्रयो विगतशोकवि्वाद्धितानां

कुर्वन्ति जन्म शुभदाः पृथिवीपतीनाम्।

पापास्तु रोगभयशोकपरिप्लुतानां

बह्वाशिनां सकललोकतिरस्क्तानाम् ॥ १२।।

12. PLANETS CONJUNCT IN THE ASCENDANT: If three plancts are together in the ascendant, the native will be devoid of miseries and will lord over the earth. But if these are (exclusively) malefics, he will be distressed due to fear, grief etc., will eat much and will be disregarded by one and all.

लग्नात्वष्ठमदाव्टमे यदि शुभाः पार्पर्न युक्तेक्षिताः

मन्त्री दण्डपतिः क्षितेरधिपतिः स्त्रीणां बहूनां पतिः ।

दीर्घायुर्गदर्वाजतो गतभयो लम्नाध्रियोगे भवेत्

सच्छीलो यवनाधिराजकथितो जातः पुमान्तौस्यमाक् ।। १३।।

13. ADHI YOGA FROM ASCENDANT: If benefics exclusively occupy the 6th, 7th and 8th, without the company of malefics, Lagnadhi Yoga is caused. This yoga confers ministership, leadership over army (or justiceship) and kingship, lordship over many women, long life, freedom from diseases and miseries, possession of virtues and happiness. So say Yavana kings.

Notes: Chandradhi Yoga may be found in slokas 20 and 21 of the next chapter. For details also please see Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra, Horasara, Phala Deepika etc.

## स्वगृहोच्चसौम्यवर्गे ग्रहः फलं पुष्टमेव विदधाति। नीचर्क्रिपुगृहस्यो विगतफलः कीतितो मुनिधिः ।।१४।।

14. A planet in own sign or in exaltation/friendly sign, according to sages, confers its effects fully. If it is in fall or is inimically placed, it is incapable of giving the results (due to the abovementioned yoga).

Notes: The effects mentioned in this sloka may be more effectively applied to Adhi Yoga apart from general context. For example, if in Adhi Yoga, the planets concerned enjoy dignity like exaltation, friendly sign, friendly aspect etc. the results ascribed will come to pass wholly. The effects will not mature pronouncedly if the planets occupy severally their inimical signs, debilitation, or be

combust. Conversely, in this case, Instead of giving good effects due to Adhi Yoga the planets may give still adverse results.

रविरविजभूमितनयाः कुटुम्बसंस्था विलोकनाद्वाऽपि । कुर्वन्ति धनविनाशं क्षीणेन्दुनिरीक्षिता विशेषेण ।।१५।। रविभौमौ धनसंस्थौ त्वग्दोषदरिद्रताकरी कथितौ। मन्दः कुर्ते शुद्धो महार्थयुक्तं बुधेक्षितस्तत्र ।१६। रविरपि विधनं जनयति यमेक्षितः शस्यतेऽन्यदृष्टश्च। सौम्या: कुटुम्बराशौ बहुप्रकारं धनं दद्युः ॥ १७। श्रुध्दृष्टस्त्रदशगुरु: कुटुम्बराशौ च निस्वतां कुरुते । सोमतनयोऽपि शशिना निरीक्षितो हन्ति सर्वधनम् ।।१८।। चन्द्रोऽपि धनस्थाने क्षोणोऽपि बुधेक्षितः सदा कुर्ते । पूर्वाजितार्थनाशं निरोधमपि चान्यवित्तस्य ॥ १६।। शुक्रः कुटुम्बभवने चन्रेण निरीक्षितः प्रधनवाता । सौम्यग्रहेण दृष्टा स एव धनवः सदाः ज्ञेयः ।।२०।

15-20. PRINCIPLES RELATED TO THE 2nd HOUSE: If Mars, Saturn and the Sun occupy the 2nd house, the native's wealth is destroyed. If week Moon aspects the 2nd house, such effects are more pronounced. Mars and the Sun in the 2nd cause afflictions to skin apart from causing poverty. If Saturn, posited alone in this house, is aspected by Mercury, he will be greatly rich. Even if the Sun is posited in the 2nd house in aspect to Saturn, without aspect of others, pennilessness will come to pass. Benefics in this house cause advent of money in many ways. But if Jupiter posited in the 2nd house remains under Mercury’s aspect, or Mercury so posited in the 2nd house in aspect to the Moon, the entire wealth earned by the native will be deprived. Weak Moon in the 2nd house in Mercury’s aspect denotes that even the ancestral property will be destroyed; not only this, other sources of income will also be hindered. Should the 2nd house Moon be aspected by Venus, plenty of wealth will be earned. Same is the result of aspect of

another benefic (i.e. Jupiter).

Notes: The 3 malefics—Saturn, Sun and Mars—jointly placed in the 2nd will not only have adverse effects on money matters of the nativc but also on his family lifc and marriage. Loss of wealth will be more severe if the 2nd house is aspected by the Moon from the 8th and devoid of rays. The 8th house is a house of destruction and hence such effects are denoted. Saturn's lonely placement in aspect to Mercury (naturally from the 8th) will make one greatly affluent. Herc again, my personal opinion is: the riches will be subjected to some risk as Mercury aspecting any planet in the 2nd particularly Jupiter or the weak Moon will cause loss of property. Similarly there is a scope of risk if Mercury aspects Saturn in the 2nd. These are general views without reference to the whole planetary array. The Moon in the 2nd if in aspect to Jupiter or to Venus will cause abundant wealth to be acquired by the native.

पापभवनं तृतीयं समस्तपापर्युतं सहजह्न्त् । विपरीतमितः श्रेष्ठं संस्यां तेषां प्रवक्ष्यामि ।।२१।। यावन्तो नवभागा यदागताः सहजराशौ तु। तत्संख्यां कुर्वन्ति च दृष्टास्त्वर्येस्तथा बह्वः ।२२।। तत्र स्थितो रविसुतः कुज्जेन दृष्टो बिनाशयति जातान् । शुक्रो गुरुसंदृष्टः पुष्णाति सदैव दायादान् ।२३। सौम्योऽ्गारकवृष्टः सुहृदां परिसंक्षयं सदा कुर्ते । भावाध्याये कथितं शेषं परिकल्पयेदत्र ।।२४।।

21-24. PRINCIPLES RELATED TO THE 3rd HOUSE: When the 3rd house is owned by a malefic and is occupied by a malefic, the co-born are destroyed. Conversely, a benefic's sign being the 3rd and occupation of benefics therein will cause happiness thereof. Now explained below is the method to ascertain the number of co-born. The number of Navamsas past in the 3rd house denotes the number of brothers following the native. If other planets

aspect the 3rd house, the said number (caused by navamsas) stands increased. Saturn placed in the 3rd in aspect to Mars causes destruction of co-born. But Venus in the 3rd and in aspect to Jupiter increases happincss thereof. Mercury placed in the 3rd house, in aspect to Mars (or in aspect to the Sun, as some texts read 'Bhaskara Drishtah’instcad of 'Angaraka Drishtah'), the native's friends are destroyed. Rest (relating to the 4th house) may be understood from Bhavadhyaya (Ch. 30).

Notes: To find out the number of co-born, not only our author, but various others suggest to count the number of Navamsas past in the 3rd house. This method is generally not found reliable as it excludes other considerations. A safcr bet is the Ashtaka Varga of Mars. All details relating to Ashtaka Varga cannot be explained in this brief space and the reader may consult relevant chapters in the work “THE SECRETS OF ASHTAKAVARGA".

Saturn in the 3rd aspected by Mars is not congenial for good relationship or gains through co-born. The native will however beget co-born as Mars is the indicator in this case. Venus placed in the 3rd in aspect to Jupiter not only ensures fortunate co-born but also abundant gains through them for the native.

सृतभवनं शुभयुक्तं शुभवृष्टं वा शुभर्मिह येवाम्। तेर्षा प्रसवः पुंसां भवत्यवश्यं न विपरीते ।।२५॥। एकतमे गुरुवर्गे शुभराशाबौरसो भवेत्पुत्रः। सम्नाच्चन्द्रादयवा बलयोगाद्रीक्षितेऽपि वा सौर्म्यः ।।२६।।

25-26. ABOUT THE 5th HOUSE: If the 5th house contains a benefic, or is aspected by a benefic or be a benefic’s Rasi, the native will surely beget children. If it is different, he will not obtain progeny. If the 5th house counted from the Moon or the ascendant, the stronger prevailing, has at least one Jupiter’s division in the various Varga calculations, or has a benefic's Rasi in it and is aspected by a benefic, the native will have legitimate issue.

संस्या नवांशतुल्या सौम्यांशे तावती सदा बृथ्टा।

शुभवृष्टे तवृद्विगुणा विलष्टा पापांशकेऽ्यवा दृष्टे ।२७।।

27. NUMBER OF CHILDREN: If the Navamsa of the 5th falls in a benefic's Rasi the number of children equals the number of Navamsas past in the 5th house. The aspect of a benefic on the 5th bhava doubles the number while malefic Rasi as 5th house navamsa and the said house receiving a malefic's aspect confers progeny with difficulty.

Notes: Here again, it is not always safe to depend on the Navamsas past in the 5th house to know the number of children. Jupiter’s Ashtaka Varga and assessment of the whole horoscope is the best guide.

## सौरक्षें सौरगणो बुधवृष्टो गुरुकुलजार्कद्ग्धीनः । क्षेत्रजपुत्रं जनयति बौघोऽपि गणो रविजदृष्टः ।।२८॥।

28. AUTHORIZING OTHER KINSMAN TO RAISE PROG- ENY FOR THE NATIVE: If the 5th house falls in the divisions (like Rasi, Navamsa, drekkana etc.) of Saturn, and be with Mercury’s aspect but without that of Jupiter/Mars/ Sun, the native obtains progeny by authorising his kinsman to raise the same for him from his (the native’s) wife. Similar result prevails if Mercury’s Rasi be in the 5th house containing Mercury in it and is in aspect to Saturn but without the aspect of the others as cited above.

## मान्दं सुतर्लेमिन्दुनिरीक्षिते यदि शनैश्चरेण युतम्। दत्तकपुत्रोत्पत्तिः कीतश्च बुधस्य चैवं स्यात् ॥२६।।

29. ADOPTED OR BOUGHT PROGENY: If the 5th house belongs to Saturn and be occupied by Saturn himself with the aspect of the Moon, one procures a child by adoption or by an act of purchase. Identical effect follows the position of Mercury in the 5th house owned by him recetving the Moon’s aspect.

सप्तमभागे कौजे सौरयुते पञ्चमे संदा भवने।

क्कृत्रिमपुत्रं विन्द्याच्छेबप्रह्दर्शनान्मुषते ।। ३०।।

30. ADOPTION SANS CONSENT: Should the 5th house contain Saturn unaspected by others and fall in a Sapthamsa owned by Mars, the native will adopt a grown up child without the consent of its natural parents.

Notes: To have a 'Kritrima Putra’means adopting a grown up child in the absence of its parents' consent. The 5th house should be occupied by Saturn and be in aspect to none. Additionally the 5th house should fall in a Sapthamsa of Mars. For calculations of Sapthamsa, please see p. 22 of Vol. 1 of this edition.

## वर्गे पञ्चमराशौ सौरे सूर्वेग वाऽत्र संयुनते। लोहितवृष्टे वाच्यो जातस्य सुतोऽधमः प्रायः ।।३१।। चन्द्रे भौमांशगते धीस्ये मन्वावलोकिते भवति।

## मूढोऽधरमश्च पुत्रः शेषग्रहदर्शनाभावे ।।३२।।

31-32. BASE CHILDREN: If the 5th house contains the Sun and falls in the division of Saturn receiving the aspect of Mars, the children so obtaincd will be abject. Should the Moon be in the 5th house and receive exclusively Saturn's aspect while the 5th house falls in a Navamsa of Mars, even then the progeny will be foolish and base.

Notes: One will obtain wretched progeny if the Sun is in the 5th in aspect of Mars. Further the Sun should be in many divisions of Capricorn/Aquarius. There will be birth of foolish and mean children if the Moon is in the 5th aspected by Saturn and falling in Scorpio/Aries in Navamsa.

## तस्मिन्नेव च भौमे शनिवर्गस्ये निरीक्षिते रविणा। वुक्षस्य भवति पुत्रोऽ्पविद्ध इति करणमुनिवचनात् ॥ ३३।।

33. If the 5th house is occupied by Mars falling in many of Saturn’s divisions and be in aspect to the Sun, one will obtain पिz so says sage Karuna.

Notes: If Mars be in the 5th in aspect to Saturn one

will obtain a son of "apaviddha” type. "Apavlddha" means a son that is adopted by a stranger after its being cast off by its original parents. For this, Mars simultaneously should occupy many Saturn's divisions.

Hindu Sastras have recognised 12 kinds of sons many important of which have been dealt with in this chapter astrologically.

## शनिवर्गस्ये चन्रे शनियुक्ते पञ्चमे सदा चवने।

## शुक्ररविभ्यां दृष्टे पुत्रः पौनर्भवो भवति ।।३४।।

34. ISSUE BY A REMARRIED WIDOW: Should the 5th house fall in Saturn's Vargas and contain both the Moon and Saturn therein, in aspect to both the Sun and Venus, progeny will be begotten by a widow given to the native in marriage.

## चन्द्रो यदार्कसक्तः कलत्रसस्यस्थेव पञ्चमे गेहे।

## रविवृष्टेऽप्यय सहिते कानीनः संभवति पुत्रः ॥ ३५।

35. PROGENY BY A VIRGIN: Should the Moon be in the 7th along with the Sun or the Moon being in the 5th in aspect to or in the company of the Sun, one will obtain issue through an unmarried girl.

## वर्गे रविचन्द्रमसो: सुतगेहे चन्त्सूर्यसंयक्ते।

## शुश्रेण दृष्टमात्रे पुत्रः कथितः सहोडश्च ॥ ३६।।

36. PROGENY BY A WOMAN PREGNANT AT MAR- RIAGE: If the 5th house falls in the divisions of the luminaries and contains the Moon and the Sun in aspect to Venus only, one will have progeny by a woman who will already be pregnant at the time of marriage.

पार्षरबेलिभिर्युक्ते पापक्षे पञ्चमे सदा राशो ।

जातोऽपुत्रः पुरुषः सौम्यग्रहदर्शनातीते । ३७।।

37. ISSUELESSNESS: If the 5th house falls in a malefic's sign and be occupied by strong malefics without benefic’s aspect, the native will be childless.

## शुक्रमवांशे तस्मिन्शुक्रेण निरीक्षिते त्वपत्यानि। दासीप्रभवानि ववेच्चन्द्रावपि कश्चिदाचार्यः ।।३८।।

38. PROGENY BY A FEMALE SLAVE: If the 5th house be in a navamamsa of Venus and be aspected by Venus, one will beget progeny by a female servant. Some scholars opine that even if the Moon’s Navamsa and the Moon so come in the picture, the said effects will come to pass.

## सितशशिवर्गे धीस्ये ताभ्यां दृष्टेऽ्यवाऽपि संयुनते।

## प्रायेण कन्यकाः स्यु समराशिगणेऽपि चान्यथा पुत्राः ॥ ३६।।

39.
