SONS AND DAUGHTERS: When the 5th house has exclusively the division of the Moon and Venus and be with aspects of Venus and the Moon alone or be occupied by these two alone or when the said house has the divisions of only even signs, the native will beget only female children. In a contrary situation (i.e. 5th house falling in divisions of odd signs only and be excluded from the occupation or aspect of Venus and the Moon but others), there will be birth of only male children.

लग्नाव्दशमे चन्द्रे सप्तमसंस्ये भूगोः पुत्रे।

पार्पः पातालस्थैर्वशच्छेत्ता भवेञ्जातः ।।४०।।

40. CHILDLESSNESS: When the Moon occupies the 10th house, Venus is in the 7th and malefics are in the 4th, the native's dynasty does not descend any further.

## भौमः पञ्चमभवने जातं जातं विनाशयति पुत्रम् ।

दृष्टे गुरुणा प्रथमं सितेन न च सर्वसंदृष्टः ॥४१।।

41. LOSS OF CHILDREN: Should Mars be in the 5th,

the children of the native will pass away as they are born. However, if Jupiter or Venus lend aspect to the said position, the loss relates only to the first child. If all planets aspect (the said Mars), then there is no loss at all of children.

धनजनसुब्रहीनः पञ्चमस्यश्च पापै-

भवति विकल एव क्मासुते तत्र जातः।

विवसकरसुते च व्याधिभिस्तप्तदेहः

सुरगुरुबुधशुर्षः

सौष्यसपद्धनाढचः ।४२।।

42. SPECIAL EPFECTS: Should the 5th house be occupied exclusively by malefics, the native will be devoid of wealth, own men and happhjess. If Mars is in the 5th house, he will be distressed (or deformed). Saturn so posited denotes sickness. The occupations of Mercury, Jupiter, or Venus will confer abundant happiness and wealth.

रिपुभावे क्षितिसुनुर्मनदेन निरीक्षितो विशति शत्रन् ।

शुभयन्तः शुभवृष्टः शत्रुभयं चैव नात्यन्तम् ॥४३।।

क्षेत्रे प्रहेन्रतुल्यां संख्यां तेषां विनिविशेत्राप्तः ।

भावाध्यायेऽभिहितं विस्तरतश्चिन्तयेच्छेयम् ।।४४।।

43-44. ENEMIES: If the 6th house is aspected by Saturn and occupied by Mars, thc native will have fear from enemies. Occupation or aspect of benefics will keep one free from such fear. The number of enemies (causing trouble) will correspond to the number of planets posited in the 6th house. Rest of the related matters may be understood from the previous pages, (Ch. 30).

शुक्रेन्दुजीवशशिजे

सकलैस्त्रभिश्च

ह्वाध्यां कलत्रभवने च तर्यककेन। एषां गुहेऽवि व गणेऽपि विलोकिते वा सराप्त स्त्रयो भवनवर्गखगस्वभावः ।४५।।

45. ABOUT THE 7th HOUSE: If Jupiter, Moon, Venus and Mercury be in the 7th, in any manner (i.e. one of them, two, three or all of them in the 7th), or other divisions falling in the 7th house or if the 7th house be in aspect to these, one will procure a wife corresponding to the planet/s and Rasi/s in question.

## एवं ऋूर्नाशो लग्नाच्चन्द्राद्मवेच्च बलयोगात्।

## शशिरविजयोः कलत्रे भार्या पुंसारं पुनर्भूः स्यात् ।।४६।।

46. Should the 7th house contain malefics or fall in malefic planets' divisions, the wife of the native will be destroyed (i.e. adversely affected). These results can be deduced from the ascendant and the Moon, the stronger of them having more significance. If the Moon be in the 7th (from the ascendant) along with Saturn, the native will marry a widow.

## भवनाधिपांशतुस्या भवत्ति नार्यो निरीक्षणाद्वाऽपि ।

## एर्व रविकुजांशे गुरुबुधयोश्चापि जामित्रे ॥४७।।

47. The number of wives will correspond to the number of heavenly bodies aspecting the 7th or the navamsas past in the 7th house. There will be a single wife, If Leo, Aries or Scorpio rise in Navamsa or if Mercury and Jupiter join together in the 7th house.

## प्रायेण चन्द्रसितयोर्वगे युक्तेऽ्यवापि जामित्रे।

## बृष्टे वा बहुपत्स्यो भवन्ति शुक्रे विशेषेण ।४८।।

48. If the 7th house has many divisions of Venus and the Moon or is occupied by these two, there will be plurality of wives. Specially Venus in the 7th will cause such an effect.

शुरुशुक्रयोः स्ववर्णा रविकुजसशिमानुर्जर्भवन्त्यूनाः ।

शुक्रे वेश्याप्रायश्वन्रेऽपि वदन्ति केतुमालाख्याः ।४६।।

49. Jupiter and Venus related to the 7th house will confer a bride of the same caste while the Sun, Mars, Saturn and the Moon denote a female belonging to a mean order. As per Ketumalacharya, Venus and Moon reiated to the 7th house will cause relationship with whores.

भौमे कलत्रसंस्थे नित्यं वियुतो भवेत् स्त्रया पुरुषः।

श्रियते वा शनिदृष्टे योविववश्यं न दृष्टेऽन्यैः ॥५०।। खूने कुजभार्गवयोर्जातः पुरुषो भवेद्विकलवारः ।

घीधर्मस्थितयोर्वा परिकल्प्यं पण्डितेरेवम् ॥५१।।

50-51. Mars in the 7th house will make one go devoid of wife. If he is, while so posited in aspect to only Saturn, the wife will certainly pass away. Should Mars and Venus be together in the 7th, or 5th or 9th, the native will beget a languild or deformed wife.

लग्नाद्वध्यरिपुगतयोः शशांकभान्वोर्वदन्ति पुरुषस्य ।

प्रभवं समस्तमुनयः क्रमेण पत्न्या सहैकनयनस्य ।।५२।।

82. It is the unanimous opinion of all the sages that if the Moon and the Sun are respectively in the 12th and the 6th, both the native and his wife will be one-eyed.

लग्नस्थे रवितनये गण्डान्ते भार्गवे कलत्रगते।

वन्ध्यापतिस्तदा स्वाद्यदि न सृतर्ष शुर्भर्युक्तम् ।।५३।।

53. If Saturn occuples the ascendant, while Venus is in the 7th identical with Ganda Rasi and the 5th is bereft to beneficial aspect, the person acquires a barren wife.

Notes: For Ganda Rasl, refer to Vol. 1 of this edition and also Kala Prakasika, Hora Sara etc.

If the 5th house has the aspect of a benefic or of the lord of the 5th then the native will obtain progeny. If however the female concerned has effective combinations of barrenness, it will be with great difficulty and after sincere

propitiations that the native will obtain progeny.

लानब्ययमदनस्थैः पापः क्षीणे निशाकरे धीस्थे। स्त्रोहीनो भवति नरः पुत्रेश्च विर्वाजितो नूनम् ॥५४।। यमभूमिजयोर्वगे द्यूनस्ये तदवलोकिते शुङ्गे। जातो भवत्यवस्यं पर्या सह पुंश्वलः पुरुषः ।५५।।

54-55. Onc will be devoid of a wife and an issue if malefics occupy the ascendant, 12th and 7th while decreasing Moon is in the 5th. Both the native and his wife will be immoral if the 7th house falls in the various divisions of Saturn and Mars while Venus is posited in the 7th in aspect to Mars and Saturn.

बुद्भार्गवयोरस्ते स्त्रीहीनो जायते हचपुत्रश्च । दृष्टे शुर्भश्च वाच्याः परिणतवयसः सह प्रमदाः ।५९।।

56. When the 7th house is occupied by Mercury and Venus, the native will be bereft of both wife and progeny. If however this combination receives the aspect of a benefic, there will be obtainment of a wife at an advanced age.

भार्गववाषपतिसौम्यैः प्रमदाभवने शशांकयुक्तेश्व ।

एर्षकेन हि तेषां पुरुषस्य विभूतयो बहुलाः ॥ ५७।

57. Should the Moon, along with even one of Mercury, Jupiter and Venus, be in the 7th, the native will be opulent.

रविवृष्टे युक्ते वाऽप्यायक्षेत्रे भवेत्सदा नृपतेः ।

भवति घनं युद्धार्यश्चोरवनचतुष्पदार्यश्च ।५८।।

58. ABOUT THE I1th HOUSE: The native will gain through kings, wars, thieves, forests, quadrupeds etc., if the Sun is placed in the 11th house, or if the 11th house is aspected or owned by the Sun.

स्त्रीजनहस्तिप्रायं शशांकवर्गे शशीक्षिते युक्ते ।

क्षीणे क्षयोऽ्य पूर्णे वृद्धिः स्यादायगे वृत्तेः ॥५६।

59. Should the Moon be in the 11th house or her division so related to the I 1th house the native will gain through females and through sales of elephants. If the Moon is declining, these are not much but an increasing Moon increases these gains greatly.

## हेमप्रवालभूषणमाणिव्यधनं कुजे भवेदेवम् ।

## साहसगमनागमने: पावकशस्त्रैश्व व

## वषतव्यम् ।६०।

60. Mars or his divisions related to the 1 1th house as above denotes gains through gold, coral, ornaments, ruby, money, valour, coming and going (ie. travels), fire and weapons.

आये बुधेऽपि बर्गे दृष्टे युक्तऽयवा भवेन्तित्यम् ।

शिल्पादिलेस्यकार्व्ययुकितप्रायं तथा सुतराम् ॥ ६१।

61. Should Mercury or his divisions be related to the 11th as above, the native will gain by writing, arts, literature and arguments.

## नागरजनोपयोगः ऋतुभिश्च तया विशिष्टपुर्ष्यैश्च ।

## हेमप्रायं वित्तं जीवेऽपि तुरंगमाकीर्णम् ॥६२।

62. Jupiter or his divisions so related to the 11th house indicates gains through headship of a city, royal mercy, sacrifices (religlous), meritorious acts, gold and horses.

वेश्यास्त्रोसंयोर्गरगमनागमनैर्धनं भवति पुंसाम् ।

आये सितेऽपि चैवं मुक्तारजतादि भृविष्ठम् ॥६३।।

63. When the planet Venus or his divisions are so connected with the 1 1th house, one will gain through connections with prostitutes, travels, pearls and silver.

## नगरपुरवृन्दयोगे: स्यावरकर्मक्रियाभिरपि वित्तम्।

## लोहबरमहिषबहुलं रविजेऽपि तथाऽस्य बगे च । ९४।।

64. Saturn or his divisions so connected with the 1 1th house indicates gains through towns and villages, acts of lasting nature, iron, donkeys and buffaloes.

## एवं फलनिर्वेशः सौम्यद्ध्टे विशेषतो बाच्यः ।

## ऋरूरेश्च समुपघातो मिर्ब्मिश्रस्तदा पुंसाम् ॥ १५।।

65. The effects attributed above stand increased if benefics aspect the 11th house, will decrease by malefic influence and will remain mixed with the interference of both benefics and malefics.

## सर्वग्रह्युतदृष्टे बहुप्रकारेण निविशेड्कत्तम् ।

## बलवान्यस्तत्र भवेदतिरिक्तं स प्रयच्छति च।६६।

66. If the 11th house is aspected by (or associated with) all the planets, advent of wealth will be in several ways. The strongest of those will have the highest influence.

## मित्रस्वगृहगतोधं स्वोच्चे पूर्ण तथास्तगः किञ्चित् ।

## शत्रुगृहस्थश्चरणं ददाति विहगस्तदाये तु । ६७

67. A planet in its own sign or in a friendly sign will give half of the effects attributed. These effects are full if it 1 is in its exaltation sign, very meagre if it is combust and Just one fourth if inimically placed.

Notes: The effects mentioned in this sloka not only relate to the 1 1th house but also be applied generally but meaningfully to befit a given context.

आजन्मतो नराणां भवति धनं निशिचितं यवनवृद्धः। क्षितिपतिमाण्डलिकानामपरिमितं प्राह लोकाक्षः ।६८।।

68. The effects relating to the 11th house as mentioned above will be experienced right from birth, according to Yavanas. But according Lokaksha (a preceptor by that name), kings and heads of zones stand to enjoy abundant gains (as against a common man).

भानी क्षीणे चेन्दौ व्ययभवने भूपतिहरति वित्तम्। भौमे बधसंदृष्टे र बहुप्रकारो भवेन्नाशः ॥६६।। गुरुचन्द्रदानवेज्या व्ययभवने वित्तपोषणं कुर्युः।

भूतनयेन न वृष्टा भावाध्यायोक्तमन्यच्च ।।७०।।

69-70. ABOUT THE 12th HOUSE: If the Sun or weak Moon is placed in the 12th, the native's wcalth will be lost to the king. Mars posited in the 12th in aspect to Mercury, will cause losses in several ways. Jupiter, the Moon (with increasing rays) and Venus in the 12th will cause growth of wealth, provided there is no aspect from Mars on them. Rest may be understood from the previous portion (Ch. 30).

लग्ने बुध्दुक्काणे शशिना दृष्ट चतुष्ट्यस्याने।

जातो नृपतिकुलेव्वपि शिल्पी स्यान्निश्चितं मुनिभिः ।।७१।

71. MISCELLANEOUS MATTERS: Even a person born in regal scion will become an artisan if the ascendant in Mercury’s decanate receives the aspect of the Moon from an angle.

Notes: The ascendant can fall in Mercury's decanate when one of the following areas rises: the first 10 degrees of Gemini/Virgo, the second 10 degrees of Aquarius/Taurus and the last 10 degrees of Libra/Capricorn. When the ascendant being in such a degree and is aspected by the Moon, the various Raja Yogas will not be of any use. The Moon’s aspect on ascendant from an 'angle' is denoted in

the sloka. The aspect from the 7th will only be full of the Moon and not from other places.

## नीचे सौरनवांशे शुक्रेऽन्ये रविशशांक्योर्मदने। मन्देन विलोकितयोर्माता दासी महाकुलेऽपि स्यात् ।७२।।

72. Should Venus be in the 12th house in Virgo identical with Saturn’s Navamsa while the luminaries are in the 7th in aspect to Saturn, the native’s mother though belonging to a noble race will become a slave.

Notes: Venus should be in the 12th in fall needing Libra to rise. He should further be in Capricorn/Aquarius Navamsa. That is, he should be within 6° 40' of Virgo. The Sun and the Moon should be in Aries in the 7th and aspected by Saturn. In effect, the native’s mother will be a slave or a harlot as the word 'Daasi' means. So to say she will take to corrupt means of livelihood or to mean professions.

## सूर्यावृद्धितीयराशौ भास्करतनये खमध्यये च्द्रे। भौमे सप्तमभवने विकलोऽस्मन्सर्वदा जातः ।।७३।।

73. Should Saturn occupy the 2nd house counted from the Sun while the Moon and Mars respectively are in the 10th cusp and 7th house with reference to the ascendant, the native will always be distressed.

Notes: Saturn should be in the 2nd from the Sun while the Moon should be in the 10th cusp and Mars in the 7th house—both the positions with reference to the ascendant and not to the Sun. As "Khamadhyage” appears in the text, the latter interpretation i.e., the 10th from the Sun is not possible. Khamadhya' is zenith point in the horoscope and even the 10th house from the Moon is not called 'Khamadhya'. Zenith point, eastern point, nadir, descedant—all these four have relationship only with the rising sign. (Likewise incidentally directional strength is also computed from the ascendant and not the Moon). The effect given by

the combination in question is unending distress to the native.

मध्ये पापग्रह्योश्चन्द्रे मदनस्यतेऽर्कजे जन्तुः ।

## श्वासक्षयविद्रधिना गुस्मप्लीहादिपीडितः सुभगः ।७४।।

74. The native will suffer from pulmonary and spleenary disorders apart from tuberculosis if the Moon is hemmed between malefics as Saturn occupies the 7th house.

सूर्याँसे यदि चन्दश्चन्द्रांशे भास्करो यदि स्लेष्मी।

सममेकराशिगतयोर्दुरबलदेहः सदा : I

75. Should there be an exchange between the Sun and the Moon in the Navamsa, the native will suffer from phlegmatic disorders. Should the Sun and the Moon be in one Rasi having identical longitudes, the native will be emaciated at all times.

Notes: When the Sun is in Cancer Navamsa while the Moon is in Lco Navamsa, one is apt to be afflicted by phlegmatic disorders. Phlegmatic disorders are: dysentcry, asthma, cough etc. If the Sun and the Moon have identical longitudes, one will have a very weak and thin body. At the very moment when the New Moon ends and the first day of bright half begins, the luminaries will be identical in longitude to the minute of arc. Say the Sun in 20°15' and the Moon in 20°15' of the same sign.

## निधनधनारिव्ययगा रविचन्द्रकुजार्कजा नियतम्।

## नयनविघातं

कुर्युर्बलवद्प्रहदोषसम्भूतम् ।।७६।

76. Should the 8th, 2nd, 6th and the 12th be respectively occupied by the Sun, Moon, Mars and Saturn, the native will lose his eyes due to the affliction caused by the strongest of the four.

धर्मायसहजसृतगाः पापाः सौम्यर्न बीक्षिता जन्तोः । श्रवणविनाशं कुर्युः सप्तमसंस्याश्च दन्तानाम् ।७७।।

77. If malefics occupy the 6th, 11th, 3rd and 5th, the native will suffer from ear defects, provided there are no aspects from benefic planets. Malefics in the 7th unaspected by benefics will produce dental afflictions.

## उद्दे दिनकरपुत्रे त्रिकोणभवने कुजे च सोन्मादः । क्षीणे शशिनि स-सौरे याते वा व्ययगहे जातः ।।७८।।

78. There will be diseases of the mind if Saturn occupies the ascendant while Mars is in the 5th or the 9th. Alternatively, Saturn while being in the ascendant should be conjunct decreasing Moon to cause the said effects.

Notes: Mental imbalances/disorders will come to pass if the ascendant is occupied by Saturn while Mars is in a trine thereof. Similarly, any two among the trio Mars, Jupiter and Saturn opposing each other from the ascendant and the 7th are known to cause mental afflictipns.,, इति कल्याणवर्मेवि रचितायां सारावल्यां लोकयात्रा नाम चतुस्विज्ञोऽध्यायः ॥ ३४।

Thus ends the 34th Chapter entitled "DIFFERENT HOUSES AND WORLDLY AFFAIRS" in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अथ राजयोगाध्यायः ।।३५।।

## Chapter 35

## Raja Yogas

राजोपयोगिशास्त्रं यस्मात्रभवो विशेषतस्तेषाम्।

सञिचन्त्या नृपयोगास्तानेवाहं प्रवक्ष्यामि ॥१।

1. This science is mainly useful to rulers. Hence I explain in this chapter the configurations that make one a king.

Notes: As in those days kings were mainly the major beneficlaries of astrology, the author hints at the better use of this science for the kings. Primarily the yogas explained in this chapter ensured kingship in olden days. In modern context, these yogas in their order will confer a high degree of position, richness, status and the like. And the word “Rajayoga” throughout the gamut of astrology be understood in this sense only and not in a literal sense of making one a king.

स्वोच्चत्रिकोणगृह्र्बलसंयुर्तश्च

त्रयार्यनुपो भवति भूपतिवंशजातः । पञ्न्वाविभिर्जनपदप्रभवोऽपि सिद्धो

हीनेः क्षितीश्वरसमो न तु भूमिपालः ॥२।

2. KINGLY/WEALTHY YOGAS: One born in a royal scion will become a ruler if 3 or 4 planets, with strength, occupy their exaltation/Mulatrikona signs at birth. If 5 or 6 planets be so placed, even a person of mean birth will attain rulership. If one or two heavenly bodies be so, the native will only be rich, being equal to a king, but not really

be a king.

Notes: If at birth 3 or 4 planets being strong be in their Mulatrikona signs/exaltation signs, one will become rich. If 5 or 6 heavenly bodies be so, he will enjoy vast estates, properties, affluence and paraphernalla. With one or two planets being so the person will acquire normal riches.

अशुभगगनवासैः स्वोच्वयः ऋूरचेष्टं

कथयति यवनेन्द्रो भूर्पात विक्रमोत्यम्। न तु भवति नरेगद्रो जीवशर्मोक्तपक्षे

भवति नृपतियोगः सत्कृतो राष्टुगलैः ।३।।

3. According to Yavanas, if only malefics are exalted, the native will become a king indulging, in cruel deeds. But as per Jeevasarma, such planets will make one valorous but not a king. One with such a Yoga (according to Kalyanavarma) will be honoured and will become the head of a region.

Notes: In ancient kings days were identifled for their liberality, cruelty, mercifulness, scholarship, truthfulness, penance etc. For example we have Sibhi empcror known for his llberality, Krishna Devaraya for his scholarship, Kamsa for his cruelty, Duryodhana for his lust for kingdom, Yudhishtira for his uprighteousness, Harischandra for his unconquerable truthfulness, and sage-turned-king Viswamitra for his penance. Such men—good or bad—were born only once as the planetary array—good or bad—forms never again identically. Coming to our subject back, if exclusively malefics occupy their several exaltation signs—while benefics are not so—will produce a rich person who will however lack in mercy. Jeevasarma seems to think that such a person will necessarily be endowed with not only the wealth of lucre but also the wealth of valour—an essential attribute to a successful king making an epoch. It follows that malefics in exaltation will confer wealth, unkindness and valour to a modern native.

येष्वह भवन्त्यवश्यं भूपतियोगेषु नीवकुलपुरुषाः । तानग्रतः प्रवक्ष्ये ययामतं शास्त्रकाराणाम् ।।४।।

4. Explained below are the yogas that will make even a person of base birth a king, according to Sastras.

स्वोच्चस्थे रविभौमसौरगुरुभिः सर्वेस्त्रिभिश्चैकने-

लग्ने षोडशबृद्धतापसगर्णः सन्दाशिताः पार्थिवाः । द्वाभ्यां चैकतमोदये स्वमवने चन्द्रे पुनः षोडशः सर्वो नीचकुलोद्भवोऽपि वसुधां पात्येव वाटीमिव ।५।।

5. If all the four of the Sun, Mars, Saturn and Jupiter or three of them, be exalted and one of them be simultaneously in the ascendant, a total of 16 kinds of Rajayogas are formed. One born even in a mean caste with such a Yoga will become a ruler. Again a different group of 16 Raja Yogas are formed if one or two out of the above four planets are in exaltation, while onc of the exalted planets occupies the ascendant as the Moon remains in Cancer. According to great sages, these 32 yogas will make one a ruler who reigns the whole earth as though it is just a garden.

Notes: The exaltation simultaneously of the Sun, Jupiter, Saturn and Mars will be mutually angular. While all the four are exalted, persons born in ascendant Aries, Cancer, Libra and Capricorn will get each a Raja Yoga. This will make up for four different Raja Yogas. In the second instance, 3 planets only featuring in the picture with exaltation and one of them in the ascendant, while the 4th is not exalted, 12 kinds of Raja Yogas can be obtained. For example, let not Saturn be exalted while Aries rises with the Sun, as Mars and Jupiter are exalted in the 4th and 10th houses. Then, Jupiter exalted in the ascendant as Mars is in the 7th in exaltation and the Sun in exaltation in 10th, will mark a combination. Again Capricorn ascending with Mars as the Sun is in the 4th in peak while Jupiter is in the 7th in Cancer will account for one Yoga. With Saturn's  $\mathbf { e x c l u s l o n }$  thus we got  $3$  comblnatlons each, i.e. 3  $\mathbf { x }$  3 - 9.

Thus a total of 12 Raja Yogas can be obtained with 3 planets exalted, one of them being in ascendant. We have thus in all 16 different kinds of horoscopcs. Again placing the Moon constantly in Cancer, another sct of 16 yogas can be obtained with the second type of yoga. One born with any one of the above 32 yogas will rule the earth with the case of controlling a domestic garden. To wit, he will occupy one of the most supreme positions and be countable among the most affluent of the world, controlling vast business with ease.

## गणोत्तमे लम्ननवांशकोव्म्मे निशाकरश्चापि गणोत्तमेऽ्यवा।

चतुर्हैश्चन्द्रविषाजितेस्तदा निरीक्षितः स्यावधमोद् भवो नृपः ।६।।

6. If at the time of birth either the ascendant or the Moon be in Vargottama Navamsa and be in aspect to 5 or 6 planets other than the Moon (when the ascendant being considered) one will become a king inspite of his having taken birth in a mean family.

Notes: This is a very popular yoga and is found in many standard texts. The ascendant should be in Vargothamamsa and aspected by 5 or 6 planets other than the Moon. In the case of the Moon sle should be in Vargothamamsa and in aspect to the remaining 5 or 6 heavenly bodies. So to say she will be Full in the combination. This will confer vast riches on the subject coupled with significant position.

उद्यगिरिनिविष्टमेवसंस्मैप्रहेनत्रैः

शशिर्धिरसुरेड्च र्जायते पार्थिवेन्द्रः ।

जलनिधिरशनायाः पालकः सर्वभूमे-

हंतरिपुपरिवारः सर्वतः फूत्करोति ।७।।

7. If at birth Aries be the ascendant containing the Moon, Mars and Jupiter one will become an emperor of the whole earth encircled by the seas, and the Udayagiri (i.e. the mountain from which the Sun etc, are supposed to rise)

and will roaringly destroy his enemies.

Notes: Aries should ascend and Moon, Mars and Jupiter together be posited in it. Then the native will beccome a supreme person enjoying an empirical status.

ह्वोच्चे गुराववनिजे त्रियगे विलम्ने

मेषोदये च सकुजे वचसामधीशे ।

भूषो भवेदिह स यस्य विपक्षसेग्यं

तिष्ठेन्न जातु पुरतः सचिवा वयस्याः ।।८।।

8. Should Jupiter be in Cancer while Mars occupied Aries identical with the ascendant, or should both Jupiter and Mars be in Aries asccndant, the native will becomc a ruler whosc enemies dare not approach him while his minister will be favourable to him.

निशामर्ता चाये भूगुतनयदेवेडच्यसहितः कुजः प्राप्तः स्वोच्वे मृगमुखगतः सूर्यतनयः। विलम्ने कम्यायां शिशिरकरसुनुर्यदि भवेत्

तदाऽवश्यं राजा भवति बहुविज्ञानकुशलः ।।६।।

9. Should the Moon be in the 11th house along with Venus and Jupiter while Aries, Capricorn and Virgo are occupied by their lord, one born in Virgo ascendant will doubtlessly become a very knowledgeable king.

ह्वक्षे नक्षत्रनाथः स्मुटकरतिकरालङ क्तः प्राप्तलग्नो शूने सोमस्य पुत्रो यदि रिपुभवनं भास्करः संत्रयातः । पाताले दानवेडघो गुरुरपि गगने सौरभौमौ तृतीये

सद्भूपालो भवेद्यः शशिकरधवलं चामर राजलक्षमीम् ।१०।

10. If Cancer ascendant contain the Moon with full bright rays while the 7th, 6th, 4th, 10th and 3rd are respectively occupied by Mercury, Sun, Venus, Jupiter and Saturn, the native will become a ruler. Simultaneously Mars should be conjunct Saturn.

शिशिरकिरणे स्वोच्चे लग्ने पयोम्बुनिधेः समे घटधरगते भानोः पुत्रे मृगाधिपतौ रविः । अलिगृहगतो वाचां नायः स्फुरत्करराजितो यदि नरपतिः स्फीतश्रीकस्तदा बहुवाहनः ॥११।।

11. Should Taurus ascend at birth with the Full Moon as Aquarius, Leo and Scorpio are tenanted respectivcly by Saturn, the Sun and Jupiter, thc native will become a king with abundant riches and with conveyances.

मृगे मन्दे लग्ने कुमुदवनबन्धुश्च तिमिग- स्तथा कन्यां त्यक्त्वा बुधभवनसंस्थः कुतनयः। स्थितो नार्यां सौम्यो धनुषि मुरमन्त्र यदि भवेत् तदा जातो भूषः सुरपतिसमः प्राप्तमहिमा ।१२।

12. If Capricorn ascend at birth along with Saturn while Pisces, Gcmini, Virgo and Sagittarius are respectively occupicd by the Moon, Mars, Mercury and Jupiter, one will become a ruler equal to Indra, Lord of the Lords.

उव्यति मीने शशिनि नरेन्द्रः सकलकलाइचः क्षितिसुत उच्चे । मुगपतिसंस्थे दशशतरश्मी घटधरगे स्याट्टिनकरपुत्रे ।१३।।

13. If the Moon is in Pisces identical with ascendant while Capricorn, Aquarius and Leo are lin their order occupied by Mars, Saturn and the Sun, one will become a ruler wellversed in all arts.

कुजे मृगांगे च शशी यदाऽस्ते स्पुटांशुसम्भारविराजितांगः । राजा तवा शत्रुभिरप्रवृष्यो वेदार्थविद्धेतुशतानुवादः ।।१४।।

14. One will become an inconquerable king, with knowledge of the Vedas, if he takes birth in Capricorn ascendant with exalted Mars in it opposed by Full Moon in Cancer.

Notes: Some texts read "Kuje Vilagne" meaning Mars occupying the ascendant without referring to Capricorn. This version is in place of "Kuje Mrugange” in our text which specifically hints at Capricorn ascending, with Mars in it.

करोत्यु कृष्टोद्यङ्गिन्दमृताधीशसहितः

स्थितस्तावृद्रूपं सकलनयनान दजनकम् । अपूर्वो यत् स्मृत्या नयनजलसिक्तोऽपि सततं

रिपुस्त्रीशोकाग्निर्वलति हृदयेऽ्तीव सुतराम् ।।१५।।

15. If exalted Sun is in the ascendant along with the Moon, the native will become a ruler who will be endowed with pleasing appearancc and with whose very reminisccnce the enemies will shed tears and will sparkle fire of grief in their hearts.

शुक्रो घटे कुजो मेषे स्वोच्चे देवपुरोहितः।

यदि राजा भयेन्नूनं स्वयशोधौतदिङ्मुखः ।।१६।

16. Should Venus, Mars and Jupiter be respectively placed in Aquarius, Aries and Cancer, one born so will become a king spreading his fame to all the directions.

उदपति गुरुरुच्चे तप्तहेमप्रभावो हरिततुरगनायो व्योममध्यावगाही । गवि शशिब्रुधशुका यस्य सृतौ नरस्य

स्वमुजविजितमूमिः सर्वतः पाविवेन्द्रः ।।१७।।

17. If the planet Jupiter, with the splendour of heated yellow metal, be in Cancer identical with ascendant, while the Sun is in the 10th in Aries and the Moon, Mercury and Venus are in Taurus (in the 1Ith), one will conquer the entire earth with his own prowess.

धनुषि सुरेडचः शशभृदुपेतो मृगमुखसंस्यः क्षितितनयश्च । उदयति तुंगे सुररिपुबन्यः शशितनयी वा यदि नृपतिः स्यात् ।।१८।।

18. The native will become a king if the Moon occupies Sagittarius along with Jupiter while Mars occupies Capricorn and Mercury in Virgo in ascendant.

ख्रापार्चे भगवान्सहस्किरणस्तत्रव ताराधिपो

लग्ने भानुसुतोऽतिवीर्यसहितः स्वोच्चे च मूनन्दनः । यद्येवं भवति क्षितेरधिपतिः सञ्चन्त्य शौर्य मयाद्

डूरादेव नमन्ति यस्य रिपवो दग्धाः प्रतापाग्निना ।।१६।

19. If the luminaries occupy the first half of Sagittarius while Saturn is exalted, with exceeding strength, in Libra ascendant and Mars is in Capricorn, one will become a ruler whose valour will provoke only honour from his foes.

वध्ठं ब्यूनमथाष्टमं शिशिरगोः प्राप्ताः समस्ताः शुभाः

ऋूराणां यदि गोचरे न पतिता भान्वालयाद्दूरतः । भूपालः प्रभवेत्स यस्य जलधेवेलावनान्तोद्मवैः सेनाम्लकरीन्द्रदानसलिलं भू गर्मुहुः पोयते । २०।। न प्राप्नोति जरामाशु नो भजत्यरितो भयम्। जातः स्यादधियोगेऽस्मिन्धृतिसौभाग्यसौष्यभाक् ॥२१।।

20-21. CHANDRADHI YOGA: Adhi Yoga is caused by all benefics being in the 6th, 7th and 8th from the Moon. These houses should exclude the Sun and not reccive any aspect from malefics. One born with this yoga will become a king possessing a kingdom boundaried by seas whose band of intoxicated elephants will eke out ichor which the black forest bees will drink (mistaking the ichor to the flower's honey). Such a native will be immune to senescence and sickness, be bereft of fear from enemies, be valorous, fortunate and happy.

Notes: The author has given more significance to Chandradhi Yoga as is done by many other authors, compared to Lagnadhi Yoga. The conditions laid down are clear and easily understandable. Also see sloka 13, ch. 34 supra. Hora Sara, Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra etc., for additlonal

information.

बुधः स्वोच्चे लग्ने तिमियुगलगावोडचशशिनौ मृगे मन्दः सारो जितुमगृहगो दानवसुहृत्। य एवं कुर्यात्स क्षितिमृदहितध्वंसनिरतो

निरालोक लोकं चलितगजसंघातरजसा ।२२।।

22. Mercury in exaltation in the ascendant, the Moon and Jupiter in Pisces, Mars and Saturn in Capricorn while Venus is in Gemini: this configuration will produce a ruler destroying his enemies. The dust formed by the movement of his elephant-army will darken the entire carth.

केसरिगो महेन्द्रसचिवो दिनकरसहितः

कुम्भगतोर्कजः शशघरः खलु भवति वृक्षे। वृश्चिकमे क्षितेस्तु तनयो मियुन इन्दुसुतो

मेषलग्नसमुदयो यदि स तु मनुजपतिः ।।२३।।

23. One will become a king with the following planetary configuration prevailing at the time of birth: The Sun and Jupiter in Leo, Saturn in Aquarius, the Moon in Taurus, Mars in Scorpio and the ascendant in Aries.

कार्मुके त्रिदशनायकमन्त्री भानुजो वणिजि चन्द्रसमेतः ।

मेषमस्तु तपनो यदि ल्ने भूपतिर्भवति सोतुल्कीतिः ।।२४।।

24. A famous king will be produced if Arics be the ascendant at birth along with the Sun therein, Jupiter be in Sagittarius and the Moon with Saturn is in Libra.

स्वक्षे केन्रेषु यार्तर्गुरुबुधभूगुजर्मन्दभान्वारयुर्तः

स्वोच्चे चन्द्रोऽधितिष्ठञ्जनयति नृर्पाति कोतिशुवलीकृताशम् । अत्युच्चे लम्नसंस्थो रविरपि भगवान् पार्थिवं ब्रूरचेष्टं

यातायातैः समस्तं चतुख्दधिजलं यस्य सेनाः पिबन्ति ।। २५।।

25. If the three planets, viz. Jupiter, Mercury and Venus occupy such Rasis which are angular to their re-

spective own Rasis and be in the process conjunct Saturn, Mars and the Sun, while the Moon is in Taurus, the native will become a king purifying the (four) directions with his unstained fame. The native born in Aries ascendant with the Sun in deep exaltation will rule the earth boundaried by the seas.

Notes: In thc first yoga explained in the sloka Jupiter, Mercury and Venus should be in angles from the respective signs owned by them. For example, Jupiter should be in any of his own or in Mercury's signs and Mercury in any of his own or in Jupiter’s signs whereby they will be angular from their own signs, and be in mutual kendras. Venus should be in an angle counted from Libra/Taurus. While these thrce planets are so placed, each of them should be conjunct a certain planct thus: Jupiter with Saturn, Mercury with Mars and Venus with the Sun. Additionally the Moon should be in Taurus. This is a very specific and rarer than the rarest array of planets which may or may not form many generations after us. Then one may ask: What usc is there by teaching such yogas? There are such many combinations explained in this chapter and also by many other standard texts. In my humble experience, in given cases, Rajayogas described by sign positions and by conjunctions (and not aspects) are applicable to not only Rasi chart but also to Navamsa chart. The importance of Navamsa chart is akin to that of Rasi chart in many contexts and these yogas be wisely evaluated. This idea may provide us a working basis and the extent of applicability be known only by further researches. Such yogas can be widely formed in the Navamsa chart. For example see the sign positions of the Yoga described above:

Moon

| Mer Venus |     | Jupiter |
|-----------|-----|---------|
| Mars      | Sun | Satum   |

The above placements can be easily seen in Navamsa chart the native of which should enjoy a fair degree of status and wealth.

The second yoga dealt with in the sloka is: Aries should rise and the Sun should be in deep exaltation.

## उद्द्चरनवांशके सुखस्यः कमलरिपुः सकलाभिराममूतिः । उदयति विहये शुभे स्वलम्ने भवति नृपो यदि केन्द्रगा न पानाः ।।२९।

26. Should the Moon be in the 4th house simultaneously falling in a watery Navamsa, and be with all her splendour while a benefic is in its own house identical with the ascendant and malefics are away from angles, a king is born.

Notes: A sign of Venus or Mercury or Jupiter should rise with its lord in it. The Moon should be in the 4th from the rising sign with all her rays and be in a watery Navamsa, viz., Scorpio, Cancer or Pisces Navamsa. And the 1st, 4th, 7th and 10th houses be free from malefic occupation. Then the yoga is formed to give wealth and status to the native.

शशांक

आपूर्णमण्डलकलाकलितं पश्यन्ति शुक्रसुरपूजितसोमपुत्राः । लग्नाधिपोऽतिबलवाम्पृथिवीश्वरः स्यात् वर्गोत्तमश्च नवमः खलु चेद्विलग्ने ।।२७।।

27. The Moon in the state of her Fullness if be in aspect to Venus, Jupiter and Mercury while the lord of the ascendant is endowed with strength, one so born will become a king. The ascendant in question should be in a dual sign and be in Vargottamamsa.

Notes: The Full Moon should be aspected by the three benefics, viz. Mercury, Jupiter and Venus, while the ascendant should be one of the signs of Mercury /Jupiter with the lord concerned being strong. Further the rising sign should be in identical Navamsa. This yoga will also produce a kingly person.

वर्गोतमे त्रिप्रभृतिग्रहेन्द्राः केन्द्रस्यता नोऽशुभसंयुताश्व । मो रुक्षत्रूमो न विवर्णदेहाः नुर्वन्ति राज्ञः प्रसवं प्रसम्नाः । २८।।

28. The native who has three or four benefic plancts in Vargothamamsa will become a king. These planets should be in angles, uncombust, be not weak and be free from the company of malefics.

## एक एव खगः स्वोच्चे वर्गोत्तमगतो यदि।

## बलवान्मित्रसंदृष्टः : करोति पृथिवीपतिम् ।।२।

29. Even if a single benefic is in Vargothamamsa, the native will become a king, provided the planet in question is strong and be in aspect to a fricndly planet.

शीर्षोदयक्षेषु गताः समस्ता नो चारिवगें स्वगुहे शशांशः ।

सौम्येक्षितः पूर्णकलो विलग्ने दद्यान्महीं रत्नगजार्वपूर्णाम् ।।३०।।

30. The Full Moon in Cancer ascendant not falling in Inimical subdivisions, will make one a king endowed with gems, elephants and horses, provided he has a bencfic's aspect while all the other planets occupy Seershodaya signs.

उपचयगृहसंस्यो जन्मपो यस्य चन्द्रात्

शुभगृहमयवांशे केन्द्रयाताश्च सेम्याः ।

## सकलबलविरुक्ता ये च पापाभिधानाः

## स भवति नरनाथः शक्रतुल्यो बनेन ।।३१।।

31. If the disposttor of the Moon is in the 3rd, 6th, 10th or 11th counted from the Moon herself, one born so will become a ruler comparable to Lord Indra provided malefics are bereft of strength and benefics be in angles identical with Rasis owned by benefics. Alternatively, these benefics instead of falling in such Rasis may be in such navamsas ruled by benefics.

अत्युचवस्या रुविरवपुषः सर्व एव ग्रहेन्द्रा मित्वर्ष्टा यदि रिपुदृशां गोचरं न प्रयाताः। कुर्युर्नूनं प्रसनमरिभिर्गजितर्वारणाग्रघः

सेनाश्वीयश्चलति चलितर्यस्य भूः पार्थिवे्द्रम् ।।३२।

32. The native will become such a valorous king whose army’s very roaring will quake the earth if in his radix all planets, being strong, occupy their respective deep exaltation points in aspect to friendly planets and without inimical aspects.

परमोच्चे स्वतश्चन्द्रो यदि शुक्रेण दृश्यते। कुर्यान्महीरपाति पूर्ण पार्परापोक्लिमोपर्यः ।।३३।।

33. Should the Moon be in her decp cxaltation degree in aspect to Venus, while all malefics are relegated to 3rd, 6gh, 9th and 12th houses, one will become a king.

दृश्येते शुभदेः स्वकेन्द्रभवने मित्रैश्च पार्वस्तथा

युद्धे नो रिपूर्भिजितौ बलयुती जन्मोदयक्षाधिपौ । मूपः स्यान्निजराशिनाथनवमे चाद्रोदये चेद्यशो

यस्येभसुतदानलुब्धमधुर्पेश्चातुदिशां गीयते ।३४।।

34. If the ascendant lord and the Moon sign lord are together in an angle at birth, in aspect to auspicious and friendly planets, unaspected by malefics and not being defeated by enemies, the native will become a plentiful king. Alternatively the Moon should be in Lagna and the lagna Lord in the 9th thereof. The black forest bees consuming the ichor of his army elephants will sing the glory of such native in all directions.

उच्चराशिर्भवेद्धोरा यस्यासी कुरुते नृपम्।

स्वांशेऽय सुहृदुच्वांशे दृष्टः केन्द्रोपर्गः शुर्भः ॥ ३५।।

35. Find out if the ascendant is the exaltation sign of any planct. If the planct in question is aspected by a benefic from an angle a king is born. However, the first mentioned planet should be in own navamsa or fricndly navamsa or exaltation navamsa.

Notes: Barring Sagittarius, Lco, Aquarius. Gemini and Scorpio, the other seven signs are signs of exaltation for the seven plancts from the Sun to Saturn. One of thesc seven signs should ascend as per the sloka. Lct us assumc that Canccr is the asccndant in a given case. The planet that will be exalted in Cancer is Jupiter. Hence Jupiter should be aspected by a benefic, namely Venus/Mercury/Full Moon from an angle. Sccondly Jupiter should be in Sagittarius/ Pisces/Cancer Navamsa or in a friendly Navamsa. Then the native will be able to enjoy royal status. This is a very simple yoga and can be obscrved many times by an cnthusiastic student.

सियतो भानोः पुत्रो विरचितबलः पश्चिमाधे मृगस्य

रविः सिहे शुत्रस्तुलिनि रुधिरो मेवगः करकिणीन्दुः। कुमारीों सम्प्राप्तो यदि भवति वा शर्वरीनाथसूनुः

प्रजातो भूपालश्छदयति महीमेकशुक्लातपत्राम् ।।३६।।

36. Saturn with strength, the Sun, Mars, thc Moon, Venus and Mcrcury respectively positioned in the second half of Capricorn, Leo, Aries, Cancer, Libra and Virgo will make one an emperor who will be endowed with spotless fame protecting his population.

वर्गोतमस्वभवनेषु गता ग्रहेन्द्राः

सर्वे यदा रुचिररश्मशिखाकलाषाः ।

उत्पद्यते जगति सोमवतीं धरित्रों

य: पालयेतिक्षतिप्तिजितशत्रुपक्षः ॥ ३७।।

37. If all the planets are in Vargothamamsa and are radiant the native will become an emperor of the whole earth subduing all his enemics.

केन्द्रे विलग्ननाथः सुहुद्भरभिवीक्षितो विहर्गः।

लग्नस्थिते च सौम्ये भूपतिरिह जायते पुरुषः ।।३८।।

38. The subject will become a ruler if the ascendant lord is in angle in aspect to a friendly planet while the ascendant is tenanted by a benefic.

सरपतियुरः सेन्दुर्लग्ने वषे समवस्थितो

यदि बल्युतो लग्नेशश्च त्रिकोणगृहं गतः।

रविशनिकुर्जर्वीयोपर्तर्न युर्तनिरीक्षितो

भवति स नृपः कीर्या युवतो हताखिलकण्टकः ।।३६।

39. Should Jupiter and the Moon be in Taurus identical with thc ascendant while its lord (Venus), is in his Moolatrikona (or in a trinc from the ascendant) unaspected by the Sun, Saturn and Mars, the nativc will be a famous rulcr controlling all his foes.

न नीचगृह्संस्थिता न च रिपोग् हं संगताः

स्वराशिमथवांशका नुदयगोच्चभशं कलाभिरतिभूविते कुमुदवंशबोधप्रदे यदि।

सुट्टव्भरभिवीक्षिताः क्षितिर्पाति विदध्युर्रहाः ॥४०।।

40. If at birth all plancts, avoiding inimical and debilitational divisions, occupy their own or friendly divisions in aspect to friendly plancts while the Moon is Full with her rays, the native will becomc a ruler.

यो यः पूर्णं शिशिरकिरणं प्रातत्र्शोत्तमांशं

सुस्पष्टाचर्गगनगमनः पश्यति स्वोच्चसंस्थम् ।

स-क्षोणीशं जनयति दशां प्राप्य सौम्यः स्वकीयां

स्यातं लोके यदि बलयुताः कष्टकस्था न पापाः ॥४१।।

41. If the Full Moon is exalted and is in Vargothamamsa, in aspect to a benefic planct, the person so born

will become a famous ruler in the dasa of the benefic planet in question. There should not be a strong malefic in an angle in the said yoga.

जन्मोदयमवनपती बलसहितौ केन्द्रभेऽ्ध हिबुके वा ।

इन्दुर्जलगृहगश्चेत्त्रिकोणगो

वा महीपालः ।४२।।

42. If the lord of the ascendant and the Moon sign lord with strength are together in an angle while the Moon occupies a watery sign idcntical with a trine, the native will become a king.

Notes: In this Raja Yoga, the Moon should be in a watery sign identical with the 5th or the 9th. The Lagna lord and the Moon’s dispositor should join in an angle from the ascendant.

## स्वगुहे मित्रभागेवु स्वांशे वा मित्रराशिषु।

## कुर्वन्ति च नर सृतौ सार्वमौमं नराधिपम् ॥४३।

43. Should all the planets at birth be in their own Rass and in friendly navamsas or vlce versa, the subject will become an emperor.

Notes: All the planets can be in their own Rasis and friendly Navamsas. Or they should be in own Navamsas and friendly Rasis. Then a ruler is born.

## परमोच्चगताः सर्वे स्वोच्चांशे यदि सोमजः ।

ब्रैलोषयाधिपति कुर्युंदेंबदानववन्दितम् ॥४४।।

44. Should all the other plancts be in their deep exaltation points while Mercury is in his exaltation Amsa, the native will rule over the three worlds, receiving honours from Gods and Demons.

Notes: All planets cannot be in exaltation at the same time. Hence the author puts Mercury in Virgo Navamsa. Barring Mercury, the other 6 planets can occupy simultaneously their deep exaltation degrees in the Rasi chart.

Such a native will occupy one of the highest positions in the world and be highly honourable.

यस्योत्तरस्यां भगवान्यसिष्टो बृहस्पतिः प्रागपरे च मार्गवः । अगस्त्यनामा खलु दक्षिणस्यां स नष्टशत्रुश्च भवेन्नराधिपः ।।४५।।

45. One will become an emperor devoid of any foes if the 4th, 1st, 7th and the 10th are respectively occupied by Vasishta (one of the Saptharshis), Jupiter, Venus and Agasthya (again one of the Saptharshis).

शशी पूर्णः स्वांशं स्वगृहमथवा स्वोच्चभं वा प्रयातो

दिवः पातुर्मन्त्री दितिजगुरुणा वीक्षितः केन्द्रसंस्थः । रविर्लग्ने स्वांशं यदि बलयुतः पश्यति स्यात्स भूपः - प्रभग्नं यस्येमेश्चनुस्दधिमूशल्लकीनामरण्यम् ।४६।।

46. If the Full Moon is in her exaltation Rasi, or in, own Rasi or in own Navamsa while Jupiter posited in an angle is in aspect to Venus, the native becomes a great emperor provided the Sun is in the ascendant with strength identical with his own house.

कुमुदगहनबश्धौ वीक्ष्यमाणे समस्त-

गगनगृह्निवार्सर्दीर्घंजीबी नरः स्यात् । फलमशुभसमुत्यं नेव केमन्नुमोत्थं

भवति मनुजनायः सार्वभौमो जितारिः ॥४७।।

47. Should the Full Moon receive the aspect of all the planets the native will live long and will not be subjected to adverse results that may be caused by Kemadruma and such other yogas.

उच्चामिलाषी सविता त्रिकोणे स्वक्षे शशी जम्मनि यस्य जन्तोः । स शास्ति पृथ्वीं बहुरत्नपूर्णां बृहस्पतिः कर्कटके यदि स्यात् ।।४८।

48. The Sun approaching his exaltation sign (i.e. being

in the end of Pisces) while Jupiter and the Moon are in Canccr, the native will become a king endowed with various prccious stones. In this yoga, the Sun should be simultaneously in a trine from the ascendant.

तुंगषु बड्विबुधमार्गवरा उपेताः

## स्वांशे मयूलनिकरें: परिपूरितांगाः। उत्पादयग्ति कुलिशांकितपाणिपादं

पृथ्वीर्पाति सगरवेनययातितुल्यम् ।४ह।।

49. Should six planets be in simultaneous exaltation, endowed with radiance and occupy their own Navamsas, with strength, the native will become an emperor comparable to Yayati, Sagara and Vena.

Notes: If six out of the scven planets occupy their exaltation Rasis and be in their own Navamsas, be strong and bright with rays, one will be an emperor akin to Yayati. When 6 planets get such enormous strength, out of which Venus and the Moon will also be so (Mercury being cxcluded), it seems the native will not only be very rich but also be insatiably libidinous. That is why Kalyana Varma compares the native with Yayati. (Yayati was a king belonging to lunar race who married Devayani, daughter of Sukra the preceptor of demons and also Sarmishta, daughter of a demon. The king was said to have been so blinded by sexual lust that he enjoyed carnal pleasures for 1000 ycars and could not still feel satisfied. This is, however, a mythological aspect and the exaltation of six planets as discussed in our sloka seems to make one lewd like Yayati apart from conferring riches so that Kalyana Varma compares the native with Yayati).

शुभभवनसमेतेः सौम्यभागेषु सौम्यः

स्फुटरुचिरकरार्थः प्रस्फुरद्भविलग्ने।

रविमुषितमयर्लस्तश्च पार्परमिश्रे-

गिरिगहननिवासी तापसः स्याम्नरेन्द्रः ।।५०।।

50. Should all the benefics with radiant rays occupy the ascendant, being uncombust and unaccompanied by malefics, the native will become a ruler who will take to penance in forests and hills. The benefics in question should occupy the Rasi and Navamsas of benefic/s.

Notes: Benefics should be in a Lagna Rasi owned by another benefic and be in such benefic navamsas only. The benefic Rasis are that of Venus, Jupiter, Mercury and the Full Moon and hence this yoga does not apply to Leo, Capricorn, Aquarius, Aries and Scorpio. Cancer will also stand excluded if the Moon remains a malefic in a given case. There should not be combustion to the occupant in question or company by a malefic. The effect makes the native very spiritual in disposition apart from affluent.,

## शुभपणफरगाः शुभप्रदा उभयगृहे यदि पापसञ्चयाः । स्वभुजहतरिपुरमहीपतिः सुरगुस्तुल्यमतिः प्रकीर्तितः ॥५१।

51. If all benefic planets be in benefics' Rasis identical with Panaphara ( qण\tau) houses (i.e. 2nd, 5th, 8th and I 1th) while malcfics occupy dual signs, the native so born will become a king, destroying all his enemies, and be equal to Brihaspati (Jupiter) in terms of intelligence.

Notes: The benefics should be in Panaphara houses from the ascendant while malefics should be in dual signs (i.e. Gemini and its angles). All Panapharas cannot simultaneously participate as benefics’ Rasis and all the four benefics cannot be distributed to four Panapharas keeping in view the limitations possible between Mercury and Venus. It is enough if the Rasis occupicd by benefics are Panapharas. It follows that even one Panaphara occupied will serve the purpose. Only for Taurus and Scorpio ascendant, all of the 2nd, 5th, 8th and 11th are owned by benefics and not for other ascendants. And for these two ascendants, viz., Taurus and Scorpio, the Panapharas themselves are dual signs. So a perfect yoga on the lines of the sloka in question cannot form for Taurus and Scorpio as both malefics and benefics fall in same Rasi. Unless all

the three benefics occupy one Panaphara identical with a sign of Venus while other malcfics go to dual signs, a typically perfect yoga cannot prevail on the lines of the sloka. The reader now himself can formulate an array as described. Such a native with the yoga will be successful over enemies and in litigations, be very intelligent and opulent. (Incidentally, the proper English term for Panaphara is succcdent and for Apoklima cadent while contrary equations appear erroncously on P. 33, Vol. 1 for which error an apology is due to the readers.)

## विलग्ननायः खलु लग्नसंस्थः सुहृद्गृहे मित्रदृशां पथि स्थितः । करोति नायं पृथिवीतलस्य दुर्वारबेरिच्यमिहोदये शुभ्रे । ५२।

52. If the lord of the ascendant is in the ascendant itself or is in a friendly sign in aspect to a friendly planet, the native will become a ruler, destroying his enemies, provided there is a benefic in the ascendant.

Notes: The ascendant lord in the ascendant in aspect to a fricndly planet or the ascendant lord being in a friendly sign in friendly aspect while the ascendant has a benefic in it: a Raja Yoga then is formed. A different interpretation that benefic should be in the ascendant irrespective of its lord’s placement is also possible.

## सम्पूर्णमूतिर्मगवान् शशांको मेवांशकस्थो गुरुणा च वृष्टः । नीचेन कमिचिन्न च बीक्षितोऽर्यैः प्राह क्षितीशं यवनाधिराजः ।।५३।।

53. If the Full Moon is aspected by Jupiter and be unaspected by others while there is no planet in debilitation, the native will become a ruler. The said Moon should occupy Aries Navamsa. This Raja Yoga is conceived by Yavanas.

Notes: The Moon should be Full and in exclusive aspect to Jupiter. She should also be simultaneously in Aries Navamsa while no planet occuples its sign of debilltation. The Raja Yoga so formed will make one a ruler.

लम्नाच्छशी f त्रिरिपुलाभनमःस्थलेषु स्तावखण्टितवपुः पृथिवीश्वरः स्यात् । दृष्टः सुरेन्द्रगुरुणा न च वीक्षितोऽर्य- जन्माधिपो दशमगः स्मरगोऽयवा स्यात् ।।५४।।

54. If the Full Moon occupies an Upachaya house, i.e. 3rd, 6th, 10th or 1 1th in exclusive aspect to Jupiter while the lord of the Moon sign occupies the 10th or 7th, the native will become a ruler.

Notes: From the ascendant the Moon should be in an Upachaya while her dispositor be in the 7th or the 10th from the ascendant. Only Jupiter should aspect the Moon to produce the Raja Yoga given.

बिश्वद्रश्मिकरालपुर्णपरिधिर्मक्षत्रसम्पालक- स्तुंगांशे समवस्थितश्च सकलैः प्रोद्वीक्षित्तो व्योमर्गः । कुर्याद्भूमिर्पाति यशस्यचरितं हस्त्यश्वसंन्यं जगत्

योथ्याच्छेषफणीन्द्रतुज्यम लिलोर्वोचारखिन्नः श्वसन् ।।५५।।

55. If the Full Moon be exalted and aspected by all the planets the native will become a famous ruler endowed with a vast army consisting of elephants, horses and armed men and will rule over the entire earth akin to Adisesha (the Divine Snake) painfully bearing the whole earth on its hood.

Notes: This yoga will produce a royal person with illimitable endurance as the author compares him to Adisesha who according to our mythology holds the entire earth on its hood. One will note that thc author draws a simile in almost many of these yogas to hint a hidden attribute in the Raja Yoga simultaneously.

सुधामृणालोपमबिम्बशोभितः शशी नवांशे नलिनीप्रियस्य । यदि क्षितीशो बहुहस्तपूर्णः शुभाश्व केन्द्रेषु न पापयुक्ताः ।।५९६।।

56. Should the Moon, being fully splendorous, occupy Leo Navamsa while all other benefics, devoid of malefic connexions are angular from the ascending sign, the native will become a ruler endowed with various elephants.

शशिबुधरुधिरांगैः स्वांशकरस्थैर्न नीच-

व्ययगृहसहजस्यर्नापि सूर्यप्रविर्ष्टः।

तनयभवनसंस्थे वावपतौ चन्द्रयुक्ते

भवति मनुजनाथः कीरतिशुक्लीकृताशः ॥ ५७।।

57. Should the Moon be with Jupiter in the 5th house while Mars, Mercury and the Moon be not in fall in Rasi and be in their own Navamsas, onc will become a king with everlasting fame. In this Yoga, Mars and Mercury should be in the 12th and or the 3rd without combustion.

Notes: One will be famous and rich if none of Mars, Mercury and the Moon is in fall, cach obtaining his own Navamsa. Further the Moon should be in the 5th along with Jupiter while Mars and Mercury are relegated to the 12th/ 3rd singly or jointly and uncombust.

नीवारिवर्गर हितीविहर्गस्त्रभिस्तु

स्वांशोपर्र्बलयुतः गोक्षीरशंखधवलो मृगलाञ्छनश्च शुभदृष्टिवृष्टः ।

स्याद्यस्य जन्मनि स भूमिपतिजितारिः ।५८।।

58. If three planets be in such signs which are not inimical or debilitational, be with full strength, be aspected by benefics and be endowed with splendorous rays, the native will become a ruler.

कुमुदगहनबन्धुं श्रेष्ठमंशं प्रपन्नं

यदि बलसमुपेतः पश्यति ब्योमचारी। उदगभवनसंस्यः पापसंज्ञो न चैवं भवति मनुजनायः सार्वभौमः सुदेहः ॥ ५६।।

59. If the Moon, who is in Vargothama Navamsa or in Cancer in Navamsa, is aspected by a strong planet while the ascending sign is free from malefic occupation, the native will become an emperor endowed with physical splendour.

## जलचरराशिनवांशक इन्दौ तनुभवने शुभदः स्वकवगे।

## अशुभकरः खलु कष्टकहीनो भवति नृपो बहुवारणनाथः ॥ ६०।।

60. If the Moon is in a watery sign/Navamsa while a benefic is in the ascendant and in his own Amsa, the native will become a ruler endowed with many elephants and the like. In this yoga, malefics should, howcver, not be in angles.

बर्गोत्तमे हिमकरः सकलः स्थितोंऽशे

कुर्यान्महीपतिमपूर्वयशोभिरामम् यस्याश्ववृन्दलु रघातरजोभिभूतो I

भानुः प्रभातशशिनोऽनुकरोति रूपम् ।६१।।

61. If the Full Moon occupies Vargothama position, the native will become a ruler of the earth whose horses’ kicks will spread dust making even the brilliant Sun (in the sky) look like an carly mornings Moon.

थोगे चक्रवर्तोश्वरो भवेत्। सर्वप्रह्कृते एकंकेन तथा जाता मण्डलानामधीश्वराः ।।६२।।

62. If at birth all planets are endowed with roles of Yoga karaka, the native will become an emperor. If one planet be so, he will rule a province.

Notes: The word ‘Yoga Karaka' in this case should not be misunderstood with the usual designation of a planet becoming beneficial for an ascendant. For details, sec ch. 6 in vol. 1 and also Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra.

एकोऽपि विहगः कुर्यात्पञ्वमांशगतो नृपम्।

समस्तबलसम्पन्नश्चक्रर्वातनमेव

च ।६३।

63. Even if one planet occupies its own Panchamsa, the subject will become a ruler. If all are endowed with strength he becomes an emperor.

Notes: Panchamsa division is a little bit different from Trimsamsa. In odd signs the Panchamsa (i.e. 6 degrees in length) rulerships arc in order: Mars, Saturn, Jupiter, Mercury and Venus. The reverse holds good in the case of even signs.

यदि पश्यति चन्द्रमसं विबुधगुरव्षभसंस्थितं प्रसवे।

अवति पृथिव मुदय्रां स्फुरन्मणिद्योतितदिगन्ताम् ।६४।।

64. If the Moon is aspected by Jupiter from Taurus, the native will rule over the entire earth.

कुर्या्तुंगे त्रिकोणे वा सत्रराशिस्यो विलोकयन्।

निषादमपि ग्रहस्तुषारकिरणं पारयिवम् ।।६५।

65. If the Moon is aspected by any planet from its exaltation/Moolatrikona/own sign, even a hunter will become a king.

स्वगृहे तृतीयमागे शशी स्थितः पार्थिवं सदा कुस्ते।

परिरर्णबलः शुभदो यदि प्रसूती महाराजम् । ६६।।

66. Should the Moon be in her own Rasi and in her own decanate, one will become a ruler. If simultaneously a benefic is endowed with strcngth, the native will become a great king.

ह्वांश्चे स दिवाकरो यस्य स्वक्षेत्रे च षपाकरः । राजा गजदानौघशीकरोक्षितमृतलः ॥६७।।

67. Should the Sun be in his own Navamsa while the Moon is in Cancer Rasi, the native will become a king, endowed with elephants.

लग्ने रविपुत्रसंयुते देवेज्येऽस्तगृहे नवोदिते ।

दृष्टेऽसुरराजमन्त्रिणा ग्रामीणो नृपतिर्मवेदिह ॥ ६८।।

68. Even a villager will become a king if he has Saturn in the ascendant and Jupiter in the 7th, one of them being in aspect to Venus.

## उदयेऽसुरमन्त्रवरो गुरुभे गुरुदृष्टिपथं च गतः ।

## कुरते नियतं स-नृपं यदि तुंगगतश्च बुधः ।६६।।

69. The native will doubtless become a king if Venus rises in a Rasi of Jupiter in aspect to Jupiter himsclf while Mercury is in exaltation.

Notes: As Venus should ascend in a Rasi of Jupiter while Mercury is in exaltation, this yoga applies only to Sagittarius ascendant. To wit, Sagittarius should rise holding Venus in aspect to Jupiter as Mercury is in the 10th.

शुक्रमास्करेन्दवो जीववृष्टमा त्रकाः भावमेकमाश्रिताः । स्यात्तदा महीपतिः ।।७०।।

70. If the Sun, the Moon and Venus occupy one single house in exclusive aspect to Jupiter, the native will become a ruler.

लग्नगाः सितशशांकजभोभाः सप्तमे शशिनि वावपतियुदते । तिम्मरश्मितनयेन च दृष्टे जायते पृय्यशाः पृथिवीशः ।।७१।

71. One will become a ruler if Mars, Mercury and Venus are in the ascendant while both the Moon and Jupiter are in the 7th in aspect to Saturn.

विबुधगुरुर्यदि भौमनवांशे रुधिरनिरीक्षितपुर्णबलश्च । जन्यति कुत्सितजन्ममहीषं क्रियपरिसंस्थितकर्मगतोऽर्हः। ७२।। 72. If strong Jupiter be in aspect to Mars while the Sun

is in thc 10th in Aries, even a base person will become a rulcr. However, it is necessary that the said Jupiter be in Aries Navamsa or in Scorpio Navamsa.

Notes: This Yoga applies only to Cancer ascendant which can have the Sun in the 10th in exaltation. Jupiter should be aspected by Mars remaining in good houses with Jupiter falling in a Navamsa of Mars. The 3 planets referred to in this yoga are auspicious for Cancer ascendant being bossom friends of the ascendant lord.

तृतीयगाः शुकशशांकभास्कराः कुजोऽस्तसंस्थो नवमे बृहस्पतिः । गणोत्तमो लग्नगृहांशकोद्गमो यदा तदा हीनकुलो महोपतिः ।७३।।

73. If the Moon, the Sun and Venus are in the 3rd house while Mars and Jupiter are respectively placed in the 7th and 9th houses, even a person of mean strata will become a king. The ascendant in this case should be in Vargothamamsa.

जीवो बुधो भूगुसुतोऽव निशाकर वा धर्में विशुद्धतनवः स्कुटरश्मिजालाः । मिर्रेनिरीक्षितवृता यदि सूतिकाले

कुर्वन्ति देवसदृशं नृ्पत महान्तम् ॥७४।।

74. One, two, three or all of Mercury, Jupiter, Venus and the Moon occupying the 9th house with strength and in aspect to a friendly planct/s, the native will be a king akin to God.

Notes: One of the 4 planets mentioned in the sloka, any one or more can be in the 9th endowed with shadbala strength in aspect to a friendly planet or planets, the subject will acquire satwic qualities attributed to a divine incarnation. See the horoscope of Maharshi Ramana:

|         |               | Ketu |
|---------|---------------|------|
| Satum   | Mars          | Moon |
| Jupiter |               |      |
|         | Rasi          |      |
| Sun     | Mercury Lagna |      |
| Rahu    | Venus         |      |

The ascendant is in Libra while the Moon (along with Ketu, a spiritual planet) is in the 9th house, the house of spiritual career. The Moon is in Vargothama Navamsa in aspect to Jupiter and the Sun, both her friends. Bhagwan Ramana was one of the greatest sages adoring the spiritual history of India, well explained in "A Search in Secret India" by Dr. Paul Brunton, a British seeker. The conditions required by our current sloka are fulfilled fully. The sage's not becoming a material king but a spiritual king also stands explained by the 9th house filled with spiritual influence of the various heavenly bodies related thereto.

## तपोगृहं यस्य भवेत्तदुच्चकं प्रहेण तेनाथ युतं निरीक्षितम् । प्रह्यं स्वोच्चगतं यदा भवेत्तदा कुटुम्बी नियतं महीपतिः ।।७५।।

75. If the 9th house is occupied by an exalted planet or the said 9th house is in aspect to the planet that will be in exaltation therein (in the 9th) while two others are independently in their signs of exaltation, the native will become a king and be a pater familias.

Notes: Find out the planet that will be exalted in the 9th, if the 9th has not that planet therein. In the case of no exaltation taking place in the 9th, if the planet referred to above aspects the 9th, one of the conditions of the sloka will remaln fulfilled. Further two other planets must be in exaltation elsewhere. Then the native will become a successful householder endowed with wealth. This yoga does

not fully apply to Aries, Libra, Sagittarius, Gemini and Pisces ascendants.

सुतभवने शशिदेवनमस्यौ भवनपतिप्रसमीक्षितदेहौ । भुगुलनयो यदि मौनसमेतो भवति नृपः खलु कुरिसितवंशः ॥।७६।।

76. Should the 5th house be occupied by the Moon and Jupiter in aspect to its lord as Venus is in Pisces, even a person of base birth will become a king.

चन्द्रस्त्रपुष्करस्थः स्वोच्चे वचसां पतिः स-लक्ष्मीकम् ।

उत्पादयति स्वामिनमुत्तमपात्रं समग्रभुवः ।७७।।

77. If the Moon is in the 3rd or the 10th house while Jupiter is exalted, the native will be a ruler of the entire earth endowed with Lakshmi, the goddess of wealth.

केन्द्रस्वोचवमुपेतः सुरमन्त्री दशमगो यदा शुक्रः।

नूनं स भवति पुरुषः समस्तपृथ्वोश्वरः द्यातः ।७८।।

78. If Jupiter is exalted and is simultaneously in an angle while Venus occupies the 10th, the subject will be a famous king, lording over the entire earth.

स्वर्षे शशी विपुलरस्मिशिखाकलापाः स्वांशे स्थिता बुधबृहस्पतिदानवेद्याः ।

पातालगा दिनकरेण निरीक्षिताश्च

## संसूचयन्ति नृर्पाति द्विजवंशजातम् १७६।।

79. One born even as a Brahmin will become a king if the Full Moon is in her own house while Mercury, Jupiter and Saturn are in the 4th house. The latter 3 should be in their own navamsas simultaneously and be aspected by the Sun.

रविर्नभस्थः स्वत्रिकोणगोऽपि वा स्वराशिसंस्थाः सितजीवचन्द्राः । तृतीयवध्ठायगताश्च लग्नात्कुर्वन्ति गोपालमिह क्षितोशम् ।।८०।।

80. If the Sun is in the 10th identical with his Moolatrikona Rasi own house or Venus, Jupiter and the Moon are in 3rd, 6th and 11th the native will become a king.

सप्तमभवने सौम्या मित्रांशगताः सुद्वद्भरिह दृष्टाः ।

## उच्चे कुजो यदि नृपः समस्तभूपालकः श्रेष्ठः ।८१।

81. If the 7th house contains a benefic planet and be in aspect to a friendly planet while Mars is exalted, one will become a ruler of the earth. The benefic in question should fall in a friendly Navamsa also.

रविशशिबुधवर्व्योम्तिमित्रांशकस्थ-

न च रिपुभवनस्यर्नाप्यदृश्यरन नीचेः ।

स तपसि भूगुपुत्रे भूपतिः स्यात्प्रयाणे

गजमदजलसेक्लीयते यत्य - रेणुः ।२।।

82. Should the Sun, the Moon, Mercury and Mars occupy the 10th house and also friendly Navamsas while Venus is in the 9th house, the native will become a rulcr, whose elephants' ichor will settle the dust emanating by their own treadings. In this yoga, the planets in the 10th shall not be in fall, in enemies' camp or be in combustion.

स्वोच्वे भानुः प्रकटितबलो व्योममध्ये सजीवः

शुक्रो धमें यदि बलयुतः स्वं नवांशं प्रपन्नः।

लग्ने वर्गे शुभगगनगो चन्द्रपुत्रेण दृष्टः

पृथ्वीपालो घवलितजगरस्यात्सितैः स्वर्यशोभिः ।८३।

83. Should the Sun endowed with strength be exalted in the 10th house in the company of Jupiter, as strong Venus is in the 9th house and in own Navamsa while the ascendant shares benefic sub-divisions or has a benefic planet in it and is aspected by Mercury, the native will become a ruler illumining the entire world with his fame.

वृचे शशी लग्नगतः सुपूर्णः सितेन दृष्टो वणिजि स्थितेन ।

बुधोऽपि पातालगतो यदि स्यात्तदान्यजातो भवति क्षितोशः ।८४।।

84. If the Full Moon is in Lagna identical with Taurus in aspect to Venus in Libra while Mercury is in the 4th, even a person not belonging to royal scion will be a ruler.

Notes: This yoga suffers a defect in that while the Moon is full or nearing her fullness in Taurus, Mercury cannot be in Leo as the Sun will be in Scorpio or ahead only. For Mercury to be in Leo, the Sun at best can be in Virgo in which case the Moon will only be decreasing. May be the text turned faulty in form with the march of time passing through so many hands.

क्षमासुतः स्वोचवमुपाश्रितो यदा

रवीन्दुवाचस्पतिर्भिनिरीक्षितः ।

भवेग्नरेन्द्रोऽपि च कुत्सितस्तदा

समस्तपृथ्वीपरिरक्षणे क्षमः ।८५।।

85. If Mars occupies his exaltation sign in aspect to the Sun, the Moon and Jupiter, even a person belonging to base strata will rule over the entire earth.

जायतेऽभिजिति यः शुभकर्मा भूपतिर्भवति सोऽतुलवीर्यः । नीववेश्मकुलजोऽपि नरोऽस्मिन् राजयोग इति न व्यपदेशः ॥ ८६।।

86. One born in Abhijit star will become a valorous king even though he may be of base order by birth.

गण्डान्तविष्टिपरिघव्यतिपातजात-

स्ताराधिपः समुदये यदि कृत्तिकायास् ।

ऋीडेत्रपाणफलकाहित बण्डवेग-

प्रोत्थापिताहितशिरोगुलिकाभिरीशः ।८७।।

87. One born with the Moon in Krittika and simulta-

neously in Gandanta Lagna or Vishti (Bhadra) Karana or Parigha yoga or Vyatipata yoga will become a king.

Notes: The Moon should be constantly in Krittika while one of the following should stmultaneously prevail:

(a) Gandanta Lagna (see page 27 vol. 1) (b) Vishti Karana (also called Bhadra Karana) (c) Vyatipata—one of the Nitya yogas. (This should not be misunderstood here to be one of the Upagrahas) (d) Parigha Nitya Yoga

For the order and calculation of Karanas, Nitya Yogas etc., refer to Panchangas and standard works on astrology. Normally these are marked in individual horoscopes also.

बुधोदये सप्तमगे बृहस्पतौ चन्द्रे कुलीरे मुखरा शिगेऽमले । वियद्गते भार्गवनन्दने गृहे प्रशास्ति पृथ्वीं मनुजो निराकुलः ।८।।

88. The Moon in the 4th house identical with Cancer while Mercury, Jupiter and Venus are respectively in the ascendant, 7th and 10th houses: the native with this yoga will become a king.

एकान्तर्गविहगः वडिभश्चक्रं क्षितीश्वरं कुर्यात् । अर्षैव शुने लग्ने सकलमहीपालको नृपतिः ।८६।। अयमेव समुद्रास्यो हो लग्ने यदि संस्थितौ। करोति नूभुजां नाचं सौम्यैः केन्द्रेषु संस्थिते ।eo

89-90. If all the planets occupy six alternative signs from the ascendant, Chakra yoga is formed producing a king. In this process, if a benefic occupies the ascendant, the native will be a ruler of the entire earth. If two planets are in the ascendant while benefics are disposed in angles in the said configuration, Samudra Yoga is formed making one a king.

निरन्तरं पवि भवनेषु षद्सु ग्रहाः स्थिता उद्यनुहात्सदह्ताः । स्वरपंक्तदन्तरपतिमेव कुर्युश्चतुष्टयन्नरपतिमन्त्रिणं ख ।।८ १11

91. Should all the planets occupy the first six houses commencing from the ascendant, one will become a king. Should these be in only four houses from the ascendant, he will become a king's minister.

सुतसुखदुश्विक्यगता यदि कर्मणि कीर्तयन्ति यवनाद्याः ।

बन्धुसृतार्यगजाढचो बहुमृत्यो जायते क्षितिपः ।।८२।।

92. Should all the plancts be relegated to the 5th, 4th, 3rd and 10th houses, one will become a king endowed with wealth, sons, relatives, conveyances and scrvants.

कर्मास्तजलहोरासु ग्रहाः सर्वे प्रतिष्ठिताः ।

कुर्वन्ति नगरं नाम यत्र स्वास्पृथिवीपतिः (६३।।

93. Nagara ( ) Yoga is formed if all the planets are disposed in the 10th, 7th, 4th and ascendant which will make one rule the earth.

सुखतनुमदगाः शुमाः \_\_ समग्राः कुजरविरविजासित्रशत्रुलाभसंस्थाः । यदि भवति महीपतिः प्रशान्तो

यवनपतिकृतो ह्चयं महीपयोगः ।।८४।।

94. Should the benefic planets be entirely in the 4th, ascendant and the 7th while Mars, the Sun and Saturn are in the 3rd, 6th and I1th houses, one born will become a ruler, according to Yavanas.

लाभधर्मस्थिताः सौम्याः पाषाः कर्मणि संस्थिताः।

नपतीनामयं योगो भवेर्कलशसंज्ञितः ।।८ ५।

95. A Raja yoga known as Kalasa (T) Yoga is formed if all benefics are in the 1 1th and 9th houses.

बयो प्रहा स्रातृसुतायसंस्यास्तया शुभौ द्रो रिपुसंगती च । कलत्रलग्नं च गतौ च शेवौ नृपस्य योगः खलु पूणकुम्भः ।८६।।

96. Again a Raja yoga named as Kumbha ( JFW ) Yoga is formed if the 3rd, 5th and 11th houses are occupied by one each while the 6th and 7th are occupied by 2 planets each.

सुविस्तरं नीचकुलोद्भवा मया

अतः विनचरित्रर्पाः कथिताः क्षितीश्वराः । परं पारविववंशजन्मनां

131. Should Jupiter with his splendorous rays be in the ascendant while the Moon is in the 11th in aspect to Mercury, and the Sun is in the 2nd from the Moon unaspected by Mars one will become an emperor of the entire earth endowed with elephants, horses etc.

133. A virtuous, famous and valorous ruler is born if Mercury, Jupiter and Venus are together in the 5th while the Sun and Saturn are in the 6th and 9th houses in their order as Mars is exalted.
