प्रथितयशा भवेक्क्षतिपतिः क्षपितारिगणः ।।१३४।।

134. If the Sun, Venus and Saturn respectively are in aspect to Jupiter, Moon and Mars while a movable sign is ascendant, a famous and successful king is born.

बुवः कन्यालग्ने सुरपतिगुरुश्चैव तिमिगः

हियतः क्षोणीपुत्र: सहजभवने वीर्यसहितः ।

श्निः शत्रुस्य ने त्रिदशरिपुपुर्यश्च हिबुके

दरदवं स्यात्सृतौ स्वमुजविजयी भूपतिरिह ।।१३५।।

135. Mercury in Virgo identical with the asccndant, Mars in the 3rd house, Saturn in the 6th house and Venus in the 4th house: this configuration will give birth to a king who will conquer the world with his own strength.

यमे व िलग्ने मकरप्रतिष्ठिते दिवाकरे चूनगते सितेऽष्टमे ।

कुजेऽलिगे कर्कटगे निशाकरे भवेतप्रसिद्धो जगतीश्वरो नृपः ।।१३६।।

136. One will be a king lording over the earth if Saturn is in Capricorn ascendant while thc Sun, Venus, Mars, and the Moon are respectively in the 7th, 8th, Scorpio and Cancer.

मृगोदये भूमिसुते सुनिर्मले शनेश्वरे धर्मगृहे व्यवस्थिते ।

दिवाकरे सप्तमगे सहेन्दुना चलस्वभावो नृपतिः प्रजायते ।१३७।

137. A fickle-minded king is born if Mars is exalted in the ascendant as the 9th and the 7th are respectively occupied by Saturn and the luminaries.

## शनश्रे लग्नगते सचन्द्रे बृहस्पतौ सप्तमराशिगे च ।

शुश्रेण दृष्टे शशिजे स्वतुंगे जायेत पृ वीपतिरप्रवृष्यः ।।१३८।।

138. Capricorn ascendant containing Saturn, Cancer containing its lord and Jupiter, Virgo containing its lord in aspect to Venus: this configuration will produce an inconquerable ruler.

वापे \_\_\_ भवेत्सुरगु्रहितदृष्टिशुद्धो लग्ने सुरारिदयितः शशिनि स्वराशौ। नरोऽ्त्र वापीतडागसुरवेश्मकरो जायेत मानवपतिदिजदेवभवतः ॥ १३६।।

139. Venus is the ascendant, Jupiter in Sagittarius with friendly aspect and the Moon in Cancer: this group of heavenly bodies will make one a king who will construct wells, tanks and temples and will honour Brahmins and Gods.

एकः स्वोच्चे शुभगगनगः संस्थितो निर्मलांशुः

केन्द्रे भानुः प्रकटितकरः केवलः पूर्णवीर्यः।

दृष्टः कुर्यादमरगुर्णा पञ्चमस्थेन जातं

भूमेर्नाचं बहुगजर्पाति सर्ववन्द्यं कृतार्यम् ॥१४०।।

140. With even a single planet with unobstructed rays if exalted in an angle and the Sun being in angle with extreme power in aspect to Jupiter posited in the 5th house, the native will become an honourable king.

बच्डे कुर्जाकरवयः सहजेऽ्यवाऽपि

दृष्टः सिहे सुरारिसचिवोऽ्य भवर्षसंर्थः । शुर्भेदिनकरेन्दु-विहीनदृध्टिः कुर्यान्तृपं स्वमुजनिर्जितशत्रुक्षम् ।।१४१।।

141. If Saturn, Mars and the Sun are in the 6th or even in the 3rd house while Jupiter occupies the 11th house identical with Leo one will become a king conquering his enemies with his own might. In this Yoga, Jupiter should be aspected by other benefics but not by the luminaries.

बहति मृ्ुसमीरे निर्मलथ्योममधये

विमलनिरुपसर्गाः खेवरा उदयति सुरबन्धे मण्डले मातृकाणां

वृत्तर्वराः।

यदि वृषभगृहस्यो भार्गवः स्यारक्षतीशः ।।१४२।।

142. If Libra is ascendant with Jupiter in it while Venus is in Taurus, a king is born. While the two heavenly bodies are so disposed at birth, there be blowing pleasant breeze, the skies be clear and the other planets should be enjoying full rays and temporary friendship (by stipulated placements).

शशिबुधरुधिरास्यैः स्वांशकस्यर्न नीचे-

व्ययगृहसहजस्वर्नापि तनयभवनसंस्थे वाष्पतौ चन्द्रयुक्ते

सुर्यत्रविष्टः ।

भवति मनुजनायः कोर्तिशुक्लोक्ताशः ।।१४३।।

143. A famous king is born with the following planetary map: Mars, the Moon and Mercury in the 12th and the 3rd avoiding debilitation/combustion; but being in their respectivc own navamsas while the 5th house is occupied by Jupiter and the Moon.

अधिमित्रगृहे केन्दरे जन्माधिपतिर्विलग्नपतियुद्तः ।

पश्यति बलपरिपूर्णो लग्नं स्यात्पुष्कलो योगः ।।१४४।। पुष्य लयोगे पुरुषा जायन्ते भूमिपालका नित्यम् ।

सुचिरं ध्रमन्ति हतरियुगजमदगन्धेन यासितदिगन्ताः ।।१४५।।

144-145. If the Moon sign lord and the ascendant lord be with strength and be posited in an angle identical with a close friend’s sign and aspect the ascendant, Pushkala Yoga is formed. The effects are: the native will become a king on whose invasion the band of elephants of his enemies will exude ichor perfuming all the corners.

## राश्यादौ लग्नपतिः करोति जातं नरेन्द्रदण्डपतिम्।

## मध्ये मण्डलनायं ग्रामपाति चैव भवनान्ते ।।१४६।।

146. If the ascendant lord is in the beginning of a sign, the native will be an army chlef or Justice with the king. If the planet in question be in the middle of a sign, he will be the head of a state. He will only be a village chief if the ascendant lord is in the end of a sign.

Notes: The hints given in this verse will lead to estimate the extent of the effects of the various Yogas explained, based on the position of the Lagna Lord while a Raja Yoga is formed.

## पौध्ये फाल्मुन्दां वा मूले पुव्ये च भास्करः कुस्ते।

## लग्नगतो नरनाथं योजनशतमात्रके देशे ।१४७।।

147. The Sun in Revati, Poorva Phalguni, Uttara Phalguni or Moola identical with ascendant will make one the head of a country extending to only hundred yojanas.

कृत्तिका रेवतीस्वातीपुध्यस्थायी भूगोः सुतः । करोति भूमुजां नाथमश्वित्यामपि संस्थितः ।।१४८।।

148. If Venus is in the ascendant, one becomes a ruler, provided the planet in question simultaneously occupies one of the asterisms, viz. Krittika, Revati, Swati, Pushyami and Aswini.

विदधाति सार्वभौमं लग्नांशपतिः स्वतुंगगः केन्द्रे।

नृपाति लम्नाधिपतिर्जन्माधिपतिर्धनसमृद्धम् ॥ १४६।।

149. If the Navamsa ascendant lord be exalted in an angle, the native will become an emperor. If the lords of the Moon sign and asccndant are in an angle, the subject will be a weaithy king.

Notes: Note who is the Lagna Lord in the Navamsa chart. If the said planet occupies his exaltation Rasi a favourable-Raja Yoga is formed. If the dispositor of the Moon and the ruler of the ascendant be together in an angle, a wealthy person is denoted.

| मीने निशाकर: पूर्णः |    | सुह्वद्गहनिरीक्षितः ।             |
|---------------------|----|-----------------------------------|
| सार्वभौमं           | नर | कुर्यात्सिद्धाताकं न संशयः ।१५०।। |

150. If the Full Moon in Pisces is in aspect to a friendly planet, the subject will become an emperor whose orders will be obeyed by all.

याते भौमे कर्मस्थानं शिशिरकरमुगुसुतस्तपः - समवस्थितः।

आये स्वोच्चे प्राप्तो भानु-

स्त्रिदशपतिसचिवसहितो यदि प्रसबे भवेत् ।।१५१।।

| क्षोणीभर्ता |             |                                           |  | याने |  | यस्य |
|-------------|-------------|-------------------------------------------|--|------|--|------|
|             |             | प्रविचलिततुरगरजसा विशः परितो गताः।        |  |      |  |      |
|             | कर्तुर्भूयो |                                           |  |      |  | भूयो |
|             |             | घरणितलपरिमलसुखं प्रयान्ति रवेह्वाः ।।१४२। |  |      |  |      |

151-152. Should exalted Sun be in the 11th along with Jupiter while Mars is in the 10th and the Moon with Venus is in the 9th, a king is born. Even the celestial horses of the Sun god’s chariot will takc pleasurc at the pleasant smell emanating from the dust spread over the earth by the treading horses of the native’s army.

शशिसहिते केन्द्रस्ये शनेश्चरे भवति जारजातत्तु।

राजा गजतुरगग्रामधर्नर्वर्धितश्रीकः ।।१५३।।

153. Even though the native may have been born out of wedlocks, the Moon with Saturn in an angle will make one a wealthy king.

शुक्रवाक्पतिबुद्धर्धनसंस्वैर्यूनर्ग शशिर विक्षितिपुत्रः ।

जायते क्षितिपतिः पृथुवक्षाः सर्वतः क्षपितशत्रुसमूहः ।।१५४।।

154. One will become a king with broad chest defeating all his enemies if the 2nd house contains Venus, Jupiter and Mercury while relegated to the 7th are the Moon, the Sun and Mars.

भानुः प्राणी शशिगृह्युतः शीतरश्मश्च तस्मि-

न्नेकः स्वोच्चे यदि गगनगो निर्मलः पूर्णरशष्मः ।

लग्नं प्राप्तः सुरपतिपुरुः षष्ठगः स्याक्क्षितोश-

श्छन्तो यस्य प्रचलितचमूरेणुभिर्थ्योममार्गः ॥ १५५।।

155. Strong Sun and Moon in Cancer, another planet with clear rays exalted elsewhere, Jupiter in the ascendant and Mars in the 6th: the five heavenly bodies so disposed will makc one a king whose army's movements will cover the skies with dust.

## कुस्भस्याव्टममागे त्रिकोणसंस्वे निशानाथे।

जातो भवत्यवश्यं राजा शुभदः समस्तलोकस्य ।।१५६॥।

156. If the Moon is in the 8th degrce of Aquarius identical with a trine one will become a king who will be favourable to all.

Notes: In this sloka and the following ones, the words 'Amsa', 'Bhaga' etc. have been rendered identically as “degree” instead of Navamsa. For 21st Amsa/Bhaga etc. appear in these slokas.

मेवस्य सप्तमांशे करोति पृथ्वीसुतः स्थितो नृपतिम् ।

एकाधिकाविशेऽशे नरमिथुनांशे भवेद्भूपः ।।१५७।

157. If Mars is placed in the 7th degree of Aries or in the 21st degree of Gemini, the native will become a king.

कुम्मस्य पञ्ववशके भागे बन्द्रः स्यतो महीपालम् ।

कर्कटकस्य च दशमे करोति पुर्षं सदा प्रभवे ।।१५८।।

158. If at birth the Moon is positioned in the 15th degree of Aquarius or in the 10th degree of Cancer, one will become a king.

धनुचि च बिशे जीवः करोति नृर्पात स्थितो जनस्यातम् ।

सिहत्व पञ्चमांशे तथा च हेलिर्बुधो नेयः ।१५६II

159. A famous king is born if Sagittarius 20° is occupied by Jupiter. Similarly Leo 15° occupied either by the Sun or by Mercury will produce a king.

## एकस्मिन्पञ्वक्तौ पञ्चदशोऽशे स्थितश्चन्त्रः ।

## भागे च बोरनृर्पात करोति भुजलब्धपृथ्वीकम् ।।१६०।

160. A valorous king is denoted who will acquire kingdom with his own might if the Moon is placed on the 15th degree of any sign but obtaining 5 vargas of one and the same sign.

## मकरस्य पञ्चमांशे करोति रविजो नरेश्वरं सुमगम्। योने भूतलतिलकं धर्मज्ञं शास्त्रनिरतं च ।।१६१।

161. If Saturn occupies the 5th degree of Capricorn, a fortunate king is born who will be adorable on the earth, be meritorious and be interested in Sastras.

कर्कटके शशिजीवी पञ्वसु भागेवु संस्थितौ कुर्तः ।

भूमिपतिमप्रधृश्यं र रविरिव सर्वग्रहगणस्य ।।१६२।

162. If both the Moon and Jupiter are on the 5th degree of Cancer, onc will become an inviolable king just as the Sun is head of the plancts.

चन्द्रः पुष्ये नृपति वर्गोत्तमकृत्तिकाश्विनीसंस्थः । विदधाति सार्वभौमं त्रिपुष्करे वाऽपि परिपूर्णः ।१६३।।

163. If the Moon is in Pushyami, or in vargothama or in Krittika or Aswini nakshatras an emperor is born. Similar effect will come to pass when birth is in Tripushkara.

Notes: The first Yoga is clear in this sloka. The second one needs clarification about 'Tripushkara'.

This is a combination of three phenomena together. That is of a certain titht coinciding with a ccrtain week day and Nakshatra. See the following:

Anyone of the Bhadra Tithis, viz. Dwitiya (2nd), Sapthami (7th) and Dwadasi (12th) of both Fortnights coinciding with Sunday/Tuesday/Saturday ruled by a three-legged asterism, constitutes a Tripushkara. These asterisms are those which have only three quarters in one sign and one quarter in the other. To wit such asterisms are: Krittika, Punarvasu, Uttara Phalguni, Visakha, Uttarashada and Poorvabhadra. To be clear, one born on Tuesday or Sunday or Saturday which is ruled by one of the said six tithis and one of the said six asterisms cited will be a king.

The above explaination for Tripushkara is from Jatakabharana (Hindi version p. 226). These are discussed as causing illegal births also in Jataka Parijata (ch. 3) and Hora Ratna of Balabhadra (Hindi version p. 127). In any context, a total of 108 Tripushkaras can be obtained, and the weekdays for this purpose in Hindi Astrology are countable from Sun rise to Sun rise of the day concerned.

## अश्वन्यनुराधास्थः स्थितः श्रविष्ठासु पार्थिवं भौमः ।

## कुर्ते स्वोचवमुपगतो वर्गोत्तमगश्च नाग्यत्र ।।१६४।।

164. If Mars is in Aswini, or Anuradha or Dhanishta, a Raja yoga is formed. This applies only if the said planet is in Vargothama Navamsa or in exaltation Rasi simultaneously.

Notes: The said Raja yogas, three in number, caused by Mars should be beneficially understood. The first one is generated by his exaltation position in Rasi in Dhanishta, i.e. in the first two quarters of Dhanishta ending Capricorn. This extends from 23° 20' to 30° of the exaltation sign of Mars. The relevant Navamsas for the planet will be in Leo and Virgo: So Vargothama Navamsa will not apply to the first yoga. The other two yogas relate to the position of Mars in Vargothama Navamsa remaining in the stars Aswini and Anuradha. He is in Vargothama in Aswini Ist quarter in Aries and in the 4th quarter of Anuradha in Scorpio. To wit, Mars in Dhanishta lst quarter, Dhanishta 2nd quarter, Aswini Ist quarter or in Anuradha 4th quarter can cause an excellent Raja Yoga. There is no specific house related in the formation of the 3 yogas. It, however, is implied that if Mars owns good houses, or posited in good houses or wellrelated to the rising sign, will produce an exemplary yoga, in these asterisms.

## व्योम्नि शंखघवलो निशाकरो भार्यवस्तपसि संस्थितः शुचिः ।

आयपाश्च यदि सर्वे एव ते स्यान्महोपतिरतुस्यपौरुवः ॥१९५।

165. An excellent king is born if the Moon with rays

akin to white conch is in the 10th house while strong Venus is in the 9th house and all others relegated to the 11th house.

Notes: The Moon should be in the 10th while Venus is in the 9th. Some people interpret "Sankhadhavalo nisaakaro” as Full Moon. But Venus will not be so close to Full Moon as to be just a sign apart. Hence I have translated the relevant phrase literally as “the Moon with rays akin to white conch" and we may understand it logically that Moon should not be in combustion in the Sun as the Sun will have access to combust the Moon in the 10th when Venus is mentioned in the 9th. The main requirement seems to be that the Moon should be unafflicted by combustion and hence Kalyana Varma uses such a phrase. All others.including the Sun should be in the 11th so that the Moon cannot be actually FULL, while she will only be decreasing.

## चन्द्रादुपचयसंस्था गगनसबः सर्व एव यदि सूतौ ।

## जायेत माननिलयः समस्तपृवीपतिः पुरुषः ।।१६६।।

166. If all the other planets are disposed in 3rd, 6th, 10th and 11th houses counted from the Moon, a king is born who will be a repository of honour and will rule over the entire earth.

## जीवनिशाकरसूर्या: पञ्चमनवमतृतीयगा वक्रात् । यदि भवति तदा राजा कुबेरतुल्यो धनर्बासौ ।१६७।।

167. If Jupiter, Moon and Sun respectively occupy 5th, 9th and 3rd houses when counted from Mars, the native will become a king, equal to Kubera (the lord of celestial riches and regent of the north).

रविस्तृतीये भुमुनन्वनः सुखे बुधस्तयान्ये यदि पञ्चमे स्थिताः । न नीचराशो न च शत्रुवेश्मगा भवेन्नरेन्रस्त्रिसमुद्रपालकः ॥ १ ६८।। 168. Counted from Mercury if the Sun is in the 3rd,

Venus in the 4th and all others in the 5th, a king is born who will have 3 seas in the form of periphery for his land. In this yoga, no planet shall be in fall or in inimical sign.

बृहस्पतेनी मदिवाकरेन्दवो गता द्वितीयाम्बुनमःस्यलं क्रमात् । विपक्षमावे परिशेषखेचरा यदा तबा भूमिरतिन् पात्मजः ॥ १६६।

169. If the 2nd, 4th and 10th houses counted from Jupiter are respectively occupied by Saturn, the Sun and 'the Moon while all others are in the 6th, a prince will become a ruler.

भुगोरपत्याद्बुप्रभास्करात्मजी चतुष्ट्यस्थी परिशेषखेवराः । तृतीवला दर्षगतास्तु ते यदा महीर्षात कुर्युरसंशयं तदा । १७०।

170. If Mercury and Saturn are in the 4th and 8th counted from Venus while all others are in the 3rd and 11th, one will become a king.

## शुक्र्धी रवितनयात्केन्द्रे वाचस्पतिर्भवेदुच्चे ।

सिहासनाधिशायी यदि राजा स्वोच्चगाश्च परिशेषाः ॥१७१।

171. If Venus and Mercury occupy an angle from Saturn while the rest are in their exaltation signs, an excellent king will be born.

## सवितुस्तृतीयपञ्चमलामर्षसमाश्रिताः सदा यस्थ ।

सर्वे प्रहाः स नृपतिर्मन्त्री सेनापतिर्वाऽपि ।।१७२।।

172. If all the plancts are in the 3rd, 5th and 11th houses from the Sun, one will become a king, or a minister or an army chief.

लग्नपतेः स्फुटरश्मेः पापा लाभे शुमाश्च केन्द्रस्याः।

यदि भवति तदा नृपतिः स्वभुजाजितसर्वभूमितलः ।१७३।।

173. Should there be a malefic in the 11th from the as-

cendant lord who has clear rays while a benefic is in an angle (from the ascendant), the native will become a king who will have self-earned kingdom.

## लाभे तृतीययच्ठे यदि पापा जन्मपस्य शुभदृष्टाः । भवति तदा धरणीशः समस्तनृपवर्दितः साधुः ॥१७४।।

174. A virtuous king is denoted, with honours from other kings, if all malefics occupy the 11th, 3rd and 6th houses counted from the Moon sign lord and be in aspect to benefics.

विचरति सुरपूज्यो मेषभेऽ्यापि सिहे

दहनकिरणदृष्टे भूमिपुत्रे स्वराशौ ।।

न च गगनविवारी कश्चिदेकोऽपि नीचे

यदि नृपतिसमुत्यो जायते पाविवेन्द्रः ।।१७५।।

175. If Jupiter occupies Aries /Leo while Mars is in his own sign in aspect to the Sun one of royal descent will become a king. It is understood that no other planet shall be in fall in the nativity.

चन्द्राद्धहैनिगदिताः सुनफादयश्च केन्द्रस्थितेयदि भवन्ति च तेऽत्र योगाः ।

विश्वम्भराधिपकुलेषु महत्मु जाता

योगेषु तेषु मनुजेश्वरतां समन्ते ॥।१७६।

176. If Sunapha Yoga etc., from the Moon are found operating in angles, one born in a royal scion will definitely become a ruler of men.

Notes: Apart from the Sunapha etc., formed by others than the Sun when counted from the Moon as is normally known (i.e. in the 2nd, 12th or 1st of the Moon), these yogas form even in lunar angles. For details, see p. 150 of vol. 1 of this edition as well Horasara.

केन्द्रगौ यदि तु जीवशशांको यस्य जन्मनि च भार्गवदृष्टौ ।

भूपतिर्भवति सोऽ्तुलकीर्तिर्नीचगो यदिन कश्चिदिह स्यात् ।।१७७।।

177. Should the Moon and Jupiter be in an angle from the asccndant in aspect to Venus, the native will become a king with widespread fame provided there is no debilitated planet in the nativity.

## ◦ उदयशिखरिसंस्थो भार्गवो यत्र तत्र

## बुधरविसृतदृष्टः स्वांशकस्योऽतिवीर्यः । जनयति नरनाथं वाष्पतौ पञ्चमस्थे

भुजबलहतशत्रुं सार्वभौमं गजाढचम् ॥ १७८।

178. If Venus is in the ascendant in aspect to Mercury and Saturn while Jupiter is in the 5th, the native will becomc an emperor. However, thc said Venus should be strong and in his own navamsa simultaneously.

## सिहे कमलिनीनाथ: कुलीरस्यो निशाकरः।

## वृष्टौ द्वावपि जीवेन पार्थिवं कुस्तस्तदा ।१७६।।

179. If thc Sun and the Moon are in their own houses (while one of them is in aspect to Jupiter), the native will become a king.

## बुधः कर्कटमारूढो वाक्पतिश्च धनुर्घरम् ।

## सूर्यभूसुतदृष्टौ च यदि स्फीतो महिपतिः ॥१८०।

180. Mercury in Cancer and Jupiter in Sagittarius in aspect to both Mars and the Sun will make the native a ruler.

## शफरीयुगले चन्द्रः कर्कटे च बृहत्पतिः ।

## शुष्कः कुम्मे यवा शक्तस्तदा राजा भवेदिह ॥ १८१।।

181. Should the Moon, Jupiter and Venus be respectively positioned in Pisces, Cancer and Aquarius, the native will become a ruler.

## सितदृष्टः शनिः कुम्भे पद्धिनीवयितो भवे।

## चन्दे जलचरे राशौ यदि जातो नृपो भवेत् ॥।१८२।

182. If Saturn is in Aquarius in aspect to Venus while the Sun is in the 11th and the Moon in a watery sign, one will become a king.

कुजोऽलिगोऽ्य मेषे वा रविजीवनिरीक्षितः ।

वृषे शो जीवसंदृष्टस्तदाऽपि पृथिवीपतिः ।।१८३।।

183. If Mars posited in his own house is aspected by Jupiter and the Sun, or Mercury posited in Taurus if aspected by Jupiter, a ruler is born.

## अमलवपुरवकः करवाणां विकासी

स्वगृहमथ नवांशं स्वोच्चभांशं गतो वा।

हितगगननिवासः पञ्चभिद् श्यमाना

जनयति जगतीशं नीचचे नो यदि स्यात् ।।१८४।

184. If the Full Moon is in her exaltation/own Rasi or Navamsa and be aspted by 5 planets having temporary friendship while no planet is fallen, the native will become a ruler.

लामे मन्दो गुरुभ्गुसुताबुद्ग्ने खे शशांको

बन्धावर्को बुधकुतनयावर्थगौ चेत्स भपः।

यत्सेनायास्ततमबजलक्षोभतो

वारणेर्दे-

म्यः सेतोः स्मरति सहसा क्षोभितान्तोऽम्बुराशिः ॥ १८५॥

185. Saturn in the 11th, Jupiter with Venus in the ascendant, the Moon in the 10th, the Sun in the 4th, Mercury in the 2nd along with Mars: this configuration will make

one a king endowed with vast army, wealth and conveyances.

हृति इह्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां राजयोगाध्यायो नाम पञ्चत्रियः II

Thus ends the 35th Chapter entitled “Rajayogas” in Kalyana Varma's Saravali.

## अय रसिमचिन्ताध्यायः ॥ ३६।।

## Chapter 36

## Planetary Rays

रमिमप्रधानमेतद्यस्माच्छात्रं वदन्ति मणिन्याः । तस्मात्प्रयत्नतोऽहं कथयामि यथामतं तेवाम् ।।१।। 1. The rays possessed by a planet are important (in delineation of a horoscope) according to Manindhacharya. I explain below the same.

Notes: Like Shadbala, the number of rays obtained by a planet in a nativity is another guide to know its strength. Planets with less number of rays will harm the bhavas they own, aspect etc., while the ones with higher number of rays will turn beneficial.

स्वोच्वस्ये दस सूर्ये नव चन्द्रे पञ्च भूमितनवेश्व। पञ्चेन्बुजे सुरेज्ये सप्ताष्टौ भार्गवे शनी पञ्च ।।२।। 2. When in exaltation, the number of rays possessed by the Sun are 10, the Moon 9, Mars 5, Mercury 5, Jupiter 7, Venus 8 and Satun 5.

Notes: The author gives the maximum number of rays obtainable by a planet when in its exaltation. These rays apply in full when the planet is on its deep exaltation point. For example, Jupiter gets 7 rays in full if he is on the fifth degree of Cancer which is his deep exaltation degree. And when he reaches the simllar degree in Capricorn his rays become nil. That is, in an angular distance of 180 degrees from 5° Cancer to 5° Capricorn he loses one ray per every 25° 43' of covered longitude. Conversely from the 5th degree

of Capricorn for every similar quantity of covered arc he gets one ray. Similarly take the case of the Sun. His total rays are 10. From Libra 10th degree for every eighteen degrees he gets one ray and when he reaches the 10th degree of Aries, his full rays of 10 will be acquired by him. Thereafter for every 18 degrces he loses one ray and gets no Rasmi bala when he comcs to 10 degrees of Libra. The reader can accordingly work out the rays for all the plancts in a given horoscope. Needless to mention, the nodes have no such Rasmi bala. To have mathematical accuracy, the rays can be worked out in decimals. The rays so obtained for all the seven planets are processed further and added together and the effects such number gives are discussed in the following verses.

एत्रं महेन्द्रशास्त्रे मणिन्धमयबादरायणप्रोक्ते। सप्त प्रत्येकस्था निदिष्टा रश्मयो ग्रहाणां वा॥३। सर्वे प्रमाणमेते मुनिवचनात् किन्तु सप्तसंस्यव। बहुवावयादस्माकं नीचगतः स्याद्विगतरश्मिः ।।४।

3-4. ANOTHER SCHOOL OF THOUGHT: The above view is held by Manindha, Maya and Badarayana as enunciated by Mahendra Sastra. However, according to another school of thought, all planets uniformly possess 7 rays each while in exaltation. While these two are authoritative schools, a majority lean towards equal distribution of 7 rays. All agree that at debilitation, the figure is zero for each planet.

Notes: Kalyana Varma gives another school of thought giving a different number of rays obtainable by the various heavenly bodies as 7 for each planet when in exaltation and zero when in debilitation. He also mentions that a majority of expounders belleve that seven rays are possessed impartially, each by the 7 planets making the total as 49 which total however agrees with the uneven distribution as given in sloka 2. He also states that the allotment made in sloka 2 as per Badarayana, Maya, Manindha etc., is also authoritative. (Some indentify Badarayana as Veda Vyasa Ma-

harshi, son of sage Parasara). Parasara also gives the number of rays as given in sloka 2 above, vide Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra, ch. 73, subscribing to his venerable father’s school.

Rasmi bala should not be mistaken to be simply exaltation strength as this is possessed further vlde following slokas.

## अभिमुखरश्मर्नोचाद्ब्ध्टः स्वोच्वात्परांमुखो ज्ञेयः । अन्तरगतेऽनुपातो यथा तया संत्रवक्ष्यामि ।५।।

5. ABHIMUKHA AND PARANMUKHA RASMI: When a debilitated planet transits towards its exaltation sign, the rays are Abhimukha (i.e. facing upwards) and when an exalted planet goes towards its debilitation sign, the rays are Paranmukha (facing downwards). For the intermediary positions, the rays should be calculated by rule of three process.

Notes: “Abhimukha” additionally means favourable while "Paranmukha” means unfavourable. This is further explained in slokas 28-29 of the present chapter.

नोचविहीनः शोध्यश्चक्रात्यङ् भवनतो यदाभ्यधिकः ।

आत्मीयरश्मगुणितात् वर् भक्ताद्रश्मयस्तस्मात् ।।६।

6. TO ARRIVE AT NUMBER OF RAYS: Note the position of the planet in Rasis, degrees etc., for which the number of rays is to be computed. Deduct from this its (deep) debilitation point. If the remainder is in excess of six signs, reduce it again from 12. Multiply the product by the maximum number of rays allotted to the planet concerned and divide by six. The resultant figure will represent the number of rays obtained by the planet by virtue of its position at birth.

Notes: In this sloka the method of computing rays is explained by the author. Let us assume that the Sun is in Scorpio 16°, 1e. 7\* 16° 0'. See the following

example:

|                                | s |  | o  |   |
|--------------------------------|---|--|----|---|
| Given placement of the Sun : 7 |   |  | 16 | 0 |
| Deep fall of the Sun :         | 6 |  | 10 | 0 |
|                                | 1 |  | 6  | 0 |

When 1\* 6° is multiplied by 10 (the maximum number of rays obtainable by the Sun) we get 12°. This divided by 6, the constant divider (for the Sun and other 6 plancts), yields 2 rays. (From the example given under sloka 2 also we get 2 rays, as the Sun covers 36 degrees from his deep debilitation point to Scorpio 16 degrees). The reader can convert the longitude as applicable from zero degree of Aries, i.e. deep fall of the Sun is 190 degrees while Scorpio 16 degrees means 226 degrees. If this system is followed, one has to divide the product first by 6, the constant divider. And the resultant quotient be again divided by 30 as 30° make one sign. For example 226 minus 190 gives 36. This multiplied by 10 and divided by 6 yields 60. This final figure 60 be divided by 30 (the degrees in one sign) and we get 2 again. Thus, the reader has before him 3 different methods and he can work out by a corvenient method. The exact number of rays (in fraction/decimal) should be worked out in this manner to be processed further as detalled below.

मित्रद्वादशभावे द्विगुणास्त्रमुणाः स्वके च दीधितयः । वश्रे पुनस्तयोच्चे स्वराशिये तद्भवत्येव ।७।। वक्रान्तस्ये द्विगुणा बक्रत्यागेऽ्ष्टभागहीनाश्वर । बैरिद्वादशभागे नीचे च भवन्ति षोडशांशोनाः IIII अस्तं गतो विरषिमः शनिसितवर्ज ग्रहो क्षेयः। एवं रश्मिविधानं पूर्वाचार्ये: समुद्दिष्टम् ।।E।।

7-9. RECTIFICATION OF RAYS: If the planet is in the dwadasamsa of its friend, double the product as obtained above. If it is in its own dwadasamsa, own Rasl, retrogression/exaltation sign the product should be trebled. If it is

in its enemy’s dwadasamsa, 1/16th of the rays will be lost. The same applies to a debilitated planet. A combust planet. excepting Venus and Saturn, loses all the rays. A planet that has just begun to retrograde will have two-fold rays while the onc that has just assumed direct motion from retrogression will lose only 1/8th of the number of rays.

Notes: In this connection, please refer to slokas 3-7 in ch. 73 of Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra. The following simple but varied instructions arc found therein:

(a) If the planet is exalted multiply the rays obtained by 3. (b) If in Moolatrikona Rasi, multiply by 3. (c) If in own Rasi, multiply by 3 and divide by 2 (i.e. increase the original rays by another 50%). (d) If in extreme friend’s sign multiply by 4 and divide by 3 (i.e. increase rays by 1/3rd). (e) If in a friend’s sign, multiply by 6 and divide by 5, (i.e. increase the original rays by another 20% or a ffth). (f) If in an enemy’s sign, the original rays should be straightaway halved. (g) If it is in its bitter enemy’s sign, multiply the original rays by 2 and divide by 5 to get net rays. (h) If it is in a neutral's sign, no rectification is involved. To wit, the original rays should remain undisturbed.

Here a clarification is necessary. The friendship, enmity etc., referred to above are by virtue of mutual relationships applicable to individual nativities and not natural. (For easy reference, the reader of Saravall may refer to page 57, infra). The sage suggests elimination of all the rays in case of combustion to the planets, excepting Saturn and Venus. These two have no reduction of rays on account of combustion in the Sun. And the Sun also does not lose on this count because he himself is the cause of

combustion.

The above views of Parasara are thus in casy grasp of the reader. Now let us resumc the present slokas of Kalyana Varma. These need clarifications and additions to enable a proper application of the principles.

(a) There are two stages suggested in slokas 7-9 hereof. Firstly multiplication. Secondly reduction. (b) In multiplication, doubling and trebling both feature. If trebling by virtue of retrogression, own sign placement, exaltation or own dwadasamsa is donc, no doubling should be done through warranted by simultaneous relative placement. For examplc, for retrogression the original rays are to be trebled and for friendly dwadasamsa etc., doubling is suggested. In a dual case, only trebling is to be done and no multiplication by 2 is needed. (c) After increasing the rays as suggested in 'b' above, the relative product is rectified further. A planet may be in its inimical dwadasama or in debilitation sign. In that case, the rays should be reduced by 1/16th of thc product. Similarly it loses one eighth if it has just assumed direct motion from retrogression. Here also, only the highest deduction be done. (d) If a planet other than Venus and Saturn is combust, then makc the rays as zero. (The Sun however has no combustion for himself). Necdless to say, this prevails over all other processes. (e) To be clear, the highest multiplication and the highest reduction be done, once each.

The reader has every need to know thc degrees of combustion. Hence the same are detailed on the next page:

| Planet      | Combustion area  | Combustlon area |
|-------------|------------------|-----------------|
|             | in direct motion | in (R) motion   |
| Moon within | 12 degrees       | Not applicable  |
| Mars        | 17 degrees       | 8 degrees       |
| Mercury     | 14 degrees       | 12 degrees      |
| Jupiter     | 11 degrees       | 11 degrecs      |
| Venus       | 10 degrees       | 8 degrees       |
| Saturn      | 16 degrees       | 16 degrees      |

The above degrees are plus or minus with reference to the Sun’s longitude in a given case. The combustion belts of Saturn and Venus are added here by way of informaţion, though not required in the context of reduction of rays.

एकादि पञ्च यावद्रश्मिभिरतिवुःखिताः कुलविहीनाः । परतन्त्रका दरिद्रा नीचरता सम्भवन्ति नराः ।।१०।। परतो दशकं यावद्भृतका जाता विदेशगमनरताः। जायन्तेऽत्र मनुष्याः सौभाग्यपरिच्युता मलिनाः ॥११॥

10-11. EFFECTS OF NUMBER OF RASMIS TOTALLY CONTRIBUTED BY THE SEVEN PLANETS: If the rays be a maximum of five, the native will be miserable, is of ignoble descent, will depend on others, be indigent and will join basc men. Should it be from 6 to 10, he will be a servant, will be intent on going to places other than of his birth (for living), be devoid of goodluck and be dirty.

ऊरध्वं पञ्चदशान्तिर्यावत्तावद्बहुश्रुताः सुजनाः । धर्माभिरताः सुमुखाः कुलस्य तुल्याः प्रजायन्ते ।१२।। आविशतेर्भवेयु: कुलाधिका धनयुता जनस्याताः। कोतिकराश्च मनुष्या ययाक्रमं स्वजनसम्पुज्याः ।।१३। पूज्याः सुभगा धीराः कृतिनो भूपास्तु शरकृतिर्यावत् । परतो भवन्ति मनुजाः संसाधितसकलकरणीयाः ।१४।।

अत उत्तरेण चग्डा नृपाश्रिता नृपतिलब्धधनसौस्याः। त्रिशद्यावत्सचिवाः पूज्याश्च भवन्ति भूपानाम् ।१५। एकात्रिशद्भस्तु प्रवराः स्याता महोभुजामिष्टाः। द्वात्रिशब्सः पुरुषाः पञ्चांशद्ग्रामपतयः स्युः ।।१६॥

12-16. If the rays are between 11 and 15, one will be respectively very learned, virtuous, righteous, beautiful and will possess merits duc to his descent. If the rays are between 16 and 20, he will be respectively supreme among his men, be wealthy, be famous among men, be glorious and be honoured by his men. The effects stated apply to the number of rays in the ascending order. If the rays are from 21 to 25 in number, the effects respectively are: honour, fortune, courage, scholarship and kingship. 26 rays denote a person who will have allround success in his efforts. 27 to 32 rays respectivecly denote: a dependant on the king, wealth and gains through the king, ministership, honours from the king, royal patronage and rulership over fifty villages.

प्रामसहस्राविर्पाते त्रिशत्यधिका करोति रश्मीनाम् । त्रिसहरुग्रामाणां पर्षं सूतौ चतुस्त्रिशत् ।।१७।। परतो मण्डलमाजो बहुकोशपरिग्रहा महासत्त्वाः। प्रस्यातकान्तियरसो भवन्ति सुमगाश्व लोकानाम् ॥१८।। त्रिशत्वड्भिः सहित। रश्मीनां यस्य जन्मसमये स्यात् । सार्व भुनक्ष्ति लक्षं स प्रामाणां पुमान्तियतम् ।१६।। त्रिसम्मद-वसु-सहिता रश्मीनां सम्भवे भवेद्येवाम्। लक्षत्रितवपतितवं ग्रामाणां जायते तेषाम् ।।२०।।

17-20. If the total number of rays is 33, he will rule over 1000 villages while with 34 rays he will obtain leadership over 3000 villages. 35 rays denote abundant wealth, strength, provincial rulership, fame, fortune etc. One will head over a lakh villages with 36 rays. Three lakh villages will come under his control with 37/38 rays.

त्रशत्सनवा गावो जन्मनि येषां प्रहोत्थिताः सन्ति । ते तोषितसकलजना भवन्ति पृथ्वीश्वराः पुरुषाः ॥२१।। दश जलधिगुणा या रश्मसंध्या नराणां दिशति पुयुलभूमेः पालकरवं च तेषाम्। हतरियुवनिताभिर्गीयतेऽ्तीव कोतिः करुणरदितगर्भेव्यदाक्रोशशब्दः । २२।।

शशिजलनिधिसंस्य रश्मिभिः श्रूयते यो

जलनिधिरशनायाः पाथिवः स्यात्स भूमेः । द्विजलधिरशनायाः पव.वेदास्यसंस्य- स्त्रिजलधिरशनाया वल्लिवेदस्तर्थव । २३।।

बदाविधिसंध्यैश्च मयूखजार्लर्जाता नरेन्द्राः खलु सार्वमौमाः । सौम्याः सुरब्राह्माणभवितिशीला दीर्घायुषःसर वयुता भवन्ति ॥२४।। परतः परतः किरणै्दीपान्तरपालका निक्पसर्गाः । सर्वनमस्याः सुमगा \_\_\_ महेन्द्रतु स्यप्रतापाश्च । २५।।

21-25. If the total rays be 39, he will become a ruler causing happiness to all. With 40 rays he will rule over vast lands, and the wives of the enemies crushed by him will reach their cries all over so that he will become prominent. One, two and three seas will be the boundary/boundaries of the kingdom of the native/s with 41/42/43 rays. One with 44 rays will be an emperor, will honour gods and Brahmins, be longlived and be mighty. According to the rays crossing 44, one will rule over other islands, be free from obstacles, be honoured by one and all and be equal to Indra in valour.

चत्वारिसचुक्ता पञ्चादिभिरत्र यस्य सूती स्थात्। शेयं तस्यारिष्टं सर्वक्षितिपालकत्वं च ।२६।

26. SPECIAL EFFECTS: If the total rays are 45 or 46, the native will always enjoy auspicious effects and will lord over the entire earth.

भुवनमरसहिष्णोः सर्वतः क्षीणशत्रो-

स्त्रिदशपतिमहिम्नः सर्वलोकस्तुतस्य ।

विदधति विहगानां रस्मयोऽतीब दीप्ता-

स्तुरगक्तिसमानाश्चकर्वातित्वमेव ।। २७॥

27. If the rays are 47, the native will undertake the responsibility of maintaining the whole earth, be devoid of enemies, be an emperor akin to Indra and be honoured by all.

अभिमुखकरप्रवाहा: फलं प्रयच्द्धन्ति पुष्टवरमाशु ।

सद्विपरीतं पुंसां पराङ्मुखास्तु ग्रहेन्द्राणाम् ।२८।। जन्मसमये ग्रहाणां रश्मनां संक्षये क्षयो भवति।

वृद्धेर्वविष्णूनामधमोत्तरता

16

28-29. ABIMUKHA AND PARANMUKHA: If a planet has Abhimukha rays the good effects stated will increase while Paranmukha rays reduce the good effects. More number of rays will multiply the results while less number of rays will diminish the effects. According to rays only, one's baseness or excellence will come to pass.

इति कल्याणवर्मविरवितायां सारावस्यां रश्मविन्ता नाम षट्त्रिशोऽध्यायः ।।

Thus ends the 36th Chapter entitled "PLANETARY RAYS” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## अथ पञ्चमहापुरुषाध्यायः ॥३७॥। Chapter 37

## Panchamahapurusha Yogas

श्रीवेवकीतिराजः पञ्चमहापुरुषलक्षणान्नुपतीन् । कययति यांस्तानह्मपि कययामि निराकुलोकत्य ।।।।

1. Sri Devakirti Raja explained the Panchamahapurusha Yogas (denoting Great Men) which I explain below clearly.

स्वक्षेत्रे च चतुष्टये च बलिभिः स्वोच्चस्थितर्वा ग्रहै:

शुक्रांगारकमन्दजीवशशिजे रेतैर्यथानुक्रमम् । मालव्यो रुचकः शशोऽय कथितो हंसश्च भद्रस्तया

सर्वेवामपि विस्तरं मतिमतां संक्षिप्यते लक्षणम् ।।२।।

2. Venus, Mars, Saturn and Mercury remaining in their respective own houses or exaltation signs, identical with angles, cause Malavya, Ruchaka, Sasa, Hamsa and Bhadra yogas respectively. The effects thereof have been elaborately explained by the ancient preceptors which I briefly narrate here.

Notes: If Venus is in his own sign or in exaltation Malavya Yoga—one of the Pancha Mahapurusha Yogas—is caused. The said occupation should be simultaneously angulr to the ascendant (or the Moon). Similarly, Mars in the context causes Ruchaka Mahapurusha Yoga, Saturn Sasa Yoga, Jupiter Hamsa Yoga and Mercury Bhadra Yoga. The effects of these Yogas are individually given in the following verses. Detailed information can be obtained from the notes given in pages 169-173 of HORASARA, and also

Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra, ch. 75.

न स्यूलोष्ठो न विषमवपुर्नातिरक्तांगसन्धि- र्मध्ये क्षामः शशघररुचिहंस्तिनादः सुगन्धः सन्दीप्ताक्षः समसितरदो जानुदेशाप्तपाणि- मालव्योऽयं बिलसति नृपः सप्ततिर्वत्सराणान् ॥ ३।।

वक्त्रं त्रयोदशमितानि दशांगुलानि वैध्येण कर्णविवरं दशविस्तरेण। मालव्यसंत्मनुजः स भुनवित नूनं लाटान्समालवससिन्धुरपारियात्रान् ।४।

3-4. MALAVYA YOGA: One born in Malavya Yoga will have thin (not fleshy) lips, even physique, grey complexion, thin waist, splendour equal to that of the Moon, majestic voice akin to that of a tusker, pleasant odour of the body, piercing sight, even and white teeth, hands extending to the knees and a longevity of seventy years. His face will be 13 inches in length and 10 inch broad while the two ear-holes are 10 inch apart. (Angula denoted here as an inch means 8 barley-corn-wide.) He rules Laata, Malawa and Sindhu.

दीर्घास्यः स्वच्छकान्तिर्बहुचिरबलः साहसावाप्तकार्य-

श्चार्नर्नोलकेशश्वरणरणरतो मन्त्रविचबोरनायः । रक्तश्यामोऽतिशूरो रिपुबलमथनः कम्बुकण्ठः प्रधानः

ऋरूरो भर्ता नराणां द्विजगुरुविनतः क्षामसज्जानुजंच ।५। लट्वांगपा शवृषकार्मुकवञवीणा-

रेखांकहस्तवरणश्च मन्त्राभिचारकुशलस्तुलया सह्त्रं

शर्तांगुलश्च ।

मध्यं च तस्य कचितं मुख्दर्ध्यतुल्यम् ॥६।। विन्ध्याचलसह्यगिरीन् भुनवित सप्ततिसमा नगरदेशान् । शस्त्रानलक्तमृत्युः \_\_ प्रयाति देवालयं रचकः ।।।

5-7. RUCHAKA YOGA: The native of Ruchaka Yoga will have a long face, pure splendour, great strength, valour, attractive eye brows, blue (very black) hair, penchant for war, knowledge of Mantras, be a leader of thieves, will have blood red complexion, be very valorous, be a conqueror of enemies, will have a neck akin to conch, be chief, cruel, will honour gods and Brahmins, thin shanks, will have marks of a staff ( gactually means a staff with a skull at the top deemed to be Siva’s weapon, and carried by ascetics), cord, bull, arrow, diamond, lute etc., on the hands and legs, will be 100 inch tall, be skilful in Mantras and black magic, will weigh 100 tulas (one tula is 100 palas) and the circumference of his loins will correspond to the length of his face. He will rule over places consisting of Vindhya and Sahya. He will live upto seventy and will attain his end through weapons/fire and will go to heavens.

तनुद्धिजास्यो ब्रुतगः शसोऽ्यं शठोऽतिशूरो निभृतप्रतापः । वनाद्रिहर्गेयु नदीषु सक्तः हुशोदरोः नातिलघुः प्रसिद्धः ।।el। सेनानायो निखिलनिरतो दन्तुरश्चापि किञ्चित् धातोर्बादे भवति निरतश्चञ्चलः कोशनेत्रः। स्त्रीसंसक्तः पर्धनगृहो मातृभक्तः सुजंघो मध्ये क्षामो बहुविधमती रन्त्रवेदी परेषाम् ।।। पर्यक्शंसहरिशस्त्रमुदंगमाला-

वीणोपमा यदि करे चरणे च रेखाः।

वर्षाणि सप्ततिमितानि करोति राज्यं

प्रत्यन्तिकः क्षितिपतिः कथितो मुनीन्द्वैः ।।१०।

8-10. SASA YOGA: One born in Sasa yoga will have small teeth small face, fast pace, be crafty, very valorous, interested in forests, hills and forts, will have weak loins and long body, be famous, be an army chief, skilful in all assignments, will have somewhat protruding teeth, be a metallurgist, be fickleminded, will have eyes resembling the lotus, be interested in the fairsex, will enjoy other’s wealth, be devoted to his mother, weak waist, be very intelligent

and will fault others. His hands will contain marks of couch ( q ), conch, wheel, garland, lute etc. According to sages he will be a ruler of some place and will die at 70.

रक्तास्योग्नतनासिकः सुचरणो हंसः प्रसन्नेन्त्रियो

शौरः पोनकपोलरक्तकरजो हंसस्वरः श्लेष्मलः । ज्ञंखाब्जांकुशदाममत्स्ययुगलः खद्वांगचापांगद-

शिवह्धं : पादकरांकितो मधुनिसे नेश्रे च वृत्तं शिरः ॥ ११। सलिलाशयेषु रमते स्त्रीषु न तृप्त प्रयाति कामारतः। षोडशशतानि तुलितोऽ्गुलानि दैध्येग वः्णबतिः ।१२। पातोह देशान् खल् शूरसेनान् मान्धारगंगायमुनान्तरालान् । ज्ञीबेदनूनां शतवर्वसंख्यां पर्वाहनान्ते समुपैति नाशम् ॥।१३।।

11-13. HAMSA YOGA: One born in Hamsa yoga will have a reddish face, elevated nose, beautiful feet, great virile power (Indriya is here interpreted as virile power coming under Jupiter), be wheatish in complexion, will have broad cheeks, reddish nails, (mellifluous) voice like that of the swan, be phlegmatic, will possess palms and soles with marks of trident, fish, arrow etc., honey-coloured and round eyes, be fond of sporting in water, will be delighted in sexually uniting with females, will weigh 1600 tulas and be 96 inch tall. He will lord over Saurasena, Gandharva and places between the Ganges and Yamuna. His end will come later on in a forest. His longevity will be hundred years.

शार्दूलप्रतिमाननो द्विपगतिः पीनोरुवक्षःस्वलो

लम्बापीनसुवृसबाहुयुगलस्त्तुल्यमानोच्छ्यः । कामोकोमलसूक्ष्मरोमनिकरेः संरुद्धगण्डस्यलः

प्राज्ञः पंकजगर्भपाणिचरणः सर्वाधिको योगवित् ॥ १४।

शंखा सिकुञ्जर गदाकुसुमेचुकेतुचक्कार्जलांगल विचिह्वितपाणिपादः । यात्रागुरुद्रपमप्रचमाम्बुसिषतचूकुंकुमप्रतिमगन्धतनु: सुकोणः ॥ १५।

14-15. BHADRA YOGA: One born with the Bhadra Yoga will have a face akin to that of a tiger, will imitate the elephant in gait, will have broad chest, long and strong hands, and square body, will be scnsuous, will have soft hair over cheeks, be learned, will possess hands and legs akin to lotus, be endowed with prowess and will be conversant with Yoga. His palms and soles will contain symbols of conch, sword, elephant, arrow, wheel, lotus, plough etc. His body will emanate smell akin to carth settled with fresh drizzles (particularly first of the season), sandal paste and elephant's ichor.
