भारस्तुलायां तुलितो यदि स्याचछ्ीमध्यदेशेष्वधिपस्तदासौ। यस्त्रचयादिमुर्यः सहितः समद्रः सर्वत्र राजा शरदामशीतिः ।।१८।।

16-18. BHADRA YOGA (continued): He will know the meanings of Sastras, be valorous, will have well-knitted eye brows, will walk like an elephant, will have genetal organs in 'concealed position' (like that of a horse or a tusker) and a beautiful belly, be righteous, will have attractive foreface, be valorous, firm in prowess, will have black and short hair, be independent in all his undertakings, will not forgive (even) his own men, will allow his friends to enjoy his wealth, will weigh 2000 palas, will rule over the midportion of the country, will possess females etc., be always happy and will die at 80.

Notes: There is a version to say that if both the husband and wife have Bhadra Yoga, the male will rule over the entire earth.

समाः स्वरैः शंख-मृदंगदन्तिनां रबौधभेरीवृवतोयदायिनाम् । समस्त भूमण्डल-रक्षणक्षमा भवन्ति भूपा जितशत्रयो नराः ।।१६।। स्निग्धैर्भवन्ति भूपा जिद्यात्वग्दन्तनेत्रनखकेशैः। रक्षेरेभिनिस्वाः स्वरंश्च ते जातके कविताः ॥२०।। स्नग्धस्तेजोयुक्ता शुद्धो वर्णः: प्रकीतितो नृपतेः। विपरीतः क्लेशमुजां सुतार्यसुखभागिनां मध्यः ।२१।।

19-21. GENERAL: He can become a monarch if his voice is similar to the sound of a conch, drum, tusker, bull or a roaring cloud. In such case he will rule over the entire earth and will conquer his enemies. One with viscid tongue, skin, teeth, eyes, nails and hair, will become a ruler. If these are rough, he will experience penury. One with glossy, splendorous and bright complexion will become a king. Otherwise he will be poor and miserable. If thcse are meddling, he will be a middle class person with wealth, children, happiness etc.

इति कल्याणवर्मविरवितायां सारावल्यां सप्तात्रिशोऽध्यायः ।।३७।।

Thus ends the 37th chapter entitled “PANCHA MA- HAPURUSHA YOGAS” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## जैथ गुण-तत्त्व-फलाध्यायः ।।३८।।

## Chapter 38

## Effects of Natural Constituents and Primordial Compounds

महीसुतात्सस्वमुदाहरन्ति गुरुत्वमिन्दोस्तनयाद्गुरोश्च । स्वरं सितात्नेहमिनेश्व पुत्राद् वर्ण बलात् पूर्णलघूनि चैवाम् ॥।१।

1. Mars denotes one’s prowess, Mercury greatness (or physical weight), Jupiter voice, Venus splendour (or friendship) and Saturn complexion (physical brightness).

मृदुर्दयालुर्बहुदारभृत्यः स्थिरस्वभावः प्रियसत्यवादी । सुरद्विजोपास्तिकर: सहिष्णुर्भवेन्नरः सत्त्वगुणप्रधानः ।२।

2. EFFECTS OF SATWA GUNA: If Satwa is predominent in a native, he will be highly merciful, will possess many wives and attendants, will be firm in disposition, will be a sweet and truthful speaker, will honour gods and Brahmins and will be of forgiving temperament.

शूरः कलाकाव्यनिविष्ट बृद्धः स्त्रीभोगसंसक्तमनाः प्रवीणः । आडम्बरी हास्यरतिः प्रगल्मो गेयाक्षविद्राजसिकः प्रदिष्टः ।।३।।

3. EFFECTS OF RAJOGUNA: One with predominant Rajoguna will be valorous, be skilful in fine arts and literature, be fond of uniting with women, be skilful, showy, jocular, spirited and be interested in music and gambling.

मूर्खोऽलसो वञ्चधिता परेषां करोधी विषण्णः पिशुनःक्षुधार्तः । आवारहीनो न शुचिर्मदान्धो लुब्धः प्रमादी तमसाभिभूतः ।।४।।

4. EFFECTS OF TAMOGUNA: With Tamoguna predominant, the native will be foolish, indolent, will cheat others, be irascible, miserable, be a talebearer, be devoid of good conduct, unkcmpt, blind due to lascivousness, miserly and careless.

ब्योसाह्ड्रुवातागिनमहोस्व भावा जीवामुरेड्चाकिमहीजसौम्यैः । छाया मरुत्पितककस्वरूपा मिर्यस्तु मिश्रा बलिभिर्नरस्य ।५।।

5. PRIMORDIAL COMPOUNDS (PANCHA BHUTAS): According to the strengths of Jupiter, Venus, Saturn, Mars and Mercury, the native will acquire the effects of the five Tatwas, uiz. Ether, Water, Air, Fire and Earth. The strongest of the planct will impart corresponding temperament, viz. windy, bilious or phlegmatic. If many planets attain strength, these derivations will be mixed.

शब्दार्थविन्म्यायपटुः प्रगल्भो विज्ञानयुक्तो विवृतास्यभागः ।

चित्रांगसन्धिः कृशपाणिपादो व्योमप्रकृत्या पुरुषोऽतिदीर्घः ॥६।।

6. EFFECTS OF AKASA TATWA (ETHER): One with predominant characteristics of ether will be skilful in grammar and meanings, be an expert in justice, be bold, knowledgeable, will have a broad face and peculiar physical joints, weak hands and feet and be quite tall.

## लावष्यवाही बहुभारवाही प्रियादिमाषी च जलप्रहृत्या।

चलस्वर्यो बहुमित्रपक्षः क्षोणीपतिर्नातिचिरप्रगल्मः ।।।

7. EFFECTS OF JALATATWA (WATER): The native with predominant Watery nature will be quite charming, will endure heavy loads, will speak sweetly, be tremuolous in appearance, will have many friends, be a king and will not

be lastingly bold.

तस्वेन वायोः पुरुषः कुशांगः क्षिप्रं च कोपस्य वशं प्रयाति । कुत्येकबुद्धिर्मणे रतश्च दाता सितो भूपतिरप्रधृद्यः ॥eII

8. EFFECTS OF VAYU TATWA (AIR): One of the pre- -dominant Airy nature will have an emaciated body, be easily given to anger, be deeply merged in his work, be of wandering disposition, be liberal, fair in complexion and be an inviolable king.

शूरः क्षुधार्तश्चपलोऽतितीक्ष्णः प्राज्ञः कशो गौरतनुवरोधी ।

विद्वाग्मुपाणिर्बहुमक्षणवच वह्विस्वमावः पुरुषोऽतिकायः ।।ह।। .

9. EFFECTS OF FIERY TATWA: One with predominant Fiery character, will be valorous, be hunger-stricken, be very sharp, learned, emaclated, fair in complexion, inimical, will have beautiful arms, will be a voracious eater and be tall.

कर्पूरजात्युत्पलपुष्पगन्धो भुनक्ति भोगान् सियरलब्धसौख्यः ।

सिहास्रघोषः स्यरचित्तवृत्तिर्महीस्वभावः पुरुवः ससत्वः ।।१०।।

10. EFFECTS OF PRITHVI (EARTH): One with Earthy nature will emanate the good smell of camphor, of jasmine and of blue lotus from his person, will enjoy abundantly, will possess voice akin to that of a (roaring) lion or clouds, be firm in disposition and be endowed with prowess.

## स्फटिकोपलकांशा स्वसच्छा गगनोत्थिता भवेश्छाया।

## निधिरिव पुंसा घन्वा त्रिवर्गफलसाधनी सौम्या ।११।।

11. BIRTH WITH AKASA TATWA: One born with the reflection of AKASA TATWA (ether) will be endowed with a physique with the brightness of crystal and of a gem ( 3प ). Just as wealth confers happiness this reflection will make one achieve effects of trivargas (i.e. the three objects of worldly existence, viz. Dharma or moral merits, Artha or

attainment of riches or worldly prosperity and Kama or desire of sensual enjoyment—these three ends form the well known triad excluding Moksha or final emancipation).

स्निग्धा सिता च हरिता कान्ता मातेव सर्वसुखजननी ।

सौभाग्याभ्युद्यशुभान्करोति जलसम्भवा दाया ।१२।।

12. BIRTH WITH JALA TATWA (water): One born with the reflection of watery tatwa will be glossy, will have the complexion of white Durva grass ( fat ), will receive protection as if from the mother: and will prosper.

असितजलदकान्तिः पापगन्धोऽतिमूढो

मलिनपर्षकायः शोकसातापतप्तः ।

स बहति बवर्दन्यव्याध्यनर्यार्यनाशान्

विचरति पवनोत्या यस्य कान्तिः शरीरे ॥ १३।।

13. BIRTH WITH VAYU TATWA (air): One born with reflection of Vayu Tatwa will be akin to black clouds (that are going just to cause heavy rains), will emanate foul smell, be very foolish, will have a dirty and coarse physique, be subjected to great miseries and will experience poverty, diseases, evils and financial destruction.

कमनदहुनदीप्ति रचण्डदण्डोऽतिहुष्टः

प्रणतसकलशत्रविक्रमाक्रान्त भूमिः ।

भज्जति मणिसुवर्ण सर्वकार्यार्यसिंद्ध

प्रशमितगदशोको बद्धिजायां प्रभायाम् ॥१४।।

14. BIRTH WITH AGNI TATWA: One born with the reflection of Agni (flery) Tatwa will be bright as the burning flame, will confer severe punishments, will conquer all his enemies and the earth by his own valour, will possess jewels, gold etc., will attain success in all his undertakings and will be free from diseases and grief.

आद्याम्बुसिर्तवसुधागरतुल्यगन्धः

सुस्निग्धदन्तनखरोमशरीरकेशः । धर्मार्थतुष्टिसुखभाग्जनसम्प्रयश्च

च्छाया यदा भवति भूमिक्ता मनुष्ये ।।१५।।

15. BIRTH WITH EARTHY TATWA: One born with the reflection of Prithvi (earth) Tatwa will emanate smell from the body akin to the smell coming out of earth when sprinkled with fresh drizzles. He will have charming teeth, nails, hair, and body. He will attain merits and happiness and be dear to all.

शीतातों बहुभाषको द्रुतगतिर्तावस्थितः कुत्रचित्

शूरो मत्सरबान् रुजाकरर्चर्दोंभाप्ययुक्तोऽनयः ।

दन्तान् खादति नातिसौहुर्मतिर्यान्धर्ववेत्ता कुशो

सित्राणां समुपार्जनेऽतिनिपुणः स्वप्ने-च खे गच्छति ।।१९॥

अपगतधृतिर्क्ष्मश्रुकेशः

कृतध्नः

विलपति स्कुटितवरणहस्तः कोधनो नष्टकान्तिः । च निवन्धी वित्तसंक्षारकारो

भवति पुरुष एवं मारतंकप्रदानः ॥१७।।

16-17. SIGNS OF PREDOMINANT WINDY TEMPERA- MENT: One with predominant windy tempcrament will be benumbed with cold and shivering, will speak much and will walk fast, be fond of moving in boats, be valorous, spiteful, sickly, unfortunate, unjust, will not be very friendly disposed, will have knowledge of music, be emaciated, be skilful in acquiring friends, will fly in the skies in dreams, be devoid of courage and gratitude, will have rough hair, will have splay feet and crooked arms, be irascible, will have lost splendour and will face financial losses.

दुर्गन्धी लघुतापनो विपुलधीः क्षित्रप्रसादः पुनः

पीनो रक्तनखाक्षिपाणिचरणो वृद्धाकृतिर्दाहवान् ।

मेधावी युधि निर्भयो हिमरुचिर्बूरते निगृह्यापरान्

नो भीतः प्रणयं प्रयाति बहुभिः कुर्यान्नतानां प्रियम् ।।१८।। स्वप्नेऽभिपश्यति सुवर्णदिनेशदीपान्

दावान्निकिशुकजपाम णिक्काणिकारान् ।

रस्ताब्जवण्डरुधिरीघतरित्समूहान्

वित्ताधिको निगदितः खलु लक्षणर्ञः ।।१६।।

18-19. SIGNS OF PREDOMINANT BILIOUS TEMPERA- MENT: One with predominant bile will emanate foul smell from his person, be less distrcssed, greatly intelligent, be easily satisfied, will have large transparcnt nails, large eyes, hands and feet, will resemble an old man, will suffer morbid heat, be a scholar, be fearless, fond of cold articles, will not fear even at the hands of his enemies, will be affectionate to all and will be fond of food. His dreams will be about gold, sun light, forest-configuration, kimsuka (a kind of tree which yields charming and blossoms which however have no fragrancc), precious stones, karnikara (the pericap of a lotus, alternatively a kind of flower with excellent hue but no smell and hence disliked), red lotus, eunuch, blood and lightning.

श्रोमात् शिलष्टांगसन्धिघु तिबलसहितः स्निग्धकान्तिः सुदेहो

ग्राही सत्वो रपन्नो हतमुरजघनब्बानघोषः सहिष्णुः । गौरो रक्तान्तनेत्रो मधुररसवचर्बद्धर्वरः कृतज्ञः

बलेशे चस्यादलिन्नः सकलजनसुहृत्पुजको वा गुरूणाम् ।२०।। सुप्तस्तु पर्यति समुद्रनदीसरांसि

- मुक्ताफलप्रकर हंससिताब्जशंबान् ।

नक्षत्रकुन्दकुमुदेन्दुतुवारपातान्

श्लेध्माधिको मुनिवरः कथितः ऋरमेण ।। २१।।

20-21. SIGNS OF PREDOMINANT PHLEGMATIC TEM- PERAMENT: One with predominant phlegmatic temperament will be wealthy, will have a clung joints, be coura-

geous and strong, will have a bright and charming physique, be a recepient (of gifts, bencfits etc.), be satwic, will have voice akin to the sound of a drum and roaring clouds, be enduring, fair in complexion, will have reddish eyes, will enjoy sweet drinks, (sometimes) will be highly inimical, be grateful, be friendly to all, and will honour clders. His dreams will be: seas, rivers, coral stones, lakes, swan, conch, stars, lily, snowfall etc. These effects are attributed by the sages.

बलरहितेन्दुरविध्यां युकर्तमी मादिभिर्गहैंमिश्राः ।

## न भवन्ति महीपाला दशासु तेवां सुतार्थयुताः।२२।।

22. LUMINARIES IN MAHAPURUSHA YOGAS: Though Mars ctc.,may be strong in the respective cases of Pancha Mahapurusha Yogas (vide sloka 2 of previous chapter), if the Sun and the Moon are weak, the person will not attain kinghood but in the period of the conccrned planct will be endowed with children and wealth.

इति कल्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां गुणतस्यलक्षणाध्यायः ।।३८।।

Thus ends thc 38th Chapter entitled "GUNA TATWA PHALA” in Kalyana Varma’s “SARAVALI”

## अथ राजयोगभंगाध्यायः ।।३९।। Chapter 39

## Obstruction to Rajayogas

विस्तरतो निदिष्टाः क्षितिपतियोगा विचित्रसंस्यानाः । भंगद्च भवति तेषां यथा तथा सम्प्रवक्ष्यामि ।१।। 1. Earlier, the yogas of kinghood have been explained. There can be some factors which prevent the effects of such yogas, which I explain in this chapter.

कुजार्कजोवाकिभिरत्र नोचर्दाभ्यां त्रिभिर्वेकतमे विलग्ने । निशाकरे वृश्चिकराशिसंस्ये विशीर्यते राजकरो हि योगः ।।२।। 2. Out of Mars, Saturn, Jupiter and the Sun, if two or more be in fall and one of them simultaneously be in the ascendant, as the Moon is in Scorpio, the Rajayogas will become futile.

Notes: In this sloka, it is said that at least two among Mars, Saturn, Jupiter and the Sun be in deep fall, one of the fallen planets, occupying the ascendant. Additionally the Moon be in deep fall in Scorpio. Then the Raja Yogas prevailing in the horoscope will only be namesake.

अन्त्याष्टमादिभागे चरराष्यादिषु शशी यदा क्षीणः। एकेनापि न दृष्टो ग्रहेण संगस्तदा नृपतेः ।३।

3. The Rajayogas in the horoscope are only namesake if the Moon being weak is in the last Navamsa of a movable sign, or in the 8th navamsa of a fixed sign or in the first navamsa of a common sign and is without aspect from any

other planet.

Notes: Decreasing Moon should be between 26° 40' and 30° of Aries, Cancer, Libra or Capricorn. Or she should be between 23° 20' and 26° 40' of Taurus, Leo, Scorpio or Aquarius. Or she should be within the first 3° 20' of Gemini, Virgo, Sagittartus or Pisces. In each case, being so placed, if the decreasing Moon is unaspected by any planet, the Raja Yogas will become defunct.

## सर्वे क्रूराः केन्दरे नीचारिगता न सौम्ययृतदृष्टाः । शुभदा व्यवरिपुरन्ध्रे तदाऽपि भंगो भवेन्तृपतेः ।४।।

4. Same effects will come to pass if all malefics occupy angles identical with debilitation/inimical signs while benefics are shunted to the 12th, 6th and 8th houses.

## लग्नं गणोत्तमोनं न खेचरेदृश्यते तदा भंगः। भवति हि नृपयोगानां दारिद्याय प्रजातस्य ॥५।

5. If the ascendant is in Vargothama position without aspect to any planet, the Rajayogas will only be a failure.

घोवये नीचगतेस्त्रिभिर्हैव्हस्पतौ सूर्ययुते च नीचगे। एकोऽपि नोच्चे तु शुभेन संगतः प्रयान्ति नाशं शतशो नृपोव्भवाः ॥६।।

6. Even a hundred kingly yogas will be of no avall if Aquarlus rises while 3 heavenly bodies are in their signs of fall. Simultaneously Jupiter shall be combust or debilitated while none is exalted or is in the company of a benefic.

Notes: For an Aquarius native, if Jupiter is in the 12th or in combustion elsewhere while three planets occupy their signs of fall, all the Rajayogas will be unavallable. If, however, there is a benefic planet in the company of the sald participants or if a planet is exalted, the Rajayogas so obstructed will be revived to the native.

शून्येवु केन्द्रेषु शुर्भनवेन्दावस्तं गर्तर्नीचमथ प्रयातः। चतुप्रहैर्वाऽपि गृहे रिपूणां प्रणश्यति क्षमाधिपतेस्तु योगः ।७।

7. If there is no benefic in an angle or with the Moon while 4 planets are either in fall or in combustion or in inimical signs, the Rajayogas are marred.

स्वांशे रवी शीतकरे विनव्टे पार्पेश्व दृष्टे शुभड्क्टिहीने । कृत्यापि राज्यं च बने मनुष्यः पश्वात्सुदुखं लमते गताशः ।८।।

8. Should the Sun be in his own Navamsa, while the Moon is in combustion and in aspect to malefics without the aspect of benefics, the native will enjoy rulership (with the help of other Rajayogas) only to relinquish the kingdom soon and will become miserable.

Notes: There may be Raja Yogas in a nativity to make one kingly, happy, wealthy etc. If simultaneously the Moon occupies her combustion belt, be aspected by malefics but not by benefics as the Sun is in Leo Navamsa, the position obtained by the native will be lost to make him miserable.

शिशिरकिरणशत्रुर्लग्नपश्चन्द्रदुष्टः

सहजरिपुमदस्या भानुम्पुत्रमन्दाः ।

शुभविर हितकेन्द्र रस्तर्यर्बाऽपि सोध्ये-

न् पतिजननयोगा यान्ति नाशं क्षणेन ।।।

9. Should the Moon lend her inimical aspect to the ascendant lord while the Sun, Mars and Saturn are in their order in the 3rd, 6th and 7th, the Rajayogas will become ineffective. Benefics should in this case be not in angles but suffer combustion.

पञ्चभिनिम्नर्गः खेर्टरस्तं यार्तरथापि वा।

प्रयान्ति विलयं योगा भूभुजां ये प्रकोतिताः ।१०।।

10. If five heavenly bodies are in fall at birth the Rajayogas will not come to pass.

उत्कायाः पतने चैव निर्घातव्यतिपातयोः ।

केतोश्व दर्शने चैव यागिति नासं नृपोद्भवाः ।।११।।

11. If meteor, noise of contending winds in the sky (or earthquake or thunder), Vyatipata or Upakctu be seen at the time of birth, the good Rajayogas stand nullified.

अन्यैः ऋूरोत्पातेस्त्रिशंकुतारा यदोदयं यान्ति।

स्थः प्रयान्ति विलयं नृपयोगा भानुजो यदि विलग्ने ।१२।

12. If Trisankhu Nakshatra (Trisankhu was a monarch of the solar race who remained suspended with his head towards the earth as a constellation in the southern hemisphere) rises at the time of one’s birth while the ascendant is occupied by Saturn as there is fall of a comet, the Raja yogas do not come to pass.

कर्तारो नृपतीनां गगनसदो युद्धकांक्षिणो मलिनाः।

## कक्षा जर्जरवेहा विघ्नं जनयन्ति राजयोगस्य ।।१३।

13. If the planets capable of causing Rajayoga are inclined to enter in war, or are becoming splendourless, or about to lose their strength, the Rajayogas will stand cancelled.

परनीचं गते चन्द्रे क्षीणे योगो महीपतेः। नाशमायाति राजेब दैवज्ञप्रतिलोमगः ।। १४।।

14. If weak Moon is in her deep degree of debilitation the Rajayogas get destroyed just as a king going against an astrologer.

तुलायां पद्धिनीबन्धुस्त्रिशांशे

दशमे स्यितः।

हन्ति राज्यं यया सोमः समस्तगुणसञ्चयम् ॥१५।

15. The Sun in the 10th degree of Libra will destroy all Rajayogas just as miserliness eclipses all the good attributes of a person.

## जूकस्य दशमे भागे स्यितः कमलबोधनः । सहलं राजयोगानां मन्दमेव करोत्यसौ । १९६।।

16. This sloka is repeated in the original. The meaning is identical with that of sloka 15 above.

## स्वत्रिकोणगृहं केचित्स्त्रोच्चं याताः स्वमन्दिरम्। अतिनीचे रविर्चको न तेषां फलसम्भवः ।१७।।

17. Even though other planets may be in their Mulatrikona, exaltation or own signs while the Sun is singly in deep fall, the Rajayogas will be unavailable.

## गुस्मृ गे विलग्नस्यो दुःर्लेः समग्तापदे नरम्।

कामार्तमवनं - वेश्यावश्यमिन्दुर्न चेत्स्वमे ।१८।।

18. If Jupiter occupies Capricorn as ascendant while the Moon is not in Cancer, the native will become miserable Just as a penniless lewdster in the place of a courtezan.

## एकेनापि शशांको प्रहेण केमद्गुमे न यदि दृष्टः ।

## विघ्नयति राजयोगं मलिनाचारः प्रसुत स्यात् ।।१ह।।

19. If in the formation of Kemadruma Yoga, the Moon is without the aspect of another planet, the Rajayogas lose their existence and the native will be devoid of good conduct.

## भिक्षामटति त्यार्यानोंचवर्षगतेः सुदुःखितो मलिनः ।

## सकुलमहीमृत्पुत्रः परिभूतो जायते निःस्वः ॥२०॥।

20. If 3 or more planets be in fall even the son of an emperor ruling over the entire earth will take to begging, be

miserable, be dirty and penniless.

अत्यरिमवनं प्राप्तेः पञ्चादिभिरस्तर्गश्च गगनचरः । नारां प्रयाति राजा यदि रविचन्दौ न तुंगस्थौ।।२१्।।

21. If 5 planets occupy inimical signs or are in combustion while the Sun or the Moon is not in exaltation, the kingly yogas will become ineffective.

सचिवो वानवेन्द्रस्य नीचांशे समबस्यतः ।

सम्प्राप्तमतुलं राज्यं नरहापयते ध्रुवम् ।।२२॥1

22. If Venus occupy his debilitation Navamsa, even a vast kingdom is deprived from the native.

राजयोगाः समास्यातास्तेर्षा भंगश्च दारुणः ।

परीक्ष्य यत्नतः प्राज्ञः फलं ब्रूयाद्बलाबलात् ।।२३।।

23. Rajayogas have been explained earlier. The combinations marring such kingly states are given in this chapter. The stronger one of these two will only prevall.

कमलभवनबन्धुः कन्ययालिगितांग- स्त्वलिनि कुजसुरेडधौ चन्द्रमा मेघसंस्थः । न च यदि परिशेवदुश्यते स्यात्स भूपः

प्रवलितगजमेघच्छादिताशानभोस्रः ।।९४।।

24. RAJAYOGA: The Sun in Virgo, Mars in Scorpio along with Jupiter as the Moon is in Aries: this configuration will produce a king the dust of whose elephants will darken the skies.

इति कस्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां एकोनचत्वारिश्योऽ्ध्यादयः ।। ३६।।

Thus ends the 39th Chapter entitled “OBSTRUCTIONS TO RAJAYOGAS” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## अथ आयुर्दायाध्यायः ॥४०॥

## - Chapter 40

## Calculation of Longevity

अस्मिन्नायुर्वयि यस्माद्स्नान्तः समस्तलोकोऽयम्।

तस्मात्पूर्वागमतः कययामि \_\_\_ निराकुलीकृत्य ॥१।।

1. The people all over remain perplexed over the question of longevity. So, I narrate the relative principles based on the Sastras, hereunder:

अंशोद्भवं विलग्नात्पण्डच मानोनिसर्गजं चन्द्रात् । एतेषां यो बलवानेकतमस्तस्य कल्पयेदायुः ।२।। लग्नविवाकरबन्द्रास्त्रयोऽपि बलरिक्ततां यदा यान्ति।

परमायुषः स्वरांशं ददति खगा जीवशर्मोक्तम् ।।३।।

2-3. SELECTION OF SYSTEM FOR LONGEVITY COM- PUTATION: Out of the trio—the Sun, the Moon and the ascendant—if the ascendant is the strongest Amsayurdaya should be resorted to. Pindayu is the best if the Sun is predominant while Nisargayu is the apt choice with Moon prevailing. If the three in question are bereft of strength, then Jeevasarma’s system can be used.

बिलग्नादिकला भाज्या व्योमशून्ययमै: समाः ।

लभ्यन्तेऽर्कहुताः शेषा: स्वमानगुणितांशकाः ॥४।।

4. AMSAYURDAYA CALCULATIONS: The longitudes (counted from Aries first point) of the (seven) planets and the ascendant should be divided by 200 to get the number of years. The quotients in later processes should be multi-

plied by the respective multipliers to get months, days and ghatis.

होरा सर्वबलोपेता वर्षाण्यन्यानि राज्ञितुल्यानि यच्छति। मासादिभार्गस्त्राशिकात्पुनः ।।५।

5. ADDITIONS FOR ASCENDANT’S CONTRIBUTION: If the ascendant is strong the number of years equal to the Rasis it has traversed should be added. Months etc., thereof should be guessed by rule of three method.

वर्गोत्तिमे स्वमवने स्वद्रेबकाणे नवांशके ।

हविगुणं सम्प्रयच्छन्ति त त्रिगुणं वत्रतुंगयोः ।६।

6. RECTIFICATIONS FOR PLANETARY CONTRIBU- TONS: The contribution made by a planet that is in Vargothama, own sign, own decanate, or own navamsa should be doubled. If it is retrograde or in exaltation the figure should be trebled.

यदा तूपचयः सर्वः समस्तवर्गणा तत्र

स्वराश्यादिस्थितर्गहैः। कर्तव्या शास्त्रचिन्तर्कः ।७।।

7. If a planet's contribution gets doubled by virtue of position in own sign, own decanate etc., (as per sloka 6 above) and further warrants trebling by virtue of retrogression/exaltation, the trebling should also be done. So say the learned.

केन्द्रादिसंस्थिते खे रे 2 सकलद्विगुणेककाम् ।

शंसन्ति वर्गणां केचित्तच्च चूडामणेर्मतम् ।।।

8. According to Chudamani Acharya, if a planet is in angle, contributions by each rectification shall be considered. If it is in Panaphara (2nd, 5th, 8th and 1 1th) only two multiplications be done. If it is Apoklima (3rd, 6th, 9th and 12th) only one (i.e. the highest) should be taken.

बहुताडनसम्प्राप्तौ यां करोत्येकवर्गणाम्। बराहमिहिराचार्या सा न दृष्टा पुरातनेः ।।EI।

9. According to Varaha Mihira, if many multiplications are warranted, only the highest be considered. But this view is not acceptable to ancestors.

रिपुराशौ त्रिभागोनमर्धोनं

निम्नगास्तगाः ।

बायं ग्रहाः प्रयच्छन्ति नास्तगौ सितभानुजौ ।१०।।

10. REDUCTIONS FOR AMSAYURDAYA CONTRIBU- TIONS: If a plent is in its inimical sign, a third of the earlier (final) figure should be taken. If it is combust in the Sun, only half need be computed. The contributions by Venus and Saturn do not suffer reductions on account of their combustion.

सर्वमर्धं र तृतीयांशश्चतुर्यः पञ्चमस्तथा । वष्ठश्वांशः क्षयं याति व्ययाहामं प्रहे स्थिते ।११।। सौम्ये चार्घमितो याति नाशं बहुभिरेक्यः।

एक एव बली हन्ति स्वायुषः सर्वदा ग्रहः ।।१२।।

11-12. VYAYADI (CHAKRARDHA) HARANA: A malefic in the 12th loses all his contribution. If in the 1 Ith half, in the 10th a third, in the 9th a fourth, in the 8th one fifth and in the 7th one sixth of the contribution suffers. Benefics in these places lose just half of what malefics lose. Further, if there are two or more planets in one and the same house, only the strongest suffers reduction.

एकोनविशतिर्भानोः शशिनः पञ्चविशतिः । तिषयः कितिपुत्रस्य हवादशेव बुधस्य तु ।।१३।।

गुरोः पञ्चवशाब्दाश्चि विशाती रविपुत्रस्य

शुक्रस्याप्येक्काविशतिः । पिण्डायुः स्वोच्चसंस्थिते ॥ १४।।

## स्वोच्वशुद्धो ग्रहः शोध्यः षड्राश्यूनो भमण्डलात् । स्वपिण्डगुडितो भवतो भादिमानेन वस्सराः ॥१५।।

13-15. PINDAYU: In this system the planet from the Sun onwards through Saturn contribute respectively 19, 25, 15, 12, 15, 21 and 20 years in thcir order when they are in (deep) exaltation. From the longitude of the planct (for which the contribution is being computed) deduet its highest exaltation degree (counted from Aries first point). In case the mlnuend is greater than the subtrahend, then increase the former by 12 signs 1.e. 360 degrces). Multiply the latest product by the number of years allotted for the planet and divide by 12 to get years. Dividers for months equal to Amsas (or degrees i.e. 30) and for days Vikalas (i.e. 60).

## पूर्वोक्तं विन्तयेत्सर्व वत्रं मुक्त्वारिराशिषु ।

## क्षयस्तत्र प्रकर्तव्यो नीचेऽर्घ वृद्धिर्चबगे ।।१६।।

16. In the case of Pindayurdaya also, the same multiplications and rectifications apply. Leaving aside a retrograde planct, one in inimical sign loses a third while the one in fall retain only half of the contribution. The planet in exaltation retains the entire contribuţion.

## लग्नवायोऽशतुल्यः स्यादन्तरे चानुदाततः ।

## तत्पती बलसम्पन्ने राशितुर्यं च स्व चाधिवे।।१७३।

17. ASCENDENTAL CONTRIBUTION IN PINDAYUR- DAYA: If the Navamsa Lagna lord is strong, then the years contributed will equal the Navamsas past. If, however, the Lagna Rasi lord is stronger, then the Rasis past up to Lagna (counted from Aries) will be the number of years.

लग्नांशिलिप्तिका हत्वा प्रत्येकं बिहमायुचा। भक्त्वा मण्डललिप्ताभिलंब्धं वर्षादि शोध्येत् ।।१्।।

## स्वायुधो लग्नये क्रे लब्धस्यार्ध शुभेक्षिते । एवमेव प्रकर्तव्यं

जीवशर्मोक्तचन्द्रजे ।।१६।।

18-19. REDUCTION FOR RISING PLANETS: If malcfics occupy the ascendant, convert the ascendant into degrees, minutes etc. Multiply this by the number of years contributed by the malefic/s in question. Then divide the product by 21,600. Respective products should be reduced from the earlier contributions of each of the malefics occupying the ascendant. (This will be the net contribution by each malefic in case he occupics the ascendant). If the malefics in ascendant are aspected by the benefics suffice it to say half of the reduction need only be done.

विशतिरेकं द्वितयं नव धृतिरिह विशतिश्च पञ्चाशत् ।

वर्षाणामपि संस्याः सूर्यादीनां निसर्गभवाः ।२०।।

20. NISARGAYURDAYA: In the Nisargayu schemc, the planets from the Sun to Saturn contribute 20, 1, 2, 9, 18, 20 and 50 respectively.

Notes: For this system also, the other reductions apply as stated above.

मीनोदयेऽशे चरमे पञ्चविशतिलिप्तिके। गवि सौम्ये स्वतुंगस्थैः शेर्रायुः परं भवेत् ॥२१।

21. FULL SPAN OF LIFE: The full span of life (120 years and 5 days) will be enjoyed by the native who has Pisces ascendant with its 9th navamsa, and Mcrcury in 25 degrees of Taurus while others are in their deep exaltation points.

काकिलम्ने

## गुरुः सेन्दुः केन्द्रौ बुधमार्गवौ।

शेर्स्त्रलाभवव्ठस्थैर मितायुर्भवेन्नरः

H२२1

22. UNLIMITED LONGEVITY: Jupiter along with the Moon in Cancer identical with the ascendant, Mercury with

Venus in an angle (or separately in angles) while thc rest are in the 3rd, 6th and 1 lth: One with this combination will enjoy a limitless life span.

द्विघ्नाः षष्ठिनिशाः पञ्च परमं नरदन्तिनाम् । ह्वारात्रिशद्वाजिनामायुश्छागादीनां तु षोडश ।२३।

खरोष्ट्रयोः पंचवर्ग एकहीनो वृषादियु । शुनां तु द्वादश प्रोष्तं विभज्य नृपरायुषा।

ततत्परत्रमागेन हत्ववामायुरादिशेत् ।।२४।।

23-24. SPAN OF FULL LIFE FOR VARIOUS ANIMALS: The full span of life of elephants and men are 120 years and 5 days. For horses it is 32 years, for goats etc., 16 years, for camels and donkeys 25 years, for cows and buffaloes 24 years and for dogs 12 years. Just as for the mankind, the longevities for the various animals can be computed. The computations should be done as explained supra, multiplied by the figure representing full life span and divided by 120.

पथ्याशिनां शीलबतां नराणां

सद्वृत्तभाजां एवंविधानामिदमायुरत्र विजितेन्द्रियाणाम् । चिन्त्यं सदा \_\_\_ बृद्धमुनिप्रणीतम् १२५।।

25. Only those who consume wholesome diet, those who are virtuous, thosc who have good conduct and those who conquer their senses will enjoy the longevity as laid down by the sages.

इति कल्याणवर्मवि रचितायां सारावल्यां आयुर्दायो नाम चत्वारिशोऽध्यायः ॥४०।।

Thus ends the 40th Chapter entitled "LONGEVITY CALCULATIONS” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## अथ मूलदशाफलाध्यायः ॥४१॥ Chapter 41

## Moola Dasa

मायुषो येन यद्ग्तं सा दशा तस्य कीतिता।

स्त्रदोषगुणयोगेन स्वदशासु

फलप्रदाः ।।१।।

1. The dasa duration of a planet is equal to the number of years it contributes (as per Moola Dasa scheme). All planets yield in their dasas pleasant or unpleasant results according to their (natal) dispositions.

दिवारात्रप्रसूतस्य

रविशुक्रपुरःसराः ।

मणित्यस्त्याह तज्ज्ञानां फलसाम्यं न सा दशा ।।२।।

2. ORDER OF THE SCHEME: According to Manitha, those who are born during day time will have the dasas commencing with that of the Sun while the night-born will have it from that of Venus. But this view is not acceptable to other preceptors.

लग्नार्कशीतरश्मीनां यो बली तस्य चाग्रतः।

तत्केन्द्रादिस्थितानां च दशाः स्युः सत्यभाविते ।।३।।

3. SATYA’S VIEW: The first dasa will be of the strongest of the ascendant, the Sun and the Moon followed by the ones in angle, Panaphara and Apoklima. This is the view held by Satyacharya.

होरादिनेशशशिनां प्रबलो भवेद्य-

स्तरकप्टकादिषु गताः कथिता दशेशाः

पूर्वा दशाऽतिबलिनः सदृशेऽब्दवृद्धेः

साम्ये भवेच्च शरदां प्रथमोदितस्य ।४।। लग्नार्कशीतरश्मीनां यदि पूर्णवलं सबेत्। तदा सत्यमतं श्रेष्टमन्यदा त्वपरा दशा ।।।।

4-5. The first dasa will be of the strongest of the ascendant, the Sun and the Moon. Then the planets in angles will be dasa lords. Thereafter the planets in Panapharas (2nd, 5th, 8th and 11th houses) will lord over the major periods while the last dasas will be headed by the planets posited in Apoklimas (3rd, 6th, 9th and 12th houses). If angles etc., are occupied by more than one planet, the strongest will have its dasa first. Even if the strengths of these be identical, then the order will be according to maximum years donated by these planets. Granting that these durations are also similar, then the risings of planets will prevail. Even if one of the triad, utz. the ascendant, the Sun and the Moon, is strong, then Satya's scheme as above should be resorted to. If none is strong enough, then the dasa schcme of other preceptors may be considcred.

स्वोच्चस्वराशिनिजभागसुह्द्गृहस्याः

सम्पूर्णवीर्यरचिरा बलिनः स्वकाले।

मित्रोच्चभागसहिताः शुभवृष्टियुक्ताः

श्रेष्ठां दशां विदधति स्ववयःसु खेटाः ।६।।

6. AUSPICIOUS PERIODS: Planets in exaltation, own Rasi, own navamsa, friendly sign, possessing full rays and those with full strength prove auspicious in their dasas. At the commencement of a dasa of a planet that is strong, or is in a friendly division or is in aspect to a benefic also yield similar good results.

नीचशत्रुगृहं \_\_\_ प्राप्ताः : शमुनिम्नांशसूर्ययाः । विवर्णाः पापसम्बन्धा दशां दुर्युरशोभनाम् ।।७।।

7. INAUSPICIOUS PERIODS: The planet that is in its sign of fall, sign ruled by an enemy, navamsa of an enemy, combust in the Sun, devoid of splendour or is in aspect to malefics will yield inauspicious effects in its dasa.

सुंगाच्च्युतस्य हि दशा सुहृदुर्वांशेऽवरोहिणी मध्या।

नोचा्रिपुनीचांशे ग्रहस्य

चारोहिणी कष्टा ।८।।

8. If a planet has departed from its exaltation, its dasa is known as Avarohini (stepping down) dasa. If, however, it is in own or friendly divisions, the effects will be medium, 1.e. neither good nor bad. The dasa of a planet approaching its exaltation, departing from debilitation, is known as Arohini (climbing) dasa. If the said planet is in inimical or debilitation divisions, it will inflict difficulties.

सविता दशाफलानां पाचयिता चन्द्रमाः प्रपोषयिता। राशिविशेषेणेन्दोरतः फलोवितर्दशारम्भे II&।।

9. The Sun is the ripener (or baker) of the effects of a dasa while the Moon is the sustaming agent. That is why the Moon’s transit position at the time of the commencement of a dasa should be duly considered.

मूलवशायामिन्दोः कन्यायामुपगते \_\_\_ चन्द्रे । - पव्यांगनाभिरनिशं समागमं प्राहुरिह यवनाः ।१०। सौम्यस्त्रीधनलाम: कुलीरगेन्दी भवेद्दशारम्मे । क्षत्यां दूधवति नरः कुजमवने हन्ति वा युवतिम् ।।११। विद्याशास्त्रतानं मत्रप्राप्त करोति बुधराशी। शौत्रेऽन्नपानमतुलं सौस्यं चन्द्रेऽरिनाशं च ।१२। सुखध्नमानाज्ञाप्त जीवगृहे दिशति शीतांशुः। परिणतबयसमर्पां सौरगृहे वर्धकों वाऽपि ।१३।। दुर्गारब्यनिवासं कर्षणगृहकरमसेतुकर्मा तम् । सिहे शशी प्रकुस्ते स्त्रीपुत्रविवादमर्राति च ।१४।।

10-14. EFFECTS OF THE MOON: If the Moon at the commencement of a Moola dasa is in Virgo, the native throughout the period will be related to courtezans and be associated with them. The view is held by Yavanas. If she is in Cancer at the beginning of dasa, the native will gain wealth through virtuous ladies. If in Aries or in Scorpio he will injure the honour of virgins and also bring evils to his own female. If the sign in qucstion be of Mercury knowledge of Sastras and acquisition of friends will come to pass. In a sign of Venus, it brings plenty of food and drinks, happiness and destruction of cnemies. In a house of Jupiter, it brings happincss, wealth, honour and commandability. Saturn’s sign endows with association with old ladies. Leo denotes movements in forts and forests, agriculture and misunderstanding with wife and children. These are the effects accruing out of the Moon’s placement at the commencement of a dasa.

## बन्त्वर्यक्षयरोगाः कुजसौराम्यां बुधेन पाण्डित्यम्।

## वृष्टे तद्योनिसमेः शेर्यश्वन्द्रे दशाफलेवोगः ॥१५।।

15. ASPECTS TO MOON AT THE COMMENCEMENT OF A DASA: If the Moon at the commencement of a dasa receives the aspect of Mars or Saturn there will be loss of relatives and of wealth followed by diseases. Mercury's aspect confers learning while the aspects of other planets will give effects according to their nature.

## पाकस्वामिनि लग्ने सुहृदां वर्गेऽयवाऽपि सौम्यानाम् ।

## श्रेष्ठं दशा तदानीं सूतिलग्नादुपचयगृहस्थे वा ।१६।।

16. MAJOR LORD’S PLACEMENT AT THE COMMENCE- MENT OF A DASA: If the lord of the dasa is in the ascendant prevailing at the moment the dasa begins or in the 3rd, 6th, 10th or I 1th from the said ascendant, the dasa then proves excellent.

मित्रोच्चोपचयस्थाने त्रिकोणे सप्तमे तथा। पाकेश्वरात् स्थितश्चन्द्रः कुस्ते सुफलां दशाम् ।।१७।। विपरीते स्थिते चन्द्रे इसादौ पर्यवस्यते। स्वोच्चगस्यापि खेटस्य दशा न प्रतिपूजिता ।१८॥ 17-18. AGAIN MOON’S PLACEMENT: If the Moon at the commencement of a major period occupy a friendly sign, exaltation sign or an upachaya house (3rd, 6th, 10th or 1 1 th house), 5th, 9th or 7th counted from the major lord, good effects will come to pass in the dasa in question. If the Moon is otherwise placed, even a planet in exaltation at birth will give inauspicious results in its dasa.

## शत्रुनीचनवांशेत्रु शस्ते राशौ प्रहस्य च। दशा मिश्रफला रिक्ता विबलस्य दशा मता ।१६।।

19. SPECIAL EFFECTS BASED ON NAVAMSA: Even though a planet may be in friendly sign, if it occupies simultaneously a navamsa belonging to its enemy or be in fall in navamsa, the effects in the dasa concerned will be mixed. A weak planet's dasa will be futile.

## ड्रेश्काणैश्च दशा मूर्तेः पूजिता मध्यमाधमाः ।

चरे, मिश्रे प्रतीपा च, स्वरे पापेष्टमध्यमाः ।२०१।

20. LAGNA DASA: The ascendant’s major period will prove auspicious if it is in the first decanate of a movable sign; medium in the second decanate and poor in the 3rd decanate. The reverse is true with a common sign ascending, ie. third, second and first decanate will respectively be auspicious, medium and poor. As for a fixed sign ascending first, second and third decanates will respectively give poor, auspicious and medium effects.

चन्द्रावनेयसोमजसितजीव दिवाकराकिहोराणाम् ऋरमशो दशापरिग्रह इष्टो नैसागकश्चैव ।२१।। I

21. NAISARGIKA DASA: The Naisargika dasa follows in the order of the Moon, Mars, Mercury, Venus, Jupiter, the Sun, Saturn and the ascendant.

स्वोच्वस्वकालवलिनः सम्पर्णबलस्य वा निसर्गभवा।

अनुपमशुभफलदातौं ग्रहस्य नित्यं दशा भवति ।।२२।।

स्वराशौ स्वत्रिकोणे च स्वांशे च शुभमःयमा।

स्वोर्चाभिलाविणश्चव

मित्रराश्यादिसंस्यते ।।२३।।

शुमाधमदता ज्ञेत्रा विपरीतमतः स्यते।

## अनेनैव विधानेन विज्ञेया पापदा दशा ।।२४।।

22-24. If the planet is exalted, or is endowed with temporal strength or full strength, in its dasa incomparably auspicious results will come to pass. The planet that is in its own sign, Moolatrikona sign, own navamsa or hemmed betwecn benefics will yield moderatcly auspicious effects. In the dasa of the planct that is going towards its exaltation (departing from sign of its fall), or is placed in friendly signs etc., feebly auspicious effects will be felt. Similar deductions should be made in regard to planets otherwise placed.

भानुदशायां लमते वनौषधाध्वविषदौजनेर्थान् ।

गिरिदन्तचर्मवह्लिकोर्यनरेन्द्राह्वार्द्यश्च 111

नृपतेर्यावाप्त घैर्य भूवस्तयोद्धमं तैक्ष्यम् ।

स्याति प्रतापवृद्धि श्रेशठर्वं भूपतित्वं च ।२६।।

25-26. EFFECTS OF FAVOURABLE SUN'S DASA: If the Sun is favourable, in his dasa, there will be gains from forests, medicines, travels, poison, evil friendship, forests, ivory, skin, fire and royal association. Courage, diligence, intelligence, fame and valour will be on the increase. Excellence and kingship will be achieved by the native.

मृत्यार्थचोरचक्षुःशस्त्राग्युदकक्षितीश्वराद्बाधाः सुतपत्नोबन्धुजरनैनिपीडितः स्याच्च पापरतिः ॥ २७॥।

क्षुत्तृव्यातिः शोको हुर्रेडा पेत्लिकास्तया रोगाः। मात्रच्छदो भवति हि सूर्यदशापामनिव्टायाम् । २८।।

27-28. EFFECTS OF ADVERSE SUN’S DASA: If the Sun is adverse, in his dasa there will be grief on account of servants, wealth, theft, eyes, weapons, fire, water and king and the native will be tormented by children, wife and relatives. He will be interested in sinful acts and be troubled by hunger, thirst (or morbid fever), heart allments, billous diseases and bodily damages.

चन्दशार्यां वित्तं स्त्रीसंगममार्दवात्पथि विहारात् ।

जलतुहिनक्षीररसँरिक्षुविकारस्तथा क्रोडा । २६।।

द्विजमन्त्राणां लब्धिः पुष्पाम्बरसेवनं मधुरता च । तैवण्यादवाप्तसिद्धिः पूजां प्राप्नोति गुरुनुषाध्यां च ।।३०१। मेधावृतिपुष्टिकरी चन्द्रदशा शोभना नित्यम् ॥ ३०३।।

29-30. EFFECTS OF FAVOURABLE MOON’S DASA: If the Moon is favourable, in her dasa there will be gains through female assoclation, soft disposition, journey, water, ice, milk, juice, jaggery, sugar, sports, initiation into Mantras through Brahmins, flowers, robes, sweet food etc. He will achieve success in his undertakings out of his intelligence, will receive honours from elders and the king and will be endowed with increased courage and wisdom.

कुर्ते भयं कुलस्य च चन्द्रदशा स्वकुलविग्रहं कष्टम् ॥३१।।

## निद्रालस्यं स्त्रीणां भयजननी शोकदा त्वशुभा ।३२।

31-32. EFFECTS OF ADVERSE MOON’S DASA: If the Moon is evil, in her dasa there will be fear in family, misunderstanding with one’s family members, difficulties, loss of wealth, enmity with royal assembly, sleep, indolence,

evils through women and grief.

भौमवशार्यां लमते नृपाग्निचोरप्रयोगरियुमईँः । व्यालविवशस्त्रबन्वनयुतव्प्य कूदेश्व धनबहुलस् ।। ३३।। क्षित्याजाविकता स्रकसुवर्णवेश्यादिभिस्तथा दूतेः । आसवकषायकुटुर्क रसंश्व धनधान्यभाग्भवति ।।३४।

33-34. EFFECTS OF FAVOURABLE MARS DASA: During the dasa of favourable Mars, one will gain through kings, firc, thieves, enemies, snakes, poison, wcapons, confinements, fictitious articies, lands, goats, buffaloes, copper, gold, prostitutes, gambling, intoxicants, pungent articles/juices, wealth and grains.

मित्रकलत्रविरोधो स्रातृसुतेविग्रहश् तृथ्णा च। मूर्च्दा शोणितदोवः शाजतच्छेदो ग्रणाश्चापि ।३५।। परदाररतिहेप्यो गुर्सत्यानामधर्मनिरतश्च । पित्त्तरपि बोर्षरभिमूतो मानवो भवति ।३६।।

35-36. EFFECTS OF ADVERSE MARS DASA: During the dasa of evil Mars, there will be enmity with friends, wife, children and brothers, sufferances from thirst, swooning, bloody defects, loss of limbs and wounds. There will be attachment to others' wives and irreligious acts, followed by aversion to elders and to truth. Bilious excesses will trouble the subject.

सौम्यवसार्यां पुत्रान् मित्रादाढचाद्धनस्य सम्प्रात्तिः ।

बीक्षितनृपतेर्युताद्र शिग्जनाञ्चापि सम्मवति ।३७।। वेसरमहीवुवर्ण शुक्तित्रव्यं यशः प्रशंसा च। बौत्यं सौव्यमतुल्यं सौभाग्यं मतिचयश्यातिः ।।३च॥। घर्मत्रियामु सिद्धिहास्य रतिः मत्रुसंक्षयो भवति। गणितालेश्यलिपीनां कोतुकमायी सदा पुरुषः ।।३ह।। 37-39. EFFECTS OF FAVOURABLE MERCURY DASA:

In the dasa of favourable Mercury, there will be gains via friends, children, wealth, king, businessmen, priests, gambling etc. The native will acquire horses, lands, gold, fame, ambassadorship, limitless happiness, fortunes, high intelligence, fame, success in religious activities, fond of amusement, destruction of foes and enthusiasm in mathematics and drawings.

## पीडां धातुत्रितयात्पार्थ्यं बन्धनं तयोद्वेगम्। मानसशोकं वाऽपि बुधस्य कष्टा दशा कुरुते ।।४०।।

40. EFFECTS OF ADVERSE MERCURY’S DASA: If Mercury is evil, in the concerned dasa the native will be tormented by imbalances of the thrcc temperaments (viz. bile, phlegm and wind), hard disposition, imprisonment, mental grief and excitement.

त्रिदशपतिगुर्दशायां मन्त्रिनरपतृत्यनीतिभिवित्तम् । मानगुणानां लब्धिरतिप्रतापः सुदृद्धिवृद्धिश्च ॥४१।। काग्तासुवर्णवेसरगजाश्वभोगी सदा \_\_ पुरुषः । मांतस्यपीव्टिकानां लामो द्विदतां विनाशश्च ।।४२।। लाभो भवति नराणां प्रीतिः सद्भूमिर्षैः सार्थम् । जनताया नृपवक्त्रा पथ्याग्राद्गुरुजनाच्च धनलामः ।।४३।।

41-43. EFFECTS OF FAVOURABLE JUPITER’S DASA: If Jupiter is favourable, the native in that dasa will gain through ministers (or advisers), kings, dances and statesmanship. He will gain honours, virtues, courage, wide friendship, life partner, gold, quadrupeds, conveyances, auspicious acts, destruction of enemies, royal acquaintance and will obtain wealth through public, king, business and elders.

गात्रश्ववनुबुशोकं पंगुत्वं गुल्मकर्णरोगांश्च । पुंस्त्वविनाशं मेदःक्षयं नृपतितो भयं समाप्नोति ॥४४।।

44. EFFECTS OF ADVERSE JUPITER’S DASA: If Jupiter be inauspicious one will in such dasa have physical defects, excessive grief, lameness, diseases of the stomach and ears, loss of semen and marrow and fear from kings.

Notes: Marrow is one of the seven dhatus in the body lying in the abdomen.

45-46. EFFECTS OF FAVOURABLE VENUS DASA: 1f Venus is auspicious one will in the dasa be successful and will gain lands, residences, bed comforts, wife, garlands, robes, good food, fame, happiness and treasure. He will have liking for music, will develop acquaintance with females, will gain through kings and lands, will derive wisdom, desired happiness and friends and gains through articles used for sexual enjoyments.

कुलगुणवृद्ध्वादो यानासनसम्मवावि कष्टानि । स्त्रीनृपन्यकक्तवश्यं लोकविरुद्धः सह प्रीतिः ।४७।

47. EFFECTS OF UNFAVOURABLE VENUS DASA: If Venus is inauspicious the native will in the dasa have litigations with virtuous people, will have troubles from conveyances, furniture, females and kings and will develop intimacy with unsocial elements.

सेरेर्देशां प्रपन्नः प्राप्नोति पुमान्सरोष्ट्रमहिवाद्यान् । कुलटां जरदंगीं वा कुलित्यतिलकोद्रवादींश्च ।४८।। वृन्दप्रामपुराणाममधिकारभवं च सत्कारम्। लोहत्रपुकावीनां स्वकीयपक्षस्थिरास्पदं चैव ।।४eII 48-49. EFFECTS OF FAVOURABLE SATURN’S DASA:

If Saturn is favourable in his dasa the native will gain camels, assess etc.. Other effccts: association with unchaste or old ladies, gain through pulses, sesamum, and grains eaten by poor people, leadership over villages and iron etc. His people will acquire a permanent position.

बाहननाशोदेगस्त्वरतिः स्त्रीस्वजनविप्रयोगश्च ।

बुद्धेब्वपजयदोषो मद्यद्यूतोद्भवो मरुत्कोपः ।५०।।

पुण्येष्वसिद्धिकलहं बन्धनतन्द्राश्रमं तथा व्यंगम्। भृत्यापत्यविरोधो भवति च कष्टा यदा दशा सौरेः ।५१।

50-51. EFFECTS OF ADVERSE SATURN'S DASA: If Saturn is adverse, there will be loss of conveyances, excitement, enmity, separation from wife and other family members, loss in battlcs, liking for intoxicants and gambling, windy diseases, loss of meritorious acts, confinemcnt, lassitude, afflictions to limbs and enmity with children and servants.

52. The good and bad effects for the dasas have been explained above in a general manner. Narrated below are the effects arising out of special considerations..

53. SUN’S DASA: There are five kinds of placements which enable us to derive five different types of Sun's dasa. These positions are: exaltation, fall, Moolatrikona, angle and enemy’s sign.

राज्यं ददाति विपुलं दशा रवेर्लग्नसंस्थितस्य नृणाम् । केन्द्रस्थितस्य दयात् कठिगलनेत्रप्रकोपमरिगस्य ॥५४।।

नीवस्य दशा भानोरक्ष्णोर्नाशं ज्वर शिरोरोगम्। बन्धनमन्याश्च रुजः कुष्ठामयदर्शनं चिद्लम् ॥५५। स्वोच्चप्राप्तस्य दशा दवाति राज्यं सहस्रकिरणस्य । तुरगातपत्रचामरकरीन्रसंबधितं सम्यक् ।५६।। सवितुर्वशा च पुंसो विवधाति पुरा त्रिकोणसंस्थस्य । उत्तमविषयपतित्वं विध्वस्ताशेवदुःखस्य ।।५७।। शत्रुगृहेऽर्दशायां नयनविनाशो भवेच्च कुब्जत्वम् । ज्वालावक्त्रजरोगा भवन्ति कुनयात् परिभवाश्च ॥ ५८।। अष्टमगतस्प भानोर्दशा क्षयं नयति सर्वगात्रंच। स्रमयति देशाह्रेशं प्रमापयत्यपि च विक्लिष्टम् ।।५६।।

54-59. The Sun in ascendant gives kingdom during his dasa; while posited in other kendras ruled by inimical planets there will be disorders of hip, throat and eyes. Debilitated Sun in his dasa causes destruction of eyes, fever and head disorders, confinement, leprosy and other disorders. If the Sun is exalted, there will be gain of kingdom with royal insignia in such dasa. Apart, there will be acquisition of elephants, steeds etc. The dasa of the Sun placed in his Moolatrikona will indicate destruction of all kinds of miseries and leadership of large provinces. In the dasa of the Sun placed in inimical sign, one will incur affliction of eyes, hump-back, disorders caused by fire, facial diseases and humiliation due to misconduct. The 8th house placement of the Sun will cause in his dasa affliction to the entire body and wanderings in various countries.

सामान्यतश्च षोढा चन्द्रदशा भिद्यते समासेन।

स्वोच्चसुङ्गच्छत्रुगम्हे नीचे क्षीणे प्रपूर्णे च ।६०। तत्रोच्वदशा राज्यं नीचदशा मरणमरिदशा बन्धम्। कथयति नलिनीशत्रोरमि त्रदशा स्वजनसम्प्राप्तिम् ॥ ६१।

क्षीणेन्दुदशायोगे चिल्लाग्येतानि लक्षयेहिद्वान् । उदरामयज्वर शिरोनयनोत्कोपप्रतिश्रयाच्चापि 1 0 बलिनः परिपुर्णस्य च शशिनः कुरुते सदा दशा पुंसाम् । दयिता सह्लुपरिवृतमन्तःपुरमुसमस्त्रीकम् 1131

भवति नरस्य स्रंशो विषयस्यान्तःपुरस्य भृत्यानाम् । अष्टमचन्द्रदशायां श्रियते च स्वजनपरिभूतः । ६४।। यन्त्रतृणकाष्ठगोमयवंशकरञ्जीफलोदकाजीवी I

भवति कबन्नकुचेली नृपोऽपि भृतकोऽरिगृहदशायाम् ।६५।

60-65. MOON’S DASA: The Moon’s dasa is generally differcntiated in six categories, viz. exalted, debilitated, friendly sign, inimical sign, and diminishing and increasing Moon’s dasas. The different dignities of the Moon give following results in the dasa conccrned: exalted—kingdom, debilitated—death, inimical sign—confinement, friendly sign—association with own men, decrcasing Moon—fever and disorders of abdomen, head and eyes. If the Moon is full and is endowcd with strength, in such dasa the native will be surrounded by thousands of superior fcmales. If she be relegated to the 8th house, the native in such dasa will be humiliated by his own men, will livc through machincry, grass, firewood, cowdung, bamboo, medicinal plants, fruits and water. He will acquire only deteriorated food and rags, and even a king will have to resort to servitude.

लग्नगृहगस्य हि दशा मण्डललाभं तथोच्वगस्यापि।

केन्द्रसिथितस्य कुरते घनवाहनदेशसम्प्राप्तिम् ॥६६।। षष्ठदसा व्यसनकरी मरणं च करोति नैधनस्यदशा। अस्तमितग्रहपाको बन्धमा त्रेण पीडयति ॥६७॥

वक्रोपंगस्य हि दशा भ्रमयति च कुलालचक्रवत्पुरुषम् । ब्यसनानि रिपुविरोधं करोति पापस्य न शुभस्य ।।६८।। 66-68. OTHER PLACEMENT OF PLANETS:’Those

exalted in the ascendant confer rulership of a province in their dasas while those in exaltation in other angles will bestow, wealth, conveyances and rulership of a country. If a planet is in the 6th, the native will be subjected to evils, misfortunes etc. In the dasa of a planet placed in the 8th, death will be inflicted. There will only be confinement in the dasa of a combust planet. The dasa of a retrograde planet will cause wanderings like that of a wheel, evils and enmity. This applies only to malefics in retrogression but not to benefics.

69. Though one may be a king, he will become a servant of his own servants in the dasas of a planct devoid of strength, a planel totally devoid of strength, a planet in fall, a planet in deep fall, a planet in inimical sign and a planet in dead enemy’s sign.

70. In the dasa of a planet placed in enemy's sign, there will be circumstances to leave one’s own country (in distress), diseases, loss of position, frequent quarrels and confinement.

महितकरिगलितमदजलसेकक्ष्मापीठवारितरजस्कः राजा कष्टसहायो रिक्तदशायां ध्रुवं स्रमति ।७१।

71. Even a king with vast number of elephants that can wet the earth with their ichor, will surely wander in difficulty in the dasa of a planet occupying an inimical sign.

अंगत्रत्यंगानां छेदं विदधाति षध्ठशत्रुदशा। कोणद्यूनारिदशा निधनारिदशा शिरश्छेदम् ॥७२।।

## रिपुभवविदेशगमनं बन्धनरोगादिपोडनं भवति। नीत्रस्वग्रह्पाके राजाभिभवो ध्रुवं पुंसाम् ।।७३।।

72-73. In the dasas of the planets placed in the 6th, 5th, 9th or 7th houses identical with inimical signs, there will be troubles of limbs and sccondary limbs (like the nose), while the dasa of the planet in the 8th house identical with its inimical house will cause beheading (or afflictions to head), fear from enemies, wanderings in foreign places, troubles of confinement, diseases etc. If the dasa of a planet that is in fall be in operation humiliation from royal sources will follow.

चिन्ता स्वप्नानुभर्वः परिणमति फलं विहीनवीर्यस्य । पञ्चमहापुरुषोक्ताध्यायांस्तान्तियोजयेद त्र

74. The native will derive good effects of a weak planet's dasa in dreams and thoughts. The cffects due to Pancha Maha Purusha Yogas will come to pass in the dasas of the concerned planets.

## आदी दशासु फलदः शीर्षोदयराशिसंस्थितो विह्मः।

उभयोदये च मध्ये स्वान्त्ये पृष्ठोवये च नीचक्षे ।७५।।

75. The planets in Sirodaya signs will give thcir effocts in the beginning of the dasas; thosc in Ubhayodaya will give in the middle of the dasas effects duc to them and those in Prishtodaya signs as well as those in fall will reveal their effects in the concluding parts of their dasas.

Thus ends the 41st Chapter entitled “MOOLA DASA" in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अथान्तर्दशाफलाध्यायः ।।४२॥

## Chapter 42

## Effects of Sub Periods

अर्धमेकस्थितो भागं त्रिभागं सुतधर्मयोः । सप्तमे सप्तमं भागं चतुर्थं चतुरश्रयोः ॥१।।

1. DISTRIBUTION OF SUB PERIODS TO PLANETS IN MOOLA DASA: A planet that is conjunct the Dasa Lord will subrule half of the period. The subrulcrship further goes to planets as undcr: one third to that in the 5th/9th from thc dasa lord; one seventh to that in the 7th from the dasa lord and one fourth to that in the 4th/8th with refcrence to dasa lord.

मूलं दशाधिनायस्य कृत्वांशं स्वगुर्णग्रहः। करोत्यन्तर्दशां सत्यां बली हरति भागश ।।२।। शुभस्य शुभवः पूर्णः करस्याशुभदो भवेत् । केन्द्रादिविधिना चाग्ये केवित्पाकक्रमेण तु ।।३।।

2-3. The sub lords will ripen the sub period effects according to their own nature. Should there be more than one planet in the houses specified above, the strongest of them will rule over the sub period (and not others). The sub periods of benefics will yield benefic results while those of malefics will give inauspicious effects. According to some authorities, planets in angles will first lord over the sub periods followed by those in Panaphara (2nd, 5th, 8th and 11th) and Apoklima (3rd, 6th, 9th and 12th.)

## सत्योवतं तुच्यते करिच्दाति बलवान् ग्रहः। नित्यं पाकत्रमात्कार्यः शेवास्तु परिपाकदाः ॥४।

4. According to Satyacharya, the sub periods will rulc according to the orders of strength in case there is more than one planet in one house.

## भायाः सदृशाः सहिता दशाब्दपिण्डस्य भागहारोऽ्यम् । पृथगंशा गुणकाराः पृथक्पृथक्त्वन्तरदशाः स्युः ॥ ५।।

5. Take the fractions (as mentioned in sloka 1) and reduce them to equivalent fractions with a common denominator. Add up the aliquot parts of the dasa representative of the various numerators leaving out the denominators. The sub periods will be arrived at when the various aliquots are converted into years within the main dasa and divided by a common factor.

## एकर्षसंस्थितदशा प्रदिशति बन्धं स्वनाशं वा। जनयति कण्टकसहितत्रिकोणसंस्थस्य सुखमतुलम् ॥ ६।

अत्र द्वित्रिचतुर्णां मध्ये रिपुदशा ग्रहाणां चेत्।

व्याधिक्लेशविवादान्नुपतेश्च भयं तदा जनयेत् ॥७।।

6-7. Planets that are conjunct the major lord will cause confinement and loss of wealth in their sub periods. If, however, the major lord is in an angle or in a trine from the ascendant, there will be great happiness. If a planet conjunct 2 or more plancts be in an inimical house, in such sub periods, only diseases, evils, quarrels and royal displeasures will come to pass.

दारमरणं च जनयति सप्तमगान्तर्दशा प्रणाशं वा।

शत्रोर्दासीकरणं परपुरुषेणोपभोगं वा ।८।।

8. In the sub period of a planet in the 7th from the Mooladasa lord, death of wife, or the wife’s becoming a servant of an enemy or getting attached to another man is denoted.

अन्तर्दशा ग्रहाणामष्टमधाम्नि प्रणाशयेत्पृरुषम्। कुर्ते च धनविनाशं शत्रुगता बन्धनं च विध्वंसम् ॥EI अन्तर्दशा यदा द्वित्रिचतुणमिकराशिसंस्थानाम्।

बन्धनविनाशर्दैन्यं विदधात्यशुभग्रहाणां तु ।।१०।

9-10. Death, destruction of wealth or imprisonment will result in the sub periods of the planet in the 8th from the Mooladasa lord. If 2 or 3 or 4 malefics join in one house other than an angle or a trine, imprisonment, destruction, and poverty will be the order of the sub period.

## मित्रारोग्यकरी निर्यं सौस्यमानविवर्धनी ।११।

11. In the sub period of a planet in the 4th from the Mooladasa lord there will be gain through friends, happiness and increased honours.

## शमयति रिपुप्रताषं नीरोगत्वं करोति घनलाभम् । भानुदशायां चन्द्रः प्रविशंस्तन्नास्ति यन्न शुभम् ।१२।

12. SUN DASA—MOON BHUKTI: In the major period of the Sun, the Moon’s sub period will subdue the valour of the enemics of the native. He will gain health, wealth and happiness.

## विद्रुमसुवर्णमणयः संत्रामजयः प्रचण्डता पुंसः ।

असृजो दरात्रवेशे सूर्यवशायां भवति सौद्यम् ।।१३।।

13. SUN DASA—MARS BHUKTI: In the sub period of Mars and in the major period of the Sun, the native will gain corals, gold, success in battle, splendour and happiness.

## बद्रविचाचिकार्यः पाम्ना कुष्ठेश्व गहितशरीरः।

## तरणिदशायां प्रविशति बुधो यदा स्यादरेवृद्धिः ॥१४।

14. SUN DASA—MERCURY BHUKTI: In the sub period of Mercury under the Sun’s major period, the native will suffer from itch, leprosy etc., and will face increase of enemies.

व्याधिभिररि भिर्व्यसर्नः पार्यश्च विमुच्यते तयाऽलक्षम्या ।

अनुयाति धर्मपदर्वों जीवस्यान्तर्दशा यदा मानोः ।१५।।

18. SUN DASA—JUPITER BHUKTI: If the sub period of Jupiter be in operation in the major period of the Sun, there will be destruction of diseases, enemies, sins and poverty and gain of virtues and happiness.

## शिरसो रुग्गलरोगश्द्रं सहसा प्वरः शूलम्।

16. SUN DASA—VENUS BHUKTI: In the sub period of Venus under the Sun’s major period, one will suffer from head and throat diseases, fever and gout, followed by defeat from enemies and leaving the native country.

आवित्यस्य दशायां शर्नश्चरान्तर्वेशा यदा भवति।

17. SUN DASA—SATURN BHUKTI: In the sub period of Saturn and in the major period of the Sun, there will be royal wrath, poverty and defeat from enemies.

## क्षयरोगभयं शौयें नृपत्रभावं सवा विभवम् ।

18. MOON DASA—SUN BHUKTI: During the Sun’s sub period, in the major period of the Moon, the native will

suffer from tuberculosis, but will be valorous, will earn wealth through kings and will enjoy all kinds of monetary gains.

19. MOON DASA—MARS BHUKTI: In the major period of the Moon and in the sub period of Mars, there will be billous complaints, diseases of the blood, fear from fire, evils, illhealth and fear from thieves.

21. MOON DASA—JUPITER SUB PERIOD: In the major period of the Moon and in the sub period of Jupiter, there will be gain of wealth, sudden acquisition of robes, jewels and conveyances.

23. MOON DASA—SATURN BHUKTI: In the sub period of Saturn in the major period of the Moon, there will be separation from one’s own men, fear from diseases and great grief.

24. MARS DASA—SUN BHUKTI: If the Sun’s sub period, in the major period of Mars be in operation, the native will be fierce, valorous, be honoured by the king, be successful in battle and will acquire wealth in various ways.

25. MARS DASA—MOON BHUKTI: In the sub period of the Moon in the major period of Mars, the native will procure wealth in various ways, be happy, will have many friends and plenty of corals etc.

36. JUPITER DASA—SUN BHUKTI: The native will be free from enemies and diseases and will be honoured by the king in the sub period of the Sun in the major period of Jupiter. He will further be endowed with valour and happiness.

50. SATURN DASA—MARS BHUKTI: During the sub period of Mars in the major period of Saturn, one will leave the country (in adverse circumstances) and will incur diseases and grief in many matters.
