विदधात्यन्तर्वंशाप्राप्तः ॥ ५१।।

51. SATURN DASA—MERCURY BHUKTI: In the sub period of Mercury in the major period of Saturn, the native will enjoy fortunes, happiness, success, and honour and will financially gain.

अनुदाति शिष्टपदर्वों प्रामाविकलत्रसौर्यसंपन्नः । रवितनदस्य दशायां प्रविशति जीवे सदा पुरुषः ॥ ५२।।

52. SATURN DASA—JUPITER BHUKTI: In the sub perlod of Jupiter and in the major period of Saturn, one will gain a high position and will acquire villages and happiness from his wife.

वर्धवति मित्रपक्षं भिनत्ति शोकान्यशः प्रकाशयति ।

सौरदशायां : पत्नीधनविजयलाभकरः ।५३।।

53. SATURN DASA—VENUS BHUKTI: In the sub perlod of Venus and in the major period of Saturn, one will have increased number of friends, frecdom from grief, increase of fame, happiness from wife, wealth and success.

नी्ोच्चादिविभेदेन शत्रुमित्रबलावलम् । अन्तर्दशासु मतिमांजिवन्तयेच्च प्रयरनतः ॥५४।।

अन्तर्दशा शुभायां मूलदशायां शुभा यवा भवति। भवति तदा बहुसौर्यं धनलब्धिरतीव पुरुषाणाम् ॥५५।। रविकिरणमुवितदीप्तेर्दशा ग्रहस्य मलिनां तनुं हृत्वा ।

मानयशोर्थविलासप्रताप्पोद्यमान् हन्ति ।५६।

होरा-जन्माधिपयोः शत्रुदशायां नरोऽतिमूढमतिः । राज्याच्च्युतो विपक्षरभिभूतोऽन्यं समाश्रयति ।।५७।।

54-57. SPECIAL CONSIDERATIONS: The effects of sub periods as above will stand modified according to the exaltation, fall etc., of the planet. One will have abundant income, happiness etc., in the major period of a benefic planet and in the sub period of another benefic planet. If a

planet is combust, during such planet’s period, one will have a dirty body and will incur destruction of wealth, happiness, valour, beauty and enthusiasm. In the dasa of planets inimical to Ascendant lord or to the Moon sign lord, one will be very foolish, will be deprived of kingdom, will be insulted by enemies and will depend on others.

व्योमलम्नप्रपन्नस्य दशायां राज्यमाप्नुयात् । नरेन्द्राणां समायोगे सुवीर्यस्याथवा पुनः ।५८।।

58. The planet that is close to the 10th cusp will confer kingdom. If there is none in the 10th, the strongest of the planets will give the said effect.

लग्ने जीवः सितबुध्युतः सप्तमस्थोऽर्हपुत्रः कर्मप्राप्तो दहनकिरणो भोगिनां जन्म कुर्युः। के द्रे सौम्या न शुभगृह्गा यत्र पापाभिधाना

यद्येवं स्याच्यवरनृपतिर्जायते वित्तवांश्च ।।५६।।

59. One will be opulent if Mercury, Jupiter and Venus occupy the ascendant, Saturn the 7th and the Sun the 10th. If angles are occupied by benefics while malefic planets are not there, the native will be wealthy becoming leader of forest tribes.

इति कस्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावस्यां अन्तर्दशाफलो नाम द्विचर्वारिशोऽध्यायः ।४२।।

Thus ends the 42nd Chapter entitled “EFFECTS OF SUB PERIODS” in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अथ दशारिष्टफलाध्यायः ॥४३॥ Chapter 43

## Evil Effects of Dasa

कूरदशायां ऋूरः प्रविश्य चान्तर्दशां यदा कुश्ते। पुंसः स्यात्सन्वेहस्तदाऽरियोगः सुरदेव महान् ।।११। क्षितितन्यस्य दशायां रविजस्यान्तर्दशा यदा विशति। बहुकालजीविनामपि मरणं निःसंशयं कुश्ते ।२।। ऋूररासौ स्यतः पापा वष्ठे स्यान्तिधनेऽपि वा। तत्स्थितेनारिणा दृष्टः स्वपाके मृत्युदो ग्रहः ॥ ३।। विलग्नाधिपतेः शत्रुलग्नेशान्तर्दशा गतः । करोत्यकस्मान्मरणं सत्याचार्य: \_ प्रभाषते ।४।।

1-4. In the sub period of a malefic planet and in the major period of another malefic, the native will be troubled by doubts and enemies. It is certain that death will be inflicled upon the native though he may have life indications, in the dasa of Mars and in the sub period of Saturn. If a malefic is in the 6th or the 8th in aspect to another malefic placed in a malefic’s sign, death will come to pass. In the dasa of a planet that is inimical to ascendant lord and in the sub period of the ascendant lord himself, there may be death, according to Satya.

इति कल्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां दशारिष्टफलं नाम

त्रि चत्वारिशोऽध्यायः ||४३।।

Thus ends the 43rd Chapter entitled “EVIL EFFECTS OF DASAS” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## अथ दशारिष्टभंगाध्यायः ॥४४।। Chapter 44

## Antidotes for Evils of Dasas

प्रवेशे बलवान् लेट: शुर्मर्वा सुनिरोक्षितः । सौम्याधिमित्रवर्गस्यो मृत्यवे न भवेत्तदा ।।१। मूलं दशाधिनावस्य विबलस्य दशा यदा। बलिनः स्यातदा भंगो दशारिष्टस्य तद्ध्रुवम् ।।२।। बुद्धे च विजयी तस्मिन्ग्रह्योगे शुचे यदि। दशायां न मवेत्कष्टं स्वोच्चादिषु च संस्थितः ।३।

1-3. If at the time of commcncement of a dasa, even if one planet occupies the divisions of a benefic or of an intimate friend and is aspected by a benefic, the evils due to come out of the said dasa will disappear. If the lord of the dasa is not strong enough to cause evil while the planet functioning as an antidote (as stated in the prcvious sloka) is stronger, the evils are then surcly destroyed. Even if a single planet occupies its exaltation sign, Moolatrikona sign or own sign and is aspected by a benefic or is conjunct a benefic, at the start of a dasa the evils of the dasa are nullified.

इति कल्याणवर्मरचितायां सारावल्यां दशारिष्टभगो नाम

चतुश्चर्वारिशोऽध्यायः ॥४४।।

Thus ends the 44th Chapter entitled "ANTIDOTES FOR EVILS OF DASAS” in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अथ उच्चादिस्थग्रहफलाध्याय ।४५॥

## Chapter 45

## Effects of Planetary Dignities

अत्युग्रमतिद्रव्यं महान्तमपि भास्करः स्वतुंगस्थः । मृष्टाशनाम्बराइच सुभूषणं शीतगुः कुर्ते ।१। तेजस्विनं कुतनयो दुष्प्रसहं गवितं प्रवासरतम् । मेधाविनं कुलाढय सुनिपुणवाद्यं बुधः स्वोच्चे ।२। विस्यातं गुरुराद् विद्वांसं सत्तं कुशलम्। स्वोच्चे भूगोश्व तनये विलासहार्यप्रमीतनृत्तरतम् ।।३।। स्वोच्चस्यो रवितनयो नृपलब्धनियोग्माभिजनयेत् । ग्रामपुराधिपतित्वमरण्यकधान्यं कुनारिलाभं च ।४।

1-4. EFFECTS OF EXALTED PLANETS: If the Sun is exalted at birth, the native will be fierce, be very wealthy and bc pre-eminent. With exalted Moon, one will enjoy swcet food, possess superior clothes and will acquire good ornaments. If Mars is exalted the native will be splendorous, valorous, blameworthy and will live in other places. When Mercury is exalted the native will be intelligent, be supreme among his family members and be a skilful speaker. One will be famous, wealthy, learned, honourable and skilful with the exaltation of Jupiter. If Venus is in its peak one will be fond of sports, amusements, music, dance etc. Saturn’s exaltation denotes association with royal assignment, leadership over villages and towns, gain of forest-born grains and mean females.

भानुस्त्रिकोणसंस्थो धनवन्तं मुस्यम तिनिपुणम् । भोषतारं गुणवन्तं शशी प्रसूतौ त्रिकोणगः पुरुवम् ।।५।। वक्रोऽपि तस्करपाति शूरं खलु निर्वयं चापि। सोम्यो विनोदशीलं जयिनं च स्वत्रिकोणगः कुर्ते ।६। जीवः पुनहितकरं महत्तर नयविदं सुखोपेतम्। दानवपूज्यो जनयेद्प्रामपुरवरिष्ठमाढयम तिसुभगम् ।।७।। आत्मत्रिकोण आकर्यनतृप्तं कुलयुतं शूरम् ।।७)।। 5-71 /2. EFFECTS OF PLANETS IN MOOLA TRIKONAS: Should the Sun be in his Moolatrikona sign, the native will be wealthy, intelligent and skilful. The Moon so placed indicates one with enjoyments and virtues. Mars in Moolatrikona will make one a leader of thieves, adventurous and unkind. The placement of Mercury in his Moolatrikona sign denotes a jocular and successful person. One will be helpful to others, be pre-cminent and will have knowledge of justice with Jupiter in Moolatrikona. Venus in such a sign will make one a prominent person in his village/town, wcalthy and fortunate. If Saturn is so posited the native belongs to a good family, be wealthy and valorous.

तीक्षणमयूखः कुस्ते महोब्रमत्युच्वकर्माणम् ।।८।। धर्मरतं हिमरश्मर्मनस्विनं रूपवन्तमात्मक्षें। आइच प्रचण्डमचलं भौमः कुस्ते स्वराशियः पुरुषम् ।६। शशितनयोऽपि विधत्ते बल्गुकचं पण्डितं वाऽपि । काव्यश्रुतिज्ञमाइच गुरुचेष्टं वाक्पतिः स्वराशिस्थः ।।१०।। दानवपूज्यः कुरुते कृषीवलं स्फीतवित्तं च । कुर्ते शनेश्वरोऽपि च मान्यमदुःखं स्वराशियः पुरुषम् ।११।

8-11. EFFECTS OF PLANETS IN OWN SIGNS: If the Sun is placed in his own sign, the native will be very flerce and will do very great acts. The Moon in her own sign denotes one virtuously disposed, who is very intelligent and

beautiful. If Mars occupies such a sign, the native will be opulcnt, impetuous and firm in disposition. Should Mercury occupy his own sign the native will be a sweet speaker and be learned. If Jupiter be in his own sign one will be versed in Vedas, literature etc., be rich and will perform great deeds, Venus so placed hints at a farmer and a rich person. Should Saturn be in his own house, the native will be honourable and happy.

मित्रगृहेऽर्कः स्यातं स्वरसौहृदमर्थदातारम् । मित्रक्षगः शतांको यतस्ततो लब्धसोस्यबहुमानम् ।१२।। अंगारकोऽपि कुस्ते सुहृद्धनारक्षणासक्तम्। शशिजः सुहृद्युह्गतः करोति चातुर्यहास्यधनवन्तम् ।।१३।। बबसामधिपः पुज्यं स्तां च सुविशिष्टकर्माणम्। मित्रय्हे भूगुतनयः सुहृत्त्रयं दवितवित्तमतिशूरम् ॥१४। भास्करसूनु: कुस्ते परान्तमोजिनमधर्मकर्मरतम् ॥ १४३।

12-141/2. EFFECTS OF PLANETS IN FRIENDLY SIGNS: If the Sun is in a friendly sign, one will be firm in friendship and be charitable. The Moon in a friendly sign denotes the native will be happy and very honourable. Mars placed in a friendly sign will make one protect the wealth of friends. One will be skilful, jocular and rich, with Mercury in a friendly sign. Jupiter in a friendly sign will make one respectable and a performer of famous deeds. If Venus be in a friendly sign the native will be fond of friends, rich and adventurous. Saturn in a sign of his friend will force the native to depend on others for food and inclined to do unethical deeds.

नीचे सविता कुस्ते प्रेव्यं बान्धवजनावघूतं च ।१५।। हिमरश्मरत्पपुष्यं रोगिणमपि दुर्भगं लोके। मीचस्वः कितितनयोऽनर्थव्यसनोपतप्तमतिनीचम् ॥ १६।।

कुर्ते हिमकरपुत्रः क्षुद्रं स्वज्ञातिबन्धुर्वरं च। नीचे गुरुः प्रकुर्ते मलिनं प्राप्तावमानमतिवीनम् ॥१७।। असुरदवितोऽस्वतन्त्रं प्रणष्टवारं विवमशीलम् । कोणो विपःनशीलं विर्याहिताचारमर्यरहितं च ।१८।

15-18. EFFECTS OF PLANET IN FALL: The various planets in fall denote the undermentioned effects: The Sun—servitude and humiliation in the hands of one’s own men. The Moon—not very meritorious in his acts, sickly and unfortunate. Mars—evils, distressed mind and meanness. Mercury—mean and enmity with his cousins and other relatives. Jupiter—dirty, dishonour and utter poverty. Venus—subordination, loss of wife and crooked disposition. Saturn—fall into adversity, blameworthy conduct and pennilessness.

कुरुते शत्रुगृहेको निःस्वं विवयत्रपीडितं चापि । तुहिनमयूखः कुस्ते हुद्रोगिणमरिगृहे नरं सततम् ।।१८। बन्यारिसंगभाजं दीनं विहलं च बुर्भगं भौमः। अज्ञानमतिविहीनं बुवोऽरिमे नेकदुःखमतिदीनम् ।२०।। क्लीबं गुरुविधत्ते नयहीनं धनविहीनं च।

शुक्रोऽरिग्हे भृतक कुतन्त्रमतिदुःखितं जनयेत् ।।२१।।

भास्करसुतोऽपि कुर्ते मलिनं व्याध्यादिशोकसन्तप्तम् ।।२१३।

19-211/2. EFFECTS OF PLANETS IN INIMICAL SIGNS: If the Sun is placed in an inimical sign, the native will be poor and be sensually tormented. Should the Moon be so placed, one will be devoid of beauty (or brightness) and will suffer from heart diseases. Mars in an inimical sign denotes that nattve will face confinement and defeat at the hands of enemies, poverty, distress and misfortunes. If Mercury be placed in a sign that is ruled by his enemy, the subject will be devoid of intelligence, will experience many miseries and will be very poor. Should Venus be in his enemy’s sign, the

person concerned will be a servant, will resort to wrong ways of doing things and be very miserable. If Jupiter is placed in a sign owned by his enemy, one will be a eunuch and be devoid of justice and wealth. When Saturn occupics an inimical camp, the native will be dirty and be distressed due to diseases and the like.

स्वेवूच्वभागेषु फलं समग्रं स्वक्षेत्रतुल्यं भवनांशकेषु । नीचारिभागेसु जघन्यमेव मध्यं फलं मित्रगुहांशकेषु ।२२।।

22. EFFECTS IN NAVAMSA: If a planet is in its exaltation navamsa, the good effects due to the planet will be full. If it is in its own Navamsa, it will give such results as applicable to its own Rasi placement. If it is in Navainsas of its enemy or of fall, the (good) effects will be quite little while moderate results will come to pass if it is in a friendly Navamsa.

द्वायुच्वगी जन्तो धनिनं कर्त्यान्वितं सदा पुर्षम् । नगरारबरमाढच चमूर्पात च त्रयः प्रथित्तम् ।।२३।। अडिच नृपात्तकोति चत्वारो राजधर्मसंयुक्तम्। स्यातं नृपतीष्टतमं पञ्चानेकविधवृद्धकोशं च ।२४।। वड् प्रहाः स्वोच्वगाः कुर्युनू्पाति पुरुषं सदा। प्रदानमानसम्पनं बहुवाहनमण्डितम् ॥ २५।

स्वोच्चं याताः सर्वे समुद्रपर्यन्तमेदिनीनाथम् । जनयन्ति चक्र्वातिनमवनीशं जातकं चिन्त्यम् ॥२६।।

23-26. EFFECTS OF MORE PLANETS IN EXALTATION: If two planets are simultaneously exalted at birth, the native will be rich and famous. Three planets in simultaneous exaltation will make one head a town, wealthy and an army chief. If four are so placed that they are in exaltation, one will be rich, be honoured by the king and will be of regal disposition. Should five heavenly bodies occupy their exaltatlon signs the native will be famous, be a king's patron

and be very rich. When six planets are posited in their exaltation signs, the person will be a king, be charitable, honourable and endowed with many conveyances. When all are in their respective exaltation signs, the native will be an emperor ruling over the land surrounded by seas.

ह्वाभ्यां त्रिकोणसंस्थाभ्यां कुटुम्बी कुलवर्धनः । श्रेठ्ठः प्रस्यातकीतिश्च ग्रहाभ्यां भूवि जायते ।।२७।। महाधनस्त्रिभिश्चैव गणग्रामाधिनायकः । आढ्यो नृपाप्तसत्कारश्चतुरुमर्लोकसंमतः । २८।। आरक्षकः प्रधानः सेनापुरनगरभूपकोशानाम् । पञ्चग्रहैशित्रकोणे भवति कुटुम्बी सुबहुसौर्यः ।।२६।।” विद्यादानधनौघः समन्वितो भवति षड्भिरेव पुमान् । राज्यं प्रशास्ति नियतं गोपालकुलेऽपि संजातः ।।३०।। स्वत्रिकोणगतेः बसुस्त्रीवलसम्पन्नो सर्वर्वेजजातो म होपतिः । विद्याशास्त्रविशारदः ।।३१।।

27-31. EFFECTS OF MORE PLANETS IN MOOLA- TRIKONA SIGNS: If two planets be so placed as to be in Moolatrikona signs, the native will be a householder contributing to the growth of his race, be pre-eminent, and famous. When three planets are similarly posited one will be quite wealthy, and will head a group of men or villages. If four planets occupy their Moolatrikona Rasis, the native will be very wealthy, will receive royal honours and be dear to all. Should five be so placed as they are in Moolatrikonas one will be a protector, be principal among men, will head an army, town and royal treasury, be a householder and be very happy. With six planets disposed in their Moolatrikona Rasis, though one may belong to a cowherd’s race, he will be endowed with learning, charitability and various kinds of wealth, will acquire a kingdom and be famous. If seven are in their Moolatrikona Rasts, one will become a king, be endowed with wealth, wife, strength, learning and be skilful in sastras.

ट्रो स्वगृहस्यौ कुर्तः कुलाधिक बन्धुपूजितं धन्यम्। बंशकरमर्यसहितं र स्वानयशोभिस्त्रयो विह्याः ॥ ३२।। ह्यातं विशिष्टखेष्टं श्रेणीपुरनगरपं च चत्वारः । पञ्चावनीश्वरसमं प्रमृतगोभूमियुवतिसम्पन्नम् ।३३।। बड्भिः प्रवृद्धशब्दं द्यृतिकोशस्वजनवाजिमानाढचः । भवति नृपवंशजातो नियतं पृथिवीपतिः स्वक्षे ॥।३४।। राजाधिनृषं स्वर्षे जनयन्ति जितारिपक्षमिह सप्त ।३४३।

32-341/2. EFFECTS OF MORE PLANETS IN OWN HOUSES: If two planets are in their own houses at birth, one will be importan: in his race. be honoured by his relatives and be fortunate. Three planets occupying own houses indicate growth of the dynasty (i.e. birth of children), acquisition of wealth, position and fame. If four planets occupy their own houses one will be famous, will perform significant acts and will lead over men and cities. If five planets be placed in their own houses, the native will be equal to a king, be endowed with abundant quadrupeds, lands and females. If six planets are in their own houses, one will acquire famous tilles, abundant wealth, relatives and honours, and one born in a royal family, will become a ruler. If seven planets be so placed one will be a king of kings and will conquer his enemies.

मित्राश्रयं सुवृत्तं दौ मित्रगृहेसमाश्रिती कुर्तः ।।३५। बान्धवसुहृदुपकर्ता त्रिभिविशिष्टो भवेद्गुर्णः स्यातः। श्वाह्मणदेवाराधनपरश्चतुमिर्घुरन्धरः स्यातः ।।३६।।

राजोपसेवकः स्यात्पञ्चभिराढचो नरेश्वरः कर्ता। विस्तीर्णभोगवाहनवसुमान्वड् भिर्नरेन्तुल्यः स्यात् ।। ३७।।

सर्वेनित्र्ष तै बहुवाह्नमृत्यसाधनो राजा ।३७]।

35-371/2. EFFECTS OF MORE PLANETS IN FRIENDLY HOUSES: If two planets occupy their friendly sign, the native will be endowed with the company of friends and will have good conduct. If three are so, one will patronise his relatives, be cndowed with good qualities and be pre-eminent. If four are so, one will honour Brahmins and gods, be skilful and be famous. If five are so, one will serve the king, be wealthy and be chief of men. When six are so, one will have wide enjoyments, abundant conveyances and wealth and be equal to a king. With seven planets in such placement one will have many servants, many conveyances, paraphernalia and be a king.

द्वाम्यां नीचे \_\_\_ न नोचश्चन्ताबह्वाग्रहसमेतः । ३८।। मूर्वोऽत्रम रतोऽस्वस्त्र भिर्गहैनोंचगर्नरः आलस्वनष्टचेब्टश्चतुभिरहि प्रेष्यः । नीचर्च् तकः । ३६।।

अमृहः प्रभिन्नदारः पञ्चभिरिह कथ्यते नरो दासः। घातभयश्रमतप्तः षड्णिर्नोची भवेतक्षामः ।४०।।

भिक्षुस्त्यक्ताशितमुर्भवति पुमान्वगतसर्वस्वः ।

नीचेः सप्तभिरखिलेदिक्चिरविघृताम्बरः सूतः ।४१।।

38-41. EFFECTS OF MORE PLANETS IN FALL: If two plancts occupy their signs of fall, one will be mean in thoughts and be very irascible. Three being so, he will be foolish, not virtuous, be poor and be a servant. Four being so, he will be indolent, be devoid of useful decds and be a servant. Five being so, he will be bereft of a house and a female and be a servant. Six being so, he will be tormented by fear and labour and be emaciated. All being so, he will eke out his food by begging, be devoid of everything and will wear torn robes or will wander without clothes.

ह्वावरिभवनसमेतो क्लेशवतां नित्यविप्रहर्चीनाम् ।

अतिपरिभूतानामपि नूर्णा जन्मप्रदौ कथितौ ।४२।

विविधव्ययदुःखमुजां त्रयः श्रमोत्पन्ननष्टवित्तानाम्। चत्वार इृष्टयोषिपुत्र्यविनाशजाधितप्तानाम् ॥४३।। पञ्चारियगृहे विह्गा इष्टव्यसनाभिधाततप्तानाम् । षड्रोगांकितवपुषां दुःखवत्तां चैव जन्मकराः ।४४।। सप्तारिमे प्रहेन्द्रा बीमत्सकुले प्रसूतानाम्। शय्याच्छादनभोजनवञ्चतकानां भवन्ति सदा ।४५।।

42-45. EFFECTS OF MORE PLANETS IN INIMICAL CAMPS: If there are two planets that are placed in inimical signs one will be miserable and ever fond of quarrels and be subjected to humiliation. With three such planets he will be tormented by many expenses and miseries and will lose wealth inspite of hard labour. With four being so placed, he will be at a loss in regard to his near and dear and wealth. Five heavenly bodies in such a state indicate that the native will be troubled by many evils. With six planets in inimical camps, he will be very miserable. If seven planets are in their inimical signs, the native belongs to a loathsome family, and will be devoid of food, robes, beds etc.

इति कस्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां उच्चादिचिन्तनं नाम पञ्चचत्वारिशोऽध्यायः ।।४५।।

Thus ends the 45th Chapter entitled “EFFECTS OF PLANETARY DIGNITIES” in Kalyana Varma's Saravall.

## अथ स्त्रीजातकाध्यायः ।।४६।। Chapter 46

## Female Horoscopy

स्त्रोणां जन्मफलं तुल्यं पुंभिः सार्ध वढुख्यते। विशेषस्तत्र यो वृष्टः कथ्यते विस्तरेण सः ।।१।।

1. Whatever horoscopic effects have been stated for males are equally applicable to females too. Explained in this chapter are rules peculiar to the fair sex.

वैधव्यं निवने विन्म्यं शरीरं जन्मलग्नतः । सप्तमे पतिसौभाग्यं पञ्चमे प्रसवस्तथा ।।२।।

2. IMPORTANT HOUSES: From the 8th house, her widowhood can be deduced while the ascendant deals with her physique, the 7th house with her husband’s good fortunes and the 5th house with her offspring.

प्रक्तिस्था लग्नेन्हो: समभे सच्छवीलरूपाइचा। भवणपुर्णयपेता शुभवीक्षितयोश्व युवतिः स्यात् ।३। पुर्षाकृतिशीलयुता दुःशीला वुःखिता विचमराशौ। कूर्वीक्षितयुतयोः पापा स्त्री स्याव्युणहीना 11४।

3-4. THE MOON AND ASCENDANT: If the Moon and the ascendant are both disposed in even signs, the female will be virtuous and extremely goodlooking. Should these two be in aspect to auspicious planets, she will be endowed with ornaments and virtues. In case these two occupy odd signs, she will be akin to a male in appearance and in disposition. With these two receiving aspects of malefics,

she will be sinful and bereft of virtues.

लग्नेन्द्वोर्यो बलवांस्त्रशांशेऽधिष्ठितैः फलं क्रमशः । भूसुतभार्गववोधनसुरगुर्मार्तण्डदेह्मर्षः

5. TRIMSAMSA POSITIONS: Note the stronger of the Moon and the ascendant. Whoever of the two is placed in the Trimsamsa of Mars, Venus, Mercury, Jupiter or Saturn, such effects will come to pass, (as detailed below).

क्ष यदवारगृहे दुष्टा भौमात्रशांशके भवेत् । कुचरित्रा तथा शौत्रे समाया बोधनेऽबला ।६।। क्लवे साध्व्यकजे दासी ज्रक्षे कौजे तु कापटी। शौश्रे प्रकीर्णकामा च बौधे गुणवती भवेत् ॥७।। र्बे सती शनो क्लीबा दुष्टा कौजे सितर्षगे। शौत्रे ख्यातगुणा बौधे कलासु निपुणा मता ।८।। जबे गुणान्विता मन्दे पुनर्मूश्चन्द्रभे ततः । स्वच्छन्दा कथिता कौजे शौक्ले च कुलपांसना ।।।  
- श्रौधे शिल्पान्विता नारी जैवे बहुगुणा शनेः। पतिघ्नी चार्कचे कौजे वाचाला भार्गवे सती ।१०। बौधे पुंश्वेष्टिता जैवे राज्ञी मन्दे कुलच्युता ।१०३।

6-101/2. EFFECTS OF THE MOON/ASCENDANT IN VARIOUS TRIMSAMSAS: If it is a sign owned by Mars, the Trimsamsa of Mars denotes a harlot, that of Venus a female devold of virtuous history, that of Mercury one with cunning disposition, that of Jupiter a chaste lady and that of Saturn one in servitude. Should a sign of Mercury house the Moon/the ascendant in the trimsamsa of Mars, the female will be cunning, in the trimsamsa of Venus one with high degree of lust, in the trimsamsa of Mercury a chaste girl, in the trimsamsa of Juplter a virtuous housewife and in that of Saturn impotent. If it is a sign of Venus, the

trimsamsa of Mars a female with wicked nature , Venusian trimsamsa one with fame for her virtues, Mercurian trimsamsa skilful, Jupiterian trimsamsa a virtuous female and Saturn's trimsamsa a woman given in marrlage as a second wife. In the sign owned by the Moon the trimsamsa of Mars bestows her freedom not due to a housewife, Venusian trimsamsa will make her unchaste, Mercurian trimsamsa will bestow skill in arts, Juplterian trimsamsa will make her quite virtuous and in Saturn's trimsamsa she will betray her husband. In Leo the trimsamsa of Mars will make the female garrulous, Venusian trimsamsa chaste, Mercurian trimsamsa male-natured, Jupiterian trimsamsa a queen and Saturn’s trimsamsa fallen.

कौजे बहुगुणार्यले शौक्रे वाप्व्यसनी तक्षा ।११।। • बौधे विज्ञानसंवृक्ता जैवे नैकगुणा स्मृता। मन्दे चाल्परतिः प्रोक्ता दासी कौजे तयाकिमे ।।१२।। सुप्रज्ञा च भवेच्छौत्रे बुधे दुःस्या खला तया ।

अबे पतिव्रता नित्यं मन्दे नीचानुसेविनी ॥१३।।

11-13. TRIMSAMSA EFFECTS (continued): In a sign of Jupiter the trimsamsa of Mars will make her a gentlewoman with many (of the seven principal) virtues, the trimsamsa of Venus a female with evil speech, the trimsamsa of Mercury one with knowledge, the trimsamsa of Jupiter one with many virtues and the trimsamsa of Saturn a female desiring limited physical union. Lastly in a sign owned by Saturn, a trimsamsa of Mars will make one servant, that of Venus intelligent, that of Mercury cruel and devold of conduct, that of Jupiter a chaste lady and that of Saturn himself one addicted to base men.

शुत्रासितौ यदि परस्परभागसंस्थौ

शंक्रे च दृष्टिपथगावदपे घटांशे।

स्त्रोणामतीव मदनाम्निमयप्रवृद्धिः

स्त्रोभिः शमं च पुरुवाकृतिभिर्लभन्ते ।१४।।

14. BEASTLY LUST OF A FEMALE: When Venus and Saturn are in mutual aspects in the case of a birth in Taurus or Libra ascendant with Saturn’s navamsa, the female concerned burnt with sexual lust will unite with a woman playing the role of a male. Venus and Saturn should simultancously be in exchange in Navamsas owned by them.

शून्येऽस्ते कापुरुषो बलहीनः सौम्यदर्शनविहीने। चरभे प्रवासशीलो भर्ता क्लीबो जमन्दयोश्व भ्रेत् ॥१५। उत्सृष्टा सूर्येऽस्ते कुजे च विधवा नवोढैव । बान्वेवा शुभदृष्टे शनेश्वरे वृद्धतां याति ।१६।। अशुभे क्षीणेऽस्तगते त्यद्ता पत्या भवेदशुभदृष्े। ऋरूरेविधवास्तगतेर्भवति पुनर्मूस्तथा मिर्षः । १७१।

15-17. THE 7th HOUSE: Should the 7th house be vacant, her husband will be a contemptible fellow. With the said house bereft of aspect of a benefic, he will be bereft of strength. He will be disposed to living in other places if the 7th house is a movable sign. He will be a eunuch with Mercury or Saturn in the 7th house. She will be discarded by her husband if the Sun is in her 7th house. Mars in the said house will cause early widowhood while Saturn in the 7th with malefic aspects will make her old soon. Again she will be given up by her husband with malefics being weak and combust in the 7th receiving aspects from malefics. Benefics and malefics combust in the 7th will give away the woman native in second marrlage.

## अन्योन्यमागगतयोः सितकुजयोरन्यपुरुषसष्त। स्यात् । खूने शिशिरकरे वा स्यायुवतिरनुज्ञया भर्तुः ।१८।।

18. UNCHASTITY: If Venus and Mars exchange their divisions, the female will go after other males. If the Moon be simultaneously in the 7th house, she will join others with consent of her husband.

सौरारगृहे तहच्छशिनि सशुक्रे विलग्नये जाता।

मात्रा साकं कुलटा ऋूरग्रहवीक्षिते भवति ।११।।

19. MOTHER AND DAUGHTER: If a sign of Mars or Saturn be ascending with Venus and the Moon in it in aspect to malefics, the female along with her mother may be deemed unchaste.

चूने तु कुजनवांशे शशिना वृष्टे सरोगयोनिः स्त्री। सद्भूगुभागे चारत्रोणी पतिवल्लभा भवति ।२०॥।

20. MISCELLANEOUS: If the 7th house falls in a navamsa of Mars and be in aspect to the Moon (Saturn, according to some texts), the female will have a discased vaglna. A Navamsa of Venus denotes a fortunate female who will be dear to her husband.

छूने वृद्धो मूर्वः सौरगृहे स्यान्नवांशके वाऽ्य।

स्त्रीलोलः क्रोधपरः कुजभेऽय नवांशके भर्ता।।२१।। शुक्र्यृहेऽथ नवांशेऽतिरूपसौभाग्यसंयुतो भर्ता । नपुणविज्ञानयुतस्तबैव बौधेऽयवा नवांशे वा ।२२।। मदनार्तो मृदुचित्तः शशिमेऽवनवांशके भर्ता। गुर्यृह्माणेऽप्यथवा गुणवान्विजितेन्द्रियो भवति ।।२३।।

अतिकर्मक दतितीक्षणो रविमेऽप्यचवांशके भवति भर्ता ।

सप्तमभवनोपेतेनिर्यं \_\_\_ स्त्रीणां \_\_\_ समवधार्यम् ।।२४।।

21-24. EFFECTS OF RULES OF 7th HOUSE/NA- VAMSA: If the 7th house or its Navamsa be ruled by Saturn, the female’s husband will be old and foolish. The rulership of Mars in such a case will give a husband who will be addicted to other women and be irascible. If Venus heads the 7th house or its Navamsa the husband will be very beautiful and be fortunate. The husband will be skilful and knowledgeable if Mercury be such a planet. A sexually

tormented husband who will, however, be soft in disposition will be bestowed by the Moon in such circumstances. The husband will be virtuous and be a conqueror of the five senses, if Jupiter rules the 7th house or its Navamsa. Leo as the 7th house or the 7th Navamsa will indicate an industrious and a very intelligent husband. Thus the effects relating to the rulership of the 7th house and 7th navamsa be declared in respect of females.

इर्व्यान्विता सुखपरा लग्ने सितबन्द्रयोर्दु द्रोश्व । सुखिता कलासु कुशला गुणशतसहिता विनोता स्यात् ।२५।। शुक्रबुधयोविलग्ने रुचिरा सुमगा कलासु निपुणा व । दास्यम्बरसौख्ययुता शुभेषु पापेवु विपरीता ।।२९्।।

25-26. EFFECTS OF PLANETS IN THE ASCENDANT: She will be jealously disposed and be happy with Venus and the Moon in the ascendant. Mercury replacing Venus in the said yoga will make the female happy, skilful in arts, virtuous and modest. If Mercury and Venus are together in the ascendant, the female will be beautiful, fortunate, and skilful in arts. Benefics in the ascendant will endow a female with servants, happiness and robes while malefics give adverse effects in regard to these.

## पापेऽपटमे तु विधवा निवनाधिपतिर्नवांशके यत्य ।

## तस्य दशायां मरणं वाच्यं तस्याः शुर्भहितीयस्थः ।।२७।।

27. EFFECTS OF THE 8th HOUSE: Malefics in the 8th will cause widowhood. This will occur in the dasa periods of the lord of the navamsa occupied by the 8th lord. A benefic in the 2nd will, however, cause the female's death in this yoga, instead of widowhood.

कन्यालिवृष्भसिहे शिशिरमयूख्लेऽल्पपुत्रा स्यात् ।

सुतभवने शुभ्युक्ते निरीक्षिते वा तर्थव स्यात् ।।२८। 28. LIMITED PROGENY: If the 5th house be one of

Virgo, Scorpio, Taurus and Leo receiving the aspect of a benefic, there will be limited number of children. Alternatively, the said 5th house may be conjunct a benefic or the Moon.

रियते बुधेन्नुभूगुजे रविजे च मध्ये

शेर्षैरबलेन सहर्तविषमरक्षलाने ।

जाता भवेत्पुरुविणी युवतो सर्देव

पुंश्चेष्टिता विचरति प्रथिता च लोके ।।२ह।।

29. FEMALE WITH MASCULINE DISPOSITION: Should Mercury, Venus and the Moon be in the ascendant and be weak while Saturn has moderate strength as the rest are strong, the female concerned will be akin to a male in appearance and in acts. The ascendant should in this case necessarily be an odd sign.

ऋरूरे जामित्रगते नक्षमे यदि खेचरो भवति नूनम्। आप्नोति प्रव्रज्यां पापग्रहसम्भवामबला ।।३०।।

30. TAKING OF RELIGIOUS ORDER: A female will be initiated into a religlous order if a malefic is in the 7th house while the 9th house is occupied by any planet. The order will relate to the planet in the 7th house.

बलिभिर्बुधगुर्शुर्र: शशांकसहितेविलम्नर्गः सममे ।

स्त्री ब्रह्मावादिनी स्यादनेकशास्त्रार्थकुशला च ।।३१।।

31. VEDIC EXPONENT: If strong Mercury, Jupiter, Venus or the Moon be in the ascendant identical with an even sign, the female will be a teacher/exponent of Vedas (or be a follower of Vedanta Philosophy). And she will be an expert in interpretation of various Sastras.

## जन्मकाले विवाहे च चिन्तायां बरणे तथा।

चिन्त्यं स्त्रीणां तु यत्त्रोषतं घटते तत्पतिष्ववि ।।३२।।

32. APPLICATION: Whatever has been stated in this chapter should be wisely applied to females relating to their births, selection of grooms, marriage and query. These effects are applicable to their husbands also.

## इति कल्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां स्त्रीजातकफलो नाम बट्चत्वारिशोऽध्यायः ।।४६।

Thus ends the 46th Chapter entitled “FEMALE HO- ROSCOPY” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## अथ निर्याणफलाध्यायः ॥४७॥

## Chapter 47

## Aphorisms of Death

शिक्षिजलशस्त्रज्वरजस्त्वामयतृट् क्षुरक्तो भवेन्मृत्युः । सूर्यादिभिनिधन्यः परदेशे पथि स्वके चराद्यश्च ।१।।

1. REASONS OF DEATH: If the 8th house is occupied by any planet from the Sun to Saturn, the reason for death in the same order will be fire, water, weapon, fever, indigestion (or constipation), thirst and hunger. According to the 8th house being a movable, fixed or dual sign, the place of death will be a foreign place, own place or on the way.

Notes: Given in this sloka are the possible reasons for death by virtue of individual placement of planets in the 8th house. Apart from these, each planet posited in the 8th house or related to the 8th house can cause one's exit by a disease or an event to be caused by the nature of the planet. See the following:

The Sun: Epilepsy, heart disease, skin disease, burning of body, serpents etc. The Moon: Diarrhoea, carbuncle, typhoid, jaundice, mental aberration, blood disease, drowning and indigestion. Mars: Excessive thirst, leprosy, eye diseasc, physical deformity etc. Mercury: Mental disorder, disease of throat, nose, skin etc., anaemia, fire, accidents etc. Jupiter: Intestinal disorder, liver/spleen diseases, car troubles etc. Venus: Venereal diseases, urinary diseases, disorders of generative organs, rickets etc. Saturn: Rheumatic pains, paralysis, hit by wood etc., fire, ghosts and the like.

Each sign as the 8th house will give peculiar informa-

tion related to death. See the following:

Aries as the 8th house: Death will be due to poison, indigestion, bilious disorder, fire, accident, diseases of heart/liver, gastric disorders. Taurus as the 8th house: vitiation of bile, phlegm and wind, fire, weapons, headache, disorders caused by heat, abdominal disease, breathing disorders, gout/colic, hit by animals etc., will cause death. Gemini as the 8th house: Disorders of thc head, asthma, colic, cough, catarrh etc., will promote death. Cancer as the 8th house: Insanity, loss of appctite, drowning, indigestion, windy diseases and the like will cause death. Leo as the 8th house: Poison, boils, fever, weapons, smallpox, wild beasts, heart troubles etc., will cause one's exit. Virgo as the 8th house: Indigestion, disease of vagina, penis or anus fall from height, quarrels, water and fire will cause death. Libra as the 8th house: Mental diseases, delirium, grief fear and troubles from animals will cause one’s death. Scorpio as the 8th house: Stones/weapons, dysentery, jaundice, spleenary disorders, veneral diseases, imprisonment, bite by snake, scorpion ctc., will cause death. Sagittarius as the 8th house: Attack by wild animals, arrows and other weapons, eating poison or poisonous stuff, quarrels, fall of a tree or from height, severe rheumatism, stomachial disorders etc., will stand to cause one’s final exit. Capricorn as the 8th house: Animal like tiger, gout pains, tastelessness, corpulcnce, snakes, stomach pains, delirium etc, will indicate death. Aquarlus as the 8th house: Lung/cardiac disorders, wild animals, fever, snake bite, etc., will cause death. Females will also be a cause of one’s end in this case. Pisces as the 8th house: Fever, dysentery, thunder, being caught in darkness, bite by snakes etc., ascitis, ear disorders etc. will cause one’s death. These indications are made by Hora Ratna, Poorva Parasareeyam, Phala Deepika and Jataka Parijatha in the context of evaluation of death.

It implies that these causes will come to pass if the 8th house falls in such vargas as above or the 8th lord placed in such houses. It may also be noted that if the occupant of the 8th be an indicator of the relative diseases/event, the

effects are certain. For example, let us take Aquarius becoming the 8th house, i.e., Cancer ascending on the east. If the Sun be there in Aquarius, heart disease will be a sure cause of death as the Sun is Karaka for heart diseases and the sign Aquarius itself is called 'Hridroga'(heart disease). These inferences will have to be made accordingly.

Death and Planets in the 8th house: Each planet in the 8th house has its own source of death for the native. If the Sun is in the 8th in aspect to malefics, death will be in miserable circumstances. If weak Moon occupies the 8th house, death will be due to fever, leprosy, fire, physical emaciation and the native will die in pitlable situation. Mars in the 8th house will indicate exit through enemies, weapons, leprosy, piles and dysentery. If Mercury be in the 8th house, death will be in a shrine and be calm. If Mercury is a malefic (by conjunction), gout, facial diseases and stomachial disorders will cause the end. With Jupiter in the 8th house in a benefic’s sign, death will be known to the native beforehand and the same will be in a religious place and if the sign be owned by a malefic, the end then will be very grievous and miserable. Venus in the 8th house will clear one of his liabilities to his father and the death will be peaceful. Saturn in the 8th will cause death in distant lands, in wicked association and due to many diseases and abundant grief. (Authority: Garga)

Sage Kasyapa minutely treats the planets in the 8th house in the context of death, with reference to Navamsas etc. The reader may find the following information more interesting.

The Sun in the 8th: If exalted, the native will give up his life by entering pyre with piety. Exalted Navamsainsulted by others. Benefic shadvargas-accidents. Debilitation sign-severe fire. Debilitation Navamsa-arrogant acts. Malefic shadvargas-burning of body. Friendly sign-consumption of poison. Friendly Navamsa-imprisonment. Vargothama Navamsa-articles made of steel. Inimical signdefects of the blood. Enemy’s Navamsa-Tuberculosis, pulmonary disorders etc. Own sign due to females.

The Moon in the 8th: If exalted-drowning. Taurus Navamsa-attack by elephants. Benefics' shadvargas-lightning. Debilitation sign-in the hands of a woman. Debilitation Amsa-billous/phlegmatic diseases. Friendly Rasiabdominal disorders. Friendly Navamsa-diseases of the anus. Vargothama Navamsa-attack by quadrupeds. Own sign-consumption.

Mars in the 8th house: If exalted-in a battle/quarrel, Exaltation Navamsa-hit by quadrupeds, Shadvargas of benefics-out of his own hands. Debilitation sign-with his own weapon. Debilitation Navamsa-before a Brahmana. Malefic shadvargas-hit by stones (and the like). Friendly sign-wood. Friendly Navamsa-fall in a well. Vargothamafall from a hill (or from height). Enemy's sign-diseases of private parts. Enemy's Navamsa-consumption of poison. Own sign-due to thieves.

Mercury in the 8th house: If exalted-fever. Exalted navamsa-phlegmatic disorders. Benefic shadvargas-windy diseases. Debilitation sign-wounds. Dcbilitation Navamsaexcessive fear. Malefic shadvargas-duc to separation from kith and kin. Friendly sign-facial diseases. Friendly Navamsa-eye discases. Vargothama Navamsa-disease of the rectum. Enemy's sign-in jall. Enemy's navamsa-stomachial disorders. Own sign-wounds in legs.

Jupiter in the 8th house: If exalted due to various diseases. Exalted Navamsa-fatly grown body. Benefic shadvargas-disorders of the ears. Debilitation sign-due to his own relatives. Debilitation Navamsa-cholera. Malefic Shadvargas-dysentery. Friendly Navamsa-discases of the blood. Vargothama-due to camels (due to quadrupeds in general). Enemy’s sign-due to his own horses (i.e. during accidents caused by conveyances). Enemy’s Navamsa-due to excessive anger. Own sign-limitless eating.

Venus in the 8th house: If exalted-thirst. Exaltation Navamsa-faclal diseases. Benefic Shadvargas-dental disorders. Debilitation sign-imbalance of the three humours. Debilitation Navamsa-cholera. Malefic shadvargas-forest animals. Friendly sign-snakes. Friendly Navamsa-con-

sumption of polson. Vargothama Navamsa-spider (and the like). Enemy's sign-worms bred in poison. Enemy's navamsa-due to anger caused at the time of sexual cohabitatlon. Own sign-with great grief.

Saturn in the 8th house: If exalted-hunger. Exaltation Navamsa-leaping. Benefic shadvargas-while sitting. Debilitation sign-due to his own relatives. Debilitation Navamsain the hands of enemies. Malefic shadvargas-the disease consumption. Friendly sign-excessive itch. Friendly Navamsa-in the hands of an urchin. Vargothama-wounds. Enemy’s sign-crushed by horses. Enemy’s navamsathrough elephants. Own sign-due to asses. (all these may indicate quadrupeds in general).

यो बलयुक्तो निधनं पश्यति तद्धातुकोपजा मृत्युः। तत्संयुक्तस्तनुजो बहुभिर्वलिभिर्व्हुप्रकार: स्यात् ।२।

2. Death may be due to the imbalance of that humour belonging to the planet aspecting the 8th house. If there is no planet in the 8th house, the aspecting one may indicate the kind of death. If many are thus related, the strongest one will prevail.

सूर्यांगारकयोः खबन्धुगतयोः शेलाप्रपातोद्भवो

मृत्पुर्भूतनयेन्दुभानुतनर्यः कूपे खसप्त।म्बुरगः। पापालोकितयोहिमोष्णकरयोः कन्यास्थयोर्बन्धतो

लग्ने सूर्यशशांकयोस्तिमियुगे तोये तदा मज्जतः ॥।३।।

3. DEATH BY DROWNING, FALL ETC.: If the 10th and 4th are occupied by the Sun and Mars, the native will die due to fall from hills. If the 10th, 7th and 4th houses are occupied by Mars, Mercury and Saturn, it may be due to fall in a well. Death due to imprisonment will follow if the luminarles are placed in Virgo in aspect to malefics. The end will be by drowning if Pisces is occupied by the Sun and the Moon.

काकिणि मन्दे मकरे चन्द्रे मृत्युर्वुरोदरक्तः स्यात्। पापान्तःस्वे चन्दरे कुजभवने शस्त्रवह्लिभवः ।४। कन्यायां पद्मिनीशत्रुः पापमध्यगतः सदा । रक्तोत्यशोषजं मृत्युं करोति प्रुवमेव हि।५।।

4-5. DEATH BY FIRE ETC.: If the Moon and Saturn respectively are in Capricorn and Cancer, death will be due to affliction of stomach. If the Moon be hemmed between malefics and occupies a sign of Mars, fire/weapons will cause death. Death will be certainly inflicted by dryness of blood if the Moon is in Virgo hemmed between malefics.

सौरक्ष शुभयोर्मध्ये शशी रज्वग्निपातजम्। कुर्यास्मृत्यु न सन्देहश्वाणव्यवचनं तथा ।।६।। नवमसुतयोरशुभयोः पापग्रहदृष्टयोर्भंवेन्मृत्युः ।

द्रेक्काणैः पाशमुजगनिग्डशिछ्रेऽ्यवा गुप्याम् ।।७।।

6-7. DEATH BY HANGING ETC.: If the Moon in Capricorn or Aquarius be hemmed between benefics, death will be due to hanging, fire or fall which is doubtless according to Chanakya. The same effect will come to pass if the 5th and 9th houses are occupied by malefics in aspect to malefics. If the drekkana of the 8th house be one of Pasa, Sarpa and Nigala, death will be reticent.

Notes: If the 22nd decanate when counted from the ascendant be one of Sarpa (snake), Pasa (noose) and Nigala (fetters) death will be in a secret manner, i.e. by suicide etc. There are varous views among the classical authors about the designations given to decanates. The reader is, however, referred to Phala Deepika, ch. 3 for details.

मीनोदये दिनकरे चन्द्रे पापान्वितेऽस्तगे मेवे। स्त्रीहेतुकं हि मरणं स्वमन्दिरे स्याइदन्त्येके IIE।।

8. DEATH DUE TO A FEMALE: If thc Sun be in Pisces ascendant while the Moon is in the 7th (in Virgo) or in the 2nd in Aries or in the 5th in Cancer, a female will be reason for the native’s end.

रुधिरे सुखेऽयवाके वियति यमे क्षोणचन्द्रसंयुक्ते।

## पापैसित्रिकोणलग्ने शूलप्रोतस्य निदिशेम्मरणम् ।।।

9. DEATH DUE TO A TRIDENT'S HIT: If the 4th house is occupied by Mars or the Sun while Saturn is conjunct weak Moon in the 10th house and malefics are in the ascendant and its trines, hit of a trident will cause the end.

Notes: The weak Moon referred to in the sloka be interpreted as the Moon bereft of other strengths than Paksha Bala. For she will be endowed with Paksha Bala (or strength by virtue of waxing) as she is required to be in the 10th as the Sun is in the 4th.

हिबुकेऽके वियति कुजे क्षीणेन्दुयुतेऽ्जेन संदृष्टे।

## काष्ठेनाभिहतः सन् स्रियते जातो न सन्देहः ।।१०।।

10. DEATH BY HIT OF WOOD: If the Sun is in the 4th while the Moon devoid of strength is in the 10th along with Mars or in aspect to Saturn, the native will be hit by a log causing death.

क्षोणेन्दुभौमर विचन्द्रजसूर्यपुत्रेः

छिद्रास्पदोदयसुर्खलंगुडाहतस्य ।

मृत्युर्वियन्तबमलग्नस्तस्थितस्ते-

र्घूमाग्निबन्धनशरीरनिकुट्टनः स्यात् ॥११।

11. DEATH BY HIT OF A CLUB etc.: If weak Moon, Mars, Sun and Mercury along with Saturn are in the 10th, 8th, 4th and ascendant (in any way), the subject will have his end due to a club beat. If these planets fill the 10th, 9th, 5th and ascendant, death will be by smoke, fire, confinement or a blow of fist.

हिबुकास्तकर्मसहितेः कुजभानुशरनेश्वर्भवति मृत्युः । आयुधहतमुग्भूपतिकोपप्रभवः सदा पुंसाम् ।।१२।

12. DEATH DUE TOROYAL WRATH: If the 4th, 7th and 10th houses are occupied by Mars, the Sun and Saturn, death will be due to weapon, fire or royal wrath.

कर्माम्बुवित्तसंस्थः कुजेन्दुमन्देः क्षतः क्रिमिङकृतोऽन्तः ।

खस्थेऽकेन्दुकुजे वा सुराप्रपानप्रपातक्तः ।।१३। सप्तमभवने भौमे क्षीणेन्दु दिवाकराकिभिर्लग्ने । मरण - जातस्य बदेद्यन्त्रोत्पीडनभवमवश्यम् ।।१४।।

13-14. DEATH DUE TO INSECTS, INTOXICANTS AND MACHINERY: One’s end will be due to insects if the 10th, 4th and 2nd houses at his birth are occupied by Mars, the Moon and Saturn. If the 10th house is constellated by the luminaries and Mars, the native will end his life on account of intoxicants or fall from height. Machinery will be the source of one’s death if the 7th house is occupled by Mars as the trio of weak Moon, Saturn and the Sun afflict ascendant by occupation.

## तुलायां रधिरे याते कुजक्षे मास्करे स्थिते।

चन्द्रे मन्दगृहं प्राप्ते विष्मध्ये मरणं भवेत् ।।१५।। गलितेन्हर्कमूपुर्वर्गतव्योमाव्टबन्धुषु

बिष्मध्ये तु भवेग्मृत्युः सिद्धसेनः प्रभाषते ।१६।।

15-16. DEATH IN FILTHY SURROUNDINGS: One will die amidst ordure if Mars is in Libra, the Sun in Aries/ Scorpio and the Moon in Capricorn/Aquarius. The Sun, weak Moon and Mars in the 8th, 10th and 4th houses will kill one amidst excrement. So says Siddhasena.

बलिना कुजेन दृष्टे क्षीणेन्दौ रन्धागेर्कजे मृत्युः। गुल्ममहावेदनया क्रिमिदाहायुधङतो भवति ।१७।।

## रबौ सद्धिरे स्ने निधने रविसंभवे। रसातलस्थे हिमगो मृत्युः पक्षिकृतो भवेत् ।।१८।।

17-18. DEATH BY THIRST, WEAPONS AND BIRDS: II the 8th house contains both the weak Moon and Saturn in aspect to strong Mars, the object will die due to spleenary disorders, collcs, thirst or weapons. The Sun and Mars in the 7th while Saturn occupies the 8th and the Moon the 4th, one’s death will be caused by birds.

रव्याराकिनिशाकरः । लग्नच्छद्र त्रिकोणेषु मृरत्रुः : स्याच्डलपातेन शास्त्रकुडचादिपातजः ।१६।।

19. If the ascendant, 8th, 5th and 9th houses are occupied by the Sun, Mars, Saturn and the Moon, death will be due to fall from a hill, hit of a weapon or fall of a wall, etc..

Notes: From slokas 3 through 19, the author has given various reasons for one’s death. For more relevant informations, the reader may refer to Horasara, Hora Ratna etc.

## उद्यनबांशाधिपतेः समानभूमौ वदन्ति यवनेन्द्राः ।

ग्रह्योगेक्षणकार्यः परिकल्प्यं चान्यदपि तज्जेः ।।२०।

20. Yavanas indicate that the place of death will correspond to the Navamsa Lagna lord. The aphorisms stated in this chapter should be suitably interpreted according to other aspects and other conjunctions.

उदितांशसमो मोहः श्वेशेन निरीक्षिते द्विगुणितः स्यात् ।

त्रिगुणः शुर्चश्च दृष्टे समस्तमुनयो व्यवस्यन्ति ।२१॥।

21. The duration of the state of loss of consciousness (or insensibility, delusion, pain etc.) will correspond to the portion elapsed in the radical ascendant. The duration will

remain doubled and trebled according to the ascendant lord or a benefic aspecting the ascendant.

उदयाद्विशतिमद्रेव्काणो भवति कारणं मृत्योः। तत्याधिपतिभवो वा निर्याणं सूचयेत्स्वगुर्णः ।।२२॥1

22. KHARA DREKKANA: The 22nd decanate from the ascendant will denote thc nature of one's death. For example, by the nature of its lord, (i.e. the humour, place etc.) मेवाये द्रेयकाणे क्रूरग्रहवीक्षिते च संयुक्ते।

अम्ब्वहिविवपित्तक्तं मरणं नृणां समादेश्यम् ।२३।। विद्यादृद्धितीयभागे मरणं जलक्मिहिमार्यः । एवं तृतीयभागे तटाककूपप्रपाताद्वा ।। २४।।

23-24. EFFECTS OF ARIES DECANATES AS RELATED TO DEATH: If the first decanate of Aries be the 22nd decanate from the ascendant, and be in aspect to a malefic the native's death will be due to water (i.e. drowning etc.), snake, poison or bilious imbalances. If the 2nd decanate of Aries be so related, water, insects, icc and forests will cause death. The 3rd decanate of Aries denotes tanks, wells etc.

Notes: From sloka 23 through 45, special effects are given concerning the native's death with reference to the 22nd decanate (also called Khara drekkana, which is again the 64th Navamsa) falling in each one third part of various signs. These are applicable to the stronger among the ascendant and the Moon. Additional information can be procured in Jataka-bharana.

व्यभाश्वखरोष्ट्रेम्यो मृत्युर्गेया पित्तान्निवातचोरार्द्वितीयभाये

वृवस्याद्ये । वयस्यैव । २५।

विद्यात्तृतीयभागे यानासनवाजिपातङतम् । पुंसां भवति हि मरणं रणशिरसि महास्त्रक्तमेव ।२६।।

25-26. DECANATES IN TAURUS: If the first decanate

of Taurus be the 22nd decanate at birth, death will be caused by animals like ox, horse, donkey, camel etc. The second decanate in Taurus denotes the end due to bilious/ windy complaints, fire and thieves. The concluding decanate of Taurus leads to one’s death in journey or due to fall from the back of a horse or due to hit by weapon in battle.

आद्ये मिथुनः्यंशे कासश्वासोद्भवो भवति । मत्युर्महिषविषाद्याद्द्वितीयभागे च संनिपाताद्वा ।२७॥। वनवासिचतुश्चरणारपर्वतनागाद्गणात्तयारप्यात्

भवति हि मृत्युः पुंसामन्ते भागे तु जुतुमस्य ।।२८।।

27-28. DECANATES IN GEMINI: If the first decanate of Gemini be the 22nd decanate at birth, one’s end will be due to tuberculosis or pulmonary disorders. The second decanate in Gemini identical with 22nd decanate denotes death through a buffalo, or through poison or delirium. If the 3rd decanate in Gemini becomes the 22nd decanate the end will be through forest tribes, quadrupeds in general or elephants in particular.

प्राहेण मद्यपानात्कण्टकदोषेण वा तया स्वप्नात्। भवति हि कर्कटकाद्ये मृत्युन्णां तृतीयमागे तु।२८।। अभिधाताद्विषपानान्मध्ये व्यंशे भयं समादिष्टम् । श्वसनप्रमेह्गुल्मास्क्तन्द्रीदोषेण

00

29-30. DECANATES IN CANCER: If the 22nd decanate is the first decanate of Cancer, the reason of death will be a crocodile (or a shark), wine, thorn or dreams. The second decanate will cause end by beating or by consuming of poison. The terminating decanate indicates death by respiratory diseases, urinary diseases (viz. gleet, diabctes etc.), spleenary disorders, affliction to blood or by swooning caused by excessive labour.

सलिलविवपादरोगात्सहाद्ये ध्यंशके भवेत्पुंसाम् ।

मध्ये र तृतीयभागे जलामयक्तो बनोव्देशे ।।३१।

## विवशस्त्रयोगदोर्षरभिशापाद्वा तथा च पाताद्वा। अन्त्ये सिहद्यंशे भवति हि मृत्युर्न सन्देहः ॥३२।

31-32. DECANATES IN LEO: The 22nd decanate identical with the first decanate of Leo will cause one’s end due to water, poison or diseases of the foot. The second decanate of Leo indicates death by diseases caused by water or movements in forests. The last decanate thereof will bring one’s end through poison, weapons, calumny or fall from heights.

आद्ये कन्यात्रयंसे मस्तकरोगात्तयानिलान्मृत्युः। व्यालगिरिदुर्गवनजो मध्ये भूपात्मजाबथवा ।।३३।। करमवरशस्ततोयादतिखातात्त्रीकृतान्नपानाद्वा

अन्त्ये कन्या यंशे नृणां मृत्युः सदा वृष्टः ।।३४।।

33-34. DECANATES IN VIRGO: Should the death decanate be the first decanate in Virgo, one's life will end due to discases of the head or by wind, (gale, hurricane or outburst of violent wind etc.). The middle decanate in Virgo will cause death by snake-bites or in hills, forts, forests etc., or by the son of a king. The concluding decanate in Virgo will bring death by camels, asses, weapons, water, deep pits, females and unhealthy eating and drinking.

## आसे वणिक्त्रिमाये युवतिचतुष्पान्निपातदोषेण ।

## मध्ये तु जठररोगरन्त्ये व्यालाम्बुजातेभ्यः ।।३५।।

35. DECANATES IN LIBRA: When the 22nd decanate at birth is held by the first decanate of Libra, death will be through a female or quadrupeds or fall from heights. The middle decanate of Libra denotes end on account of stomachial diseases while last decanate indicates snakes or animals living in water as a source of death.

आद्योऽलिनस्त्रिभागे विषशस्त्रस्त्रीकृतान्नपानभवः । मध्ये तु वस्त्रभारस्रंसनरोगर्भवति मृत्युः ॥ ३६।। अन्त्ये तृतीयभागे लोष्टकपाषाणजनितवेदनया । भवति हि मरणं ह्याय वा नृणां जंघास्थिभंगङतम् ।।३७।।

36-37. DECANATES IN SCORPIO: The first decanate in Scorplo if designated as the 22nd decanate at birth, potson, weapons, females, (unhealthy) food and drinks will bring one's death. The second decanate in Scorpio indicates death through robes, weights, falls and discases. The last decanate of Scorpio will inflict death by being stoned or by breakage of shank bones.

चापस्थाद्ये द्यंशे गुवानिलसमुद्भर्वविविधरोगैः।

मध्ये विवगुरुदोर्यरनिलकतर्वा - भवेस्मृत्युः ।।३८।। अन्त्ये तृतीयमागे जलमध्ये तत्समुत्थितर्वाऽपि । मृत्युन् णां बृष्टो जठरामयदोषसंभूतः ।।३६।।

38-39. DECANATES IN SAGITTARIUS: The first decanate of Sagittarius being the 22nd decanate at birth, denotes that death will be due to various windy disorders or due to diseases of the anus. The second decanate will cause one’s end to poison, or faults of elders or windy diseases. The concluding decanate will inflict death in water or through abdominal disorders.

मकराद्ये ड्रेक्काणे ऊर्विनाशावयवा नृपहिसाव्याधकारणान्मृत्युः। जलचरसत्वादिवेकशफसर्पात् ॥४०।

बहनास्त्रतस्करेभ्यो

ज्वरादमानुषविभेदनान्मध्ये ।

अन्त्ये मकरत्यंशे स्त्रीभ्यो मृत्युः सदा दृष्टः ।।४१॥

40-41. DECANATES IN CAPRICORN: The opening decanate of Capricorn will bring death through royal torture or through tigers. The middle decanate will inflict death on account of damage to thighs or through crea-

tures/animals living in water, poison, snake, fire, weapons, robbers, fever, and living beings other than human beings. The last decanate will bring end through a female.

कुम्मे प्रयमत्रयंशे स्त्रीभ्यस्तोयस्तथा जठररोगः ।

| ज्ञेयो      |  | मृत्युन्णां                                                                 | पर्जतगहनद्विपादेर्वा ।४२।। |
|-------------|--|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|----------------------------|
| मध्ये अग्ये |  | स्त्रकृतदु खर्गुह्याजरोगेर्भवति मियुनचतु प्पदमुखरोगक्रत्मवेत्पुंसाम् ।।४३।। |                            |

42-43. DECANATES IN AGUARIUS: The first decanate in Aquarius will bring death through females, water, hills, elephants and stomachial disorders. The middle decanate denotes one's end through miseries caused by a female, venereal diseases etc., while the third decanate will cause death through copulation, faclal diseases and quadrupeds.

त्रयंशे मोनयु गाद्ये गुल्मग्रहणीत्रमेहपुवतीभ्यः । जंघाजठरज-रोगेर्गजग्रहहुतैः समादिशेन्मृत्युम् ॥४४।। नौभेदाज्जलमध्ये क्षे वृकाणद्वितीयजातानाम् । अन्त्ये भवति हि मरणं कुत्सितरोगेर्ने सन्देहः ॥४५।।

44-45. DECANATES IN PISCES: If the first decanate of Pisces be related to death, it will be due to diarrhoea, spleenary or urinary disorders affliction to stomach/thighs, females, elephants or planetary evils. If the second decanate be so related, one’s end of life will come through a tragedy in a boat and the third one denoles death due to dreaded diseases.

इति कल्याणवर्मविरवितायां सारावस्यां निर्याणफलं सप्तचत्वारिशोऽध्यायः ।।

Thus ends the 47th Chapter entitled “APHORISMS OF DEATH” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALl.

## अथ नब्टजातकाध्यायः ॥४८।। Chapter 48

## Lost Horoscopy”

जन्मविधावज्ञाते प्रश्नोत्थविकल्पतो भवेत्रष्टुः। जन्मसमयो नराणामतः प्रयत्नेन संचिन्त्यः ।।१।।

1. When the time of birth or time of conception is unknown, through horary method the lost birth details can be traced. • दशविधचिह्मज्जात्वा जातं पुर्षं प्रसाधयेल्लग्नम् । अत एव प्रथमतरं तानि समस्तान्यहं वक्ष्ये।।२।।

2. The effects of ten divisions (Dasa Vargas) will help to arrive at the natal ascendant. These ten divisions are explained infra, the first being the effects of ascendant.

मेषविलग्ने जातः प्रचण्डरोषो विदेशगमनरतः । लुब्धः कुशोऽल्पसौर्यः सेर्ष्यः स्वलिताभिवायी च ।।३।। वित्तानिलोष्णरोगरभितप्ततनुः मेषक्ष धर्मपरश्चलोऽल्पमेधाः क्रियापटुर्भीरुः । परानशकर: NI भोक्ता स्यातः कुनखौ सातृविहीनस्तथा पितृत्यव्तः । शीघागतिर्मन्दसुतो विविधार्थयुतः सुशोलश्च ।५। कुकुलोद्गतां विशीलां स्वजनेऽपि च निधुणां स्त्रयं लभते । अपक्क्टोद्यसोद्यः कुधर्मसंविधितार्थश्च ।६।।

3-6. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN ARIES ASCENDANT: One born in Arles ascendant will be highly susceptible to ire, be (\*) The reader may consult ‘NASHTA JATKAM an exclusive English translation of Mukundacharya's treatise on lost horoscopy.

interested in visiting foreign places, be miserly in disposition, emaciated, not happy, be jealous, stammering in speech, troubled by billous and windy diseases and complaints of heat, be skilful in performance, timid, religious, not very intelligent and will destroy others' wealth. He will be fickle-minded. He will enjoy pleasures, be famous, will have bad (ugly) nails, be bereft of brothers, be discarded by his father, will walk fast, will have inactive (or unlucky) children, be endowed with various kinds of wealth, and be virtuous. His wife will belong to a base family, be virtuous and be reproached even by her own people. The native will derive happiness and wealth in undesirable manners.

वृवभविलग्ने शूरः क्लेशसहिष्णुः सुखी रिपुनिहन्ता।

बाल्ये संवययुक्तः पृथुपीनललाटघोणगण्डोष्ठः ।।७।। उद्युक्तकर्मसुभगः : पितुर्जन्याः सुशर्मकुट्टाता। विवियव्योऽतिरौद्रः कफानिलात्मा पिताकुमारीणाम् ॥द।। स्वजनावमर्दनपरो धर्मनिवृत्तोऽबलात्रियश्वपतः । भोजन राननिगृध्नुर्तानाम्बर भूवर्णकमतिः

7-9. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN TAURUS ASCENDANT: One born in Taurus ascendant will destroy his enemies, will have the tendency of gathering wealth right from boyhood, will have broad and strong forehead, nose, cheeks and lips, be industrious, fortunate, dcvoted to parents, liberal, will have many expenses, be very short-tempered, :e phlegmatic and windy in humour, be the father of (many) daughters, will insult his own men, be devoid of religion, be interested in women, fickle minded, be fond of food and drinks and be endowed with various kinds of robes and ornaments.

## मिथुनविलग्ने जातः प्रियदारो भषणप्रदानरतिः।

पूज्यतमः सुवदस्वी हिमातृको रिपुविनीतः स्यात् ॥।१०। गान्धर्वशिल्पकुशलः श्रुतिशास्त्रार्थप्रहास्यकाव्यमतिः । सौम्योऽब मण्डनरुचिर्मदवश्यः स्यात्सतां सत्यः ।।११।

असहिष्णुरनिष्टसुतः शठोऽल्पबन्धुश्व संस्थितो भवति । हीनाधिकांगपादो विनीतवृत्ताक्षिपक्षमा च । १२। चण्डाकारो वश्यो दारुणरिपुपक्षसंहरणशीलः। सूरत्नकाञ्चनोर्मिकजलार्थमागी मवेत्पुरुषः ।।१ ३।।

10-13. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN GEMINI ASCENDANT: One born in Gemini ascendant will have a dear wife, be fond of ornaments, charitable, respectable, will have two mothers, will be favourable even to his enemies, be interested in music, fine arts, Vedas, Sastras, amusement, poetry etc., gentle, fond of beautification, be conceit, truthful, impatlent, will have evil sons, be crafty, will have a few relatives, will possess limbs of irregular sizes, will have round eyes, be fierce in appearance, will destroy even fearful enemies, and will be endowed with lands, jewels, gold etc.

ककिणि लग्ने भीरुनेकनिवासश्चलप्रज्ञः । मेधान्वितोऽतिघुर्यो गुद्धार्गार्तो निहन्ति रिपूरन् ॥१४।। अन्ताविषमः कामी द्विजदेवात्यर्चनप्रदानरतः । धर्मरतः कफबहुलो युबतितनुः संस्तुतो गुर्णनियतम् ॥१५।। कन्यानुजो न बन्धुर्ट्टाल्पसुतो विर्गाहितकुटुम्बः । बहुमतकुतिसितयुवतिः परार्थभागी दृढ़प्राही ।१६।। परदेशगः सुधीरः साहसकर्मा जलाधिगतवित्तः। स्त्रीभूषणाम्बरसुखलेभोगश्च समन्वितो भवति ।।१७।।

14-17. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN CANCER ASCENDANT: One born in Cancer ascendant will be timid, will have many residences, be fickle minded, learned, intelligent, enduring, troubled by discases of the anus, will destroy his enemies, be crooked in disposition, sensuous, will honour Brahmins and gods, be religious, very phlegmatic, will possess a physique akin to that of a female, be honoured for his virtues, will be preceded by sisters in birth, be bereft of relatives, will have a few children, will have a censured

family and a vile wife, will enjoy others' wealth, be firmly obstinate, will live in forelgn places, be valorous in his deeds, be courageous, will earn through water (i.e. products related to water, sea travel etc.) and be endowed with wife, ornaments, robes and happiness.

सिहोदये प्रस्तो मांसरुचिनु पतिर्लब्धमानधनः । धर्माच्च्युतोऽप्यसंस्यः कुटुम्वकार्येषु रतवामः ।।१८।। सिहस्य समानमुखः स्यतिमान्गाम्भीर्यसत्वसंयुवतः । धृष्टोऽल्पवचा सुब्धः परघातकरो बुभुक्षावान् ॥ १६। पर्वतबनानुसारी सुरोषणो बृढसुहृत्त्रमादो च । दुष्प्रसहो हतशत्रुः ध्यातसुतः प्रणतसाधुजनः ।२०। कृष्यादिकर्मधनवान् व्यापाररतो बहुव्ययो भवति। बेश्या-नटी-नियमनाद्भार्यातश्चातिदन्तरोगाच्व 1 11

18-21. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN LEO ASCENDANT: One born in this ascendant will be fond of eating meat, will earn wealth and honour through the king, be bereft of religion, be dutiful in family affairs, will have a face resembling that of a lion, will hold a high rank of office, be majestic, strong, bold, will speak sparingly, be a miser, will trouble others, be desirous of eating or worldly enjoyments, will wander in hills and forests, be irascible, be firm in friendship, be careless, inaccessible (or inviolable), will destroy his enemies, will have famous sons, will honour the virtuous, be rich through agriculture etc., interested in business and will spend heavily on prostitutes, dancers and his own wife and will suffer dental diseases.

बच्ठे साधुत्वयुतः शिक्षागान्धर्वकाव्यशिल्पपटुः। प्रियवल्गुकयाभाषी प्रणयी वानोपचाररतः ।२२।। कन्याविलासिसस्वस्थितिर्दयावान्परस्वभोक्ता च। भोक्ता देश्रमणः स्त्रीप्रकृतिविनयवाक् कितवः ।२३।।

ममण्डलवर्धनमार् सुभगः कामी यशोऽमलं लभते । ऋजुधर्मवान्सुरूपः सुरुचिः भक्तो गुरुणां च ।२४।। पापरहार्यवृतेः सहर्जश्व समं विरुद्धश्च ।

कन्याप्रजोऽनिलकफो नीचारिविर्वाजतकथश्च ।।२५।

22-25. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN VIRGO ASCENDANT: One born in Virgo ascendant will be gentle, skilful in training (or tcaching others), music, poetry and fine arts be a swect speaker, be affectionate, interested in charitability and serving others, will sport with virgins, be endowed with goodness, merciful, will enjoy others’ wealth, will wander, will have a female’s disposition, be modest, be a liar, will attempt to enlarge his landed properties, be fortunate, sensuous, famous, virtuous, beautiful, will honour elders, be sinful, inimical to brothers, will have (more) daughters, be phlegmatic and windy in humour and will be away from base and inimical people.

सप्तमलग्ने जातो विषमांगः शीलवजितश्चपलः । उपचितहीनद्रविणः सुखहुट्ेहानुसारी स्यात् ।।२६। कफवातिककलिरुचिको दीर्घमुखशरीरधर्ममतिवेत्ता । बहुदुःखभावसुमेधाः परावगर्दी सुचार्कृष्णाक्षः ।२७।। अतिथिह्विजदेवरतिः ऋतुत्रियावान्गुरुषु भवतः । पूज्यः पितान्यभाजां जातः सत्यश्च मृदुशुक्लः ।२८।। भ्रातृप्रियोर्र्यमुख्यः शुचिश्च पापोपचारबन्धुश्च ।

दाता कुत्सितवृत्तो धर्मव्यवसायनीचमतिः ॥२६।।

26-29. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN LIBRA ASCENDANT: One born in Libra ascendant will have a crooked physique, be bereft of virtues, be fickleminded, will have increasing and decreasing wealth, be bereft of physical happiness, be phlegmatic and windy in humour, be fond of promoting quarrels, will have a long face and a long physique, be virtuous, learned, very miserable, very intelligent, will in-

jure others’ honour, will possess beautiful and black eyes, will honour guests, gods and Brahmins, will perform Yagnas etc., will respect preceptors (or elders), be respectable, will hclp the helpless, be truthful, will have a beautiful body, will be affectionate to brothers, be pre-eminent, chaste, will yet help sinners, will have mcan profession, be charitable, be religious and base minded.

बृश्विकलम्ने पुर्षः पीनपृयुध्यायतांगतीक्णश्च ।

अन्तविषमः शूरो मातुरभीष्टो रणोद्यतस्यागी ।३०।। गम्भीरापिगलोद्धतद्क्सुमहाहुन्निमग्नजठरश्च I अन्तविलग्नधोणः साहसनिरतः चिरश्वण्डः ।।३१।। विश्वासहासवश्यः पित्तर्गार्तः कुटुम्बसम्पन्नः । गुरुसुङ्दां द्रोहरतः परांगनाकर्षणानुरतः ।३२।। बन्धोत्वणवक्त्रः स्यात्मूपतिसेवी सशत्रुपक्षः स्यात् ।

प्रयतोऽर्यदः सुयुवतिर्धमें प्रति वत्सलः क्षुद्रः ॥ ३३।

30-33. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN SCORPIO ASCENDANT: One born in Scorpio ascendant will have fleshy and broad limbs, (as though) perfected by exercises, be clever, crooked in innate disposition, valorous, attached to his mother, inclined to quarrel, generous, will have majestic and tawny eyes, a prominent chest, a sunken belly, and depressed nose, be adventurous, firm, fierce, trustworthy, amusing, be troubled by bilious complaints, will have a big family, will betray his preceptors (or elders) and friends, be engaged in attracting other women, will have an attractive face, will serve a king, will have enemies, be pious, will give his money to others, will possess an amiable wife, be religious, affectionate and base.

कार्मुकलग्ने जातः स्थूलरदस्तुंगपृयुलमूर्धा ।

प्रणतानां प्रियकारी धृतिसत्वसमन्वितः सुनयः ॥३४।।

मलिनासिकोळ्ठकुनखी ह्वदोमानतिपीवरोर्जठरश्च । विज्ञानशास्त्रकुशलः प्रत्यप्रमतिः प्रकोप्च । ३५।। बलिनाममर्षणपरः कुलमुस्यो नाशितारिपक्षश्च । संग्रामपवश्रेश्श्छलबहुलच्छद्बन्धुगुणः  $.$  113511

शिल्पादिकर्मनिरतः स्वकर्मवान् बन्धुवर्गशुभदश्व। कान्तो वदनाक्षिरोगो नृपाद्धतार्थः सुधर्मरतः ।।३७।।

34-37. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN SAGITTARIUS ASCEN- DANT: One born in Sagittarius ascendant will have big teeth and prominent forehead, will patronise dependants, be courageous, strong, just, will have very fat thighs and bellv, be skilful in Sastras, be of high intelligence. be irascible, short-tempered even with mighty men, be important among the men of his race, will destroy enemies, be famous through battles, will fault his relatives, be interested in fine arts etc., will have own profession, will do good to his men, be splendorous, be afflicted with facial and eye diseases, will earn wealth through royal favours and be virtuous.

मुगवदने लम्नस्थे कुशगात्रो भीररेणवक्त्त्रश्च।

वातव्याधिभिरार्तः प्रदीप्ततुंगोग्रनासः स्यात् ।।३८। लघुसत्वोऽमिततनयो रोमचितः पाणिपादविस्तीर्णः। आचारगुर्णहोंनस्तृषार्तरामाभिराममतिः 113611 गिरिवनचारी शूरः शास्त्रश्रुतिशिल्पगेयवाद्याजः। क्षुद्रबलः सकुटुम्बो द्विष्टो दुष्टश्च बन्धुशठः ।४०।। कुत्सितशीलः कान्तः कुत्सितदारोऽनसूयको धनवान्। धर्मरतो नृपसेवी न चातिदाता सुखी सुभगः ।४१। 38-41. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN CAPRICORN ASCEN- DANT: One born in Capricorn ascendant will have an emaciated body, be timid, will have a face akin to that of a deer, be troubled by windy diseases, will have a bright and

elevated nose, be not mighty, will have many sons, be hairybodied, will have broad feet and hands, be devoid of conduct and virtues, be tormented by thirst (or morbid heat), be dear to the fair sex, will wander in hills and forests, be valorous, will have knowledge of Sastras, Vedas, fine arts, music and musical instruments, will have little strength, be a householder, be hostile, wicked, illdisposed to relatives, splendorous, will have a wicked wife, will not be a talebearer, be wealthy, virtuous, will serve a king, be very liberal, happy and fortunate.

कुम्भविलग्ने पुरुषः सुनीचकर्मा कुलाधिको मूर्खः। स्कुटिताग्रनसो नीवः सक्रोधपरोऽलसात्मा च ।४२।। बैरप्रियोऽप्रह्ुष्टः पार्प्यद्यूतनीचदासीष्टः । उपहुतबन्धुः क्षुब्धः क्षयोदयी प्राप्तवित्तश्च ।।४३। पिशुनः शठो बरिद्रो विनष्टबन्धुर्बहिष्कृतो लोके। नो संमतः परेषां प्रक्ष्टसम्पद्गुरुरतिश्च ।४४।। कुम्भोदयो न शस्तो लग्नविधौ सर्वथैव सत्यमते। यवनैर्वर्गोऽपि तथा चाणक्यो वदति नो वर्गम् ॥४५।।

42-45. EFFECTS OF BIRTH IN AQUARIUS ASCEN- DANT: One born in Aquarius ascendant will indulge in mean acts, be principal among the men of his race, be foolish, will have blown and sharp nose, be base, irascible, indolent, inclined to enmity, dissatisfled, harsh, be fond of gambling and of base females, will not be helpful to relatives, be agitated, will have checkered prosperity, will acquire wealth, be a talebearer, be crafty, will suffer penury, will face loss of relatives, be prohibited (in social circles), be not agreeable, will acquire great wealth and will honour eldcrs. At birth Aquarius is not an auspicious ascendant for ever, according to Satyacharya. Yavanas go a step further stating that even Aquarius Vargas are not auspicious (at birth) while Chanakya does not consider such divisions inauspicious.

मीनविलग्ने जातो धन्यः स्फुटनासिकोऽह्युटाक्षश्च । विज्ञानकाव्यबुद्धिमानादरलब्धकी तिश्च विवृतोष्ठरदः कुष्ठी विदारितास्यो वृषादिसंतुब्धः । बाक्षिण्यप्रत्ययवान् मेवच्छागादिसम्पन्नः ।४७।। शौचाचारश्रुतिवाग्धृतिमान् कन्याप्रजो विनीतश्व । सौम्यमतिः सत्त्वयुतो गान्धर्वस्त्रीरतिज्ञश्च ।४८।। बहुशीलोदारमतिर्चातृधनोऽमर्षणः सुबन्धुश्च । बलवति राशावेतत्तदधिपतौ वा फलं सर्वम् ॥४६।।

46-49. BIRTH IN PISCES ASCENDANT: One born in Pisces ascendant, will be fortunate, will have wellblown nose and unblown (not wide open) eyes, will be interested in profane knowledge and poetry, be respectable, famous, will have exposed lips and teeth, be tormented, will suffer from leprosy, will have an afflicted face, be courteous, trustworthy, will be endowed with goats, she-goats etc., will have pure conduct, will have knowledge of Vedas, be courageous, will have (more) daughters, be modest, gently disposed, be mighty, will have knowledge of music and sexual union, be virtuous, liberal, will gain through brothers and will have good relatives. If the ascending sign or the lord of the ascendant is strong, the effects stated will fully come to pass (in regard to all ascendants).

इति कल्याणवर्मविरवितायां सारावल्यां नष्टजातके लग्नगुणो नामाष्टवत्वारिशोऽध्यायः ।४८II

Thus ends the 48th Chapter entitled "LOST HOROSCOPY (through rising signs)” in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अथ होरागुणकथनाध्यायः ॥४&॥ Chapter 49

## Effects of Horas"

रिक्तोत्कटवृक् क्रूरो धनपः शुक्लाधिकोप्रदाररतः। पीनोन्नतः प्रचण्डस्तस्करनाथः क्रियादिहोरायाम् ॥१।। चोरः प्रमावबहुलः खराग्रपादांगुलिद्वितीयायाम् । स्निग्धायताक्षचतुरः पृथुपोनतनु: सुमेधाश्च ।२।।

1-2. HORAS IN ARIES: If the first Hora of Aries ascends at birth, the native will roll his eyeballs unnecessarily, be cruel, rich, very bright in appearance, will have a fierce wife, be tall, irascible and be a leader of thieves. In the case of second Hora, the native will be a thief, be careless, will have afflicted foot, fingers etc., large eyes and tall stature and be very intelligent.

श्यामो विशालचक्षुललाटवक्षा: प्रगल्भरतिवश्यः ।

स्यूलास्थितनुर्वभप्रयमाधे स्याद्वपुर्मांश्च ।३।।

पृथ्वायतवृत्ततनुमुदारसर्वं सुमूर्घजं जनयेत् । व्यस्तरकाटि वृषभाक्षं वृषभे होराद्वितीयायाम् ॥४।।

3-4. HORAS IN TAURUS: If the first Hora of Taurus ascend at birth, the native will be dark in complexion, will have broad eyes, foreface and chest, be eminent, disposed to carnal pleasures and will have strong bones and strong physique. In the second Hora, he will have broad, long and round (i.e. muscular) limbs, be liberal, will have attractive (\*) Ch. 49, 50 and 51 apart from 48 and 52 also directly relate to lost horoscopy. hair, thin waist and eyes akin to that of an ox.

मध्यायतोऽतिवक्षो मध्यतनुम् दुशिरोर्हांधिश्च । मियुनादार्घे शूरः सुरतेप्सुः स्याद्धनी प्राज्ञः ॥५। मधुरायताक्षकामी शूरो मृदुकर्मठो वचस्वी च। परदारदत्तदेहो भवेन्नृमिथुनद्वितीयहोरायाम् ।६।।

5-6. HORAS IN GEMINI: If the first Hora of Gcmini be the ascendant, the native will have a long waist, be very skilful, will have a moderate physique and soft hair and feet, be courageous and fond of sexual union, be wealthy and learned. Born in the second Hora of Gemini, onc will have beautiful and large eyes, be sensuous, soft, be a gifted speaker and be attached to others' housewives.

उद्धतमूतिः सुशिराः प्रगल्भधर्मन्दवृक्चलांगशठः । श्यामतनु: सुङ्गतघ्नो भन्नाग्रदः कुलीरहोरायाम् ।७।। द्यते रतोऽध्वनिरतः पृयुवक्षाः सत्त्रमाणसम्पन्नः ।

कठिनशरीरः क्रोधी जायेत कुलीरद्वितीयायाम् ।।८।।

7-8. HORAS IN CANCER: If the birth be in the first half of Cancer, one will be endowed with elevated body, (i.e. be prominent in appearance), a beautiful head, eminence and intelligence, be weaksighted, will have limbs moving off and on, be crafty, dark in complexion, ungratcful and will have broken teeth. If it is the second half of Cancer, he will be fond of gambling and travels, will have a broad chest, be quite truthful, irascible and will have a harsh physique.

9-10. HORAS IN LEO: One born in the first Hora of Leo will have blood-red eyes, be bold, be endowed with a large

and long physique, be crooked, happy and decidedly firm in his undertakings. One born in the later half of Leo will be fond of women, sweets, foods, drinks and robes, will move frequently, will have a harsh physique, be liberal, fond of travels, will have a few sons, will enjoy pleasures, and be firm in friendship.

सुहुमारमूतिका तः गान्धर्वविद्युवत्याः सुवावयगीतांगनारतिर्मधुरः । सुभगः पूर्वर्धिजः श्रेष्ठः ॥११। हस्वो हठीश्रुतार्यः स्यूलशिराः सम्मतो विवादी च। सेवालेश्यलिपिज्ञः क्षयवृद्धियुतः सुक्षी द्वितीयार्षे ।१२।।

11-12. HORAS IN VIRGO: If the birth be in the initial half of Virgo, the native concerned will have a soft and beautiful body, be a sweet speaker, be fond of music, females and sexual union, be sweet, fortunate and excellent. In the second half of Virgo one will be short-statured, will have knowledge of Hatha Yoga and Vedas, will possess a big head, be agreeable, argumentative, learned in service, drawing and writing, will have checkered prosperity and (yet) be happy.

वृत्तानन उच्वनसस्त्वसितायतसुनयनो विलासी स्यात् । पीनायतोऽस्थिसारो घनवान्स्वजनप्रियस्तुलाद्यधे ॥ १३।। बल्धुर्यभाक् स्यरार्यः भ्यामाकुञ्वतशिरोरहश्व शठः । वृत्ताक्षस्त्वपराधे

सुत्वग्धीनाप्रपादश्च ॥१४।।

13-14. HORAS IN LIBRA: If the first Hora of Libra ascends at birth one will have a round face, elevated nose and black and large eyes, be sportive, will have a strong body and strong bones, be wealthy, and will be affectionate to his men. If in the later half of Libra, he will be very wealthy, will have firm wealth, will be endowed with black and curly hair, be crafty, round-eyed, will have a strong lower body and attractive skin and will have defective feet.

रक्तान्तपिगदृष्टिः साहसकर्मान्वितो रणे शूरः। दुष्टस्वभाव रामाप्रियोऽर्यभाम्वृश्चिकाद्यधे 118 11

विस्तीर्णोपचितायतपीनांगः क्षमाधिषोपसेवी स्यात् । बह वृणमित्रसमेतः स्फुटिताक्षो वृश्चिकापराधे स्यात् ॥1१६।

15-16. HORAS IN SCORPIO: One born in the first half of Scorpio will have blood-red eyes, be adventurous in acts, be valorous in battle, wicked, fond of females and be rich. If born in the second half of Scorpio, one will have broad, well grown, long and fleshy limbs, will serve a king, will have many debts and many friends and will possess blown eyes (expanded as a flower).

दारितपृथुमुखवक्षाः परिकुञ्चितनेत्रमण्ड: स्यात् । बाल्ये त्यक्तात्मगुरुश्चापाद्धे तपस्वी च ।।१७।। पद्याक्षो दीर्घमहाबाहुः शास्त्रार्थवित्सुमूतिः स्यात् ।

वाक्सुभगो धन्योऽपि च धनुरपरे निर्वतो यशस्वी च ।।१८।।

17-18. HORAS IN SAGITTARIUS: One born in the first nalf of Sagittarius will possess a wide and split face and chest, with eyes and neck contracted (i.e. small), will be discarded by elders in boyhood and be plous. If the later half of Sagittarius rises, he will possess eyes akin to the petals of a lotus and long arms, will have knowledge of Sastras, be good in appearance, will speak sweetly, be fortunate and famous.

स्यामो मुमाक्षधन्यः स्त्रीष्वजितः सौम्यमूतिशठ आइचयः । मृष्टाशनः सुचेष्टो मृगाद्यभागे तनृच्बधोणः स्यात् ॥१६।। रक्तान्तदृष्टरलसो गुरुदीर्घाटनपरो भवति सूर्खः। श्यामो रोमचितांगस्तीक्ष्णः सहसा सुरौद्रकर्मा च।।२०।।

19-20. HORAS IN CAPRICORN: If the first half of Capricorn be on the ascendant, the native will be dark in complexion, will possess eyes like that of a deer, be fortu-

nate, will conquer women, be beautiful, crafty, opulent, will eat purified food, will do good acts, and will havc clevated nosc. If born in the second half of Capricorn onc will have blood-red eyes, be indolent, be interested in long travels, be foolish, dark in complexion, will have a hairy physique, be clever and fierce.

स्त्रीमित्रमागरसविन्मृदुलोऽल्पसुतश्च सद्गुणः शूरः । तास्रो भास्वरवर्णो यानमतिः कुम्भपूर्वाधि ।२१।।

आता स्रदारिताक्षः हृशः रियरोऽत्यल्पम्तिरलसः स्यात् ।

## नक्तिकः सुविषादी कृपणः कुम्मापरे सुशठः ।२२।।

21-22. HORAS IN AgUARIUS: One born in thc first half of Aquarius will be endowed with a female and with friends, be a person of tastes or feelings (or be an alchemist), be soft in disposition, will have a few sons, be virtuous, valorous, will be endowed with physical complexion akin to copper, be radiant and interested in travels. If born in the second half, one will have copperbrilliant eyes, be emaciated, firm, very insignificant in appearance, indolent, not outspoken, very miserly and very crafty. dejected,

## ह्रस्वः पृथुवार्तनुरमहाललाटो बृहद्दन्क्षाः ।

ह्त्र दयितो मीनार्धे प्रथमे सुयशाः क्रिपापटुः शूरः ।२३।

दाता सुतुंगनासो नि्ुणी मेधान्वितः शुभदनेत्रः।

नृपदवितः स्त्रीसुमगश्चार्मोनापरे सुवाष्यः स्यात् ॥२४।।

23-24. HORAS IN PISCES: One born in the first 15 degrees of Pisces will be short-statured, be endowed with broad and beautiful body, a large foreface, large face and broad chest, be dear to women, be very famous, skilful and valorous. If born in the second Hora of Pisces, he will be liberal, will have an elevated nose, be skilful, intelligent, will possess charming eyes, be dear to king, and will speak affably.

चन्द्वार्कयोरेकतरं बलस्यं होरापतिः पश्यति केन्द्रगो वा ।

होरा ययोद्दिष्टफलप्रदा स्याद्गभस्थसर्वस्य समुद्भवेषु ।।२५॥

25. The (good effects) due to a Hora will come to pass in full measure if cither the Sun or the Moon is strong in aspect to the ascendant lord or if the ascendant lord is himself in an angle.

इति कल्याणवर्म रचितायां सारावल्यां नष्टजातके होरागुणो नाम एकोनपञ्चाशत्त मोऽध्यायः ॥४६।।

Thus ends the 49th Chapter entitled “EFFECTS OF HORAS (Lost Horoscopy)” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## अथ द्रेष्काणफलाध्यायः १।५०।11 Chapter 50

## Effects of Decanates

दाता हर्ता बीप्तः क्षयोदयी संगरप्रचण्डः स्यात्। प्रियविग्रहस्त्रिभागे मेवाद्ये बन्धुषुप्रदण्डश्च ।।१।। स्त्रीचञ्चलो विहारी रतिमान्गीतप्रियो मनस्वी स्यात् । मित्रार्थभास्सुरूपः स्त्रीवित्तर्चिद्वितीये च ।२। गुणवान्परदोषकरश्वलसर्वयुतो नरेन्द्रसेवी स्यात् । स्वजनप्रियोऽतिघर्मस्तृतीयभागे प्रियादरोऽ्जश्व ।३।।

1-3. DECANATES IN ARIES: One born in the first decanate of Aries will be charitable, be a thief, be splendorous, will have checkered prosperity, be fierce in battle (or in quarrels), pleasing in appearance and will inflict punishment on relatives. If born in the second decanate, he will be attached to females, will wander, be interested in carnal pleasures and music, be intelligent, be endowed with friends and wealth, be good-looking and will have an eye on women’s prosperity. If born in the third decanate, he will be virtuous, will fault others, be fickleminded, mighty, will serve a king, be attached to his own men, be very righteous and honourable but not learned.

त्रियपानभोज्यनारोवियोगतप्तो

वृषर्य पूर्वांशे।

वस्त्रालंकारयुतो युवतिप्रक्तानुसारी स्यात् ॥४।।

सौम्यवपुस्त्रोसुभगो महाघरो रूपधनयुक्तः । घनवान्स्थरो मनस्वी लुब्धस्त्रीणां प्रियो हितीये स्यात् ।।५।

## चतुरोऽल्पभाग्यवोरो मलीमसः र्याद्धनान्युपादाय । सः्तप्यते तु पञ्चाङ्षस्य भागे तृतीये च।६।।

4-6. DECANATES IN TAURUS: If the birth be in the first decanate of Taurus, one will be fond of food and drinks, be tormented due to separation of his wife, endowed with robes and ornaments and will act according to the desires of his wife. If in the second decanate he will be beautiful, liked by women, will have large lips, be endowed with beauty and wealth, firmly wealthy, intelligent and attached to mean women. If born in the third decanate, he will be skilful, be not very fortunate, be valorous, dirty and will repent after spending money.

भियुनादिमे दृगाणे पृथूत्तमांगो धनान्वितः प्रांशुः। कितवो गुणी विलासो नृपाप्तमानो वचस्वी स्यात् ।७।। ह्हस्वाननस्वरूपः सौम्यवयुः सुक्ष्ममूर्धजतनुः स्यात् । धन्यो मृदुर्महाधीद्धतीयभागे प्रतापवान्सुवशाः ।।। स्त्रीदेषणो वपुष्मान्महाशिराः शत्रुसंयुतः प्रांशुः।

रक्षनखाड़ि घकरतलश्चलार्घविभृतो दृढस्तृतीये स्यात् ।।।।

7-9. DECANATES IN GEMINI: One born in the first decanatc of Gemini will have broad and excellent limbs, be wealthy, lofty, deceiving, virtuous, sportive, honoured by the king and be a good speaker. If in the second decanate, he will be miserly, dreaming, conquered by the fair scx, selfrespected, endowed with co-born, fickle-minded, sportive and tormented by many diseases. If in the third decanate, he will hate women, will have a large head, will have enemies, be lofty, will possess rough nalls and will not have firm wealth.

कर्कटकादिमभागे बेवब्राह्यणरतस्चलो

गौरः ।

क्रत्वकरद्च परेषां सुत्रीः सुनूतिः शुमांगनः सुमगः ।।१०।।

लुब्धः स्वाद्वनरतः स्वप्नरतः स्त्रोजितोऽभिमानो ह्यात् । सहजान्वितो विलासी चपलो बहुरुग्द्वतीये च ।।११। स्त्रोचञ्चलोऽर्यभागी विवेशनिरतः प्रियासवः साधुः। कानमतोयानुरतो दुबृ श्टिर्माल्यवांस्तृतीये र्यात् ।।१२।।

10-12. DECANATES IN CANCER: One born in thc first decanate of Cancer will honour Brahmins and gods, be ficklc-minded, fair in complexion, will help others, be intelligent, pleasing in appearance, will possess a fortunate wife and be himself fortunate. If in the second decanate, he will be miserly, dreaming, conquered by the fair sex, self respected, cndowed with co-born, fickleminded, sportive and be tormented by many discases. If in the third decanate, he will lose his firmness to females, be wealthy, intent on living in foreign places, fond of wine, be wealthy, virtuous, will have liking for forests, will have diseased eyes and will like garlands etc.

सिहादिद्रेष्काणे बाता भर्तारिनिजिगीषुः स्यात् ।

बहुधनयोषित्सुसुद्बहुनृपजनसेवकः सुसर्वश्च ।।१३।। सुरुविरकारी वाता स्थिरो वपुष्मानारणेप्सुः स्यात्। सुखभाक् \_\_\_ श्रुतिधर्म धर्चिविस्तीर्णम्तिद्वितीय च ।।१४।। लुब्धः परस्वहरणे कल्पः स्तब्धो महामतिः हित्वः। नायततनुमूतिः स्वाग्नैकापर्यः प्रगल्भोऽ्यये ।१४॥।

13-15. DECANATES IN LEO: Onc born in the first decanate of Leo will be liberal, will maintain people, will conquer his enemies, will have abundant wealth, many wives and good friends, will serve many kings and be very mighty. One born in the second decanate will perform beneficial acts, be liberal, firm, fond of quarrels, happy, will follow the rules of Vedas and be intelligent. If the birth is in the 3rd decanate, one will be a miser, will steal others’ wealth, be free from sickness, hard-hearted, very intelligent, roguish, will have a narrow physique, be pre-eminent

and will have many children.

श्यामः सुवाविविनीतः प्रांशुः सुकुमारमूतिरबलाये। स्त्रोष्योऽर्दभागनिष्टो दीर्घशिरा मधुसमाक्षर्च ।१६।। धीरो विदेशभागी शिल्पकयापण्डितः समरशौष्डः। बाचाट: श्रुतवाव्यो वनौकसां संमतो द्वितीये स्यात् ।।१७।। गीतापरार्थभागी संगीतरतर्नरेन्द्रदयितः स्यात् ।

ह्वस्वस्वरूपवेषश्वान्ते

पृथुद्र्शिरस्कश्च । १८।।

16-18.
