DECANATES IN VIRGO: One born in first decanate of Virgo will be dark in complexion, will speak affably, be modest, lofty, beautiful, will gain wealth through females, be ominous and will have a long head and honeycoloured eyes. If the birth be in the second decanate he will be bold, will live in foreign places, be skilful in fine arts, fables and battles, be garrulous, will honour Vedic teachings and be dear to forest tribes. In the third decanate a birth indicates one enjoying music and other’s wealth. He will be dear to king, short in stature and will be endowed with large head and large eyes.

कन्दर्पेरूपनिपुणस्तुला विभागेऽध्वसेवज्ञः I श्यामकलापण्यरतो \_\_\_ नियोगधीर: \_\_\_ सुमेधावी ।१६।। पंकजविशालनेत्रः सुरूपवाक्साहसः कलापी स्यात् । स्यातः स्ववंशर्वाधितबृद्धानुवरो द्वितीये च ।।२०। चपलः शठः कृतघ्नो विख्पजिद्योपचितमूर्तिः ।

नव्टसुहृद्द्रविणयशाः स्वल्पमतिर्भागके तृतीये स्यात् ।२१।

19-21. DECANATES IN LIBRA: If the ascendant be in the first decanate of Libra, one will be akin to Cupid in physical charm, be skilful, will enjoy travels, be dark in complexion, fond of arts and business, will fulfil appointed tasks and be very learned. If it be the second decanate, one will have eyes of the lotus-kind, be charming, affable in speech, valorous, famous and will perform acts to increase

the reputation of one’s race and family. If it be the third decanate one will be fickle minded, crafty, ungrateful, crooked, will lose friends and wealth and will not be very intelligent.

तौरः स्थरः प्रचण्डो रणोत्कटः स्यान्तरो विशालाक्षः । ह्वूलविशालशरीरः कलित्रियो वृश्विकायांशे ।२२।। मृव्टाननपानचतुरश्चलेक्षणो हेमगौरगूतिः स्वात्। कान्तः परवित्तयुतः शीलकलावान् द्वितीयेऽशे ।२३।। निःश्मश्रुरो माहिरः विगाक्षमहोदरः प्रहर्ता च। सहजच्युतस्तृतीये पीवरबाहुः सुधीरहृदयश्च ।२४।।

22-24. DECANATES IN SCORPIO: If birth be in the first decanate of Scorpio, the native will be fair in complexion, firm in disposition, flerce in temperament, interested in battle, broad-eyed, fat and broad physiques, and fond of promoting quarrels. One will be fond of purified food, be skilful, will have rolling eyes, be gold-coloured in complexion, splendorous, endowed with other’s wealth, virtuous and skilful in arts If the ascendant be in the second decanate. One born in the third decanate will be devoid of beard, moustache etc., be tortuous, will have tawny eyes and large belly, be an assailant, will incur loss of brothers and will possess long hands and brave heart.

परिमण्डलाक्षवकत्रो गणेषु मुश्यो धनुदुगाणाद्ये ।

स्वोपचितस्वाचारस्तथा मृदुर्भवति संजातः ।२५।। शास्त्रार्यवित्रवक्ता ऋतुशतहर्ता द्वितीये च ।

मन्त्रभृत्तां श्रेष्ठतमस्त्वनेकतीर्थायतनचारी । २६।

बन्धुप्रधानबतुरः सतां गतिर्घर्माक् तृतीयेऽपि ।

कामी परांगनाभावरूपयशोभाजनो विजिष्णुश्च ।।२७।।

25-27. DECANATES IN SAGITTARIUS: One born in the first decanate of Sagittarius will have a round face and round eyes, be chief among his men, will advance due to his

own efforts, will have good conduct and be soft in disposition. If the birth be in the second decanate, one will be expert in Sastras, be a good speaker, will perform hundred yagnas (i.e. many sacrifices), will have knowledge of Mantras, be excellent in disposition and will visit many shrines. If born in the third decanate, one will be chief of his relatives, be skilful, will always take to virtuous path, be sensuous, will enjoy others' women, be charming, famous and successful.

व्यालम्बभुजः श्यामः प्रथितयशोर्पकान्तिशठः । स्मितमाषी मरराद्ये स्त्रीषु जितो वत्गुचेव्टधनयुक्तः ।।२८।। अल्पवदनश्च मध्ये चलः परस्त्रीधनापहर्ता स्यात्। चतुरः सतां गतिज्ञः प्रदानशीलो दुरन्तपादः स्यात् ।।२६। बाचालः कलुवक्शो दीर्धांगः पितृवियुष्तश्च । लमते विदेशगमनाइचसनान्यपि मृगमुखस्यान्ते ।।३०।।

28-30. DECANATES IN CAPRICORN: One born in the first decanate of Capricorn will have long hands, be dark in complexion, charming, famous, crafty, sparing in speech, be conquered by ladies will perform superior acts and be wealthy. If it be the second decanate, one will have a small face, be fickle minded, will steal the wealth of other women, skilful, be a fortune-teller, be liberal and will suffer from diseases of the feet. If born in the third decanate one will be garrulous, dirty, emaciated, long-limbed, devoid of father and will be reduced to evils in foreign places.

स्त्रीमानयशोभूतिः स्फीतप्रभवो घटस्याद्ये । प्रांशुः कर्मसु निष्ठो धनवान्नपसेवको जातः ॥ ३१। लुष्प्रः समर्यमधुरो गौरः पिगोद्धताक्षहास्यधनः ।

उद्घृष्टवचा मतिमान् बहुमित्रः स्यातृद्वितीये तु ।३२।। दीर्घः शठः प्रतापी कुशोऽत्यबाहुः सुतार्थभाक्स्तब्धः। बह्मनुतोऽन्तविषमो विदारिताक्षो रतिविदन्त्ये । ३३।।

31-33. DECANATES IN AQUARIUS: One born in the first decanate of Aquarius will be endowed with wife, honour and fame, be very efficacious, lofty, devoted to his duties, wealthy and will serve a king. If born in the second decanate one will be a miser, be skilful, sweet, fair complexioned, tawny and broad eyed, will earn wealth through amusement, will speak unhesitatingly, be intelligent and will be endowed with many friends. If born in the third decanate, one will be tall in stature, crafty, valorous, emaclated, short-armed, be endowed with sons and wealth, hard-hearted, will tell many lies, be crooked in nature, will have afflicted eyes, and will have knowledge of sexology.

मधुपिंगाक्षो गौरो मेधावी

सत्क्रियारतिर्शश्च । सुखभागी मोनाद्ये जलचारयुते विनोतश्च ।३४।। नार्युपचारप्रवरो मृष्टान्नरुचिः परार्यभुक् कामी।

स्त्रीसज्जनातिदधितो व्दतां श्रेष्ठो द्वितीये तु ।।३५।

श्यामः कलासु निपुणः पृथुपादसुहृत्त्रदानश्च। मृष्टान्नपानभोगी मीनयुगान्त्ये भवेत्युरुषः । ३६।

34-36. DECANATES IN PISCES: If the ascendant is in the first decanate of Pisces, one will have honey coloured and tawny eyes, be fair in complexion, very learned, will perform virtuous deeds, be happy, will undertake trips by sea etc., and be modest. If it be the second decanate, one will be skilful in serving the fair sex, will eat purified food, will enjoy others’ wealth, be sensuous, dear to females and virtuous people and be an excellent person. If born in the last decanate of pisces one will be dark in complexion, be skilful in arts, will have wide feet, will be generous with friends and will consume purified food and drinks.

इतीरितोऽ्यं स्वगुणस्वभावो

द्रेरकाणजाना गुणविद्धिकर्त्यः ।

द्रेष्काणभे बीर्रवति स्वदृथ्टे ब्रेषकाणकल्पं तु फलं विदध्यात् ।३७।।

37. Thus spake of the decanates the preceptors wellversed in that branch. Should the sign holding the decanate be strong or be aspected by its lord, these effects will mature in full.

## इति कल्याणवर्मविरवितायां सारावल्यां नष्टजातके द्रेक्काणाध्यायो पञ्चाशोऽध्यायः ।।५०।।

Thus ends the 50th Chapter entitled "EFFECTS OF DECANATES (Lost Horoscopy)” in Kalyana Varma's SARAVALI.

## अथ नवमांश-फलाध्याय ।।५ १।

## Chapter 51

## Effects of Navamsas

अतोंऽशके लग्नगते तु वक्ष्ये वर्णस्वभावाक्तिलक्षणानि । प्रधानवीयेऽशपती शशीव तत्स्वामिराशिक्रमशो विधत्ते ।१।।

1. A native's complexion, disposition and appearance can be deduced based on the rising Navamsa at birth. The effects being stated infra will depend on the stronger of the two, viz. the Moon’s Navamsa dispositor and the Navamsa Lagna lord.

Notes: The effects given in this chapter relating to births in various Navamsas and those given in chapters 48 to 50 specifically relate to lost horoscopy apart from genethllacal astrology. Hence the reader will find a detailed account of the native’s physical description, mental disposition and the like receiving special treatment in order that by known qualities and seen form, one's horoscope if misplaced can be reconstructed.

The current chapter throws light in this direction keeping in mind the nine Navamsas of each ascendant. Additional information has been appended in translation of Nashta Jataka from Nadi granthas also. Here I correspondingly add special effects of the Moon’s position in each Navamsa, 1.e. 108 Navamsas of the zodiac as culled out from Yavana Jataka and Keeranuru Nataraja's Jatakalankara for the benefit of the reader.

अमसंस्थानमुखः स्ान्मेवाद्यांशेऽल्पनासिकांगभुजः ।

चण्डध्वनिविरूपः संकुचिताक्षः क्रशोऽ्लतांगश्च ।२।।

श्यामगुरस्कन्धमुजो ह्हस्वललाट: सुजत्रुकः स्फुटबृक् । दीर्यास्यनसो मुद्रुवागृद्धितोयमागे कुशाडि घसन्धिश्व । ३।। व्यालुप्तकेशगौरो वाष्पण्डितस्तृतीये व्यस्त भुजश्चारुनयननासश्च । जातस्तु हुशोरजानुजंघञ्च ।।४।। विश्रान्तदुक्रचण्डो ह्वस्वनसोऽटनवराडि धरोमा च । अध्यातृकः क्शः स्याच्चतुर्यग्वमागजः पुरुषः ॥५।। दृप्तोगजेन्द्रनयनः पृथुनासा ख्रूललाटको मध्ये। पीनोप चित प्रतनुः खरतररोमाङ् घितनुकेशः ॥ ६।। श्यामो मृदुम् गाक्षो गुरः ह्शस्किवकठोरचरणः स्यात् । ब्यस्तोदरकमुजांसः षण्डो भीरुश्च बहुभावी ।७।। दूर्वांकुराभचयलः सितनेत्रः सप्तमे भवेत्पुरुषः । कुलटापतिनु शंसो विशालविस्तीर्णमूतिः स्यात् ।८।। वानरमुखप्रवन्ता खरपिगतनुश्व गुह्चगदः । हिस्रोऽनृतधातरतः सुन्टृतिप्रयोग्रः सदाष्टमजः ।।। द्वीर्धः हृशो विहारी व्यस्तललाटश्वोऽस्ववदनश्च। बन्दुभिधानाभिरतस्त्वन्जुर्नवमांशजो भर्वति ।।१०।

2-10, EFFECTS OF NAVAMSAS IN ARIES: Following are the effects of birth in the nine Navamsas in Aries

ascendant:

First Navamsa: The native will have a face resembling that of a he-goat, with nose and shoulders not being very prominent. He will have a fierce voice, ugly appearance and narrow eyes. His body will be thin but free from defects.

Second Navamsa: The native will be dark in complexion, will have broad shoulders and long arms, small forehead, strong collar bones, sharp sight and prominent face and nose. He will be an affable speaker and will possess weak legs.

Third Navamsa: The native will suffer loss of hair, be fair in complexion, will have irregular (defective) arms, charming eyes and nose, will be a scholar in direct poetic • ability (otherwise called अft who authors poems just verbally within the parameters of Grammar on demand and on the spot) and will have weak thighs.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will have an erratic sight, be irascible, short-nosed, wandcring-natured, will have rough legs and coarse hair, be bereft of co-born and emaciated.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will be fierce, and will have eyes resembling that of a supreme elephant, a fat nose, thick eye-brows, wide foreface, fat body and coarse hair.

Sixth Navamsa: The native will be dark in complexion soft in disposition, will have eyes akin to that of a deer, be tall in stature, will have irregular (defective) stomach and hands, be a eunuch, be timid and garrulous.

Seventh Navansa: The native will have complexion akin to green sprout, be fickle minded, will possess white eyes, will marry an unchaste lady, be malicious and will have a broad physique.

Elghth Navamsa: The native will have a face akin to that of a monkey, be a good speaker, will have an afflicted and tawny body, will suffer from secret diseases, be torturous, be a liar, be fond of friends and be fierce.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will be tall, emaciated, wandering, will have defective forcface and ears, will have a face akin to that of a horse, will possess many names and be crooked.

Notes: The Moon in the various Navamsas1 of Aries will glve varied results as under. Ist: The native will be a thief, a talebearer, be gloomy, wicked, short, be not merciful and will indulge in vanity. 2nd: He will act childishly, be charming in appearance, will read others' minds, be helpful 1. (arising out of Janma Nakshatra Padas or Moon's stellar quarter positions in various Rasis)

to others, will comment on the meanings of Sastras, and be splendorous. 3rd: He will be skilful in mathematics, will be able to impart superior knowledge, will be endowed with excellent ideas, will be fortunate and bè liable to suffer from piles. 4th: He will be humpbacked, very intelligent, be a skilful astrologer, be fond of sexual pleasures, will enjoy like Lord Indra and be long-lived. 5th: He will be charitable, endowed with tawny complexion, be physically very strong, be skilfui in the branch of justice, will destroy his enemies like an elephant or like a lion, will have curly hair and will be addicted to eating meat etc. 6th: he will be wealthy, happy, endowed with a superior (mental) heart, be virtuous, intelligent, attached to sexual pleasures, be charitable and will be an exponent of Sastras. 7th: He will indulge in fierce acts, be very irritable, be wealthy, honourable, virtuous, delighted and be tall in stature. 8th: He will be utterly poor, emaclated, given to anger, will serve the king and be egoistic. 9th: He will be endowed with physical splendour, be virtuous, scholarly, very patient, valorous, will increase his wealth, will suffer physical pains, be liked by his people and be wise.

समकुष्णतनुः स्तब्धः पूर्वमचान्येऽन्त्यकर्मा स्यात् । नीचः प्रकृतिविरुद्धो विषमाक्षिनिरीक्षणो वृदस्याच्ये ।११।। गम्भीरदृगलसात्मा विनतशिराववत्रकर्च लघुमेधाः। प्रतिकूलकर्ममिथ्याबहुप्रलापी द्वितीये स्यात् ॥१२।। विस्रान्तवृकाचण्डी ह्वस्वनसोऽटनखराडि घरोमा च । मृहं गवान्वपुष्मान्सुनसस्पष्टायताक्ष वृहदंगः

यज्ञादिकर्मनिरतः स्थरपाष्णिकरस्तृतीयनवमांशे ।१३। हृदस्वोटनः सुरोयो मेषाक्षः पिगलस्त्वधनयुक्तः । परधनहरणाभिरतश्वतुर्थमागे म वृषस्य : I व्यालः सुतुंगघोणो महर्वभाकारवक्रयनकेशः । स्यात्पञ्चमे विलासी बृहद् मुजस्कन्धकटिगौरः ।।१५।।

स्वक्षः सियरः सुकेशः स्नग्धतनुर्वल्गुवाक्प्रगत्मः स्यात् । माधुयंहास्यनिरतः क्रशः सुनिपुणो भवेत्वध्ठे ॥१९। मृतसुतयुवतीषु रतो मनाक्प्रलम्बाद्रनासिकाक्षः स्यात् । उव्बद्धांगः स्वजनद्वेषी गुरुपादसूक्ष्मकेशश्च ॥ १७१। व्याघ्र क्षणः सुदशनस्त्वजितस्फुटनासिकोऽल्पकर्मा स्यात् । उद्वृतनीलकेशोग्रनखो मुखरस्तथाष्टमजः ।१८।। मान्योऽल्पसर्वभीरः क्रोधी समरविरमुतिकितवः स्यात् । सञ्चितधनः प्रसिद्धः क्रशस्त्वधस्तात्त्रलाप्यन्ते ।१६। 11-19. EFFECTS OF NAVAMSAS IN TAURUS: Following are the effects due to births in the Navamsas belonging to Taurus ascendant:

First Navamsa: The native will have an even and dark. coloured physique, be hard-hearted, will perform obsequies in the beginning and ending parts of his life, be base, will indulge in unnatural acts and will have crooked sight.

Second Navamsa: The native will be endowed with majestic looks, be indolent, will have a bent body, be not very intelligent, will indulge in hostile acts and be a great liar.

Third Navamsa: The native will be erratic in sight, be fierce, will have a short nose, be wandering natured, will have coarse legs and hair, will have soft limbs, be beautiful, will have broad eyes and big limbs, be interested in Sacrifices etc., and will have stiff legs and hands.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will be short in stature, will be wandering-natured, be easily irritable, will have eyes akin to that of a he-goat, be tawny in complexion, be poor and will steal others' wealth.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will be vicious, will have a well-elevated nose, will appear like a glant ox, will have crooked hair, be sportive, will have large shoulders and hips.

Slxth Navamsa: The native will have beautiful eyes and hair, be firm, be endowed with a fair complexioned physique, will speak sweetly, be pre-eminent, be fond of amusements, be emaciated and skilful.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will be interested in females who lost their sons, will have somewhat elevated nose and prominent eyes, will possess a strong physique, will hate his own men and will have stout feet and exquisite hair.

Eighth Navamsa: The native will have eyes akin to that of a tiger and charming teeth, be inconquerable, will possess a full-blown nose, will work sparingly, will have curly and bluish hair and sharp nails and be garrulous.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will be honourable, will not be very strong, be timid, given to anger, will possess an even and charming body, be a rogue (or cheat), will gather money, be famous, will have a thin lower body and will prattle.

Notes: The Moon in Taurus Rasi disposed in its nine Navamsas will reveal the following results.

1st: He will be learned in Sastras, be not endowed with praiseworthy attributes and will befriend those who will be successful. 2nd: He will be miserable, will join unworthy females, be not endowed with intelligence, will harm others, be very valorous, will not be happy in respect of his income, be not capable of receiving education, be miserable, impatient and sickly. 4th: He will be extremely fortunate, beautiful, will wander without any firm footing, be attached to pleasures of sex, will inflict harm on others, be impatient, dejected, honourable and sickly. 5th: He will be oppressed, be very merciful, virtuous, sickly, eloquent in speech and will enjoy the blessing of elders, be endowed with knowledge of various sastras, be learned in astrology, be a skilful singer, will have knowledge of Mantras and be endowed with pleasures of the life. 7th: He will be truthful, will be after having others' possessions and will subdue to women (out of lust). 8th: He will be so affluent that everyone

around him will recognise him; he will conquer his enemies, will be corpulent, irascible, devoid of good conduct and will be demonic in disposition in the eyes of others. 9th: He will be thievish in disposition, will hold important legal positions, will have longsighted policies, be patient, will lead an ascetic’s life, will keep up his promise and will be a debaucher.

रोमोपचितांसभुजो नरासितापांगदृक्तथोच्चनसः । दूर्वाकाण्डस्यामः क्शाङ् घिपाणिस्तृतीयभवनाच्ये ॥ २०॥ घटशीर्षोऽशुचिकर्मा घातरुचिर्मव्यनिम्नघोणः स्यात्। बहुभाषी बहुचेष्टो द्वितीयभागे तु विग्रहाधिपतिः ।।२१। गौरोऽतिरक्तनयनः मुनासिकः समतनुः सुमेधा स्यात्। दीर्धाननोऽसितश्चूर्बाचाचतुरस्तृतीयेऽशे 11 सुखूललाटकामी नीलोत्पलमूतिविपुलवलाः स्यात् । सितदन्तो मुदुवस्ता प्रशस्तरोमाचितश्चतुयेऽशे ॥२३। पृथ्वाननो बृहत्स्किष्पोवरवक्षोभुजश्च खलः । स्यूलशिरा मायावो सितानुकूलेक्षणस्तु पञ्चमजः ।।२४।। मध्वीक्षणः प्रलापी व्यस्तललाट: समस्तुतनुः । कितवश्वलश्च रुचिरोष्ठरदः बच्ठे तु सस्वयुतः ।२५।। तास्रारुणाक्षवर्णः समुन्नताक्षो विशालवक्षाः स्यात्। शिक्षा सुशिल्पनिपुणो हास्यरतिः सप्तमे जातः ।२६। श्यामो गुर्मनस्वी ललितो मधुराभिभाषीच। व्यस्तविवृद्धशरीरो बीर्घासितव्क्कलाविदष्टमजः ।२७। वृत्तासितद्वसुतनुः सिद्धो मेघाबलो रतिजः स्यात् । विज्ञानकाव्यनिरतो नक्षमे जायेत मियुनस्य ।।२८।। 20-28. Following are the effects of births in the rine

Navamsas of Gemini ascendant:

First Navamsa: The native will have hair on the shoulders, will possess charming and dark eyes and an elevated nose, be akin to green (Doorva) grass in complexion and will possess thin legs and thin hands.

Second Navamsa: The native will have a pot-like head, will do dirty acts, be fond of torturous deeds, will have depressed nose, will speak much, will work much and will lead in strife and quarrels.

Third Navamsa: The native will be fair in complexion, will possess blood-red eyes, charming nose and even physique, be very intelligent, will have a long face and dark eyebrows and be a skilful speaker.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will possess charming eye-brows and lorehead, be tustful, will possess a physique with the splendour of a blue lotus, will be broad-chested and white teethed, be soft in speech and will have attractive hair.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will have a broad face, strong chest and big head, be wicked, cunning and will possess charming and friendly looks.

Sixth Navamsa: The native will possess eyes with the hue of honey, be garrulous, will possess a broad foreface, even body and charming lips, be a rogue, be fickle-minded and be strong.

Seventh Navamsa: The nattve will possess a coppercoloured physique, copper red and prominent eyes and a broad chest, be skilful in teaching and arts and be jocular in disposition.

Eighth Navamsa: The native will be dark in complexion, be great, intelligent, soft in disposition, sweet in speech, will have a broad and tall physique and large and black eyes and will be an expert in arts.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will have round and dark coloured eyes and charming body, be successful, very intelligent and be fond of sexual cohabitation, poetry and worldly knowledge.

Notes: If the Navamsa occupied by the Moon be due to her sign position in Gemini at birth, she will produce following effects:

1st: He will enjoy abundant pleasures, will be able to answer anybody with ease, be excellent, be not given to anger, be always happy, be very intelligent, will possess excellent conduct and be rich. 2nd: He will be wealthy, endowed with corns etc., will be in a position to give worthy suggestions just on demand, be truthful, will take to good path of living, will be very charitable, be strong, irascible, will sometimes cheat and will not lag behind in seeking carnal pleasures. 3rd: He will be a spendthrift, will please one and all with his eloquent specch, be endowed with good qualities and be liked by young girls. 4th: He will render advice in timcs of difficulties and will enjoy sexual pleasure in a limited manner. 5th: He will be shortlived, will have a bent back, will suffer rheumatic disorders be bereft of divine grace, be a talebearer, will long illegal relationship with women and will be a bad person. 6th: He will be a thief, be adventurous, wise, virtuous, helpful to his relatives, will forget frequently, will not maintain physical cleanliness and will spread bad odour thereby and will perform ghastly acts. 7th: He will be happy, corpulent, mean in disposition, deaf, dishonourable, will have sparse hair and will be after sexual pleasures and pleasures in general. 8th: He will be learned, will suffcr bilious complaints, be indolent, bereft of cleanliness and good conduct and will always suffer miseries. 9th: He will suffer from leprosy and other diseases, will always wander, will face dental deficiencies, will have knowledge of "do and do not" as per Sastras longlived. and be

निर्मल वारसुगौरः सुमूर्धनः स्वाद्विशालकुक्षिश्च ।

मंगलमुखोन्नताक्षस्तन्वंग भुजः कुलोराद्ये ।२१।

रवतच्छवी रणोप्रः कलाप्रियः स्याद्विडालमुखनेत्रः । किद्वितीयभागे त्यागी कराजानुजंघश्च ।३०।।

गौर: सुनेत्रवाग्मी सुकुमारस्यूलयोविदंगश्व । धीमान्मृदुकर्मरतस्तृतीयभांगे भवेदलसः ।३१।। श्यामञ्छविर्नत्रृविलासपीनोन्नतः सुनासाक्षः । धीरः पुरुवो दाता सुजातिकायें शठश्चतुर्थे स्यात् ॥ ३२। घण्टार्बरो नतास्यः सुसंहतस्रः सुवीर्वबाहुः स्यात् । सेवारतो विकर्मा मध्ये दुर्मवणोऽलपमेधाश्च ।। ३३।। क्षीर्धविशालशर रः प्रशस्तनयनो बहुप्रतापः स्यात् । गौर: सुबंशधोणो वषता षध्ठे च पुवुदन्तः ।।३४।। भिन्नशिरोशहरोमा वृहत्तनुः स्यात्सिरालजंघश्व । परगुहरक्षणशोलः काकाकारश्च सप्तमजः ।।३५। घण्टाशिरा कुशिल्पी सुमुखभुजांगश्व कूर्मगतिः । मः्यविलग्ननसः स्थादष्टमभागे तु कृष्णश्च ।।३६।। गौरो क्षवनेत्रगुरुम् दूदरोऽय पृयुषीनवक्षाः स्यात् । दीर्घह्नुर्लम्बोष्ठो महोरक्कशजानुगुरुफोऽन्त्ये । ३७।।

29-37. Following are the effects for births in various Navamsas related to Cancer ascendant:

Ftrst Navamsa: The native will have a clean, charming and fair coloured physique, beautiful hair, broad belly, impressive face, prominent eyes, thin body and thin shoulders.

Second Navamsa: The native will be blood red in complexion, be flerce in quarrels, will like fine arts, will possess face and eyes akin to that of a cat, will be wêll disposed to sacrify for others and will have weak knees and shanks.

Third Navamsa: The native will be fair in complexion, will possess beautiful eyes, be an eloquent speaker, will have a soft body akin to that of a female, be intelligent, be a sparing and light worker and be indolent.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will be black in complex-

ion, will have pressed eyebrows, be graceful in appearance, will have charming eyes and nose, be courageous, liberal, will perform acts prescribed for superior castemen and be crafty.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will possess voice akin to the sound of bell, crooked or stooping face, allied cyebrows and very long arms, be interested in worship, be bereft of dutifulness, will injure others and be not very intelligent.

Sixth Navamsa: The native will have a long and broad physique, charming eyes and great courage, be fair in complexion, be a good speaker and will possess beautiful nose and big teeth.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will have scattered hair, big body and sinewy knees, will be disposed to protect others’ families and will be akin to a crow in appearance.

Eighth Navamsa: The native will have a head with bell shape, charming face, shoulders and limbs, will be a degraded artisan (i.e an infamous workcr), will have the gait of a tortoise, and crooked nose and be dark in complexion.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will be fair complexioned, will possess eyes resembling fish, be great, be soft-belied, broad-chested, will have prominent chin and lips, large but weak knees and similar ankles.

Notes: The various Navamsa positions of the Moon in Cancer Rasi will reveal the undermentioned effects:

Ist: He will be untruthful, short in stature, beautiful and will be gifted with the power of assessing the good and bad of anything at the very first opportunity. 2nd: He will be endowed with a long lease of life, be charitable, very intelligent, irascible, given to bad ways and be not virtuous. 3rd: He will be endowed with imperishable wealth, yet thievish, be scholarly, will face only failures in all his undertakings, will cheat others and grab their wealth and be famous. 4th: He will enjoy various pleasures, will patronise his relatives, be always happy, intelligent, highly skilful, be successful in any undertaking and be logical in approach. 5th: He will be very wise, will quarrel frequently

with anger, be fond of female pleasures, yet be bereft of vices and will be charitable. 6th: He will be devoid of progeny, will resemble a female in appearance, will amass splendorous, goiden coins (i.e. be so wealthy), will not have moustache on the face, will always be happy, be endowed with supreme wisdom and will be praised by all for his excellent deeds. 7th: He will do others' jobs, be in servitude, be beautiful, be deterministic in his undertakings, will take to performing bad acts, will cheat others, be miserly and jealous. 8th: He will suffer from diseases, will torment women, be indolent and ascetic (i.e. be not a successful householder). 9th: He will suffer a severe cut in longevity, will successfully manouever his cause in any context, will spend heavily, will indulge in sinful acts like murder, destruction etc., will join base women and will acquire discases through them.

मन्वोदरः प्रचण्डो रक्ताग्रनसो बृहच्छराः शूरः। उन्नतमांसलवक्षा: सिहे प्रथमे भवेद्भागे । इदा। उन्नतविततललाटश्चतुरस्तनुविशालनेत्रश्च I दीर्यमुजोन्नतवक्षाः पृथयूप्रघोणो नद्वितीयेऽ्शे । ३६। रोमान्वितायतभुजश्वकोरनयनश्चलः शुभस्त्यागी । उन्नासिकस्तृतीये स्निग्धतनुर्बाहुवृत्तगलः ॥ ४०।। पृतमण्ड्गौरगात्रो दीर्घासितलोचनो मुटुशिरोजः। भिन्तष्यनिश्वतुथें पृयुकरचरणश्च भेककुक्षिः स्यात् ।।४१। घण्टाशिरोऽल्पकेशो सितघोणाक्षश्च लोमणाग्रतनुः। लम्बोदरप्रचण्डो दंष्ट्रोत्कटपीनहुन्मध्ये ॥ ४ २।। सुस्तात्परोममूर्तिः स्नग्धसमासितविलोचनो दीर्घः । श्यामः स्त्रीणां चतुरो विकत्वनो वादयप्टितः घः्ठे ।४३।। दीर्घाननः सिरालः पीनतनुः स्त्रीषु दुर्भगः क्रृष्णः। स्यात्सप्तमे सुचण्डो रोमचितः कूटनिष्ठुराचाधी ।४४।।

## उत्कृष्टवारिस्थरांगः सुभगो गम्भीरवृत्विकर्मा च । निःस्वः कूटकरः स्यादध्टमभागे प्रसूतश्च ॥४५।। रासममुखोऽसिताक्षो व्यालम्बभुजः सुपाव्णजंघश्च । श्वासनिपीडितवक्षा नवमांशे जायते मनुजः: ४९१।

38-46. Following effects will mature in the various Navamsas prevalling in Leo ascendant at birth:

First Navamsa: The native will have an even belly (like a llon), be fierce, will have sharp and blood-red nose and a big head, be valorous, and will possess a prominent and fleshy chest.

Second Navamsa: The native will have a prominent and broad foreface, a square body, broad eyes, broad chest, long arms and big nose.

Third Navamsa: The native will have hairy and broad arms, eyes akin to that of (Greek) patridge (said to live on Moon-beams), be fickie-minded, charitable, will have an elevated nose, pure white physique and round neck.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will have ash-coloured body akin to ghec, large and black eyes, soft hair, peculiar vokce, big hands and legs and stomach resembling that of a frog.

fith Navamsa: The native will have a bell-shaped head with limited hair, charming nose and eyes, hairy body and long belly, be fierce, and will have unsightly teeth and strong and broad cheek.

Stxth Navamsa: The native will have limited but soft hair on his physique, white and large eyes, be tall in stature, dark in complexion, will have proven skill (only) among females, be swaggering and be learned.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will have a long face, be sincwy, will have a prominent physique, be unfortunate in the matter of wife (or females in general), dark-complexloned, flerce, hairy-bodied, and be cunning and harsh in speech.

Elghth Navamsa: The native will be endowed with excellent speech, firm limbs, charming and majestic looks, be undutiful, poor and crafty.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will have a face akin to a donkey’s, will possess dark eyes, long arms, charming legs and be troubled by breathing disorders.

Notes: Remaining in the sign Leo, the Moon in various Navamsas, will produce specific effects thus:

Ist: He will be devoid of sons, will have blood-red eyes akin to that of Lord Yama, the Hindu god of death, will have a fearful’ body, be greedy tactful and will have abundant hair. 2nd: He will be a spendthrift, be happy, will have yellowish (or pale) eyes and will suffer from diseases of the ear. 3rd: He will suffer from a serious disease, apart from many other diseases, be strong, will take to both good and bad ways, be greedy, dejected and be virtuous. 4th: He will be learned, be endowed with a pure mind, will submit to females, will suffer from itch etc., be worried and will attach himself to virtuous affairs. 5th: He will be skilful, will face any peril with courage, be eloquent and delighting in speech, be irascible, mean and will live by trading. 6th: He will be a charitable king, be an agriculturist, a househokder, will frequently feel thirsty, be helpful and be not famous. 7th: He will be very famous, will incur some disease or the other continually, will protect living beings and be kind. 8th: He will be inauspicious, shortlived, will never be free from worries, will torment others and will blame others. 9th: He will be learned, eloquent, pure-hearted and be affectionate to his co-borns.

सारंगाक्षो वक्ता प्रदानसम्भोगवान्धनाइ्चश्च। श्यामोन्नतहुदयः स्यात्कन्याप्रथमांशके : In पूर्णाननः सुचक्षुः स्निग्धो मृदुवादशीलश्च। लम्बोदरश्चलः स्यातृद्वितीयभागे महोरुम्च ।४८II

स्सुटनासिका टः स्यात्प्रशर्तपादः सुपोनबाहुश्व । विस्पष्टवाक्च गौरः कन्यासु सुङ्क्तृतीयेऽशे ॥ ४६।। श्रुतवान् स्त्रीषु च रमते सुकुमारो मधुररक्तगौरश्च। तीर्णश्वतुर्यमागे प्रबोधनोधः हृशः सुमूर्धाक्षः १५०१।

स्यूलोव्ठबाहुरुन्नततनुः पृषुशिरोर्हांसः स्यात् । पञ्चमजः पृथुवक्षा: पराश्रयोव्बद्धजंघश्व । ५१।। स्तिग्धच्विः सुवाव्यः शर्ततनुः शास्त्रक्कन् मतिप्रचुरः । लिपिलेस्यकलाभिज्ञः सुमनाः षष्ठांशजो बिहारी च ॥५२।। हस्ववदनोन्नतांसः स्निग्धभुजोऽ्तीवगौरः स्थात् । सप्तमजः पृयुजठरः पृयुकरचरणोऽम्बुभीर्श्च ।।५३।। सुकुमारगौरवीर्घश्वित्रोन्नतद्वप्रचण्डमानी स्यात् । व्यालम्ब्रपीनबाहुः पिगलरोमाष्टमे : 11½४II ध्यातो मृदुसुखमूर्तिविशालनेत्रो बल सदृशसर्वः । चतुरो नवमेंऽशे स्यान्नतांसलेख्यादिविट्ठांश्च ।।५५।

47-55. The various Navamsas emanating from Virgo ascendant at birth will give the following effects:

First Navamsa: The native will possess eyes akin to that of an antelope, be a good speaker, be charitable, will enjoy sexual pleasures, be very rich, dark in complexion and large-hearted.

Second Navamsa: The native will have a charming face, charming eyes and fair complexion, be soft, argumentative, fickleminded and long-bellied.

Third Navamsa: The native will have blown nose, prominent feet, long arms, pure speech, fair complexion and be friendly.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will be learned, will be sportive with the fair sex, be beautiful, sweet, blood red in complexion, sharp, intelligent, emaclated and will have

charming eyes and face.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will have large lips and hands, big body, broad chest, strong ankles and will depend on others.

Sixth Navamsa: The native will have charming appearance, impressive speech, splendorous body, be an exponent of Sastras, be very intelligent, skilful in writing and fine arts, be good hearted and will take pleasure in walking or roaming.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will have a small face, elevated nose, compact arms, very fair complexion, prominent belly, hands and legs and will have fear for water.

Eighth Navamsa: The native will be very beautiful, fair in complexion, tall in stature, will have charming cycs, be fierce, honourable and will have long and stout arms and brown hair.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will be famous, will have charming physique, broad cycs, incomparable vigour, be skilful, be with stooping shoulders and be a learned writer.

Notes: The following effects will be realised by the various natives with the Moon’s Navamsa positions caused by her occupying sign Virgo at birth:

lst: He will be equal to a king (according to Yavanas), will suffer penury (according to Nataraja), be foolish, will have some physical defect, be fickle-minded and will live by begging (i.e. will not have a proper living). 2nd: He will be successful, will not give up any of his possessions, will violate his promise, be not endowed with good attributes and will live by breeding quadrupeds. 3rd: He will be religious, grateful, be not respectful of others, will blame others and will lose his mother right in boyhood. 4th: He will be valorous, argumentative, be a llar, will promote misunderstandings, be affectionate to quadrupeds and be charitable. 5th: He will be sickly disposed, be endowed with superior attributes, will be able to guess the future happenings, will be bereft of mother, be fond of dance, music etc., be very intelligent and be very thoughtful. 6th: He will be

endowed with wealth and grains, be virtuous, be deprived of father in his very childhood, be sickly, will live by trading, be tactful in his undertakings and be endowed with knowledge of Tantra. 7th: He will be wealthy, will enjoy a big formal position, be irascible, merciful, delighted, be a pet of his mother and be very famous. 8th: He will be a thief, will indulge in evil acts, wlll suffer from eye diseascs, be fond of music and will move in many distant places. 9th: He will be an artist, be attentive of his health, will face many evils, be ugly and be a fraudulent ascetic.

गौरो विशालनेत्रः श्लाधी दीर्घाननोऽर्थगोप्ता स्यात्। नवपथ्यकर्मकुशलस्तुलाधराद्यंशजः सुविर्यातः ॥५६।। प्लुतमण्डलनेत्रः स्यात्करालदन्तो निमग्नमध्यस्तु। सुगलो विस्तृतहृदयः कुतनुर्वनसंहतस्रृश्च ।५७।। गौरोऽश्वमुखः सुरदो महोन्नताक्षः कुशोऽपि लब्धयशाः । दीर्घशिरोर्ह्घोणस्तृतीयजः स्यात्सुचरणश्च ।।५८।। तन्वेसबाहुभीरस्तून्नतदन्तः कृशो - मृगतरलदृक् । ह्वस्वनसः सुविवादी श्यामोऽशीलश्चतुर्थजो भवति ।५६।। गम्भीरदृक्षस्वरात्मा सुहृतिप्रियः पञ्चमे ह्यामानो स्यात् । खरकेशः समनेत्रो मध्यप्रतिलग्नघोणरुश्चैव ।।६०। पीनांगो गौर: स्यादिशालनेत्रः सुनासिकार्यशः । स्निग्धनखः सुनयज्ञः वष्ठेऽशे शास्त्रविज्जातः ।६१। रक्तावदातमतिमान्गुरुहस्ततनुमहाललाटः स्यात् । लुब्: प्रचण्डदुर्गः सप्तमभागे मनस्वो च ।६२।। तंगांसगण्डभोक्ता कठिनतनुर्दीर्घकृष्णस्रः । निरणिक्तवाक्प्रशान्तः सङ्क्षस्त्वर्धमस्तकोऽप्टमजः ।।६३। स्वक्षः प्रसन्नगौर: समचार्तनुः पटुः कलाभिरतः ।। दाक्षिष्यहास्यनिरतो विटस्वभावो भवेन्नवमे ।६४।।

56-64. The various Navamsas out of Libra ascendant at birth will produce following effects:

First Navamsa: The native will be fair complexioned, broad eyed, praiseworthy, long-faced, skilful in business, happy and famous.

Second Navamsa: The native will have squint and round eyes, elevated (ill formed) teeth, depressed waist, charming neck, large (physical) heart, ugly body and compact brows.

Third Navamsa: The native will be fair in complexion, will have a face akin to that of a horse, be thin-bodied, famous, long-haired and long-nosed and will have beautiful legs.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will have weak hands, be timid, will havc ill-formed teeth (some placcd over others), weak body, rolling eyes, small nails, dark complexion, and be devoid of virtues and be miserable.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will have majestic looks, be firm in disposition, be not proud, rough-haired, even-eyed and will possess a beautiful nose.

Sixth Navamsa: The native will havc flcshy limbs, be fair in complexion, will have broad eyes, beautiful nose and white nails, be diplomatic and be learned in Sastras.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will be blood-red in complexion, be intelligent, will have long physique and long arms and a big head, be miserly, fierce and intelligent.

Eighth Navamsa: Thc native will have elevated shoulders and prominent neck, will enjoy pleasures, will have a coarse physique, long and dark brows, be a polite speaker and will have a beautiful chest and bruised head.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will have charming eyes, pleased mind, be fair complexioned, even and beautiful bodied, be skilful, fond of arts, be charitable and jocular.

Notes: Should the Moon be in Libra at birth, her various Navamsa positions will yield following results:

1st: He will take the side of others, will be famous,

valorous, endowed with knowledge of many branches of learning be versed in branches dealing with the Supreme Spirit, be very virtuous and highly intelligent. 2nd: He will be learncd, will ensure that his cnemies submit to him of their own and be innocent in disposition. 3rd: He will be always in dreams, be thievish, ascetic, will join the learned and will live by giving educative lecturcs. 4th: He will suffer a cut in the span of life, be very garrulous, wise, attentive, truthful, will not reveal his mind and will be liked by many women. 5th: He will be religious, will eke out his living by dealing in weapons, will indulge in evil deeds, be a householder and be devoid of virtues. 6th: He will be akin to a king, will keep up his promise, will exceed the boundaries of anger, be a talebearer, will befriend evil people and be addicted to females. 7th: He will be a justice, will also chcat, be skilful in mathcmatics and astrology, be a poet and a trader. 8th: He will be learned in Sastras, will be eclipsed with superiority complex, will havc knowledge of Mantras, will eat abundantly, be truthful, sickly, libidinous, happy and virtuous. 9th: He will be argumentative, will be ever strong, be short in stature, will join beautiful women to gratify his lust, and be talkative.

ह्वस्बोन्नतोब्ठघोणः सुललाट: स्याद्वृतंगगौरस्व ।

दर्दुर कुक्षिर्घटकोऽब्टमराशौ प्रथमनव मागे ।६५।। गौरः पृथ्वायतहुद्वाहुस्तास्रोप्रवृत्हितीये स् स्यात् । उट्टृत्तबल निहन्ता साहसक्दनल्पकेशश्च ।६६। प्राज्ञो दृढांसबाहुः प्रयतसुकेशो विशुद्धवाष्यः स्यात् । क्षानीनको बपुच्मान्यौरो रुचिराधरस्तृतीयेऽशे ॥ ६७। परदारद्रोहरतिः क्षेप्ता धीरश्चतुर्थजो वीर्घः । ह्दामोऽसितकेशाक्षो नटप्रगल्मश्च पोरोमांसः । ६८।। शर्चीरस्ताम्याक्षो मम्ननसः पञ्चमे धीरः । वृत्तोदरोग्रकर्मा व्यस्तबृढांगो यशस्वी स्यात् ॥ ६९

घृष्टो बरिष्ठबृद्धिः स्पष्टोब्बनसो गभीरसत्वः स्यात् । सुनयः प्रचण्डकर्मा वध्ठे दोऽल्पकचघन्रृश्च ।।७०।। बारितमुखः स्थिरांगः प्रविकीर्णरदः शिरावनद्धांगः। निम्नोदरः प्लुताक्षः शस्ततनुः सप्तमे भवेवंशे ।७१। स्कुटिताप्रनसः कालो विपम्मशीलो मलीमसांगःस्यात् । भिन्नोत्कर्दैः शिरोजे: सन्त्यक्तमतिस्तथाष्टमजः ।।७२। गौरो मृगाक्तिमृदुः प्रशान्तपियाक्षरोमदृढपीनः । सत्सम्मतो गुरुर्णां मतः प्रजातो नवमभागे।७३।। 65-73. The ascendant Scorpio will produce various following effects according to the nine Navamsas thereof.

First Navamsa: The native will be short in stature, will have prominent lips and nose, charming forehead, strong and fair complexioned body with belly akin to that of a frog and will act as a marriage broker (ascertaining gencalogies and negotiating marital alliances).

Second Navamsa: The native will be fair in complexton, will possess a strong and broad chest and shoulders and reddish eyes, will conquer his enemies, be valorous and will have abundant hair.

Third Navamsa: The native will be learned, will have strong shoulders and arms and beautiful hair, be endowed with clear speech, fair complexion and charming lips. He is born of a virgin.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will be intent upon joining others' wives, will induce others to be active, be valorous, tall, dark in complexion, dark-haired and dark eyed.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will be majestic, will possess copper-red eyes, depressed nose, be courageous, proud, will perform fearful acts, be famous and will have a strong physique.

Sixth Navamsa: The native will be impudent, intelligent of a high order, will have elevated nose and great

strength, will be endowed with knowledge of justice (or be diplomatic), be skilful, will possess less hair and compact brows.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will have a split face, strong body, teeth in various sizes, depressed belly, squint sight and be very splendorous.

Eighth Navamsa: The native will have blown nose, be dark in complexion, devold of virtues, dirty in appearance, will posscss stiff hair and be foolhardy.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will have a fair coloured physique, be beautiful like a deer, will possess calm and tawny eyes and similar hair, and strong body and be amiable to elders.

Notes: The Moon in the sign Scorpio will produce the following effects through the various Navamsas thereof:

1st: He will be long lived, wise, pious, irasciblc, will have a single marriage acquiring a beautiful wife and be cunning in speech. 2nd: He will be sharp, truthful, endowed with knowledge of past and future events, virtuous, versed in Mantras, Yagnas etc., and be well-mannered. 3rd: He will be righteous, skilful in playing musical instruments and will join many women. 4th: He will be long lived, be exceedingly charming in appearance, will speak below the whisper, be very learned and very rash in speech. 5th: He will be endowed with a wife devold of character, be poisonous in speech, will have elder brothers/sisters, will be cheating in disposition and will suffer from itches etc. 6th: He will be cruel, egoistic, sarcastic, splendorous and happy. 7th: He will enjoy pleasures, will please one and all with his knowledge of music and poetry, be righteous, very sensuous and sickly. 8th: He will be very learned, will suffer eye diseases, be inflicted with evil tendencies, will take credit for other's work, and be an ascetic. 9th: He will be endowed with progeny, be cunning, harmful and will speak high of his own people.

सुबहन्नसोजदृष्टिः स्फुटाप्रभाषी सुदन्तरोमा च। शौर: सुबद्धवृवणश्चापाद्यांशे प्रचण्डः स्यात् ॥७४।। प्रोत्तुंगशिराः स्थिरा धीविस्तीर्णाक्षो गुरस्फयूरुश्च । विहृताग्रनसो बीर्घो महाहनु: स्यावृद्धतीयेऽशे ।७५।। शिक्षाशास्त्रमतिज्ञः . प्रगल्भगम्भीरमूतिसुनयश्च । स्त्रीवल्लभो मनस्वो तृतीयजो हास्यशिल्पनः ।।७६।। इश्षो मधुमण्डलवृक्गौरः कचद्धपविवृद्धकुक्षिः स्यात् । प्राज्ञोऽ्टनः सुकेशः पृथुशुममूतिश्वतृ्ये स्यात् ।७७।। पृथुकर्णनेत्रवदनः प्रवृद्धहरिविग्रहो महास्रः स्यात् । पीनोन्नतांसहस्तः पञ्चमजो गूढरोमदृढबुद्धिः ॥७८।। सिन्धासितान्तपृथुदृक् महाललाटः सुबृत्तिकाध्यरतः । पृषुपोनमुलो होनः वच्ठे विद्वत्क्याभिरतः ।७६।। श्यामो मृदुर्वचस्वी तुंगशिरा: संग्रहानुसन्धिरतः । बीर्घो विशालनयनो दाक्षिष्यपटुश्च सप्तमजः ।८०।। चिपिटाग्रनासिकः स्याह्वस्तीर्णशिराः सुबद्धवरस्च । विस्रान्तवृक् प्रभाची गुरुव्वभिमतोऽथ्टमांशमवः ॥८१।। गौरो ह्याकृतिमुखो दीर्घासितदृक् तयाल्पवाष्यः स्पात् । सत्यः सतां विषादी नवमे कुटिलोरजंघर्च ।८२।। 74-82. The native born in Sagittarius ascendant but in its various Navamsas will obtain following effects:

First Navamsa: The native will have charming big nose, will possess sight akin to that of a goat, be a glfted speaker, will have charming teeth and hair, be fair in complexion, will have inset testicles and be flerce.

Second Navamsa: The native will have a prominent

head, be firm in disposition, will have large eyes, strong waist and knees, ugly nose, tall stature and firm cheeks.

Third Navamsa: The native will have skill in educating and in finc arts, be majestic, just, fond of females, intelligent and jocular.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will be skilful, be tawny in complexion, will possess round eyes, fair-coloured physique and a belly akin to tortoisc, be intelligent, wanderingnatured, will have charming hair and charming appearance.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will have large ears, eyes and face, will possess a (majestic) physique like a lion, widely spread eye brows, strong shoulders and arms, hairless physique and firm disposition.

sixth Navamsa: The native will have beautiful and large eyes, broad forehead and broad face, be a poet, be mcan and interested in scholarly discussions.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will be dark in complexlon, soft in disposition, will keep up his promise, will have a prominent head, will be interested in accumulating savings, be tall in stature, will possess broad eyes and be liberal.

Elghth Navamsa: The native will be flat-nosed, broadheaded, inimical, erratic sighted, garrulous and be dear to elders.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will be fair in complexion, will have a face akin to that of a horse and broad and dark eyes, will speak sparingly, be truthful, miserable and will possess crooked walking limbs.

Notes: The Moon placed in the sign Sagittarius will reveal the undermentioned effects due to the various Navamsas thereof:

ist: He will enjoy pleasures, be fond of vegetables grown under the earth (like cabbage, potato, onion etc), be sinful in acts, will always suffer from discases and bilious imbalances. 2nd: He will be charitable, will have the sky as

limit in scholarship, be wise, be very honourable and be kind to all. 3rd: He will be endowed with virtuous friends, be versed in black magic etc., be splendorous, lazy and be fond of fruits. 4th: He will be kingly in status, valorous, bereft of anger, successful over his enemies and will suffer throat disorders. 5th: He will be an excellent person, be devoid of progeny, be valorous, tale-bearing and will enjoy only a medium span of life. 6th: He will enjoy a royal status, be splendorous, famous, will receive help from friends in times of evils and will join whores. 7th: He will be a sweet speaker, be ever happy, very wealthy, will lose his mother in his mide-life, will suffer from diseases throughout his life, will have superior disposition and will be versed in many ways. 8th: He will be very wealthy, will suffer pains of the back, will indulge in unjust acts, be cunning and will lose his father in boyhood. 9th: He will enjoy royal status, be very intelligent, be versed in the branch of justice and be modest.

विरलाप्रदः श्यामः प्रभिन्नवाक् खरशिरोर्हः स्यातः । गीताध्वहास्यनिरतो मकराद्ये चलधनः कुशांगः स्यात् ।।८३।। अलसराठः कुटिलनसो गीताभिरतिविशालवेहश्च । प्रचुरांगनामु निरतो बहुभाषी स्याद्द्वितीयजः कल्प्यः ।।८४।। गान्धर्वकलाकामः स्यातो गौरोच्द्वृक् सुनसः । बहुमित्रबन्धुरतिमांस्तृतीयजः स्विष्टकर्मा रक्तासित्त्ताक्षो महाललाटस्तु दुर्बलांगकरः । भवति हरि विकीर्गकेशश्चतुर्यजो विरलदन्तवाष्यः स्यात् ।।८६।। उद्गण्डघोणकुक्षिर्भवति हि भोक्ता सुवासिनीशक्तः । श्यामो वृत्तोरुमुजः पञ्चमभागे स्वरारम्भः ।च७।। स्न/धच्छविः सुबेवः कामरतः सूक्ष्मसमरदसुवक्ता । बध्ठांशजः पृयुहनुर्महाललाट: पुमान् भवति ।८८।।

श्यामोऽलसः सुभाषो कुञ्चितकेशः बृहत्तनुः कठिनः । मृदुपादपाणिमतिमान् सप्तमजः शीलसम्पन्नः ।।८६।। गम्भीरवृक्मुघोणो रक्तास्यो भिन्ननखशिरोजः स्यात् । उद्बद्धतनुः शक्तोऽव्टमजो घटपृथुललाटश्च ।।६०।। विपुलाक्षिहृत् सुमेत्राः पूर्णमुखो गीतवाद्यनिरतश्च । माधुर्यसरवयुक्तः साधुर्नवमे भवेत् सुनयः ॥ ११। 83-91. The various Navamsas resulting in the ascendant Capricorn will emanate the following effects:

First Navamsa: The native will have intersticed (l) and outwardly visible teeth, be dark in complexion, will speak with broken words or be stammering ( uf T%), will have coarse hair, be famous, be interested in music and amusement, be emaciated and will have fluctuating wealth.

Second Navamsa: The native will be indolent, crafty, crooked nosed, fond of music, broad bodied, be interested in many females, will prattle much and be skilful.

Third Navamsa: The native will have lust for music, be famous, fair complexioned, be endowed with superior looks and charming nose, will be fond of many friends and relatives and achieve fulfilment of desires.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will have round eyes with a mix of blood-red and black hue, large forehead, emaciated body and thin arms, scattered hair, intersticed teeth and broken speech.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will have prominent neck, and belly, will enjoy pleasures, be attached to women, be dark in complexion, will have round knees and arms and will attain successful beginnings in his undertakings.

Sixth Navamsa: The native will possess a splendorous body, will attire charmingly, be libidinous, will possess small and even teeth, be a good speaker and will have big cheeks and large forehead.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will be dark in complex-

ion, be indolent, be an eloquent speaker, be short-haired, big-bodied, be harsh in disposition, will possess soft hands and legs, be intelligent and very virtuous.

Eighth Navamsa: The native will be endowcd with majestic sight and charming nose, reddish face, uneven nails and hair, grotisque body and forehead protruding like a pot.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will have broad chest and large eyes, high intelligencc, fully devcloped facc, interest in musical studies, be endowed with sweetness and strength, be gentle and diplonatic.

Notes: The following effects will come to pass if the Moon is placed in Capricorn in the various Navamsas thereof:

Ist: He will be inimical to his own friends, be not charitable, be mean-minded, be strong and will attain supreme position in his native country. 2nd: He will be honourable, be a skilful speaker, be cgoistic, corpulent and irascible. 3rd: He will be righteous, valorous, strong, charitable, learned in many matters, will trade with many countries and be affectionate to his own people. 4th: He will be honourable, corpulent, devoid of progeny, be after sexual pleasures, be always happy and egolstic. 5th: He will be virtuous, devoted to the Supreme Spirit, endowed with knowledge of the past, present and future (i.c. be an astrologer), be interested in pleasures of the world and be not merciful. 6th: He will be learned, libidinous, always subjected to diseases, very wealthy, be able to answer about anything and be unkind. 7th: He will be charitable, attached to courtezans, wealthy and be an agriculturist. 8th: He will be long-lived, unable to tokerate hunger, be after women, be doubtful in disposition, be dark bodied and corpulent. 9th: He will be learned, will keep up his promise, be wise, will undergo many ordeals and will be versed in many branches.

स्यामो मृदुः कशांगः पीनहनुः शास्त्रकाव्यमतिः। कामी रतिमान् कान्तः कुम्मस्याद्यांशके भवेज्जातः ।।ह२।। त्वङ्नखदृष्टिशिरोजः खरश्च सुविपम्नवत्सलः सातुः। दीर्षो विशिरा मूर्वों द्वितीयभागे भवेउजातः ।ह३।। संसक्ततनु: प्रमदाप्रियश्व वैडूर्यकान्तिधरः । शासत्रार्षवित् प्रयोव्ता तृतीयनवभागसंजातः ।।E४।। कान्तानुर्तो गौरो विदारितास्यो रिपुप्रणाशकरः । गम्भीरधीरसत्त्वश्चतुर्वजो भोगरतियुक्तः ॥ ५॥ स्पष्टार्थवित्कलातः खररोमधराङ् घरुत्रः स्यात्। संरुद्धगण्डकर्णः पञ्चमजः कृष्णवर्णंस्व IE ९।। व्याधाननः प्रगल्मः कृञ्चितकेशः सुनिश्वितार्यश्व । व्याघमृगोरगहन्ता षष्ठेऽशेवल्लभो : 18 मेषाक्षिमुस्तीक्ष्णो ग्राम्यरतिः स्त्रीवु परिभूतः। पित्तरगदितदेह सप्तमजः सत्वधृतियुक्तः ॥E८।। स्वरसस्वबुद्धिरतिमान्नरेन्रयोधो नरेश्वरः सुभयः । स्यूलरदो विपुलाक्षः कुम्भे स्यादव्टमेंऽशके पुरुषः ॥CEI श्यामः भ्यामाप्रदो विश्लेषितः सुधनदारपुत्रयुतः । नवमांशजः सुवाव्यः प्रथितः शक्तो भवेत्युरुषः ।।१०o11 92-100. The various Navamsas ascending while Aquarius is on the cast will produce following effects:

First Navamsa: The native will be dark in complexion, be soft in disposition, will have an emaciated body and prominent cheeks, be learned in poetry and Sastras, be li. bidinous, interested in carnal pleasures and be splendorous.

Second Navamsa: The native will possess coarse skin, nails, sight and hair, be kind to the helpless, be gentle, be tall in stature, foolish and will have a ‘distinct' head.

Third Navamsa: The native will have a compact body (limbs sticking close to each other), be fond of females, will possess the splendour of lapis lazult, be learned in the meanings of Sastras, and will act accordingly.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will be fond of women, be fair in complexion, will have a split face, will destroy enemies, be majestic, courageous and be fond of pleasures and sexual enjoyments.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will be learned in clear meanings of Sastras and in fine arts, will have coarse hair on legs, concealed (not prominent) neck and ears, and be dark in complexion.

Sixth Navamsa: The native will have a face resembling that of a tiger, be bold, short-haired, will have unchanging aims, will kill living beings viz tiger, deer, snake etc., and be dear to king.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will have eyes and face resembling that of a goat, be fierce in disposition, be delighted in village life, insulted by females, will suffer diseases of bilious imbalances and be endowed with strength and courage.

Eighth Navamsa: The native will possess an undiminishing strength, firm, by disposition and affection, be a warrior with the king or be a king himself, be beautiful and will have strong tecth and broad eyes.

Ninth Navamsa: The native will be dark in complexion, will possess unclean and elevated teeth, be disunited from his wife, children and wealth, be an affable speaker, and be famous and skilful.

Notes: The Moon in the various Navamsas rising out of her position in Aquarius Rasi will give the following effects:

1st: He will be timid, virtuous, lean, short, valorous and fair in complexion. 2nd: He will always move among the fair sex, will possess inestimable wealth, will indulge in evil acts, be corpulent and be endowed with charming hair. 3rd: He will be learned, splendorous, learned, affectionate to

quadrupeds and other living beings and be excellent in disposition. 4th: He will be affluent, irascible, doubtful, and will belong to a mean race. 5th: He will be happy, disposed to eating often, will complete his undertakings successfully, will suffer bilious complaints, be in servitude and be very intelligent. 6th: He will be endowed with children, be capable of protecting his ownself from evils and will be longsighted in disposition. 7th: He will be adventurous, endowed with broad face, affectionate to his wife and be a successful warrior. 8th: He will achieve the efficacy of supreme Mantras to see the Almighty, will not be vexed even with the most difficult assignments and will adjust himself with others to achieve his aim. 9th: He will be very intelligent, be free from misery, be virtuous, learned in Vedas, be fond of eating in others’ houses and will suffer from bilious disorders.

शौरोऽपि रक्तदेहप्रमोमृदुस्त्रीमतिः प्रचलचित्तः ।

ह्वस्वगलः क्कशमध्यो मीनस्याद्यांशके पुरुषः ॥१०१।। पृबू रोन मुग्ननासः क्रियाप दुमाँससुग्रुविरदेहः । काननपर्वतचारी बृहचिधराः स्यादिद्धतोयांशे ।१०२ ॥ गौरः शठः सुवक्षुः शस्ततनुर्वर्मवान्मुविद्वांद्व । द्वाक्षिष्यवान्विनोतस्तृतीयजो रूपवांश्चतुरः ।।१० ३। गुणवान्विपन्नशीलः प्रवृद्धसेवी क्रियापटुविद्वान् । सर्वाधिको • नयतस्तुंगनसः स्य रवतुथे तु ।१०४।। बीर्घोऽसितः प्रतापी त्वशान्तधीः स्वल्पनासिकः स्वक्षः । हिसारतिः शुभरबो दुष्प्सहः पञ्बमे प्रलापी हद्दात्।।१०५। कान्तः प्रतापगु गवान्त्रसन्नवंशोऽल्पनासिको मानो । तिर्यग्वदनः स्यातः बव्ठेऽधे स्यात्तया निपुणः ।।१०६।। पुरुषाभिमानपरकुद्धर्मरुचिः श्रेठकश्च सचिवः स्यात्। प्रबलो विषादशीलः शठोऽस्वरः सप्तमे भ्रादे ।१०७।।

वीर्घो बृहचि्रो स्यात्कृशोऽलसो रूक्षनेत्रकेशश्च । मन्दात्मजोऽर्यनिरतो रणकुशलो ह्वार्टमे भागे ।१०८।। ह्वस्वो मृदुः सुधीरो विशालवक्षोऽक्षिनासिकः सिनग्धः । विहितांगबृद्धिगुणवान्नवमेंऽशे स्यात्पुमान्ध्यातः ।१०६।

101-109. Should Pisces ascend at birth, the various Navamsas thereof will yield following specific effects:

First Navamsa: Though the native may be white in complexion, his body will reveal the splendour of blood-red hue; he will be soft in disposition, be akin to a female in mental make up, (ie. will act like a female), be fickleminded and will have a short neck and emaciated waist.

Second Navamsa: The native will have a big nose, be skilful in his assignments, will eat meat and the likc, be endowed with a charming physique, will wander in forests and hills and will have a big head.

Third Navamsa: The native will be white in complexion, be crafty, will possess beautiful eyes, be beautiful, righteous, learned, courteous, modest and charming in appearance.

Fourth Navamsa: The native will have praiseworthy attributes, will fall into adversity ( fवपीt: ): will serve aged people, be skilful in his assignments, learned, very strong, versed in justice and will have elevated nose.

Fifth Navamsa: The native will be tall in stature, dark in complexion, be valorous, be not peaceful, will have a small nose and charming eyes, be fond of torturing others, be impatient, will possess beautiful teeth and prattle.

Sixth Navamsa: The native will be self-respected, righteous, excellent, strong, miserable, crafty, unsteady and be a minister.

Seventh Navamsa: The native will be self-respected, will show interest in other religions, be excellent, be a minister, be strong, miserable, cruel and unsteady.

Eighth Navamsa: The native will be tall in stature, will

have a big head, be emaciated, indolent, will have uneven (or dirtied) eyes and hair, will have foolish children (or a few children), be interested in earning money and be skilful in war (or quarrels).

Ninth Navamsa: The native will be short, soft in disposition, courageous, broad chested, broad eyed, big nosed, be bright, will have a broad physique, be intelligent, virtuous and famous.

Notes: The Navamsas arising out of the Moon’s placement in the sign Pisces will yield the undermentioned effects.

1st: He will enjoy all pleasures, be highly virtuous, wealthy and happy. 2nd: He will enjoy royal status, be very wealthy, endowed with the nine kinds of gems and will have thick hair on his person. 3rd: He will always be dejected, patient and will undergo many miseries. 4th: He will be endowed with progeny, be very pious, irascible, be always endowed with happiness and be belligerent. 5th: He will be happy, will possess many vices, be very learned and be an agriculturist. 6th: He will create quarrels by speaking swectly, be learned and be always happy. 7th: He will be endowed with health, boldness and female pleasure. 8th: He will be eloquent in speech, be foollsh, and indigent. 9th: He will be very miserable, bold, belongs to a mean family, will win over his enemies and be adventurous.

यस्प्रोक्तं राशिफलं द्वादशमागेऽपि तत्फलं वाच्यम्।

सत्तमभागसमानं शेषेतु विनिदिशेत्राज्ञः ।।११०।

110. ABOUT OTHER DIVISIONS: The effects described in regard to individual signs apply to Dwadasamsa of each sign as well. Regarding effects for various other divisions not explained, the effects related to Sapthamsas will apply.

इति कल्याण वर्मविरवितायां सारावल्यां नष्टजातके नवमांसचिन्ता-

नामैंकपञ्वाशोऽध्यायः ।।५१।।

Thus ends the 51st Chapter entitled "EFFECTS OF NA- VAMSAS (lost horoscopy)” in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अथ नव्टजातकाध्यायः ॥५२॥

## Chapter 52

## Lost Horoscopy

प्रश्नकाले \_\_\_ वलगस अपरे बक्षिणे ब्रूयाञ्जन्म पूर्वाधिप्युत्तरायणे । सम्पृच्छतो बुधः ।११।

1. THE NATAL AYANA: If the ascendant at the time of query (related to tracing the lost birth data) be within the first 15° of a sign, the birth was in Uttarayana and beyond these degrees Dakshinayana.

ऋतुर्वाचयो दकाणांशे लग्नसंस्येऽपि वा प्रहे। अयनस्य बिलोमे तु परिवर्र्य परस्परम् ।।२।। शशिज्ञगुरुभिः सार्ध सितलोहितसूर्यजैः । ड्रेषकाणेऽ्धे भवेत्पूर्वे मासः पूर्वः परे परः ।३।। अनुपातात्तिथिः कल्प्या केचिदाहुरिनांशजाम् ।। ३३।

2-31/2. BIRTH RITU ETC.: The strongest of the planets in the Prasna Lagna indicates the Ritu (season) at birth. Should there be none in the ascendant, the lord of the ascendental decanate is the ruler of Ritu at birth. Should there be a clash in the Ayana and Ritu arrived at, the Ritu lords arrived at should be interchanged thus: the Moon for Venus, Mercury for Mars and Jupiter for Saturn. The respective month of the Ritu is decided depending on the first or second half of the decanate being ascendant. The degrees elapsed in the decanate will lead to the knowledge of the natal Tithi. Some say that this will lead to locating the natal Sun.

लग्नभागाद् द्विरभ्यस्तात् पंचभिर्लभ्यते गुरुः ।४।। ब्योनुमानाद्वर्षाणि द्वादश द्वादश क्षिपेत् । द्युरात्रिनामधेयेष् विलोमाज्जन्मसम्भवः ॥III लग्नभार्गः क्रमेणैव बेला मृग्याउनुपाततः । लग्नत्रिकोणराशीनां यो बली जन्महद्भवेत् ॥ ६।। शोर्यादि संस्पृतान् प्रष्टा पृच्छेत्राशिमाविशेत् । यावत्गतः शशी लग्नाचबन्द्रात्तावति जन्मभम् ।।७।। मीनोदये वदेःमीनं, लग्नांशसदृशोदयम् । लग्नाद्भानुद् काणे च यावत्यकाच्च तावति ।८।। विलग्नं कथयेत्राज्ञ इति शास्त्रस्य निश्चयः। लग्नगे वीर्यंगे वाऽपि बद्धायांगुलहते हुते ॥ EI रविभिर्जन्म शिष्टं हि कययेदविशंकितः ।।04।।

4-91/2. OTHER NATAL FACTORS: The query ascendant be multiplied by 2 and divided by 5 to get the natal Jupiter’s placement. This should be adjusted to actual placement guessing the native's age approximately, be increasing or decreasing multiples of 12. If the query ascendant is in a day sign, declare birth during night. Otherwise, the contrary prevails. The degree elapsed in the query ascendant will lead to the knowledge of elapsed portion of night or day as the case may be. Out of the query ascendant, the 5th and the 9th therefrom will indicate the natal Rasi (Moon sign), the strongest of the three prevailing. The parts of the body, viz. head etc., touched by the querist at the time of query will also indicate the Moon sign at birth. Alternatively the distance between the query ascendant and the query Moon may be added to the query Moon to get the position of the natal Moon. This does not apply to the Moon in Pisces at the time of query, (i.e. the natal Moon is in Pisces itself). The Navamsa Lagna at the time of query

may also be the natal ascendant. The number of decanates between the query ascendant and query Sun, when added to the query Sun, will yield the natal ascendant. The longitude of the planet in the query ascendant should be multiplied by the figure indicated by Chayangula (length of shadow) at that time. This should be divided by 12 the remainder of which will indicate natal ascendant.

तिष्ठतः शयनस्यस्य निविष्टस्योतिथितस्य च ।।१०।। लग्नादिकेन्द्रवेश्मानि पृच्द्धतो जन्मभं बवेत् । भावं विचार्य सकलं यग्यत्तुल्यं तु तत्तया ।११।

10-11. POSITION OF THE QUERIST: The following positions of the querist will indicate the natal ascendant. Standing, lying on the bed, sitting or getting up with respectively denote the first, 4th, 7th or 10th from query ascendant becoming natal ascendant.

संस्कारनाममात्रा त्रिघनविभवत च्छेषं द्विगुणाश्छायांगुर्लैः समायुक्तः । नक्षत्रं तद्धनिष्ठादि ।।१२।

12. PERSONAL NAME: The name formally given by parents to the native will indicate the natal star. Multiply the Matras (units) of the given name by 2 and add to it the Chayangula length. Divide this by 27 and the remainder when counted from Dhanishta will lead to the knowledge of birth star.

क्ष्तिहो दशगुणितो वसुर्भिमियुनालिको वर्णिंमेषौ । चुविधिः कन्यामकारी बार्णः शेवाः स्वसंमितेरेव १३। शुरुणा कुजेन भृगुणा बुधेन्दुभान्वाकिमिः कमशः ।

बर्वर्तुमासतिययो द्युनिशाभनवांशवेलार्च ।१४।।

एक्षं ऋरमेण हत्वा स्वविकल्पविभाजिताच्छेषम् । छुज्रं चबन्ति सर्वे नवदानविशोधने च पुनः ।।१५

13-15. ANOTHER METHOD: Note the longitude of the query ascendant. It should be multiplied by 10 if the ascen-

dant is Leo or Taurus, by 8 in case of Gemini or Scorpio by 7 in case of Aries or Libra, by 5 in case of Virgo and Capricorn, while for the rest of the signs by the number denoted when counted from Aries. (That is 4 for Cancer, 9 for Sagittarius, 11 for Aquarius and 12 for Pisces.) If the ascendant is occupied by Jupiter, the ascendant converted into minutes of arc by multiplied by 10, if occupied by Mars by 8, if by Venus by 7 and if by others by 5. Both the products so multiplied be added together. This product be multiplied again by 10 to know the year, Ritu and month; by 7 to know the Tithi and by 5 to arrive as Navamsa lagna and Ishta Ghati (birth time) and be respectively divided by their individual dividers (i.e. 120 for year, 2 for Ritu, 15 for Tithi and the like). Addition or reduction of 9 may be made in case of anomalistic answers.

यवनेन्द्रमदशनार्यः कथितं तदिहात्र सर्वमेव मया । किन्तु स्फुटं न सर्वं स्पष्टं सारं स्वतशिवन्त्यम्।।१६।।

16. I have explained lost horoscopic rules as expounded by Yavana, Bhadasana etc. But some of their rules are ambiguous. The student better understand using his Judgement.

पादत्रितयं विवलं यथा यथा संगतं भवेद्राशेः। पादत्रितयं विदलं दिनरजनीमानयोस्तयोत्क्रमशः ।।१७।। पृच्छक राशिसमानेदिवसनिशासंजिर्तज्यम् I अथवा तनुवद्राशेस्तद्गतखेटेश्च साधयेत् पिण्डम् ॥ १८।। क्षारच्नमविह्तान्नं प्रोद्गच्छति तावदेव नक्षत्रम्। अश्वमधामूलाद्यं विनवं सनवं क्रमादृक्षम् ।।१९।। तल्लिप्ताः सप्तहुताश्शेषाद्वारो भवेच्च रव्यादिः । शेषं प्राम्बत्कार्यं पृच्छकसूर्यादिभिर्दायम् ॥ २०। उद्यतदशा व्यतीता गम्याय विलोमतो भवेन्नित्यम् । तावत्संस्या योज्या नष्टविधौ कालपरिमाणे ।२१।।

17-21. LOST HOROSCOPY BASED ON MOON: Just as the ascending degrec helps trace back the lost details, the Moon sign may also be used. If the Moon sign at the time of query be a night sign, natal Moon was in a day sign and vice versa. Note in what quarter of the sign is the Moon at the time of query. Proportionately understand the natal Moon during day to night as the case may be. Multiply the query Moon by the same Rasi multiplication factor as explained supra and if necessary by planetary multipliers as applicable. Multiply the resultant product (arrived by adding the two) by 7 and divide by 27. The remainder will represent the birth star counted from Aswini. In case of rectification by adding 9 the counting is from Moola and if subtraction of 9 is done count from Magha. The pre-division product (i.e. the product before dividing by 27 as above) should be processed variously as suggested earlier to know the Ritu, month, Tithi etc., at birth.

इति कल्याणवर्मविरचितायां सारावल्यां नष्टजातकाध्यायो

द्विपञ्चाशोऽध्यायः ।५२।।

Thus ends the 52nd Chapter entitled "LOST HOROS- COPY” in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अया ब्टकवर्गाध्यायः ।।५३।। Chapter 53

## Ashtakavarga

उक्तो हि पवनवृद्धरष्टकवर्गो निवेदयति पुंसाम्। हेतुं शुभमशुभं वा प्रतिदिवसं सम्भबन्तमिह ।१।

1. In order to evaluate the auspicious and inauspicious effects in day-to-day life due to planetary transits, Yavanacharyas have explained Ashtakavarga (which are detailed below).

स्वात्केन्द्रायनवाष्टवित्तगृहगो भौमार्हसुन्वोरवि- जोवादायनवात्मजारिषु सितात् वड्द्वादशास्तस्थितः । चन्द्राद्वृद्धिषु बोधनात्सनबधीरिःकेषु लम्नाच्छुमः साम्बुद्वादशगोऽध्टवर्गविधिना संशोधितो भास्करः ।२।।

2. THE SUN'S ASHTAKAVARGA: The Sun contributes benefic dots in his Ashtaka Varga as under. From his ownself in the 1st, 2nd, 4th, 7th, 8th, 9th, 10th and 11th signs; from Mars and Saturn in similar signs; from the Moon in the 3rd, 6th, 10th and 11th signs; from Jupiter in the 5th, 6th, 9th and 1 1th signs; from Venus in the 6th, 7th and 12th signs; from Mercury in the 3rd, 5th, 6th, 9th, 10th, 11th and 12th signs and from the ascendant in the 3rd, 4th, 6th, 10th, 11th and 12th signs.

लम्नाद्भ्रातृदशायशत्रुवु शशी सास्तादिवु स्वाच्छमः भौमात्सार्थनवात्मजेषु रवितः साष्टांगनास्थो गुरोः। केन्द्राष्टार्कमवेषु धर्ममुखधीत्यायास्तखस्यः सितात् केन्द्रायत्रिसुताष्टयः शशिसुताद्धीत्र्यायवट्स्वर्कजात् ॥ ३।

3. THE MOON’S ASHTAKAVARGA: The Moon contributes benefic points in her Ashtakavarga as under. In the 3rd, 6th, 7th, 8th, 10th and 1 1th signs from the Sun; in the 1st, 3rd, 6th, 7th, 10th and 11th signs from her position; in the 2nd, 3rd, 5th, 6th, 9th, 10th and 1 1th signs counted from Mars; in the 1st, 3rd, 5th, 6th, 9th, 10th and 11th signs counted from Mercury; in the 1st, 4th, 7th, 8th, 10th, · 11th and 12th signs from Jupiter; in the 3rd, 4th, 5th, 7th, 9th, 10th and 1 1th signs counted from Venus; in the 3rd, 5th, 6th and 11th signs from Saturn and in the 3rd, 6th, 10th and 11th signs counted from the ascending sign.

भौमो वृद्धिषु सात्मजासु रवितः साद्यासु लग्नाच्छुभः

खन्द्राद्दिग्विफलेषु केन्द्रविवरस्वाप्तिस्थितः स्वाद्गृहात् ।

धर्मावाष्टमक्टकेषु रविजाज्सात्यायधीशत्रुषु शुक्कादन्त्यमवारिमृत्युवु गुरोः वड्लाभकर्मान्त्यगः ।४।

4. THE ASHTAKAVARGA OF MARS: Mars in his Ashtakavarga drops auspicious points in the various signs from the various planets and ascendant as denoted below. From the Sun in the 3rd, 5th, 6th, 10th and 11th signs; from the Moon in the 3rd, 6th and 11th signs; from his own position in the 1st, 2nd, 4th, 7th, 8th, 10th and 1 1th signs; from Mercury in the 3rd, 5th, 6th and 11th signs; from Jupiter in the 6th, 10th, 1 1th and 12th signs; from Venus in the 6th, 8th, 1 1th and 12th signs; from Saturn in the 1st, 7th, 8th, 9th, 10th and 1 1th signs and from the ascending sign in the 1st, 3rd, 6th, 10th and 11th signs.

ज्ञोऽध्टायाविशुभार्थबन्धुषु सबीच्रातृस्थितो भार्गवात् मौमार्योः सदशास्तगो रिपुभवच्छदान्त्यसंस्यो गुरोः । प्राप्त्यान्त्यारितपः सुतेवु तपनातस्वात्सत्रिककर्मादिषु स्वाज्ञायरिसुखाष्टमेषु शशिनो लम्नात्सपूर्वः शुभः ॥५।।

5. THE ASHTAKAVARGA OF MERCURY: Auspicious points are contributed by Mercury in his Ashtakavarga as under: From the Sun in the 5th, 6th, 9th, 11th and 12th

signs; from the Moon in the 2nd, 4th, 6th, 8th, 10th and 11th signs; from mars and Saturn in the 1st, 2nd, 4th, 7th, 8th, 9th, 10th and 12th signs, from his ownself in the 1st, 3rd, 5th, 6th, 9th, 10th, I lth and 12th signs; from Jupiter in the 6th, 8th, 11th and 12th signs; from Venus in 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 5th, 8th, 9th and 11th signs and from the ascending sign in the 1st, 2nd, 4th, 6th, 8th, 10th and 1 1 th signs.

केन्द्रायाष्टधनेषु भूमितनयात्स्वार्सत्रिषु ब्राह्मणो

भानोः सत्रिनवेषु घर्मदशधीस्वायरिगो भार्गवात्। धर्मायास्तधनात्मजेषु शशिनः कोणातृत्रिवड्धीव्यये

स्वाज्ञायाम्बुनवारिपुत्रतनुषु ज्ञात्सास्तगस्तृदयात् ।६।

6. THE ASHTAKAVARGA OF JUPITER: Jupitcr’s Ash. takavarga gains auspicious points as under. From the Sun in the 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 7th, 8th, 9th, 10th and 11th signs; from the Moon in the 2nd, 5th, 7th, 9th and 11th signs; from Mars in the 1st, 2nd, 4th, 7th, 8th, 10th and 11th signs; from Mercury in the 1st, 2nd, 4th, 5th, 6th, 9th, 10th and 11th signs; from his ownself in the 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 7th, 8th, 10th and 1 1th signs; from Venus in the 2nd, 5th, 6th, 9th, 10th and 11th signs; from Saturn in the 3rd, 5th, 6th and 12th signs and from the asecnding sign in the 1st, 2nd, 4th, 5th, 6th, 7th, 9th, 10th and 11th signs.

लग्नादातनयायरन्धानवगश्चन्द्रात्सितः - स्वात्साजेषु शुभो यमान्नवदशात्र्यायाष्टयंवास्बुषु ।

सव्ययः

व्यग्यायारिसुहुन्नवेषु रुधिराद्रिः फायर्ध्रेब्विनात्

ज्ञात् त्यायारिनवात्मजेव्वय गुरोर्धीखाष्टवर्मायगः ॥७।।

7. THE ASHTAKAVARGA OF VENUS: Mark benefic dots in the following places. From the Sun in the 8th, I 1 th and 12th signs; from the Moon in the 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 5th, 8th, 9th, 1 1th and 12th signs; from Mars in the 3rd, 4th, 6th, 9th, 1 1th and 12th signs; from Mercury in the 3rd, 5th, 6th, 9th and 1 1th signs; from Jupiter in the 5th, 8th,

9th, 10th and 11th signs; from his ownself in the 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 5th, 8th, 9th, 10th and 11th signs; from Saturn in the 3rd, 4th, 5th, 8th, 9th, 10th and 11th signs and from the ascending sign in the 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 5th, 8th, 9th and 11th signs. This is called the Ashtakavarga of Venus.

स्वादायात्मजवट्त्रिकेषु रविजः साम्त्राम्बरस्यः कुजात्

भानोः केन्द्धनायवृत्युषु तनोस्र्यायायारिखाद्याम्बुगः ।

आज्ञायाष्टनवान्त्यशत्रुवु बुधादिन्दोर्भवारित्रिषु

शुकावन्त्यभवारिषु हविजवरात् प्राप्यन्त्ययीतश्रुगः ।८।।

8. THE ASHTAKAVARGA OF SATURN: Saturn’s Ashtakavargs is prepared by inserting benefic dots in places as denoted below. From the Sun in the 1st, 2nd, 4th, 7th, 8th, 10th and 11th signs; from the Moon in the 3rd, 6th and 11th signs; from Mars in the 3rd, 5th, 6th, 10th, 1 1th and 12th signs; from Mercury in the 6th, 8th, 9th, 10th, 11th and 12th signs; from Jupiter in the 5th, 6th, 11 th and 12th signs; from Venus in the 6th, 1 1th and 12th signs; from his ownself in the 3rd, 5th, 6th and 11th signs and from the ascending sign in the 1st, 3rd, 4th, 10th and 1lth signs.

इत्युक्तं शुभमन्यदेवमशुचं चारक्रमेण ग्रहाः शस्ताशस्तविशेवितं विवधति प्रोत्ष्टमेतत्फलम् । स्वर्षस्वोचबसुहृद्गृहेषु सुतरां शस्तं त्वनिष्टं समं नीबारातिगताह्यानिव्टबहुलं शस्तं न सम्यक् फलम् ।।। रविवधिरो भवनं प्रविशन्ती गुरुस्गुजी गृह्मध्यसमेतौ ।

शनिशशिनौ खलु निर्गमकाले शाशितनयः फलवस्तु सदैव ।।१०।।

9-10. The places mentioned above are auspicious for the various planets and the others are inauspicious. Planets transiting signs with more benefic dots will reveal auspicious results while inauspicious results will come to pass in case of transits in signs with less benefic dots. Benefic results will be predominant if these signs happen to be exaltation, own, friendly etc. and malefic effects will be ordinary. Similarly in debilitation/inimical signs, malefic

results will be more and benefic effects will be ordinary. In giving transit effects, the Sun and Mars will be effective while in the beginning of the sign, Jupiter and Venus while in the middle of the sign and lastly Saturn and the Moon while in the end of the sign. Mercury is effective in his transit through the whole sign.

इति कल्याण वर्मविरचितायां अष्टकवर्गाध्यायो नाम त्रिपञ्चाशोऽ्ध्यायः ।।५३।।

Thus ends the 53rd Chapter entitled “ASHTAKAV- ARGA” in Kalyana Varma’s SARAVALI.

## अयाष्टकवर्गफलाध्यायः ।।५४II

## Chapter 54

## Effects of Ashtakavarga

तिर्यग् विश्वोर्र्वनन्दं गिरिगिरिशपदं न्यस्य चक्रं तद्र्व मेवाद्या राशयः स्पुग्रह्मणसहिताः शिष्टमिष्टस्य सद्म । तस्याघस्ताच्च मुख्यप्रह्गणमुदयं चाप्यधः क्षमां क्रमेण व्यस्यावः स्वीयचक्रे स्वपदसहितभाक् स्वाव्टके बिन्दुरेखाः ॥१।।

1. Draw 13 horizontal lines intersected by 9 parallel (vertically) lines (thus getting 96 squares) noting the names of the 12 signs from Aries onwards in a rightward manner and the names of the 7 planets and ascendant in the left hand side vertical columns. Mark the benefie dots for each planets' Ashtakavarga and malcfic points (where benefic points are absent) as per the positions told earlier.

पूर्णेबिन्दुभिरष्टभिः पदगतेहोनोऽपि भूपो भवेद् एर्ककोनतया क्रमात् फलविधिः सर्वेप्सिताप्तिर्यशः । वित्ताप्तिश्व सुर्वेः सहप्रियसुहृत्प्राप्ति विपच्छ न्यता वित्तानामपि हानिराधिक्शता शून्यक्रमे संक्षयः ।।२।।

2. GENERAL EFFECTS: If a planet is associated with 8 bindus—in the sign occupied by it, even an ordinary person will become a king with 7 bindus—fulfilment of all desires; with 6 bindus—fame and financial gains; with 5 bindus—happiness and friendship; with 4 bindus—eradication and misery; with 3 bindus—loss of money; with 2 bindus—worries; with 1 bindu—bodily emaciation and with the complete absence of bindus there will be evils at all times.

सूर्यस्याष्टसु बिन्दुषु क्षितिपतेराप्ता विभूतिर्वनं सप्तस्वद्मृतकान्तिसौस्यविभवः वट्सु प्रतापोन्नतिः । पञ्वस्वर्यसमागमः, सदसतोः साम्यं चतुष्के, त्रिकहे मार्गश्रान्तिरयो द्विके गदमयं रूपेऽय शून्ये मृतिः ॥३।।

3. EFFECTS OF THE SUN’S AV: The association of the Sun with particular number of bindus (auspicious points) in the sign occupled by him will lead to respective effects, thus; with 8 bindus—wealth from the king: 7 bindus-- transcendental beauty (Adbhutakanti), happiness and riches, 6th bindus—increase of valour and fame; 5 bindus—influx of money; 4 bindus—neither gainful nor losing; 3 bindus—fatigue due to (frequent) travels (or difficulties on account of travels, proneness of accidents and the like), 2 bindus—fear of diseases; 1 bindu—difficulties and absence of bindus—death (or troubles of a very severe nature).

इन्दोर्भोगविभूतयोऽ्य विभवा वस्त्राम्नगन्धोव्भवाः सन्मेत्री द्विजसंगमाद्धृतिमतो दुःखाड़चसोध्यस्थितः । द्वेवो बन्धुजनेः प्रियार्थविरहोऽ्कस्माद् विपद्दुस्तरा

शोकोढेगजकष्टमत्र नियतं प्रोष्तं फलानामिद्म ।४।।

4. EFFECTS OF THE MOON’S AV: Note the bindus in the sign occupied by the Moon. If she be associated with 8 bindus, the native will enjoy affluence and pleasures; 7 bindus—financial gains through robes, scented articles etc.; 6 bindus—acquaintance with the virtuous; 5 bindus—acquisition of courage and intelligence due to association with Brahmins (or the learned); 4 bindus— equilibrium of happiness and grief; 3 bindus—enmity with relatives; 2 bindus—separation from kith and kin and deprival of wealth; 1 bindu—evil and absence of auspicious points—grief and difficulties following excitement.

तारस्यार्थमहीसपत्नविजयाः सौभाग्यकान्तिप्रदाः राज्ञां बल्लभता प्रसिद्धगुणता साम्यं विपत्सम्पदोः भातृस्त्रीविरहो विपत्परिभवो राजार्नि पित्तज्रैः स्कोर्ट्दुवितगात्रता जठररुङ् मूर्धाऽक्षिरुङ् मृत्यवः ॥५।।

5. EFFECTS OF THE AV OF MARS: If Mars be associated with various benefic points as denoted below in the sign tenanted by him, the results due will be: 8 bindus—acquisition of wealth and land and victory over foes; 7 bindus—increase of fortunes and splendour; 6 bindus—royal favours; 5 bindus—increase of fame; 4 bindus—equality of wealth and calamity; 3 bindus—separation from co-born and conjugal partner; 2 bindus—troubles from king, fire and billous disorders; 1 bindu—ulcer and stomachial diseases and absence of bindus—diseases of the eye and difficulties comparable to death.

ज्ञस्य क्ष्मा पतिमायता द्रविणतो विज्ञानसौस्याप्तयः

सर्वोद्योगकलोदयो नवसुद्दत् प्राप्तिनिरुद्योगता । f वितथ्याकुजताऽर्हानिवरातः स्त्रीपुत्रभित्रादिभि- बेराद् घैर्यमतिक्षयोऽय सततं सर्वस्वहानिम्तिः ।।।

6. EFFECTS OF MERCURY’S AV: The effects of Mercury’s association with various auspicious points in the sign occupied by him will be: 8 bindus—honour from the ruler; 7 bindus—wealth, knowledge and happiness; 6 bindus--success in all undertakings; 5 bindus—new acquaintances; 4 bindus—unemployment; 3 bindus—mental worries on account of financial losses; 2 bindus—enmity with wife, children and friends causing loss of courage and wisdom; 1 bindu—evils of all kinds and absence of bindus will bring about death.

जीवस्याच्द्यशः मुखार्थनिचयः सौभाग्यसौस्याप्तयो

वासो वाहनहेमलब्धिरहितः वंसत्रियासिद्धयः ।

लाभच्छेदविहीनता श्रवणवु रुपुंस्त्वप्रणाशादयो

भूमृतकोपभयं गर्दैविकलता बन्ध्वर्वपुत्रक्ष्यः ।७।।

7. EFFECTS OF JUPITER'S AV: Based on the number of auspicious points, the sign occupied by Jupiter will produce the following effects. 8 bindus—spotless fame, happiness and growth of wealth;  $7$  bindus-fortunes and happiness; 6 bindus—acquisition of robes, conveyances, gold etc.; 5 bindus—destruction of enemies and success in undertakings; 4 bindus—no loss, no gain; 3 bindus—loss of hearing, sight and masculine vigour; 2 bindus—incurring royal wrath; 1 bindu—distress due to diseases and absence of bindus—destruction of relatives, wealth and progeny.

• शुक्रस्याखिलभोगवस्त्रवनिता-पुष्पान्नपानाप्तयो

भूषामौक्तकपौष्ट्यः प्रियबधूलाभः सुट्टत्संगमः । मध्यत्वं शुभपापयोर्जनपदग्रामोच्च विद्वेविता

स्वान्स्रंशरजः कफर्च विषमः सर्वापदां संगमः ।।८।।

8. EFFECTS OF AV OF VENUS: The association of Venus with various number of bindus will generate the undermentioned effects. 8 bindus—enjoyments of all kinds and acqulsition of robes, wife, scents, food, drinks etc.; 7 bindus—plentifulness of ornaments and pearls; 6 bindus— acquisition of equality of inauspicious and auspicious results; 3 bindus—enmity with pcople of his community and senior members of the village; 2 bindus—displacement; 1 bindu—phlegmatic disorders and absence of bindus— becoming a synonym of all kinds of evils.

सौरेर्रामपुरप्रजाग्यधिपता क्षासी खरोष्ट्राप्तयः

पूजा-चोरनिषादरसन्यरपतिमिर्धान्यार्यसौश्यागमः ।

अन्योपाश्रितसौस्यता

सुतवधूभृत्यार्थविध्वंसनं

बन्धोडेगरुजः महाऽध्नतया भार्यादिसर्यक्षयः ।।।

9. EFFECTS OF SATURN’S AV: If Saturn is associated with a certain number of bindus, the effects due will be: 8 bindus—lordship over villages, towns and pcople; 7 bindus—acquisition of female servants, asses and clcphants; 6 bindus—gain from thieves, hunters and army heads; 5 bindus—advent of wealth, corn and happiness; 4 bindus happiness caused by association with others; 3 bindusdestruction of progeny, wife, attendants and money; 2 bindus—imprisonment, emotions and diseases; 1 bindu/ absence of bindus—loss of wealth, family etc.

इत्रं होराशास्त्रं रचितं कल्याणकोविदेनेव। पूर्वाचार्येनिमितमवलोष्य मुदं प्रयातु द्वज्ञः ॥12011 पौलिश-वसिष्ठ-रोमश-यवन बादरायणा: शक्तिः । अत्रिर्च भरदाजो विश्वामित्रो गुणाम्निकेशी च।१३॥1 गर्ग-पराशरजीवा एतरन्येश्व विस्तरं रचित्तम्। कथयति शास्त्रेवूष्तं जातकमिति चित्रयुप्तकृत्तम् ।।१२३।
